Text:

vaiyākaraṇabhūṣaṇasāraḥ
darpaṇavyākhyā
ramāpremā''jjajjagadavanadakṣaṃ madhuhṛta-śrutistomāhṛtyā parijanitavedānanamudam|
akhaṇḍānandāḍhayaṃ nikhilajanahṛtkañjanilayaṃ hayagrīvaṃ vande prakṛtakṛtivanaghnakṣatikṛte ||
satyekasminnapi bādhake samavahitasādhakasahastrādapi kāryotpatteradarśanādapekṣitaprāripsitapratyūhāpohāyānuṣṭhitaṃ bhagavatstutirūpaṃ maṅgalaṃ granthakṛcchiṣyaśikṣārthamādau nibadhnāti śrīlakṣamīramaṇamiti| adhikaraṇavyutpannalyuḍantaramaṇaśabdena kartṛṣaṭhayantalakṣmīpadasya samāsaḥ| tena ca śrīsahitaśabdasya madhyamapadalopī samāsaḥ, śākapārthiṃvāderākṛtigaṇatvāt| tadupādānañca saśaktikasyaiva bhagavato jagannirmāṇakāraṇatvamiti dhvayitum| naimīti "ṇu stutau" iti dhātorasmacśabdāprayoge'pi gamyamānatadarthasāmānādhikaraṇyasya lakārārthakarttari sattvād varttamānakriyādyotakalaḍuttamapuruṣaḥ| `asmadyuttamaḥ' ityatra pūrvasūtrāt sthāninītyasyānuvṛtteḥ| stutiścotkṛṣṭaguṇavattvaprakārakabodhānukūlo vyāpāraḥ| sa eva ca prakṛtadhātvarthaḥ| bodharūpadhātvarthaphalāśrayatvāllakṣmīramaṇasya karmatā| evañca vaiyākaraṇamate prakate śrīmahitalakṣmīramaṇāśrayakotkṛṣṭaguṇavattvaprakārakabodhānukūlo madabhinnaśrayako varttamānaśabdhaprayogarūpo vyāpāra iti vākyārthaḥ| nanvetacchāstrapravarttakatvena śivasyābhyarhitataratvāttatstutyātmakamaṅgalamapi kuto nācaritamata āha gaurīramaṇarūpiṇamiti| svarūpapararūpapadena gaurīramaṇapadasya ṣaṣṭhīsamāsastato'dantatvanibandhano matvarthoya itiḥ| gaurīramaṇo rūpaṃ svarūpaṃ yasyeti iti sūtre "asubvataḥ" iti prayogamudāharatā bhāṣyakṛtā'nādṛtatvānna matvarthoyānupapattiḥ| dṛśyate cedṛśasthale prayogadvaividhyam-
śivāya viṣṇurūpāya viṣṇave śivarūpiṇe| ityādi|
tathāca tadabhinnamityarthaḥ| pramāṇaṃ cātroktapurāṇavākyameva| evañca kāryavaicitryasya kāraṇavaicitryaniyamyatvājjagadavanādikāryavaicitryopapattaye tattad‌guṇapradhānamūrttiparigraho bhagavato na pāramārthiko bheda iti stutikarmatā śivasya nānupapanneti bhāvaḥ| utkṛṣṭaguṇavattvamevāviṣkariti yata iti vādāntimate māyāśabalasya brahmaṇo jagadupādānatvāt "janikarttuḥ"  ityupādānahetāvapādānasaṃjñānibandhanā pañcamī| `guṇa' ityukteḥ "vibhāṣā"  ityasya na prasaktiḥ| tathāca yasmādupādānahetorityarthaḥ| sarvaṃ jagat sphoṭarūpaṃ vivarttata iti yojanā| sphuṭatyabhivyaktībhavatyartho'smāditi sphoṭo nāmādyatmakaḥ śabdaḥ| bāhulakādapādāne dhañ, rūpyate-nirūpyate iti rūpamarthaḥ, tayoḥ samāhāradvāndve napuṃsakaikavacanāntam| vācyavācakasvarūpamiti tadarthaḥ| "nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇi"  iti śruteḥ etat atyantasānnidhyena viṣayīkriyamāṇam vivarttate utpadyata ityarthaḥ, dhātorupasṛṣṭatvāt| nanu nāmarūpātmakasya "sadeva somyedamagra āsīt"  iti śrutyā sanātanatvabodhanāt kathamutpattiśālitvamata āha jagaditi vartamāne pṛṣat" iti nipātanātsādhu| tathāca gacchati-tirodhatta iti vyutpattyā tirodhāna ukte yattirodhatte tadāvirbhavatīti niyamenāvirbhāvasya lābhātsa evātropasṛṣṭārthaḥ| evañcaja sattvenaitasya sanātanatve'pi nāmatvādinā''virbhavādyabhyupagamānnoktaśrutivirodho nāpi "tasmādetasmādvā ātmata ākāśaḥ sambhūtaḥ"  ityādyutpattibodhakatadvirodhaḥ| yatkiñcinnāmarūpātmakasyānyato'pi sambhavādāha sarvamiti yāvadarthakam| utpattirupalakṣaṇam-avanādeḥ| tena jagatkartṛtvādirūpotkṛṣṭadharmasya stutyatā'vacchedakasya lābhaḥ| atra ca "yato vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante yena jātāni jīvanti yat prayantyabhisaṃviśanti"  ityādyāḥ śrutayaḥ pramāṇam|
matāntare yata iti pañcamī nimittahetutāyām| vyupasṛṣṭatudhātorādyakṣaṇasambandharūpotpattirarthaḥ| nanu sarvāntargataviyadādyātmakapadārthasyānāditayā sathamutpattimattvamata āha jagaditi sthāvara-jaṅgamātmakamityarthaḥ| tathāca gacchati-naśyatīti vyutpattyā vināśitve bodhite vināśibhāvasya janyatvaniyameva tasyotpattirna viruddheti bhāvaḥ| etatkalpe sphoṭapadaṃ śāstrānumānapratītiparam| tādṛśaṃ rūpaṃ yasyeti vyutpattyā sphoṭarūpamiti bhagavato viśeṣaṇaṃ bodhyam| śeṣaṃ pūrvavat| yadvā stutyatā'vacchadekarūpaṃ pradarśayituṃ viśiyituṃ viśinaṣṭi sphoṭarūpamiti vakṣyamāṇavyulpattyā samastārthaprakāśakasvarūpamityarthaḥ| tadupapādayanneva stutyatā'vacchedakāntaramapi darśayati yata iti yasmādupādānāditi sārvavibhiktikartāsanā yasminnadhiṣṭhāne iti vā'rthaḥ| vivarttaścātāttviko'nyathābhāvaḥ| aparityaktapūrvarūpasya rūpāntaraprakārakapratītiviṣayatvamitiyāvat| jagacchabdaśca rūḍhayā nāmarūpaparaḥ| tathāca yasminnadhiṣṭhāne etatsarvaṃ jagad bhūtendriyādi tattadrūpeṇa pratibhātityarthaḥ| "neha nānā'sti"  iti śrutyā bodhitabādhasyāpi jagataḥ pratibhāse adhiṣṭhānasattāyā eva niyāmakatvāt samastārthaprakāśakatvopapattirapracyutasvarūpasyaiva vrahmaṇo jagadrūpeṇa vivarttanādavikāritvarūpotkṛṣṭadharmāvagatiśca| atra ca "tameva bhāntamanubhāti sarvam" ityādyaḥ kūṭasthasvarūpapratipādikāśca śrutayaḥ pramāṇatvenānusandheyā iti bhāvaḥ| atra bhagavadviṣayakaratibhāvasya hetvalaṅkāro'ṅgam  jagadupādānatvena stutvā apavargasādhanajñānaviṣayatvenaitacchāstravyākhyātṛśeṣabhūṣaṇatvena ca stuvaccheṣoktasamāstasiddhāntapratipattaye prārthayate aśeṣeti phalaṃ pravṛttyuddeśyam svargādiyatkiñcit‌phaladātṛtvasya devatāntarasādhāraṇyādāha-aśeṣeti tathācāpavargarūpaphalasyāpyaśeṣapadena kukṣīkṛtatvāt taddātṛtvasyānanyasādhasṇyāt prārthyatā'vacchedakadharmalābhaḥ| tadeva viśadayati bhavābdhīti bhava evabdhiriti rūpakam, abdhīyagrāhādisadṛśakāmalobhādyākrāntatvād duravagāhatvena bhave tadāropaḥ| tena ca bhavasya dustaratvaṃ vyajyate| tasya taraṇaṃ pāradeśaprāptistasminnityarthaḥ  tarim sādhanam| yadyapi tarantyanayā iti vyutpattyā taraṇasādhanaṃ taripadārthastathā'pi tadaghaṭakībhūtataraṇasya sannihitapadenaiva lābhād dviruktirmā bhūditi viśeṣyamātraparatayā vyākhyātam| suktisādhanajñānaviṣayamiti yāvat| muktuśca vedāntimate'jñānanivṛttisamakālāsvarūpaprāptiḥ| nanvajñānanivṛtteradhikaraṇānatiriktāyāḥ svarūpasyāpi ca siddhatvāttadrūpāyāstasyāstattvājñānasādhyatvānupapattiriti cenna, pareṣāṃ prāgabhāvasyeva siddhāya evājñānanivṛtteryogakṣemasādhāraṇyāḥ sādhyatāyāḥ sambhavāt| adhikamanyato'vadheyam|
naiyāyikāstu-ātyantikaduḥkhadhvaṃso mokṣaḥ| dhvaṃse ātyantikatvañca-svasamānādhikaraṇaduḥkhāsāmānakālikatvam| atraiva tattvajñānasyopayogaḥ| viśeṣyāṃśasya svāsādhāraṇasāmagrayā eva sambhavānnityasukhābhivyaktirvā sa ityāhuḥ|
śeṣaśeṣeti karmadhārayottarapadaḥ ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣaḥ| tasya ca lābhapadena samāso lābhāyedamiti vigrahe caturthyantalābhapadasyārthapadena saḥ| śeṣaḥ-patañjaliḥ,śeṣāṃśatvāttasya| tathāca śeṣoktabhāṣyapratipāditanikhilasiddhāntajñānāyetyarthaḥ| śeṣabhūṣaṇamiti śeṣo'nantaḥ, sa bhūṣaṇaṃ yasyetyarthaḥ| yato'sya bhūṣaṇaṃ śeṣo'to'sya śeṣoktasiddhāntalabdhyai prārthanamiti sābhiprāyaviśeṣyasyopādānātparikarāṅkurālaṅkāro'tra bhagavadviṣayakaratyaṅgam  prekṣāvatpravṛttaye itaragranthebhyaḥ svagranthasyotkarṣaṃ pradarśayanneva darśanīyaviṣayaprayojanasambandhādhikāriṇāṃ madhye'vaśyapradarśanīyaviṣayaṃ darśayati pāṇinyāditi pumbhāvavāgdevatāmiti piturviśeṣaṇam| puṃso bhāvo yasyā iti vyadhikaraṇabahubrīhipūrvapadakaḥ karmadhārayo vāgdevatāpadena| athavā pumbhāvaśabdo'rśa ādyajantaḥ| tathācā''viṣkṛtapuṃvigrahāṃ sarasvatīmityarthaḥ| dvaitadhvānteti dvābhyāṃ prakārābhyāmitiḥ-jñātaḥ-viruddhobhayadharmaprakārakajñānaviṣayo dharmoti yāvat| yadvā dvayorbhāvo dvitā| svaniṣṭhaikaviśeṣyatānirūpitaprakāratāsambandhena viruddhanānādharmāṃśe dharmiṇo'pi bhāvatvasambhavād dvitāpadārtho'pi dharmyeva, tatra bhavaṃ dvaitaṃ saṃśayātmakajñānam| athavā dvayoranekasya bhāvo dvitā nānātvaṃ tatsambandhijñānaṃ dvaitam, paramārthadaśāyāmadvitīyasyaiva brahmaṇo'ṅ‌gīkāreṇa śrutibādhitanānātvovagāhyantaḥ| karaṇavṛttiviśeṣarūpamavidya'paraparyāyaṃ mithyājñānam, tadeva dhvāntaṃ tamaḥ, tattvā''cchādakatvāttannivāraṇamekaṃ mukhyaṃ phalaṃ yasyāstāmityarthaḥ| anadhikāriṇāṃ svāśritānāmekatarakoṭikajñānotpādakatvenādhikāriṇāṃ sanmātrāvalambanajñānapradatvena pumbhāvavāgdevatāyāstannivartavamiti bhāvaḥ| ḍhuṇḍhim gaṇapatim| praṇamyetyanuṣajyate| siddhāntāniti bhāṣyakāradinirṇātārthāḥ siddhāntāstānityarthaḥ| upapattibhi sādhakapramāṇopanyāsapūpābhiryuktibhirityarthaḥ dūṣaye upapattibhireva bādhakapramāṇopanyāsarūpābhirityarthaḥ|
etenānyagranthebhya svagranthasyotkṛṣṭatvaṃ dhvanitam| tathāca vaiyākaraṇasiddhānto'tra viṣayaḥ pradarśitastenaiva tajjñānaṃ prayojanaṃ pratipādyapratipādakabhāvaḥ sambandhaḥ siddhāntajijñāsuradhikārīti trayo'nye'pi darśitaprāyā iti bhāvaḥ  granthādāvanuṣṭhitasya maṅgalasya tannibandhanasya ca prayojanamāha prāripsitetyādinā samāptaye iti vihāya `pratibandhakopaśamanāya' ityuktayā alokikā'vigītaśiṣṭācārānumitaphalamaṅgalasya vighnadhvaṃsa eva phalam, janmāntarīyasamāptimuddiśya śiṣṭānāṃ maṅgalānanuṣṭhānānmaṅgalasamāptyoḥ śarīraniṣṭhapratyāsattyaiva kāryakāraṇabhāvasya kalpanīyatayā tasya ca nāstikasamāptau vyabhicāreṇa samāptestattvāsamabhavāt, kintu kāryamātraṃ prati pratibandhakābhāvasya hetutayā durītarūpapratibandhakanirāse eva maṅgalasyopayogo na tu samāptāvapi, vighnadhvaṃsenānyathāsiddhatvāt, samāptistvanyatra klṛptatatkāraṇakalāpāditi sūcyate| phaṇismaraṇeti phaṇī-śeṣaḥ| "śeṣakhyaṃ dhāma māmakam" iti bhagavaduktestatsmaraṇasya vighnanivārakatvāt| maṅgalasya natistutyādibhedena nānātvamiti sūcayituṃ smaraṇetyuktam| pratijānīta iti pratijñāphalaṃ tu śiṣyāvadhānaṃ bodhyam| uddharaṇakartrakāṅ‌kṣāyāmāha asmābhiriti mūle kathyate iti haryādinibaddhakārikābhiriti śeṣaḥ  tatra sphoṭasyārthabodhajanakatvāt tenaivā''kāṅkṣānivṛtteśca vākyasphoṭasya `vākyasphoṭo'tiniṣkarṣe' ityādinā siddhāntayiṣyamāṇatve'pi prativākyaṃ saṅketagrahāsambhavāt tadānvākhyānasya laghūpāyenāsukaratvācca tatra kalpanayā padāni vibhajyateṣvapi prakṛtipratyayabhāgāṃstathaiva pravibhajya kalpitābhyāmanvayavyatirekābhyāṃ tattadartheṣvapi prakṛtipratyayanāmanādisaṅketabodhanadvārā sādhutvānvākhyāyake maharṣipraṇote śāstre prakriyānirvāhāya padavarṇasphoṭāvapi sammatāviti darśaṃyam̐stiṅantārthapradhāne suptiṅcayādirūpe vākye dhātvarthasya prādhānyāttamevādau nirupayitumāha mūle phalavyāpārayoriti tatra dhāturityasya `smṛtāḥ' iti viśeṣaṇam; vibhinnavacanāntopasthāpitatvāt| viśeṣyavācakapadottaravibhaktitātparyaviṣayasaṃkhyāviruddhasaṃkhyāyā avivakṣāyāṃ viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇavācakapadayoḥ samānavacanakatvaniyamāt| ata eva nīlo ghaṭā ityāderna sādhutvam| tadvivakṣāyāṃ tu `vedāḥ pramāṇam' `jātyākṛtivyaktayaḥ padārthaḥ' ityādau vibhinnavacanatvamapi| na ca prakṛte tadvivakṣāyāṃ bījamastītyato vacanavipariṇāmenānvayaṃ darśayati bhūṣaṇasāre dhāturityatreti
nanu yaduddeśena śruterlokato vā pravṛttiravagatā tattvaṃ palatvam-anyecchānadhīnecchāviṣayatvamiti yāvat| yaduktaṃ hariṇā-
yasyārthasya prasiddhayarthamārabhyante pacādayaḥ|
tatpradhānaṃ phalaṃ teṣāṃ na lābhādi prayojanam || iti ||
`svaritañitaḥ'  iti sūtre'pi phalapadasya pravṛtyuddeśyamukhyaphale eva prasiddheḥ| tathāca tādṛśaphalasya dhātuvācyatve viklittyanukūlavyāpāradaśāyāṃ taṇḍulasya dhātvarthatādṛśaphalāśrayatvābhāvāt karmatvānupapattistajjanyatādṛśaphalāśrayasya tadāpattiṃśacetyata āha-phalam-viklittyādīti ayamāśayaḥ-phalapadasya loke rūḍhayā tādṛśārthopasthāpakatve'pyatra pāribhāṣikārthaparataiva| anyathā sakarmakatvādivyavahāro durupapādaḥ syāditi| tattvañca taddhātvarthajanyatve| sati taddhātujanyopasthitiviṣayatvam, taddhātvarthatvaṃ vā| janyatvaṃ ca janyatvaprakārakapratītivaṣayatvam| tena sattādau nāprasaṅgaḥ| gamyādyarthavyāpārajanyavibhāgādāvatiprasaṅgavāraṇāya viśeṣyam, tadarthavyāpāre'tiprasaktinirāsāya ca satyantam| tacca viklittyādīnāmakṣatam, tasyāpi prakṛtapacyādiśakyatā tattadrūpeṇaiva tathābodhasya sarvānubhavasiddhatvāt, dhātutvādikamajānato'pi tadvodhācceti bhāvaḥ|
pare tu kartṛpratyayasamabhivyāhāre taddhātvarthajanyatve sati taddhātvarthaniṣṭhaviśeṣyatānirūpitaprakāratāvattvam| janyatā cā''ropitā'nāropitā vetyanyadetat| vibhāgajanyasaṃyogādirūpe patatyarthe vibhāgasaṃyogayoḥ phalatvavāraṇāyobhayam| karmākhyāte phalasya viśeṣyatayaiva bhānāttatrātiprasaṅgavāraṇāya kartṛpratyayasamabhivyāhāra iti vadanti, taccintyam, anugataphalatvānirukteḥ| vibhāgajanyasaṃyogo (niravayavatvātpratyakṣatvāt) patatyarthatvābhāvasya vakṣyamāṇatayā vyāvarttyā'prasiddheścetyadhikamagre vakṣyate|
nanu vyāpāratvaṃ na phalaprayojakakriyātvam, ātmāśrayāt, kriyātvasyaiva hi tattvād, daṇḍādivyāpārasyāpi dhātvarthatvāpatteśca| naceṣṭāpattiḥ, dhātvarthavyāṇarāśrayasyaiva kartṛtāyā vakṣyamāṇatayā tadāśrayasya daṇ‍ḍādeḥ kartturākhyātenābhidhānād `daṇḍena devadattaḥ pacati' ityādau daṇḍādipadottaraṃ tṛtīyā'nāpatteḥ, prathamāpatteśca| kartṛkaraṇasaṃjñayorākaḍārīyatayā saṃjñādvayasamāveśāsambhavenābhimatakaraṇasaṃjñāyāḥ parayā kartṛsaṃjñāyā bādhāt| kiñca, prayojakatvaṃ yadi sākṣājjanakatvaṃ, tadā `pacati' ityādau kartṛvyāpārasya dhātvarthatvānāpattiḥ, tasya karaṇavyapāradvāraiva phalajanakatvāt| yadi janakājanakasādhāraṇaṃ tadā taṇḍulakrayāṇasyāpi phalaprayojakakriyātvāt taddaśāyāmapi devadattādau `pacati' iti prayogāpattiḥ, taṇḍulādikrayaṇādivyāvṛttasya tādṛśaprayojakatvasya nirvaktumaśakyatvācca| ata eva na tajjanyatve sati tajjanyajanakatvarūpaṃ tantrāntaraprasiddhaṃtat| tadavacchinnasya dhātuvācyatve sākṣādviklittijanakāntimāgnisaṃyogadaśāyāmeva 'pacati' iti prayogaḥ syānnatvadhiśrayaṇādidaśāyāmapi, teṣamantimāgnisaṃyogenānyadhāsiddhatayā viklittyajanakatvādityata āha vyāpārastviti|
sādhyatveneti viśeṣaṇe tṛtīyā| tathāca padāntarasamabhivyāhārāprayojyasādhyatvaprakārakā'bhidhānaviṣayatvaṃ vyāpāratvamityarthaḥ| abhidhānasya prakāratāvattvaṃ viṣayatānirūpakatvaṃ caupacārikam| tādṛśasādhyatvaprakārakapratītiviṣayatvamiti yāvat| 'ghaṭaṃ karoti' ityādau kṛñ‌samabhivayāhāre ghaṭādīnāmapi sādhyatvena pratīteratiprasaṅgabhaṅgāya padāntarasamabhivyāhārāprayojyeti| pratītiviśeṣaṇam, tadasamabhivyāhāre teṣāṃ tattvenāpratīteḥ| adhiśrayaṇādyadhaḥśrayaṇaparyantakriyākalāpasya padāntarasamabhivyāhāramantareṇāpi sādhyatvena pratīyamānatvāduktavyāpāratvamavikalamiti tadavacchinne pacyādidhātuvācyatā nānupapanneti bhāvaḥ||
uktarthasya svotprekṣitatvaṃ nirasyati uktañceti yāvaditi| sarvamityarthaḥ| sarvapadārthameva vivṛṇotisiddhamityādi| siddham-vidyamānadhvaṃsapratiyogi, `apākṣīt' ityādau| asiddham-bhūtabhinnaṃ varttamānam, tādṛśaprāgabhāvapratiyogi ca, `pacati'`pakṣyati' ityādau| sādhyatvena vakṣmāṇasvarūpeṇa| abhidhīyate tatprakārakapratītiviṣaya ityarthaḥ| kvacidvivakṣyate iti pāṭhaḥ|
etena kriyāśabdasya rūḍhiḥ pradarśitā| yaugikatvamapyāha āśriteti āśritaṃ kramarūpaṃ yasyāstattvāt pūrvāparībhūtāvayavakatvādityarthaḥ| tadīyā'vayavānāmadhiśrayaṇādyadhaḥ śrayaṇaparyantānāṃ krameṇotpatteḥ kriyāpadena sā ucyate| yatra ca na kramikāvayavako vyāpārastatra rūḍhirevādaraṇīyā, paurvāparyāropo vā| phalasyāpi svajanakavyāpāragatapaurvāparyāropeṇa tathaiva bhānam| ata eva tanmātravācakasya dhātutvasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ| pāka iti yadyapi sādhyatvenopasthitirapyatra kathamanyathā `stokaṃ pākaḥ' ityādau stokādīnāṃ karmatā, tathā'pi tatra dhātūpasthāpyabhāvasya guṇatayā pradhānībhūtaghañupasthāpyasya tasya siddhataivetyāśayaḥ| paryavasitārthamāha tathā ceti kriyāntareti ayamāśayaḥ-yaddharmavattājñānāt kriyāntarākāṅ‌kṣodayastādṛśajñāne prakāratayā'vacchedakaṃ yattasiddhatvam, tadbhinnatvaṃ jñānaniṣṭatādṛśā''kāṅ‌kṣotthāpakatvābhāvāvacchedakaṃ vā sādhyatvam| sādhyatvajñānasya tu nākāṅ‌kṣāpratibandhakatvam, klṛptakāraṇābhāvādeva tadanutpādāt| na ca kriyāntarākāṅ‌kṣānutthāpakatāvacchedakadharmarūpasādhyatvena dhātvarthopasthitau `kṛṣṇaṃ nameccet sukhaṃ yāyāt' ityādāvākāṅ‌kṣābhāvena kriyāntarānvayo na syāditi vācyam, tatrāpi cetpadasamavadhānenākāṅ‌kṣotthāpanāt| hirugādīnāṃ kriyārthakatvavyavahārastu kriyāmātraviśeṣaṇatvāt, ata eva na hirugādyarthe kārakāṇāmanvayaḥ, teṣāṃ tadarthasya kriyāntarasākāṅ‌kṣatvāditi|
vastutastu, siddhatvajñāne sādhyākāṅ‌kṣāvat pacyādyarthe sādhyatayā'vagate `kimasya sādhanam' ityākāṅ‌kṣodayena sādhanākāṅ‌kṣottthāpakatāvacchedakarūpattvameva sādhyatvam| ata eva `etadevādāya' ityagrimagranthasaṅgatiḥ, anyathā pacati bhavatītyādau pūrvoktarūpābhāvena tadasaṅgatiḥ spaṣṭaiva|
uktañca hariṇā-
prayogārheṣu siddhaḥ san mantavyo'rtho viśeṣaṇaiḥ|
prāk‌ ca sādhanasambandhāt kriyā naivopajāyate || iti |
sādhanaṃ hi kriyāṃ nirvartayati `suṭ kāt'  iti sūtrasthabhāṣyamapyetakalpe'nuguṇamityavadheyam|
kecittu, pacatītyanugatavyavahārādasti pacitvādikaṃ jātiḥ| tathā ca kramikānekavyaktivṛttijātireva kriyā| sādhyatvañca tasyā vyaktidvārakameva| sphoṭavadasyāḥ kramavadbhiḥ kṣaṇaivabhivyaktiḥ|
jātimanye kriyāmāhuranekavyaktivartinīma|
asādhyā vyaktirūpeṇa sā sādhyevopalakṣyate||
iti vākyapadīyaṃ cā'tra kalpe mānamityāhuḥ|
sādhyatvāsattvabhūtapadayoḥ paryāyatāmāha tadrūpavattvamiti| etadeveti niruktarūpasattvabhūtatvamevetyarthaḥ asattvabhūta iti `bhāvapradhānamākhyātam' iti niruktārthapratipādakametat| sattvasvabhāvamāpannā vyaktirnāmabhirucyate|
iti asya pūrvārddham| bhāvaśceti| co'vadhāraṇe| uktabhāva evetyarthaḥ tena sattvabhūtasya tasya vyavacchedaḥ| tiṅ‌padairiti bahuvacanasvārasvena tiṅantapadairityarthaḥ abhidhīyate bodhyate ityarthaḥ| abhidhiyate ityasya prādhānyenaiveti śeṣaḥ| tena kṛdantapadairasattvāvasthabhāvā'bhidhāne'pi na kṣatiḥ| tiṅ‌padairiti bhāvā''khyātābhiprāyeṇeti kaścit|
ayaṃ ceti sādhyatvena pratīyamāno vyāpāraścetyarthaḥ| vācya iti pacatyādaśakya ityarthaḥ| anubhavasiddhatvāditi| ata eva vyajanādinā vahniprajvalanadaśāyāṃ phūtkāra eva gṛhītaśaktikasya na pacatīti prayogaḥ| yāgaḥ, icchā, jñānaṃ veti vipratipattistu svīyabodhānusārādeveti bhāvaḥ| na ca nāneti nānādharmavacchinnaśaktinirūpakatāvacchedakaikadharmavattvaṃ nānārthatvam| taccha pacyādāvakṣatamiti bhāvaḥ| phalāṃśamādāya tattvaṃ tvāśaṅkiturapīṣṭameveti bodhyam| buddhiviśeṣāderiti svanirūpitaviṣayatāvacchedakatvasambandheneti śeṣaḥ| buddhiviśeṣaviṣayatāvacchedakatvasyeti yāvat| ayaṃ bhāvaḥ-tadādibhyaścaitratvāditattadrūpeṇa bodhaḥ sarvānubhavasiddhaḥ| sa ca caitratvādyanugamakadharmaṃ vinā'nupapanna iti buddhiviśeṣaviṣayatāvacchedakatvaṃ tadanugamakadharmamaṅ‌gīkṛtya tadanugatīkṛtatattadavacchinneṣu caitrādiṣu tadādīnāṃ śaktiḥ| buddhiviśeṣaviṣayatāvacchedakvasyopalakṣaṇatvopāmācca na caitratvādīnāṃ tena rūpeṇa śābdabodhe bhānāpattiḥ| ananugatanānādharmā'vacchinnaśaktinirūpakatā'vacchedakaikadharmavata eva nānārthatvam, na teṣāṃ tadipīti tadādiśaktinirūpaṇe nirṇitamanyatra, tadvadihāpyavaseyamiti|
nanu pūrvāparībhūtakṣaṇanaśvarakramikavyāpārāṇāṃ melanā'sambhavāttadātmakakriyāyā anaikyād `ekā ca kriyā' iti "praśaṃsāyām"  iti sūtrasthabhāṣyāsaṅgatirata āha ākhyāteti| nirvibhaktikaḥ pāṭhaḥ sādhuḥ| ākhyātapratipādyakriyaikatvavyavasthāpītyarthaḥ| savibhaktikapāṭhe saptamī vaiṣayikādhāre adhyāhṛtajñānakriyāmādāyabodhyā| buddhiviśeṣaikyamiti saṃkalanarūpabuddhiviśeṣātmakavicchedakaikyamādāyetyarthaḥ| tathā cāvayavā''śrayaṃ paurvāparyyam, samudāyāśrayamekatvamādāya tatsaṅgatiriti bhāvaḥ| tatra mānamupanyasyati uktañceti| guṇeti kramikaitad‌vyapārasamūhaṃ prati guṇabhūtastattadrūpeṇa bhāsamānairavayavairupalakṣitaḥ saṅkalanātmakaikatvabuddhayā prakalpito'bhedo yasya tad‌rūpaḥ samūhaḥ kriyeti vyavahriyate iti kārikārthaḥ| tatra kṣaṇanaśvarāṇāṃ vyāpārāṇāṃ vastubhūtasamudāyā'bhāvād buddhayetyuktam| nirūpthetyasya saṅkṣepeṇetyādiḥ| pradhānībhūtaprakṛtyarthanirūpaṇānantaraṃ viśeṣaṇapratyayārthasyā'vaśyavaktavyatayā'vasarasaṅgatyā tannirūpaṇamityabhipretyāha tiṅarthamiti| anyalabhyatvāditi `ananyalabhyo hi śabdārthaḥ' iti nyāyāditi bhāvaḥ| etena kṛtimato vyāpāravato vā kartuḥ śakyatve'nekakṛtivyāpārāṇāṃ śakyatā'vacchedakatvakalpane gauravamiti naiyāyikādyuktaṃ dūṣaṇamasmanmatā'jñānavijṛmbhitamiti sūcitam|
nanvāśrayasya tiṅ‌rthatve āśrayatvaṃ śakyatāvacchedakaṃ vācyam| tasya ca svarūpā'bhinnatayā nānāsambandhā'vacchinnatayā ca nānātvādananugamo'ta āha śakyatāvacchedakaṃ ceti| vakṣayate iti asya dvitīyārthanirūpaṇā'vasara iti śeṣaḥ| tathā ca tatrā'khaṇḍopādhirūpasyānugatasya vakṣyamāṇatvānna tatra nānātvaprayukto doṣa iti bhāvaḥ|
mīmāṃsakādimataṃ prabalapramāṇena nirākariṣyan kartṛkarmaṇoruktaṃ tiṅarthatvaṃ vyavasthāpayituṃ śaṅkate nanviti nanu `yad yadvodhakaṃ tattadarthakam' iti niyamādākhyātajanyakarttṛ karmapratītireva mānamata āha pratīteriti| sambhavādityanenānvitam| sambhavahetumāha lakṣaṇayeti| nanvākhyātasyā''śrayalakṣakatve bhāvanābhānānupapattistadupapattaye tatra tasya śaktikalpane tu nā'śraye lakṣaṇāsambhavaḥ, yugapad‌vṛttidvayavirodhāt| bhāvanāśrayatvena bhāvanāśraye lakṣaṇāyāṃ tu bhāvanāyāḥ prādhānyānupattirata āha ākṣepāditi| ākṣepo'nupapattistata ityarthaḥ| tathā hi-anvitābhidhānavādimate padānāṃ viśeṣaṇatvenaivārthopasthāpakatayā''khyātārthabhāvanāyā viśeṣaṇatvā'nupapattyā bhavati tasya āśrayā''kṣepakatvam| yadvā, anumānaṃ saḥ| tatprayogaśca-`ākhyātapadabodhyā bhāvanā, kiñcidāśritā, viśeṣaṇatvena gṛhītaśaktikatvāt| ghaṭapadabodhyaghaṭatvavat' iti| samānasaṃvitsaṃvedyatvaṃ tu nā''kṣepaḥ, prameyamityāśrayabhāne bhāvanāyā abhānāt, bhāvanāpadajanya bhāvanābodhe aśrayā'bhānācceti|
nanu vyāptyādipratisandhānaṃ vinā'pyāśrayabodhasyānubhavasiddhatvānnākṣepādāśrayabhānopapattiḥ, kvācitkatvāt tathābhānasya| kiñcoktarītyā tadbhānasamarthane ākhyānajanyabodhe bhāvanāyāḥ prādhanyānupapattiḥ, śaktigrahe tasyā viśeṣaṇatvenaivopasthitatvād ghaṭatvavat, etattattvasya vicārā'sahatvāccetyata āha prathamāntapadādveti samabhivyāhṛtaprathamāntacaitrādipadādevāśrayayapratītyupapatterityarthaḥ| evañca nāśrayapratītyanupapattirāśraye ākhyātaśaktisādhiketi bhāvaḥ|
mānamiti yadyapyāśrayatvenāśrayabhānārthaṃ tatrākhyātasya śaktirāvaśyakītyevottaraṃ vaktumucitaṃ tathā'pi dhātoḥ phalavyāpārayoḥ śaktirākhyātasyāśrayatve śaktiriti mate āśrayasya āśrayatvenāpi lābhānna sāmānyata āśraye svābhimatākhyātaśaktiḥ sid‌dhyatīti tanmanirāsārthamapi sūtraṃ pramāṇatvenopanyastamiti bodhyam| nanu kartṛpadāghaṭitamidaṃ sūtraṃ kathamākhyātasya kartṛvācakatve mānamityāśaṅkāṃ nirasyati atra hīti nanvetatsūtraṃ lakārāṇāmeva kartrādiśaktibodhakaṃ nākhyātasyetyata āha bodhakatārūpāmiti bodhajanakatvarūpāmityarthaḥ| idaṃ cādeśiṣveva śaktirādeśānāmādeśismṛtidvārā'rthopasthāpakatvamiti matanirāsāyoktam| yathā caitat tathā vakṣyate| vidhiyante iti tathāca varṇasphoṭapakṣe śrūyamāṇavarṇānāmeva vācakatāyā vyāvasthāpayiṣyamāṇatayā `la karmaṇi' ityādisūtrāṇāṃ tibādiśaktigrahe eva tātparyāvadhāraṇena sambhavati tasyā'khyātaśaktisādhakapramāṇatvamiti bhāvaḥ| nakāravisargādīti `rāmān' `rāmaiḥ' ityādāvityarthaḥ| kartṛrmapade iti asyānuvṛttopātte ityādiḥ| kartṛtvakarmatvapare iti ākṛtiśaktivādanabhipretya bhāvapradhānanirddeśādveti bhāvaḥ| kartṛtvam-kṛtiriti kṛño yatnavācakatvena tatprakṛtikakartrarthakatṛjantakartṛśabdasya kṛtyāśrayavācakatayā taduttarabhāvapratyayena prakṛtyarthaprakārībhūtakṛtirūpapadharmabodhanāditi bhāvaḥ| karmatvañca phalamiti karmaśabdasya dhātvarthaphalāśraye pāribhāṣikatvāditi bhāvaḥ| śaṅkayamiti tathāca tatsūtrabalānna kartṛkarmaṇorākhyātaśaktiḥ siddhayatīti bhāvaḥ|
darśanāntarīyeti darśanaṃ śāstraṃ nyāyādiḥ, anyaddarśanaṃ darśanā'ntaraṃ tatra bhavā darśanāntarīyā, gahāditvācchaḥ| `pacati' ityasya pākaṃ karotīti vivaraṇāt phalaṃ pacyādyarthastiṅ‌tu vyāpāravacana iti mīmāṃsakā manyante| tanmate vyāpārasyānyalabhyatvābhāvāttatra lakāravidhau na kiñcid bādhakam| yadyapyuktarītyā vyāpāre śaktisiddhāvapi phalaśaktibodhakaṃ karmaṇītyanupapannameva, tathāpyabhidhānā'bhidhānavyavasthārthaṃ tadapyāvaśyakameva, dvedhāṃ bhānaṃ tu kathañcit pariharaṇīyamiti bhāvaḥ| kṛtāmiti kṛtsaṃjñakānāṃ ṇvṛltṛjādīnāmityarthaḥ| nanu `karttari kṛt' ityeva tatra śaktisādhakamata āha kartari kṛditi ceti  tatsūtrādeva ca kartarīti atrānuvarttate| tathāca pūrvasūtrasthakartṛpadasya dharmiparatvameva, atra dharmaparatāyāṃ tu tatrāpi dharmaparataiva syāt| tadevāha tulyayogakṣemamiti alabdhalābho yogaḥ, labdharakṣaṇaṃ kṣemam| evañca `laḥ karmaṇi' ityasyānanyalabdhabhāvanāyā ākhyātārthatvapratipādakatvopagame nyāyasāmyāt, kartari kṛt' ityasyāpi kṛtāṃ bhāvanārthatvapratipādakatvam| tasyā ākṣepādeva lābhānna kṛtāṃ tacchaktipratipādakaṃ taditi yadi, tarhi `laḥ karmaṇi' ityapi nā''khyātasya bhāvanāsaktipratipādakamiti samānamityarthaḥ|
nanu pūrvasūtrasthakarttṛpadasya dharmiparatve'pyanuvṛttasya tasya śabdādhikārā''śrayaṇena dharmaparatvasambhavānnoktadoṣaḥ| yadi ca śabdādhikārā''śrayaṇaṃ satyeva gamake, prakṛte ca tadāśrayaṇe pramaṇābhāva iti vibhāvyate, tadā'pi `śaktiḥ kārakam' `śaktimatkārakam' iti pakṣadvayasyāpyākarasiddhatayā tadvikalpasya tiṅ‌kṛtorvyavasthitatvāśrayaṇādarthādhikārāśrayaṇe'pyabhimatārthalābho'kṣata evetyata āha api ceti tathāca vikalpavyavasthāyā evāprāmāṇikatvenaikārthaparataiva nyāyyeti bhāvaḥ| nanvākhyātasya vyāpārāvācakatve kathaṃ tatastad‌bodho'ta āha bhāvanāyā iti| kṛdādivaditi `tatra tasyeva' iti vatiḥ, kṛdādisthale ivetyarthaḥ| ādipadāt karttavihitataddhita ityādeḥ parigrahaḥ| tathāca bhavanmate dhātorvyāpārāvācakatvena kṛdarthakartrākṣiptabhāvanāyā bodhaviṣayatvaṃ tathā''khyāte'pīti bhāvaḥ| tathā satīti bhāvanāyā ākhyātāvācyatve satītyarthaḥ| tasyā bhāvanāyāḥ| prādhanyam mukhyaviśeṣyatvam| ghaṭamānayeti `jātiḥ padārthaḥ' iti mate jātyākṣiptavyakteryathā prādhānyaṃ tathā kartrākṣiptabhāvanāyā api prādhānyamupapatsyata ityarthaḥ| śaktigrāhakeṣu parigaṇitādvivaraṇādbhāvanāvācakatvamākhyātasyā''pyāsyatītyāśaṅkate bhāvanāyā iti vivaraṇeti tatsamānā'rthakapadā'ntareṇa tadarthakathanasya vivaraṇatayā prakṛte pacatītyasya pākabhāvanetyākhyātasya bhāvanāpadena vivaraṇāttasya bhāvanāvācakatvamityarthaḥ| vivaraṇasya svasvabodhānusāritayā na tenā'rthanirṇaya ityāha pākā'nukūleti pacatītyasyaikakarttṛkā pacikriyeti kartturvivaraṇasyā'pi darśanenā'khyātasya bhāvanāyāṃ śaktiruta karttarītyatra vinigamakā'bhāvāditi bhāvaḥ| itaretarayogadvandva iti samāhāradvandve uttarapadalakṣyā'rthasamāhārasya caśabdena vivaraṇāduktamitaretareti| dhavakhadirāvityādidvandvaghaṭakapadā'śakyasāhityasya vigrahavākyasthacaśabdena vivaraṇavadityarthaḥ| idaṃ cā'nvayaprayojakarūpavattvaṃ yogyatā| evañca, ghaṭena jalamāharetyatra jalā'haraṇaprayojakatayā chidretaratvavat prakṛte dvitvā'nvayaprayojakatayā'padārthasāhityasyā'pi bhānamityabhyupetyoktam| tatra sāhityasya dvitvā''dyatiriktasyā'nanubhavāttasya dvivacanādinaivopasthiterna tatra samāsaghaṭakapratyekapadavṛttiḥ| tasyaiva cena vivaraṇamiti matā'ntarantu vakṣyate| tulyatvāditi bhāvanāpadenā''khyātasya vivaraṇamapyapadārthavivaraṇamityasyāpi vaktuṃ śakyatvādityarthaḥ| svasvabodhā'nusārivivaraṇasya nā'rthanirṇāyakatvamityapyubhayoḥ| samānamiti bhāvaḥ|
nanvākhyātasya karttṛvācitve tadākṣiptabhāvanāyāḥ prādhānyā'nupapattiḥ| na cākṛtivāde jātyākṣiptavyaktivat‌ prādhānyamupapāditameveti vācyam,dṛṣṭāntadārṣṭāntikayorvaiṣamyāt, tathāhi-kartrā svasvarūpanirūpakatayā bhāvanā''kṣeptavyā,bhāvanāvirahiṇaḥ karttṛtvāsambhavāt| tataśca dharmigrāhakamānasiddhaguṇatvena prādhānyā'sambhavaḥ kṛtīva| jātyā tu paricchedyatayā'vagatasya dravyasya tathaivā''kṣepād bhavati tasya prādhānyena bhānamiti prādhānyā'nurodhādbhāvanāyā vācyatvamāvaśyakamityo dūṣaṇā'ntaramāha kiñceti| abhedā'nvayadarśanāditi devadatta'bhinnaikakarttṛko varttamāno vyāpāraḥ ityākārakabodhasyā''karasammatatvādityarthaḥ| tadanurodheneti abhedā'nvayā'nurodhenetyarthaḥ| āvaśyakamiti anyathā'nubhūyamānatādṛśabodhā'palāpā''pattiriti bhāvaḥ|
niyāmakamiti tadvibhaktyantanāmārthaniṣṭhā'bhedasaṃrgāvacchinnaprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣyatāsambandhena śābdabuddhiṃ prati tadvibhaktyantapadajanyopasthiterhetutvasya `nīlo ghacaḥ' ityādau kluptatvādityarthaḥ| tacceti abhedānvayabodhaprayojakasamānavibhaktikatvaṃ cetyarthaḥ| atra devadattaḥ pacatītyādau| tādṛśaniyamavyatirekavyabhicārasthalāni darśayati-
somena ityādināḥ-
nanvatra yāge somasya karaṇatvenaivānvayāt kathamuktaniyamasya vyabhicāra iti cenna, somasya yāgabhāvanāyāṃ karaṇatvenā'nvaye yāgasyā'pi karaṇatvenaivā'nvayāt `somena yāgeneṣṭaṃ bhāvayet' ityubhayavidhāne vākyabhedaprasaṅgaḥ| somasya yāgavat phalabhāvanākaraṇatvena prādhānyāpattiryāgārthatvānupapattiḥ, pratyayavācyaphalabhāvanāyāḥ samānapadopāttayāgasya karaṇatvenaivānvayādākāṅ‌kṣānivṛttervibhinnapadopāttasya somasya karaṇatvenā'nvayānupapattiśca| `somena yāgam' ityanvayābhyupagame yadyapi somasya na yāgārthatvānupapattiḥ, karaṇatvenaivānvayāt, tathā'pi pratyayārthabhāvanākaraṇatvena upasthitasya sādhyatvenā'nvayānupapattirduṣpariharaiveti somapadasya satvarthalakṣaṇayā `somavatā yāgeneṣṭaṃ bhāvayet' iti viśiṣṭārthavidhānādvākyabhedaprasaṅgo neti mīmāṃsakasiddhāntaḥ| tatra coktaniyame vyabhicāro durvāra iti bhāvaḥ|
stokaṃ pacatīti atrāpi dhātvarthavyāpārajanyaphale vibhinnavibhaktikastokapadārthasyābhedānvayādityarthaḥ| nanu samānavibhaktikatvaṃ nāmā'rthayorevā'bhedā'nvayabodhe prayojakam| ata eva vyutpattau dvivacanasyaivollekhaḥ| nāmatvañca vakṣyate| prakṛte cā'nuyogipratiyoginornāmārthatvābhāvānnoktadoṣo'ta āha rājapuruṣa iti|
yadyapi `somena yajeta' ityatreva `devadattaḥ pacati' ityatrāpi samānavibhaktikatvasyā'tantratayā'bhimatabodhānvayānurodhāt karttṛvācitvaṃ siddhameva, tathā'pi vyutpatternāmā'rthadvayaviṣayakatve'pi vyabhicāro duruddhara evetyāśayena nāmārthadvayasthalamupāttam| naiyāyikamatena cedam, taiḥ karmadhāraye, ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣe ca pūrvapadaśakyalakṣyānyatarasyottarapadārthe'bhedānvayopagamāt| svamate tu jahatsvārthavṛttyabhyupagamena pūrvottarapadārthayoranvayasyaivā'bhāvenoktavyutpatteraprasarāditi bodhyam| idamupalakṣaṇam-ṣaṣṭhayarthabahuvrīherapi|
apare tu-uktavyutpattau nāmāṃśe'samastatvasyāpi viśeṣaṇānnoktasthale vyabhicāra ityāhuḥ| adhikamagre vakṣyate| sāmānādhikaraṇyamiti padayorekārthā'bhidhāyitvamityarthaḥ| yaugikānāmiti yogo'vayavavṛttistanmātrapuraskāreṇa pravṛttānāmityarthaḥ| dravyavācitvā'nāpatteriti| dharmiśaktatvā'nāpatterityarthaḥ| evamiti lakṣaṇayaiva sāmānādhikaraṇyopapādane ityarthaḥ, itthameveti vā'rthaḥ| vaiśvadevītyādīti dravyavācittvā'nāpatterityanuṣajyate| tathāca bālabāla'dhikaraṇecchedā''pattiriti bhāvaḥ|
tathāhi-`tapte payasi dadhyānayati sā vaiśvadevyāmikṣā vājibhyo' iti śrūyate| atrāyaṃ saṃśayaḥ-kimanayā śrūtyā''mikṣāvājinobhayaguṇakamekaṃ karma vidhīyate? athavā tattadguṇadevatabhinnaṃ karmadvayamiti| vājenānnenā''mikṣārūpeṇa sambandhādvājino viśvedevāḥ, tānanudya vājinaguṇo vidhīyate| tenobhayaguṇakaṃ vaiśvadevamekaṃ karmeti pūrvapakṣe, `vaiśvadevyāmikṣā' ityatra taddhitasya devatāsambandhyarthakatā śrautaḥ sambandhaḥ, `vājibhyo vājinam' ityatra tu vākyena saḥ| satyāmapi padāntarāpekṣāyāṃ svābhinnā'rthakapadasyaivāpekṣaṇānna śrautatvabādhaḥ sambandhasya| yatra tu bhinnā'rthakapadā'pekṣā, tatraiva vākyasyaviniyojakatvāt| evañca, `vaiśvadevyā''mikṣā' ityetadvihitavaiśvadevayāgānuvādena na vājinaguṇavidhānam, śrautadravyeṇa nirākāṅ‌kṣatvāt| kintu karmāntaravidhiḥ| `vājibhyaḥ' iti cāpūrvadevatāvidhānamiti rāddhāntaḥ| yadi ca taddhitasya devatāsambandhe eva śaktistadā vājinā''mikṣayorvākyaviniyojyatvasya sāmyādāmikṣādravyeṇa devatāsambandhasya śrautatvamiti siddhāntavyākopaḥ| lakṣitā''mikṣādravyamādāya devatāsambandhe śrautatvasambandhāttadanupapattyabhāvena taddhitasya dravyārthakatvakalpanamatyayuktaṃ syāditi|
anuśāsanalābhāditi upalakṣaṇamidam, yukterapi| tathāhi-ghaṭapadād ghaṭatvaprakārakabodhavad daṇḍayādipadāddaṇḍasambandhavāniti pratītiḥ sarvasiddhā| tatrā''kṛtyadhikaraṇanyāyena sambandhamātraṃ matubādivācyaṃ syāt| ata eva `devadattasya gomattvam' ityatra tvapratyayasya sambandhabodhakatvaṃ saṅgacchate| ghaṭatvamityādau prakṛtyarthaghaṭatvavat prakṛtijanyabodhaprakārasya bhāvapratyayārthatvād, viśeṣasya saṃsargasya kathaṃ tato bodha iti tu nā''śaṅkanīyam, yatastasya svakṣiptavyaktyaṃśe prakāratayaiva bhānāt| evaṃ ṣaṣṭh‌yarthavihitabahuvrīherapyekaṃ hāyanamasyā iti vigrahe, daivatārthakataddhitasyāpi viśve devā devatā asyā iti vigrahe ca pratyayārthasambandhasyaiva prādhānyadarśanāt tasyaiva vṛttivācyatvanirṇayaḥ| uktañca--
yasminnanyapadārthe ca bahuvrīhirvidhīyate|
tasyā'pi pratyayārthatvāt sambandhasya pradhānatā || iti ||
na ca gomānityādau sambandhipratītyanupapattiḥ, ubhayā''śritena sambandhenā''kṣepāttadupapatteḥ| tava kartṛvat sambandhavacca| nacā'sattvabhūtasambandhasyaiva vibhaktyarthatayā tadvihitamatubādestādṛśasambandhabodhakatvena tatra liṅgasaṅkhayā'nanvayā''pattyā sattvabhūtasambandhe evānuśāsanā''dareṇa śaktirabhyupeyā| evañca dravyamevārtho matvarthīyasyā'stu, sambandha ākṣepalabhya eva kiṃ na syāt| kiñca, matubādeḥ sambandhasāmānyārthakatve tasya sambandhādipadaparyyāyatā''pattyā sambandhaviśeṣārthakatvamevorarīkaraṇīyam| tattvañcā'nuyogipratiyogiviśeṣyanirūpyatvam| tathācaikasya nirūpakasya prakṛtyā lābhe'pya nuyogirūpanirūpakabhānārtha tatra śaktikalpane tvatīva gauravamiti vācyam, matubāderhyasattvabhūtasambandha eva vācyaḥ| ata eva sambandhānāmanekatve'liṅgakatve'pi ca daṇḍayādiśabdānāmekavacanāntatvaṃ nānāliṅgakatvañca, sambandhināṃ tathātva eva tādṛśaprayogadarśanāt| tathāca, matubarthasambandho liṅgādyanvayāsambhavāttairevā''kṣepaḥ sambandhinaḥ, ākhyātārthasaṃkhyayeva kartrādeḥ|
vastutastu liṅgasaṃkhyā'nanvayitvarūpāsattvabhūtatvamapi ṣaṣṭhayarthādāvarthasiddham, pratyayārthaliṅgasaṃkhyādeḥ pratyayārtha eva'nvayāyogāt, `pratyayānām' iti vyutpatteśca| tasmānnāsattvabhūtasambandhaḥ ṣaṣṭhīvācyaḥ|
yattu, anuyogirūpanirūpakabhānārthaṃ dravye śaktikalpane gauravamiti, tadapi na sambandhapratyakṣe hi yāvadāśrayabhānāpekṣā, na śābde, yena tacchaktikalpanāgauravaṃ sambhāvyeta| tasmāt tatra matubādeḥ śaktau na kiñcid, bādhakamiti| prapañcitaṃ caitadadhikamanyatra|
evañcaikahāyanyādipānāṃ sambandhavācakatāyāmanuśāsanasya tadupaṣṭabdhānekayuktīnāṃ ca sattvāt tatraiva śaktiḥ sihyenna sambandhinītyakhaṇḍārthaḥ|
tathāceti sāmānādhikaraṇyasya lakṣaṇayaivopapādana ityarthaḥ| aruṇādhikaraṇeti tathāhi-`aruṇayāpiṅgākṣyaikahāyanyā somaṃ krīṇāti' iti śrūyate| tatra kimaruṇim vākyaṃ bhaṅ‌kyaṃ prakaraṇe niveśanīyaḥ?, kimvā krīṇātinā sambandhyate? iti saṃśaye, krīṇātinā sambadhyamānastadyogāt krayaṇakaraṇaṃ syāt, nacā'mūrttasya tadyuktamiti nāsya krayaṇasambandha iti pṛthageva prakaraṇe niveśanīya iti pūrvapakṣaḥ|
siddhāntastu, sambhavati sannihitapadārthānvaye prakaraṇaniveśānaucityāt piṅgākṣyādipadānāṃ dravyavacanatayā tasminnanvayasaulabhyena tad‌dvārā krīṇātyarthenā'pi samanvayalābhād vākyaghaṭakatvameva tasyeti| sa cā'nupapannaḥ, piṅgākṣyādiyaugikānāṃ padānāmuktayuktyā sambandhārthakatayā dravyasya tairanupādānāt|
kiñca, pūrpakṣo'pyanupapannaḥ, piṅgākṣyaikahāyanīśabdārthasambandhasyāpyamūrtatvāt, krīṇātikaraṇatvā'sambhavena krīṇātikaraṇatvasyaitadvākyādalābhādāruṇyasyaiva vākyabhedaśaṅkāyāṃ mūlaśaithilyācca| bahuvrīhyoraruṇapadasya ca lākṣaṇikadra
vyavidhāyakatāyā aviśiṣṭatvena bahuvrīhyādereva dravyavidhāyakatvam, nāruṇapadasyetyatra vinigamakābhāvāt| adhikamagre vakṣyate|
uktarītyeti sambandhilakṣaṇākalpanenetyarthaḥ| prapañcitamiti prapañcastūktaprāyaḥ| bhūṣaṇe iti prakṛtasthale, samāsaśaktinirūpaṇā'vasare cetyarthaḥ| `padā'rthaṃ nirūpya' ityanena padārthanirūpaṇānantaraṃ vākyārthanirūpaṇe tayoḥ saṅgatiḥ sūcyate| tat‌phalaṃ tu pūrvāparagranthaikavākyapratipattirekaprayojanavatī jñānaphalikā|
kecittu, unmattapralapitatvaśaṅkānidānāsaṅgatatvajñānanirāsaḥ prayojanamityāhuḥ|
sā cā'nantartyā'bhidhānaprayojakajijñāsājanakatāvacchedakadharmarūpā ṣaṅ‌vidhā| taduktam-
`saprasaṅga upoddhāto hetutā'vasarastathā|
nirvāhakaikakāryatve ṣoḍhā saṅgatiriṣyate ||' iti ||
tatra prakṛtasiddhaya'nukūlacintākālāvacchinnaprakṛtānukūlatvamupod‌ghātaḥ| anukūlatvañcātra ghaṭakatvajñāpakatvādirūpaṃ yathāyathaṃ grāhyam| tatrāvacchedakāṃśaḥ svarūpasan, itarāṃśaśca jñāta upayogi| evamagre'pi| smṛtikālāvacchinnopekṣānarhatāvacchedakadharmavattvaṃ prasaṅgaḥ| hetutā prasiddhā| pratibandhakībhūtaśiṣyajijñāsānivṛttikālāvacchinnāvaśyakaktavyatvamavasaraḥ| nirvāhakatvam-ekakāryyajanakatvaṃ kāraṇatadavacchedakasādhāraṇaprayojakatvarūpam| ekakāryyatvam- ekasya kāryatā sā ca janyatā, janyajñānaviṣayatvarūpā ceti| udāharaṇāni kānicittvatraiva vakṣyante| anyāni tu svayamūhyāni| ākṣepodāharaṇādīnāṃ tu tantrāntare saṅgatitvena prasiddhānāmeṣvevāntarbhāva iti na teṣāmādhikyasambhāvanā| prakṛte ca padārthatatsaṃsargaviśeṣarūpavākyārthayoḥ prasaṅgasaṅgatiḥ| tathāhi-nirūpiteṣu padārtheṣu prāyaśo'saṃsṛṣṭapadārthā'bhāvena tatsaṃsargasya sāmānyarūpeṇa smaraṇam| tataḥ sāmānyadharmaprakārakajñānasya viśeṣadharmaprakārakajijñāsājanakatvācchiṣyasya `ka eṣāṃ saṃsargaḥ' ityākārā viśeṣadharmajijñāsā, tatastad‌bodhanāya śiṣyasya śabdaprayogaḥ| tataḥ śiṣyajijñāsajñānaṃ guroḥ| tato jijñāsāviṣayajñāne iṣṭasādhanatājñānāt kaṇṭhatālvādyabhighātādisampādanena guroḥ śiṣyajijñāsānivṛttiphalakaṃ viśeṣasaṃsargarūpavākyārthābhidhānamiti| saṃsargagatasāmānyasyopekṣā'narhatāvacchedakatvāttadvatyasya tatra sattvāllakṣaṇasaṅgatiḥ| itthamanyatrā'pi lakṣaṇāni yojyāni|
viśeṣaṇe iti ādhārā''dheyabhāvasambandhena prakārāvityarthaḥ| saṅkhayayā ākhyātā'rthakartṛkarmā'nvayitve prayojakamāha samānapratyayopāttatvāditi| etena samānā'bhidhānaśrutervākyā'pekṣayā balavattvāt samānapadopāttānvayitvaṃ vihāya pathamā'ntārthe'nvayo'nucita iti vyajyate, ekapratyayajanyopasthitiviṣayatvāditi tadarthaḥ| laḍādīnāṃ vācakatvakalpe ākhyātajanyopasthitiviṣayatvasya kāle'pi sattvāttabra vyabhicāravāraṇāya kartṛkarmaviṣayatvena tāṃ viśinaṣ‌ṭi|
tathāceti| ghaṭa ityādāvekatvādisaṅkhayāprakārakabodhe vyabhicāro mā bhūditi kāryyatāvacchedakakoṭau ākhyātā'rtheti tadarthakālaprakārakabodhe tannirāsāya saṅkhayeti svoktamākhyātasya karttṛkarmārthakatvaṃ darśayituṃ prasaṅgānnyāyamataṃ nirākaroti naiyāyikānāmityādinā| anvayāditi| ekapadopāttayorekārthānvayitvasya nyāyyatvāditibhāvaḥ| ākhyātārthakālaprakārakabodha prathamāntapadajanyopasthiteranapekṣaṇādāha-saṅkhayeti|
itarāviśeṣaṇatveti| prathamāntārthetaraviśeṣaṇatvāghaṭita ityarthaḥ| tathācetaraviśeṣaṇatā'nāpannārthaviṣayakopasthitiḥ kāraṇamityarthaḥ| evañca, candra-bhojanādīnāṃ prathamāntārthatve'pi ivārthasādṛśyavrajanādiviśeṣaṇatvānna vyabhicāra iti bhāvaḥ| ghaṭita iti| tadaviṣayakapratītyaviṣayaḥ sa ityaviṣaya ityarthaḥ| tadavicchannāvacchedakatākakāraṇatāgrahaviṣaya iti yāvat| itarāviśeṣaṇatvañca-itaraviśeṣaṇatvatātparyyāviṣayatvam| nātaścaitra iva pacatītyādau, khale kapotanyāyena bodhe caitrīyaṃ sādṛśyamityavāntarabodhā'bhāvena caitrasyetaranirūpitaprakāratāśūnyatvarūpetarā'viśeṣaṇtavasattve'pi kṣatiḥ aviśeṣaṇatve'pi viśeṣaṇatvatātparyyaviṣayatvasattvāt| tathā''khyātārthasaṅkhayāprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣyatāsambandhena śābdabuddhiṃ prati itaraviśeṣaṇatvatātparyāviṣayārthavṛttiviśeṣyatāsambandhena prathamāntapadajanyopasthitirheturiti paryavasito'rthaḥ|
`caitreṇa supyate' ityādibhāvā''khyātasthale, `caitrakarttṛkaḥ svāpaḥ' ityādibodhādya thoktaviśeṣaṇakadhātvarthasvāpe tadananvayād vyabhicāraparihārāya prathamānteti padaviśeṣaṇam| yadyevamapi, `cetra eva pacati na maitraḥ' ityādāvanyayogavyavacchedarūpaivakārapadārthaikadeśe'nyatve caitrasya viśeṣaṇatvāt tatra saṃkhyā'nvayā'nupapattiriti vibhāvyate, tadā prathamāntārtho viśeṣaṇatvamātratātparyāviṣayatvena viśeṣaṇīyaḥ| avaśiṣṭāṃśasyāpi niveśe prayojanā'bhāvāduktasthale caṃ caitrasya mukhyaviśeṣyatvaviśeṣaṇatvābhyāṃ tātparyaviṣayatvānna saṅkhayā'nvayā'nupapattiriti tanmataniṣkarṣaḥ|
atigauravamiti| anyalabhyakṛtau śaktikalpane saṅkhyānvayabodhahetūpasthitau vibhinnapadajanyatvaniveśa eva gauravam| prathamāntapadārthasya viśeṣaṇatvamātratātparyā'viṣayatvena niveśe tvatigauravamityarthaḥ| idamapīti upadarśitakāryyakāraṇabhābe darśitalāghavamapītyarthaḥ|
yadyapi tanmate bhāvanānvayini saṅkhayānvayaniyamādākhyātena bhāvanā'viṣayakasaṅkhayānvayabodhasya kvāpyajananādbhāvanānvayabuddhitvasya saṅkhayānvayabuddhitvavyāpakatayā vyāpyadharmāvacchinnāsāmagryā phale jananīye vyāpakadharmāvacchinnasāmagsyā apekṣaṇād viśeṣaṇatvamātratātparyā'viṣayatvaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭaprathamāntārthopasthitirūpāyāstasyāstatrā'bhāvādevoktasthale vyabhicārāprasaktena tad‌vyāvṛttaye saṅkhayānvayakāraṇatāvacchedakakoṭāvuktaviśeṣaṇapraveśā'vasaraḥ, tathā'pi kṛtiśaktivādimate uktasthale tadāpattivāraṇāya bhāvanānvayabodhakāraṇatāvacchedakakoṭāvavoktaviśeṣaṇavitaraṇaprayuktagauravam| asmanmate tu prathamāntapadārthasyākhyātārthe viśeṣaṇatayaivānvayena candrāderekatra viśeṣaṇatayā'nvitasya nairākāṅkṣyeṇā'paratra viśeṣaṇatayānvayā'yogāduktavyabhicārā'bhāvena tannivārakaviśeṣaṇopādānaprayuktagauravamityabhipretyoktam idamapīti| adhikamanyatrānusandheyam|
atrā''hurnavyāḥ| lāghavopabṛṃhitoktayuktikadambenā''khyātasya dharmavācakatvasiddhānuttarakālakalpyatādṛśakāryyakāraṇabhāvaprayuktagauravam, niṣādasthapatyadhikaraṇanyayenākiñcitkaram, phalamukhatvāt| kiñca, candrādyupamāne ākhyātārthasaṃkhyādyanvayopagam āvaśyakaḥ| tatra tadanvayā'nupagame sādṛśyaprayojakadharma'lābhena vākyasyā'paripuṣṭārthatāpatte-| ktvāntārthabhojanādau tu saṅkhayādīnāmayogyatvādevānanvayenā'natiprasaṅgāt| evañca, nāsmanmate doṣaleśo'pīti| karttṛkarmaṇoḥ saṅkhyāyāśca viśeṣaṇatāṃ vyavasthāpya kālasyāpi tāṃ pratipādayati kālastvityādinā| vyāpāre iti dhātvarthavyāpāre ityarthaḥ|
nanvākhyātasya kālaśaktiparicchedakeṣu "vartamāne laṭ"  ityādiṣu vyāpāravācakapadānupādānāt kathaṃ kālasya dhātvarthavyāpāraviśeṣaṇatetyatastatsūtraprāmaṇyenaiva tāṃ sādhayati tathāhīti| tacca dhātupadaṃ cetyarthaḥ| dhātvarthamiti phalavyāpārobhayātmakamityarthaḥ| `sattāyām' ityādyarthanirdeśāt, bhūvādisūtraprāmāṇyācceti bhāvaḥ| vadat bodhayat| `san brāhmaṇaḥ' itivacchatā| prādhānyāt dhātujanyabodhe viśeṣyatayā bhāsamānatvādityarthaḥ| vyāpāramevetyevakāreṇa phalavyavacchedaḥ| prādhānyādityuktayā yanmate karmā''khyāte phalasya prādhānyaṃ tanmate phala evānvaya iti dhvanitam| grāhayatīti akhyātārthakālaviśeṣyatayā bodhayatītyarthaḥ| "guṇānāñca parārthatvāt" iti nyāyāt, pratyayārthasya sākṣātprakṛtijanyabodhaviśeṣyā'nvayitvaniyamācceti bhāvaḥ| karttṛkarmaṇoriti samānapratyayopāttatvaprattyāsatteriti bhāvaḥ| pacatītyāpatteriti| karttṛrvidyamānatvāditi bhāvaḥ| evamagre'pi| nāpi phala iti
kecittu-phalavyāpārayordhātoḥ pṛthak‌śaktau tayoruddeśyavidheyabhāvenā'nvayā''pattiḥ| pṛthagupasthitayostathā'nvayasyauttsargikatvāt| tattaddhātujanyatādṛśabodhasya tathā'nvayabodhe pratibandhakatākalpane tu gauravam| tasmāt phalā'vacchinnavyāpāre, vyāpārāvacchinnaphale ca dhātūnāṃ śaktiḥ| karttṛkarmārthakatattatpratyayasamabhivyāhāraśca tattad‌bodhe niyāmakaḥ| gauravañca prāmāṇikatvānna doṣāya| ittthañca kartrā''khyāte vyāpārasya prādhānyam, karmākhyāte tu phalasya| yadā yasya prādhānyaṃ tadā tasmin kālā'nvayaḥ| sūtrasthadhātupadādubhayorapi prādhānyenopasthitattvānnoktaniyamāvakāśaḥ| phalānutpattidaśāyāmārabdhe pāke phale varttamānatvāropādvā, pacyate ityasyopapattiḥ, ekā'vayave'pi samūhā''ropādadhiśrayaṇakāle pacatīti prayogavat| ārope ca pratītireva mānam| vyāpāravigame phalasattve `apāci devadattena' ityādiprayogāstu bhūtatvasyotpattighaṭitatvādityāhuḥ|
tadapare na kṣamante, pṛthagupasthitayostathānvayabodhābhyupagame nānārthaharyyādipadopasthitānāmaśvasūryādīnāmapyuddeśyavidheyabhāvāpattirato vibhinnapadajanyopasthitereva tantratā tatrā'nusarttavyā| tadbhāvācca prakṛte kathaṃ tadanvayabodhā''pādanam tāddaśaphalavyāpārayordhātuśaktau mānābhāvo gauravañca| viśiṣṭadharmasya śakyatāvacchedakatve uktaprayogāṇām āropeṇopapādanaṃ tvagatikagatiriti spaṣṭameva vivekinām| etadanurodhena bhūtatvasyotpattighaṭitatvā'bhyupagame paramatā'nusṛtirapiti|
vyāpāre ākhyātārthakālā'nvaye dūṣaṇamāśaṅkaya nirācaṣṭe naceti| yatnasattvāditi yatrarūpavyāpārasya varttamānatvena viṣayābādhāt| mama tu tadānīṃ phalā'nutpattyā na tāddaśaprayogā''pattirityarthaḥ| aprayogāditi nanu parayatnasya vijātīyamanaḥsaṃyogāśrayasamāvāyarūpahetvabhāvādapratyakṣatve'pi ceṣṭādipakṣakeṇa talliṅgakena vā'numānena tadavagatāvuttiṣṭhatīti prayogā''pattirduḥsamādheyaivetyāśaṅkyeṣṭāpattyā pariharati kiñcicceṣṭādineti yatkiñciccharīrāvayavakriyayetyarthaḥ| tadavagatau yatnānumitau| anumānaprayogaścaivam-devadattīyaceṣṭā prayatnajanyā, ceṣṭātvāt, macceṣṭāvat| athavā, ayamutthānā'nukūlayatnavān, vijātīyaceṣṭāvattvāt, ahamiveti| ceṣṭāprayatnayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvaśca prakṛte'nukūlastarkaḥ| svāśrayāvacchedyatvasya hetutāvacchedakasambandhatvānna svarūpāsiddhiriti|
pare tūktāpattimanyathaiva pariharanti-tathāhi-āmavātajaḍīkṛtalevaraprayatnasyotthānoddeśyakatve'pi tadajanakatvānnoktaprayogāpattiḥ| ata eva `utthānāya yatate' ityeva tatra prayogaḥ| yāgapākādyuddeśyakakuṇḍanirmāṇataṇhulakrayaṇādidaśāyāṃ `yajati pacati' ityādiprayogavāraṇāya svarūpasambandharūpaprayojakatāviśeṣasyaiva sambandhatāyā abhyupeyatvāditi|
taccintyam, taduddeśyakatvaṃ hi tadicchā'dhīnecchāviṣayatvam, upāyecchāyāḥ phalecchādhīnatvāt| upāyatvañca prayojakatvameva| kathamanyathā tatreccheti, prakṛte yajatītyādiprayogo durvāra eva| tasmāt sākṣājjanyajanakabhāva eva phalavyāpārayoḥ sambandha ityevābhyupeyam, tāvataivoktaprayogavāraṇāt| prakṛte śaktirūpasahakārikāraṇābhāvena tadanutpattāvapi dīyayatne sākṣājjanakatvasya nirābādhatvenottiṣṭhatīti prayogasyaiveṣṭatvāt prakṛtadhātvarthatayā'vagate tasmin sādhyatvasyaiva sattvenoddeśyatvasya tadānīmasambhavācca| etadabhipretyaiva sārakṛtā, `utthānānukūlayatnasattve' ityuktam| ata eva pākānukūlaprakṛtadhātvarthayatnasattve pacatītyeva prayogo'nyadā tu `pākāya yatate' iti|
vastutastu kṛtirūpavyāpāramātrasya dhātvarthatvavivakṣāyāmuttiṣṭhatīti prayogaḥ| prayatnasāmarthyaceṣṭādighaṭitasya tasya samudāyasya tadvivakṣāyāntu neti sārakārāśayaḥ, ata eva tadotthānāya yatate iti prayogo'pi sūpapāda iti bodhyam|
upasaṃharati evañceti viśeṣaṇamevetyevakāreṇa tiṅarthasya viśeṣyatvavyavacchedaḥ bhāvanaiveti dhātvarthabhāvanaivetyarthaḥ| atrāpyevaśabdena phalasya tiṅarthakartrādesca vyavacchedaḥ| prakṛtipratyayārthayoriti vyutpattivirodhamāśaṅkate yadyapīti| anyatra paktotyādau| dṛṣṭamiti tathāca prakṛtyarthabhāvanāyāḥ prādhānyopagame tadvirodha iti bhāvaḥ| bhāvapradhānamiti nāmā''khyātopasarganipātabhedenoddiṣṭaṃ caturvidhaṃ padaṃ prakalpya nāmā''khyātalakṣaṇapratipādake ime vākye| atrā''khyātaṃ bhāvapradhānamiti yojanā| yathāśrute yacchabdayogaḥ prāthamyamityādyuddeśyalakṣaṇam|
ityabhiyuktokteḥ, śābdabodhe praṅ‌nirdiṣṭasyaivoddeśyatayā vivakṣitārthā'lābhāt| ata eva `vṛddhiśabdasya prāṅ‌nirddeśo maṅgalārthaḥ' ityā''karoktaṃ saṅgacchate| evamagre'pi| ākhyātatvā'vacchinne pradhānabhāvārthapratipādakatvarūpalakṣaṇatātparyāvadhāraṇācca lakṣaṇavākyācchabdamaryādayā prakṛtapadadvayārthābhedasyā'dhikasya bhāne'pi na kṣatiḥ| lakṣyatā'vacchedakañcatiṅantatvarūpā''khyātatvam| `ākhyātamākhyātena' ityādau tatraivā''khyātapadasya śaktyavadhāraṇāt| `bhāvakālakārakasaṅ‌khyāścatvāro'rthā ākhyātasya, tatra bhāvaḥ pradhānam' iti prakṛtaniruktabhāṣyācca| etena tiṅaḥ pradhānabhāvārthakatvasya pratipādakameva taditi vadantaḥ pratyuktāḥ|
tacchabdaparāmṛśya bhāvādicatuṣṭayasyaikadā tiṅā pratipādanā|sa़mbhavācca| bhāvatvaṃ niṣpādyatvam| nātaḥ karmā''khyātasya phalaviśeṣayakabodhakatāvādimate tatrā'prasaktiḥ| pradhānapratipādakatvasya nāmāmātre, bhāvapratipadakatvasya bhuktvetyādināmnyatiprasaktatvāttadvāraṇāṃya viśiṣṭamupāttam|
prādhānyañcopasthitatvāt svavācakraprakṛtikapratyayārthanirūpitameva| nātaḥ `kāṣṭhaiḥ pākaḥ' ityādau pratyayārthakaraṇaniṣṭhaprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣyatāśālibodhajanake pākādināmnyatiprasaṅgaḥ| navautsargikaikavacanāntabhāvavihitatavyādyantanāmni saḥ, tathāca tiṅ‌bodhyārthaniṣṭhaprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣyatātātparyapratipādakatvaṃ tat paryyavasyati|
itarāviśeṣaṇatvarūpaṃ tu na tat, pacati bhavatītyādivākyaghaṭakapacyādyarthakriyāyāṃ tadasattvena pacatītyākhyāte'vyāpteḥ| ktvaḥ pūrvakālārthakatvasya nirākariṣyamāṇamamatvānnoktalakṣaṇasyālakṣye'tiprasaṅgaḥ| "kṛdabhihito bhāvo dravyavat prakāśate" iti bhāṣyeṇa, pāka ityādāviva tasya sattvā'tideśena sādhyatvarūpabhāvatvā'bhāvādeva nā'tiprasaṅga iti tu na sat| yataḥ sādhutvānvākhyāyakavyākaraṇasmṛtyā yatkṛdarthabhāve liṅgādyanvaye'nubhavasiddhastasminneva tadatidiśyeta| yathā pāka ityādau ghañarthabhāve| ktvārthe tu liṅgādyananubhavena tatra tadatideśaviṣayasyānyāyyatvāt, avyayakṛttvena tasya dhātvarthanuvādakatvasya vakṣyamāṇatvācca|
tathāca bhāvapradhānakatvaṃ tiṅarthaniṣṭhaprakāratānirupitaviśeṣyatāśālibhāvapratipādakatvaṃ paryavasyati| etena, pacati bhavatītyādivākyaghaṭakā''khyātasaṃgrahāya kvacit, kriyāntaraniṣṭhaviśeṣyatānirūpitaviśeṣaṇatvābhāvavattvaṃ tat| pacyādyarthakriyāyā bhavatyarthaviśeṣaṇatve'pi kvacittadasamabhivyāhāre viśeṣyatayaiva bhānānna tatrātiprasaṅga ityuktaṃ nirastam| uktāpekṣayā abhāvaghaṭitatvenāsya gurutvāt| agrimalakṣaṇe prādhānyasya viśeṣyatvarūpasyaiva vakṣyamāṇatayā'trāpi tasyaiva nyāyyatvāt| tatra `bhāvapradhānam' iti bhāṣyeṇa maduktārthasyaiva lābhācca|
sattvapradhānānīti atrāpi pūrvavaduddeśyavidheyabhāvakalpanā| lakṣyatāvacchedakaṃ ca kṛdādyantaprakṛtikavibhaktyantatvarūpaṃ nāmatvam| prakṛtiḥ, pratyayaḥ, vibhaktirityetannām, sattā, dravyam, liṅgam, saṃkhyā, ca nāmārtha iti tadbhāṣyāt| `kriyāpradhānamākhyātaṃ sattvapradhānaṃ nāma| yataḥ kriyāṃ pṛṣṭastiṅācaṣṭe-kiṃ karoti? pacatīti| dravyaṃ pṛṣṭaḥ kṛtā''ceṣṭe-kataro devadattaḥ, yaḥ pācaka' iti `praśaṃsāyām' iti sūtrasthamahābhāṣyācca| tatra kṛtetyasya tatprakṛtikasubantenetyarthaḥ| kṛtā dravyā'bhidhāne'pi, `na kevalāḥ' iti nyāyena kevalasya prayogā'narhatvāt, niruktabhāṣyasthavibhaktipadasvārasyācca| vyutpattipakṣamavalambya cedam| nāto'vyutpattipakṣe nāmā''diṣvatiprasaṅgaḥ|
lakṣaṇamāha sattvapradhānānīti sattvam-dravyaṃ vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇaṃ tatpradhānaṃyatreti vigrahālliṅgādiniṣṭhaprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣyatāśālyarthapratipādakatvaṃ nāmalakṣaṇamityarthaḥ| viśeṣyā'rthakatvasya nipātā''khyātādāvatiprasaktatvāt tadvāraṇāya prakāratānirūpitatvāntaṃ viśeṣaṇam| vyaktibodhasya dharmaviṣyakatvaniyamena jātiprakāratānirūpitatvasyātiprasaṅgā'vārakatvāttadanupādānam| lakṣyatāvacchedakā'nākrānteṣu yādṛcchikeṣvatiprasaṅgastu lakṣaṇe tad‌vyatiriktatvaviśeṣaṇenaiva pariharaṇīyaḥ| evaṃ dhātvarthā'nuṃvādakapratyayānteṣvaprasaṅgo'pi lakṣyatāvacchedakasaṃkocenaiva parihāryaḥ| tatra nāmatvavyavahāro'pi svādyantatvanibandhano bhākta iti tadbhāvaḥ| upasargādilakṣaṇaṃ tu vakṣyate| bhūvādīti|
itthaṃ hi tatra bhāṣyam-`kā tarhīyaṃ vācoyuktiḥ `pacati bhavati' `tvaṃ pacasi bhavasi' pakṣyati bhavatīti| saiṣā vācoyuktiḥ, pacādikriyā bhavatikriyāyaḥ kartryo bhavanti" iti| tena cā''hatyaiva pacādyarthakriyākarttṛkabhavanakriyāyāḥ prādhānyaṃ pratipādyate| dhātvarthakriyāyā ākhyātārthaviśeṣaṇatvābhyupagame tu svasvārthaviruddhatvena padārthāntare viśeṣaṇatayā'nvaye nairākāṅ‌kṣ‌yeṇa bhavatyarthakriyāyāmākhyatārthaviśeṣaṇapacādikriyāṇāmanvayā'sambhavena tadvirodho'pratirodha eva syāditi bhāvaḥ| sutrādītyādinā "praśaṃsāyām" iti sūtraparigrahaḥ|
nanu " bhāvapradhānamākhyātam" iti niruktena liṅante bhāvānā'paraparyyāyabhāvaprādhānyaṃ pratipādyate| sā ca naiyāyikamate kartrākhyāte karttṛtvaṃ kṛtiḥ, karmākhyāte karmatvaṃ phalam, tannirūpitā''śrayatvaṃ vā| taiḥ saṅkhayākālātiriktā''khyātārthasya bhāvanāpadavyapadeśyatvopagamāt| bhāvā''khyāte tu dhātvarthavyāpāra evā'nuvādakatvāt tadākhyātasya| prādhānyaṃ ca tasyāḥ prakṛtyarthāpekṣam|
samānapratyayopāttatvapratyāsattyā phalamātraṃ dhātvartha iti vadatāṃ mīmāṃsakānāmetadapi bhāṣyaṃ mūlam| tatra laḍādyarthakālānvayopagame tu tadapekṣamapi| uktabhāṣye'pi kriyāpadārthastatsammatabhāvanaiva| ata eva "kriyāṃ pṛṣṭastiṅā''caṣṭe" ityevoktam, na tu tiṅanteneti|
kṛttyādirūpabhāvanāyā ākhyātārthatve mānaṃ tu bhūvādisūtrastham "kathaṃ jñāyate'yaṃ prakṛtyartha ayaṃ pratyayārthaḥ" iti praghaṭṭake'nvayavyatirekābhyāṃ śaktigrāhakam-"iha pacatītyukte kaścicchabdaḥ śrūyate, pac‌śabdaścakārānta atiścapratyayaḥ| śrūyate pratyayārtho'pi kaścid gamyate, viklittiḥ, karttṛtvam, ekatvam| `paṭhati' ityukte kaścicchabdo hīyate, kaścidupajāyate, kaścidanvayī pacchabdo hīyate, paṭh‌śabda upajāyate, atiśabdaścānvayī| artho'pi kaściddhīyate, kaścidupajāyate, kaścidanvayī| viklittirhīyate, paṭhikriyopajāyate, karttṛtvamekatvaṃ cānvayi| tena manyāmahe yaḥ śabdo hīyate yasyā'sāvartho yo'rtho hīyate,yaḥ śabda upajāyate tasyā'sāvartho yo'rtha upajāyate, yaḥ śabdo'nvayī tasyā'sāvartho yo'rthonvayī" iti bhāṣyam| tatrā'teḥ pratyayasya kaiścitpūrvācāryaiḥ kalpanād, atiśca pratyaya ityuktamiti kaiyaṭaḥ| `pacyate' `paṭhayate' ityādāvapyuktā'nvayavyatirekoddeśena karmatvasyāpyākhyātārthatvaṃ nyāyasāmyāt tata evā'vadhāryyate| kiñci, bhāvapradhānam" iti niruktaṃ tad‌bhāṣyasvarasāt sakalakārakasaṅkhayāviśiṣṭabhāvanāyā dhātvarthatvamāśritya pravṛttam| ata eva; nāmalakṣaṇanirvacanena kārakasya tatra nāmārthatvenollekhaḥ, tasya dhātulabhyatvena nāmārthatvābhāvāditi tadāśayāt|
naca rūpapsūtrasthabhāṣyaprāmāṇyāt kriyāyā ekatvasattve'pi dvitvā''disattve mānā'bhāvāt kathaṃ tathoktasaṅgatiriti vācyam, pare tvityādinā vakṣyamāṇarītyā kārakagatāyāstasyāstatrā''ropasambhavāt| kālasaṅkayayordyotyatvavat kārakasyā'pi dyotyatvam, nyāyasāmyāt| tasya pratyayārthatvopagame `pācakaḥ' `pakvaḥ' ityādāviva prādhānyāpatteḥ| jātyatiriktapadārthasya viśeṣaṇatāyā dyotyatvasyaiva tantratvāt| ata eva "striyām" iti sūtre bhāṣye prādhānyā''pattibhiyā liṅgasya vācyatāpakṣaupekṣitaḥ| vācyasya'pi prakṛtyarthāviśeṣaṇatve tadasaṅgatiḥ spaṣṭaiva|
nacoktakalpe `grāmaṃ gacchati' ityatra karmabodhavaddhātorapādānādikārakaviśiṣṭakriyābodhā''pattiḥ| pañcamyādirūpasamabhivyāhṛtadvitīyādivibhaktiśca dyotikā, anyeṣāṃ tvanyāḥ| nahi svaprakṛtikapratyayatvameva svārthadyotakatāyāṃ tantram, upasargāṇāṃ tadanāpatteḥ| kintu, svasamabhivyāhṛtaprattyayanipātānyataratvam| tacca taṇḍulādipadottaravibhakterapyakṣatam| dravyasya saṃkhyā'viśeṣitasya tattve nāmnaḥ saṅ‌khyābhānā''pattyā tadarthakatvamāvaśyakamiti tad‌bhāvaḥ| ata eva " bhāvakālakārakasaṅkayāścatvaro'rtho ākhyātasya" iti bhāṣyasthakārakapadasyā'saṅkucitavṛttitā'pi saṅgacchate|
yadi cānyaprakṛtikavibhakteranyaprakṛtyarthadyotakatāyāḥ kvāpyadṛṣṭacaratvānnoktā''śayavarṇanaṃ sādhīya iti vibhāvyate, tadāpi saṅ‌khyākālakarttṛ karmakāraviśiṣṭakriyāyā dhātvarthatvamādāya tatpravṛttiḥ, ākhyātasyaiva sādṛśakriyādyotakatāsambhavāt|
nacāstvevamevā''śayaḥ, vācyatākalpoktakāryakāraṇabhāvakalpanaprayuktalāghavasyopodvalakatvāditi vācyam, yat tādṛśamataṃ prakriyādaśāyāṃ cakṣuḥśrutyuktiviśvastairatyāśrayitumaśakyam, prāgeva tu prakṛtipratyayā'rthavivecanacaturairākṣapādaiḥ| tathāhi-kārakaviśiṣṭakriyāyā dhātvarthatve `laḥ karmaṇi' iti vidhivaiyarthyāpattiḥ| naca tasyā dyotakatvapratipādakatā, śānajāderapi tathātvā''pattyā tatrā'pi kartrādīnāṃ kriyāviśeṣaṇatayā bhānāpatteḥ|
kiñca, `caitrau pacana-' ityādau caitrādigatasaṅkhyāyāstatrārope mānābhāvaḥ, vācakatāmate kartrādyaṃśe tad‌bhānopagamena prayogaprāmāṇyopapattāvāprāmāṇyā'bhyupagamasyā'nyāyyatvāt, svāśrayā''dheyatvasambandhena dvitvādestatra sattve'pyavyāvarttakatayā viśeṣaṇatvā'bhāvena tadvaiśiṣṭhayasya tatrā'sambhavācca| nahi dvitvāśrayaśritaṃ ghaṭā''dirūpaṃ kriyā vā, dve iti vyāvahriyate| adhikamagre vakṣyate|
kiñca, karttṛkarmaṇoḥ padārthaikadeśatayā tatra devadattādīnāmanvayā'sambhavaḥ, "padārthaḥ padārthenā'nveti" iti vayutpatteḥ| tadanvayā'bhyupagame'pi dhātvarthe'bhedena viśeṣaṇatayā stokādipadavannapuṃsakatvā''pattiśca| apica, kriyāviśeṣaṇatvenopasthitasya kartturviśeṣeṇā''kāṅkṣāvirahāt pacatītyukte, kaḥ kīdṛgiti praśnā'nupapattiḥ| vakṣyati hi granthakṛtta-'nahi guṇabhūtaḥ karttā niṣedhaṃ svāṅgatvena grahītumalam' iti| ata evaitatprāmāṇyāt pratyayavācyatvam, kartturdhātvarthaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ ceti garjjayantaḥ parāstāḥ, pratyayasya dyotakatāyāmeva tanniyamasya darśitatvāt, pratyayārthaprādhānyasyaiva tato lābhācca|
naca bhāvanāyāḥ pratyayavācyatāpakṣepyuktapraśnā'nupapattirduḥsamādheyaiveti vācyam, śaktibhramāllakṣaṇayā vopasthitasya karttuḥ sākāṅkṣatvasambhavāt|
kiñca, `pratyayānāṃ prakṛtyarthā'nvitasvārthabodhakatvam' iti vyutpattisiddhavibhattayarthasaṅ‌khyāprakārakabodhaṃ prati viśeṣyatayā prakṛtijanyopasthiterhetutvasya kartrādāvabhāvena saṅkhayā'nvayā'sambhavaḥ| ākhyātārthasaṅkhayāprakārakabodhe ākhyātārthakarttṛkarmopasthiterhetutvakalpanamapyāgrahamūlakameva, padārthayorādhārādheyabhāvenā'nvaye vibhinnapadajanyopasthitestantratvāt| vibhattayarthakartturliṅgānanvayino'dravyatayā tatra saṅkhayānvayāsambhavāt, "guṇānāñca parārthatvāt" iti nyāyācca| manmate ayogyatayā tasyāḥ svaprakṛtyarthānanvaye'pi prakṛtyarathānvayaniyamo'kṣata eva, "aṇurapi viśeṣo'dhyavasāyakaraḥ" iti nyāyāt|
kiñcā''khyātasya kartrarthakatve rājasambandhipuruṣā'bhinnakarttṛkaṃ gamanamityabhiprāyeṇa, `rājñaḥ puruṣo gacchati' itivat svasvāmibhāvādisambandhena rājñaḥ karttṛtvena vivakṣayā `rājā puruṣo gacchati' iti prayogā''pattiḥ| naca tatra ṣaṣṭhīprasaktiḥ, tadarthasya prātipadikārthaviśeṣyatayā vivakṣāyāmeva tatprasarāt,| vyaktībhaviṣyati caitadupariṣṭāt| ākhyātārthakarturanāmadhātvarthatvena "nāmārthadhātvarthatvayoḥ iti vyutpattiviṣayatāyā apyabhāvāt|
naca tavāpi caitrasya pākānukūlā kṛtiriti `caitrasya pacati' iti prayogāpattiriti vācyam, ṣaṣṭhayarthasya nāmārthasākaṅkṣatayā''khyātārthakṛteśca prathamāntārthasākāṅkṣatayā parasparākāṅakṣāvirahāt, "guruvipratapasvidurgatānām" ityādāviva pākādisambandhitayā caitrādivikṣāyāṃ ṣaṭhayantaprayogasya sarvairevopagamācca| tasmāddhātvarthakriyāyā ākhyātārthaṃ prati prādhānye niruktamānopanyasanaṃ mudhaiveti kovido vidāṃkurvantu|
pacatibhavatītyādau pākānakūlā''khyātārthakṛtau bhāvanāśrayatvarūpabhavatīti tiṅantā'rthabhāvanā'nvayopagamena pākaviśiṣṭakṛtau bhavanakarttṛtve'vagate tadviśeṣaṇapacyarthakriyāyāmapi tallābhāt `pacādayaḥ kriyāḥ bhavatikriyāyāḥ kartryo bhavanti' bhāṣyasthādipadasya hetuparatayā tena vyāpārā'rthakapacādiśabdasya samāse pacyādyarthahetavaḥ kriyāḥ kṛtyātmakavyāpāra bhavanakriyānirūpitakarttṛtvavatyo bhavantītyarthasya tasmāllābhād vā tādṛśaikavākyatvasyā'smābhirabhyupagamānna bhūvādisūtrasthabhāṣyavirodho'pi| tādṛśabhāṣyapramāṇyādeva ca bhavanmate dhātvarthaprakārakabodhe dhātujanyopasthiterhetutvavanmanmate'pyā''khyātārthabhāvanāprakārakabodhe tiṅjanyabhāvanopasthiterapi viśeṣyatāsambandhena hetutvasya kalpyatayoktaikavākyatvasya sūpapādatvāt|
nacoktabodhābhyupagame akhyātena kartturabodhanāccaitrādipadottaraṃ tṛtīyā''pattiriti vācyam, tādṛśaprathamāntārthaprakārakabodhe viśeṣyatayā tiṅjanyopasthiterhetutayā tasyāṃ caitrāsyā''dheyatāsambandhenā'nvayābhyupagamena mukhyaviśeṣyatayaiva tadbhānena āśrayātiriktāṃśe viśeṣṇatayā kṛtyākṣayabodhanarūpasyābhidhānasya tatra sattvāt tṛtīyā''pattyasambhavāt|
naca caitraḥ pacatītyetāvanmātraprayoge'pi caitrasyā''dheyatāsambandhenānvayā''pattiriti vācyam, prathamāntā'rthaprakārakabodhe tiṅarthaviśeṣyatāpannabhāvanopasthiterhetutvasyāpi kalpanenoktadoṣā'prasakteḥ|
naca caitre pākānukūlā kṛtirityatreva caitraḥ pacati bhavatītyādau saptamyā''pattirdurvāraiveti vācyam, dhātvarthaviśeṣaṇabhūtādheyatvasya prātipadikārthaviśeṣyatayā vivakṣāyāmeva saptamīvidhānāt, evamādheyatvasya prakāratayā'vivakṣaṇācca| idaṃ cākhyātārthakṛtimukhyaviśeṣyakabodhamabhipretyoktam| vastutastu, caitraḥ pacati bhavatītyādau prathamāntārthaviśeṣyakabodhābhyupagame'pi naikavākyatākṣatiḥ, bhavanakartṛtvā'nvitāyā eva pākaviṣayakabhāvanāyāścaitrāṃśe prakāratayā bhānasambhavāt|
etena kṛtiviśeṣyakabodābhyupagame prathamāntārthaprakārakabodhe tiṅarthabhāvanopasthiterhetutvakalpane gauravamiti dūṣaṇamalagnakam, viśeṇatayaiva tadanvayābhyupagamādityarucerāha apiceti| abhedānvayāditi idaṃ ca tanmate'pi kartrākhyātārtha iti svīyavāsanā'nusāreṇa| naiyāyikamate tu kṛterākhyātārthatayā tasyā āśrayatvenaiva prathamāntapadā'rthe'nvayādityavadheyam|
bhāṣyasiddheti "kriyā'pi kṛtrimaṃ karma, kriyā'pi hi kriyayepsitā bhavati, kayā, sandarśanaprārthanādikriyayā" iti "karmaṇāyam" itsūtrasthabhāṣyasiddhetyarthaḥ| tvanmate dhāvanā'nukūlakṛtimān mṛga ityāvāntaravākyārthabodhasya tatra jāyamānatvena dhāvanasya kartravaruddhatayā viśeṣṇatayā'nyatrā'nvaye nairākāṅkṣayeṇa darśane karmatayā'nvayā'yogāt| kintu dhāvanā'nukūlakṛtimān mṛgo darśanāśrayastvamityeva bodha eṣṭavyaḥ| evañcaikamukhyaviśeṣyakabodhajanakatvarūpaikavākyatvā'bhāvāt tadvirodho durvāra iti bhāvaḥ|
nanu dhāvanā'nukūlakṛtimanmṛgasyaiva karmatayā dṛśyarthe'nvayānnoktadoṣo'ta āha-prathamānteti karmatvā pattāvitikarmatvena prakāratāpattavityarthaḥ| karmatāsambandhena mṛgasya darśanakriyāyāmanvayastvabhambhavī, "nāmārthadhātvarthayoḥ" iti vyutpattivirodhāt, vakṣyamāṇadvitīyā''pādanavirodhācceti bhāvaḥ| dvitīyāpatteriti karmaṇo'tra tiṅā'nabhidhānena anabhihitādhikārīya "karmaṇi dvitīyā" ityasya jāgarūkatvīditi bhāvaḥ|
evamiti mṛgapadottaraṃ dvitīyāprasaṅge ityarthaḥ| śatṛśānaj‌vidhau laḍarthakartuḥ prathamāntā'rthasāmānādhikaraṇyena viśeṣitatvānmṛgapadasya dvitīyāntatve tadāpattyā dvitīyā na bhaviṣyatīti śaṅkiturāśayaḥ| nahi śatrāpattirdvitīyāpratibandhikā, kintu karmaṇo'bhidhānm, tadabhāvasattvācca dvitīyā durvaretyaśayena samādhatte-evamapīti| asambhavāpatteriti| mṛgasya karmatayā dṛśyarthenvayopagame 'dhāvantaṃ magaṃ paśya' iti vākyasyaivocitatvādityarthaḥ|
nanu " arthokyādekaṃ vākyaṃ sākāṅkṣañcedvibhāgesyāt" iti jaiminisūtrād vicchidya pāṭhe sākāṅkṣatve satyekārthapratipattijanakatvavamekavākyatvamityartho labhyate| prakṛte tamityukte kamityākāṅkṣāsattvādyathākathañcidvākyadvayasya dhāvanā'nukūlakṛtīmanmṛgakarmakadarśanarūpaikārthapratipādakatvācca na vākyabhedeprasaṅgo'ta āha ukṭeti| adhyāhāra iti pūrvā'nupāttākāṅikṣatapadasyā'nusandhāne ityarthaḥ| ananvayāpatteśceti tatpadena mṛgasya dhāvanā'nukūlakṛtīmanmṛgasya vā parāmarśe'pīpsitasya tādṛśadhāvanakriyāyā dṛśyarthakarmatvānvasya tadvākyād bhānā'nupapatterityarthaḥ| upasaṃharati-enañceti ākhyātārthaviśeṣyakabodhā'ṅgīkartṛnaye ekavākyatā'nupapattirūpadūṣaṇadāḍharye cetyarthaḥ|  nādaraṇīyamiti uktadoṣagrastatvāditi bhāvaḥ| svamataniṣkarṣamāha-kintviti phale vyabhicāranirāsāyopasthitiviśeṣyatāṃ viśināṣṭi-bhāvanātvāvacchinteti|
nanu tvanmate'pyākhyātasya dhātvarthamukhyaviśeṣyakabodhajanakatvaniyamabhaṅgaprasaṅgo'ta āha paśyeti tathācoktaniyama etadatiriktaviṣyaka iti bhāvaḥ|
nanu dhāvanakartṛtvaviśiṣṭamṛgarūpavākyā'rthasya karmatayā darśane'nvayaḥ| tathāca na "nāmārthayoḥ" iti vyutpattivirodhaḥ, vākyasyānāmatvāt| ata eva na tato dvitīyāpattirapi| dhāvanaviśiṣṭasya darśanakarmatvā'vagatau viśeṣaṇībhūtadhāvanasyāpi karmatālābhācca na pratipipādayiṣitānvayālābho'pi| 'caitraḥ pacati bhavati' ityādau tūktadiśaikavākyatvamupupāditameva| tathācoktakāryyakāraṇabhāvakalpe kathaṃ tadanurodho bījamata āha digiti tadarthastvevaṃrītyā vākyaikavākyatāpratipādane bhāṣyakṛtsammatapadaikavākyatayā pratipipādayiṣitānvayālābha eva| kiñcābhedena nīliśiṣṭaghaṭasya karmatve pratipipādayiṣite, 'nīlaṃ ghaṭaṃ jānāti' ityādau dvitīyānupapattiriti|
atra naiyāyikāḥ-dhāvanakriyākarttṛtvāvaruddhamṛgasya karmatāsambandhena dṛśyarthe'nvayopagamānna padaikavākyatābhaṅgaḥ| tadavaruddhatvañca tadanvayitayā tātparyyaviṣyatvam| nacaivaṃ karmatāsaṃsargeṇa taṇḍulasyā'pyanvayasambhavāt `taṇḍulaḥ pacati' iti prayogāpattiriti vācyam, yato na vayaṃ sāmānyataḥ karmatvādibhedasambandhena nāmārthasya dhātvarthe'nvayamabhyupemaḥ, kintu kriyāntarakarttṛtvā'varuddhasya| naca taṇḍulasyathā, yena pacyādyarthe tasya karmatāsaṃsargaḥ syāt| ata eva `kāṣṭhaṃ bhasmarāśiḥ karoti' iti na prayogaḥ, bhasmarāśeḥ kartṛtvānavaruddhatvāt| `kāṣṭhaṃ bhasmarāśiḥ kriyate' ityādau karmatvāntarāvaruddhakriyāyāṃ tu tadanavaruddhasyāpi vyutpattivaicitryāt| tasya tatra tatsaṃsargatvā'nabhyupagame vākyebhedā''pattyā tadvākyajabodhasya, `kāṣṭhaṃ nāśyate' `bhasma kriyate' iti vākyadvayajabodhasyeva `bhasmakāṣṭhavikṛtiranyavikṛtirvā' iti saṃśayanivarttakatvānāpatterityādyanyatra prapañcitam|
evañcā'tra padaikavākyatāyā anāyāsenaivopapattau bhāvanāprakārakabodhe dhātvarthabhāvanopasthirhetutvaṃ niṣpramāṇākameva| nā'pi mṛgapadottaraṃ dvitīyā''pattiḥ, prātipadikārthaviśeṣyatayā karmatvasyā'vivakṣaṇāt| ata eva na tatra ṣaṣṭhīprasaktirapi, saṃsargasya prātipadikārthaviśeṣyatayā vivakṣāyāmeva, "ṣaṣṭhī śeṣe" ityanena tadvidhānāt| evam, `ghaṭo nāsti' ityādāvapi bodhyam| kintu prātipadikārthe tatra prathamaiva| nā'pi `nīlaṃ ghaṭaṃ jānāti' iti prayogā'nupapattiḥ, ghaṭasya kriyāntarakarttṛkatvā'navaruddhatvena karmatvāntaraviśeṣaṇatānāpannāyāṃ jānātyarthakriyāyāṃ karmatāyāḥ saṃsargatvāsambhavāt|
`paśya lakṣamaṇa pampāyāṃ bakaḥ paramadhārmikaḥ'|
ityādāvastītyadhyāhāryyam| vastutastvetat dṛṣṭāntena `taṇḍulaṃ pacati' ityādau bodhā''pādanaṃ na vicārasaham, karmatā'nvayasaṃsargakapacyarthaviśeṣyakabodhasyāprasiddhau tathā''pādanāsambhavāt| `taṇḍulaṃ pacati' ityādau vibhaktyarthakarmatvasambandhena tādṛśabodhaḥ prasiddha iti cettarhi tādṛśabodhe dvitīyāntataṇḍulapadasamabhivyāhṛtapacatītyānupūrvvojñānasya hetutayā tadabhāvādevā''pādyasyāpya'sambhavāt| taduktam-'yādṛśaṃ phalaṃ kvacit prasiddhaṃ tādṛśasyaivā''pattiḥ sambhavati' iti|
etena `paśya lakṣmaṇa' ityatra " āstirbhavantīparo'nuprayujyamāno'pyasiti" iti bhāṣyasthā'piśabdasvārasyena vākyaparipoṣakakriyāntarā'bhāve'steradhyāhāra ityarthalābhena prakṛte vākyaparipoṣikāyā darśanakriyāyāḥ sattvena tadadhyāhāre mānābhāvāt karmatāsaṃsargeṇā'nvayo'nupapanna iti duṣaṇamalagnakam| evañcā''khyāte prathamāntapadasamabhivyāhāre prāyaḥ prathamāntārthaviśeṣyakabodho nirābādhaḥ| ata eva, `yo yaḥ śūdrasya pacati dvijo'nnaṃ so'tininditaḥ' ityatra pradhānaparāmarśitado dvijaparāmarśakatvamanāyāsena saṃgacchate|
nanu prathamāntārthaviśeṣyakabodhavādimate, `pacataḥkalpaṃ caitrau" ityādau kalpabādyantasya nāmatayā, tadviśeṣaṇavācakatayā ca prathamāntacaitrādipadasamānaliṅgavacanatvā''pattirākhyātārthakṛteḥ prathamāntārthacaitrādāvabhedānvayāpattiśceti cenna, svārthikānāṃ prāyaśaḥ prakṛtyarthatā'vacchedakagatakutsādidyotakatayā prakṛte prakṛtyarthatā'vacchedakakriyāgateṣadasamāptatvasya kalpapā dyotanāt, sattvāsattvābhyāṃ prakṛtyarthatāvacchedakasamānasya bhāveṣadasamāptatvapratipādakakalpabādyante napuṃsakaikavacanāntatvayoraucityāt| kalpabādyantasya nāmatvena samānavibhaktikatvena ca tadarthasya caitre'bhedā'nvayā''pattiriti tu mūrkhapralapitatvādanādeyam, lakṣyārthakartṛrebhedānvayasyeṣṭatvāt| śakyārthasya tvayogyatvādevānanvayāt, `ghaṭaḥ' `paṭaḥ' ityādau vyabhicāravāraṇāya vyutpattāvasati bādhake iti viśeṣaṇāccetyāhuḥ|
atredamābhāti-prathamāntakarttṛvācakapadasamabhivyāhāre prāyaḥ prathamāntarthaviśaṣyakabodha eva naiyāyikānām| kvacittu, paśya mṛgo dhāvatītyādyekavākyatā'nurodhāt tiṅarthabhāvanāviśṣyako'pi saḥ| tathāhi-vaiyākaraṇairuktasthalīyaikavākyatānurodhāddhātvarthabhāvanāprakārakabodhe dhātujanyabhāvanopasthiterhetutā kalpyate|
naiyāyikaistu karmatāsambandhena tiṅarthabhāvanāprakārakabodhe dhātujanyabhāvanopasthitestaditi samameva| `devadattaḥ pacati bhavati' ityekavākyatānurodhātprathamāntārthakartṛprakārakabodhe tiṅarthabhāvanopasthiterapi tatkalpyate| tathācoktasthala ādheyatāsambandhena mṛgaviśiṣṭadhāvanā'nukūlakṛteḥ karmatayā dṛśyarthe'nvaye mṛganiṣṭhā yā dhāvanā'nukūlā kṛtīstatkarmadarśanā''śrayastvamiti vākyārthabodhaḥ| dhāvanaviśiṣṭakṛtau ca bodhitakarmatvasya, "saviśeṣaṇe hi" iti nyāyāddhāvane paryavasānācca pratipipādayiṣitārthasyā'pi lābhaḥ| nā'pi mṛgapadottaraṃ dvitīyā''pattiḥ, mṛgakarmatāyā etad‌vākyādalābhāt| evañca, `pacati bhavatī' ityatrā'pi `prathamāntārthakartṛviśiṣṭā pākakṛtirvarttamānābhāvanā''śrayikā' iti bodhāt tatrā'pyekavākyatvā'kṣatiḥ| evañca `prakṛtipratyayārthayoḥ' iti nyāyā'virodhenaiva bhāṣyakāroktapadaikavākyatā nyāyanaye'pi sūpapādetyalaṃ paramatā'nuvarṇanena|
phalitamāha ityavceti ākhyātā'rthakarttṛprakārakabodhaṃ prati dhātvarthabhāvanopasthitestādṛśakarmaprakārakabodhe tādṛśaphalopasthiterhetutve cetyarthaḥ| bhāvanetyasya varttamānetyādiḥ| karmā''khyāte bhāvanāviśeṣyakabodhopavarṇanantu prācāmanurodhena|
navyāstu-karmakṛtiphalaviśeṣyabodhasya dṛṣṭatvena karmākhyāte'pi tad‌viśeṣyakabodha eva nyāyyaḥ| evañca pratyayārthe sākṣātprakṛtyarthaviśeṣyānvayitvamiti vyutpattirapi na viruvdyate| ata eva `iṣyate putraḥ' ityarthe putrīyatīti na, bhinnārthakatvāt| kintu, putrāmicchatītyartha eveti, "supa ātmanaḥ"  iti sūtre bhāṣye uktam| anyathobhayatrā'pi vyāpāraviśeṣyakabodhena vibhinnārthakatvakathanā'saṅgatiḥ, asmanmate tvekatra vyāpāro viśeṣyo'nyatrecchārūpaṃ phalamiti bhinnā'rthakatvaṃ spaṣṭameva, kyajantāttu karmaṇi pratyayo durlabhaḥ, akarmakatvāt| uktañca kaiyaṭena-`yadā kriyāphalasya prādhānyaṃ tadā vākyameva-iṣyate putraḥ, natu kyajantaḥ, tasyā'karmakatvāt karmaṇi pratyayā'nutpādāt' iti | itthañca `bhāvanājanyaikāśrayikā varttamānā viklittiḥ' iti bādha ityāhuḥ|
bhāvā''khyāte tu bhāvanaiva viśeṣyā, tathaivā'nubhavāt| tiṅ‌vanuvādaka eva, kintu laṭ‌tvādinā kālārthakaḥ| saṃkhyā tu na tadarthaḥ, kartrādirūpā'rthopasthityā'tmakahetvabhāvena tadarthaṃsaṃkhyāyā anvayā'mbhavāt| kintu, `na kevalāḥ' ityādinyāyena sādhutvārthamekavacanameva| "ekavacanamutsargataḥ kariṣyate" iti siddhāntāt|
"kāle yuktabhisaraṇe muhyante mṛgamohikāḥ|
uṣṭrāsikāḥ samāsyante śayyante hataśāyikāḥ||"
ityatra vahuvacanantu bhāṣyakāraprayogādeveti prāñcaḥ|
navyāstu, ekavacanasyaitsargikatvaṃ satyeva gamake, yathā'vyayāt tadutpattau "avyayādāpsupaḥ" iti| ata eva "ṅyāp" iti sūtrā'bhāve tiṅantebhyastadutpattimāśaṅkaya tiṅā saṃkhyāyā uktatvānneti parihṛtaṃ bhāṣye| tvaduktarītyā tiṅā kārakagatāyāstasyā uktatve'pi prakṛtyarthagatāyāstasyā anuktatayautsargikatayā vaikavacanasyāśakyavāraṇatvena tadasaṅgatiḥ spaṣṭaiva|
"dvyokayoḥ" ityāderlaḥ karmaṇi ityanenaikavākyatā'pi-lakārāḥ karmādau bhavanti teṣāṃ sthāne caikatvādau tibādayo bhavantītyākāraiva| nahi tatra dvitīyā karmaṇātyādyasti, yena saṃkhyāyāḥ kārakaviśeṣaṇatā pratīyeta| āvṛttau ca na mānam| tasmāt sarvatra saṃkhyāyāḥ prakṛtyartha evā'nvayaḥ|
"kasyaikatvādau" ityākāṅkṣāyāmupasthitatvāt prakṛtyarthā'nvayasyaivocitatvāt| karmaṇītyāderapi vidheyaṃ prati viśeṣaṇatvena, `guṇānāñca parārthatvāt' iti nyāyena saṃkhyāyāmasambandhāt| karmaṇītyādeḥ kasya karmaṇītyā'kāṅkṣāsattvena tatra dhātvarthānvayasya klṛptatvāt| kriyāyāmapyekatvamastyeva "ekā kriyā" iti rūpapsūtrabhāṇyāt| evañca tasya prakṛtyarthe'nvayādekavacanam| dvitvādikantu svottaratiṅvācyakārakagataṃ tatrāropya karttṛkarmakeṣu tiṅṣu dvivacanādisaddhiḥ| ata eva na sādhane dvitvādisaṃśayaḥ| kriyāgatasaṃkhyāropasya sādhanajñānanimittatvāt| anekaḥ pacatītyādyanurodhena sādhane'pi saṃkhyāropasya tavā'pi vācyatvāt, tasyā'pya'nvayaḥ prakṛtyartha eva| prakṛte, hataśāyikāḥ śayyante ityādau hataśayanasadṛśāgatāyā evāropo nā'nyagatāyā ityatra dhātvarthaśaktisvabhāva eva bījam| ata eva bhāve dvivacanādiḥ| taduktam-
"ekatve'pi kriyā'' kriyā''khyāte sādhanāśrayasaṃkhyayā|
bhidyate na tu liṅgākhyo bhedastatra tadāśrayaḥ||" iti |
evañcākhyātārthaprakārakabodhe dhātujanyopasthitiḥ| kāraṇamityeka eva kāryakāraṇabhāva iti lāghavam, " pratyayānām" iti vyutpattiparipālanañcetyāhuḥ|
tadapare na kṣamante, autsargikaikavacanasya samayaparipālanārthakatayā tatra gamakā''kāṅkṣāyā abhāvena `satyeva gamake' ityuktirvyarthā| yadapi gamakatayā ṅyāpsūtrabhāṣyopanyasanam, tat tathaiva, yatastadeva bhāṣyaṃ kriyāyāṃ saṃkhyāsāmānyābhāvabodhakam| tathāhi-ṅyāpsūtrā'bhāve tiṅantāt subutpattiśaṅkā pratyayārthakārakasaṃkhyāmādāya, prakṛtyarthagatasaṃkhyāmādāya vā vaktavyā| tatra prakṛtyarthasyā'sattvarūpatayā saṃkhyānvayā'yogāt| kārakagatasaṃkhyāmādāyaiva prasaktā, sā tiṅoktatvādityanena nirākriyate| samayaparipālanasya tiṅaiva kṛtatvāt, tadaprāptyaiva tannirākaraṇasyā'prasakteḥ| `sup‌tiṅantaṃ padam' `tiṅ‌ṅatiṅaḥ' ityādisūtrasvārasyāduktārthajñāpanāpekṣayā tiṅ‌ṅantādautsargikaikavacanābhāvasyaiva tena jñāpayitumucitatvācca|
`ekā ca kriyā' iti bhāṣyamapi na kriyāyāmekatvaṃ bodhayati, kintu saṃkhyāsāmānyā'bhāvameva| kathamanyathā sādhāraṇaikavacanenaivopapattau vacanārambhasārthakyam| kiñcopamāne bahutvopapādanasāmarthyeneti vadatā kārake dvitvādyupapādanasāmarthyenetya'pyuktaprāyam| evañca, hataśāyikā ityatropamāne bahutvopapādakabahuvacanavat prakṛte kārake kasyacid dvitvādyupapādana syābhāvāt tadāropāsambhavaḥ|
naca caitramaitrāviti dvivacanāditastadavagatiḥ, caitro maitraśca pacata ityādau tadabhāvāt,kārakānvayātprāgeva caitrādyanvitāyāstasyāḥ kārakānvitatvācca| bhāvā''khyāte svottaratiṅ‌vācyakārakā'prasiddhayā dvivacanāsambhavācca| `kasyaikatve' ityākāṅ‌kṣāyā yogyajijñāsyasambandhinyā evaucityāt, kriyāyā asattvena paricchedakākāṅkṣāyā evāsambhavena tatra tadanvayokteḥ parāhatatvācca| pacatītyādijanyabodhānantaraṃ jāyamānasya sarvasiddhaikatvādivaiśiṣṭ‌yena jijñāsyakārakabodhaka `kaḥ kau' ityādipraśnasyā'nupapanattyā siddhāyāḥ kārake saṃkhyānvayapratīteranupapattereva karmaṇītyādipadānuvṛttau mānatvāt| uktavākyavadīyasyāpyākhyātārthakriyāyā ekatve'pi saṃkhyāsāmānyābhāvavattve'pi kvacit karttṛkarmādibāhulyena sā nāneva pratīyate ityarthānna tadvirodho'pi| tataḥ śabdamaryyādayā dhātvarthe tiṅarthasaṅ‌khyā'nvaya ityarthasyālābhācca| "guṇānāñcaparārthattvāt" iti tu pradhānasyānvayayogyatve satyeva| ata eva kaiyaṭādibhistiṅarthasaṃkhyāyāḥ kāraka evā'nvayaḥ svīkṛtaḥ| evañca bhāṣyakāraprayogādanyatra bhāvākhyāte dvivācanādyasādhvevetyadhikaṃ svayamūhyam|
ākhyātārthakartrādibhiriti `samānavibhaktikayorevā'bhedānvayaḥ' iti niyamasya prāgeva dūṣitatvāditi bhāvaḥ| akarmakasthale'pi phalavyāpārobhayārthakatvaṃ dhātoranyairapi svīkāryyamiti sūcayañcchābdabodhaprakāramāha ghaṭo naśyatīti| tad‌vyāpārā''kāṅ‌kṣāyāmāha sa ceti| pratiyogitveti pratiyogitvavaiśiṣṭyantu nirūpakatā sambandhena bodhyam| tatsamavadhānimeva tadanukūlo vyāpāra ityarthaḥ|
ata eveti yato nāśamātraṃ nārthaḥ, pratiyogitvaviśiṣṭanāśaphalakatattatsāmagrīrūpo vyāpāro'pyata evetyarthaḥ| phala-vyāpārayoraikādhikaraṇyācca na sakarmakatvam| etadarthameva pratiyogitvaviśiṣṭetyupāttam| yanmate nāśamātraṃ na dhātorarthastanmate nāśotpattyanantaraṃ nāśasya sadaiva sattvānnaśyatīti prayogāpattistasya dhvaṃsā'pratiyogitvācca naṣṭa iti prayogā'nupapattiriti sūcanāya, tasyāṃ satyāsityādinā traikālikaprayogapradarśanam| naiyāyikamataṃ tu luṅarthanirūpaṇe vakṣyat|
jānātyādisaviṣayārthakānāṃ jñānādyatiriktavyāpārādyarthakatvānabhyupagame sakarmakatvā'nupapattiṃ sūṃcayan svamate śābdabodhaṃ darśayati devadatto jānātītyādi| ādipadagrāhyā'rthamāha  icchādīti| kvacijjānātītyādāvityeva pāṭhaḥ|
sa ceti| jñānānukūlo vyāpāraścetyarthaḥ| tadanveṣaṇāyātmamanaḥ| saṃyogasyaiva sulabhatvādāha antata iti tadanupasthitāvityarthaḥ āśrayataiveti tasyā anavayavatve'pi sā'vayavatvāropād vyāpāratvaṃ nirvāhyamiti bhāvaḥ|  upoddhātasaṅgatyā śabādīnāṃ dyotakatāṃ nirūpayitumāha nanviti nanu `sakṛduccaritaḥ śabdaḥ sakṛdarthaṃ gamayati' iti vyuttpatternaikadā'rthadvayabodho'ta āha karttṛmātrabodhavaditi mātraśabdo'vadhāraṇe tātparyyeti tattadarthapratīticchayoccaritvarūpatātparyagrahajanakamityarthaḥ| ata eva karttari śabityādau kartrādigrahaṇaṃ sārthakam| anyathā `laḥ karmaṇi' ityetadvihitalādeśaireva kartrādibodhenānyalabhyatvāttadvaiyarthyaṃ spaṣṭameveti bhāvaḥ|
mūle phalavyāpārayostatreti| tatreti dhātūpasthāpyayorityarthaḥ| `phalavyāpārayoḥ' iti nirddhāraṇe saptamītyāśayena vyācaṣṭe sāre phala iti|  vyabhicāra iti| yagādīnāmanvaye, śabādīnāṃ vyatireke cetyarthaḥ| yagādisattve'pi phale āśrayā'nvayā'bodhācchabādyabhāve'pi vyāpāre tiṅartha āśrayānvayāt tadevopapādayati karmaṇaḥ karttṛtvavivakṣāyāmityādi| saukaryyātiśayadyotanārthā ca tadvivakṣā taṇḍulādigatāgnisaṃyogādirūpavyāpārasyaiva phalajanakatayā dhātvarthatvavivakṣāyāmisyarthaḥ|
vastutastu phalasyāpi janakavyāpāragatapaurvāparyyāropeṇa vyāpāratvena bhānāt iti pūrvamuktatvāttaṇḍulagataphalasyaiva vyāpāratvena vivakṣāyāmityarthaḥ| sādhanāditi| tathāca vyābhicārānna taṅādīnāṃ dyotakateti bhāvaḥ|
kramādityāditi| karmakarttṛviṣayādāviti mūlasthādipadenetyarthaḥ sāmānyetyādikramādityasya vivaraṇam, saviṣayadhātvarthanirūpitakarmatvasya viṣayatve paryyavasānamityabhipretyāha viṣayaketi| sakarmakadhātusamabhivyāhṛtayagādīnāṃ karmadyotakatvādāha sakarmaketi pacyate taṇḍulaḥ svayamevetyādau yagādi tu na tathā, kintvātideśikam| `karmavatkarmaṇā' iti vihitātideśatvasyā''kare siddhāntitatvānna tatra vyabhicāra iti bhāvaḥ| bhāvasādhāraṇeti| bhāvakarmaṇoḥ sārvadhātuke yagityādivihitetyarthaḥ| tena, `dīpajana' ityādivihitvyāvṛttiḥ||  evamiti uktaprakāreṇetyarthaḥ| nanu phalavyāpārayorityanena sāmānyato dhātoḥ phalavyāpāravācakatva pratījñātasyottarakṣaṇe evopapattibhistad‌vyavasthāpanaṃ karttumucitam, natu vākyārthanirūpaṇānantaram, tena jijñāsāvicchedādityāśaṅkāyāmāha sūcīkaṭāhanyāyeneti| tathāca sāmānyataḥ phalavyāpārayordhātvarthatve, āśrayasya tiṅarthatve ca jñāte, nidṛsanīyavādavipratipattikatvena supratipādyatayā ca prathamaṃ vākyārthe eva śiṣyajījñāsitatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
`laḍādyante' ityatad‌guṇasaṃvijñānabahuvrīhiṇā dhātuparam| tathāca, yajeta, pacetetyādau yajyādidhātāvityarthaḥ| avācyatvam vācyatānirūpakatvā'vā'bhāvam| `prabhākaradīn' ityādinā naiyāyikasaṃgrahaḥ|
yadyapi vyāpāro dhātvarthatvena naiyāyikasammatastathāpi nā'sau tanmate bhāvanāpadavyapadeśya iti bhāvaḥ| `vyāparo bhāvanā saiva' ityādipūrvārddhasya yo `guṇabhūtairavayavaiḥ' ityādyuktalakṣaṇalakṣito'rthastadvācakavyāpārapadaṃ bhāvanādipadaivivriyate| vivriyamāṇasya vivaramasamānārthakatvena dhātorbhāvanāvācakatvasiddhirityarthaḥ| tatroddeśyavidheyayoraikyamāpādayataḥ sarvanāmnaḥ paryayoṇānyataralliṅgakatvasya, `śaityaṃ hi yat sā prakṛtirjalasya `ityādibahuṣu sthaleṣu darśanānna `sā' iti strīliṅganirddeśānupapattiḥ| pum̐lliṅgatve'pi,`so'ci lope cet' iti lopena sādhutvācca| bhāvanaiva vā'stu tatpadaparāmṛṣyā|
nanu sambhavedevaṃ dhātorvyāpārārthakatve, tatra ca kiṃ mānamityāśaṅkakṣa kṛñā dhātuvivaraṇe mānaṃ darśayati sāre pacati pākamutpādayatīti nanu `yā kriyā bhāvanā saivotpādanā'pi ca sā smṛtā' ityukte'pi kriyāyā bhāvanātvalābhe kimiti vyāpārapadopādānamata āha-vyāpārapadañceti tathāca vyāpriyate'neneti karaṇavyutpannena, bhāvavyutpannena vā vyāpārapadena phalānukūlayāvatāmadhiśrayaṇādīnāṃ bhāvanātvalābhāya tadupādānam| kriyāpadena tu kṛtipadavadyatnamātrārthakatvasyāpi sambhavena tanmātrasyaiva tadvivaraṇena bhāvanātvalābhaḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ|
ata eveti tattadrūpeṇa phūtkāravādīnāṃ dhātuvācyatvādevetyarthaḥ| sarvasiddha iti| ata eva pacatītyukte phūtkārādimānna veti sandehānudayāditi bhāvaḥ| yadrityāṃ naiyāyikairvyāpāratvasyākhyātavācyatāvacchedakatvaṃ nirasya kṛtitvasya tad‌vyavasthāpitaṃ tadrītyaiva dhātoḥ kṛtitvamātraṃ śakyatā'vacchedakaṃ bhavivyatīti taṭasthā''śaṅkāṃ nirākaroti nacaiva meṣāmityādi `naca' iti sādhvītyanenānvitam|
eṣām adhiśrayaṇatvādīnām| gauraveti dhātuvācyatvāvacchedakatve gauravāpattyetyarthaḥ| teṣāṃ kṛtitvāpekṣayā gurutvānnānātvācceti bhāvaḥ|
tadavacchedakamiti dhātuvācyatā'vacchedakamityarthaḥ| nanu kṛtereva dhātvarthatve rathādāvacenate tad‌bādhāttādṛśaprayogānupapattirata āha ratho gacchatītyādi vyāpārādityādinā''śrayatva-pratiyogitvaparigrahaḥ| jānātītyādāvityādipadagrāhye ghaṭo naśyatītyādau nāśāśrayatvasya bādhātpratiyogitve eva lakṣaṇāyā draṣṭavyatvāt|
naiyāyikarītiriti ākhyātārthavicāre hi tairvyāpāre lakṣaṇām, kṛtau ca śaktiṃ svīkṛtyā'yaṃ prayoṃgaḥ samarthirastadvanmayā'pi samarthanīya iti sādṛśyāt rītipadopādānam, na tvanyāṃ'śe tanmatādara iti bhāvaḥ| lāghavopaṣṭhabdhamānādevā'rthasiddha rna kevalāllāghavādityāśayena mīmāṃsakarītyā tadrīterevā'sādhutvamāviṣkaroti śakyatā'vacchedakatvasyeti tathāca asati bādhake śaktirguruṇā'pi dharmeṇāvacchidyate, kṛtitvāllakṣaṇāvadityanumānātteṣu śaktiḥ setsyatīti bhāvaḥ|
nanu teṣāṃ kṛtītvā'pekṣayā gurutvameva bādhakamata āha tayoriti śakyatāvacchedakatva lakṣyatāvacchedakatvayorityarthaḥ| bījābhāvāditi sambhavati samaniyate laghau dharme gurau tadabhāvādityasya prakṛte'navatāreṇa yadyavacchedakatvaṃ svarūpasambandhaviśeṣastadā tatsvarūpatvasya gurūṇāmapi sattvātkimanupapannam| athātiriktaṃ tadā gurudharme'pi tatsvīkāra āvaśyakaḥ| gaṅgātīratvaṃ lakṣyatā'vacchedakamiti vyavahārāt, vṛttyavacchedakakoṭipraviṣṭatvasyobhayatra samyācca| tatra nyūnā'tiriktavṛttitvarūpā'vacchedakatvamādāya tādṛśavyavahāro yadi, tadā sarvatraiva tathāstviti bhāvaḥ|
nanu lāghavādeva na tasyā'vacchedakatvaṃ vadāmaḥ, kintu mānādapītyāśaṅkate naceti yatnārthakakarotinetyuktayā vyāpārajanyatvapratisandhānā'viśeṣe'pi ghaṭāṅkurayoḥ kṛtā'kṛtavyavahārātkṛño yatnārthakatvaṃ siddhameveti dhvanayati vivaraṇāditi tadarthapratipādakapadakathanarūpādityarthaḥ| dhātumātraṃ yatnatvaviśiṣṭe śaktam, bādhakaṃ vinā yatnatvaviśiṣṭā'rthakakarotipratipāditā'rthakatvāt, yadyadviśiṣṭabodhakapadapratipāditārthakaṃ bhavati tattatra śaktam| ghaṭatvaviśiṣṭaśaktaghaṭapadapratipādatārthakakalaśādipadavaditi sāmānyato dṛṣṭā'numānavidhayā śaktigrāhakatvādvivaraṇasyeti bhāvaḥ| yatna evārtha iti pāṭhaḥ| yatna eva ākhyātārtha iti pāṭhastu prakṛtasandarbhaviruddhaḥ| arthaḥ dhātvarthaḥ| evakāreṇa vyāpārasya vācyatvavyavacchedaḥ|
kṛño yatnārthakatve eva dhātumātrasya tadarthakatvasambhāvanā| tadeva gaganakusumopamānamityāha ratho gamanaṃ karotītyādi rathādāvacetane yatnasya bādhena tādṛśaprayogā'nupapattyā na kṛño yatnārthakatvam apinu vyāpārārthakatvameveti, tena vivaraṇāddhātorvyāpārārthakatvameva sivdyatīti bhāvaḥ|
nanu ratho gacchatīti prayogasthadhātvarthapratipādakakṛño vyāpāre lākṣaṇikatvānna tenaṃ yatnārthakatvakṣatirata āha bījādineti tathāca kṛño vyāpāre prayogaprācuryyeṇa tatraiva śaktirnyāyyeti tena vivaraṇāddhātorvyāpārārthakatvamavaśyamaṅgīkaraṇīyamiti bhāvaḥ|
atredamavadheyam-vaiyākaraṇamate'pi pacatyādeḥ phūtkārādivyāpāre vyāpāratvenaiva śaktiḥ, vyāpārasāmānyārthakakṛñā vivaraṇāt| evañca vyāpāratvena kṛterapi saṃgrahānmatto bhūtaṃ natu `mayā kṛtam' iti tatra tatroktaṃ saṅgacchate| tattadrūpeṇa kvacid bodhastu pacatyādīnāṃ tattadrūpāvacchinne lakṣaṇayopapādyaḥ|
śakyādanyena rūpeṇa jñāne bhavati lakṣaṇā| ityabhiyuktoktoḥ|
ata eva vākyārthapradarśanā'vasare viklittyanukūlo vyāpāraḥ, jñānā'nukūlo vyāpāra iti vyāpārasāmānyārthakavyāpārapadena sotkīrttitasya saṅgatiriti| `kṛño'karmakatāpatteḥ' iti mūlamavatārayati kiñceti| avācyatve dhātvaśakyatve| dvitīyā syāditi dhātvarthotpattirūpaphalā''śrayatvāt|
nanu ghaṭo bhavatītyatra kṛtyāśrayatvarūpakarttṛtvabodhe'pi dhātvarthaphalā'nvitā''khyātārthavyāpārāśrayatvarūpakartṛtvasattvāttena karmatvasyā'vaśyaṃ bādhena noktasthale dvitīyāprasaktiḥ, anyathā karttari lakāro'pyanākaratāmāpadyetetyāśaṅkaya nirākurute naceti| ghaṭasyeti ghaṭaṃ bhāvayatītyatreva, ghaṭo bhavatītyatrā'pi dvitīyā''pattiḥ| manmate tu dhātūpāttavyāpārā''śrayatvena parayā karttṛsaṃjñāyā bādhānna tadāpattiriti bhāvaḥ|
