nirākaraṇaprakāratāmāha anugateti cetanā'cetanasādhāraṇetyarthaḥ| tvanmate bhavadīyamate| durvacatveneti vaktumaśakyatvenetyarthaḥ| tadevā''ha kṛtyāśrayatvasyeti tasyā'cetane'vyāptatvāditi bhāvaḥ| kāracakraprayoktṛtvasya veti taddhātvarthoyanikhilakārakapravarttakatvarūpasya cetyarthaḥ| naiyāyikamata nirasya mīmāṃsakaṃ pratyāha dhātvartheti| kārakamātreti mātrapadaṃ kṛtsnā'rthakam| sarvasyā'pi kārakasya dhātvarthā'nukūlayatkiñcit‌kriyāśrayatvāt| kriyājanakatvasyaiva kārakatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
nanvacetane kṛtyāśrayatvarūpakartṛtvasya bādhe'pītaravyāpārā'nadhīnatvaprakārakavivakṣāviṣayavyāpārāśrayatvarūpakārakacakraprayoktṛtvarūpameva karttṛsaṃjñāniyāmakam| vyāpāraśca kvacit kṛtiḥ, kvacit, saṃyogādireva| tādṛśavyāpārāśraya eva `svatantraḥ karttā' iti sūtre svatantrapadārthaḥ, tatsūtrapraṇayanasāmarthyāt| taduktam-`kārake' iti sūtre bhāṣye-pradhānena samavāye sthālī paratantrā vyāvāye svatantrā| kiṃ punaḥ pradhānam? karttā| kathaṃ jñāyate karttā pradhānamiti? yatsarveṣu kārakeṣu sannihiteṣu karttā praparttayiti bhavati' iti| sthālī kāṣṭhauḥ pacatītyādau sthālyeva tattvena vivakṣitā iti tadbhāvaḥ| vakṣyate cā'dhikamupariṣṭāt|
mīmāṃsakamate prasaktadūṣaṇod‌dhṛtirapi vakṣyamāṇaiva| evañca ghaṭagatatādṛśavyāpārasyaiva svātantryavivakṣayā kartṛtvasya nirābādhena na dvitīyāprasaṅgo'ta āha
apiceti
svarthaphaloti śakyatāvacchedakakoṭhyapraviṣṭā''śrayakatvena phalasya viśeṣaṇānna jīvatyādāvatiprasaṅgaḥ| evamavivakṣitakarmakatvā'bhāvavattvenā'pi svārtho viśeṣaṇīyaḥ, nā'to'vivakṣitakarmatayā'bhyupagatabhāvalakāraprakṛtismṛdhātau saḥ vaiyadhikaraṇyaṃ tvagre vivecayiṣyate|
asambhavīti tathāya vyāpāravācakatvaṃ dhātorāvaśyakamiti bhāvaḥ|
anyatamatvamiti pacyādidhātūn śrṛṅgagrāhikayopādāya tāvadbhinnabhinnatvamityartha tattvamiti anugataṃ sakarmakatvamityarthaḥ| darśanāditi yathā jidhātorabhibhavārthe'karmakatvasya, nyūnīkaraṇārtha sakarmakatvasyābhyupagamātsakarmakadhātulakṣaṇe tatpraveśe'karmake tasminnativyāptirapraveśe ca nyūnīkaraṇe'rthe tatraivā'vyāptirityarthaḥ|
yadyapi tattadarthaviśiṣṭānevoktarītyopādāya sakarmakatvanirvacane doṣābhāvastathāpi `bahulametannidarśanam' iti smaraṇāttatra tatrāvṛttikaraṇācca tāvaddhātūnāṃ yugapad grahaṇā'sambhave tāttparyamiti bodhyam| tadetaditi pūrvoktadūṣaṇagaṇamityarthaḥ|
nanu kṛño'karmakatāpatterityasya kṛño yatnārthakatvanirāsapratipādakasya kathamuktārthā'bhisandhāyakatvamityāśaṅkayā'bhisandhānaprakāraṃ viśadayati ayambhāva iti| phalamātrāmiti mātrapadena vyāpāravyāvacchedaḥ, karotītyādau ityādinā saviṣayārthadhātusaṃgrahaḥ|
tanmātramiti yatnādirūpaviṣayimātramityarthaḥ akarmakatāpattiriti vyāpārasya dhātvavācyatve uktalakṣaṇā''krāntasyeti bhāvaḥ| kecittu, akarmakatvāpattiriti viṣayyarthakakṛñādyabhiprāyeṇaiva teṣāmeva prakāntatvāt, evaṃ ca na tadaprasiddhirityāhuḥ| uktarītyeti| uktaprāyarītyā-sakarmakadhātubhinnadhātutvamityākārikayetyarthaḥ, yathāśrute dhātorvyāpārā'vācakatve svārthaphalasamānādhikaraṇavyāpāravācakatvarūpā'karmakatvāpādanasya'sambhavaduktikatvāpatteḥ|
nanvidānīṃ dhātumātrasyaiva vyāpāravācakatvasyā'siddhitvāt sakarmakarūpapratiyogya'prasiddhyā bhavaduktārthāpādanasyā'pyasambhavaduktikatvaṃ samānam| anyatamatvarūpasya tu svayameva nirākṛtatvāduktapadena tad‌grahaṇā'sambhavāditi cenna, nayatyādidvikarmmakāṇāṃ saṃyogā'dyanukūlā'jādiniṣṭhakriyātmakaphalārthakatvasya dhātoḥ phalamatrārthakatvavādinā'pyabhyupagamādajādiniṣṭhakriyāyāḥ saṃyogarūpaphalajanakatvāllokaprasiddheśca vyāpāratvāttadarthakadhātūn ṇyantādidhātūn vopādāyaiva kathañcitprasiddhisambhavāditi noktavyākhyāne doṣaḥ|
nanvetāvatā jñākṛñādīnāṃ vyāpārthakatvaṃ siddham, na dhātumātrasyetyataḥ kṛñ‌padaṃ dhātumātraparatayā vyācaṣṭe-kṛñaiti| dhātumātropalakṣaṇamiti kṛñ‌padaṃ śakyalakṣyasādhāraṇadhātutvenā'jahatsvārthalakṣaṇayā dhātumātrabodhakam| tathāca dhātumātrasyā'karmakatāpattyā na phalamātrārthakatā, kintu vyāpārā'rthakatā'pītyarthaḥ| akarmakateti pūrvoktabhinnasarvadhātūnāṃ setyarthaḥ. yadi phalavācakatvameva sakarmakatvaṃ, tadā sarveṣāmaviśeṣṇa sakarmakataiva syādityāha sakarmakateti
vastutastu, uktayuktayā dhātorvyāpārārthakatvasiddhistadaiva syād yadi svārathaphaletyādisakarmakalakṣaṇaṃ sakarmakadhānumātrasādhāraṇaṃ syāt, tadeva na, adhyādyupasṛṣṭaśīṅādiṣva'vyāpteḥ| tasmād bhvādīnāṃ dhātutvā'nāpattiḥ| maitreṇa pācayatītyādau maitrādīnāṃ karttṛtvā'nupapattiśca tathā| ata eva `nivṛttapreṣaṇāddhātoḥ prākṛte'rthe ṇijiṣyate' ityādyupapattiriti dik‌|
nanu, `kṛño'karmakatāpatteḥ' iti grantho na mīmāṃsakamatanirākaraṇaparaḥ| tasya, tasmāt karotirityādinā nirākariṣyamāṇatvāt, taiḥ kṛñā yatnārthakatvā'nabhyupagamācca| nāpi naiyāyikamatanirāsaparaḥ, naiyāyikaiḥ phalavyāpārayorubhayorapi dhātuvācyatvā'bhyupagamāt| taduktamākhyātavādadīdhitau `stāṃ vā phalavyāpārau pṛthageva dhātvathau' iti| dhātoḥ phalā'bacchinna vyāpārārthakatve karmākhyāte phalasya dvedhā bhānāpattyā tayorviśakalitayoreva śaktirjyāyasīti tadbhāvaḥ| phalasya dhātuvāccattve paraṃ keṣāñcinnyāyavidāṃ vipratipattiḥ, karmākhyātena dvitīyayā ca tad‌bhānasambhavāt| kiñcaitad‌granthasya dhātumātrasya vyāpārā'rthakatvasādhakatve dhātorakarmatāpatteḥ, `phalamātrā'rthatā na hi ' ityeva brūyānna tu kṛña ityaparitoṣādanyārthaparatayā mūlamavatārayati athaveti|
prasādhyeti pūrvā'rddhasya vivaraṇaparatayā vyākhyānaditi bhāvaḥ| taditi dhātuvācyatvamityarthaḥ| jānātikarotyāderiti nirdiṣṭayatnārthakabhinnasaviṣayārthakadhātumātropalakṣaṇam| kriyāmātreti mātrapadena phalavyāvacchedaḥ| sakarmakāṇāṃ prāyaśaḥ phalāvacchinnavyāpāravācakatvam, viśakalitaphalavyāpārobhayā'rthakatvaṃ vā, jānātyādīnāṃ tu jñānādivyāpārārthakatvameva| akarmakāṇāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ na phalārthakatvam, tasya tato'nanubhavāt, kintu vyāpārā'rthakatvameveti prācīnanaiyāyikābhyupagamāt| vakṣyamāṇarītyā kṛñaḥ phalārthakatve tu tulyanyāyāt sarveṣāmeva tatsiddhiriti bhāvaḥ|
nanu kṛñaḥ phalāvācakatve kathamakarmakatvamata āha ayaṃ bhāva iti| vācakatvādītyādinā svārthavyāpāravyādhikaraṇaphalavācakatvasya naiyāyikanaye phalā'vacchinnavyāpārā'rthakatvasyā'pi sakarmakatvāttasya ca saṅgahaḥ| jānātyādīnāmeva phalārthakatvānabhyupagama ucchedapadārtho'vyāptiḥ, sarveṣāmeva tadanabhyupagame tvasambhavaḥ| tathāca kṛñadhātuḥ phalavācakaḥ, sakarmakatvāt, pacyādivadityanumāne vipakṣabādhakatarkapradarśakaṃ kṛño'karmaketyādīti bodhyam|
vastutastu, vyāpāro bhāvanā saiveti pūrvā'rddhameva vivaraṇapradarśanena dhātoḥ phalavyāpārobhayārthatvasādhakam| tathāhidhātorvyāpārārthakatve'siddhe bhāvanādipadairvyāpārapadavivaraṇamātrānna tasya tāvatsiddhiriti sphuṭameva| ataḥ pacati karoti, pākabhāvanā, tādṛśyutpādanā, ityādivivaraṇaparataiva tasya vācyā| vyākhyātaṃ ca tathaiva sārakṛtā| tathāca vyāpārārthakakṛñeva viklittyādyarthakapākādipadenā'pi vivaraṇādubhayorapi dhātvarthatvasiddhiḥ tatra jānātyādau phalāṃśe vipratipannaṃ naiyāyikaṃ pratyuktārthāpaṣṭambhakatayā kṛño'karmakatetyādyuktam‌| dhātumātre vyāpārā'vācakatvaṃ vadantaṃ mīmāṃsakaṃ prati tu tadupapādakatayaivatasmātkarotirityādi|
yadyapi vyāpārāṃśe vipratipannaṃ pratyapi kṛña ityādyupaṣṭambhakam, tathāpi vivaraṇasyaivā''khyātaparatayā sakarmakatvasya vyāpārthakatvaṃ vinā''pyanyathāsiddhāvudbhāvitāyāṃ tad‌granthasyā''vaśyakatvāditi|
navyanaiyāyikāstu-sakarmakadhātūnāṃ phale tajjanakatvena vivakṣitavyāpāreṣu copalakṣaṇībhūtasādhanā''kāṅkṣottthāpākatā'vacchedakadharmasamānā'dhikaraṇadharmatvā'nugatīkṛtatattaddharmā'vacchinneṣu śaktiḥ, sambhavati phalajanakatayā vivakṣitānāṃ teṣāṃ vibhinnadharmavattvam| yatra tvekadharmā'vacchinnasyaiva phalā'nukūlatvaṃ vivakṣāviṣayastatra tadavacchinna eva dhātoḥ sā| nahi sarvatra samudāya eva śaktiriti rājā''jñā'sti|
`kriyā nāmeyam' ityādi bhāṣyaṃ tu bāhukālasādhyoktadhātvarthaviṣayamiti na tad‌vyākopaḥ| anyathā sarvatrā'gatikagatyāropāśrayaṇāpatteḥ| teṣāmeva ca vyāpārapadavyāpadeśyatvam| kṛtitvasya tādṛśadharmasamānādhikaraṇadharmatve'pi na tasya 3 pacyādyarthatā'vacchedakatvam, tadaṃśe tasyopalakṣaṇatvā'naṅgīkārāt| ata eva vyutpannānāṃ na kadācidapi tattātparyeṇa yāgapākādipadaprayogastathā prayuktādapi tasmātpākādikṛtibodhaśca| etena tasyā api vyāpāratayā dhātoreva lābhānna tiṅstatra śaktiriti nirākṛtam|
jānātyādīnāṃ tu viṣayatvaṃ phalaṃ tannirvāhakajñānādivyāpāraśca śakyam| kṛñastu sādhyatvam, yatnaścā'rthaḥ| tena taddhātvarthaphalasambandhitvarūpaṃ mukhyakarmatvaṃ ghaṭādīnāṃ nirābādham| evaṃ svārthaphalavyadhikaraṇatvarūpaṃ sakarmakatvamapi| yatnādyakarmakāṇāṃ tu tattaddharmāvacchinnayatnādirūpavyāpāra eva sā| bhojanaṃ yatate' ityaprayogeṇa tadviṣayatvasya dhātuśakyatvā'nupagamāt| `yatate' ityukte karmā'kāṅkṣāyā abhāvācca|
adhyādyupasṛṣṭaśīṅādīnāṃ tu phale nirūḍhalakṣaṇaiva, śīṅāditaḥ sarvatra phalābodhāt| tadarthe'nāditātparyyagrāhakatayaiva ca "adiśīṅsthāsām"  ityādisūtropayogaḥ| karttetyatra tu kṛño yatnabodhakatvādeva nā'cetane svārasataḥ karttṛpadaprayogaḥ|
evañca karttṛbodhanirvāhā'rthaṃ pacatītyādāvākhyātasya kṛtau śaktirāvaśyakī| tadarthaprayatnasyā''śrayayibhāvena prathamāntārthe'nvayāt tadvodhopapatteḥ| yatra tu na tadvodhasiddhi ratho gacchatītyādau tatra vyāpāre, āśrayatvādau vā yathāyathaṃ lakṣaṇā| `svatantraḥ karttā'  itisūtrasāmarthyena, dhātvarthavyāpārā'śrayatatpratiyogināmapi karttṛtvasya paribhāṣaṇānna karttṛbodhā'nupapattiḥ| yatra prathamāntasamabhivyāhāraḥ, yatra tu kṛtyādiviśeṣyaka eva tātparyaṃ tatra tadviśeṣyako'pi bodhaḥ| anabhihita ityasyāpyabodhita ityevā'rthaḥ| abodhanaṃ tvāśrayā'tiriktarthe viśeṣaṇatayā kṛtyādyarthaṃprabodhanameveti nā'bhidhānā'nabhidhānavyāvasthā'nupapattiḥ| caitraḥ pacatītyādau karttṛbodhasya kṛtiśaktayaiva nirvāhe tatrā'khyātaśaktikalpanā'naucityācca| vyāpāravyāpadeśābhāvena viklittyādīnāmuktadharmatvasyopalakṣaṇatvā'nupagamāt, pṛthak‌śaktiradṛṣṭaiva| phalatvaṃ tu pūrvaṃ nirupitameva| evaṃ sati kvacid dūṣaṇaṃ prasaktamavagaṇayiṣyate ityāhuḥ| evaṃ sakarmakatvādinirvacanā'nupapattyā dhātorubhayārthakatvamavaśyakamiti paryavasitam|
vyāpāramātraśaktivādinastu-dvitīyādyarthānvayyarthakatvaṃ sakarmakatvam| pacyādisamabhivyāhāre dvitīyāyā viklityādyarthakatvāt| jānātyādisamabhivyāhāre ca viṣayatvā'dyarthakatvāt, tadanvayitvaṃ tadarthe'kṣatamityāhuḥ| jānātyādiyoge ghaṭādīnāṃ gauṇakarmatvamiti vadatāṃ matamāśaṅkaya nirācaṣṭe naceti| bhākta iti bhajyate-āmṛdyate sevyate vā śakyārtho'nayeti bhaktirlakṣaṇā, tayā, śakyā'rthasya tirodhānāt, svajñāne ghaṭakatayā śakyārthasyā'pekṣaṇacca| tata āgato bhākta ityarthaḥ|
bhāktatve'pi| lakṣaṇānibandhanatve'pi| karmaṇīti| mukhyasakarmatvamādāya śāstrasya cāritārthye gauṇe tad‌vyāpāre mānā'bhāvāditi bhāvaḥ|
tatra laukikaṃ dṛṣṭāntamāha-nahītyādi bhāktarājapadavyavahāryyeṇa purohitādinā tadīyakāryapravarttanānmahadbhūtaścandramā ityādāvātvavāraṇāyā''śritagauṇamukhyanyāyasya viśiṣyārthopasthāpakaviśiṣṭarūpopādānapurassarapadakārthaviṣayakatvasyā'nyatra vyāvasthāpanācca nā'tra tadviṣayatetyanye|
paretvetacchāstrīyakarmasaṃjñākārthānvayyarthakatvameva sakarmakatvam, sakarmakapadāt karmaṇā sahetyarthakācchabdamaryādayā pūrvoktārthālābhācca `laḥ karmaṇi' ityādau sakarmakapadād bodhā'nāpatteḥ| tadananvayyarthakatvamakarmakatvam| ata eva''dhyāsitā bhūmaya ityādau karmaṇi ktopapattiḥ| anvayasya pṛthagupasthitasaṃsargarūpatayā na jīvatyādiṣu doṣa ityāhuḥ|
ayameva pakṣaḥ phalāvacchinnavyāpārabodhakatvaṃ sakarmakatvamiti vadadbhirāśritaḥ, phalasya karmatāyāḥ sarvairevopagamāt, phalā'nvayyarthakatvasya sakarmakeṣu sattvāt| yadi caikopasthitiviṣayatvānnoktaḥ prakāraḥ sanbhavatīti vibhāvyate tadā pṛthak‌śaktivādimate nirduṣṭatvameva, avacchinnapadasya sambandhaparatvena karmobhūtaphalasambandhivyāpārā'rthakatvasya susthatvāt| akarmakāṇāṃ phalārthatvā'naṅgīkāreṇā'tivyāptyavyāptyoranavakośādityavadheyam| `adhyāsitā bhūmayaḥ' ityādau yathoktopapattistathopapāditaṃ prāk  nanu `karmavat syād yagadyapi' iti mūlasya karmavad yagādyapi bhavedityartho labhyate| sa ca bādhito'vivakṣitaścetyata āha-karmavatsyāditi padeneti| sūtraṃ lakṣyate iti| sūtravihitaṃ lakṣyate ityarthaḥ| yathāśrute karmapadāt svaghaṭitatvena lakṣaṇayā sūtropasthitāvapi tasya yagādikamityanenā'nanvayāpatteḥ| etad‌bodhanāyaiva syāditi lakṣyakoṭāvupātta ghaṭāvṛttitvāditi ghaṭamātravṛttitvā'bhāvena karmasthakriyakatvābhāvāditi tu paramārthaḥ  evamiti karmavadbhāvānurodhena karaṇā'rthakaroterutpattisahitayatnā'rthakatvā'bhyupagame ityarthaḥ| jānātyāderapītyādipadād gamyādiparigrahaḥ|| āvaraṇabhaṅgādītyādipadena tu saṃyogādeḥ saḥ| āvaśyakamityanena tatra jñānasyaiva phalatvāṅgīkāre tadanāśrayatvād ghaṭādeḥ karmatvānupapattirapi sūcyate| āvaraṇabhaṅgasyā'pyatītādau yathā phalatvaṃ tathā sāre eva subarthanirṇaye vakṣyate| sakarmakatā'nāpatteriti svārthaphalavyadhikaraṇetyādyuktarūpasya tatrā'sambhavāditi bhāvaḥ|
vastutastu jānāterjñānarūpaphalā'rthakatvameva yuktam| `naca kartṛsthabhāvakānāṃ karmaṇi kriyāyāḥ pravṛttirasti' iti bhāṣyeṇa kartṛsthabhāvakānāṃ kriyāphalasya karmamātre vṛttirnāstītyarthakena karttṛkarmobhayasādhāraṇaphalārthakatvaṃ karttṛsthakiyakatvaṃ vadatāṃ karmamātravṛttiphalārthakatvarūpakarmasthakriyakatvasyaivā'bhyupagamena tatrā''varaṇabhaṅgasya phalatve tatpakṣe karmavadbhāvasya durnivāryatāpatteḥ| ādṛtaścāyameva pakṣo jānātītyādau jñānecchādyanukūlo vyāpāra iti śābdabodhaprakāraṃ darśayatā granthakṛtā'pi| prakṛte āśaṅkāsamādhāne tu bhāṣyaharyyadyuktaprathamakalpā'bhiprāyeṇeti sāre eva sphuṭam|
nacoktakalpe jñānasya samavāyeva ghaṭādāvasattvāteṣāṃ jānātikarmatvānupapattiḥ, jñānasya tadanukūlavyāpārasyaśrayatādeśca sāmānādhikaraṇyādakarmakatvāttiḥ, kartṛsthabhāvakatvanupapattiśceti vācyam, śāstre jñānam ghaṭe jñānamiti pratītyā viṣayatayā tasya jñānāśrayatābhyupagamenādyadoṣasya phalatā''vacchedakasambandhaghaṭitasāmānādhikaraṇyaghaṭitā'karmatvasya tatrā'bhāvena dvitīyasya viṣayatā-samavāyābhyāṃ karmakartrubhayavṛttitvenā'tyadoṣasya vābhāvāt| adhikamagre vakṣyate|
prakṛtamanusarāmaḥ| kinna syāditi kriyate ghaṭaḥ svayamevetyatra ghaṭasyotpattirūpaphalāśrayatvavadāvaraṇabhaṅgarūpaphalāśrayatvasyāpi sattvāditi bhāvaḥ| nanu nirvarttya-vikāryakarmaṇoḥ karmavadbhāvo na prāpyakarmaṇītyatra kiṃ mānamata āha mūle-siddhānto'treti atra "karmavat karmaṇā tulyakriyāḥ" | iti sūtrabhāṣye| tatra hi dhātorekāca iti sūtrādanuvarttamānadhātugrahaṇena dhātuvācyakriyayā ityarthalābhena `sādhvasi śchinatti' `sthālī pacati' ityādau karaṇādhikaraṇābhyāṃ tulyakriye karttari vastutaḥ sato'pi vyāpārasya dhātuvcyatvā'bhāvādevā'natiprasaṅge karmagrahaṇamatiricyamānaṃ karmasthakriyāṃ lakṣayatīti siddhānto vyāvasthita ityarthaḥ| karmasthakiyakatvaṃ ca prāpyabhinnakarmaṇyeva sambhavatīti darśayitumādau vakṣyamāṇamapi vibhāgaṃ sūkhabodhāyā''ha- īpsitaṅkarmeti yadyapi dveṣyā''dyanīpsitasyā'pyeṣvevāntarbhāvāt karmma trividhamityevaṃ vaktuṃ yuktam| tathāpi hariṇā-
"tayorebhyastridhā matam| taccepsitatamam"-
ityādinā pārthakyena vibhajanāt tadanusṛtyā'trāpi tathaivoktamiti dhyeyam| prāpyatvañceti ādyayorlakṣaṇe tu subarthanirṇate vakṣyete| vakṣyata iti kriyākṛtaviśeṣāṇāmiti kārikayetyarthaḥ| karmagataviśeṣādhāyakakriyārthakatāvaṃ karmasthakriyayakatvaṃ karmavadbhāvaprayojakamiti darśayituṃ tadanvayavyatirekau darśayati-nahītyādi
somaṃ sunotīti vikāryya iti| nanvādyayoḥ karmasthakriyakatvena karmavadbhāvaviṣayatve'pi tatra kathaṃ somaṃ sunotītyasya vikāryyalakṣaṇākrāntatvam, prakṛteḥ somasya pariṇāmitvā'navagamāt| taduktam, `prakṛtestu vivakṣāyāṃ vikāryyam' itivivakṣāyāmityasya pariṇāmitvenetyādiriti ceditthaṃ cūrṇikaraṇarūpā'bhiṣavaikadeśacūrṇaṃ prati prakṛtisomasya pariṇāmitva vivakṣayā tadupapattiḥ|
anye tu-kriyā yaddharmanāśakaṃ phalaṃ janayati tadvikāryyamiti lakṣaṇā'bhiprāyeṇa some vikāryyanirddeśaḥ| tathāhi-ārambhakasaṃyoganāśakavibhāgānukūlā kriyā sunoterarthaḥ|somapadaṃ ca prakṛte tadavayave lākṣaṇikam| tathāca somāvayavarūpasomapadā'rthavṛttyārambhakasaṃyogasya dhātvarthakriyājanyavibhāgena nāśādvikāryyatvopapattirityāhuḥ|
jñātuṃ śakyamiti etena kriyākṛtaviśeṣadharmaprakārakapratītiyogyatvaṃ karmavadbhāvaprayojakamityuktaṃ bhavati| prācīnanaiyāyikamate tu prakṛte kṛña utpattirūpaphalāvacchinne yatne lakṣaṇayā karmavadbhāvopapattiḥ| noktātiprasaṅga iti na karmavadbhāvāpattirityarthaḥ|
prācīnaiyāyikamate tu jñānaṃ karotītyādyaprayogāt sādhyatve phale yatne ca kṛñaḥ śaktireva| tathāca ghaṭādau sādhyatvarūpaviśeṣadharmaprakārakapratīteḥ sārvajanīnatvāt, tādṛśapratītiyogyatāyāstatrā'napāyādanāyāsenaiva karmavadbhāvopapattiḥ| yadi ca kartṛgataviśeṣā''dhāyakakriyārthakatvaṃ tatsthakriyakatvamiti lakṣaṇe pacyādikarttari śramarūpaviśeṣadarśanāj‌jānātyādikarttari tadadarśanāccā'tivyāptyādidoṣāduktabhāṣyeṇa,
viśeṣadarśanaṃ yatra kriyā tatra vyavasthitā|
kriyāvyavasthā tvanyeṣāṃ śabdaireva prakalpitā||
iti harikārikottarārddhena ca pūrvoktalakṣaṇayoreva nirbharaḥ pratīyate ityucyate, tadā'pi na kṣatiḥ| karmasthakriyakalakṣaṇe jānātyādibhinnatvasya ṣopadeśalakṣaṇe ṣvaṣkādyanyatamatvasyeva kartṛsthakriyakalakṣaṇe jānātyādyanyatamatvasyāpi niveśena karotau karmasthakriyakatvasya jānātyādau kartṛsthakriyakatvasya copapateteḥ| ata eva hariṇā'pi, `śabdaireva prakalpitā' ityatra śabdapadamupāttam| śabdapratipādya'rthagataviśeṣaiḥ śabdaiśceti tadarthāditi| darśanāntarīyāṃ yuktimupanyasyati-dhātūnāmiti tyajigamyorityupalakṣaṇaṃ tādṛśadhātumātrasya| paryāyatāpattiriti tacchakyatā'vacchedakā'vacchinnaśaktatvasyaiva paryyāyatāpadārthatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
nanu tyajigamyoḥ krameṇa vibhāgasaṃyogopalakṣitavyāpārā'rthakatvamevañca paryyāyalakṣaṇe tadupalakṣaṇetarānupalakṣitatvasyā'pi viśeṣaṇānnoktā''pattirata āha-upalakṣaṇatve'pīti upalakṣaṇatvaṃ ca tatpadajanyabodhaviṣayatvena śaktayaviṣayatve sati śaktiviṣayatvamā| janakatvāditi| tathāca saṃyogavibhāgayoḥ svārthavyāpārajanyatvā'viśeṣād gamyarthavyāpāre saṃyoga evopalakṣaṇaṃ, no vibhāga ityatra vinigamakā'bhāvādubhayasyaivopalakṣaṇatve uktāpattirdurvāreti phale'pi śaktirāvaśyakīti bhāvaḥ| atra grāmaṃ gacchatītyādau dvitīyā'rthasaṃyogādirūpaphalā'nvayitvasyaiva vinigamakatvād gamanakriyāyāṃ saṃyogasyaivopalakṣaṇatvamityasya suvacatayā'nyatra lakṣaṇayopapatteśca noktarītiḥ sādhvītyasvaraso'pi vadantibhyāṃ sūcitaḥ| upasaṃharati-tasmāditi uktayuktisaṅgaterityarthaḥ|
nirvivādameveti svārthaphalasamānā'dhikaraṇavyāpāravācakatvarūpā'karmakatvasya dhātoḥ phalavācakatvaṃ vinā'nupapannatvāditi bhāvaḥ| nanu sakarmakatvaṃ pūrvoktameva, tadbhinnadhātutvaṃ cā'karmakatvamiti noktā'nupapattirata āha- bhū sattāyāmiti gaṇeṣu phalasyaiva nirddeśāditi bhāvaḥ| ata eveti dhātumātrasya palavācakatvādevetyarthaḥ| dvyartha iti dvyarthapadaṃ phalavyāpārobhayārthakamityāśayaḥ| nanu `dvyarthaḥ paciḥ' iti bhāṣyaṃ pacerupādānātsakarmakāṇāṃ phalārthakatve sādhakamastu, na tvakarmakāṇāmata āha-digiti tathā sati sattādyarthanirddeśavaiyarthyā''patteḥ| dhātusāmānyaśaktyā pacyāderiva bhavatyāderapi vyāpārabodhasambhavāditi digartho'vaseyaḥ  nanu `vyāpāro bhāvanā saiva' ityuktavivaraṇena na vyāpārasya dhātvarthatānirṇayaḥ, pacati-pākaṃ karotīti vivaraṇena ākhyātārthatāyā api vaktuṃ śakyatvāditi mīmāṃsakāśaṅkāsamādhānaparatayā mūlamavatārayati-evamityādinā| vācyatvamiti tiṅantajanyabodhaviṣayatvamityarthaḥ| tathāca pākā'nukūlā bhāvanetyādivivaraṇenā'pi pacatītyasya pākā'nukūlabhāvanābodhakatvamastvityarthaḥ| yathāśrute vivaraṇena dhātorbhāvanāvācakatvanirṇaye `kintvākhyātavācyaiva sā' ityuttaragranthāsaṅgateḥ| `prakṛtipratyayau' iti nyāyasya saṃkhyādau vyabhicāramāśaṅkyāha-tadāgame hīti
idaṃ ca yaḥ pradhānaṃ sa pratyayārtha iti nyāyā'kāra ityabhipretya tanmūlasūtrālocane tu yaḥ pratyayārthaḥ sa pradhānamiti nyāyākāro labhyate| tathāhi-`paśunā yajeta' iti śrūyate, tatraikavacanopāttamekatvaṃ yāge viśeṣaṇamuta prakṛtyarthe iti saṃśaye paśupadārthasya yāgaṃ prati guṇatvāt sambhāvitayāvatpaśukaraṇena pradhānāvṛttau pradhānabhaṅgāpattyā yāvad‌guṇapradhānā''vṛtterayogādekatvaviśiṣṭapaśorvidhāne tvekatvasya paśuviśeṣaṇatayā yāgāṅgatvā'bhāvāttadavivakṣitameva syādityarthakena "tatraikatvamayāgāṅgamarthasya guṇabhūtatvāt" iti sūtreṇa pūrvapakṣite, "śabdavattūpalabhyate tadāgame hi dṛśyate tasya jñānaṃ yathā'nyeṣām" iti siddhāntasūtram| śabdavat-śabdavācyam, yathā bhavati tathopalabhyate| ekatvaṃ hi yatastadāgame-ekavacanasamavadhāne dṛśyate| atastasya prādhānyena pratītiryathānyeṣāmaruṇādīnāmityarthaḥ| yadvā `tadāgame' tat-ekatvam, āgame-ekavacanavidhāyakāgame `dhdyekayoḥ'ityatra dṛśyate ekavacanarūpapratyayārthatvena pratīyate, anyeṣām-karaṇādīnāmivetyarthaḥ|
evañca samānapratyayopāttatvapratyāsattyā vibhaktyarthakaraṇā'nvitasyā'ruṇā'dhikaraṇanyāyena prathamaṃ kriyāyāmanvayaḥ, paścāt prakṛtyarthe| tathāca paśvekatvobhayakaraṇako yāga iti bodhenaikatvasya yāgāṅgatvā'vagamānnānekapaśubhiryāga iti tadbhāvaḥ| prādhānyaṃ ca prakṛtyarthā'viśeṣaṇatvameva, pratyayārthamādāya nyāyāvatārādyaḥ pratyayārtha iti bhāgasya nyāyaghaṭakatvam| prakṛte ākhyātā'samavadhāne bhāvanā'bodhāt tasyāprādhānyena bhānamiti tatsaṅgatiriti bodhyam|
nanu sakarmakatvā'nugatakartṛtvayordurvacatvamevaitanmate dūṣaṇamata āha evañceti bhāvanāyā ākhyātavācyatve cetyarthaḥ| tathācā''rabdhe pāke phalā'nutpattidaśāyāṃ pāko jāto naveti praśne, bhaviṣyatītyuttarā'nurodhāt phalamātraṃ dhātuvācyamiti mīmāṃsakāśayaḥ|
prayogāpattiriti bhāvalyuḍantagamanapadārthasaṃyogātmakaphalasya grāme sattvād grāmaḥ saṃyogavānitivad grāmo gamanavāniti prayogasya prāmāṇyāpattirityarthaḥ| evamagre'pyūhyam| phalā'nutpādeti phalānutpāde tādṛśaprayogasya sarvānanumatattvāduktam vyāpārasattva iti| bhavatītyanāpattiriti pākapadārthasya viklitteravarttamānatvādityarthaḥ| phalotpādadaśāyāmarthā'bādhāttādṛśaprayogasyeṣṭatvādāha  vidyata ityāpattiśceti vyāpāraṃśaparityāgena phalamātrasya dhātvarthatvā'bhiprāyeṇa tu bhaviṣyatītyuttaraṃ pākamutpādayatīti vivaraṇañceti bhāvaḥ|
anukūleti anukūlatvotkīrttanaṃ tvanvitā'bhadhānavādināṃ matametaditi sūcayitum| nānupapattiriti bhāvavihitalyuṅghañādyarthavyāpārasya grāmādau bādhānna pūrvoktaprayogāpattirityarthaḥ| viklityanukūlaghañādyarthavyāpārasya karttṛtayā bhāvanādāvanvayānna pūrvoktaprayogā'nupapattyāpattiriti bhāvaḥ|
nanu karttari kṛdīti vihitasya kṛtaḥ paktā pācaka ityādau kartrarthatvadarśanāttadvihitaghañādīnāṃ vyāpārā'rthatvadaurlabhyāttadarthatvabodhanāya `bhāve' ityanuśāsanaṃ cāritārthyamityāśaṅkāmapākariṣyam̐stadanuguṇaṃ dṛṣṭāntamāha kartrākhyātavaditi| tathāca laḥ karmaṇīti vihitakartrākhyātasya tvanmate vyāpārā'rthakatvavat, karttari kṛdīti vihitaghañādīnāmapi vyāpārā'rthakatvasambhavādityarthaḥ|
virodhā''patteśceti co heto, yato vaiyarthyamatastadvirodhāpattirityarthaḥ| uktivaicitryametat| yadvā bhāva vihitapratyayānāṃ dhātvarthānuvādakatvāt prayogasādhutāmātrārthakatvamiti tvadīyasiddhāntavirodhāpatterityarthaḥ| vyākhyānamityasya vivaraṇam-vivaraṇamiti|
atastadapīti pacatītyasya pākaṃ karototi vivaraṇamapītyarthaḥ| apinā duṣayiṣyamāṇaprakṛtipratyayāviti nyāyasamuccayaḥ| dhātoḥ pṛthagvivaraṇaṃ tu tavā''khyātārthasaṃkhyākālavyāpārāṇāṃ madhye vyāpārasya kṛñā saṃkhyādestaduttaratiṅā vivaraṇavaddhātvarthavyāpārasyā'pyupapannamiti na tadākhyātārthatvasādhakam| ata eva "kathaṃ jñāyate kriyāvacanāḥ pacyādayaḥ yadeṣāṃ karotinā sāmānādhikaraṇyam, kiṃ karoti? pacati" iti bhūvādisūtrabhāṣye uktama| tena vivaraṇamapi dhātoreveti spaṣṭameva bodhyate iti bhāvaḥ| pakvavāniti mūlamapi karoterākhyātavivaraṇaṃtvā'bhāvasādhyatayā vyācaṣce pakvavānityādāvitī| ananvayameva viśadayati ayambhāva ityādi|
sahārthamiti sambaddhārthamityarthaḥ| brūtaḥ bodhayataḥ, tayoḥ prakṛtipratyayā'rthayormadhye, pratyayā'rthaḥ pratyayavṛttigrahaviśeṣyaḥ, pradhānamiti prakṛtyarthaprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣyatāśālītyartha-. tādṛśavyutpattisiddhakāryyakāraṇabhāvamabhinayena darśayati viśeṣyatayeti pratyayasya prakṛtyarthaprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣyatāsambandhena śābdabodhaṃ pratītyarthaḥ| heturityasya viśeṣyatāsambandheneti śeṣaḥ|
aruṇādhikaraṇoktarītyeti aruṇayeti padopāttā''ruṇyasya prathamaṃ karaṇatvenetarakriyāyāmanvayaḥ, nāmārthā'nvayastu pārṣṭhika iti tatroktam| vakṣyamāṇeti kārakatvasya kriyājanakatvarūpatvena kriyājanakamiti jñāte, kā hā kārakā'nvayitā'vacchedakamiti subarthanirṇaye vakṣyamāṇarityetyarthaḥ|
sambandhamātramiti "jyotiṣṭomena yajena" ityatra yajeteti śrutyā dhātvākhyātārthayāgabhāvanayoḥ sambandhasāmānyaṃ pratipādyate| tayoḥ kriyākārakabhāvarūpasambandhiviśeṣabodhane tu nā'syāḥ śruteḥ kaścidabhidhādirūpo vyāpāraḥ, tadvācakapadā'bhāvāditi tadarthaḥ| tathāca yajetetyatra yāsambandhibhāvaneti bodhaḥ, na yāgakaraṇakabhāvaneti, tadvatprakṛte pākasambandhī kartteti bodhaḥ| karmatvādinā tatsamasargakabodhe eva kriyāyā apekṣaṇādityāśaṅkiturāśyaḥ. phalasya kārakatvenā'yogyatvāttādṛśā'nvayabodho'pyasambhavaduktika ityāha yogyatāvirahāditi|
nanu tatpādārthe tatpādārthavattvaṃ yogyatā, ca sā prakṛte, pākaprayojakatvavatpākasambandhitvarūpā'karturata āha anvayaprayojakarūpavattvasyeti tathāca tatpādarthe tatpādārthatā'vacchedakāvacchinnatvasyaiva yogyatve jalāharaṇe ghaṭakaraṇatvasya sattvāttasmin suṣiraghaṭasyāpi karaṇtevan'nvayāpattiḥ| asmanmate tu sacchidretaraghaṭatvasya jalā''haraṇānvayaprayojakatvena tādṛśaghaṭe tādṛśā'haraṇānvayaprayojakatvamiti na tadanvayāpattiriti bhāvaḥ|
nanu `ghaṭena jalamāhara' ityatra ghaṭe chidretaraghaṭatvavāt prakṛte kiṃ kārakānvayitāvacchedakaṃ yadabhāvādananvayāpattirudbhāvyate'ta āha kriyātvameva hīti|
nanu kṛdarthakartrākṣiptabhāvanāyāṃ pākādīnā karmatvenānvayasaulabhyānnodūṣaṇamata āha vastutastviti pradhānapratyayārthavacanamiti sūtrasvaraso yaḥ prādhānaṃ sa pratyayārthaḥ| tadāgame dīti nyāyaśca, yaḥ pratyayā'rtha ityarthe mūlamiti dhyeyam| ajetyādau viśeṣyājādipadārthasya pratyayārthatvā'bhāvena vyābhicārasya sphuṭatvāt tadupekṣya, dvitīyakalpa eva vyabhicāraṃ darśayati-ajeti
iṣṭāpattāvāha-chagyāderiti strītvaviśeṣaṇachagyādeśchāganiṣstrītvāderiti vā'rthaḥ| ekatra viśeṣaṇatayā'nvitasyāparatra viśeṣaṇatayā'nvaye nairākāṅ‌kṣyeṇa chāgīmānayetyādau karmatvādikārake chāgī gacchatītyādau chāganiṣṭhastrītvasya chāgīpadārthasyāyogyatayā''khyātārthakarttari vā'nanvayāpatteśceti bhāvaḥ| chāgyāderanāpatteraceti pāṭheca prādhānyānāpatterityarthaḥ|
nanu sā vyutpattirapārthaiva syādata āha kintviti tathāca tādṛśavyutpatteḥ kṛttaddhitamātraviṣayakatvaṃ na pratyayamātraviṣayakatvamiti na tad‌balād‌ vyāpārasyākhyātārthatvamiti bhāvaḥ| nanu kathaṃ tarhi vyāpāraviśeṣyako bodhe'ta āha viśeṣyatvādineti  tathāca vyutpattyanusārī bodhaḥ, natu bodhānusāriṇī vyutpattiḥ| evañca tādṛśavyutpattyaṅgīkartṝṇāṃ tathaiva bodhaḥ, anyādṛg‌vyutpattimatāṃ tvanyathāpīti bhāvaḥ| tadeva viśadayati ata eveti|
śakyatā'vacchedakaprakāraka iti `lakṣṇā''ropitā kriyā' iti kāvyaprakāśīyasūtraparyyālocanayā śakyatāvacchedakāropaviṣayaniṣṭhasaṃsargasyaiva lakṣaṇātvalābhāt, `gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣaḥ' ityatra gaṅgātvenaiva lakṣyārthapratītirata eva tato vaiyañjanikī śaityapāvanatvādipratītiḥ. `jātā latā hi śele' ityādau ca kanakalatātvenaiva rūpeṇa bodhasya camatkārā''dāyakatvāditi tadāśayaḥ|
naiyāyikādināmiti sambandhitā'vacchedakarūpeṇaiva lakṣyārthasya bhānam| ata eva `kacatastrasyati vadanam' ityādau sambanadhitā'bacchedakarāhutvādinaiva bodhasya trāsā''dhāyakasyopapattiḥ| kacatvādiśakyatā'vacchedakarūpeṇa lakṣyā''rthabodhāṅkīkāre tadanupapattiḥ spaṣṭaiva| kiñca, śakyatā'vacchedakam, na lakṣyatāvacchedakam| śakyādanyena rūpeṇa jñāte bhavati lakṣaṇā|
ityabhiyuktoktirodhāditi tadbhāvaḥ|
ghaṭaḥ karmatvamiti navyamate ekapade'parapadavyatirekaprayuktānvayānanubhāvakatvasyākāṅ‌kṣātvopagamena ghaṭapade karmatvapadavyatirekaprayuktāṃnvayānanubhāvatvajñānadaśāyāṃ tādṛśavākyāttaiḥ śābdabodā'bhyupagamādityabhipretya `navyānām' ityuktam| nirākāṅ‌kṣamityasyā''dheyatāsaṃsargeṇa bodhe iti śeṣaḥ| ata eveti bodhasya vyutpattyanusāritvādevetyarthaḥ|
vyutpattyanusāritvāditi kāryakāraṇabhāvagrahādhīnatvādityarthaḥ| bodhānusāriṇī hi vyutpattiḥ, nahi bodho vyutpattyanusārī| ata evārope sati nimittānusaraṇam, natu nimittamastītyāropa iti prāmāṇikāḥ|
arthasya viśeṣyatvādinā pratīyamānasya| anyapramāmatvāt svaviśeṣaṇībhūtapratītyanurodhakavyutpattikatvādityarthaḥ| `pradhānaṃ pratyayārthaḥ' iti niyamasya pāṇininā'nādṛtatve'pi, `yo yaḥ pratyayārthaḥ sa pradhānam' ityarthasya `tadāgame hi' iti sūtrāyatā jaimininā''dṛtasyā'duṣṭatvasya viśeṣaṇaccoktakāryakāraṇabhāvasya nirduṣṭatvādityanye| pratīyamānārthasya prādhānyamiti `tadāgame hi' iti nyāyākāra ityāśayenāha-tadāgame hīti| ativyāptamiti `napuṃsakamanapuṃsakena' iti klibaśeṣataikavacanāntatāyāṃ ca vyabhicaritamityarthaḥ| tadeva viśadayati kṛñoti `pakvam' ityatrā'pinā'nupadokta `pakvavān' ityasyā'pi saṃgrahaḥ, tatra bhāvanāyā ākṣopāt pratīyamānatve'pi pratyayārthatvā'bhāvena vyabhicārasambhavāt| kiṃ kṛtamityato vyāpāraviṣayasya jijñāsyatvā'vagamāt `pakvam' ityatastadanavabodhenā'syottaratvā'nupapattyā vyāpārabodhakatvadhrauvye tasya pratyayārthatvā'bhāvādavyabhicāra ityarthaḥ|
vivaraṇamiti vivaraṇañcā'tra praśrottararūpameva| tad‌bodhanārthameva kimanullekhaḥ, anyathā kevalakṛtaśabdasya pakvapadavivaraṇatayā lokā'prasiddhervivaraṇaṃ cā'tivyāptamityasyā'saṅgateḥ| vācyā syāditi pratyayavācyā syādityarthaḥ| `api'śabdaḥ pūrvoktā'rthasyaiva na samuccāyakaḥ, kintvarthāntarasyāpīti vaktumāśaṅkate-nanviti tathācoktāpattiriṣṭaiveti bhāvaḥ|
apisamuccitārthaṃ prakaṭayati-tathāceti| ubhayeti ākhyātakṛdantobhayetyarthaḥ| sādhāraṇyeneti sādhāraṇyamatra samabhivyāhāraviśeṣaḥ| pratīteriti bhāvanāpratīterityarthaḥ| ubhayasādhāraṇa iti ghaṭakatayā prayogadvayā'nusyūta ityarthaḥ| tathāca prayogadvaye niyatavṛttitvena dhātoreva bhāvanāvācakatvasiddhiḥ, dhātutvasya śaktatā'vacchedakatvena lāghavāt, natu tiṅ‌kṛtoḥ, tiṅtvasya cānanugatasya śaktatā'vacchedakatve gauravāt, parasparavyabhicārāccetyuktāpattenerneṣṭatvasambhāvaneti bhāvaḥ|
nanu dhātutvasya śaktatāvacchedakatāyāḥ prāgeva nirākṛtatvānnoktadūṣaṇasya `api'śabdasamuccayatvamata āha-bhavadrītyeti svamate bhāvanāyāḥ prakṛtyarthatvāduktam-bhavaditi| pratyayārthatvādityanena pūrvoktamatadvayamadhye tvasyaiva mīmāṃsakasammatatvamiti sūcyate| vyākhyātañca tathaiva| avataraṇe tannyāyavirodhopadarśanaṃ tu pratyayā'rthatvaprādhānyayoḥ prāyaśaḥ samanaiyatyena, pradhānasya pratyayārthatvasādhakatvasyā'pi tato lābhena prakṛtivācyatve tadanupapannamityāśayeneti bodhyam  sādhakāntaramityasya dhātorvyāpāravācakatve ityādiḥ| kiṃ kāryamityatra praśrottarabhāvarūpavivaraṇenaiva vyāpārasya dhātvarthatvasiddhirityatra na tātparyam, kintu praśrottarabhāvarūpavivaraṇasya prākāntatvāt kima ullekhra iti sūcayannāha kāryamityatreti| ṇiniriti "bhūte" ityādhikṛtya "karaṇe yajaḥ"  iti sūtreṇeti śeṣaḥ| ete ṇyadādaya ityarthaḥ|
kriyāyogamantareṇeti kriyānvayināmeva kārakatayā dhātorvyāpārāvācakatve `kāryyo ghaṭaḥ' ityādau karmādisaṃjñānāṃ, `kārake' ityadhikṛtya vihitānāṃ ghaṭādāvasambhavena tatra vidhīyamānaṇyadādyanupapattirdhātostadākṣipati, devadattasya pīnatvānupapattiriva rātribhojamiti bhāvaḥ| tadevāha-vinā kriyāmiti| gamyamāneti asya tadupasthāpakapadābhāve'pityādiḥ| tathātvāpattāviti gamyamānakriyāmādāyaiva karttṛkarmā'rthakapratyayopapattāvityarthaḥ| tatrā'pi tiṅ‌saṃjñakā''khyāte|
liṅgasaṅ‌khyānvayā'nurodhāditi asya pācaka ityādāvityādiḥ| āvaśyakam ityasya kṛtīti śeṣaḥ| kṛnnirūpitavācyatvaṃ kartturavaśyamaṅgīkaramīyamityarthaḥ| tenakartrā| ākṣepāt ityasya svarūpanirūpakatayetyādiḥ| pratyaya iti bodha iryarthaḥ| tasya vācyatvasyetyarthaḥ|
asāmarthyāditi viśiṣṭarthopasthityajanakatvādityarthaḥ, nirākāṅkṣatvāditi yāvat| trāta-bhinnapadāntargatadhātvarthakriyayoḥ karttṛkaramakārakasākāṅkṣatvena, hariṇetyādipadopastitakarttṛkaramayośca kārakatvena kriyāsākāṅkṣatvena parasparasākāṅkṣatvarūpasāmarthyaṃ sughaṭam, vyāpārasya dhātvarthatāvādimate| bhavanmate tu `puruṣo rājño bhāryā devadattasyaṃ'ityādivannirākāṅkṣatvāt samāsā'nupapattiriti bhāvaḥ| idamupalakṣaṇam-kārakavibhakterapi|
adhyāhṛtakriyāmādāyeti karotyarthavyāpāramādāyetyarthaḥ| sāmarthyamiti tathāca `takhaiḥ kṛtvā bhinnaḥ' iti bodhābhyupagamenādhyādṛtārthamādāya sāmarthyopapattirityarthaḥ| adhyāhṛtakriyāmādāya sāmarthyā'bhyupagame dṛṣṭāntamāha-dadhyodana ityādi| vidhyānarthakyāditi annena vyañjanam' ityāderapi padavidhitvena samarthaparibhāṣāviṣayatayā svate'samarthadadhyādipadānāṃ samāsavidhānavaiyarthyāpattyopasecanādikriyādhyāhārā'bhyupagame'pītyarthaḥ|
harikṛtamiti kṛdhātorvyāpārārthakatvasvīkārāttannirūpitakarttṛtvasya harau sākṣātsattvādadhyāhāraṃ vinaiva śāstracāritārthye `haritrātaḥ' ityādāvadhyāhṛtakriyādvārakasāmarthyamādāya tatpravṛttau mānābhāvādityarthaḥ| kṛño vyāpārārthakatvānabhyupagametvadhyāhṛtārthasyāpi sāmarthyānupapādakatvācchāstravai3yarthyameva syāditi sūcanāya `kṛtam' ityuktam| ādinā `śarakṛtam' ityādikaraṇatṛtīyātatpuruṣaparigrahaḥ|
kecittu, kṛdarthakartraiva tṛtīyārthakartrākṣiptabāvanāmādāya `haritrātaḥ' ityādau, `nakhairbhinna' ityādau tu karaṇasāmarthyenādhyāhṛtāṃ tāmādāya sāmarthyopapattau na tadanupapattirdhārorbhāvanāvācakatvasādhikāḥ| evañca kṛñaḥ phalārthakatve'pi na samāsānupapattirityāhuḥ, taccintyam|
ekakriyānvayitvameveti `dhavakhadirau'ityādau tasya dṛṣṭavenānyatrāpi sākṣātparamparayā vā tasyaiva samāsaprayojakatvamiti bhāvaḥ| asūryyampaśyā iti nañarthaprasajyapratiṣedhasya kriyānvayitvasya vakṣyamāṇatayā sūryyasyāpi karmakārakatayā tadanvayitvena bhavaduktasāmarthyasya tatra sattvāditi bhāvaḥ| kṛtasarvamṛttika iti mṛttikāyāḥ karaṇatayā, sarvapadārthasya karmatayā kṛdhātvarthā'nvayitvāditi bhāvaḥ| yogavibhāgasyāniṣṭānāpādakatvādāha-kiñceti|
akarttṛtāpattāviti ākhyātārthavyāpārānāśrayatvāditi śeṣaḥ| asmanmate tu ṇicprakṛtyarthavyāpārāśrayatvena karttṛtvānna tadanupapattiriti bhāvaḥ| nanu viṣṇumitrasyā'kartṛtve'pi karaṇatvavivakṣayaiva tatra tṛtīyā sulabhetyata āha-grāmaṃ gamayatīti `kartṛrīpsitatamam' ityanena prakṛtikarttṛniṣṭhavyāpārajanyaphalasambandhina eva karmasaṃjñāvidhānena tvanmate viṣṇumitrasyākarttṛtayā tad‌vyāpārajanyaphalākaśrayasyāpi grāmasyākarmatvena tadvācakapadottaraṃ dvitīyānāpattiḥ, viṣṇumitrasya karaṇatve ṇico'sambhavaśca| dhātvarthavyāpārajanyaphalāśrayasyaiva karmatvāṅgīkāre tu ṇijantakarmataiva syānna gamikarmateti bhāvaḥ| iṣṭapattāvāha-tathāceti|
eteneti ākhyātārthavyāpārāśrayasya kartṛtvanirāsenetyarthaḥ| ṇijante pācayatītyādau| ubhayamiti prayojya-prayojakatvāpāradvayamityarthaḥ| tadāśrayatvāditi ākhyātārthavyāpāradvayāśratvādityarthaḥ|
apāstamiti ayaṃ bhāvaḥ-ākhyātasya vyāpāradvayā'bhidhāyakatvena viṣṇumitrasya karttṛtvopapādane'pi tatkartta़tvasyākhyātābhidhānāddevadattapadottaramiva viṣṇumitrapadottaraṃ tṛtīyādaurlabhyam, evamākhyātārthavyāpārāśratyatvasyobhyorapyaviśiṣṭatvāt pradhānavyāvahārocchedāpattyā "hetumati ca"  ityanuśāsanavirodhaśceti|
nanu ṇijākhyātārthānyatarārthavyāpārāśrayatvaṃ karttṛtvam| asmanmate phalamātrasya gaṇapaṭhitadhātvarthatvena tadanukūlaṇijarthavyāpārāśrayatvena viṣṇumitrasyā'pi karttṛtvaddaurlabhyena noktā'nupapattirata āha kiñceti ākhyātārthavyāpārāśrayatvasya kevalānvayitayā avyāvarttakattvenā''khyātaṃ tadvākyasthatvenā'vaśyaṃ viśeṣṇīyam| evañca `cetraḥ paktā' ityādāvākhyātaśūnye vākye kṛdarthakarttaryavyāptirdurvāretyarthaḥ|
nanu 'astirbhavantīparaḥ' iti bhāṣyeṇākhyātaprayogasya tatrā'vaśyambhāvānnoktāpattiḥ| kiñca' `dhātuvihitapratyayavācyatadākṣiptā'nyataravyāpārāśrayatvaṃ karttṛtvam'| dhātuvihiteti pratyayaviśeṣaṇācca na karaṇatṛtīyārthavyāpārāśrye'tivyāptiḥ, uktasthale kṛdvācyakartrākṣiptavyāpārāśrayatvānnoktadoṣo'ta āha-digiti tadarthastvitaranivṛttitātparyakatādṛśavākye'styadhyāhārasyānāvaśyakatvāduktaprakārasya ya sādhīyastvam| kiñcaivaṃrītyā karttṛtvanirvacanā'pekṣayā dhātorvyāpārārthaṃkatvamabhyupagamya tadāśrayatmeva karttṛtvaṃ nirvaktumucitam, lāghavādityādiḥ|
svotprekṣitatvamiti nirmūlasvakalpanāviṣayatvamityarthaḥ|
hirugiti yathāśrutamanurudhya cedam, tadarthasya kālānanvitatvena kriyātvāsambhavāt| kintu tāvanmātroktau `āṇavayati' ityādāvativyāptirbodhyā, teṣāṃ dhātutve ca śāstraviṣayatayā sādhutvāpatteriti bodhyam  tāvanmātroktāvityatra mātrapadena kriyāvācakatvadalavyavacchedaḥ| iṣṭāpattimāśaṅkayāha tathāceti `yāḥ' ityasya dvitīyāntatvaṃ sūcayitumāha paśyasīti|
lakṣaṇapratīti lakṣyate-anvākhyāyate sādhuśabdo'neneti lakṣaṇam-sūtram| tadanusandhānasāpekṣatvopacāreṇa lākṣaṇikamapi lakṣaṇaśabdenocyate| yathā `pai' ityasya `pā' iti rūpaṃ vilambopasthitikam, lakṣaṇā'nusandhānasāpekṣatvāt| pratipadoktaṃ tu tadvibhaktiviśeṣānupādena paṭhitaṃ śīghropasthitikam, lakṣaṇānusandhānānapekṣaṇāditi| yathā pibateḥ `pā' iti rūpam| tathāca śīghropasthitikatvaṃ pratipadoktagrahaṇe bījam, tacca `yā' iti gaṇapaṭhite eva, sarvanāmni lakṣaṇānusaṃdhānasāpekṣeti bhāvaḥ| avyaye-vetyādāvityādinā, su-ityupasargasya, mā māṅiti svarādyośca saṃgrahaḥ| ṣatvaprakaramapāṭhādupasargavyāvṛttirna śaṅkayā, ṣidhvādisaṃgrahāya pāribhāṣitaṣopadeśatvasya tatrā'pi sattvāt|
prayogaḥ syāditi upalakṣaṇañcaitat-suvati, māti, mimīte, ityādiprayogāṇām| ṣūṅ‌ prāṇiprasave, mā māṅ‌ māne ityādidhātūnāṃ sattvena tannirṇayāsambhavāta| satyantoktau tu na doṣaḥ, vādyarthasya vikalpāderbhūtādikālāsambandhena kriyātvābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ| niyāmaka iti| gaṇe paṭhitānāmeva dhātutvasyābhipretatve'rthanirddeśavaiyarthyāpattyā nirdiṣṭārthānāmeva dhātutvaṃ jñāpyate ityarthaḥ| tasya arthanirdeśasya | arthānādeśanāditi arthasya sattādeḥ, anādeśanādanāmnānādityarthaḥ|
ādhunikatvalābhāditi bhīmasenādipraṇītatvāvagaterityarthaḥ| tathāca bhūvādisūtre `parimāṇagrahaṇaṃ karttavyam, kuto hyetat, bhūśabdo dhātusaṃjñako bhavati, na punarbhvedhaśabda iti', yadyapyarthanirdeśasya sūtrakṛtpraṇītatvamapi| tathāca "cuṭū"  iti sūtre-yadayamiritaḥ kām̐ścinnumanuṣaktān paṭhati, ubundir niśāmane, skandirgatiśoṣaṇayoḥ, iti| tena neritāmididvidhiriti bhāṣye uktam| tathāpi bhāṣyadvayaprāmāṇyātsarvadhātvarthanirdeśasyāpāṇinīyatvena na tannirddeśasya niyāmakateti bhāvaḥ| culumpadīnāṃ stambhvādīnāṃ ca `kāsyanekāca ām' itivācanādudit‌karaṇācca dhātuteti dik  dhātvarthatvameveti phalamevetyrathaḥ. tathāca naitatsūtrānupapattirdhātorvyāpārārthatvāsādhiketi bhāvaḥ| utpattāviti yathābījā'ṅkurādyutpattau anāditvena tatparihāra ityanyat| yathā vā caitraputraścaitra ityādau, tatrāpyekotpattiṃ vinā'parānupapatteḥ| jñaptāviti| halantyamityādau prasiddhaḥ|  asaṅgatyāpatteriti| yathā pṛthivi-jalayorādhārādheyabhāva ityādāvubhayoḥ parasparādhārādheyabhāvena sthiteḥ sarvairevābhyupagamāt| prakṛte vastuto dhātvarthasya kriyātve kriyātvasya dhātvarthatve bādhakābhāvena tatra dūṣaṇadānasyānucitatvāpatterityarthaḥ|
anyatamatvamiti| bhvādibhinnabhinnatvamityarthaḥ||
nanvasmanmate purvāparībhūtāvayavasamudāyarūpakriyāyā dhātvarthatvena tad‌gṛhītapaurvāparyyāropeṇa phalasya kriyātvopapattiḥ| bhavanmate tu kriyāyā dhātvarthatvānupagamena kathaṃ tatra tad‌vyavahāro'ta āha  dhātvarthatvāditi tathāca tatra phalatvavyavahāra iva kriyātvavyavahāro'pi dhātvarthatvaprayojakatvā'bhyupagamena tadutpatteriti bhāvaḥ| bhāṣyādau kṛta iti "karmavatkarmaṇā" iti sūtre 'bhūvādi' ca bhāṣyādāvityarthaḥ| ādipadāt vṛttyādiparigrahaḥ  asyaiveti kriyāvācakasyaivetyarthaḥ| evakāreṇa phalamātravācakatvavyavacchedaḥ| etadavācakatve hetuḥ kriyāpratītyabhāvāditi tadavācakānām-vyāpārāvācakānāmityarthaḥ| adhātutvaprasaṅga iti| āpatteriṣṭatvaṃ tu na, tato lakārādyanutpattiprasaṅgāditi bhāvaḥ|
dharmyaṃśe phalarūpe ityarthaḥ| aṃśapadopādānāt dharmiṇo dharmaghaṭitamūrttikatvādakarmakasthale karttṛvṛttitvācca phalasyāpi tadaṃsatvāt tasya bhāvyatāyāmeva vivādādāha bhāvyatveneti bhāvanāniṣpādyatvenetyarthaḥ| phalasādhyavyāpārasya jijñāsitatve iti yāvat| ata eva vakṣyati `āsannavināśam' iti| vivakṣāsthasameva praśnottarabhāvena darśayati sa tato gato na veti gatyarthā'karmaketi karttari ktaḥ| praśna ityanena bhāvyatvasya jijñāsyatvaṃ dhvanyate| tathāca gato na vā kimiti praśnā''kāraḥ|
prayoga iti praśnanivarttake vākya ityarthaḥ| pratīyate eva ityasyās‌dhātuneti śeṣaḥ| nanu sa tato gato na veti vākyād‌ gehādyavadhikavibhāgānukūlavyāpārāśrayatvatadabhāvānyatarasya jijñāsyatvamavagamyate| mahatā yatnenā'stīti vākyāttstervyāpārārthakatvābhyupagame'pi caitrādikarttṛkasattā'nukūlavyāpāra eveti kathametayoḥ praśnottaratvam? tathāhi-yaddharmāvacchinne jijñāsityaddharmā'vacchinnasya sambandho yatpraśnavākyāt pratīyate taddharmāvacchinne jijñāṃsitataddharmāvacchinnasambandhabodhakavākyasyottaratā| yadāhuḥ-
jijñāsitapadarthasya saṃsargo yena gamyate|
taduttaramiti proktamanyadā''bhāsaśabditam|| iti|
yathā ghaṭatvāvacchinnaṃ jijñāsitadharmāvacchinnasambandhabodhakasya, kasmād ghaṭa iti praśnavākyasya ghaṭatvāvacchinne jijñāsitadaṇḍatvā'vacchinnasya hetuhetusadbhāvabodhakam `daṇḍād ghaṭaḥ' iti vākyamuttaram| prakṛte tu, gata iti, neti vetyuttaraṃ yujyat|
kiñcoktapraśnena sattāsādhakavyāpārasya jijñāsyatvānavagamāt kathametasyottaratvam| tathātve'pi pūrvottaravākye yatnenetyupādānādyatnapadopasthāpyayatnaniṣpādyatvabodhenaivottaratvasid‌dhyā, na tato dhātorvyāpārārthakatvasiddhirata āha utpattyādīti sutarāmityasya vyāpārārthakatvamiti śeṣaḥ| atrāśrayatābodhasyobhayamatasiddhatvena saiva sādhyatvenābhidhiyamānatvād vyāpāraḥ| paurvāparyyaṃ ca paramate sattāyāmiva kālagataṃ tatrāropitameveti bhāvaḥ|
nanu tatrāpyāśrayatāyā dhātvarthatvatalpanamapyāgrahamūlakameva| tatratpattimātrārthakatve'pi rohitāśrayikā bhūtānadyatanotpattirityabhimatārthabodhalābhādata āha kiñceti| tasyā eveti| bhāvanāyā evetyarthaḥ| evakāreṇa phalavyāvacchedaḥ| yathā caitattathoktaṃ sāra eva| tadvidhānāt laḍādividhānāt| tatra harisammatimapyāha kriyābhedāyeti| tumarthāditi caturthi, vārttikena tādarthye vā| kriyāṃ bhettum-viśeṣayitumityarthaḥ| kālaḥ laḍādibodhyaḥ, astīti śeṣaḥ| laḍādyarthakālaḥ kriyāṃmātrabhedaka iti yāvat| vibaktayartharūpā saṅ‌khyā tu sarvasyākhyātaprātipadikārthasya bhediketi tadarthaḥ| matāntare tu dhātuprātipadikārthasyeti bodhyam| evam-dhātorvyāpāravācakatve|
mūle-aśeṣabhāvāditi śeṣaḥ-phalavyāptyatiriktaḥ, bhavatyasminniti bhāvaḥ-adhikaraṇaṃ yasyeti bahuvrīhiṇā śeṣabhāvaśabdaḥ phalavyadhikaraṇārthakaḥ bhāvapradhānanirddeśastasmānnañ‌samastāt pañcamyekavayane phalavyadhikaraṇatvābhāvādityarthāvagatiḥ  phalitamāha sāre bhāvanāyāḥ phaleti ekamātrādhikaramakaphalasāmānādhikaraṇyasya svarūpato bhāvanāspaṣṭabhānapratibandhakatvā'bhyupagamānna spaṣṭaṃ tatpratītiḥ| spaṣṭavañca-phalapratītyanabhibhūtatvamiti bhāvaḥ| evam astyādyakarmakāṇāṃ vyāpārā'rthakatve| phalaniyatatvāditi phalavyāpyatvādityarthaḥ| bhāvanāvācakatvasya phalavācakatvavyāpyatvāditi yāvat sakarmakatāpattariti phalavyāpārobhayavācakasya capacyādeḥ sakarmakatvadarśanāditi bhāvaḥ|
nanu phalavyāpārayorekaniṣṭhatvasyākarmakatāyāṃ tantratve gamyādināmapyakarmakatāpattiḥ, tadarthaphalavyāpārayorekakarttṛvṛttitvādato vyācaṣṭe ekamātraniṣṭhatāyāmiti ekamātraniṣṭhatvam-eketarāvṛttitve satyekavṛttitvam| tatra prayojanābhāvādviseṣyāṃśamamapahāya palitārthamāha bhinnādhikaraṇeti bhinnatvañṭaca vyāpārādhikaraṇāpekṣayā bodhyam|
tathāca svārthavyāpārānadhikaraṇāvṛttiphalavācakatvaṃ parpyavasitaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ lakṣye grāhayati, tatra sattā''tmakavyāpārasyaikamātraniṣṭhatvasyā'vikalatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
nanu mūle `tayordharmibhede' ityanena svārthaphalavyādhikaraṇavyāpāravācakatvam, svārthavyāpāravyadhikaraṇaphalavācakatvaṃ vā sarkamakatvamuktam| tatra vaiyādhikaraṇyaṃ tadadhikaraṇā'vṛttitvam| tathāca grāmadāvavyāptiḥ, tadarthaphalavyāpārayoḥ parasparā'dhikaraṇavṛttibhinnatvābhāvādato vyācaṣṭe bhūṣaṇasāre-āśryabheda iti| tathāca prathamalakṣaṇe karttṛbhinnatadadhikaraṇā'vṛttitvarūpasya dvitīye tadanadhikaraṇāvṛttitvarūpasya niveśānnānupapattiriti bhāvaḥ|
asteḥ phalavyāpārobhayārthakatve harisammatimapyāha-uktañceti| vyapadiśyate-vyavahriyate| antarbhāvāditi dhātvarthatāvacchedakakoṭipraviṣṭatvādityarthaḥ|
pṛthagupasthitayorevānvayaniyamāt karmasaṃjñakārthānvayyarthakarūpasakarmakatvasya tatrānavakāśāditi bhāvaḥ| svoktalakṣaṇānusāreṇa sārakṛd‌ vyācaṣṭe-phalāṃśeneti svakarmakadhāraṇarūpaphalena saha tadanukūlavyāpārasyaikādhikaraṇyādityarthaḥ| ātmarūpakarmaṇā sakarmakatvaṃ kuto netyākāṅkṣāyāmetatsamādhānasyodakṣaratvadoṣaduṣṭasya yogyatvaṃ sudhībhirvibhāvanīyam|
nanu `ātmānaṃ jānāti' ityādāvanupadoktasakarmakalakṣaṇasyāvyāptiḥ, tatra jñānarūpaphale tadarthavyāpārasya vaiyādhikaraṇyābhāvādativyāptiśca phalasamānādhikaraṇetyādyakarmakalakṣamasyetyāśaṅkaya samādhatte-ātmānaṃ jānātītyādinā| antarātmeti antaḥkaraṇāvacchinnātmā śarīrātmā tadavacchinnaḥ saḥ| tathācā''tmana ekatve'pi tataḥca śarīrarūpopādhibhedaparikalpitaṃ bhedamādāya vaiyadhikaraṇyasya sūpapādatvāduktalakṣaṇayornāptyativyāptī ityāha-bhinnādhikaraṇeti asattvabhūtāyā iti kriyāntarākāṅkṣānutthāpakatāpacchedakadharmākrāntāyā ityarthaḥ| taditi tena rupeṇa pratītyāpattirityarthaḥ| kṛdabhihita iti kṛdartha ityarthaḥ| dravyavaditi dravyeṇa-nāmārthena tulyaṃ prakāśate-bhāsate ityarthaḥ| dravyadharmāṇi-liṅgasaṅkhayākārakatvāni gṛhṇātīti yāvat| yadvā dravyam-kriyākāṅkṣotthāpakatāvacchedakarūpasiddhatvavat, tena tulyaṃ prakāśate ityarthaḥ, siddhatvena bhāsata iti yāvat| tathāca tādṛśabhāṣyeṇāpādyavyayatirekanirṇaye taddhetubhūte prāmaṇyopagamādiṣṭāpatterasukaratvāditi bhāvaḥ|
ākhyāte śabādau yasminniti bahuvrīhiṇā vākyamanyapadārtha ityāśayenāha-paśyetyādi| tiṅantābhyām paśya-dhāvatibhyām| dhāvateḥ sādhanatvena, paśyateḥ kriyābodhakatvād dṛṣṭāntatopapattirata eva vakṣyati `iyān paraṃviśeṣaḥ' iti| prakṛtyādīti vyākhyānam vivaramastham| ākhyātaśabda iti karmadhāraya iti tadbhāvaḥ.| apavyākhyāne hetumāha pacatītyatreti tathāca tiṅantarūḍhena kevalākhyātapadenaiva pacatyāditiṅantarūpārthalābhe śabdapadavaiyarthyam| manmate tu tasya bahuvrīhilābhasampādakatayā''khyātaśabdapadasya tiṅantadvayaghaṭitavākyaparatopapattiḥ, pacatyādairbhāgadvayasya sādhyamādhanavarttitve'pi kriyāyāstataḥ sādhanatvenā'bodhād dṛṣṭāntatvāsambhavaśceti bhāvaḥ| tadeva viśadayati-sādhanarūpateti| prakṛtyā-`pākaḥ'ityādāviti śeṣaḥ|
nanvanayorliṅgasaṅkhayānvayitvānvayitvena vaiṣamyāt kathaṃ dṛṣṭāntadārṣṭāntikabhāvo'ta āha iyān paramiti tathāca yatkiñciddharmavattvena sādṛśyavivakṣaṇānnoktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ|
nanu dhāvatyarthasya kārakatvenā'nanvayitve kārakānanvayitvarūpamasattvaṃ tasyā bhajyeteti cet tatra kriyānādhāratvaviśeṣaṇena kartarvyāpārā''dhāratvena karmaṇaḥ phalādhāratvena karttṛtvena karmatvenānvaye'pyuktarūpasyāsattvasyākṣatatvāt| uktañca--
`kriyā na yujyate liṅgaṃ kriyā nā''dhārakārakaiḥ|
asattvarūpatā tasyā iyamevā'vadhāryatām'|| iti|
kriyāntarākāṅkṣānuttthāpakatāvacchedakadharmavattvam-asattvabhūtatvamiti mate tvatra doṣaḥ prāgukta eva| sādhanākāṅkṣotthāpakatāvacchedakadharmavattvarūpatayā pariṣkṛte tu na kvāpi doṣa iti| tathābhidhāne iti uktasādhyatvena rūpeṇa pratītāvityarthaḥ|
odanasyeti atraudavapadaṃ taṇḍulādiparam, anyathā viklinnasyaivaudanapadārthatayā tasya pākāsambhavena bādhitārthakatā''patteḥ| karmaṣaṣṭhayā iti karmatvena karmaśaktāyāḥ ,ṣaṣṭhayā evetyarthaḥ. mānatvāditi anupapattirūpapramāṇatvādityarthaḥ| ghañantena siddhabhāvasyaivā'bhidhāne kārakāṇāṃ sādhyasvabhāvakriyā'nvayitvaniyamenaudanasyākārakatayā'karmatvena tatra ṣaṣṭhayanupapattereva tasya sādhyasvabhāvakriyārthakatve mānatvāditi bhāvaḥ| kriyānvayāditi sādhyasvabhāvā'dhyāhṛtabhavatyarthasya paramparānvayādityarthaḥ|
nanvodanasya kṛdantārthaphalāśrayatvarūpakṛdantayogyasyāpi sattvena kathaṃ tadanupapattirata āha-na loketi idañca samādhisaukaryāduktam| pratyasattyā yaddhātvarthaphalāśrayatvaṃ taddhātvarthakārakasyaiva karmatālābhena pākapadārthasya kriyātvena bhavatyarthasya kriyātve'pi tadarthaphalāśrayatvābhāvenaudane karmatvasyaiva durūpapādatvāt| nanu tatra mā bhūtkṛdyogaṣaṣṭhī, śeṣaṣṭhayaiva tathā preyogopapatteriti cenna, sarvatra sambandhaṣaṣṭhayaiva prayogopapattyā kartṛkarmaṇorityādividhivaiyarthyāpatteḥ| yadi ca `jagataḥ karttā' ityādau karmmatvena karmatvaprakārakasarvasiddhabodhopapattyarthaṃ samāsasthale "gatikārakopapadātkṛt"  ityādivihitasvanirvāhārthañca tasyāvaśyakateti vibhavyate, tadā prakṛte kiṃ tatprayojanaṃ pāṇipihitamiti bhāvaḥ| nanvasattvabhūtakriyāyā bhāvapratyayaprakṛtyarthatve kāṣṭhānāṃ tannirūpitakaraṇatvasyāpi sambhavādbhavatyarthā'nadhyāhāre'pi `kāṣṭhaiḥ pākaḥ' ityādyapi prasajj‌yete tyāśaṅkayeṣṭāpattyā pariharati-evaṃrītyeti|
anye tu bhāvapratyayasthale sambandhibhedenaikasyaiva pitṛtva-bhrātṛtvavaddhāvatyarthasya mṛgadarśanasambandhebhedāt sādhanavacchekasyā eva kriyāyā vācakapratyayarūpasambandhibhedāt sādhyatva-sādhanatvābhyāmupasthitirna tābhyāṃ bhinnabhinnakriyopasathitirityāhuḥ| evamiti yathā `odaṃnasya pākaḥ' ityādāvodanasya karmatvā'nupapattyā'sattyā'sattvabhoūtavyāpāro dhātuvācyastathetyarthaḥ| tādṛśaphalasyāvacyatve'nupapattiṃ darśayati-
ata eveti anyathā tādṛśaprayogānupapattistādṛśaphalasya vācyatve tu tasya vyāpadeśivadbhāvena svasambandhitayā karmatvāt tatsamānādhikaramastokādiśabdebhyo dvitīyopapattiḥ| tatra stokādibhyaḥ karttṛkarmaṇoriti ṣaṣṭhī tu na, kartṛsāhacaryyeṇa bhedānvayina eva karmaṇastatra grahaṇāt| ata eva "pūjānātpūjitam" iti sūtre bhāṣye `dāruṇaṃ yathā bhavati tathā''dhyāpaka ityarthe dāruṇādhyāpakasidhdyarthaṃ malopavacanamārabdhaṃ bhāṣte'| anyathā, karttṛkarmaṇoriti vihitaṣaṣṭhayantena samāse tadvaiyaryyaṃ sphuṭamevetyanyatra vistaraḥ|
anye tu ojaḥsaho'mbhasā varttate, ityadhikāre `tatpratyanupūrvamīpalomakūlam'  iti sūtre dvitīyāntatacchabdagrahaṇaṃ jñāpakam-`kriyāviśeṣaṇād dvitīyā' ityasyārthasya natu stokādyarthasya karmatvamapi, phalasya phalāśrayatvā'bhāvāt| evañca na tadvācakebhyaḥ ṣaṣṭhīprasaktirityāhuḥ  uktatvāditi kriyāntarākāṅkṣā'nutthāpakatāvacchedakarūpavattvaṃ sādhyatvamityādinetyarthaḥ, kriyāntarākāṅkṣānutthāpakatāvacchedakarūpeṇopasthiteḥ kriyānvayāsabhbhavāditi bhāvaṛ| tena rūpeṇopasthiteḥ `paśya mṛgo dhāvati' ityādau vyabhicāritatvādāha-stokaḥ pāka iti ghañā siddhāvasthakriyāyā abodhane dhātvarthaviśeṣaṇavācakastokādiśabdasya liṅsarvanāmanapuṃsakatvāpattiriti bhāvaḥ| tadeva viśadayati-tasmāditi| eteneti ghañādīnāṃ siddhavasthā''pannakriyāvācakatvavyavasthāpanenetyarthaḥ| prayogasādhutāmātramiti mātrapadena vācakatvavyavacchedaḥ|
naiyāyikanavyoktamiti dhātūnāṃ subvibhaktayaprakṛtitvāttadarthe subarthasaṅkhākarmatvādīnāmanvayānupapattyā dhātoriva ghañantasyāpi kriyāvācaktavamabhyupeyam, prakṛttyekadeśārthe tadanvayā'bhyupagame tu yatra pākakartrāderdvitvādikaṃ bādhitam, pākādeśca tadabādhitam, tatra `pacantau paśyati' ityādiprayogāpatteḥ| dhātūpasthāpyārthe subarthānvayabodhaṃ prati taddhātūttaradharmikakiñcidarthaparatvajñānasya pratibandhakatvāvagame tu gauravam| evañca yatra dhātūpasthāpyārthe subarthānvayabodhaṃ prati taddhātūttaradharmikakiñcidarathaparatvajñānasya pratibandhakatvāvagame tu gauravam| evañca yatra dhātumātrasya pākādau tātparyyaṃ tatra tadviśeṣatāvācakastokapadād dvitīyaiva| yatra tu kṛdantasamudāyasya tatra tadviśeṣaṇavācakapadaṃ tatsamānavibhaktikameveti kātantrapariśiṣṭaḍakṛnmataṃ navyanaiyāyikamatatvenānudya dūṣayati-naceti| gauravāditi|
nanu bhāvākhyātavadbhāvavihitaghañādīnāṃ dhātvarthanuvādakatve sthite prāguktaprayogopapattaye'nāyattyā ghañantānupūrvyāḥ śaktatāvacchedakatvasvīkāra āvaśyaka ityata āhanta anuśāsanāditi tathāca gurubhūtānupūrvyāḥ śattatāvacchedakatvāpekṣayā'nusāsanānuguṇyāduktaprayogopapattaye ghañatvasyaiva tattvamucitamiti bhāvaḥ|
nanu ghañādīnāṃvyāpārāvācaktave'pi `kriyāviśeṣaṇād dvitīyā'ityatra kriyāpadasya pākārthakatayā tathānupasthāpyaparatvāt `stokaḥ pākaḥ' ityādau dhātūpasthāpyapākasyoktaviśeṣaṇavaikalyānna tadviśeśaṇavācakapadād dvitīyā''pattirata āha digiti tadarthastu-syādevaṃ yadi kriyā viśeṣaṇād dvitīyeti svatantramanuśāsanaṃ syāt, kintvasmaduktaphalitārthakathanameva tat| tathātve vā tvanamate `stokam'ityanāpatteśceti  kriyāyāmiti pravarttanāviṣayakriyāyāmityarthaḥ| tatra tadananvaye nighātā'nupapattiṃ pramāṇayati-nighātā'nurodhāditi| samānavākya iti| `ākhyātaṃ saviśeśaṇaṃ vākyam' iti samarthasūtrabhāṣyatsākṣātparamparayā vā padārthāntarā'nvitakriyābodhakākhyātasyaitālabhena sambodhanāntārthasya kriyāyāmananvaye `tvaṃ brūhi devadatta' ityādau tadabhāvāt `samānavākye' iti, prakṛtyāmantritsayeti nighātasyānupapatterityākhaṇḍalārthaḥ| tatra `śālīnāṃ-te odanaṃ dāsyāmi' ityādau `sālīnām' ityādeḥ samānavākyasthatvasiddhaye `paramparayā' iti| `pacati bhavati' ityādisādhāraṇaṃ caitat| prakṛte va sambodhanasyānuvādyaviṣayatayā'nuvādyasya vidheyakākāṅkṣatayā, vidheyatāyāśca kriyāniṣṭatvena tatraiva sambodhanāntārthānvayaucityāt samānavākyasthatvamakṣatamiti bhāvaḥ| bodhastvatrābhimukhībhavanānukūlavyāpāraviṣayadevadattoddeśyakapravarttanāviṣayatvadabhinnakartṛkaṃ bhāṣaṇamiti| tatra harisammatimāha-uktañaceti|
sambodhanapadamiti tadvodhyamityarthaḥ| viśeṣaṇamiti svoddeśyakapravarttanāviṣayatvarūpaparamparāsambandhenetyarthaḥ| vrajānītyasya hi `jānīhi' iti śeṣaḥ,
siddhasyābhimukhībhāvamātraṃ sambodhanaṃ viduḥ|
prāptā''bhimukhīkhyo hi janaḥ kriyāsu viniyujyate||
iti kārikāntare `viniyujyate' iti vadatā pravarttanāviṣayakriyāyāmeva tadanvayabodhanāt| pravarttanoddeśyasyaiva tadviṣayakriyoddeśyatvāduddeśyavidheyabhāvasya tayoḥ saṃsargamaryyādayā lābhādekavākyatayā nighāta iti tadarthaḥ| śābdabodhastvatra sambodhanaviṣayadevadattoddeśyakapravarttanāviṣayo matkartṛkavrajanakarmakaṃ jñānamiti| sūtrabhāṣyādirītyeti bhūvādisamarthasūtrasthabhāṣyoktarītyetyarthaḥ|
syādeveti āmantritadevadattapadasyetyarthaḥ| atra `jānīhi' iti pādadhyāhāreṇoktarītyā devadattapadasyaikavākyasthatvā'kṣateriti bhāvaḥ| tatra sūtrakārasammatimāha sūtrabhāṣyādirityeti bhūvādisamarthasūtrasthabhāṣyoktarītyetyarthaḥ|
sammatiriyaṃ sūtrakṛta ekavākyatāṃśe nighātāṃśe ca| bhāṣyasammatistu na, tenātiṅgahaṇasya pratyākhyānāt|
nanvevaṃ khatamatiṅgrahaṇapratyākhyānasaṅgatiḥ, pacati bhavatītyatra nighātavārakatayā tasyā'vaśyakatvāditi cenna, tatra kārakānvitakriyābodhakatvarūpaikavākyatvasya sup‌cayamuptiṅantacasādhāraṇasya sattve'pi vārttikakāraparibhāṃṣitaśrūyamāṇaikatiṅ‌tvarūpaikavākyatvasya tatrāsattvādityāśayāt| tathācoktasathale tādṛśaikavākyatvābhāvanniyatāprasaktirevaitatsūcanāyaiva  syādeva nighāta iti sāmbhāvanādyotakatiṅa upādānam| tadetadāvaṣkaroti-vārttikakārāṇāmiti param kevalam| yathāśrutasūtrarudhdyāha vastutastvati| helārājīya iti
bahuṣvapi tiṅanteṣu sākāṅkṣeṣvekavākyatā|
tiṅntebhyo nighātasya pratiṣedhastathā'rthavān||
iti vākyapadīyavyākhyā'vasara iti śeṣaḥ| tanmate'pi bhavatyeveti idañca niruktalaukikavākyatvasyaiva nighātaprayojakasvamityāśayamūlakamityavadheyam|
punaḥ punarjanmeti|| ata eva sakṛtpravṛttau, dviḥ pacatīti na| `pañcakṛtvo bhuṅkte' ityādau phañcādiśabdāḥ saṃkhyotpattiparāḥ| tasyā eva kṛtvasūjādirdyotakaḥ| tad‌dyotyārthasya kriyāyāmanvaya iti bhāvaḥ| kriyā'nvayināmeva saṃjñetyapapādānādimadhyam| kvacittathaiva pāṭhaḥ| spaṣṭamiti
anye tu-uttarabhāṣye karoti-kriyāṃ nirvarttayatiti vyutpattipradarśanātkārakatvam| ata eva brāhmaṇasya putraṃ panthānaṃ pṛcachatītyādau brāhmaṇasya na kārakatvam, putreṇā'nyathāsiddhatvāt| ata eva teṣāṃ kriyāyāmanvayaḥ| kriyājanakamiti jñāte kā sā kriyetyākāṅkṣodayetana, kriyāyā api janakākāṅkṣayā ca tatraivānvayasyaucityāt| ata eva "gatikārakopapadātkṛt"  ityādau kārakaśabdasyāpādānādiparatā saṅcchete| `kārake' iti tu prathamārthe saptamītyāhuḥ|
ityuktatvāditi tathāca brāhmaṇavaditi vṛttau brāhmaṇaśab‌dasya brāhmaṇakarttṛkādhyayanatulyamekakartṛkamadhyayanamiti `brāhmaṇavadadhīte' ityato bodhaḥ| tatra tasyeveti vihitavaterdravyaguṇānvayitvānmūle prathama ityuktam| vidhānāditi tatra dhātuśabdasya dhātvarthaparatāyāḥ `vasan dadarśa ityatra atītavāsakartṛkarttṛkaṃ darśanamityādiritya bodhāditi bhāvaḥ||
prasajjayeti pratiṣedhaśabdaḥ karmaghañantaḥ, bāhulakāt| prasajjaya-āpādya pratiṣedhaḥ-pratiyogiyasya tādṛśā'bhāvo'rtho yasyeti vyutpattyā'bhāvārthaka ityarthaḥ| yathāśruteti `na brāhmaṇamānaya' ityādau nañarthāropasya brāhmaṇādāvanvayena vyābhicārāpatteriti bhāva-| niṣedhapratīteriti atyantābhāvapratīterityarthaḥ| tathāca tādṛśapratītirevoktanañarthasya kriyā'nvayitve mānamiti bhāvaḥ|
tasya pratiyogitayā yuṣmadādyarthā'nvayitve tu yuṣmadādau lakāravācyakartṛvācakatvarūpasāmānādhikaraṇyābhāvānmadhyamapuruṣādyanupapattiryuṣmadarthapratiyogikābhāvasyaikatvena dvivacanādyanupapattiśceti sūcayitum-na tvaṃ pacasi na yuvāṃ pacathaḥ ityuktam| evaṃ caitrapratiyogikābhāvakartṛtvopagame pāke tatkartṛkatvabādhaḥ| pākānukūlakṛtyabhāvārthakatve tvanmane pūruṣavyavasthāpakābhāvena kadācinmadhyamottamayorāpattiśceti bodhayitum-cautro na pacati ityuktam| ata eveti nañaḥ kriyāpratiyogikābhāvabodhakatvādevetyarthaḥ||
tādṛśaprayoga iti `ghaṭo na jāyate, ityākāraka ityarthaḥ| ghaṭasya vidyamānatādaśāyāṃ ghaṭakartṛkotpattyanukūlavyāpārābhāvā'hādhāttvanmate tu ghaṭā'bhāve utpattyanukūlavyāpārakartṛtvā'bhāvāduktaprayogānupapattiriti bhāvaḥ| uktarītyā nañaḥ kriyāpratiyogikā'bhāvabodhakatvadhrauvye ghaṭo nāstītyatra ghaṭā'bhāvakartṛkatvasya dhātvarthe abodhe'pi tad‌bodhamanādṛtya ghaṭāstitvābhāvabodhābhyupagama ucitaḥ, vakṣyamāṇakāryyakāraṇabhāvabalādityāha tathāca iti| tadevopapādayati nahītyādinā|
nanu yatra na kriyāvācakasamabhivayyāhāro bhūtale na ghaṭa ityādau tatra nañā kathaṃ kriyāpratiyogikābhāvo bodhanīyo'ta āha-tathāceti| adhyāhāryyamiti| `astirbhavantīparaḥ' ityādibhāṣye kriyāntarābhāve'pyastikriyāmādāya vākyaparipūrttibodhanāditi bhāvaḥ| prakāratāsambandheteti nañarthābhāvaniṣṭhaviśeṣyatānirūpitaprakāratāsambandhena śābdabuddhitvāvacchinnaṃ prati viśeṣyatāsambandhena dhātujanyopasthiterhetutvasya ` na tvaṃ pacasi' ityādyanurodhena klṛptavādatrā'pi tad‌bodhopapattaye'styadhyāhāra āvaśyaka iti bhāvaḥ|
nanu nañarthābhāvasya sābadabodhe prādhānyopagame tadadarthabhedātmakābhāvasyāpi prādhanyamevābhyupeyam, abhāvatvāviśeṣāt, tathāca `asarvasmai' ityādau sarvanāmatāprayuktasmāyādikāryānupapattiḥ, `atisarvāya' itivāt| asarvaśab‌dāt `sarvabhinna' iti bodhena bhedāṃśe sarvapadārthasyopasarjanatvāt mahāsaṃjñākaraṇena saṃjñopasarjanārthānāṃ sarvā'dibahirbhāvāt| kiñcānekamanyapadārthe ityādāvanekaśabdādekavacanānupapattirata āha-śeṣamiti-
nanvatvaṃ pacasītyādāvivātrā'pi nañaḥ paryyudāsadyotakatvā'bhyupagamād yuṣmallakṣyārthatvadbhinne sāmānādhikaraṇyābādhānna puruṣavyavasthā'nupapattiḥ| saṅ‌khyāyāstatrābādhānna dvivacanā'nupapattirapīti| tādṛśakāryyakāraṇabhāvasyaivāprāmāṇikatayā kathaṃ tadanurodho'styādhyāhāraniyāmakaḥ| `ghaṭo na jāyate' ityādau prasajjyapratiṣedhārthakanañ‌sthale tu yathā nānupapattastathā vakṣyate| tathāca bhūtale ghaṭo netyādau bhūtalavṛttighaṭābhāva iti bodhe bādhakābhāvo'ta āha-śeṣamiti| tathāca yadā yuṣmadarthabhinnakartṛko na pākastadā na tvaṃ pacasīti prayogā'nāpattiḥ| paryudāsadyotakatāvādimate'tastatra prasajjyapratiṣedhārthakatvamevā'ṅgīkaramīyamityuktayuktayā kāryyakāraṇabhāvasyāvaśyakatetyādiśeṣapadārthaḥ| naiyāyikamatamapi tatraiva vyaktībhaviṣyati| `nañarthanirūpaṇe' ityasya `viśeṣaṇaṃ viśeṣyo vā nyāyatastvavadhāryatām' iti kārikāvyākhyānāvasare iti seṣaḥ. vakṣyate iti-`etattadoḥ iti sūtre `anañ‌'samāsagrahaṇena nañ‌sāmāsāntargatopasarjanībhūtārthapratipādakānāmapigaṇapaṭhitānāṃ sarvāditvajñāpanānnoktānupapattiḥ| tathā anekamanya' ityatra viśeṣyavācakaikavacannānta `sup‌' śabdānurodhāt, `sevyate'nekayā' ityādau pratyekaṃ yoṣāsu sevanānvayabodhānurodhādekavacanamiti na tadananupapattirapīti vakṣyate ityarthaḥ| tad‌yoga iti-kriyānvaya ityarthaḥ  sādhutvā''khyānalābhāditi ayambhāvaḥ-"yasya ca bhāvena bhāvalakṣaṇam"  iti sūtreṇa kriyājñāpakakriyāśrayavācakapadāt saptamī vidhīyate| `bhāvalakṣaṇam' ityupādānājjñāpakatvarūpasaptamyarthasya kriyāyāmevānvayaḥ| ata eva `goṣu duhyamānāsu gataḥ' ityādau dohanakriyā''dhārakālena gamanaparicchedakatvaṃ budhyate iti|
āyātīti| samastasūtrameva tatrānuvarttate ityarthaḥ| tathācā'nādare gamyamāne yanniṣṭhakriyāyāḥ kriyāntarajñāpakatvaṃ tad‌vācakateti tadarthāt tad‌vihitayorapi tayoḥ ṣaṣṭhīsaptamyoḥ kriyāyoge eva sādhutvaṃ labhyata ityarthaḥ| nanūktānāṃ kriyānvayābhāve'pi śaktatvarūpasādhutvaṃ sūlabhamata āha-tatsvarūpamiti sādhutvasvarūpamityarthaḥ| vakṣyata iti sādhutvanirvacanavasara iti śeṣaḥ|
sādhutvaṃ na śaktatvam, apaśabdādapi bodhodayāt, kintu vyākaraṇaikavyañjikā puṇyajanakatāvacchedakajātiriti vakṣyata ityarthaḥ| prakṛte ca vyākaraṇena kriyānviteṣveva tad‌ vyajyata iti tadanyasyāsādhutaiveti bhāvaḥ|
nanu `kriyayaivavadhāryyatām' iti mūlena kriyā'nvaye evaktānāṃ sādhutvaṃ labhyate iti satyameva, tathāpi bhāṣye kriyāpadasya phale'pi prayogātsākṣātparamparayā vā kriyāpadārthaphalānvayenāpi teṣāṃ sādhutvopapattau noktarītirdhātorvyāpāravācakatvasādhiketyāśaṅkayā''śayaṃ prakāśayati-ayambhāva iti| prāyaśaḥ kriyāśabdeneti tathāca prayogabāhulyaṃ rūḍhisadbhāve prayojakamiti bhāvaḥ|
sāṅketikīti saṅketābhivyaṅgayā rūḍhirityarthaḥ| tatra hetuḥ-kvācitka iti| tatra niyāmakamāha-yaugika iti kriyate-vyāpāreṇa niṣpadyate iti vyutpatteriti bhāvaḥ| anapekṣyeti prakṛtipratyayaśaktimananusandhāyetyarthaḥ| ata eveti saṃjñāśabdasyānapekṣya pravṛttikatvena balavattvādevetyarthaḥ|
navame iti mīmāṃsānavamādhyāye ityarthaḥ| tathāha-rathantaraṃ hi "yadyonyāṃ taduttarayorgāyati" iti śrūyate| tatra rathantarayoneḥ parato bṛhadyoneḥ paṭhitatvādrathantaraṃ tasyāṃ geyam? utottaragranthe, "na tvā vā manyate" ityasya paṭhitatvāt tatra geyamiti saṃśaye'viśeṣādubhayatraiva geyamiti pūrvapakṣe uttaragranthe uttarāśabdasya saṃjñārūpeṇa prasiddhaḥ' bṛhadyonau tu tasyāḥ pūrvagranthāpekṣikottaratvabalādyaugikī setisaṃjñāśabdasyānapekṣya pravṛttikatvena balavattvāduttarāśabdasaṅketita-"na tvā vā manyate" ityādyukṣveva tadbhayamiti navame nirṇitam, tadvattatrāpīti bhāvaḥ| nanu bhāvanāyāṃ kriyāpadasya sāṅketikī śāktiḥ, phale tu yaugikaḥ prayoga ityatraiva na dṛḍhataraṃ mānam, yenoktādhikaraṇāvatāraḥ sambhāvyeta, prāyeṇa tasya tu yaugikatbenā'pyupapattarata āha-kiñceti|
tathābhūtānīti viśeṣaṇānītyarthaḥ| kartrā''khyātasthale dṛṣṭatvāditi bhāvaḥ| guṇānāmiti viśeṣaṇānāṃ parārthatvāt-anyopakārakatvādata eva samatvāt-samānadharmatvātteṣāṃ parasparaṃ sambandho na bhavediti nyāyārthaḥ| tathāca yathā phalasyopakārakattavād viśeṣyabhāvanāyāmanvayasyathā kārakāṇāmapi sākṣātparamparayā vā tatraivānvayaḥ, anyathā kārakatvasyaivā'nupapatteḥ| natu phale, nirākāṅkṣatvāditi bhāvaḥ| tadanusārilaukikanyāyamapyupanyasyati `nahi bhikṣuka' ityādi|
anviyantīti-vīgatītyatra praśilaṣṭasya rūpam| mīmāṃsakā apīti uktanyāyena kārakaprakārakabodhaṃ pratyākhyātajanyabhāvanopasthiterhetutāyāstaiḥ klṛptatvāditi bhāvaḥ|| kārakādītyādinā laḍādyarthakālaparigrahaḥ| guṇabhūteti tṛjādyarthakarttari viśeṣaṇībhūtetyarthaḥ| etena pūrvoktanyāyasya tatrāsambhavo darśitaḥ| tasyāmeveti dhātvarthabhāvanāyāmevetyarthaḥ| prapañcitamiti phalāṃśe tu na kārakāṇāmanvayaḥ, mīmāṃsakamate'pi tasyāvyutpannatvāt, kārakānvitasya kārakatvāsambhavena yāgarūpaphalasya karamatayā kriyāyāmananvayā'ṣa''patteriti hi tatroktam|
adarśanāditi `sarvaṃ hi vākyaṃ kriyayāparisampyate' iti nyāyena tatrastijānātyādikriyāmantareṇānvayabodha-nirākāṅkṣatvayoradarśanādityarthaḥ| tatra `caitraḥ sundaraḥ' iti kriyāsākāṅkṣavākyasyāsādhutāpattiḥ| phalāṃśānvayalābhenaivopapattau bhāvanāvācakatvālābhaścetyasvarasaḥ `āhuḥ' ityanena sūcyate  upapattimiti uktāṣṭakasya bhāvanāyāmanvaye sādhakopanyāsarūpāṃ tāmuktvānanvaye bādhakopanyāsarūpāṃ tāmāhetyarthaḥ| `svayamupapattim' iti pāṭhe `svayam' ityanena vakṣyamāṇayukteranākaratvaṃ sūcyate| nanu sambodhanāntādīnāṃ kriyānvaya eva sādhutve, parvato vahnimān mahānasavadityādau vatyarthasādṛśyasya kathaṃ parvate'nvayaḥ? kathaṃ vā bhūtale na ghaṭa ityatra saptamyarthabhūtalādheyatvasya nañarthā'bhāve ityata āha mūle yadi pakṣe'pīti pakṣe-pratijñāvākyajanyabodhaviśeṣye sandigdhasādhyake dharmiṇi parvatādāviti yāvad kārakam adhikaraṇādi| nañādiṣu nañarthābhāve, itaranāmārthe cetyarthaḥ| tameva viśadayati sāre-parvato vahnimānityādinā ādipadāditi nañādiṣvityādipadādityarthaḥ| tadeva vivṛṇoti evamādiṣviti bhūtale ghaṭa ityādāvityarthaḥ|
anvayaśceti mahānasasadṛśaḥ parvataḥ, bhūtalavṛttitvābhāvān ghaṭaḥ, bhūtalavṛttirghaṭābhāva iti vā bodhaśecatyarthaḥ| tena tulyamityanuśāsanena kriyāgatasādṛśyabodhena eva vateḥ, `kārake' ityadhikṛtya saptamyadhikaraṇe cetyanuśāsanena kriyānvaya eva saptamyāśca sādhutvabodhanāduktam yadīti| caturthyā iti puṣpebhya ityatra "spṛherīpsitaḥ"  iti vihitacaturthyāḥ spṛhayatyupāttarthe eva svārthānvayabodhakatvam, na padāntaropātte, adhyāhṛte vā, tadarthe'nuśāsanavirodhāditi svasiddhāntaktyajyatāmityarthaḥ|
arddhajaratīyamiti idamarthe gahāditvācchaḥ| kvacidanuśāsanānurodhaḥ kvacinnetyarddhajaratāsadṛśamityarthaḥ| tathāca svīyasiddhāntapracyutirūpavādhakabhiyā'trāpi parvato vahnimān bhavitumarhatītyeva pratijñā'ṅgīkaraṇīyā, natu kriyāvācakapadaśūnyā seti noktaniyame vyabhicāraḥ| `bhūtale na ghaṭaḥ' ityādau yad vaktavyaṃ tattūktam| vakṣyate cā|ṭadhikamiti bhāvaḥ  kṛtapūrvotyatra bhāvavihitaktena siddhā'vasathāpanno bhāva ucyate| tad‌viśeṣaṇaṃ ca prakṛtyarthakriyetyāśayena mūlamavatārayati-evamityādinā kartrādāvityādinā bhāvaparigrahaḥ, inyādināmityādinā ktvā-tumunnādīnām|
sambadhyate iti kārakapa3kārakabodhaṃ prati viśeṣyatayā dhātujanyabhāvanopasthiterhetutāyāḥ pūrvamuktatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
kṛtapūrvvo kaṭamiti nanvatra karmaṇi kte kaṭaśabdādanabhihitādhikārīyadvitīyā'nupapattiḥ, bhāve ktastu sakarmakādnaganakusumāyamāna eva, tayoreveti niyamāt| ata eva napuṃsake bhāve'pi na saḥ| tathāca kathamuktadārṣṭāntikavākyopapattiriti cedatrāhuḥ-karmaṇo viśeṣarūpeṇa prāgavivakṣayā akarmakatvādbhāve pratye tadantasya pūrvaśabdena samāse `sapūrvācca' itīniḥ, īdṛśavivakṣā'vivakṣe ca tadbhāṣyaprāmāṇyāt kṛtapūrvotyādiviṣeye eva, nānyaviṣaye iti bodhyam|
inyādibhiriti tadarthakartrādibhirityarthaḥ| vastutastu dārṣṭantike svavācakaprakṛtikapratyayārthe'nyasmin vā guṇībhūtā kriyā kaṭādikarmādibhiriti vyākhyeyam| nātaḥ kṛtapūrvvoti prayogaghaṭakenyarthakarturviśeṣyatvena prakṛtārthāsaṅgatiḥ, pūrvakālikākriyākartteti tato bodhena tādṛśakriyāyā inyarthakarttari guṇatvāt| nāpyayaṃ bhāvādhikārīyaḥ, svātantryeṇāprayogāt| naca `pūrvaṃ kṛtamanena' iti vigrahāt dhātvarthakriyāviśeṣyaka eva tato bodhaḥ, tathā sati tasya avigrahatvābhāvena mūlavirodhaprasaṅgat| dhātvarthānuvādakapratyayānte pratyayārthāviśeṣaṇībhūtadhātvarthakriyāyā eva pratītyā tad‌vihitapratyayārthakarturviśeṣyatāyā evaucityāt, anyathā `pacatikalpam' itivat nupaṃśakatvāpatteśca| ata eva bhoktuṃ gatametenetyādiprayogopapattiḥ, bhāvānadhikārīyabhāvavihitapratyayasya dhātvarthānuvādakatvena tat prakṛtyarthakriyāyāḥ sādhyamātrasvabhāvatvāt, aguṇatvācca| evam `avyayakṛto bhāve' ityukte `pākaḥ' ityādiprayogasthaghañarthāttumunnādyarthe vailakṣaṇyānubhavāt tumunādīnāmapi sādhyamātrasvabhāvabhāvārthakatvamityanyatra vistaraḥ|
vṛttimātre iti mātrapadaṃ kṛtsnārthakam| vakṣyamāṇatvāditi `samāse khalu bhinnaiva' ityanena ityādiḥ, tatra samāsapadasya vṛttisāmānyaparatvena vyākhyāsyamānatvāditi bhāvaḥ| kathamiti gata ityādau kṛd‌vṛttisattvena gamanakartṛrūpagatapadārthaikadeśabhāvanāyāṃ karmmānvayāsamabhavād dṛṣṭāntāsiddhiriti bhāvaḥ| nityasāpekṣasthale hi pakṣadvayam-caitrasya gurukulamityatra viśeṣaṇasya caitrādergurvādidvārakasambandhena kulādirūpaviśeṣya evānvaya iti tatraikaḥ pakṣaḥ| sa cokto hariṇā-
samudāyena sambandho yeṣāṃ gurukulādinā|
saṃspṛśyāvayavām̐ste tu yujyante tadvatā saha|| iti|
uktasthale samudāyanirūpitasambandhe ṣaṣṭhī avayavadvārakaśca samudāyena sambandha iti sāmarthāda avayavamapīti viśeṣaṇaṃ spṛśatīti tadarthaḥ| aparastu gurvādinirūpitasambandhe eva devadattasyeti ṣaṣṭhī tatraiva tadanvayaḥ| uktañaca-
sagbandhiśabdaḥ sāpekṣo nityaṃ sarvaḥ samasyate|
vākyavat sā vyapekṣā hi vṛttāvapi na hīyate|| iti||
nityamiti-sā'pekṣapadā'rthānvayi, hi-yato devadattasya guroḥ kulamityādivyaste yā vyapekṣā sā samāse'pi na hīyata iti kārikārthaḥ| padārthaḥ padārthenā'nvetīti vyutpattistu nityasāpekṣātiriktaviṣeyeti tadbhāvaḥ| tatrāntimaṃ pakṣamabhipretya samādhatte-nityasāpekṣeṣviti
tathāca gurūtvāderniyatānyanirūpyatvena tadupasthitau niyamena jāyamānāyā nirūpakākāṅkṣāyā nivṛttaye devadattādipadārthānvayavat prakṛte'pi kiṃ sādhakaṃ gamanarūpasādhyamiti sādhanākāṅkṣānivṛttaye grāmādirūpakarmakārakānvayasyā''śyakatvānnadṛṣṭāntāsaṅgatiriti bhāvaḥ| ādinā `śaraiḥ śātitapatraḥ' ityādeḥ saṅgrahaḥ| avataraṇikāsthādipadagrāhyaṃ spaṣṭayati-evaṃ bhoktuṃ pāka ityādāviti atrā'pyadipadagrāhyam-bhuktvā pāka iti ghañarthabhāvaguṇabhūtāyāṃ pacyarthakriyāyāṃ tumunnādyantārthabhojanādikriyānvaya iti bodhyamityarthaḥ| ghañādyontaprayogasthale prakṛtipratyayābhyāmasattvāsattvabhāvā viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvāpannā kriyā'bhidhīyate| tatra pratyayārthatvena ghañādyarthakriyāyāḥ prādhānyam| ata eva prakṛtyarthasādhyamātrasvabhāvāṃ tāmādāya kārakānvayopapattiḥ, uktadharmākrāntakriyopapadatvena ṇvulaḥ sambhave'pi `kārako gataḥ' ityādīnāmanabhidhānameveti bhāvaḥ|
navyāstu-tumun‌vidhāyake kriyārthāyāṃ kriyāyāmityuktyā prattyāsattyā kriyayorekajātīyatvalābhena svāvācakaprakṛtikapratyayārthaṃ pratyaguṇībhūtāyāḥ sādhyamātrasvabhāvatvābhāvāt, prakṛtipratyayārthayostayorviśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvācca| dhvanitaṃ cedamupapadamatiṅti sūtrabhāṣye| yatra tu kriyāmātrārthakasya grahaṇaṃ tatra kriyāmātraviṣaṇādapīti gatapākādiyoge'pi suktvādītyāhuḥ|
teṣāmayamāśayaḥ-ghañādyantasthale prakṛtipratyayābhyāṃ viṣeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvāpannasattvāsattvasvabhāvakriyādvayamupasthāpyate| tatra pratyayārthakriyāyāḥ prādhānyam| evañca dhātupasthāpyakriyāyāḥ sādhyamātrasvabhāvatve'pi tasyā ghañartha prati guṇatvānna tatra tumunnādīti|
tatredaṃ cintyam, bhāvānadhikārīyadhātvarthānupādakapratyayāntayoge tumuno durvāratvāt| kiñca sujvidhāyake'pyuktaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭāyāstasyā grahaṇamāvaśyakam| tattad‌viśeṣaṇībhūtakriyāyāḥ sādhyamātrasvabhāvatvena tajjanmaṇane dvau pākāvityanna sujāpatteraśakyavāraṇatvāt| naca tatraṣṭāpattiḥ, kriyāpadopādānavaiyarthyādabhyāvṛttipadasāmarthyainaiva kriyāyā lābhādivakṣyamāṇatvāt|
kiñca `sādhyatvena kriyā tatra' iti haripadyavyākhyā'vasare, ekasyaiva sambandhibhedādācāryatvamātulatvādivadekasyā eva kriyāyāḥ prakṛtipratyayarūpavācakasambandhibhedādupasthitiriti svoktivirodhaḥ kriyādvayābodhāṅgīkāra iti|
atiprasaṅgamiti svamate guṇībhūtakriyāmādāya kṛtvo'rthapratyayāpattirūpaṃ tamityarthaḥ-kriyayoḥ pūrvakāla iti kriyayormadhye pūrvakālavṛttikriyārthakād dhātorityarthaḥ| tumannādayaḥ ityādipadāt ktvādiparigrahaḥ| vidhiyamānā iti `avyāyakṛto bhāve' ityukverbhāva iti śeṣaḥ| pūrvakāloddeśyatvādīnāṃ saṃsargavidhayā bhānasya vakṣyamāṇatvāt| dvirvacanam ityādipadāt dviḥprayogadvirāgamanādiparigrahaḥ, tatra lyuḍ‌ghañarthaguṇībhūtakriyāmādāya sucodarśanāditi bhāvaḥ|
atiprasaṅgastviti `sakṛtpākaḥ' ityādiprayogāpattirūpastvityarthaḥ| anabhidhānāditi śiṣṭāprayuktatvādityarthaḥ| tasyā eveti pūrvāparibhūtāvayavakasamudāyarūpakriyāyā evatyarthaḥ| evakāreṇa dravyagumavyavacchadeḥ, tayoḥ sakṛdevotpatteḥ| kriyāyāstu naikasyā api nivṛttibhedāyā dhātuvācyatvopagamena tajjanmani paunaḥpunyasambhavāditi bhāvaḥ|
sāmarthyāditi abhyāvṛttipadopādānasāmarthyādityarthaḥ| sāmarthyañca-tadākṣepārthakatvam|
sādhyamātreti yadyapi dhātūpasthāpyāyāṃ tasyāṃ tattvamakṣatam, prāguktayuk‌testathāpi sādhyamātrasvabhāvapadasyoktapratyayārthaguṇībhūtasādhyasvabhāvāmātraparatvānna doṣaḥ| tādṛśīti ghañupasthāpyoktarūpetyarthaḥ|  vyutpattyeti  vivaraṇaparabhāṣyakāraprayogādityarthaḥ jñāpakamiti tādṛśasūtrakṛtprayogarūpamityarthaḥ| `hataśāyikāḥ śayyante' itivaditi bhāvaḥ| āhuriti sāragrāhiṇa iti śeṣaḥ, āhuriti-āhurityasvarasaḥ sūcitaḥ| tadvījantu krayāpadasyoktārthaparatve'pi uktātiprasaṅgasyāparihāreṇa tasya spaṣṭapratipattyarthataivocitā| anabhidhānāśrayaṇenaivokātapattivāraṇe dvirvacanamityādiprayogāṇāṃ lokasiddhānāṃ jñāpakānusaraṇenopapattiścānuciteti  siddhānta iti vaiyākaraṇasiddhānte| `indriyāṇāṃ svaviṣayeṣu' ityādinā pratipāditā bodhakatārūpā śaktirākhyāte'phyavādhitaiva, tato bhāvanābodhasya jāyamānatvādityarthaḥ| sādhutvapareti| tathāca hariḥ-
sādhutvajñānaviṣyā saiṣā vyākaraṇasmṛtiḥ| iti|
tatra sādhuśabdajñāne| avacchedakatayā sādhuśabdajñānaniṣṭhabodhajanakatā'vacchedakatayetyarthaḥ| atiriktaśiktivāde'pīti sādhutvaṃ na śaktatvam, āsādhorapi bodhāt| kintu bodakatvamiti vakṣyamāṇāsiddhintimate'pītyarthaḥ|
āsādhutā bhāvanāyāṃ syādeveti ākhyātācchaktibhrameṇa bhāvanābodhe'pi tatra tasyāsādhutā syādevetyarthaḥ| tathāca bhāṣyakṛtā-"siddhe śabdārthasambandhe"  ityatra "samānāyāmarthavagatau sādhubhirbhāṣitavyaṃ nāsādhubhiriti gamyā gamyetivanniyamḥ kriyate iti paspaśāyāmuktam| evañca yādṛśaviśeṣaṇānvitayādṛśaviśeṣyabodhe yādṛśānupūrvvayāḥ sūtrakṛdādyanyatamasammatatvaṃ tādṛśabodhe tādṛśānupūrvvokaḥ śabdaḥ sādhurnānyatra| ata evāsvaśabdo'śve na sādhuḥ kintu niḥsve| aśvaśabdo nisve na sādhuḥ, kintu turaga iti saṅgacchate| nanvastu asādhutetyata āhaevañceti yājñe karmaṇītyanena "nānṛtaṃ vadenānāpabhraṃśitaṃ vā" ityādiśruteḥ kratuprakaraṇe pāṭhāditaratra tatprayoge pratyavāyābhāvaṃ sūcayati| syādeveti bhāvanābodhakatvenākhyātasya sūtrakārādibhirananuśiṣṭatvāt karttṛbodhakatvenaiva tadanuśāsanāditi bhāvaḥ|
mīmāṃsakaḥ śaṅkate-nanviti| tvanmate vaiyākaraṇamate| nānṛtamiti darśapūrṇamāsaprakaraṇe hi `nānṛtaṃ vadet' iti śrūyate| tatra kimayaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ pūruṣārthaḥ| āhosvit kratvartha iti saṃśraye, vadedityākhyātāt karttṛpratītyā karttṛvācakatvanirṇayaḥ, laḥ karmaṇītyanuśāsanāttasya karttṛparatvalābhācca| pratyayārthatvena vadanaṃ prati prādhānyād vadanaṃ tāvat pūruṣārtho'taḥ pratiṣedho'pi puruṣārtha-| niṣedhapratiyogitvena vadanakriyāmanuvadatā vadediti śabdena pūruṣārthatayaiva bodhanāt tādṛśasyaivānṛtavadanasyāniṣṭasādhanatāyā viparītasvabhāvabodhakanañā bodhanādanyādṛśānṛtavadanasya cānupasthitatvāt| ata evānārabhyādhītasmārttavadananiṣedhasyāpyetadeva mūlaṃ bhavatīti lāghavām| anyathā tanmūlabhūtaḥ pūruṣārtho'nyo niṣedhaḥ kalapya iti gauravaṃ prasajyeteti pūrvapakṣe, `ananyalabhya eva śabdārthaḥ' iti nyāyādbhāvanaivākhyaptavācyā, karttā tvākhyātavācyabhāvanāyā avinābhāvādākṣepalabhya eveti na tatra tasya śaktiḥ| laḥkarmmaṇītyādismṛtirapi dvayekayorityanenaikavākyatayā tatsaṅkhyāvācitvaparaiva| `pacati devadattaḥ' ityādau ca `gauḥ śuklaḥ' itivat sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ lakṣaṇayaiveti nākhyātasya karttṛvācitvamiti karttuḥ śruteranupastitatvānna pūruṣārthatā tasya, kintu prakaraṇāt kratvarthataiveti nirṇitaṃ kartradhikaraṇe| tat‌sthitaṃ pūrvapakṣamanuvadati-ākhyāteneti tiṅetyarthaḥ| tacceti prakaramañceryarthaḥ| śrutivirodha iti nirapekṣasvarūpaśrutiprātikūlya ityarthaḥ|
tathāhi-śrutyādayaḥ ṣaḍiha viniyojakāḥ| ta6 viruddhayorekatropanipāte samuccayadaurlabhyādekenāparasya bodho vaktavyaḥ| sa ca balavatā durvalasyeti kasya daurbalyamityākāṅkṣāyāṃ pāradaurbalyapratipādakam-`śrutiliṅgavākyaprakaramasthānasamākhyānāṃ samavāye pāradaurbalyam, arthaviprakarṣāt' iti sūtraṃ praṇināya maharṣirjauminiḥ| śrutyādīnāṃ paṭhitānāṃ samāvāye ekatropanipāte pāradaurbalyamiti| parameva pāram-uttarapaṭhitam| tathācaiṣāṃ madhye yadapekṣayā yatparaṃ tad‌durbalamityrathaḥ| tatra hetumāha-arthaviprakarṣāditi-artasya-viniyojakasya liṅagādito viprakarṣāt-uttaravarttitvāt, liṅgādervilambena pūrvāpekṣayā viniyojakatvāvagateriti yāvat  tatra śrutyādīnāmudāharaṇāni-nirapekṣo ravaḥ śrutiḥ| itaraprāmāṇyānadhīnaprāmaṇyakatvañca nirapekṣatvam-yathā `vrīhīnavahanti' iti| atra kriyājanyaphalabhāgitvaṃ karmatvaṃ bodhayantī dvītīyā śrutiritaranirapekṣaiva vrīhīṇāmavaghātaśeṣitvaṃ pratipādayati  arthaviśeṣaprakāśanasāmarthyaṃ liṅgam| yathā `barhirdevasadanaṃ dāmi' iti| atra lavanārthaprakāśakatayā lavane asya viniyogaḥ  parasparākāṅkṣāvaśādekasminnarthe paryyavasannāni padāni vākyam| yathā `devasya tvā savituḥ prasave'śivanorbāhubhyāṃ pūṣṇo hastābhyām `agnaye tvā juṣṭaṃ nirvapāmi' iti| atra mantraliṅgena nirvāpe prayujyamānasya samavetārthabhāgasyaikavākyatābalena devasya tvetyādibhāgasyāpi tatraiva viniyogaḥ  labdhavākyabhāvānāṃ padānāṃ kāryyāntarapekṣāvaśādvākyāntareṇa sambandha ākāṅkṣāparyavasannaṃ prakaraṇam| yathā-`samidho yajati' iti| asya hi darśapūrṇamāse kathambhāvākāṅkṣāyāḥ prakaraṇapāṭhavaśāccheṣatvam  sthānām-kramaḥ| sa cānekasyāmnātasya sannidhiviśeśāmnānam| yathā-dadhirasītyatrāgneyo pāṃśavagnīṣomīyayāgāḥ krameṇa paṭhitam| tatrāgneyāgnīṣomayordvayorliṅgenaiva viniyogasiddhiḥ| dadhirasityatra tu na liṅgādiviniyojakam kintu yasmin pradeśe brāhmaṇa upāśuyāgavidhānaṃ tasmin pradeśe mantre'pyasya pāṭha iti kramādupāṃśuyāgānumantreṇa tasya viniyogaḥ  samākhyā-yogabalam| yatā `hautramaud‌gātram' ityādi| tatra hi-hoturidaṃ hautramityādiyogabalena hautrādisamākhyātāni karmāṇi hotrādibhiranuṣṭheyānīti 
virodhodāharaṇādi yathā-śrutilaṅgayorvirodhe śrutirbalīyasī, liṅgaṃ tu darbalam, arthaviprakarṣāt| yathā, kadācanastarīrasītyādikāyā ṝco viniyojikā śrutīḥ `aindrayā gārhapatyamupatiṣṭhate' iti indraprakāśanasāmarthyālliṅgādindropasthāpane viniyogo'syāḥ pratibhāti, anprakāśakasyānyatra viniyogāyogāt| śrutyā gārhapatyopasthāpane sa pratīyate| gārhapatyamiti dvitīyā hi karmavibhaktitayā kāmapi kriyāmapekṣya prakṛtyarthasya śeṣitvaṃ bodhayati, kriyājanyeṣṭaphalabhāgitvasya tadarthatvāt| evamaindrayeti tṛtīyāprakṛtyarthasya śeṣatvam, kriyāṃ prati sādhakatamatvarūpakaraṇatvasya tadarthatvāt, tadanayorvirodhaḥ|
tatrāyaṃ pūrvapakṣaḥ-sāmārthyajñānamapekṣya śrutirviniyojikā| nahyasamarthaṃ śrutisahastramapi vinoyoktumalam, ato liṅgasya balavattvādindropasthāpane'syā viniyogaḥ| tadanurodhitayā dvitīyā saptamyarthatayā vyākhyeyā| gārhapatyasamīpe `indro'nayā upasthātavyaḥ' paryavasyatīti|
siddhāntastu-śrutiḥ svarūpasatsāmarthyamapekṣate, natu tajjñānamapi, yena liṅgaṃ balavadbhavet| tasya ca pūrvaṃ sata eva viniyogo'nyathānupapattyā jñānaṃ paścādupajāyate| liṅgantu viniyojane śrutimapekṣate| nahyanayendra upasthātavya iti liṅgāt svārasataḥ protīyate, kintvindra etādṛ-gityetāvanmātram| tathāca prakaraṇāmnasāmarthrayādindraprakāśanasamarthāyā ṝco'nyathā nupapattyā liṅgonānayendra upasthātavya iti śrutiḥ kalpanīyā, yayā viniyogo bhavet| tato yāvalliṅgaṃśrutikalpanāyaai prakāntavyāpāraṃ tāvat pratyakṣaśrutyā viniyogaḥ siddha yaiti prakaraṇe nirākāṅkṣe kayā'nupapattyā śratiḥkalpanīyā| tasmācchruterbalavattvāttadanuguṇatayā sāmarthye nīyamāne indrapadaṃ paramaisvaryāśrayavācakatayā gārhapatyatātparyyakamityavadhāryyata iti| śrutiprakaraṇayorvirodhodāharaṇantu yathā pratiṣṭhākāmadarśapūrṇamāsayāgo ekaviṃśatimanubrūyāt pratiṣṭhākāmasyeti śrutyā ekaviṃśati sāmadhenyanuvacanānāṃ tadaṅgatvāvagatau nairākāṅkṣānpañcadaśasāmadhenyanupacanāṅge tvāvagamakaprakaramasya bādhaḥ|
virodhodāharaṇāntarāṇi tvanyato'vadhāryyāṇi, gauravabhānneha prapañcitāni| tadayaṃ nirgalito'rthaḥ-śrutirnirapekṣatvāt sarvato balavatī, liṅgaṃ tu viniyoge ekāntaritatvād dvayantaritavākyād balavat| evaṃ vākyādavapyūhyam| samākhyā tu pañcāntaritatvātsarvato durbaleti prakṛte ākaṅkṣāparyavasannaprakaraṇena vākyaṃ kalpanīyam| tena ca liṅgam, liṅgena śrutistayā'nṛtavadananiṣedhasya kratau viniyogaḥ| yāvacca prakaraṇaṃ vākyakalpanāyai prakāntavyāpāraṃ tāvatpratyakṣayā kartrarthaikākhyātaśrutyā tasya pūruṣārthatvena viniyoge siddhe klṛptamapi vākyādyatkiñcitkaramiti śrutivirodhe bādhyata ityasyārthaḥ, liṅgādīnāmuktaprakāreṇa śrutyapekṣayā durbalatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
samādhatte-tiṅarthastu viśeṣaṇamiti tathācākhyātaśrutyā karttṛrupasthāpane'pi na bhāvanāvat tasya prādhānyena bhānam, yena niṣedhasya puruṣārthatā śaṅkayeta, kintu viśeṣaṇatayaiva, tathopasthitasya ca viśeśaṇāntaranairākāṅkṣayeṇa karttuḥ śrautanañarthasambandhābhāvānnātra śrutyā prakaraṇabādha ityāśāyaṃ viśadayati-svāṅgatveneti svaviśeṣaṇatayetyarthaḥ|
nanvevaṃ niṣedhasya kvāpi śrautapuruṣārthatā na syāt, `na kalañjaṃ bhakṣayet' ityatrāpyuktarītyā karttari nañarthāsambandhena śrautatvāt| yadi ca kartturaprādhānye'pi karttṛvṛttibhāvanāyā prādhānyāttasyā nañarthānvaye tad‌dvārā''khyātārthakarttṛrapyanvayānna tasya śrautapuruṣārthatvānupapattiriti vibhāvyate, tadā prakṛte|ṛpi tulyamata āha-astu veti|
anuṣṭhāna iti naca nānṛtaṃ vadediti niṣedhasya kratuyuktapūruṣadharmatve yajamānasya prādhānyātpradhānībhūtakartrevānṛtavadanaṃ varjyaṃ syāt kratvarthatve ca kartranapekṣatvena yajñapravṛttena yajamānenartvigbhiśca varjanaṃ sidhyatīti viśeṣāt kathametaditi vācyam, śrutiprakaraṇābhyāṃ kratusambandhapūruṣakartṛkānṛtavadanābhāva iti bodhena kratukarttṛketibodhe mānābhāva iti kratuyukteti vadato'bhipretatvāditi|
tatra dṛṣṭāntamāha-jañjabhyamāna ityādi darśapūrṇamāsaprakaraṇa evaitanmantravajanaṃ śrūyate-'jañjabhyamāno'nubrūyānmayi dakṣakratū" iti| tatra kimetamntravacanaṃ śuddhapuruṣadharmaḥ, uta kratuyuktapuruṣasaṃskārakatayā kratvaṅgamiti sandehe, vākyena tāvajjañjabhyamānapuruṣadharmatayaivāyaṃ pratīyate, kṛtpratyayasya śānacaḥ kartṛvācakatvāt| prakaraṇena tu kratvaṅgatayā, prakaraṇaṃ ca vākyāpekṣayā durbalamiti puruṣadharmataiva yuktā| satyāṃ ca phalākāṅkṣāyām "prāṇāpānavevātmānaṃ dhatte" iti vākyaśeṣe śrutasya phalasya rātrisatranyānena kalpanāditi pūrvapakṣe siddhāntaḥ-vākya-prakaramayorvirodhe bādhyabādhakabhāvo natvatrāsti virodhaḥ| jañjabhyamānapadasya karttṛvācakatayā tādṛśapūruṣasya kratāvapi sattvāta tatsam̐skāramukhena kratvaṅgatāsambhavāt| evañcārthavādopasthitaphalakalpanāgauravamapi neti| tathāca balābalacintāyāmeva virodha evaucityena jañjabhyamānavākyanuvacanasya vākyaprakaraṇābhyāṃ kratuyuktapuruṣadharmatāvat prakṛte'pyanṛtavadananiṣedhasya śrutiprakaraṇābhyāṃ kratusambandhapurudharmataivocitā| uktañca bhaṭṭapādaiḥ-
stryupāyamāsabhakṣādipuruṣārthamapi śritaḥ|
pratiṣedhaḥ kratoraṅgamiṣṭaḥ prakaraṇāśrayāt || iti bhāvaḥ|
prapañcitamiti prapañcastu uktaḥ|
nanu sādhutvajñānaṃ vinā'pi śābdabodhodayena vyabhicārāt kathametat, sādhutvasya śaktatvarūpatāyā nirākariṣyamāṇatvāccāta āha-vastuta iti tad‌vyatirekanirṇaya iti āsādhutvaniścaya ityarthaḥ| tanniścaye'pyapabhraṃśād bodhadarśanāt| tathācā''khyātādbhāvanābodhe bādhakābhāva iti bhāvaḥla 
iti vaiyākaraṇabhūṣaṇasāradarpaṇe dhātvākhyātārthanirūpaṇam || 1 ||

|| atha lakārārthanirṇayaḥ ||
 lakārasāmānyārthe nirūpite tadviṣayakajijñāsānivṛttāvavaśyakaktavyatvajñānādviśeṣarthanirūpaṇe'vasarasya saṅgatitvaṃ sūcayannāha-pratyekamiti| daśalakārāṇāmiti laṭṛliṭ ityādikrameṇa paṭhitānāmityarthaḥ| ādeśānāṃ vācakatvamiti pakṣe'pi tiṅādyādeśaniṣṭhāṃ bodhakatārūpāṃ lādiṣvāropya tadvidhānāt tadarthakatvaṃ teṣāmakṣatamiti bhāvaḥ|
