prāravdhāparisamāptatvamiti vinaṣṭādyavayavaprāgabhāvakatve satyanutpannadhvaṃsāntyāvayavakatvamityarthaḥ| bhāvibhūtakriyayoratiprasaṅgavāraṇāya krameṇa pada(dala)dūyam| lakṣye lakṣaṇaṃ grāhayati-pacatīti| adhiśrayaṇādīti sādhyatvaprakārakapratītiviṣayapūrvāparībhūtāvayavasamudāyasyaiva kriyātvāditi bhāvaḥ|
tadīti uktavarttamānatvamityarthaḥ| yadyapi bhāva-kāla-kāraka-saṃkhyāścatvāro'rthā ākhyātasyeti niruktabhāṣyāt kriyātiriktakālasyaiva varttamānatvādikaṃ pratīyate, tathāpi nirupādhikasya tasaya varttamānatvādinā pratyetumaśakyatvādupādhibhūtakriyāmādāyaiva tatra tattvasya vakkavyatayā''vaśyakatvāt kriyāyāmeva tattvamuktamityavadheyam|
vastutasyu daṇḍamuhūrttadyātmakaḥ khaṇḍakāla eva varttamānatvādinā laḍādivācyaḥ| tadgataṃ varttamānatvaṃ ca tattacchabdaprabogādhikaraṇtvameva| `tadādi'nyāyena śabdaprayogādhikaramatvenānugamācca na śaktyānantyam|
yadyevamapi tattatkālatvena tattatkabodhasyānubhavasiddhānupapattiriti vibhāvyate tadāstu śabdaprayogādhikaramavṛttikālatvavyāpyadharmatvenopakṣitatattatkālatvāvacchinna eva laṭaḥ śaktiḥ| kriyārambhāt pūrvvaṃ samāptyuttaraṃ cādhīte pacatītyādiprayogāpattistu svavṛttiprāgabhāvāpratiyogivṛttitva-svavṛttidhvaṃsāpratiyogiprakṛtakriyānuyogikatvābhyāṃ viśeṣitena ādheyatāsambandhenātvayaniyamopagamena vāraṇīyā|
yatra tu akhaṇḍakriyaiva dhātvarthastatra suddhādheyatāsambandhenaiva tadanvayānnā''stītyāda doṣa iti| bhūtabhaviṣyadbhinnatvaṃ vā ityatra vākāro'nāsthāyām, pūrvalakṣaṇāpekṣayā'sya gurutvādbhaviṣyattvasyāpi varttamānatvaghaṭitatvena tadgahe, bhaviṣyatvagrahaḥ, bhaviṣyatvagrahe ca tadavaccinnabhinnatvarūpavarttmānatvagraha ityanyo'nyāśrayācca| evaṃ bhūtatvamādāyāpyanyonyāśrayo draṣṭavyaḥ|
nanu yatra sattāśrayatvādīnāṃ dhātvarthatvaṃ tatrāgatiḥ, teṣāmakhaṇḍatvenāvayavaghaṭitoktalakṣaṇavarttamānatvāsambhavādata āha-ātmā'stīti| anityatvāditi sāvayavatvena prāgabhāvapratiyogitve sati dhvaṃsapratiyogitvādityarthaḥ| tadviśiṣṭasya anityarājakriyādiviśiṣṭasattādirūpārthasyetyarathaḥ| tathāca kevalasattādirūpārthe tadasattve'pyuktaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭe tasmin viśeṣaṇāṃśamādāya tatsattvaṃ nānupannamiti bhāvaḥ|
yadvā tadviśiṣṭetyatra tadā avyavahitatvānityatvasyaiva paramarśāt tādṛśakriyāgataṃ varttamānatvamāropya sattādyarthako laḍityarthaḥ| tatra svotprekṣitatvaṃ nirasyati--uktaṃ hīti| tiṣṭhateradhikaraṇamiti ādheyatāsambandhena tādṛśakriyātmakakālaviśiṣṭatvāt(tasya)tāsāmiti bhāvaḥ| anena bhāṣyeṇa, vastutastvityādinā uktakalpasyaiva sādhutvaṃ labhyate| tasminnuktalakṣaṇalakṣitakālasyaiva sarvatra kriyāyāmādheyatayānvayalābhat|
? parato bhidyate iti| sarvam pacyādidhātvarthasāmānyaṃ parataḥ-svāpekṣayā anyena varttamāna vādinā| bhidyate-vyāvartyate| ātmā tu ityatrātmādipadamātmasattādyarthakam, na vikampate sanātanatvena niravayavatvāduktaviśeṣaṇasambandhābhāvena bhedānumitiviṣyo na bhavatītyarthaḥ| tathācā''mā'stīti prayogānupapattiriti pūrvārddharthaḥ| samādhatte-tasmāditi viśeṣātiriktapadārthasya svato vyāvṛttatvāsambhavādityarthaḥ| parvatādītyādinā''tmādiparigrahaḥ|
pararūpeṇeti parasya-rājakriyādirūpārthasya, rūpeṇa-dharmeṇa varttamānatvādinā bhidyate ityukta evārthaḥ| tama ityādi tama āsīdityādayo bhūtakriyāgatabhūtatvāropāt| bhaviṣyāmi ceti bhāvikriyāgatabhaviṣyatvāropācca yojyā ityarthaḥ| pakṣadvayasyaivā''karārūḍhatāṃ pradarsayan pāthamadyotakatvapakṣe upapattimāha-tacceti| kriyāsāmānyeti kālasāmānyavṛttikriyāvācakasyetyarthaḥ|
tadviśiṣṭe varttamānakāladiviśiṣṭe, varttamānatvādiviśiṣṭe vā vyāpāre ityarthaḥ| laḍāderityādinā liḍādiparigrahaḥ|
upayogāditi sa ca prāk pratipāditaḥ| tathāca laḍādyadeśatvena tibādīnāṃ varttamānatvādidyotakatvānna `apākṣīt' ityādau varttamānakriyāpratītiḥ, kriyāsāmānyaśaktadhātostattadviśiṣṭe lakṣaṇayaiva tattadviśiṣṭakriyābodhopapattau pṛthagvarttamānatvādau teṣāṃ śaktikalpane gauravāditi bhāvaḥ| vācakatvapakṣamavalambyā''ha anvayavyatirekābhyāmityādi|
naca `takrakauṇḍinya'nyāyāt kartrādyarthabādhaḥ, śaktatā'vacchedakabhedāt| kiñcaivaṃ "laḥ karmaṇi" iti sūtraṃ niravakāśaṃ syāt, sthalāntare'pi vidhyādyarthairbādhasambhavāditi bhāvaḥ| anyathā anvayavyatirekayoḥ śaktayagrāhakatve| vilopāpattiriti sarvatra prakṛtyaiva tattatpratyayārthatvābhimatabodhasambhavāditi bhāvaḥ| adhikaṃ subarthanirṇaye vakṣyate|
anadyatane bhūte iti "bhūte" iti, "anadyatena laṅ"  iti sūtrābhyāṃ tattatpadānuvṛtteriti bhāvaḥ| na liṭ‌prayoga iti uktārthasya bādhāt papācetyādijabodhānantaramanadyatanātvādisandehānudayād bhūtatvādivattayorapi tadvākyajabodhe kriyāṃśe viśeṣaṇatayā bhānamabhyupeyam| tacca vācakatāṃ dyotakatāṃ vā vinā'nupapannamiti liṭastadabhyupagama āvaśyaka iti bhāvaḥ|
nanu parokṣatvaṃ pratyakṣānyajñānaviṣayatvam| tathāca pratyakṣakriyāyāmapi śrutānumitatvayoḥ sattvāt papācetyādiprayogāpattirata āha-parokṣatvañceti yathāśrutapratyakṣāviṣayatvasyopanītakriyāyāmabhāvena tatra papācetyādiprayogānupapattirata āha-sākṣātkaromīti sākṣātkaromītyetatpratītisākṣikalaukikaviṣayatvābhāvavatvamityarthaḥ|
uktasthale pratyakṣaviṣayatāsattve'pi tādṛśavilakṣaṇaviṣayatvābhāvavattvamakṣuṇṇamiti na tadanupapattiḥ| pratyakṣaṃ tādṛśaśabdaprayokturgrāhyam| nanu `daśamastvamasi' ityādau śabdādapyaparokṣajñānasya dṛṣṭatvena śabdajanyatādṛśajñānaviṣayendriyāsambandhakriyāyāṃ liḍanupapattiriti cenna, tatrāpi śābdajñānasahakṛtamanasaiva daśamasākṣātkāraḥ, natu śabdāt, laukikaviṣayatāniyāmakendriyasaṃyogāderabhāvena śābde tadvattvāsambhavāt| ata eva `tattvamasi' ityādyacāryyopadeśaśamadamādisaṃskṛtaṃ mana ātmadarsane kāraṇamiti gītābhāṣye uktamiti sarvaṃ samañjasam|
"kriyā nāmeyam" iti bhāṣye sā'sāvanumānagamyeti śeṣaḥ| kriyā sādhyatvena pratīyamānā| iyam pacatītyādivyavahāriviṣayībhūtā, yataḥ pūrvāparībhūtā-kramikānekavyāpāramamūhātmikā, ato'tyantāparadṛṣṭā-pareṇa-pratyakṣapramāṇenātyantamadṛṣṭā-tajjanyajñānāviṣayā iti yāvat| piṇḍībhūteti avayavānāmāśuvināśitvena piṇḍasyaivābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ| nanu tarhi tasyāṃ kiṃ pramāṇamata āha-sākṣāditi viklittyādirūpakāryānumeyeti| anyatheti kriyāyā avayavaśo'pi sākṣātkārānabhyupagama ityarthaḥ| pratibhātīti uktārtha evāsmad‌buddhiviṣayo bhavatītyarthaḥ|
nanu kriyā'vayavā āpi kriyāvadatīndriyā eva| yathā pacerudakasecanādayo'vayavāstathā teṣāmapyavayavāste'pi samūharupeṇaiva dhātuvācyāḥ| yastu kṣaṇāvacchinnastadrūpo'vayavaḥ sa tu na dhātuvācyaḥ, kintvavayavasamūhā''tmanaiva| āśritakramarūpatvāttadvācyasya| evañca sadasadrūpā kriyā na sadviṣayendriyagrāhyā| taduktam-
kramāt sadasatāṃ teṣāmātmanā na samūhinaḥ|
sadvastuviṣayairyānti samban‌dhaṃ cakṣurādibhiḥ| iti|
atyantāpadṛṣṭeti bhāṣyasyāpyavayavarūpeṇa samūhātmanā cādṛśṭetyevārthaḥ| ata eva "varttamāne laṭ" iti sūtre bhāṣye, asti varttamāno kālaḥ, parantvā''dityagativannopalabhyate" ityuktam, tasya pratyakṣeṇeti śeṣaḥ|
evañca varttamānatvākrāntakriyāṇāṃ spaṣṭamevāpratyakṣatvaṃ tata upalabhyate| paśya mṛgo dhāvatītyādau tu dṛśirjñānārthakaḥ, dhāvatirvā phalapara iti na kaściddoṣaḥ| kiñca avayavasya pratyakṣatve prakṛtaḥ kaṭamityādāvavayavātītatvena samudāyātītatvamādāya ktādyupapādanavadavayavapratyakṣatvena samudāyapratyakṣatvamādāya `parokṣe' ityasya cāritārthye bhāṣyasthapūrvapakṣasya nirddalatvāpattirityaparitoṣānmatāntaramāha-vyāpārāviṣṭanāmiti evañcātītānadyatanavyāpārāviṣṭaparokṣakartṛkakriyāvṛttāddhātorliḍiti "parokṣe liṭ"  iti sūtrārthaḥ|
ayaṃ papāceti tadārni karturapārokṣye'pi vyāpāradaśāyāṃ pārokṣyasattvāduktaprayogopapattiriti bhāvaḥ| ityapi vadantīti matāntarānādare atrāpiḥ, pūrvoktarthasyā''pātaramaṇīyatvaṃ manvānā bhāṣyā'nusāriṇa iti śeṣaḥ|
atrāhurnaiyāyikāḥ-pūrvā'parībhūtā'vayavasamudāyātmakapāribhāṣikakriyāyā apratyakṣatvaṃ yuktameva, samūhasyaivābhāvāt, piṇḍībhūteti bāṣyasvārasyena samūhasyaiva tattvāvagateśca| tadavayavānāmapi pārokṣyaṃ tu sarvānubhavaviruddham, tatra pratyakṣakāraṇacakṣuḥsaṃyuktasamavāyādeḥ sattvāt| anyathā ghaṭapustakāderapyapratyakṣatvā'pattiriti bahuvyakopaḥ. adhiśrayaṇaṃ sākṣātkaromīti sārvajanīnapratīteśca| pratīteḥ padārthāsādhakatve vyāvahāramātrocchadā''pattiśca| "kriyā nāmeyam" iti bhāṣyasyāpi iyam-sāvayavapacyādyartharūpetyarthānna tadvirodhaḥ| sadasatāṃ teṣāmityasya vidyamānāvidyamānāvayavarūpāṇāmityarthāt samūhātmakakriyāyāḥ pārokṣyapratipādanaparatvānna harivākyavirodho'pi| anyathā `kriyā sadasadrūpā' iti vadato vyāghātaḥ spaṣṭa eva|
kālasya pratyakṣatvābhāvena tadanuvādakabhāṣyopaṣṭambhanyāso'pi mānābhāvena nāvayavānāṃ pārokṣyopapādakaḥ| prakṛtaḥ kaṭamityatrāpyavayavātītatvamādāyaiva prayogopapattau samudāyātītatvārope mānābhāvena tad‌dṛṣṭāntāsaṅgatiśca| dārṣṭāntike'vayavapratyakṣatvena samudāyapratyakṣatvamāropya pūrvapakṣasya nirdalatvāpādānamapi svāgrahamūlakameva| ata eva bhāṣye paśya mṛgo dhāvatītyanena laukikaviṣayatāśālyarthakadṛśikarmatvaṃ dhāvateḥ pratipāditam| kathamanyathā āgārāntarasthitasya na tādṛśaprayogaḥ| mṛgakarttṛkadhāvane jñānaviṣayatvarūpajñānakarmatva'bādhāt| dhāvateḥ saṃyogarūpaphalārthakatve vyāpārāvācakatvābhāvādadhātutvaprasaṅgaśca| svajanakavyāpāragatapaurvāparyāropeṇa kriyātvopapādane cāpratyakṣatvapattitadavasthaiveti na kiñcidetat| tasmāt pūrvoktamatameva nirābādham| ata eva granthakṛtā'pi api-vadantibhyāṃ tatrāsvarasa eva sūcita iti| vyāsaṅgādineti yathā " matto'haṃ kila vilalāpa" ityatra madādinā mano'sannidhānāt kriyāpārokṣyaṃ tadvadityarthaḥ|
liḍasambhavāditi| atredaṃ cintyam-na vāstavaṃ pārokṣyādi liḍādiniyāmakam, `adhyāsta sarvarttusukhāmayodhyām' ityatra laṅo'sādhutāpatteḥ| kintu vaivakṣikaṃ tat, tathā vakṣyati ca svayameva sārakṛt-`anadyatanabhūtatvena vivakṣite laṅ, tatraiva pārokṣyavivakṣāyāṃ liṭ' iti| prakṛte cā'nāyāsaniṣpannatva-śīghraniṣpannatva-pratītiphalakabhūtānadyatanatvavivakṣāyāḥ, evaṃ sūkṣṇakālena kariṣyāmi yatra kāle mayā'pi sākṣātkarttumaśakyamiti pratītiphalakapārokṣyavivakṣāyāśca sattvāllijupapattisambhavanāditi|
anadyatam iti tītāvyavahitarātripaścārddhaviśiṣcā''gāmirātripūrvārddhopetaṃ dinamadyatanam, etadbhinnakālatvamanadyatanatvamiti vṛttikārādayaḥ| kaiyaṭastu-luṅasūtre'tītarātrerantyayāmenā''gāmirātrerādyayāmena sahito divaso'dyatana ityūce|
atredamavadheyam-anadyatanabhūte yadā bhūtatvamātravivakṣā tadā luṅeva| tena hyo'pākṣīdityādau bhūtatvamātravivakṣāyāṃ luṅi, paścād hyaḥpadena sambandhe nādyatanatvapratītiriti na luṅo'sādhutā| evamanadyatanabhaviṣyati bhaviṣyattvamātravivakṣayā lṛṭ| śvo yakṣyamāṇe-`devatā yakṣyati' ityatra paścāddhayanadyatanatvapratīririti|
pare tu sāṅgayāgasya dvayahātmakakālasādhyatvāttadadhikaraṇadvayahātmakakālasyānadyatanatvābhāvena luṭo'navasarāllaḍupapattiḥ| `adya prabhṛti me dāśa! brahmacaryyaṃ bhaviṣyati' itivadityāhuḥ|
nanu `ahamekaḥ prathamamāsaṃ varttāmi baviṣyāmi ca' ityatra dhātvarthasattāyāṃ varttamānaprāgabhāvapratiyogitvasya bhaviṣyattvasya bādhāt, tadarthakādbhaviṣyadarthakapratyayāprasaktirata āha tattvañceti varttamānaprāgabhāvapratiyogyutpattikasamayavattvamiti pāṭhaḥ| tathāca tadarthavyāpārādhikaraṇasya kālasyānityarājakriyā''dyātmakasya varttamānaprāgabhāvapratiyogitvarūpabhaviṣyattvasatt‌vāt tadādāyaiva lṛḍupapattiriti yathāśrutārthānusāriṇaḥ|
anye tu-nanu yeṣāṃ mate naśyatyādidhātūnāṃ nāśamātramarthasteṣāṃ bhaviṣyattvasya varttamānaprāgabhāvapratiyogitvarūpattve, śvo bhāvināśake paraśvo naṅkṣayatīti prayogāpattiḥ, varttamānaprāgabhāvapratiyogini tannāśe paraśvastanakālavṛttitvasattvāt|
naca paraśvastanakālasya bhaviṣyattvaghaṭakaprāgabhāve'nvayābhyupagamāt tasya ca tatprāgabhāve bādhānnoktaprayogāpattiriti vācyam, tathā sati śvo bhāvināśake'pi `śvo naṣṭā' iti prayogānupapattiḥ| tannāśaprāgabhāve śvastanakālasambandhabādhādata āha-tattvañceti atra samayapadopādānaṃ kālo laḍādyartha ityabhipretya, natu tasya lakṣaṇe niveśaḥ, prayojanābhāvād vidyamānaprāgabhāvapratiyogyutpattikatvasyaiva samyaktvāt| yatra tūtpattireva dhātvarthastatra tadaghaṭitameva tannirvaktavyam, anthotpatterdvedhā bhānaprasaṅgāt| evañcektasthale bhaviṣyattvaghaṭakotpattau paraśvastanakālavṛttitvabādhānnoktaprayogāpattirititthamavatārayanti| naiyatyādīnāṃ phalavyāpārobhayārthakatvavādināntu vyāpāre varttamānaprāgabhāvapratiyogitvābādhannānupapattiriti prāgovoktam|
atredamavadheyam-ihādya ghaṭo bhaviṣyatītyādau deśakālaviśeṣāderadhikaraṇatvena dhātvarthotpattāvevā'nvayaḥ| nanu bhaviṣyattvaghaṭakaprāgabhāve utpattimati vā, tathā sati kapālanāśajanyaghaṭanāśasthale, iha kapāle ghaṭadhvaṃso bhaviṣyatīti prayogā''patterghaṭarūpapratiyogyātmakaghaṭadhvaṃsaprāgabhāvasya kapāle sattvādevaṃ śvo bhāvinyadya bhaviṣyatītita prayogaprasaṅgaśca, adyatanakālavṛttitvasya prāgabhāve sattvāt| tathā bhavate śvaḥ samutpadya prāṅgaṇe paraśvo gamiṣyati maitre prāṅgaṇe paraśvo bhaviṣyatīti prayogā''pattiḥ, utpattimato maitrasya prāṅgaṇavṛttitvāt, paraśvastanakālavṛttitvācca| yathokte tu tasya paraśvastanakālavṛttitve'pyutpattestatkālavṛttitvānna kaścana doṣaḥ| `ghaṭo bhaviṣyati' ityādau utpattyarthakadhātuyoge utpattyaghaṭitabhaviṣyatvasyānvayo lāghavāt, utpattyaghaṭite tasminneva mṛdādeḥ śaktyabhyupagamāt, utpattighaṭitalakṣyārthasya tu `naṅkṣayati' caityādau nāśe'nvayādanyamate'pi nānupapatti-| evañca `bhaviṣyati' ityādau yautpatterdvedhābhānaprasaṅgaśaṅkāpi na| śābdabodhastvatra varttamānaprāgabhāvapratiyogyutpattyanukūlo ghaṭāśrayako vyāpāra iti vaiyākaraṇamate bodhaḥ|
vidhyādyāśiṣa iti āśīstu sambodhyahitaviṣayakaloḍādyantaśabdaprayoktricchā| bhavatu te iti hitaviṣayakamadicchāviṣayatvasambandhiśivaprasādakarttṛkaṃ bhavanamiti bodhaḥ| etayoriti leṭ‌loṭorityarthaḥ trayāṇām  leṭ‌loṭ‌liṅāmityarthaḥ|  tannirṇayenaiveti vakṣyamāṇaliṅ‌rthanirṇayenaivetyarthaḥ  hyaḥpadasyāvyavahitātītadivase rūḍhatayā tadātmakakālasya laṅarthatvenaikadināntaritabhūte laṅanupapattirata āha anadyatana iti tathāca hyaḥpadaṃ lakṣaṇayā'nadyatanatvenānadyatanamātrabodhakamiti noktadoṣāvakāsaḥ| evaṃ śvo bhāvinītyatrāpi bodhyam|
asya patra iti varttamānadhvaṃsapratiyogyanadyatanakālavṛttiretatsambandhiputrakarttṛkotpattyanukūlo vyāpāra iti bodhaḥ| adhyāstetyādau tu bhūtā'nadyatanatvamātravivakṣayā laṅupapattiḥ| sūtrād vidhyādīnāṃ liṅarthatvapratīteḥ kathaṃ preraṇādervidhyartatvamata āha tatreti teṣu madhye ityarthaḥ| vidhiḥ-preraṇamiti preraṇaparyāyo vidhiśabda ityarthaḥ| preraṇāya liṅādiśabdasvarūpatāṃ vadatāṃ mataṃ nirākarttṛ preraṇāyāḥ svarūpaṃ darśayati bhṛttyāderityādi|
ayambhāvaḥ-yadviṣayakatvena jñānasya pravṛttijanakatvaṃ saiva pravarttanā| tādṛśaviṣayatānirūpakatvaṃ ceṣṭasādhanatāyā eva tadviṣayakatvenaiva svaparajñānātpravṛtteḥ sarvasagmatatvāt| natu liṅādinām, tadviṣayakajñānāt pravṛttyanudayāt| sā yadi vaktrapekṣayā'pakṛṣṭoddeśyakapravarttakajñānaviṣayībhūtā tadā preraṇetyucyate| anyādṛk tu nimantraṇādipadavyapadesyā| tathāca svābhilaṣitopāyājñānādapravṛttāpakṛṣṭaprayojyasyopāyaviṣayakapravṛttyanukūlo vyāpāraḥ preraṇeti paryavasyatīti| adhikamagre vakṣyate|
taū vaidikamudāharaṇam-`svargakāmo yajeta' ityādi| svargakāmābhinnakarttṛkayāgabhāvanā svargādīṣṭasādhanamiti vaiyākaraṇamate bodhaḥ| bhāvanāyāmiṣṭasādhanatvaṃ tu pratyāsattyā svargakāmasyaiva| tādṛśaścāhamiti abhisandhāya yāgādau niyojyasya pravṛttirnirvahati| laukikaṃ tu `jalamāhara' ityādi| bodaḥ svayamūhyaḥ|
nimantraṇādi svamatena vyācaṣṭe-niyogakaraṇamiti `vipraṃ nimantrayat' ityādito viprāśrayakapravṛttijanakabhāvanābodhena prakṛte niyogapadārthaḥ-pravṛttistatkaraṇam-tadanukūlo vyāpāra ityarthaḥ|
āvaśyaka iti śraddhabhojanādiśca tathā preraṇeti pravarttanetyarthaḥ| asya dauhitrāderityādiḥ| tatrāpravṛttasya pravṛttyanukūlavyāpāra iti yāvat| yathā `iha bhuñjīta bhavātan' ityādi|  kāmacārānujñeti svecchayā pravṛttasyetaratra pravṛttipratibandhaphalakasvabhilaṣitaviṣayakapravṛttyanukūlo vyāpāra ityartha-| yathā `ihāsītā''rabdhaṃ kuruta tatkuruṣva yathā hitam' itāyadi| upaveśānādivyāpāra iṣṭasādhanatājñānāt pravṛttāvitaratrāpravṛttirarthataḥ phalati| satkārapūrvako vyāpāra iti adhyāpanādivyāpāre sammānapūrvakaprakṛttyanukūlo vyāpāro'dhīṣṭapadārtha ityarthaḥ| a(i)yamevā'bhyarthanenetyucyate| yathā `māṇavakamadhyāpayed bhavādan iti| ukteṣviṣṭasādhanatve anukūlatvañca pravṛttijanakatā'vacchedakakoṭipa3viṣṭatayā bodhyam|
sampradhāraṇamiti upasthitakriyayormadhye ekatarakriyādharmikeṣṭasādhanatvanirṇayecchetyarthaḥ| yathā kiṃ bho vedamadhīyīyota tarkamiti| svoddeśyakatvasambandhena sambodhyaviśiṣṭa mannaṣṭhā ca vedādhyayana-tarkādhyayanānyatarabhāvaneṣṭasādhanatvaprakārakanirṇayecchāviṣaya iti bodhaḥ| evamagre'pi| vidhiḥ preraṇamiti preraṇam-pravarttanamityarthaḥ| apravṛttaprayojyasyājñātasvabhilaṣitopāya iṣṭasādhanatābodhanamiti yāvat| apakṛṣaṭoddeśyakatādṛśeṣṭasādhanatvabodhaviṣayāprayuktaṃ liṅādighaṭitaṃ vākyamevā''jñopadeśapadābhyāṃ vyāpadiśyeta| tadevā''ha-bhṛtyāderiti ādinā śiṣyādiparigrahaḥ| tatra laukikamudāharaṇam-bhavāñjalamānayedityādi| vaidikam-svargakāmo yajetetyādi| niyogakaraṇamiti nimantryate-niyujyate'neneti vyutpattyā niyogānukūlo vyāpāro nimantrṇapadārtha ityarthaḥ|
(āvaśyaka iti śraddhabhojanādāvityartha-| preraṇetyasya dauhitrāderityādiḥ| tathācāvaśyake śraddhabhojanādau dauhitrāderiṣṭasādhanatābodhanaṃ nimantrṇamiti phalitam| yatheha bhuñjīta bhavāniti| kāmacāreti svābhilaṣite kāmacāreṇa pravṛttasyeṣṭasādhanātābodhanamityarthaḥ| yathehāsīteti anujñātuḥ pravṛttiprayojanasyetarapravṛttipratibandhenaitatpravṛttiviṣaya iṣṭasādhanatābodhanam| yathārabdhaṃ kuruta, tatkuruṣva yathā hitamiti ca| etadādi sarvopalakṣaṇam|
katkārapūrvako vyāpāra iti sammānapūrvakamadhyāpanādivyāpāra iṣṭasādhanatābodhanamityarthaḥ| ayameva cā'bhyarthaneti vyāvahriyat| udāharaṇam-māṇavakamadhyāpayedbhavāniti|) sampraśraḥ-sampradhāraṇam| prārthanā tu svābhilaṣitavastudānādau svīyecchābodhanm| yathā-bhojanaṃ labheyeti| etaccatuṣṭayeti vidhyādicatuṣṭayetyarthaḥ| lāghavāditi tasya śakyatā'vacchedakatvakalpane lāghavādityartha-| vidhivākyādvidhyādīnāṃ bodhasya sarvasiddhatve'pi tattadrūpasya śakyatāvacchedakatvakalpane gauravāduktānugatarūpasyaiva śakyatā'vcacheaidakatvamucitamiti bhāvaḥ| tatra harisammatimāha-uktañceti| kimbhedasyeti tattadrūpeṇa (śaktibhedasya) pārthakyasyetyarthaḥ, tattadrūpeṇa śakteranaṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ| tarhi tathopādānaṃ sūtrakṛtā kimiti kṛtamata āha-nyāyeti nyāyaḥ-śaktiḥ, tad‌vyutpattaye tad‌grahāyetyarthaḥ|
nanu pravarttanātvena pravarttanāyāṃ śaktigraho vidhyādisamasastadharmika evetyata āha-prapañcārthamiti pravarttanā-pravṛttikaraṇamiti vyutpatterṇyantāda yuci niṣpannapravarttanāśabdāt pravṛttyanukūlavyāpāralābha ityāha pravartanātvañceti| viṣayatā'vaccedakatvamīti prakāratā'vchedakatvamityarthaḥ|
tacceti niruktā'vacchedakatvañcetyarthaḥ|
tadeveti iṣṭasādhanatvamevetyarthaḥ| evakāreṇa kṛtisādhyatvādivyavacchedaḥ| iṣṭatvañca samabhivyāhṛtapadopastāpitakāmanāviṣayatvam| svargakāmo yajetetyatra svargakāmapadopasthāpitakāmanāviṣayatvasya svarge sattvādiṣṭatvaṃ tasyākṣatam| tenaiva ca svargādīṣṭānāmanugamaḥ| yadi ca sāmānyata iṣṭasādhanatvajñāne pravṛttyanudayāt tattadrūpeṇa svargatvādinā svargasādhanatājñānaṃ tathā vācyam, tathāca tena rūpeṇeṣṭasādanatvasya śruteranavagamadvidhivākyāt kathaṃ pravarttakajñānanirvāha iti vibhāvyate tarhiṣṭatā'vacchedakatvena svargatvādīnanugamayya tadavacchinnasādhanatve vidheḥ śaktirūpagantavyā, evañca na vidhernānārthatvamapi, tadādivat| iṣṭatā'vacchedakatvasyopalakṣaṇatvopagamācca na svargatvādīnāṃ tadrūpeṇa bhānā''pattiḥ|
atha `svargakāmo jyotiṣṭomena yajeta' ityatra śrutyā svargasādhanatvaṃ jyotiṣṭome bodhanīyam, tacca na sambhavati, vājapeyādapi svargotpattervyabhicārāt vaijātyasya cottarakālakalpyatvena pūrvamanupasthitatvāditi cenna, `ghaṭaṃ dravyatvenaiva jānāti' ityādau ghaṭatvopalakṣitatad‌vyaktiviśeṣyakadravyatvaprakārakajñānāvagamavat svargatvāśrayayatkiñcid‌vyaktisamānādhikaraṇābhāvapratiyogitvameva śrutyā yāgo'vamyate| athavā yāgānuttarakālavṛttitvameva svargatvasāmānādikaraṇyeneti na kaściddoṣaḥ| ata eva sādhyeṣṭakatvasya vidhyarthatvamāmananti| tatra anyathāsiddhatvaṃ tu vidheḥ pravṛttitātparyyakatvāvadhāraṇāduttarakālakalpyam|
itthañca grahaṇaśrāddhādau nityatva-naimittikatvayoriva nityaśrāddhasandhyādau nityatvakāmyatvayorapyavirodhāllāghavādrātrisatranyāyena-
dadyādaharahaḥ śrāddhaṃ pitṛbhyaḥ prītimāvahan|
sandhyāmupāsate ye tu satataṃ saṃśitavratāḥ|
vidhūtapāpāste yānti brahmalokamanāmayam||
ityādyarthavādopasthitapitṛtṛptibrahmalokā'vāptyādikameva phalaṃ kalpyate| yadyapyanyatra samāvāyaghaṭitasāmānādhikaraṇyenaiva yāgṛsvargayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ klṛptastathāpyuktavākyaprāmāṇyācchrādvapitṛtṛptyoruddeśyatvasamāvāyaghaṭitasāmānādhikaraṇyena sa kalpanīyaḥ| karttṛniṣṭhādṛṣṭasya tu na pitṛtṛptijanakatvam, vyādhikāraṇatvāt, śrāddhānantarakṛtakarmanāśājalasparśādinā tannāśena pitrādisvargāpatteśca| pitṝṇāṃ muktatve nu anuṣṭhātaryye va tatkalpyatām| ata eva śāstrā''deśitaṃ phalamanuṣṭhātarītyutsarga iti vṛddhāḥ|
svataḥ puruṣārtho duḥkhaprāgabhāvaḥ, pratyavāyānutpādo vā tathā kalpanīyaḥ| tatsādhanatvañca yogakṣemasādhāraṇam| nitye kṛte pratyavāyānutpādaniyamāt, tatsattve duḥkhaprāgabhāvāvaśyambhāvācca| tathāca sarvaśaktau pravṛttiḥ syāt tathā bhūtopadeśāditi sarvaśaktayadhikaraṇapūrvapakṣasūtre pūruṣārthasya bhāvyasyobhayatra taulyena yathā kāmyaṃ phālayaṃ alaṃ tathā nityamapiti vyākhyātaṃ bhāṭṭaiḥ|
nanviṣṭasādhanatvasya vidhyarthatve sādhanatvasyā''khayātenā'bhidhānājjayotiṣṭomenetyatra tṛtīyā'nupapattiriti cenna, `dravyaṃ kārakam' iti pakṣe śaṅkā'navakāśāt| `śktiḥ kārakam' iti pakṣe'pi yajyarthavyāpāraniṣṭhabhāvanābhāvyanirvarttakatvarūpakaraṇatvasyabhidhāne'pi yāganiṣṭhasya tasyānabhidhānaṃna tatsamānādhikaraṇajyotiṣṭomapadāttadupapatteḥ| `na kalañjaṃ bhakṣayet' ityāderniṣedhavidhitvopapattaye nañ‌masabhivyāhāre vidheraniṣṭasādhantave śktirūpagantavyā| tasyāpi dhātvarthe'nvayaḥ| nañ tātparyyagrāhakaḥ| kalañjakarmakabhakṣaṇamaniṣṭasādhanamiti tu bodhaḥ|
yadvā nañarthābāvaviśeṣyaka eva bodhaḥ, nañā vidhernivṛttitātparyakatvāvadhāraṇāt| tena ca svapa3tīyoginyaniṣṭasādhanatvākṣepāttato nivṛttyupapattiriti bhāvaḥ| balavadaniṣṭājanake iṣṭasādhane candramaṇḍalānayane balavadaniṣṭānanubandhini sādhye niṣphale, tṛptirūpeṣṭasādhane kṛtisādhyamadhuviṣasaṃpṛktā'nnabhejane ca pravṛttyadarśanāt pravarttakajñāne viṣayatā'vacchedakakodau teṣāṃ niveśasyāvaśyakatayā vidhivācyatvaṃ śruteḥ pravarttakatvanirvāhārthamāvasyakamiti naiyāyikamataṃ nirākartumāśaṅkete-yadyapīti| etat pravartakajñānaviṣayatā'vacchedakatvam| tatstave yuktimāha tajjñānasyeti kṛtyasādhye'pi tajjñānāt pravṛttidarśanāditi bhāvaḥ| nirācaṣṭe-tathāpīti|
lokata eveti laukikapramāṇādevetyarthaḥ| na tacchakyamiti `ananyalabhyo hi śabdārthaḥ, iti nyāyāditi bhāvaḥ| vastutastu uktanyāyasya padāntaralabhyaiva śaktiḥ, anyathā kasyāpi padasya śaktirna sidhdyet sarvasyāpi śabdātiriktapramāṇagamyatvāt| kiñca, iṣṭasādhanatvādīnāṃ svātantryeṇa yadi śakyatvaṃ syāt tadā syādapyuktarīrirnirākaraṇakṣamā kintu pravarttakajñānaviṣayatā'vacchedakatvopalakṣaṇānugatīkṛteṣu teṣu sā| upalakṣaṇasya ca kṛtīsādhyatvāsādhāraṇatayā tatrāpi sidhyantī śaktiḥ kena pratiṣedhdyeti santa evā'dhārayantu|
yattu kṛtisādhyatve vedādavagate'pyaśaktasya yāgādau pravṛttyanudayāt madaṃśaviśiṣṭatajjñānasyaiva pravṛttihetutvaṃ vācyam| tasya ca `yāgo matkṛtisādhyaḥ' matkṛtisādhyatvavirodhidharmānadhikaraṇatvāt matkṛtisasādhyapākavat, ityanumnenaivāvagamānnāsya śakyatvamityaśaya iti tanna, iṣṭasādhanatājñānasyā'pi madaṃśaviṣayakasyaiva pravarttakatayā tasya vidhivākyādalābheneṣṭasādhanatve śaktayabhyupagame'pi pravarttakatvānupapatterapahihārāt|
yadi tu pratipuruṣaṃ vidherāvṛttyā bodhakatvāttenaiva tatpuruṣīyeṣṭasādhanabodhakatvātpravartakatvopapattau nānumānāvaśyakatvamiti vibhāvyate tathā kṛtisādhyatve'pi tulyamiti balavadaniṣṭānanubandhitvasya vidhyaśaktatve, "na kalañjaṃ bhakṣayet" ityatra bhakṣayedityasya bhakṣaṇe'niṣṭasādhanatvābodhakatayā niṣedhavidhitvānupapattiriti tatrāpi vidhiśaktirabhyupeyā| aniṣṭe balavattvaṃ ceṣṭotpattināntarīyakaduḥkhātiriktatvam| tena nā''yāsasādhye karmāṇi pravṛttyanupapattiḥ| `kaṣṭaṃma karma' iti nyāyena sukhamātrajanakakarmāsambhavacca noktārthasya siddhiḥ| yadi ca kadācitttsambhavastadeṣṭotpattyanāntarīyakatvameva tadvācyam|
etena balavadiniṣṭānanubandhitvajñānasya pravṛttekatvamate (meva) brahvāyāsasādhye karmaṇi pravṛttyanāpatteḥ, bahutaraduḥkhasya jāyamānatvādalpaduḥkhasyāpi kutaścit balavattvenānugatabalavattvasya nirvaktumaśakyatt‌vācceti na (kvi) tasya vidhiśakyatvsambhāvaneti keṣāñcit pralapitaṃ pratyuktam|
athoktayuktayā'niṣṭatvenāniṣṭasādhanatvābhāvajñānasya na pravarttakatvam, kintu `agamyāṃ na gacchet' `śākaṃ na bhūñjita' ityādivākyānāṃ prāmāṇyopapattaye viśaṣya narakatvādirūpeṇa tadasādhanatvasya jñānasya tathātvaṃ vācyam| tathāca śaktayā''nantyam, śaktigrahā'nupapattiśceti cenna, dveṣaviṣayatā'vacchedakatvopalakṣitanarakatvādyavacachinnasādhanatātvāvattchinnā'bhāva kūṭe tādṛśā'bhāvatvenānugate ekaiva vidhipratyayasya śaktiḥ| upalakṣaṇatvādeva ca dveṣaviṣayatā'vacchedakatvaṃ parityajya narakatvādyavacchinnā'sādhanatvamātraṃ pratīyate| kvacicca narakāsādhanatvam, kvacidbhogā'sādhanatvaṃ pratīyate ityatra tātparyameva niyāmakam|
nacātiprasaktasyopalakṣaṇattvābhyupagame prameyatvasyaiva tathātvamastu, sāmyāditi vācyam, sukhādyasādhanatvasya vidhyarthatāviraheṇa tatsādhāraṇaprameyatvasyopalakṣaṇatvāsambhavāt| atyantājanakasādhāraṇarūpasya kāraṇatāvacchedakatvavad (a) dviṣṭasādhāraṇarūpasyāpi dviṣṭopalakṣaṇatākalpanānaucityāt|tātparyābhāvādeva sukhādyasādhanatābodhānāpattau tasya vidhyarthatve'pi na kṣatiriti tu na yuktam, tathā sati tu tattātparyeṇā''dhunikānāṃ na bhuñjīteti prayogāpatteḥ|
tasmād dvaṣaviṣayatāvacchedakatvopalakṣitanarakatvādyavacchinna sādhanatātvāvacchinnapratiyogitākābhāvatvenānugate tādṛśābhāve vidhipratyayasya śaktiriti pariṣkṛtaṃ prācīnanaiyāyikamataṃ navyarītimavalambya dūṣayati-balavadaniṣṭeti niruktabalavadaniṣṭājanakatvājñāñcetyarthaḥ| na hetuḥ-na pravṛttihetuḥ| kvacit tathaiva pāṭhaḥ| tathāca na tadviṣayasya vidhivācyatvamiti bhāvaḥ| tasya pravṛttyajanatve hetumāha-dveṣābhāveneti madhuviṣasaṃpṛktānnabhojane dviṣṭasādhanatājñānāt dveṣaviṣaye pravṛttivāraṇāya tadviṣayakapravṛttimprati tadviṣayakadveṣasya klṛptapratibandhakatayā pratibandhakābhāvavidhayā'vaśyaklṛptaniyatapūrvavarttitākadveṣābhāvenaivopapatto balavadaniṣṭāsādhanatvajñānasya na hetuttvam, tenānyathāsiddhaṃrityarathaḥ|
nanvanvayavyatirekasahacārajñānasya kāraṇatvasyobhayatra sāmyāt tenaivāsyānyathāsiddhirdurvāretyata āha āstiketi sa ca paralokāṅgīkāravān| tadanaṅgīkartturnarakasādhanājñānāsambhavāttadviśeṣaṇamupāttam| āstikasya narakasādhanatājñānena tatra dveṣāvaśyambhāvena pravṛttyasambhavādāha-kāmuketi| tattvañcotkaṭarāgavattvam| rāge utkaṭatvaṃ jātiviśeṣaḥ, viṣayitāviśeṣo vā| pravṛtterityasyāgamyāgamane ityādiḥ| dveṣābhāvadaśāyāmityanena dveṣasāmagrīvaikalyaṃ darśitam| tacca viṣayatayā dveṣamprati utkaṭecchāsāmagrīvirahaviśiṣṭadviṣṭasādhanatājñānasya hetutayā tādṛśasāmagryāḥ svātantryeṇa pratibandhakatayā bodhyam|
vyabhicārācceti na heturiti pūrveṇānvitam| ata eva "śyenenābhicaran yajeta" ityatra na vidhyarthabādhaḥ, balavadaniṣṭānanubandhitvajñānasyāpravarttakatvena tadviṣayasya tasya vidhyarthatvasyaivābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ| upasaṃharati-tasmāditi| iṣṭasādhanatvameva ityevakāreṇa kṛtisādhyatvabalavadaniṣṭānanubandhitvayorvyavacchedaḥ| ādyasya laukikapramāṇenaivāvagamādantyasya pravarttakajñānāviṣayatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
pravarttaneti liṅādyartha ityarthaḥ| iṣṭābhyupāyatvādityādi iṣṭasādhanatvāt| anyaḥ kṛtisādhyatvādiḥ| kriyāsu yāgādirūpāsu| puṃsāṃ pravarttako na jñānaniṣṭhapravṛttijanakatāyāṃ viṣayatayā'vacchedako natyarthaḥ| yajetetyādivākyāntargataliṅādiśakyo neti yāvat| co hetau| yataḥ pravṛttihetuṃ taddhetutāvacchedakameva pravarttanāpadena vyapadiśantītyarthaḥ|
pare tu-balavadaniṣṭānanubandhitvajñānasya dveṣābhāvenānyathāsiddhatayā'pravartakatve'pi kṛtisādhyatājñānasya pravarttakatāyāḥ sarvasiddhatvena tadviṣayasya tasya vidhyarthatvamāvaśyakam| naca taddhetutāyāmeva mānābhāvaḥ, kṛtyasādhye pravṛttistu kṛtyasādhyatayājñānasya pratibandhakatayā tatra tadabhāvādeva neti vācyam, kṛtyasādhyatājñānasya pratibandhakatve kṛtisādhyatvā'bhāvatad‌vyāpyatadavaccedakadharmadarśanādīnāṃ pratibandhakatvasya vaktavyatayā tāvadabhāvanāṃ pravṛttiṃ prati hetutā kalpanā'pekṣayā kṛtisādhyatājñānatvena hetutāyāṃ lāghavasya sphuṭataratvāt, kṛtisādhyatājñānābhāvasya sarvadā sattvena pravṛttyāpatteśca|
apica kṛtisādhyatājñānasya pravartakatve kṛtisādhyatvaprakārakapākaviśeṣyakajñānatvāvacchinnābhāvamānasaṃ prati pākādigocarapralṛttisāmagryāḥ pratibandhakatvaṃ na kalpyate iti lāghavam, tādṛśapravṛttisāmagrīkāle pākaviśeṣyakakṛtisādhyatvaprakārakanirṇayasyā''vaśyakatvena, tasya ca svarūpasata evoktajñānābhāvamānase klṛptapratibandhakatayaivopapatteḥ|
bhāvanmate tādṛśamānase tādṛśapravṛttisāmāgryāḥ, pratibandhakatvakalpanena gauravaṃ spaṣṭameva| evamaprāmāṇyaviśiṣṭapākaviśeṣyakakṛtisādhyatvaprakārakajñānavānahamiti mānase pākagocarapravṛttisāmāgryāḥ pratibandhakatvā'kalpanenāpi lāghavam| tādṛśaviśiṣṭaviṣayatāśālimānasasāmagrīkāle āprāmāṇyajñānāskanditoktajñānasyā''vaśyakatvenāprāmāṇyajñānānāskanditapākādiviśeṣyakakṛtisādhyatvaprakārakajñānaghaṭitatādṛśapravṛttisāmagrītādṛśamānasasāmagnyormelanasyaivābhāvāt|
bhavanmate kṛtisādhyatvaprakārakapākādiviśeṣyakajñānābhāvaghaṭitatādṛśpravṛttisāmagrītādṛśamānasasāmagnyormelane bādhakābhāvena tādṛśamānase tādṛśapravṛttisāmagnyāḥ pratibandhakatvasyāvaśyakalpanīyatvena gauravasyātisphuṭatvādanayaiva diśā'niṣṭasādhanatvajñānābhāvādīnāmapi hetutā niranīyetyāhuḥ|
guravastu-neṣṭasādhantave vidhyarthaḥ, kṣaṇikatayā'vagate yāge bodhayitumaśakyādevaṃ paramparāsādhanatvamapi dvārā'nupasthiteḥ śābdabodhaprayojakānvayaprayojakatvarūpavattvayogyatāyāstatrābhāvāt| sādhanatāsāmānyabodhastvekaviśeṣabodhe taditaraprakāraprakārakatvaniyamādasambhavaduktikaḥ| kiñca yāgo na kartavyatayā pratīyate kāmyādanyatkāmyāvyavahitapūrvavarttisādhanameva kāmī kartavyatayā'vaitītyasyānyatra klṛptatvāt| dvārībhūtaṃ kāmyāvyavahatapūrvavartisādhanamapūrvameva kāryyatvena śakyam|
kāryyatvañca kṛtyuddeśyatvam| tatra viśeṣaṇībhūtakṛterāśrayaviṣayayorākāṅkṣāyāṃ viṣayatayā yāgā āśrayatayā svargakāmaḥ sambadhdyate| sukhaduḥkhābhāvāderlaukikapramāṇenaivāpūrvasyāpi vedena kṛtyuddeśyatvabodhanādapauruṣeye tasminnaprāmāṇyaśaṅkānavakāśāt tasya tattvam| tathāca svargakāmo yajetetyatra svargakāmakṛtyuddeśyo yāgāviṣayako niyoga iti bodhaḥ|
atrāpūrvasya yāgaviṣayakakṛtyuddeśyatvameva yāgaviṣayakatvam| yāgasya tādṛśakṛtiviṣayatve puruṣasyā''śrayatve cānvayitā'vacchedakamapūrvakāraṇatvam| saiva yogyatā| yathā `ghaṭena jalamāhara' ityatra chidretaraghaṭatvaṃ yogyatāvacchedakopasthitirghaṭonetyādāvarthādhyāhārāt| prakṛte caupādānikapramāṇavaśāttacchak‌tyaiva| tacchaktayaivāśakyopasthityabhyupagame lakṣaṇoccheda iti na sāṃpratam, svaśaktayā'nvayabodhakasyaiva svopapādakasakalapadārthabodhakatvopagamāt|
naca `gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣaḥ' ityādau gaṅgādipadānāṃ svaśaktayānvayabodhakatvam, yena tadupapādakasakalapadārthabodhanāyaupādānikapramāṇāvakāśaḥ syāt| `ajahat‌svārthalakṣaṇā' tu nāsmānmatasiddhā| tathāca svīyatvena kāryyabodho niyojyaḥ, samabhivyāhṛtapadopasthāpitaphalakāmaśca tatheti kāmye svargakāmo niyojyaḥ|
evañca vedabodhitamapūrvamuddiśya pūruṣapravṛttyupapattau neṣṭasādhanatvaṃ vidhyartha-| icchāviṣayasādhanattvena tatra śaktau pravarttakajñānānirvāhaḥ, svargajanakatātvena śaktau nānārthatāpatteriti tanmatasya vikalpagrastatvācca|
apica nityanaimittikasle śucitvakālajīvino rāhūparāgavataśca mama sandhyāsnānaviṣayako niyoga iti bodhānna phalāpekṣā, bhavanmate tu `candrasūryyagrahe snāyāt' aharahaḥ sandhyāmupāsīt' ityādau phalāśravaṇādvidhyarthabādhaḥ prasajjayeta| mama tu vaidika karmaṇi vedabodhitamapūrvamuddiśyaiva pūruṣaḥ pravarttate, tasyaiva vedena puruṣārthatvabodhanāt| kāmye phalāvāptirānuṣaṅgikyeva| ata eva nitye'pi taduddeśena pravṛttirupapadyate ityāhuḥ|
tadapare tu na kṣamante, loke pravarttakatvena sarvasammataṃ yadiṣṭasādhanatvajñānaṃ tadviṣayeṣṭasādhanatve vidhiśaktikalpanayaiva vidheḥ pravarttakatvopapattau niyogasya vidhivācyatve mānābhāvāt, vedabodhitasvargamādhanatvānupapattyā hi tatkalpanāt| naca tasya pūrvamupasthitirasti yena tatra śaktigrahaḥ| ekaviśeṣabādhe sāmānyajñānasya taditaraprakārakatvaniyame'pi sākṣātsādhanatātiriktasādhanatātvena bodhe bādhakābhāvāt| tādṛśasādhanatve yogyatāvacchedakaṃ ca paramparāghaṭakamapūrvam| tathāca svargasādhanatvaśaktayaiva nirvāhe aupādānikapramāṇavaśādapūrvopasthitāvapi na tadvācyam| apūrvānupasthitau paramparāsādhanatvamapi kathaṃ vedo bodhayediti tvanupasthite'pūrve kathaṃ śaktigraha ityanena tulyayogakṣemam|
evaṃ kāmyāvyavahitāpūrvasādhanaṃ kāmī karttavyatayā'vaitītyapi na, kāmyasādhanatājñānasyaiva pravarttakatvānna tu tatrāvyāvahitatvanivaśo'pi, gauravānmānābhāvāccha| dṛśyate ca tṛptyarthinastatprayojake jalā''haraṇādau pravṛttiḥ| iṣṭasādhanatve yathā śaktigrahastathoktam| apūrvasyāśakyatave'pi yathā nityanaimittike pravṛttistathopapāditam|
pratyuta tatra tavaiva sā durupapadā, niṣphalatvāt, duḥkhaikaphalatvācca| pravṛttimātre iṣṭasādhanatājñānasya hetutvāvadhāraṇāt| pravṛttiviṣyasādhyatve sadīṣṭatvarūpasādhyatvamapi nāpūrvasya, yena taduddeśenāpi pravṛttiḥ sambhāvyeta| niṣphalatayā'vagate pravṛttistu śrutisahastreṇāpyutpādayitumaśakyeti vedabodhitatvānnitye pravṛttiriti chātrapratāraṇāmātram|
ata eva yajño dānaṃ tapaḥ karma' ityādinā'nirdiṣeṭo'pi pratyavāyaparihāraḥ svargādiphalamanuddiśya kriyamāṇānāmeṣāṃ phalaṃ bhavatītyarthakenāṣṭādaśādhyāye bhagavad‌gītāsu pāvanatvamuktam| kiccha pacettyādilaukikavidheriṣṭasādhanatvabodhakatvena pravarttakatvavad vaidikavidherapi tacchaktayaiva pravarttakatvāt jñānanirvāhe tasyanupasthitāpūrve śaktikalpanaṃ niṣpramāṇakameveti|
paretu-liṅādiśravaṇe `ayaṃ māṃ prerayati' iti niyamena pratīterabhidhānāmakaḥ preraṇāparaparyyāyo vyāpāro liṅādivācyaḥ| sā ca liṅgasaṅkhyānanvayitvād vyāpārapadena pravarttakaniṣṭhatvāt pravarttanāpadena ca vyāvahriyate| preraṇātvena tasyāḥ saviṣayatvamapi śakyatā'vacchedakaṃ ca preryyādidhātvarthatāvacchedakatayā siddhaṃ preraṇātvamakhaṇḍopādhiḥ|
sa ca vyāpāraḥ sambhavālloke pravarttakapuruṣaniṣṭho'bhiprāyaviśeṣa eva| tatpravartakatve ca liṅādyuccārayitṛtvam| vede tu prayokturasambhavālliṅādyantaśabdasvarūpa eva, pravarttakatvañca liṅādyuccārayitṛtve sati svatantratvam| rājādipreritapadāterliṅādyuccārayitṛtve'pi pravarttakatvenāvyāvahriyamāṇatvāt| svatantreti adviṣṭayā''ptoktayā ca tayā svaviṣaya iṣṭasādhatvānumānam| tataśca prayojyapravṛttiḥ| śatrūktādviṣaṃ bhuṅ‌kṣvetyato viṣabhojane iṣṭasādhanatvānavagate  āptoktayeti āptoktayāpi `viṣaṃ bhuṅ‌kṣva' `mā cāsya gṛhe bhukṅ‌thāḥ' ityādikayāpi tasminniṣṭasādhanatvānanumānāt adviṣṭeti tatra viṣabhejanaviṣayakābhiprāyasya tadīyadveṣaviśiṣṭatvānna vyabhicāraḥ|
bhavatu vā liṅādisamabhivyāhāre vyāpārāntarasyānanubhavāt sarvatra liṅdyantaśabdasvarūpaiva sā| ata eva "pṛcchatu māṃ bhavān' ityatra ṇic kasmānna bhavatīti praśne, akarttṛtvādityuttaram| sakriyapreraṇāyāṃ ṇic, niṣkriyapreraṇāyāṃ loḍādi| `tatprayojakaḥ' ityatra tatpadena vyāpārāviṣṭaḥ karttocyate iti tadbhāvaḥ|
athāpi kathañcit karttā syāt, evamapi na doṣaḥ, loṭoktatvāt preṣaṇasya ṇij na bhaviṣyatītyuktam| tata āhatyaiva preraṇāyā vidhyarthatvaṃ labhyate| vidhinimantreti sūtrabhāṣye'pi vidhiḥ-preraṇamityukteśca| tasmānneṣṭaḍasādhanatvādi vidhyarthaḥ|
kiñcedaṃ te iṣṭasādhanam, tasmāt tvaṃ kurviti svāramikavyāvahārādapi na tasya vidhyarthatvam, paunaruktayāpatteḥ, paunaruktayasya sarvatheṣṭasādhanatvabodhanatātparyyakatve'pi tasmādityasyānanvayāpatteścetyāhuḥ| sa ca prāyaśo bhaṭṭamatānuvāda eva| yato bhaṭṭamate liṅartho'bhidhākhyo vyāpāraḥ| sa ca loke prayoktṛpuruṣaniṣṭo'bhiprāyaviśeṣa evetyādi prāgvat| vede tvanyasyā'|sambhavālliṅagadyantaśabdaniṣṭha eva saḥ, samavetaścāta eva sā `śābdī bhāvanā' iti vyavahriyate|
bhāvanāyāḥ śabdārthabhedena dvaividhyādākhyātavācyā''rthoti vyavahriyate| bhāvanātvena liṅarthasābdabhāvanāyāḥ saviṣayatvamapi bhāvanāyā bhāvyakaraṇetikarttavyatārūpāṃśatrayaviśiṣṭatvādabhidhābhāvanāyāḥ saviṣayakatvenāvagatāyāḥ kiṃ bhāvayediti bhāvyākāṅkṣāyāṃ samānapratyopāttatvapratyāsattyāṃ'śatrayaviśiṣṭa svaviśayapravṛttirūpākhyātavācyārtho bhāvanā viṣayatvenopatiṣṭhate| iyameva sādhyākāṅkṣeti vyavahriyate, sādhyatākhyaviṣayatayaiva tasyāḥ saviṣayatvāt|
eva़ñca tasyāḥ sādhyatvāvagate keneti karaṇākāṅkṣāyāṃ liṅādipadajñānaṃ karaṇatvenā'nveti| karaṇatvañca bhāvanābhāvyanirvarttakatvameva, kuṭhārāderapi puruṣavyāpāranirvarttyacchidā nirvarttakatvenaiva karaṇatvāt| iyameva bhāvanā karaṇākāṅkṣetyucyate|
`kaṣṭaṃ karma' iti nyāyādupāye dveṣaṇā''lāsyādinā vā apravarttamānaṃ puruṣaṃ prati praśastaphalavattābodhanena dveṣādyapasāraṇenārthavādavākyajanyā prāśastyarūpā stutiḥ sahakārikāraṇamitīyamitikarttavyatetyucyate| evamārthobhāvanāyā apyaṃśatrayavaiśiṣṭayena tadavagatau kiṃ bhāvayedityākāṅkṣāyāṃ svargakāmādipadasanndhānāt svargo bhāvyatayā'nveti svargaṃ bhāvayediti| yadapi sa vyāpārastyāgaviṣayo'pi tathā'pi tasyā'sukharūpatayā'pūruṣārthatvena karaṇatvenaivopasthityā ca na bhāvyatvam|
ata eva tatsamānādhikaraṇajyotiṣṭomādipadāttṛtīyā| kenetyākāṅkṣāyāṃ yāgaḥ karaṇatvenā'nveti| karaṇatvaṃ tūktameva| sahakāryakāṅkṣārūpe kathaṃ bhāvākāṅkṣāyāṃ ca "samidho yajati" ityādivākyavagatasaminnāmakayāgādikriyājātamitikartavyatayā'nvetītyadhikamanyato'vadhārtyam| taduktam-
abhidhābhāvanāmāhuranyāmeva liṅādayaḥ|
arthātmabhāvanā tvanyā sā cākhyātasya gocaraḥ||
liṅo'bhidhā saiva ca śabādabhāvanā bhāvyā ca tasyāḥ puruṣapravṛttiḥ|
sambandhabodhaḥ karaṇaṃ tadīyaṃ prarocanā cā'ṅgatayā prayujyate|| iti|
ukto'rthaḥ| tatra pūrvoktamate bhaṭṭamatādiyān viśeṣaḥ-yadbhaṭṭamate'bhidhākhyo vaidikaliṅartho vyāpāraḥ śabdasamavetastadatiriktaśca| tasmiṃstu liṅādhyantaśabdātmaka eva lokavedasādhāraṇaśca| saviṣayakatvaṃ tu matadvaye'pyaviśiṣṭamiti|
atra vadanti-abhidhā-liṅādinirūpitā śaktireva, natu tadatiriktaḥ kaścana śabdasamaveto vyāpāraḥ, mānā'bhāvāt| vidheḥ pravarttakatvasya pūrvamupapādanāt tadanupapatterapyabhāvāt| anyathā śabdā'tiriktaḥ śabdasamaveta eko vyāpāraḥ kalpanīyaḥ| tasya ca svapravṛttau parapravṛttau vā kāraṇatvenā'klṛptasya tattvena kāraṇatvam| pravarttanātvena paraniṣṭhavyāpārabodhakatvena klṛptasya śabdasya svaniṣṭhavyāpārabodhakatvaṃ saviṣayakatvaṃ ca bhaṭṭaiḥ kalpaniyamiti gauravasya sphuṭatvācca|
preraṇāyā vidhyrathatve'pi sāmānyasya viśeṣe paryyavasānāt ko'sau vyāpāra iti viśeṣagaveṣaṇāyāṃ samīhitasādhanatvameva sa ityeva vaktumucitam| liṅastadarthakatvaṃ vinā pravarttakatvasyaivā'sāmbhavāt| preraṇāviṣayaḥ pāka iti bodhasyeṣṭasādhanatvādyaviṣayakasya pravarttakattvaṃ lokaviruddhameva| na khalvāgamasahastraprerito'pi sveṣṭasādhanatvamajñātvā pravartate kaścidityapi sarvasiddhameva| makhakaraṇasya svaniṣṭhavyāpāradvāraiva phalajanakatvamiti niyamastu nāstyeva vyāptijñānādai vyabhicārāt| tasmālliṅādijanyabodhaviṣayā'bhidhāyā iṣṭasādhanatvādijñānanirapekṣāyāḥ pravarttakatvaṃ niryuktikameva|
śabdarūpapreraṇāvādinā'pi tadviṣaya iṣsādhanatvānumitidvāraivoktayuktyā pravartkatvaṃ vācyam| tacca vaidikavidhisthale na sambhavati| "tasyaiva mahābhūtasya niśvasitametad yaddag‌vedo yajurvedaḥ sāmavedaḥ" iti śrutyupaṣṭambhena niḥśvasitavadvede tairjanyatvā'ṅgīkāreṇa yathārthajñānavaduccaritatvarūpā''ptoktatvarūpaviśeṣaṇavaikalyenoktānumānāpravṛtteḥ| evaṃ saviṣayatvamapyātmaviśeṣaguṇātirekiṇyāstasyā sarvatantraviruddham|
naca preraṇaiva svatantryeṇa pravṛttihetuḥ, svatantrapravṛttau vyābhicārāta, "sarvaime svabhūtyartha pravarttante" iti samīhitasādhanatājñānapravarttakatāpratipādakahetumaticetisūtrabhāṣyavirodhācca| "kaḥ prayojyasya preraṇayā'rtho yadabhiprāyeṣu sajjate" iti tatsūtrasthabhāṣyeṇa ṇijarthapreraṇāyā api svātantryeṇāpravarttakatvabodhanācceti vaidikavidheḥ preraṇā'rthakatvāsambhavena pravarttakatvānupapattyeṣṭasādhanatvā'rthakatvamevā'ṅgīkaraṇīyam|
evañca laukikavidherapi tacchaktyaiva vravarttakatvopapattau na tadartho'pi sā| abhiprāya eva tatra vidhyartha iti kalpastu vakṣyamāṇā''cāryamatanirāsenaiva nirastaḥ| vidhiḥ preraṇamiti bhāṣyaṃ tu preryyate pravartyate'neneti vyutpattyā pravarttakajñānaviṣayeṣṭasādhanatvaparam| jñānasya tadviṣayakatvenaiva pravarttakatvāt|
yadapi tadupaṣṭambhakatayā `pṛcchatu māṃ bhavān' ityādibhāṣyopanyasanaṃ tadapi nā'smākaṃ pratikūlam, yatastena pravarttakajñānajanakatvena pravarttanāpadavyapadeśyāyā loṅādyantaśabdaprayogarūpāyāḥ preraṇāyā ṇijarthatvaṃ pratipādyate, natu vidhyarthatvamapi, arthabhedasya darśitatvāt| akarttṛtvādityanenā'pi gamayatyādergacchantaṃ prerayatītivat, pṛcchatvityasya pṛcchantaṃ prerayatīti `vigrahasya'darśanena kartrasambandhadhātvarthaviṣayakapravṛttijanakajñānā'nukūlāyāstasyā ṇijarthatvā'bhāvapratīpādanāt tadvirodhābhāvāt|
athā'pītyādyuktistvekadeśinaḥ, vidhyarthakapratyayavakṛtyarthasya svābhāvyena bhaviṣyattayaiva pratīteḥ| tadānīṃ prayojyasya nirvyāpāratvena kartṛtvāsambhavāt, evaṃ loṭā preṣaṇāsya uktatvādityapi loṅarthapreṣaṇasya dhātvarthaviśeṣaṇatayaiva bhānāt tadviśeṣyatayā preraṇāvivakṣāyāṃ ṇico durvāratvāt, loḍantāddhātvarthaviśeṣyatayā preraṇāyā apratīteḥ pūrvapakṣasyaiva nirddalatāpatteśca| idaṃ te iṣṭasādhanamityādyuktistu sarvatheṣṭasādhanatvabodhanaparaiva, iṣṭasādhanatvasya pravarttanātvena vidhivāttyātvamiti pakṣe paunaruktyasambhavāt, naiyāyikamate kṛtyātmakavyāpārasya pacyādyarthatvābhāvena tanmate iṣṭasādhanatātveneṣṭasādhanatvasya vidhyarthattve paunaruktyāpādanānavakāśācceti|
vastutastu bhāṣye bahuṣu sthaleṣu preraṇāyā vidyarthakatvakathanāt tatprāmāṇyālliṅādyantaśabdaprayogarūpāyā eva tasyā vidhyarthatvamucitam| śrutestu "tasmādyajñātsarvahuta ṝcaḥ sāmāni jajñire" "dhātā yathāpūrvāmakalpayat" ityādiśrutibalāt pauruṣeyatvamabhyupetyaikarūpeṇaiva lokavedayostadviṣaye iṣṭasādhanatvā'numānena vidheḥ pravarttakatvaṃ nirvāhyamiti sarvaṃ caturastramiti||
ācāryastu-ātmā'bhiprāyo vidhyarthaḥ, pākaṃ kuryāḥ, pākaṃ kuryāmiti madhyamottamapuruṣayorliṅa icchāviśeṣātmakājñā'dhyeṣaṇānujñāpraśanaprārtanārthakatayā prathamapuruṣe'picchāyāmeva śakterucitatvāt| tatra bhayajanikecchā ājñā| adhyeṣaṇīye prayokturanugrahadyotikā sā'dhyeṣaṇā| niṣedhābhāvavyañjikecchā'nujñā| uttaraprayojikā tu praśnaḥ| prāptīcchā prārthaneti vivekaḥ| tathāca `svargakāmo yajeta' ityatra yāgaḥ svargakāmaniṣṭhatayāptecchāviṣaya iti bodhaḥ|
yadvā karttavyatvenecchaiva vidhyarthaḥ| tathāceṣcasādhanatvā'numānam| tadhyathā svargakāmasya mama yāga iṣṭasādhanam, karttavyatvenā''ptoktaliṅ‌bodhyecchāviṣayatvāt matpitraṣyamāṇamadbhojanavat| viṣabhojanāderapi kasyacitkṛtisādhyatayā karttavyatveneśvarūpāptecchāviṣayatvena tatreṣṭasādhanatvarūpasādhyābhāvāt vyābhicāravāraṇāya liṅ‌padabodhyeti| āptastu baidikasthale īśvara eva| tasmin vidhireva tāvat, kumāryā garbha iva puṃyoge mānamityāhuḥ|
tadapi na kṛtyasādhyaniṣphalā'niṣṭe pravṛttivāraṇāyeṣṭasādhanatādijñānahetutāyā āvasyakatvena tadviṣayeṣṭasādhanatvādīnāṃ vidhyarthatvopagamenaiva pravṛttinirvāhe āptabhiprāyasya vidhyarthatve mānābhāvāt| nahīṣṭasādhanatvajñānamantareṇāptecchāviṣayatvajñānamātrāt pravṛttiḥ kasyā'pi dṛśyate| svatantrapravṛttau vyabhicāreṇāptā'bhiprāyajñānasya pravartakatvā'sambhavāccetyalam| prakṛtamanusarāmaḥ| prapañcitañcaitaditi balavadaniṣṭā'nanubandhitvajñānasya pravartakatānirākaraṇamiṣṭasādhanatājñānasya pravartakatvā'vadhāraṇaṃ cetyarthaḥ| prapañcastūktaprāyaḥ|
ādineti preraṇādau cetyatropāttādiśabdenetyarthaḥ| yobrāhmaṇāyā'vagurediti yatkartṛko brāhmaṇoddeśyako vadhodyamastatkarmikā tādṛśavadhodyamajanyā śatasaṃvatsarayātaneti bodhaḥ, "hetuhetumatorliṅ‌"  ityanena prakṛtyarthayorbhāve liṅo vidhānāt| pravartakatvena nivartakatvena vā vedasya prāmāṇyāttena vadhodyamanivṛttiparatvagrahe tasmānnāvaguritavyamiti vidhiḥ kalpyata iti bhāvaḥ| mātrapadaṃ kṛtsnārthakamityabhiprāyavānāha-bhūtasāmānya iti tena viśeṣavivakṣāyāṃ laṅādīnāmavakāśaṃ sūcayati-bhūte luṅiti| `bhūte' ityādhikṛtya, luṅ' iti sūtrādityarthaḥ| nanu varttamānadhvaṃsapratiyogyutpattikatvaṃ bhūtatvam| ata eva ghaṭanāśasya vidyamānatādaśāyāmapi, vinaṣṭo ghaṭa iti prayogaḥ, tadutpattervartamānanāśapratiyogitvāt| tathāca yatrotpattireva dhātvrathastatrotpatterdvedhābhānaprasaṅgo'ta āha atreti bhūta iti sūtre ityarthaḥ. varttamāna ityādi| nacoktadoṣaḥ, asmanmate naśyatyādernāśānukūlavyāpārārthakatayā kālānvayasyāpi vyāpāra eva samarthitatvena ca tadaprasakterutpattyaghaṭitatvena dvedhābhānā'sambhavācca| vastutastu `ātmā āsīt' iti prayogopapattaye bhaviṣyattvavad bhūtatvasyāpi utpattighaṭitasya laṅarthatvāmāvaśyakamiti bodhyam| kriyāyāmiti phalānukūlāyāṃ tasyāmityarthaḥ| vidyamāne'pi ityapinā samānapratyayopāttatvapratyāsattyā''khyātārthakārake kālānvayopagama uktaprayogānupapattiḥ sūcyate|
naiyāyikāstu-utpattyaghaṭitatvena lāghavāduktārtha eva bhūtapadasya śaktiḥ| parantu naśyatyādau nāśātiriktasya vyāpārāntarasyānanubhavāttatrotpattighaṭite pratyayasya lakṣaṇā'vaśyakītyāhuḥ|
bhūte bhāvinīti| kāryakāraṇabhāvāpannaprakṛtyarthayorbhūtatve bhaviṣyattve vā gamyamāna ityarthaḥ| hetuhetumadbhāvādīti ādinā śaktayādiparigraha| tatra bhaviṣyadarthe udāharaṇamāha-suvṛṣṭiścoditi atrai3kasyaiva dhātvarthasya kartṛrūpopādhibhedena kāryyakāraṇabhāvāvagatiḥ, uttaratra tu svarūpato bhinnayostayoriti viśeṣaḥ| vyāpārābhāva iti  dhātvarthayorbhaviṣyattvapradarśanārthaṃ paramabhāvaviśeṣyakabodhotkīrtanam| vahnayabhinnāśrayakabhāviprajvalanajanyaudanā''śrayakaviklityanukūlavyāpāra iti śābdabodhastūcitaḥ| prayojya iti vyāpārābhāvānvayi prayojyatvotkīrtanaṃ ca vahniprajyavalanaudanapākayoḥ kāryyakāraṇabhāvapradarśanāya, `yadabhāvo yadabhāvaprayojyaḥ sa tajjanya' iti niyamāt| tathāca prakṛte odanapākābhāve vahniprajvalanābhāvaprayojyatve'vagate tatpratiyoginostayoḥ kāryyakāraṇabhāvaḥ subodho bhavatīti bhāvaḥ| evaṃ bhūte-
paraspareṇa spṛhaṇīyaśobhaṃ nacedidaṃ dvandvamayojayiṣyat|
asmin dvaye rūpavidhānayatnaḥ patyuḥ prajānāṃ viphalo'bhavaṣyat||
`maña uño vetyavakṣyat' yaṇañcānvavartayiṣyadityādi bodhyam| yadi devadatto nābhaviṣyadityādi bodhyamityāha evaṃrītyeti| ayañceti varttamāne laḍityādinopātta ityarthaḥ  arthāntare'pi arha praśaṃsāprāptakālādyarthe'pityarthaḥ| eṣu varttamānatvādiṣu śaktiḥ saṅketarūpā, padārthantararūpā vā anyatra ārhadyarthe| anubandhakrama iti akāradyanubandhakrama ityarthaḥ| nanu yatra pratyayasya, tibāderadarśanaṃ tatradyatakatvaṃ vācakatvaṃ veti pakṣadvaye'pyanupapattiḥ, vācakasya dyotakasya vā'ttvena vartamānatvādibodhasyopapādayitumaśakyatvādata āha digiti tadarthastu `padaṃ vācakam' iti pakṣe prakṛtipratyayavibhāgakalpanayā vācakatvaṃ tatrāropya padaśaktireva vyutpādyate| yatra prakṛtervā aśrūyamāṇatvaṃ tatra śrūyamāṇasyaiva tadarthakatvaṃ kalpyate| tadāhuḥ "śiṣyamāṇaṃ lupyamānā'rthabhidhāyi" iti| ata eva, `vyatise' iyānityādau pratyayamātrātprakṛtyarthaviṣayako bodha ityādiḥ
|| iti bhūṣaṇasāradarppaṇe lakārārthanirṇayaḥ || 2 ||
 avasarasaṅgatyā subantārthe prātipadikārthaviśeṣyatvāt kriyākāṅkṣitatvāccādau (kāraka) vibhaktyarthanirūpaṇamityabhiprāyavānāha subarthamiti|
āśrayo'rtha iti kiñciddharmānavacchinnāśrayatāvānityarthaḥ| tena caturtho-pañcamyorarthayoruddeśyāvadhyorāśrayātmakatve'pi na kṣatiḥ| tatra dvitīyāyā āśrayārthakatve mānamāha-tathāhītyādinā|
nanu yathāśrute `kartturīpsatatamam' ityanena karttṛsambandhiprāptīcchāviśeṣyasyaiva karmatvaṃ pratipādyate, natu kriyājanyaphalāśrayasyetyata āha kriyājanyaphalavattveneti ayaṃ bhāvaḥ-`īpsitatama' śabad āpnotericchāsannantāt karmaktāntāt prakṛtyarthātiśayadyotake tamapi niṣpannaḥ| tadyogācca karttṛśabdāt, "ktasya ca varttamāne"  iti karttari ṣaṣṭhī| tathāca kartropasthitatvāt svīyavyāpāreṇa vyāptumiṣyamāṇatamaṃ vyāpārajanyaphalasambanditvaprakārakakarttṛniṣṭhotkaṭecchāviṣayaḥ karmeti paryyavasānāt kriyājanyaphalāśrayasya karmatālābha iti| nacecchākarmobhūtārthaṃdhātereva "dhātoḥ karmaṇaḥ" iti sūtreṇecchāyāṃ sano vidhaānād vyāptīcchārthakasannantadhātunā vyāptirūpakarmopasaṃgrahājjīvatyādivadakarmakatayā tataḥ kathaṃ karmaktopapattiriti vācyam, dhātvarthopasaṅgrahītakarmatvamityatra karmaṇā taddhātvarthoyakarmāntarānākāṅkṣatvaviśeṣeṇa sanprakṛtidhātvarthakarmaṇaḥ karmāntarasākāṅkṣatvenoktākarmakatvasya sannante asambhavāt| evaṃ sannantasya vṛttitayā tad‌ghaṭakakarmā''dāya karmmasaṃjñakānvayyarthakatvarūpasakarmakatvābhāve'pi tad‌bahirbhūtakarmānvayitvamādāyaiva sakarmakatvaṃ bodhyam| evaṃ phalatāvacchedakasambandhena taddhātvarthaphalavyadhikaraṇavyāpāravācakatvarūpaṃ tadapīti neṣsatyāditaḥ karmapratyayānupapattiḥ| jighraterapi svārthāntarabhūtakarmaṇastadīyapuṣpādikarmāntarasākāṅkṣatvamastyeva| gandhanirūpitalaukikaviṣayitāyāṃ puṣpādeḥ svaniṣṭhādheyatvīsāṃsargikaviṣayatānirūpitaprakāratānirūpakatvenānvayena karmatvāt|
`kaṣṇāya rādhyati' ityasya `kṛṣṇasya śūbhāśubhaṃ paryālocayati' ityarthānna dhātvarthāntarbhūtaśubhāśubhakarmaṇaḥ karmāntarasākāṅkṣamiti na tatrātipraṅgaḥ| bubhūṣatyādīnāṃ tu bhavanā''dikarmaṇaḥ karmāntarānākāṅkṣasya dhātvarthatāvacchedakakoṭipraviṣṭatvādakarmakatvaṃ vyaktameva| evaṃ `svaryate' iti bhāṣyaprayogasthasvaravad‌rūpavikṛtikarmaṇaḥ sannihitaśabdarūpaprakṛtikarmasākāṅkṣatayā karotyarthaṇijantāt karmapratyayopapattiriti bodhyam| kecittu-sanādyantasya dhātvarthopasaṅgṛhītatvenākarmakatve'pi tatprakṛte sakarmakatvamādāya tasmāt karmapratyayopapattimāhaḥ|
nanu tathāpīṣṭāghaṭitasya bhavaduktasya kathaṃ karmalakṣaṇatvamata āha-tathāyuktamiti karmavyavahārasya dveṣodāsīnayorapyaviśiṣṭatvāttatsādhāraṇasyaiva lakṣaṇatvamucitam| icchāghaṭitasyaiva tasya lakṣaṇatve tu dveṣyādikarmaṇyavyāptiḥ syāt| tathāca tatsādhāraṇyāyedamevāśrayituṃ yuktamiti bhāvaḥ| evameveti itthamevetyarthaḥ| nanu dvitīyāyā uktakarmārthakatve `hariṃ bhajati' ityādau haryyāderīpsitatvādinā śābdabodhe bhānānupapattirata āha-īpsitānīpsitatvayoriti| bhānābhāveneti sārvajanīnatayā'nubhavābhāvenetyarthaḥ. nanu tarhi kriyājanyaphalākṣayaḥ karmetyeva sūtryatāmata āha-saṃjñāyāmeveti
ayamāśayaḥ- kriyājanyaphalāśrayaḥ karmeti sūtrapraṇayate yadyapīpsitānīpsitayoraviśeṣaṇa saṃjñā siddhayati, tathāpyagnermāṇavakaṃ vārayatītyādau kriyājanyaphalabhāgitveneṣṭasya māṇavakasyāpi viśeṣavihitena "vāraṇārthanām"  ityanenāpādānasaṃjñā prasajjayeta| sūtrantu `hariṃ bhajati' ityādau caritārtham| tatparihārāyepsitatamatvasya saṃjñākoṭau niveśe aprāptadveṣodāsīnasaṃjñāvidhānārthamuttarasūtramiti tathā bhoktamiti| nanu tathāpi pañcamyā iva na dvitīyāyā apyāśrayamātrārthakatā, kintu viśiṣsyaivetyata āha-
tathāceti| āśraya eva ityevakāreṇa viśiṣṭārthasya śakyatvavyavacchedaḥ| nanvāśrayatvasya tattatsvarūpātmakasya tattadāśrayabhinnatvāt, kathaṃ tasya śakyatāvacchedakatvam, anugatadharmasyaiva tattvādata āha-tattvañceti āśrayatvaṃ cetyarthaḥ| akhaṇḍaśaktirūpamiti śaktiḥ-dharmaḥ; itarapadārthaghaṭitamūrttikadharmarūpamiti yāvta| āśraya ityanugatavyavahārādāśrayapada śakyatāvacchedakatvācca tatsiddhirdravyatvādivaditi bhāvaḥ|
naiyāyikastu-āśrayatāyā niyatānyanirūpitasvarūpāyā anugamakatvā'sambhavaḥ| śakyatāvacchedakatvasyāpi vibhitvānyataratvādau vyabhicāreṇa nākhaṇḍatvasādhakatvamiti na dvitīyāyā āśrayo'rthaḥ, tasya prakṛtyaiva lābhāt, prakṛtyarthabhinnatvena tata āśrayasyānanubhavācca| kintu phalānvayini vṛttireva tadarthaḥ| karmaṇi dvitīyetyasya phalaniṣṭhā'dheyatvānvaye pravṛtitātparyye taduttaraṃ dvitīyetyarthāt na tadvirodho'pi anāditātparyagrāhakatvena paramparayā śaktigrāhakatvācca na tadvaiyyarthyam| āśrayatvantu na tacchakyam| tasya nirūpakatāsambandhenaiva phalānvayitvasyabhyupagantavyatayā nirūpakatvasya ca vṛttyaniyāmakatayā'bhāvapratiyogitā'navacchedakatvena vihago bhūmiṃ gacchati, naca sūtrasvarabhaṅgaḥ, `śaktiḥ karakam' iti pakṣe tadayogāt| ādheyatāyā api nirūpakatayā karakadharmatvādityāhuḥ|
viklittyāśrayatvāt karmateti karmasaṃjñetyarthaḥ| etena karttagataprakṛtadhātvarthavyāvāraprayojyatad‌vyadhikaraṇaprakṛtadhātvarthaphalāśrayatvaṃ karmatvamuktaṃ bhavati| yad vakṣyati `uktaprayatvāt iti'| `agnermāṇavaka vārayati' ityādāvagnyādāvati vyāptinirāsāya vyāpāre kartṛgatatvamupāttam| vārayateśca saṃyogānukūlavyāpārābhāvānukūlavyāpāro'parthaḥ| `cainno grāmaṃ gacchati' ityādau caitrādervāraṇāya vyādhikaraṇeti| vaiyādhikaraṇyañca-tadanadhikaraṇavṛttitvam, natu tadadhikaramāvṛttitvam, tasya saṃyoge asat‌vena grāmādāvavyāpteḥ| tādṛśavaiyadhikaraṇyaviśiṣṭādhikaraṇatāyāścaitrādāvasattvānnātiprasaṅgasambhāvan'pi| `māṣeṣvaśvaṃ badhgāti' ityādau bandhaprayojyabhakṣaṇā''śrayamāṣāṇāṃ tattvavāraṇāya prakṛtadhātvarthattvaṃ phalaviśeṣaṇam| prakṛtatvañca-tatra tatra viśiṣyopādeyam| `payasaudanaṃ bhuṅkte' ityādau tu karmatvāvivakṣaṇānna dvitīyā, kṛtabhonasya payolobhena pravṛttāvīpsitatamatvasyāpi payasi sambhavāditi|
nanu paraiḥ kṛño yatnamātrārthakatvābhyupagamāt kathamutpatteḥ prakṛtadhātvarthatvamata āha-utpatteriti tathāca, kṛña utpattirūpaphālarthakatvasya prāgupapādanānnārthāsaṅgatiriti bhāvaḥ| dhātvarthatvāt ityukt‌yā kṛñaḥ kevalayatnarthakatve ghaṭādīnāṃ karmatvānupapattirapi doṣatvena dhvanyate| āvaraṇabhaṅgeti prācīnamatānusāreṇedam| niṣkṛṣṭamate tu jñānarūpaphalasya viṣayatayā ghaṭādau sattvātkarmateti bodhyam| nacākarmakatā''pattiḥ, phalatāvacchedakasambandhena sāmānādhikaraṇyābhāvāt| naiyāyikamate tvetādṛśasthale yathā ghaṭādīnāṃ karmatvaṃ tathopapādita dhātvarthanirūpaṇāvasare|
nanu vidyamānaghaṭe jñānajanyāvaraṇabhaṅgasya sambhave'pi bhāvivinaṣṭaghaṭe nirāśrayasya tasyāsambhavena tasya karmatvānupapattirata āha-atītānāgateti| tatrā''śrayatāṅgīkāreyuktimāha-anyatheti tatrāvacaraṇabhṅagāsvīkāre ityarthaḥ| yathāpūrvamiti jānātikriyāviśaṣaṇam| padārthānativṛttāvavyayībhāvaḥ| pūrvamanatikramyetyartha-| pūrvajñānānatikramoyasmim̐staditi yāvat| ghaṭasya vidyamānatādaśāyāṃ tātkālikajñānasya tad‌ghaṭe āvaraṇabhaṅgajanakatayā'tītadaśāyāmādhunikājñanasya tadajanakatvena pūrvātikramasya tattvāt pūrvānatikrāntajñānābhāvasya viṣayasyābādhāttadṛśaprayogasya prāmāṇyāpatterityarthaḥ| atītaghaṭe ādhunikajñānenāvaraṇabhṅgetpādābhyupagame tu tena pūrvajñānānatikramāt tadabhāvasya bādhādbhavati tasyāprāmāṇyamiti bhāvaḥ|
nanu vinaṣṭe ghaṭe vāstavatadāśrayataiva kathamata āha-atītāderiti ādinā bhāviparigrahaḥ| naiyāyikānāmiveti yathā'nubhavānurodhena tasmin viṣayatayā jñānāśrayatāṅgīkārasteṣāṃ tathāsmākamapīti bhāvaḥ| nanvatīto ghaṭo jñānaviṣaya iti pratīterastu nāmatādṛśaghaṭasya viṣayatayā jñānāśrayatā; āvaraṇabhaṅgaśrayatāyāntu na tathā sādhakopalabdhirata āha-satkāryyeti| tameva darśayati- "sadeva somyedamagra āsīt" iti śruteḥ kāryyajātaṃ sūkṣmarūpeṇa prāgavasthitameva| paramate janakatvenābhimatasāmagrīsambadhānāttu tadabhivyaktirbhavati| kāraṇe sūkṣmarūpeṇāvasthānameva tatprāgabhāvaḥ| tasyāmevāvasthāyāṃ sa bhaviṣyatīti vyāvahṇiyate| tataḥ pracyutamabhivyaktilakṣaṇaṃ dharmamaprāptaṃ jāyata iti prāptābhivyaktistvastīti|
nanu sarvadaiva sati tasmin kathaṃ nāsti naśyatīti vyāvahāro'ta āha-tirobhāvābhyupagama iti tirobhāvābhivyañjakasāmagyā tirobhāve'bhivyañjite sati, `nāsti naṣṭaḥ' `iti vyāvahāraḥ| labdhakramāyāntu tasyāṃ naśyatīti satkāryavādasiddhāntaḥ| prakṛte ghaṭasya tirodhāne'pi sūkṣmarūpeṇa sattvāttatrāvaraṇabhaṅgarūpaphalāśrayatvaṃ sūlabhamiti bhāvaḥ| uktaniṣkṛṣṭa karmatvaṃ viśadīkartumāśaṅkate-nanviti| vibhāgasya ityasya kriyājanyetyādi| nanu karmasaṃjñāyāḥ parayā kartṛsaṃjñayā bādhane'pi vāstavakarmatvasya tatrābādhāt syādiva tādṛśaprayogo'ta āha-dvitīyotpattāviti idañca prakṛtābhiprāyeṇa| kārakavibhaktyutpattimātrasyaiva tanniyamyatvāta| anyatheti saṃjñāyā aniyāmakatve ityarthaḥ|
dvitīyā''patteriti`gamayati kṛṣṇaṃ gokulam' ityatra ṇijarthavyāpārajanyasaṃyogānukūlavyāpārarūpadhātvarthaphalāśrayatvasyeva, `pācayati kṛṣṇenaudanam' ityatrāpi tādṛśavyāpārajanyaviklittyanukūlavyāpārāśrayatvasya tatra sattvāditi bhāvaḥ| śābdabodha iti caitraniṣṭhasaṃyogānukūlaścaitrakartṛko vartamāno vyāpāra iti śābdabodha ityarthaḥ, tadviṣayasyābādhāditi bhāvaḥ| tathāvyutpannānāmiti sāmānyato dhātvarthaphalaviśeśyatānirūpitaprakāratāsambandhena śābdabodhe āśrayopasthiterhetutvavādināmityarthaḥ| bodhasya svasvavyutpattyanusāritvāditi bhāvaḥ|
caitrakarmakacrakartṛkavyāpāraśābdhabodhasya `caitro grāmaṃ gacchati' ityādāvananubhavādāha-ucyatāṃ veti kāryyakāraṇabhāvāntaramiti sāmānyataḥ kāryyakārabhāvakalpene uktātiprasaṅgenā'śrayaṃ prakṛtadhātvarthavyāpārānadhikaraṇatvena viśeṣya tādṛśāśrayopasthiterhetutvasya kalpanānna tādṛśaśabdodhapattiriti bhāvaḥ| samabhivyāhṛteti prakṛtetyarthaḥ| uktaprāyatvāditi dhātvarthaviklittyāśrayatvāt karmatetyādigranthenetyasyā'diḥ| gamestatra pārthakyenānupādānāduktam-prāyeti vastutastu phalvayāpārayorityatra phalatvasya taddhātvarthajanyatve sati taddhātujanyopasthitiviṣayatvarūpasya nirvacanād(tad) vyāpāravyadhikaraṇaphalāśrayatvarūpakarmatvoktayaivānatiprasaṅgenoktaviśeṣaṇasya pārthakyena niviśa itidhyeyam|
naiyāyikamataṃ dūṣayitumupanyasyati-naiyāyikāstviti| ādyadoṣeti `caitraścaitraṃ gacchati' iti prayogāpattirūpetyarthaḥ| dvitīyeti prayāgāt kāśīṃ gacchati caitre `prayāgaṃ gacchati' iti prayogāpattirūpetyarthaḥ| dhātvarthatā'vacchedakatvamiti phalāvacchinnavyāpārasya dhātvarthatvamitivādināṃ matenedam| tathāca tanmate dhātvarthatā'vacchedakaphalaśālitvaṃ karmatvaṃ dvitīyārthaḥ paryavasyati| kāryyakāraṇabhāveti dvitīyārthaparasamavetatvasya svaniṣṭhaprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣatāsambandhena śābdabuddhi prati bhāvanātvācchinnaviśṣyatāsambandhena dhātujanyopasthiterhetutvāntarakalpanādityarthaḥ|
nanu bhedasya kevalānvayitayā sarvasyaiva paratvena caitrakriyāyā api maitrānyasamvetatvāt tadvācyatāṅgīkāre'pyuktadoṣatādavasthyamata āha-paratvañceti vyutpattivaicitryāttu padārthaikadeśe'pi paratve pratiyogitayā prakṛtyarthānvayaḥ, prakṛte caitrasaṃyogajanakakriyāyāḥ caitrabhinnasamavetatvabādhānna `caitraścaitraṃ gacchati' iti prayogaḥ| yatra cobhayakarmabhyāṃ malalayoḥ saṃyogastatra mallāntarakriyāyāḥ svaniṣṭhasaṃyogajanikāyāḥ svabhinnasamavetatve'pi tādṛśakriyāyāḥ svasmin bādhānna `mallaḥ svaṃ gacchati' iti prayogaḥ, svaniṣṭasaṃyogajanakasvakriyāyāṃ svabhinnasamavetatvasyāyogyatvenābhāne'pyavādhitasaṃyogajanakatvaṃ viśayīkṛtya `abhicaran yajeta' ityādāviva śāvdabodasambhavāt tadarthatātparyyeṇa svaṃ gacchatīti prayogo durvāra iti tu nāśaṅkanīyam, parasamavetatvāṃśāviṣayakasya dvitīyā'dhīnaphalajanakatvabodhasya kūtrāpyanabhyupagamena tādṛśabodhe tadbhānasāmagyā apyapekṣaṇāt tasyāśca prakṛte'sattvāt|
atha svasyāpi dvitvāvacchinnabhinnatvāt `svaṃ gacchati' itiprayogavāraṇāya dvitīyāprakṛtyarthasya prakṛtyarthatāvacchedakāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvasambandhenānvayo vācyaḥ| tathāya `caitro dravyaṃ gacchati', `mallo mallaṃ gacchati' ityādivākyānāmaprāmāṇyāpatti-, caitra-mallādiniṣṭhakriyāyā dvitīyāprakṛtyarthatā'vacchedakadravyatva-mallatvāvakchinna bhinnāsamvetatvāt tad‌vyaktitvānupasthitāvapi śābdabodhasyānubhavasiddhatayā tattad‌vyaktivāvacchinnapratiyogitayānvayāsambhavāt, tad‌vyaktitvopasthāpakapadābhāvācca| ekadharmavacchinnapratiyogitāyā anyavarmāvacchinnasaṃsargatve mānābhāvācca pratiyogiviśeṣitābhāvabuddherviśiṣṭavaiśiṣṭayā'vagāhitvaniyamāt|
taduktaṃ dīdhitikṛtā-`pratiyogiviśeṣitābhāvabhānaṃ tu viśiṣṭavaiśiṣṭhayabodhamaryādāṃ nātiśeti' itīti cet tarhi kriyānvayibhedāpratiyogitāvacchedakatvaṃ dvitīyārthaḥ| bhede prakṛtyarthasyādheyatayā'navayāmna pūrvoktāpattyanupapattī| ऩñ‌samabhivyāhāre ca nañā tātparyyavaśāccaitraścaitraṃ na gacchatītvādau dvitīyāprakṛtyarthavṛttitvaviśiṣṭatadapārarthabhedapratiyogitāvacchedakatvābhāvaḥ| kvacicca caitro grāmaṃ na gacchatītyādau dvitīyā'rthavṛttītvaviśiṣṭadhātvarthasaṃyogajanakatvābhāvaḥ| kriyāyāṃ bodhyate|
ye tu prakṛtyarthavṛttitvaviśiṣṭasaṃyogādirūpaphalasyaiva janakatvāsvāśrayaniṣṭhabhedapratiyogitāvacchedakatvobhayasambandhena kriyāyāmanvayopagamād bhedapratiyogikāvacchedakatvasya dvitīyārthatvamantareṇāpyuktāpattimuddharanti, tanmate vṛttyaniyāmakasambanadhsayābhāvapratiyogitā'navacchedakatayoktasthale uktasambandhena nañā saṃyogābhāvabodhānupapattiḥ, `parasamavetvaṃ dvitīyārthaḥ' iti dīdhitīvirodhaśca|
evañca `caitro grāmaṃ gacchati' ityatra grāmavṛttisaṃyogajanikā grāmamavṛttibhedapratiyogitāvacchedikā ca yā kriyā gadanukūlakṛtimām̐ścaitra iti naiyāyikamatapariṣkāraḥ|
atra pratyavatiṣṭante-nañrathaprasajyapratiṣedhasya pratiyogitayā kriya'nvayitvamiti vyāvasthāpanāt kathaṃ `caitro grāmaṃ na gacchi' ityādau nañaḥ saṃyogajanakatvābhāvabodhakatvam| uktañca-
paryyudāsaḥ sa vijñeyo yatrottarapadena nañ|
prasajjayapratiṣedhastu kriyayā saha yatra nañ || iti |
yatra nañarthāṃbhāvaḥ kriyayā-dhātvarthavyāpāreṇa pratiyogitayā sambaddho bhavatītyuttarārddhārthaḥ| tasmāt tiṅante nañ‌samabhivāyāhāre kriyāpratiyogikābhāvabodha eva nyāyyaḥ, natu kriyāyāṃ phalābhāvasya vibhaktarthābhāvasya vā prathamāntārthe vā tiṅarthābhavasya, klṛptakāraṇavirahāditi|
atra vadanti-yaddharmmāvacchinne yaddharmmāvacchinnatvaṃ yena saṃsargeṇa nañ‌asabhivyāhāre pratiyate taddharmmāvacchinne taddharmmāvacchinnasya tatsaṃsargāvacichinnapratiyogitākābhāvo nañcasamabhivyāhāre budhdyate iti sakalalokānubhavasiddho'yamarthaḥ| `pratiyogyabhāvānvayau tulyayogabhemau, iti vadatā dīdhitikṛtopanibaddhaḥ| evañca caitro grāmaṃ gacchatītyatra nañaasamabhivyāhāre kriyāyāṃ dvitīyā'rthavṛttitvaviśiṣṭasaṃyogajanakatvasya bodhāt tatsamabhivyāhāre tatra tadabhāvasya bodho jāyamānaḥ kena vāraṇīyaḥ| naca kāraṇabhāvaḥ, nañarthābhāvaprakāratānirūpitaviseṣyatāsambandhena śābdabodhe pratiyogyanvayayogyārthopasthirhetutayā tasyāśca kriyāyāṃ sattvāt| natu nañarthaniṣṭhaviśeṣyatānirūpitāprakāratāsambandhena bodhe dhātujanyabāvanopastiterhetutā, `caitraścaitraṃ na gacchati' ityādivākyānāmaprāmāṇyāpatteḥ, caitrakarmakacaitrakarttṛkagamanakriyārūpapratiyogino'prasidhdyā nañā tadabhāvasya bodhayitumaśakyatvāt|
manmate tu caitrakartṛkakriyāyāṃ caitravṛttisaṃyogajanakatve'pi caitravṛttibedapratiyogitāvacchedakatvasya tasyāmasatt‌vena nañā tadabhāvasya pratyayanāt prāmāṇyopapatteḥ| ata eva parasamavetatvasya dvitīyārthatvamapyāvaśyakam| naca tvanmate'pi caitrasyākarmatvāt kathaṃ tadvācakād dvitīyopapattiruksthele iti vācyam, maitro gacchati caitram na caitra ityatra maitrakriyā'vacchinnapratiyogitākabhedavattvatadīyasaṃyogāśrayatvayorubhayordvitīyārthayorabādhena tadupapatteḥ| adhikamagre vakṣyate|
tasmāccaitro gacchatītyādau caitro na karmeti vyāvahārācca, sarvāthā kartṛvyāvṛttadhātvarthavyāpāraprayojyatad‌vyadhikaraṇadhātvarthaphalā''śrayatvaṃ karmatvaṃ nirvācyam| tatra phalavyāpārayordhātulabhyatve'pi vṛttitve bhedapratiyogitavacchedakatvapararyyavasite vaiyadhikaraṇye ca dvitīyādeḥ śaktirananyalabhyatvāt svīkaraṇīyate; prakṛtamanusarāḥ|
viklittyanukūleti idaṃ taṇḍulānyasamavetakriyā'nvayi|
yathāśrutaṃ dūṣayati-tanna royacāmahe ityādinā| nanu parasamavetatvasya dvitīyārthatvaṃ vinā'tiprasaṅgānirāsād gauravaṃ na doṣāyetyata āha-atiprasaṅgaḥ kimiti|  tāvaditi parasamavetakriyājanyadhātvarthatā'vacchedakaphalaśālitvarūpakarmatvasya dvitīyārthatvasvīkāre'pi dvitīyā'tiprasaṅgasya tādavasthyādityarthaḥ| tamevāha-gamayati kṛṣṇamityādinā| dvitīyāpatteriti dvitīyotpattāvuktakarmatvasya tantratve, `gamayati gokulaṃ kṛṣṇam' ityatra ṇijarthanirūpitaniruktakarmatvasyeva paciprakṛtiṇijarthakarmatvasya kṛṣṇe sattvena pācayatyādiprayoge'pi prayojyakarttṛvācakād dvitīyāpatterataḥ saṃjñāyā eva dvitīyotpattau prayojakatā'ṅgīkaraṇīyā| evañca tata evātiprasaṅgabhaṅge kṛtaṃ tatra dvitīyāśaktayeti bhāvaḥ|
nanu tādṛśakarsatvameva dvitīyāniyāmakam| niruktasthale kṛṣṇe tatsattve'pyupāttagatyarthakādyatiriktaṇijantadhātuyoge "gatibuddhi" iti sūtreṇa tadavivakṣābodhanānnoktāpattiḥ, vivakṣāyā eva vibhaktyutpattau niyāmakatāyā vakṣyamāṇatvādata āha-taṇḍulaṃ pacyata iti taṇḍule bhavaduktadvitīyāniyāmakakarmatvasya sattvāpattiḥ, manamate tu karttṛsaṃjñāyā karmasaṃjñāyā bādhānna tadāpattiriti bhāvaḥ| taṇḍulānyasamavetetyanena parasamavetakriyājanyatvaṃ viklittatāvastīti bodhitam| dhātvarthāśrayatvādityanena karttṛsaṃjñāsattvamāvedyate| uktarītyaiveti tathāvyutpannāmityādyuktarītyaivetyarthaḥ, dhātvarthavyāpārānadhikaraṇāśrayopasthitirūpahetvabhāvāditi bhāvaḥ| nanu nāsmanmate'pyuktaprayogāpattiḥ, tatra vyāpāratvenaiva, phalasya bhānāṅgīkāreṇa taṇḍulasya dhātvarthatāvacchedakaphalaśālitvābhāvāt, niṣkṛṣṭabavanmate'pi vyāpāragatapaurvāparyyāropeṇa phalasya vyāpāratāyāḥ svīkārāccetyato dūṣaṇāntaramāha-parasamevatatvasyeti|
iṣṭānvayeti iṣṭasya-prakṛtyarthasya pratiyogitāsambandhena parasamavetatvasyaikadeśe paratve parasamavetatvasya cāśrayatayā kriyāyāmanvayasya lābhāya-bhānāyetyarthaḥ| anekaśa iti prakṛtyarthaprakārakaśābdabuddhiṃ prati dvitīyā'dhīnādheyatvopasthiteriva tadadhīnaparasamevatatvopasthiteḥ prakāratāsambandhena, evaṃ parasamavetatvaprakārakabuddhiṃ prati dhātujanyopasthitervyāpāratvāvaccinnaviśeṣyatāsambandhena hetutvakalpane cātigauravādityarthaḥ| manmate tu phalaprakārakaśābdabodhaklṛptahetutākavyāpāropasthitiviṣayavyāpārāṃśe dvitīyāprakṛtyarthāvṛttitvavaiśiṣyaniveśenaiva nātiprasaṅga iti bhāvaḥ|
nanu `cautro maitraśca parasparaṃ gacchataḥ' ityādau dhātvarthavyāpārasya dvitīyāprakṛtyarthavṛttitvāt tatra phalānanvaya prasaṅgo'ta āha-spaṣṭamiti tathāca dvitīyāprakṛtyarthāvṛttītyanena dvitīyāprakṛtyarthavṛttibhedapratiyogitā'vacchedakatvasya vivakṣaṇāt `parasparaṃ gacchataḥ' ityādau kriyāyāḥ parasparāvṛttitvabādhe'pi parasparaniṣṭhabhedapratiyogitāvacchedakatvasyābādhānna phalānvayānupa pattirityādi tatroktamiti|
harikṛtaṃ karmavibhāgamāha-tacceti kriyājanyaphalā''śrayarūpaṃ karmetyarthaḥ|
yadasajjāyata iti asatkāryyavādamavalambya| sadvetyādi tu sāṅkhyamatena| prakāśata ityanantaram-
"prakṛtestu vivakṣāyāṃ vikāryyaṃ kaiścidanyathā|
tannirvattayam" ityeva vākyapadīyapāṭhaḥ, anyathā sāmānyadharmeṇa alakṣitasya vikāryyasya viśṣyarūpeṇa pradarśane'rthāntaratāpatteḥ| tadarthasyu kaiścit prasiddhairācāryyaiḥ prakṛtivivakṣāyāṃ niṣpādyaṃ vikāryyamityucyate| anyathā tadavivakṣāyāṃ tanniṣpādyaṃ nirvarttyamiti tadarthaḥ| tatra ghaṭaṃ karotītyādau satyā api mṛdādiprakṛteḥ pariṇāmitvenāvivakṣā| bhasma karotītyādau tvasatyā eva tasyāstattvena sā|
yadyapi nirvaṃrttyate-niṣpādyate iti vyutpattyā nirvarttyatvaṃ taṇḍulānodanaṃ pacatītyādāvodanādau vikāryyakarmmaṇyapyasti tathāpi prakṛtivācakapadāsamabhivyāhṛtapadopasthāpyatve sati niṣpādyatvarūpapāribhāṣikanirvarttyatvasya vivakṣaṇānna dopaḥ|| taduktam-
satī vā vidyamānā vā prakṛti pariṇāminā|
yasya nā''śrīyate tasya nirvarttyatvaṃ pracakṣate|| iti|
yasya-vikṛtikarmmaṇaḥ, nāśrīyate-na prayujyate, na vivakṣyata iti yāvat| anyat-uktārtham| vikāryyantviti tallakṣaṇaṃ tu pratīyamānaprakṛtibhāvakatve sati kriyānirvāhyaviśiṣṭāsattvotpattyanyatara phalavattvam| ghaṭaṃ karotītyādinirvartye kriyānirvāhyotpattimatyapi prakṛtīvikṛtibhāvābhānānnātiprasaṅgaḥ| prakṛtikarmaṇastādṛśaviśiṣṭāsattvād vikṛtikarmaṇaśca tādṛśotpattyāśrayatvāllakṣaṇasaṅgatiḥ| īdṛśasthale phaladvayaprayojakavyāpārārthakatvaṃ dhātorāvaśyakam| uktañca bhāṣye-`hyarthaḥ paciḥ' iti, anthaikaphalaikavyāpārārthakatvasyākarmakeṣvapi sattvād viśeṣopādānānarthakyaprasaṅgat|
pare tu-"dvayarthaḥ paciḥ" iti bhāṣyād vikledanaṃ nirvarttanaṃ ca pacerarthaḥ, `taṇḍulānodanaṃ pacati' ityasya `taṇḍulān vikletayannodanaṃ nirvarttayati' iti vivaraṇāt| tathāca prakṛte taṇḍulāśrayaviklittyanukūlavyāpārāśrayaka odanāśrayakotpattyanukūlo vyāpāra iti bodhaḥ| evaṃ `kāṣṭhaṃ bhasma karoti' ityādāvapyūhyamityāhuḥ|
tadasat, bhāvanāprakārabodhe hetutvena klṛptāyāḥ kṛddhātvanyatarajanyopasthiterviśeṣyatāsambandhenākhyātārthe'bhāvena tādṛśabodhasambhavāt, ekayaiva kriyayā phaladvayotpattisambhavena tadvākyajanyabodhasya vyāpāradvayaviṣayakatvakalpanānaucityādākhyātaśabdānnivṛttibhedāyā eva kriyāyā avagamāditi rūpapsūtrīyakaiyaṭācca| kiñca pacervyāpāradvayārthakatve navyamate kriyāyāṃ dvitvābodhena `caitro taṇḍulān odanaṃ pacataḥ' iti dvivacanāpattirdurvārā|
vastutastu vikledanam-viklittiḥ, nirvarttanam-utpattireva, uktapadayorbhāvalyuḍantatvāt| tathāca phaladvayānukūla eka eva vyāpāro dhātvarthatayā bhāṣyakṛtsammataḥ| vyāpāradvayārthakatvoktistu keṣāñcit, tpadayorṇijantaprakṛtikalyuḍantatvabhramanibandhanaivetyavadheyam| yadyapi vikāryyaṃ dvedhā-prakṛtirvikṛtiścetyeva vaktumucitam, tathāpi vikṛtiddhaividhye'vagate prakṛtāvapi vikāryatvaṃ jñātaprāyaṃ bhavatītyaśayena tadanuktvaina vikṛtikarma vibhajat-prakṛtyucchedeti etena kriyānirvāhyābhāvaḥ pratiyogitāvacchedakadharmavattvaṃ prakṛtikarmatvamiti dhvanitam|
kāśān kaṭaṃ karoti' `kusumāni strajaṃ karoti' `suvarṇaṃ kuṇḍalaṃ karoti' `mṛdaṃ ghaṭaṃ karoti' `kāṣṭhaṃ bhasma karoti' `taṇḍulānodanaṃ pacati' ityādau kriyānirvāhyo yaḥ pūrvabhāvaviśiṣṭakāśakāṣṭhādipratiyogikābhāvo viśiṣṭasattvarūpastatpratiyogitāvacchedakavaiśiṣṭhayavattvaṃ kāśakāṣṭhāderiti tattatprakṛtau lakṣaṇasaṅgatiḥ| sarvatra viśiṣṭābhāvapratiyogini tādṛśapratiyogitāvacchedakavaiśiṣṭayasattvādātiprasaṅgavāraṇāya kriyānirvāhyoti prakṛtadhātvarthavyāpāranirvāhyarthakam| kvacit kāṣṭhāṇḍulādidharmināśādeva viśiṣṭābhāvo bhasmaidanādisampādakaḥ| kvaciddharmiṇaḥ sattve'pi kaṭastragādisandarbhaviśeṣaviraharūpapūrvabhāvaviśiṣṭāsattvaṃ kaṭasandarbhādiniṣpādakaṃ kriyāto nirvahati|
sambhūtamiti prakṛtināśaprayojyojyotpattimadityarthaḥ| idañca bhasmavadityanvayi `kāṣṭhādi' iti pṛthak padam anyathā kāṣṭhasuvarāṇādesturīyatāpatteḥ| bhasmavaditi tattaulyena varttamānamanyadapi vikārajātamityarthaḥ| sādṛśyañca prakṛtināśaprayojyotpattimattvenaiva| evamagre'pi| tathāca prakṛtivikṛtibhāvāpannakāṣṭhabhasmādivikāryyamityarthaḥ| paryyavasyati| evañca `kāṣṭhaṃ bhasma karoti' ityatra karoteḥ| (pratiyogitvaviśiṣṭa) nāśa utpattiśca phaladvayam, tadanukūlavyāpāraścārthaḥ| nāśe pratiyogitayā kāṣṭhasyotpattau bhasmana ādheyatayā'nvayāt kāṣṭhapratiyogikanāśānukūlo bhasmotpādako varttamāno vyāpāra iti bodhaḥ| evaṃ `taṇḍulānodanaṃ pacati' ityādāvapi|
naiyāyikāstu-`taṇḍulānodanaṃ pacati' iti vikāryyasthale prakṛtikarmottaradvitīyāyā nāśakatvamarthaḥ| vikṛtikarmottaradvitīyāyāścotpādakatvam| taṇḍulādyanvitaṃ nāśakatvaṃ ca pāke'nveti| odanānvitotpādakatvasya nāśakatvaviśiṣṭe pāke'nvayaḥ| nośe cotpatteḥ prayojakatvamuddeśyavidheyabhāvamahimnā bhāsate| tena pākasya taṇḍulārambhakasaṃyoganāśakatve'pi `saṃyogamonaṃ pacati' iti na prayogaḥ, taṇḍulārabhakasaṃyoganāśasyaudanotpattau taṇḍulanāśenānyathāsiddhatayā aprayojakatvāt|
vastutastu vikṛtikarmāsamabhivyāhṛte `taṇḍulaṃ pacati' itivat `saṃyogaṃ pacati' iti prayogavāraṇāya nāśadvārā nāśakatvameva dvitīyārtho vācya iti prakṛte prayojyaprayojakabhāvabhāne'pi na kṣatiḥ| tathāca taṇḍulanāśaka odanotpādako yo vyāpārastadanukūlakṛtimāniti bodhaḥ| īdṛśasthake ca karmākhyātena payrakṛteḥ karmatvamevābhidhīyate| ata eva `kāṣṭhāni bhasmarāśiḥ kriyante' ityādau kāṣṭhapadasamānavacanatvamākhyātasya, natu bhasmādivikārāvācakapadasamānavacanatvam, bhāvanānyini saṅkhyānvayaniyamāt|
nanu pradhānāpradhānakarmavācakakarmapadasamabhivyāhṛtanī-vahādi (duhādi) prakṛtikakarmākhyātasyaivāpradhāne duhādīnām, `pradhāne nīhṛkṛṣvahām' ityānuśāsanāt karmatvadvayānabhidhāyakatva niyamena prakṛte karmatvadvayasyaiva tulyatayā vikṛtigatakarmatvasyāpi lakāreṇābhidhāne bādhakābhāvaḥ| ākhyātasya vikṛtisamānavacanatvaprasaṅgastu-
"gṛhṇāti vācakaḥ saṅkhayāṃ prakṛtervikṛterna hi"|
ityanuśāsanasiddhavikṛtisaṅkhyānvayabuddhiṃ prati prakṛtisaṅakhyānvayaparatvenāgṛhyamāṇākhyātajanyopasathiterhetutvakalpanayā nirasanītaḥ| kathamānyathā "bhasmībhavanti kāṣṭhāni" ityādiprayogā iti cenna, `ghaṭapaṭau dṛśyete' ityatra dvitvasyaivobhayasādhāraṇaviśeṣyatāvacchedakāparicayena vikāravikāriṇordvayorevākhyātārthaviśeṣtve vā vākyabhedāpatteḥ| naceṣṭāpattiḥ, tathā sati ` kāṣṭhaṃ nāśyeta-bhasma kriyate' iti vākyajabodhasyeva `kāṣṭhaṃ bhasma kriyate' iti vākyajabodhasyāpi bhasma kāṣṭhavikṛtiranyavikṛtirveti saṃśayanirvarttakatvānupapatteḥ, samūhālambanabodhajanakatvāviśeṣeṇa kāṣṭhabhasmanoḥ prakṛtivikṛtibhāvasyoktavākyādalābhāt| satyāmevaikavākyatāyāṃ karttṛpratyaye prayojyaprayojakabhāvasya viśiṣṭānvayavivakṣāniyamena lābha iva karmapratyayasthale'pi vivakṣāyā niyamavaśādeva karmātmanepadena bhasmani kāṣmaṭhocchedaprayojyotpattikatvarūpakāṣṭhavikṛtitvasya lābhāt|
naca vikṛtivācakād dvitīyāpattiḥ, lakāreṇa vikṛtikarmatvasyānabhidhāne'pi karmatvāntaraviśeṣaṇatāpannakriyāyāṃ tasya vikṛtyarathasaṃsargatvābhyupagamenaiva dvitīyāpattyasambhavāt, prātipadikārthaviśeṣyatayā karmatvādivivakṣāyāmeva dvitīyādyavasarāt|
nacaivam, `ghaṭa karoti' ityāpattiḥ, karmatvāntaraviśeṣaṇatānāpannakriyāyāṃ kriyāntarakarttṛtvaviśeṣyatāpannārthakasyaiva karmatāsaṃsargeṇa bhānābhyupagamāt| ata eva `kāṣṭhaṃ bhasmarāśiḥ karoti' iti na prayogaḥ, bhasmarāśeḥ kriyāntarakarttṛtvaviśeṣyatānāpannatvāt| upapadyate ca `paśya mṛgo dhāvati' ityādiḥ|
prakṛte ca `nīlo ghaṭo bhavati' ityādāvasādhāraṇadharmarūpabhavane nīlāderādheyatvarūpakarttṛtāsambandhenānvayavad‌vyutpattivaicitryeṇa prakṛtikarmatvaviśeṣaṇatāpannāyāṃ kṛdhātvarthakṛtau bhasmādirūpavikṛtikarmaṇaḥ karmatāsambandhenānvayopagamena ekāvākyatvopapattyoktasaṃśayanirvarttakatvopapatteḥ| tathācotpādakatvena bhasmasambandhikṛtiprayojyanāśapratiyogīni kāṣṭhānīti `kāṣṭhaṃ bhasmarāśiḥ kriyate' iti vākyajo bodhaḥ| sambandhasya prātipadikārthaviśeṣyatayā avivakṣaṇādeva na tatra ṣṭhayapi|
evaṃ "mṛdo na ghaṭo na" ityatrāpi prakṛtivikṛtibhāvasthale karmatvāntaraviśeṣaṇatānāpannadhātvarthānvaryyabodhane dvitīyāyāḥ sādhutvamiti niyamopagame noktasthale dvitīyāprasaṅgaḥ, nāpi `duhyate gauḥ kṣīram' ityādau tadanupapattiḥ| evamekatvāvacchinna dvitvāvacchinnāropasthale'pi `eko dvau jñāyeta' ityādau āropyaviśeṣyavācakasamānavacanatvamākyātasya| prayujyante ca kavayaḥ-"eko'pi traya iva bhāti kanduko'yam" iti| naca tatrāpi `bhānti' ityeva pāṭhaḥ, kathamanyathā-
"amāni tattena nijāyaśoyugaṃ dviphālabaddhāścikurāḥ śiraḥsthitam"|
ityatrāropyanijāyaśoyugasamānavacanam `amāni' ityākhyātaṃ prayuktaṃ śrīharṣeṇeti vācyam tatrāpi pūrvārddhasamarthitāyaśoyugasya vāstavatvena tasmin phāladvayabaddhacikurāṇāmeva rājña aropyatvena kaverutprekṣitatvāt| ata eva na tatrāpimṛṣṭavidheyāṃśatādoṣāvakāśa itāyāhuḥ|
atra kecit-"punarāvṛttaḥ suvarṇapiṇḍaḥ punaraparayā''kṛtyā yuktaḥ khadirāṅgāsavarṇe kuṇḍale bhavataḥ" iti mahābhāṣya prayogādākhyātasya vikṛtisamānavacanatvameva|
guhṇāti vācakaḥ saṅkhyāṃ prakṛtervikṛterna hi|
iti tu cvipratyayamātraviṣayakam| eva़ñca `kāṣṭhāni bhasma rāśiḥ kriyante' ityādiprayogāṇāmasādhutvameveti|
apare tu-`khadirāṅgārasavarṇe' iti viśeṣaṇena tatra kuṇḍalayoḥ prakṛtitvaṃ gamyate, siddhāyā vikṛteḥ pākānapekṣaṇāt| `punaraparayā''kṛtyā yuktaḥ' iti viśeṣaṇena tu suvarṇapiṇḍasya vikṛtitvam, ākṛtyāntarayogasya prakṛtitvāprayojakatvāt| yujyate caitat| "mṛttiketyeva satyam" itivat saṃhṛtimukhena `punarāvṛttaḥ' iti viśeṣaṇenāpi suvarṇapiṇḍasya vikṛtitvaṃ gamyate|
tathāhi-pūrvopakrāntā''kṛtiparamparāyāstatparyyavasāyitvena siṣādhayiṣitasuvarṇanityatvasya siddheḥ, tasmāt pūrvanirdiṣṭākṛtiviśiṣṭasuvarṇasya kuṇḍalādiprakṛterapyanirdiṣṭā'parākṛtiviśiṣṭasya tasya vikṛtitvameva bhāṣyakṛttātparyaviṣaya ityasyāpi vaktuṃ śakyatayā na tannirdeśa ākhyātasya vikṛtisamānavacanatve upaṣṭambhaka iti|
atredamābhāti-vaiyākaraṇamate prakṛte prakṛtivikṛtyordvayorapi karttṛtvenākhyātena karttṛdvayameva abhidhānīyam| evaṃ tadgatasaṃkhyā'pi tatraikenākhyātena viruddhasaṃkhyāvatostayoḥ sakhyayāyāḥ abhidhātumaśakyatayā bhagavatā kuṇḍale bhavata iti vikṛtisaṅkhyaivoktā| natu prakṛtigatā nākhyātapratipādyeti munitrayasyā''jñāsti| evañca prayogavaśena paryyāyeṇānyatarasamānavacanatvamākhyātasya nīrābādhamiti|
kriyākṛteti kriyāprayojyāsādhāraṇadharmaprakārakapratītiviṣayatānāśrayatve sati phalā''śrayatvam| nirvarttyādāvativyāptivāraṇāya satyantam| tatrāpi viṣayatānāśrayatvasya dharmaprakārakapratītiviṣayatānāśrayatvasyāsādhāraṇadharmaghaṭitasya vā tasya `ghaṭaṃ jānāti' itāyādau ghaṭādāvasambhava iti prayaṭojyāntaṃ dharmaviśeṣaṇam| tatraiva kriyāprayojyāsādhāraṇadharmasaṃyogapratītiviṣaye grāmādikarmaṇyavyāptinirāsāya asādhāraṇeti| kriyājanyaphalānāśraye atiprasaktinirāsāya viśeṣyam|
naca `ghaṭaṃ jānāti' ityādau kriyājanyaphalābhāvāt sāmānyalakṣaṇasyāvyāptiḥ, tadvāraṇāya tatrā''varaṇabhaṅgābhyuṣagame tu tasyaiva kriyāprayojyāsādhāraṇadharmatayā `ayaṃ ghaṭa etena jñātaḥ, etad‌vyavahāraviṣayatvāt' iti anumānajanyapramāviṣayatvena satyantābhāvādviśeṣalakṣaṇāvyāptiriti vācyam, karttṛbhinnatve sati karttṛbhinnatve sati karttṛkarmobhayasādhāraṇakriyājanyaphalāśrayatve uktalakṣaṇavākyatātparyyādviṣayatayā jñānarūpaphalāśrayatvācca na sāmānyalakṣaṇā'vyāptiḥ, āvaraṇabhaṅgaphalamādāya jānātyādikarmmaṇi lakṣaṇalṛsaṅgamane'pīcchatyādikarmaṇvyavyāptivāraṇāyoktarīterevānusartavyaṃtvāditi| nirdeśakrameṇodāharaṇānyāha-ghaṭamiti| adyamiti nirvarttyamityarthaḥ| evamagre'pi|
saṃjñāntarairanākyātamiti apādānatvāditattad‌rūpaviśeṣairavivikṣitamityarthaḥ| akathitañcetyatrākathitaśabdo'vivakṣitaparaḥ, natvapradhānaparaḥ| tathā sati `pāṇinā kāsyapātryāṃ dogdhi' ityatra karaṇādhikaraṇayoḥ karmasaṃjñā prasajjyeteti du hādīnāṃ parigaṇanāt tatsaṃjñayoḥ karotyādiyoge sāvakāśatvāditi bhāvaḥ| tathāca pūrvavidhiprasaktipūrvakaṃ tadavivakṣāyāṃ sarvathā pūrvavidheraprasaktau ca tatpravṛttiriti bodhyam| yadyapi vibhāgānukūlavyāpārānukūlavyāpārasyāpi duhyarthatvena kartturīpsitatamamityanenaiva karmasaṃjñā sidhdyati, kartṛniṣṭhadavyāpāra viśeṣaṇaphalāśrayatayā goḥ pradhānkarmatayā na tatra tādyanupapattiḥ, payoniṣṭhavibhāgānukūlagoniṣṭhavyāpārāmanukūlavyāpārasya śabdataḥ prādhānyādanyathāsiddhaprayojanakametat sūtramityābhāti, tathāpi vibhāgānukūlavyāpāra eva yadi dhātvarthastadā'pādānatvādyavivakṣāyāmanyasūtrāprāptakarmatvārthamidamāvaśyakam| etasminnartha'pradhāne duhādīnāmityanuśāsanād gavādāvena lādayaḥ|
apradhānakarmatvaṃ coktakarmabhinnatvam| taccoktarthe gavādīnāmakṣatam dhātvarthaphalānāśrayatvāt| tathācaitatkalpe sthitaṃ dravadravyaniṣṭhavibhāgānukūlavyāpāro duherthaḥ| tatra gorapādānatvāvivakṣāyāmanena karmatvama tadvivakṣāyāṃ pañcamī| goḥ payasyanvaye tu ṣaṣṭhī| evañca gokarmakapayoniṣṭhavibhāgānukūla ekakarttṛko vyāpāra iti bodhaḥ| vibhāgāvadhitvameva goḥ karmatvam| ādyakalpe tu payoniṣṭhavibhāgānukūlagoniṣṭhayāpārānukūla ekakarttṛ varttamāno vyāpāra iti| tatra vibhāgāśrayatvāt payasaḥ karmatā| tadanukūlavyāpārāśrayatvāttu goḥ, natu vibhāgāśrayatvena, payoniṣṭhavibhāgīyasambandhasyaiva palatāvacchedakatvāt| evamanyatrāpyūhyam|
anyapūrvakamiti anyasaṃjñābādhanapūrvaṃ śāstrabodhitamityarthaḥ| `krūramabhikrudhyati' ityatra krudhadruherṣyetiprasaktaptampradānasaṃjñāyāḥ "krudhadruhorupasṛṣṭayoḥ karma"  itibādhanāt| evaṃ `vaikuṇṭhamadhiśete' ityādāvapi bodhyam| `ajāṃ grāmaṃ gamayati' `śiṣyaṃ sāstraṃ bodhayati' brāhṇamannaṃ bhojayati' `yajamānaṃ mantraṃ pāṭhayati' `ghaṭaṃ janayati nāśayati' ityādau gatibuddhīti sūtreṇa gamyādidhātvarthakartuḥ karmasaṃjñāvidhānād gamyādiprakṛtikaṇijantadhātusamabhivyāhṛtadvitīyāyā apyāśrayo'rtha| yadyapi ṇijantadhātvarthapreraṇājanyavyāpārarūpaphalāśrayatvādajādīnāmapi karmatvaṃ kar‌tturīpsitatamamityanenaiva siddham, tathāpi ṇijantena āpyamānasya cedbhavati gatyādyarthavyāpārāśrayasyaiveti niyamārthaṃ sūtram| tena `pācayatyodanaṃ devadattena' ityādau na| taduktam-
guṇakriyāyāṃ svātantryāt preṣaṇo karmatāṃ gataḥ|
niyamāt karmasaṃjñāyāḥ svadharmeṇābhidhīyate|| iti|
vācakatāsambandhena svaniṣṭhayā tṛtīyayetyarthaḥ| yadyapi karttṛsaṃjñāyāḥ paratvāddhetumati cetyanuśāsanāt svakārakaviśiṣṭakriyāyā ṇijarthasambandhāvagaterantaraṅgātvācca karmasaṃjñāyāstayā bādha evocitastathāpyanyānadhīnatvalakṣaṇārthaprādhānya-śābdaprādhānyayoḥ prayojakavyāpāre sattvena pradhānakāryyasya ca sarvato balāvattvasyā''kajāra  sūtre kaiyaṭenoktatvāt `hetumati ca' iti sūtrasthabhāṣyaprāmāṇyācca tadanurodhikarmatvasyāntaraṅgādapi karttṛtvād balavattvāniyamatvameva sāmpratam|
pare tu-`hetumati ca' ityanuśāsanādanyaniṣṭhakartṛtvanirvāhakavyāpārarūpā svatantrapreraṇā ṇijarthaḥ| kartṛtvaṃ ca kvacit prayatnaḥ, kvacidāśrayatvādi| yādṛśadhātūttarākhyāte yādṛśaṃ kartṛtvamabhidhīyate tādṛśadhātūttaraṇic‌pratyayena tādṛśakartṛtvanirvāhako vyāpāro'bhidhīyate| nirvāhakatvaṃ ca svarūpasambandhaviseṣaḥ, nāto `nāśayati' ityādāvanvayānupapattiḥ|
evañca pākādikartustatkarmatvavirahe'pi ṇyantadhātupratipādyatāvacchedakakartṛtvasya phalatvena tadāśrayatvāt pākādikartuḥ karmatvaṃ tādṛśakartṛtvaviśeṣaṇatayā sahāyādirūpasvatantrakartṛvṛttitvavivakṣāyāṃ `pācayatyodanaṃ sahāyam' ityāpi prayogaḥ sādhureva| ata eva "ajigrahattajjanako dhanustat", iti bhaṭṭiprayogaḥ svarasataḥ sahgacchate| graherjñānalākṣaṇikatayā tadupapādanaṃ tvagatikagatiḥ| yadā tu ṇic‌prakṛtyarthavyāpāre svatantrakartṛvṛttitvasya viśeṣaṇatayā vivakṣā tadā sahāyenetyeva| `gatibuddhi' iti sūtraṃ tu gatyādyarthakadhātuyoge tādṛśakartṛtvaviśeṣaṇatayā svatantrakartṛvṛttitvavivakṣā niyatā nānyatretyarthabodhanadvārā tatra kartṛpratyayāsādhutvajñāpakam| tena `pācayatyodanaṃ sahāyena' itivat `ajayā grāmaṃ gamayati' iti na|
evañca `ajāṃ grāmaṃ gamayati' ityatra grāmaniṣṭhasayogānukūlavyāpārasya yadajāniṣṭhakartṛtvaṃ nirvāhakatvasambandhena tadviśiṣṭo yo vyāpārastadanukūlakṛtīmāniti bodhaḥ| `pācayatyodanaṃ sahāyena' ityatra sahāyakartṛko yo viklittyanukūlo vyāpāro nirvāhakatvasambandhena tat kartṛclasambandhena tatkartṛtvaviśiṣṭavyāpārānukūlakṛtimāniti bodhaḥ|
`caitreṇa grāmaṃ gamyate ajā' ityātra karmākhyāte tu tādṛśabyāpāranirvāhyakartṛtānirūpakastādṛśadhātvarthaḥ karmātmanepadopasthitakartṛtve'nveti, saṃyogaphale ca dvitīyārthagramavṛttitvānvayaḥ evañca caitrakartṛkavyāpāranirvāhyakartṛtvanirūpakaṃ yad grāmavṛttisaṃyogānukūlaṃ gamanaṃ tatkartryajeti bodhaḥ| sahāyena pācyate taṇḍulaścaitreṇa' ityatra tu caitrakartṛkapreraṇānirvāhyakartṛtvanirūpako yaḥ sahāyarūpastatantrakartṛvṛttivāyapārastajjnyaviklittyāśrayastaṇḍula iti bodhaḥ| arthavivekaḥ svayamūhyaḥ|
ye tvanukūlavyāpāra eva ṇijarstadanvayini gamanādikriyaiva dhātvarthatāvacchedakaṃ phalaṃ, tacchāliyayā'jādīnāṃ prāptameva karmatvaṃ "gatibuddhi"  iti sūtreṇa niyamyate iti vadanti| teṣāṃ mate `pācayatyodanaṃ sahāyena' ityādau dvitīyātṛtīyayorniyāmakālābhaḥ| adhikamanyatrānusa-dheyamityāhuḥ|
