`vaikuṇṭhamadhitiṣṭhati'ityādāvapi dvitīyāyā ādhāra evārthaḥ, adhyādyupasṛśīṅādiyoge "adhiśīṅ"  ityādisūtrairādhārasya karmasaṃjñāvidhānāt| yadāpi kṛñarthavyāpārapāntarbhābeṇādhyādyupasṛṣṭaśīṅādīnāṃ lakṣaṇāmupagamya `kartṛrīpsitatamam' ityanenaiva karmasaṃjñā sūsādhā, tatāpi mukhyārthakatadyoge'pi karmapratyayasādhutvabodhanārtham `adhiśīṅ‌sthāsām' ityādisūtrāṇāmapyāvaśyakateti| karmapravacanīyayogavihitavibhaktestu yathāyathaṃ lakṣyalakṣamabhāvādiḥ sambandho dyotya iti vakṣyate|
upapadavibhaktīnāṃ tu ṣaṣṭhayapavādakatvāt sambandha evārthaḥ| "kālādhvanoratyantasaṃyoge"  iti vihitadvitīyāyā vyāpakatvamarthaḥ, `māsamadhīte' ityādau māsavyāpakādhyayanamiti bodhāt, dvitīyādīnāmāśrayo'rtha ityatra vibhaktipadasya pārakavibhaktiparatvena tadavirodhādityanyatra vistaraḥ|
kramaprāptaṃ tṛtāyārthaṃ nirūpayati-kartṛtīyāyā iti karaṇatṛtīyāyā vyāpārārthatvasyāpi vakṣyamāṇatvāduktam-kartriti| āśraya iti āśrayamātramityarthaḥ| āśrayārthakatve mānamupanyasyati tathāhītyādinā| nanu svātantryamitaravyāpārānadhīnavyāpāravattvam, tacca `kāṣṭhaṃ pacati' ityādau kāṣṭhādāvaprasaktam, tad‌vyāpārasya cetanavyāpārādhīnatvādata āha-svātantryañceti vyāpāraśrayatvasya karaṇe, dhātvarthāśrayatvasya karmaṇyatiprasaktatvāt tad‌vyāvṛttaye krameṇobhayam|
anye tu-kartṛpratyayasamabhivyāhāre vyāpārātāvacchedakaṃsambandhena dhātvarthaniṣṭhaviśeṣyatānirūpitaprakāratānāśryataddhātvarthāśrayatvaṃ svātantryam| tadeva ca kartṛtvam| kālikasambandhena vyāpārāśraye'tiprasaṅgavāraṇāya vyāpārātāvacchedakasambandeneti| `grāmaṃ gacchati' ityādāvuttarasaṃyogātmake phale tādṛśadhātvarthatvābhāvānna tadāśraye'tiprasaṃṅgaḥ| `pakvastaṇḍulo devadattena' ityādau phalasya viśeṣatvena devadatte'vyāptiparihārāya kartṛpratyayasamabhivyāhāra iti| sāmagrīsādhyāyāṃ kriyāyāṃ sarveṣāṃ svasvavyāpāre svātantrye'pyuktasvātantrya yugapatsarveṣvabhāvānna sūtrānarthakyamiti| tattu karmapratyayasamabhivyahṛtakartṛvyāvṛttatvād dhātvarthavyāpārāśrayatvasyaiva samyaktve guruviśeṣaṇasyānatiprayojanakatvāccopekṣyam| karmakarttari tu navyāmate phale vyāpāratvā''ropānnā'vyāptiḥ| vyāpāratvena kṛterapi saṅgrahānna tadanāśraye'cetane svarasataḥ kartṛvyāvahāraḥ| tadatiriktavyāpārasya dhātunā vivakṣāyāṃ tu kvacit kartṛtvavyāvahāro'pi| vakṣyati ca-`yadā yadīyo vyāpāraḥ' ityādi| evameva `matto bhūtaṃ na tu mayā kṛtam' ityādyupapādyamiti bhāvaḥ| tatra harivākyaṃ pramāṇayati dhātuneti| uktakriya iti viśeṣaṇadasmaduktameva svātantryaṃ irisammatamiti sūcayati|
ata eveti uktasvātantryasya kartṛtvādevetyarthaḥ|
saṅgacchata iti kṛtyāśrayatvādestattve tu tadasaṅgatiriti bhāvaḥ| uktavivakṣāyāḥ prayoganiyāmakatvaṃ draḍhayituṃ pṛcchati-nanvevamiti evaṃ vivakṣāyā eva prayoganiyāmakatve manomayasya-manomayaśabdapratipādyasyetyarthaḥ| jīvatve-saṃkalpakartṛrūpatve-brahmabhinnatve iti yāvta| etamitaḥ ityāderitaḥ-śarīrāt pretya-niḥsṛtya-etaccharīraṃ parityajyeti yāvat| etam svaprakāśatvādiguṇaviśiṣṭaṃ pūrvoktamātmānam|
abhisambhavitāsmi prāptāsmītyarthaḥ| kathaṃ saṅgacchatāmiti bhavanmate vaivakṣikayoḥ kartṛtvakarmatvayorekasminnapi sambhavāditi bhāvaḥ|
saṃjñānibandhanavirodhaṃ samarthayan kartṛtvakarmatvayorvāstavāvirodhaṃ darśayati-ucyata ityādinā| jīvasyaiva jñeyatva iti `sa kratuṃ kurvita' ityanena jīvasyaivopāsyatvabodhane ityarthaḥ| prāptikarmatvamiti anyopāsanāyā aparaprāptyaphalakatvāditi bhāvaḥ| ākhyāteneti abhisambhavitā'smītyetad‌ghaṭakenetyarthaḥ|  bādhāditi saṃjñāyorākaḍārīyatayā samveśāsambhavena parayā kartṛsaṃjñayā bādhādityarthaḥ| yagādyā''pattiriti| abhyuccayavādaḥ, prāpye karmaṇi karmavadbhāvāprasakteḥ| śadbavirodhadvārā-ekadā saṃjñādvayaprayuktakāryyānutpattidvārā, saḥ-saṃjñādvayavirodhastatra kartṛkarmatvavyavahāraheturityarthaḥ| natu kartṛtvakarmatvayoḥ pāramārthikaṃ bhedamādāyeti bhāvaḥ|
nanu viśiṣṭāśrayasya tāvatā tṛtīyā'rthatvamāyātamata āha-evañceti dhātupāttavyāpārāśrayasya karttṛtve cetyarthaḥ| āśrayamātramiti ananyalabhyatvāditi bhāva-| matāntaraṃ dūṣayati-kṛtyāśrayatvamityādinā| avyāptamiti acetane tasmiṃstad‌bādhāditi bhāvaḥ|
atra vadanti-daṇḍādiniṣṭhavyāpārasya dhātvarthatvavivakṣayā bhavaduktasvātantryavad vāstavakārakacakraprayoktṛtvasya daṇḍādāvabhāve'pi tadāropāt kartṛtvavyāvahārastasmin sūpapādaḥ| ata eva hariṇā-
prāganyataḥ śaktilābhānnayagbhāvā'ṣa़pādanādapi|
tadadhīnapravṛttatvāt pravṛttānāṃ nivarttanāt||
adṛṣṭatvātpratinidhervyatireke ca darśanāt|
ārādapyupakāritve svātantryaṃ kartturiṣyate||
ityanena karaṇādivyāpārāt pūrvaṃ karaṇādisampādakaśiktimattvena karaṇādīnāmātmādhīnatvasampādanena ca tadāyattavyāpāratvenātipravṛttānāṃ nivarttanena karttuḥ phalaprāptau svata eva nivṛttyā pratinidhyabhāvena kārakāntarābhāve'pyastyādau kartturdaśanena ca ārā (dūrā)dapyupakāritve svātantryamityarthakena kārakacakraprayoktutvameva svātantryamabhihitam|
vastuta eṣāṃ dharmāṇāmabhāve'pi śabdena yasyaite pratipādyante sa kartteti tadāśayaḥ| `pācayati caitreṇa maitraḥ' ityādau prayojyasya prayojakavyāpārādhīnatve'pi ṇiceprakṛtyarthasādhanāntaraviṣaye uktasvātantryasya sattvāt karttṛtvopapattiḥ, dhātuvācyakriyākṛtasvātantryasyaiva vivakṣaṇāt, karttṛparatantrakaraṇāderna svavyāpāramādāya karttṛtvam, prayojyasya svārthasidhdyarthamapi pravṛttyā karaṇādīnāṃ kartradhīnatvavat prayojyasya prayojakānadīnatvācca| nahi phaloddeśādhīnā karaṇādīnāṃ vyāpāravattā| prayojyasya tu tādṛśapravṛttau "sarva ime svabhūtyarthaṃ pravarttante" iti, hetumati ceti sūtrasthaṃ bhāṣyamanubhavaśca sārvajanīnaḥ pramāṇamityāhuḥ|
kṛtyāśrayatvaṃ daṇḍādāvavyāptamityuktayā naiyāyikoktakarttṛtvasyopekṣaṇīyatvamāveditam| teṣāṃ tvayamāśayaḥ-karttṛtvaṃ mukhyaṃ pūrvoktameva, āśrayapratiyogitvādirūpaṃ tu gauṇam, ubhayamapi kartṛtṛtīyayā pratipādyate|
tathāhi-`caitreṇa pacyate' ityādau kṛtimadabhedena tajjnyatvaṃ vā kriyāviśeṣaṇatayā tṛtīyayā'bhidhīyate| acetanakāṣṭhachāderapi kartṛtvavivakṣaāyāṃ tu `kāṣṭhaṃ pacati' itivat `kāṣṭhena pacyate taṇḍulaḥ' ityapi prayogād vāyapārarūpe kartṛtve tajjnyatvarūpe sakartṛkatve vā tasya lakṣaṇā| evaṃ `caitreṇa jñāyate' ityādāvāśrayatvarūpe kartṛtve ādheyatvarūpe kartṛmattve vā, naśyate ghaṭenetyādau pratiyogitvātmakakartṛtve'nuyogitvarūpakartṛmattve vā lakṣaṇā| caitraḥ pacatītyādavuktasthale sukhyaṃ gauṇaṃ vā kriyāviśeṣyatayā''khyātena bodhyate| acetane svarasataḥ kartṛvyavahārābhāvena tatra kartṛpadamapi bhāktameva|
naca yatnavata eva mukhyakartṛtve tadādāyaiva "kartṛkaraṇayoḥ"  ityādeścāritārthye gauṇakartṛtve tṛtīyādyanupapattiriti vācyam, tatsūtrasthakartṛpadasya pāribhāṣikakartṛparatvāt, tatsūtrapraṇayanasyaiva tasya tatparavye mānatvāt| sūtre svātantryañca-samabhivyāhṛtakārakāntarānadhīnatve sati kārakatvam| pūruṣavyāpārādīnakriyānukūlavyāpāravatāmeva kāṣṭhādīnāmanyānadhīnatvavivakṣayā kāṣṭhaṃ pacatītyādau kartṛtvam| anadhīnatvāntaviśeṣaṇaṃ ca, caitraḥ kāṣṭhaiḥ sthālyāṃ pacatītyatra caitraḥ kaiṣṭhāni sthālī pacantīti prayogavāraṇāya| caitraḥ pacati,sthālī pacatītyādau yatra kārakāntarāprasiddhistatrānadīnāntaviśeṣaṇaṃ na pratīyata eva, tannirvāhaśca svātantraśkatikalpanāt| viśiṣṭaśakterviśeṣaṇāviṣayakaviśeṣyabodhājanakatvāt, viśiṣṭaśaktyā nirvāhāt| caitreṇa pācayatītyādau hetukarttṛsamabhivyāhāre prayojyasya hetukartradhīnatve'pi ṇyantapratipādyapācanādikriyāyāmeva prayojakasya kārakatayā prayojyasya javyādyarthakriyākārakānadhīnatāyā avaikalpena na tadvācakapadottaraṃ tṛtīyānupapattiḥ, svatantrakartṛprayojakavyāpārasya taṇḍulakrayaṇādivat pākādāvanyathāsiddhatvādakāraṇatvena tasya pākādiprayojakavyāpāravattve'pi tatkriyākārakatvābhāvācca| ata eva caitreṇa maitraḥ pācayatītyatra, maitraḥ pacatīti na|
nanvastupadarśitarītyā caitrādeḥ svatantrakartṛtānirvāhastathāpi tadvācakapadottaraṃ tṛtīyā'nupapattiḥ, kartṛtvanirvāhakavyāpārasya ṇijarthatvena svatantraniṣṭhasya tasya ṇicā'bhidhānāditi cenna, kartṛtvanirvāhakatvasambandhena pākādyanvitahetukartṛvyapārasya ṇijarthatāpakṣe'doṣāt| kartṛtvarūpaphalāvaccinnavyāpārasya ṇijarthatāmate'pi taduparaktāśrayasyānyato bhānānirvāheṇānabhidhānasyāśratatvāt, abodhanasyaiva tatrānabhidhānapadārthatvādabhidhānasya "tiṅ‌kṛttddhitasamāsaiḥ" iti parigaṇanācca|
akhyātasya dharmyavācakatve'pi kṛtyādiviśiṣṭakartṛbodhakatvarūpābhidhānasya tatra sattvānna caitraḥ pacatītyādau tṛtīyā''pattiḥ| `caitraḥ pacati bhavati' ityādau mukhyaviśeṣyatayā tadvivakṣāyāṃ caitreṇa pacatītyādiprayogāpattirityapi na, āśrayātirikte viśeṣaṇatayā kṛtibodhanasyaivānabhidhānapadarthatvāt| tathābhidhānaṃ ca `lakṛttadvitasamāsaireva' iti ta evābhidhāyakatayā vṛttikṛtopāttāḥ|
yadyapi svatantravyāpāra eva tṛtīyāderanuśāsanam, tathāpi kṛtirūpakartṛtva eva lāghavācchaktiḥ, vyāpāradau tu lakṣaṇetyuktamanuśāsanasyānāditātparyyamātragrāhakatvāllāghavasahakṛtasyaiva tasya śkatikalpakatvāt, vyāpāre tatsattve'pi gauraveṇa tadasiddheḥ|
evañca kṛtirūpakartṛtvabodhasthale kārakāntaravyāpārānadhīnatvaṃ pratīyata eveti na tadantarbhāveṇa śaktiḥ, pramāṇābhāvāt, vyāpāralākṣaṇikakartṛpratyayena lakṣaṇayā tadantarbhāveṇa vyāpārabodhanādeva pūrvoktātiprasiṅgasya vāraṇāt, anyathā tadantarbhāveṇa kṛtau śktāvapi pūrvoktātiprasaṅgasya duṣpariharatvāt| `ghaṭo jāyate' `odanaḥ sidhdyati' ityādau ghaṭādeḥ kārakatvaṃ kartṛtvaṃ ca satkāryavādamavalambyaivopapādanīyam|
mukhyaṃ kriyākartṛtvaṃ ca na tadanukūlakṛtīmātram, ekakriyāviṣayakakṛterthatra nāntarīyakakriyānirvāhastatra tatkriyākartṛtvāvyapadeśāta| kintu tatkriyāviṣayakatve sati tadanukūlakṛtistatkartṛtvam| gurutakabhāroktolanādau tu yatra kriyāyā aniṣpattistatra gatiruktaiva| anyoddeśena nārācakṣepād yatra brāhmaṇavadhastatra maraṇānukūlanārācakṣeparūpavyāpārasya kṛtiviṣayatve'pi hiṃsālakṣaṇasyoddeśyatāghaṭitatayā tasya vipramaraṇānukūlavyāpāratvenānabhisaṃhitatvāttena rūpeṇa ka-tiviṣayatvasya tādṛśavyāpāre asattvena na brāhmaṇatvaviśiṣṭavadhakartṛtvaṃ tādṛśavyāpārakarturiti na sampūrṇaṃ prāyaścitaṃ tasyeti|
kartṛvibhāgāmāha-ayañceti anupadaṃ tṛtīyārthatvena niruktaḥ, karttetyarthaḥ| ghuddha iti hetutvakarmakartṛtvānadhikaraṇamityarthaḥ| preraṇārthakaṇicprakṛtidhātūpāttavyāpārāśraya iti yāvat| heturiti ṇijarthapreraṇāśraya ityarthaḥ| karmakartteti dhātūpāttavyāpārā''śrayatve sati ṇijarthavyāpāreṇā''pyamānatvena vivakṣita ityarthaḥ| krameṇodāharaṇānyāha-mayā hariḥ sevyāta ityādinā atra vyāpāraviśeṣyakabodhotkorttanaṃ tu kaiyaṭānusāreṇa tattvaṃ tu prāgevāvocāmaḥ| āśrayavyāpārāviti āśrayaviśiṣṭayāpāraśaktau tu ekadeśānvayaprasaṅga iti bhāvaḥ|
nanu vyāpārasya dhātutvenaiva lābhāt tatra śaktikalpanamapārthamityāśaṅkayaikasya kartṛniṣṭhavyāpārasya dhātorlābhe'pyanubhūyamānakaraṇaniṣṭhavyāpārasya padārthatvamantareṇa śābdaviṣayatānupapattyā śaktirāvaśyakī| ākhyātānnivṛttibhedāyā eva kriyāyā avagamādvyāpādadvayasya dhātvarthatvāsambhavāccetyabhipretya svoktārthasya sapramāṇatāṃ darśayituṃ karaṇasaṃjñāpratipādakaṃ sūtramupanyasyati- tathāhītyādinā| sādhakatamamiti sūtrasya kārakādhikārīyatvāt sutropāttasādhakaśabdādeva prakarṣaṃlābhe'pi gauṇādhāre ādhikaraṇasaṃjñāpalakasya `kārakaprakaraṇe śabdasāmarthyagamyaḥ prakarṣo nāśrīyate' ityarthasya bodhanāya tamabupādānamiti sūcannāha tamabartha prakarṣa iti prakarṣaśca-kārakāntarāpekṣaḥ, natu karaṇāntarāpekṣaḥ, kārakasāmānyāvācisādhakaśabdādutpannena tamapā tadavadhikaprakarṣasyaiva dyotanāt| tenāśveyā vrajatīti prayogopapattiḥ, anyathā karaṇāntarāpekṣikaprakarṣasya karaṇāntare abhāvena tadanupapatteḥ|
tādṛśeti phalotpattyavyavahitapūrvavarttotyarthaḥ| pare tu- phalāyogavyāvacchinnakāraṇatvam karaṇatvam| svavyāpyetarayāvatkāraṇasamavadhāne sati yadavyāvahitottarakṣaṇe phalaniṣpattistattvamiti yāvat| sa eva tṛtīyārthaḥ| satyantaniveśāccakṣuṣo'ndhakāre phalāyoge'pi na tatrāprasaṅgaḥ| prayojake'tiprasaṅganirāsāyāvyavahiteti vadanti|
tatra harisammatimapyāha uktañceti| atra kriyāpadaṃ phalaparam| `vāṇena hataḥ' ityatra vāṇavyāpārāvyavahitottarakṣaṇe prāṇaviyogarūpaphalāvaśyambhāvāllakṣaṇasamnvayaḥ| vivakṣyate ityanena vivakṣaiva vibhaktau prayojikā, na vāstavakaraṇatvādisattetyuktam| ata eva dāvānādareṇa balasya tatvavivakṣāyāṃ balena lunātīti prayogaḥ| vastuta iti yata itameva vastu karaṇamiti na niyamo'ta eva niścitādhikaramatvāpi sthālī tanutarakapālatvādvaivakṣikakaraṇatvavatītyarthaḥ| avakāśamiti taṇḍulaṃ sthālyāṃ pacatītyādau karaṇatvāvivakṣāyāṃ tattatsaṃjñāprayuktadvitīyāderavakāśamityarthaḥ| tathāca kāṣṭhaiḥ pacatītyādau prakṛtyarthakāṣṭhāderabhedenāśrayarūpatṛtīyārthe'nvayaḥ| tasya ca vyutpattivaicitryeṇa samānapratyayopāttatvapratyāsattyā vyāpārarūpatadarthāntare ādheyatāsambandhena phalopahitatādṛśavyāpārasya ca anukūlatāsambandhena vivilattyanukūlavyāpāre| evañca kāṣṭhābhinnāśrayako yaḥ phalopahitavyāpārastad‌viśiṣṭo viklittyanukūla ekāśrayako varttamāno vyāpāra iti bodhaḥ| vaiśiṣṭayaṃ ca dhātvarthavyāpāre janyajanakabhāvasambandhena bodhyam| atra ca `yatsannihiteṣu sarveṣu kārakeṣu karttā pravarttayitā bhavati" iti sakalakārakavyāpārasya kartradhīnatvapratipādakam "kārake" iti sūtrasathaṃ bhāṣyaṃ pramāṇam|
apare tu-`kriyāyāḥ pariniṣpattiryad‌vyāpārādanantaram' iti vākyasthānantarapadasvārasyāt tṛtīyārthavyāpārasya janyatāsambandhena phale'nvayaḥ| tathāca janyatāsagbandhena kāṣṭhāśrayakavyāpāraviśiṣṭaviklittyanukūlo vyāpāra iti bodhamāhuḥ| tṛtīyārthakaraṇatvasya vyāpāra evānvayaḥ, śabdaśaktisvabhāvānna phalāṃśe| ata eva matvarthalakṣaṇayā somādipadasya nāmadheyatvaṃ siddhāntitam| anyathā vyāpāraṃ prati karaṇatvenanvite phalāṃśe karaṇatvenānvayopapattau lakṣaṇāyā aprasaṅga eva| ata eva somena yajetetyatra somapadasya matvarthe lakṣaṇeti mīmāṃsakā ityante|
vastutastvāśrayo'vadhiruddeśya iti mūlasvārasyāt karaṇatṛtīyāyā apyāśrayamātramarthaḥ| prakṛtyarthānvitasya tasya svaniṣṭhavyāpārajanyatvasambandhena dhātvarthavyāpāre'nvaya ityeva jyāyāḥ|
naiyāyikāstvasādhāraṇaṃ kāraṇaṃ karaṇam| kāraṇe asādhāraṇyaṃ ca vyāpāravattvāmeva, natu phalāyogavyāpacchinnatvam, tasya yāvatkāraṇaghaṭitatayā gauraveṇa taddharmāvacchinne śaktayasiddheḥ| sa eva ca prakarṣassādhakatamamiti tamapā bodhyate| vyāpāraśca tajjanyatve sati tajjanyatve sati tajjanyajanakatvarūpaḥ| evañca janakatvasyāvyāvadhānāghaṭitatvena lābhasyāntarīyakatvānna tatra pṛthak‌śāktiriti vyāpāramātraṃ karaṇatṛtīyārthastasmin prakṛtyarthasyādheyatāsambandhenānvayaḥ| śeṣaṃ pūrvavadityāhuḥ|
evamiti ekasminnapi dharmiṇi kartṛtva-karamatvayoraṅgīkāra ityarthaḥ| karttṛkaraṇaśaktayoravirodha iti yāvat| śaktiviparyyayāditi "kartā śāstrārthavattvāt" iti sūtreṇa `eṣa hi draṣṭā śrotā'numantā boddhā vijñānā''tmā puruṣaḥ' ityādīnāṃ pathā karttuḥ karttvayāviśeṣapratipādakānām `yajeta' `juhuyāt' ityādīnāṃ ca sārthakyāya jīvasya kartṛtvabodhite vuddhipadavyāvahāryyavijñānamevāstu kartrityāśaṅkāyāṃ vijñānaśabdavācyabuddheḥ karttṛtvābhyupagame loke samarthasyāpi kartṛḥ sarvārthakārikaraṇāntarasāpekṣasyaiva kāryyakāritvadarśanena vijñānapadavyapadeśyatvabuddheḥ karaṇaśaktihānyāpattiratastadatiriktajīvasyaiva kartṛtvamucitamityarthapratipādakena "śaktiviparyayāt" iti sūtreṇetyarthaḥ|
na yujyeteti kartṛkaraṇaśaktayoravirodhasyaiva bhavadbhirabhidānāditi bhāvaḥ| samādhatte- tadevaiteṣāmiti| prāṇānām indriyāṇām| vijñānam grahaṇaśaktim| vijñānena vijñānaśabdavācyena manasā tajjanyabudhdyātmakavṛttayeti yāvat| ādāya gṛhītvā hṛdayaṃ svāpe gacchatīti śrutyarthaḥ|
karaṇatayeti vijñānenetyatra karaṇatayā klṛptasyetyarthaḥ. niṣpramāṇetyādeḥ karttari karamaśaktisvīkāre'pi dvayoḥ śaktayorekadā vivakṣāyāmasambhavena tadviparyayaprasaṅgābhāvādityāśayādityarthaḥ|
nanvidamayuktam, āpādanapratipādakagranthasyaivāpattāvaprāṇāṇyapratipādakatvāsambhavāt, āpatteḥ prāktanasūtrādaprakāntatvāccetyata āha-vastutastviti| abhyuccayamātrametaditi etat-śaktihānyāpattirūpaṃ dūṣaṇam| mātrapadena tātparyaviṣayatvasya vyavacchedaḥ| abhyuccayatvañca tātparyyajñānaviṣayārthasannihitatvam, upekṣāviṣayatvaṃ vā| tacca buddheḥ karaṇaśaktiviparyaye'sti| dṛṣṭāntapratipādakasūtrād buddheḥ kartṛtvanirāsasyaiva sukhyatvāvagateriti bhāvaḥ|
bhūṣaṇe tu tadevaiteṣāmityādyabhiprāyādityantaḥ pāṭho nāstyeva| nanvevaṃ karttetyādirna yujyeteti cet satyam, "anvācayamātrametad" iti pāṭhaḥ| anvācayeti vijñānasabdārthasya jīvatve śaktiviparyyayāpattiḥ sambhāvyate, natu nirbharastatretyarthaḥ| upādhivaśena paramātmanyeva tatsambhavāditi bhāvaḥ| tadeva viśadayati-yathā ceti sūtre caśabdastvarthe, parābhimatabuddhikartṛtvanirāsārthaḥ| nahi paramātmano'nyo jīvo nāma karttā bhoktāvā sambhavati, nityaśuddhamuktātmanaḥ kartṛtvādyasambhavāt, anirmokṣaprasaṅgācca|
kintu "dhyāyatīva lelāyativa" iti śruteradhyastaṃ tatttra vācyam, avidyopasthāpitaṃ tu tat parasyāpi sambhavati, "yatra hi dvaitamiva bhavati taditara itaraṃ paśyati" iti śruteḥ| vidyā'vasthāyāṃ tu tadabhāvaḥ "yatra tu sarvamātmaiva syāt kiṃ kena paśyat" oiti śruteḥ| yathā takṣā vāsyādikaraṇahastaḥ karttā duḥkhībhavati, sa eva gṛhastho vimuktavāsyādinirvyāpāraḥ sukhībhavatīti tadarthaḥ| tathāca śaktiviparyyayādityasya parābhyupagatabuddhikartṛtvanirāse tātparyam, natu baddheḥ kartṛtvena karaṇaśaktiparityāgā iti na tad‌virodha iti bhāvaḥ|
saptamyarthaṃ nirūpayati-saptamyā apīti| nanu tāvatā sūtrādādhārasya vācyatvaṃ labhyate, nāśrayasyetyata āha-tattvañceti ādhāratvañcetyarthaḥ| āśrayatvamiti āśrayatvaviśiṣṭatvamitvarthaḥ| viśiṣṭe sūtreṇa śaktibodhane'pyāśrayāṃ'śe eva sā, natu viśeṣaṇībhūtāśrayateve'pi tatprakārakaśābdabodhe āśrayaśaktijñānasya hetutākalpanayaiva tadbhānopapatterityāśayenāha-tatrāśrayāṃśaḥ śakya iti| yadvā-nanu tāvatā paramparayā kriyā''śrayasya saptamyarthatvaṃ labdham, nā''śrayamātrasyetyata āha-āśrayāṃśa iti pūrvoktarītyā kriyāyā dhātoreva lābhadanyalabhyāśraya eva śakya ityarthaḥ| tattvamiti āśrayatvamityarthaḥ| āśrayatvamātremeti śakyatā'vacchedakasya kartrādisādhāraṇyāt karaṇatṛtīyāyāśca vyāpāro'pityapinā āśrayārthatvapratipādanāditi bhāvaḥ| karttṛkarmadvāreti etādṛśārthalābhaśca tatsūtrasāmarthyādeva|
tathāhi-kārakādhikārīyeṇaitena kriyā'nvayyārasyādhikaraṇasaṃjñā vidhīyate| kriyāca dhātvarthaḥ| sākṣāttadādhārayoḥ parābhyāṃ karttṛkarmasaṃjñābhyāmākrāntatvādidaṃ sūtramanavakāśaṃ sat paramparayā dhātvarthaśrayasyādhikaraṇasaṃjñāvidhānena caritārtham, paramparāghaṭake ca kartṛkarmaṇī eveti bhāvaḥ| parayā karmasaṃjñayā bādhādeva `mokṣe icchāsti' itivanmokṣe icchati bhavatīti na prayogaḥ|
aupaśleṣikamiti saṃyokasambandhanibandhanamityarthaḥ| sāmīpyasyāpi saṃyuktasaṃyogarūpatayā saṃyogapadena tasyāpi saṅgrahāt ādheyavyāpyatā'vacchedakayatkiñcidavayavakamityarthaḥ|
vaiṣayikamiti viṣayatāsambandhanibandhanamittharthaḥ| abhivyāpakamiti ādheyavyāpyatāvacchedakayāvadavayavakamityarthaḥ| tatrādyasya kaṭe śete, gurau vasatītyudāharaṇe| dvitīyasya mokṣe icchāstīti, antyasyāntyam| `kaṭe śete' ityatra paramparayā kaṭābhinnāśrayakaḥ śayanānukūlo vyāpāra iti bodhaḥ| evamanyatrā'pyūhyam|
bhāṣye spaṣṭamiti tatra hi saṃhitādhikārakhaṇḍanāya "ayaṃ yogaḥ śakyo'vaktum| adhikaraṇaṃ nāma triprakāram-vyāpakamaupaśleṣikaṃ vaiṣayikamiti| ik‌śabdasya ac‌śabdena ko'nyo'bhisambandho bhavitumarhatyanyadata upaśleṣāt| "ikoyaṇaci" upaśliṣṭasyeti tatrāntareṇa saṃhitāgrahaṇaṃ saṃhitāyāmeva bhaviṣyati" ityuktam| atropaśliṣṭapadādhyāhāreṇa saptamītyarthe idameva bhāṣyaṃ| saṃhitāyāmityupalakṣaṇam-"tatra ca dīyate kāryyaṃ bhavavat" ityādisūtrasthabhāṣyavasyāpi tatrādhikaraṇatraividhyapratipādanāditi|
naiyāyikāstu-`saṃyogenā''dhāraḥ' samavāyenā''dhāraḥ' ityādyanugatapratītibalādādhāratvamakhaṇḍopādhiḥ| saṃyogādirūpaṃ tu na tat, kuṇḍasaṃyokino badarāderapi kuṇ‍ḍā''dhāratāpatteḥ|
nanu badarapratiyogikatvaviśiṣṭasaṃyogānuyogitāyāḥ kuṇḍādhāratāvyavahāraniyāmakatvābhyupagamānnoktadoṣaḥ, na vā kuṇḍe kuṇḍasaṃyogānuyogitāsattve'pi kuṇḍe kuṇḍamiti pratītyāpattiḥ, kuṇḍapratiyogikatvaviśiṣsaṃyogānuyogitāyāstatravirahāditi cedevamapi malle mallāntarasaṃyogasattve uktānuyogitādhrauvyānmalle malla iti vāyavahārāpatteraśakyavāraṇatvenoktaprakārasyaivānusarttavyātvāt| tathāca tādṛśamādhāratvam, ādheyatvaṃ vā saptamyarthaḥ| kasyādhāratvaṃ ityākāṅkṣāyāṃ kriyānirūpitasya tasya bādhādupasthitatvāt kartṛkarmaṇorādhāra eva seti tayoreva tadanvayaḥ|
evañ‌ca `bhūtale ghaṭaḥ' ityādau na yatra kriyāśravaṇaṃ tatrāpi kārakatvanirvāhāya tadadhyāhārasyā''vasyakatā| īdṛśasthale paramparāsambandasyāpi kriyā'nvayitvarūpakārakatvaghaṭakatā, śāstraprāmāṇyāt| ata eva `akṣaśauṇḍaḥ' ityādau sāmarthyopapattiḥ| evañcabhūtale ghaṭo'stītyatra bhūtasavṛttirghaṭo varttamānasattā''śraya iti bhodhaḥ| nañ‌samabhivyāhāre tu tādṛśasaptamyarthābhāvo ghaṭāṃśe bhāsate ityāhuḥ|
pañcamyarthaṃ nirūpayati-avadhiḥ pañcamyartha iti| viśleṣa iti vibhāga ityarthaḥ| avadhitvañca prakṛtadhātvarthavibhāgajanakatatkriyājanyavibhāgasya tatrāpi sattvādatiprasaṅgatādavasthyamatastadviśeṣaṇam prakṛteti evañca vṛkṣāt patatītyādau vibhāgajanakakriyā patatyarthaḥ| yadvakṣyati-yatra dhātunā gatirnirdiśyate tannirddiṣṭaviṣayamiti| adhikaṃ haripadyavyākhyā'vasare granthakṛtaiva sphuṭīkariṣyate|
udāsīnam-vibhāgajanakakriyānāśrayaḥ| yad‌dvaividhyamāha-calamityādi| calaḥ-aśvādiḥ, acalaḥ-grāmādiḥ|
tatkriyānāśrayatvāditi tadvibhāgajanakatatkriyā'nāviṣṭatvādityarthaḥ| kriyāśrayatvāditi tathācāvyāptiriti bhāvaḥ|
viśeṣaṇīyamiti tathāca vibāgastattad‌vayaktitvena niveśanīya iti bhāvaḥ| samādhatte pravibhaktetvityādinā tattanmeṣavṛttitvaviśiṣ‍ṭatvena bhinnatayā'vagate ityarthaḥ| vyavasthite iti tattanmeṣavṛttitvaviśiṣṭavibhāgajanakakriye tattanmeṣānāśrite bhavata evetyarthaḥ. tadevā''ha meṣāntarakriyāpekṣamityādīti| atradhitvam tadvibhāgajanakatatkriyānāśrayamityarthaḥ| parasparapadopāttayorapādānatvam tattatkriyāśrayatvānmeṣapadopāttayoḥ karttṛtvañcopapadyata ityarthaḥ| tattadvayaktitvenāpi dhātvarthakriyāviśeṣaṇīyetyāha-kriyā ceti| dhātvartha iti prakṛtadhātvarthaṃ ityarthaḥ| natu spanda iti yathākathañcitpratīyamānakriyā'pītyarthaḥ| vṛbhakarmajavibhāgavati vastre ityuktayā vastradvārakasambandhena kriyā'nvayitvaṃ vṛkṣasya sūcyate| `kriyājanakatvaṃ kārakatvam' iti mate tu tatra vṛkṣasyā'pādānatvaṃ durupapādameva| saṅgacchata iti tādṛśavṛkṣaniṣṭhakriyāyāḥ prakṛtapatadhātvarthatvābhāvena tadvati vṛkṣe nāvyāptiriti bhāvaḥ|
atra vadanti-pañcamyā āśraya iva vibhāgo'pyartho'vibhāgārthakapatatyādisamabhivyāhāre karaṇatṛtīyāyā vyāpāravad‌ anekadhātūnāṃ vibhāge śaktikalpanāpekṣayā ekasyāḥ pañcamyāstatra śaktikpanāyāṃ lāghavāt| vibhāgasya dhātvarthatve dvitīyādisthale iva vibhāgasya dhātunā lābhādāśrayamātraṃ pañcamyartha ityeva brūyāt, avadherāśrayāt pṛthugupādānavaiyarthyāt, tyajipatyoḥ paryāyatāpatteśca| prakṛte'dhodeśasaṃyogānukūlakriyaiva patatyarthaḥ| adhodeśaśca-saṃyogāṃśe upalakṣaṇam, gamyupāttasaṃyogaphala uttaradeśavat, vibhāgādhīnapūrvadeśasaṃyoganāśamantareṇa kriyāyāḥ saṃyogajanakatvāsambhavāt| tasyāvibhāgajanakatvamakṣuṇṇameva| samānapratyayopāttatvapratyāsattyā vyutpattivaicitryeṇāśrayarūpapañcamyarthasya vibhāgarūpāparatadarthe ādheyatayānvayaḥ, tasya cānukūlatāsambandhena patatyarthakriyāyām| evañca vibhāgasya dhātvarthatvābhāvānna karmatāprasaṅgaleśo'pi|
yadvā-avidhirityanugatavyāvahārādavidhitvamāśrayatvavyāpyamakhaṇḍopādhistadavacchinna eva pañcamyarthaḥ| ata evāvadherāśrayāt pṛthagupādānam| tasya ca svaniṣṭhāvadhitvanirūpakavibāgajanakatvasambandhena kriyāyāmanvayaḥ| evañca pūrvakalpe `vṛkṣāt parṇaṃ patati' ityatra vṛkṣābhinnāśrayakavibhāgajanakaparṇā''śrayakasaṃyogānukūlavyāpāra iti bodhaḥ| dvitīye tu vṛkṣābhinnāvadhiviśiṣṭā tādṛk kriyeti vibhāgajanyasaṃyogo vibhāgajanakakriyāyā na patatyarthaḥ, `patlṛ gatau' ityanuśāsanād gatiśabdasya gamlṛ gatāvityādau saṃyogānukūlavyāpāra eva prasiddheḥ, tyajarthe gativyavahārābhāvācca, phalāvacchinnavyāpāre vyāpārāvacchinnaphale ca dhātoḥ śaktimaṅgīkurvataḥ svoktaphalatvānākrāntavibhāgasya dhātvarthavyāpārāvacchedakatvāsambhavācca|
patateḥ saṃyogarūpaphalārthakatve grāmaṃ gacchatītivad grāmaṃ patatītyāpattiriti tu na śabdaśaktisvābhāvyena tadupasthitasaṃyogasyādhodeśa evānvayāt| grāmasya tattave tu `vihago grāmaṃ patati' iti tviṣṭameva| ata eva `narakaṃ patitiḥ' iti vigrahe `dvitīyā śrita' iti patitaśabdayoge dvitīyāsamāsavidhānaṃ saṅgacachate| naca vṛkṣamajahatyapi parṇe bhūmiṃ spṛśati, vṛkṣāt parṇaṃ patatītyāpattiḥ, tadupasthāpyakriyājanyadeśāntaravibhāge vṛkṣāvadhikatvānupagamāt| anyathā kriyājanyasaṃyogamātrasyaiva vibhāgajanyatvena bhūmiparṇasaṃyogasyāpi tathātvāt tvanmate'pi tādṛśaprayogasya durvāratāpatteḥ| ata eva
gatirvinā tvavadhinā nāpāya iti kathyate|
vṛkṣasya parṇaṃ patatītyeva bhāṣye nidarśitam||
ityavadhyanvayayogyavibhāgānukūlatvaṃ vinā saṃyogajanikā kriyā nāpāyaḥ| ata eva parṇaviśeṣaṇatayā vṛkṣavivakṣāyāma pārasyāvivakṣaṇād vṛkṣasya parṇaṃ patatatītyatra bhāṣye ṣṣṭh‌yuktetyetadarthakaṃ hariṇoktam| (vibhāgajanyasaṃyogasya pūrvāparībhūtānavayavakatvena ca vyāpāratvābhāvena vibhāgasya taduktaphalalakṣaṇānākrāntatvena tadubhayārthakasya pataterdhātutvānāpatteśca)|
yatra ca gṛhādapasaratītyādau vibhāga eva dhātvarthastatrāśrayaḥ, avadhimātraṃ vā pañcamyarthaḥ| tasyādheyatayā svanaṣṭhāvadhitvanirūpakatvena vā yathāyathaṃ vibhāge'nvayaḥ| parasparasmānmeṣāvapasarata ityādau tu meṣapadavācyatvāvacchedenaiva karttṛtvavivakṣā, naca parasparavācyatvāvacchedeneti na pañcamyanupapattiriti|
uktāpādāntvasya pañcamīvācyatāvacchedakatve gauravaṃ manasyādhāyā''ha-vastuta iti|
lakṣaṇamātramiti mātrapadena śakyatāvacchedakatvavyāvacchedaḥ| tathāca vyāvṛttyādiphalakaṃ niruktāvadhitvameva| pañcamyrathastu vibhāgāśrayamātrameveti bhāvaḥ| dvitīyārthoktarītyeti caitro grāmaṃ gacchatītivaccaitraḥ svaṃ gacchatīti vāraṇāya parayā kartṛsaṃjñāyā bādhādityuktarītyetyartha-| nanvevaṃ vṛkṣāt parṇaṃ patatītyatra parṇe'pi vibhāgāśrayatvasyāvaikalyāt taduttaraṃ pañcamyāpattirata āha atiprasaṅgasyeti syandasya prastravaṇajanyavibhāgāśrayatvasya vṛkṣe sattvādatiprasaṅgamāsaṅkaya niṣedhati-naceti| evamiti vibhāgāśrayatvamātrasyāpādānasaṃjñāniyāmakatve ityarthaḥ|
syāditi prastravaṇasyādhodeśasaṃyogānukūladravadravyākriyārūpatayā vibhāgasya dhātvarthābhāvena paramate āpatyasambhavādityāśayaḥ| iṣṭatvāditi calatyarthakriyājanyavibhāgāśrayatvasyevaitadarthakriyājanyavibhāgāśrayatvasyāpi pañcaminiyāmakatve kṣativirahādityarthaḥ| calatergatyarthakatve'pi `rājyāccalitaḥ' iti prayoge vibhāgānukūlakriyāparatvavat syanderapi tadarthatvamupagamyā''patteriṣṭatvamiti bhāvaḥ. jalamatra karttṛ, vṛkṣasyaiva karttṛtvavivakṣāyāntu parayā karttṛsaṃjñayā bādhenoktāpatterasambhavāditi bhāvaḥ|
nacaivamapīti evamapi-dhātvarthavibhāgā''śrayatvasyāpādānasaṃjñāniyāmakatve'pityarthaḥ| durvāramiti vṛkṣasya tyajyarthakriyājanyavibhāgāśrayatvāditi bhāvaḥ| karmasaṃjñayeti vṛkṣasya tyajarthavyāpārajanyavibhāgāśrayatvāditi bhāvaḥ| pūrvamatiprasaṅgaḥ karttṛsaṃjñāmādāyaivoktaḥ| idānīṃ tu karmasaṃjñāmādāya prāptaḥ sa nīvārita iti bodhyam| anubhavasiddha iti vastuto vibhāgāvadhitvasya vṛkṣe abādhāttajjanyabodhasya prāmātvaṃmiṣṭameva, dhātvarthavyāpārajanyavibhāgāśrayasyaiva pūrvagranthenāvadhitvokteḥ| tyajateḥ patatyarthe śaktibhramadaśāyāṃ lakṣaṇāgrahadaśāyāṃ vā `vṛkṣāt parṇaṃ tyajati' iti prayogasya prāmaṇikatvena kāraṇatāvacchedakakoṭau tyajādibhinnatvasya dhātvaṃśe niveyitumaśakyatvādityabhipretyoktam-yadīti| balāhakāditi dhūmamarujjayotiḥsamūho balāhakaḥ| tato'vayavabhūtajyotiṣāmeva vibhāgavivakṣaṇāt so'vadhiḥ| dyutiśca lakṣaṇayā niḥsaraṇapūrvakavidyotanabodhakaḥ| tathāca balāhakāvadhikaniḥsaraṇottarakālikaṃ vidyut‌karttṛkaṃ vidyotanamiti bodhaḥ| niḥsṛtyeti niḥsaraṇapūrvake vidyotane dhātorvṛttimupagamya pañcamyupapādyotyarthaḥ| ata evopāttaviśeṣyasvāntarbhāvo vakṣyamāṇaḥ saṅgacchete|
buddhiparikalpitamiti bauddha (āropita) vibhāgāvadhitvamāśrityetyarthaḥ| tādṛgapādānatvāśrayaṇe pramāṇaśaithilyaṃ matvā''ha-pṛthagiti| sākṣāditi mukhyavṛttyetyarthaḥ| dhātunetyasya samabhivyāhṛtetyādiḥ| gatiriti vibhāgajanakakriyetyarthaḥ| upāttaviṣayamiti samabhivyāhṛtadhātunopāttaḥ lakṣito gatirūpo viṣayo yatretyarthaḥ| apekṣitakriyamiti anupāttadhātvarthakriyāsākāṅkṣamityarthaḥ|
adhyāhṛtyeti arthādhyāhārābhiprāyeṇa| padādhyāhāravāde tu ākaṅakṣitakriyovācakapadānusandhāne'pyanupāttatvarū6paviśeṣaṇaghaṭitalakṣaṇāvaikalyaṃ bodhyam| pramāṇaṃ tatraśabdādhyāhāravāde `puṣpebhyaḥ spṛhayati' ityādau spṛhayatyādiyoge sampradānādisaṃjñāvidhāyakāni `spṛherīpsitaḥ'  ityādisūtrāṇi, taistattaddhātuyogeva taddhodhanāt| arthadhyāhāre tu `kriyārthopapadasya' ityādisūtram "lyablope" ityādivārttikāni ca|
anye tvidamitthaṃ vyācakṣate-yatra vibhāgāstajjanakakriyā cobhayaṃ dhātunā'bhidhīyate tadādyam| yatra vibhāgo'dhyāhṛta dhātunā tathā taddvitīyam| yatra tūbhayamapyadhyāhṛtadhātvabhidheyaṃ tattṛtīyamiti| adhikamanyato'vadhāryyam|
caturthyarthaṃ nirūpayati-uddeśya iti nanūddeśyatvaṃ kāmanāviṣayatvam, tacca `viprāya gāṃ tatāti' ityatra kriyājanyaphalabhāgitayā kāmanāviṣaye gavādāvatiprasaktamatastadhdyāvṛttam, tatsūtraprāmāṇyena darśayati-tacca karmaṇetyādi karmaṇetyanantaraṃ sambandhumiti śeṣaḥ| karma cātra pāribhāṣikam, "karmaṇā yamabhipraiti"  iti kriyāvyāpyamānasya sampradānasaṃjñāvidhānāt| ata eva tadakarmakaviṣayam|
nanu `grāmaṃ gacchati' ityatra grāmarūpakarmajanyasukhādiphalabhāgitayoddeśye karttari atiprasaṅgo'ta āha-karaṇabhūteneti karaṇatvañca sambandhakriyā'pekṣam, kriyāvyāvahitavyāpāravata eva karamatayā vyāpāre karmavṛttitvalābhena tad‌vṛttitvasya dhātvarthatāvacchedakaphala eva sambhavāt| karmavṛttitajjanyatvasya grāmajanyasukhe asattvānnoktātiprasaṅga iti bhāvaḥ| yamityasyānupādāne yo'bhipraitītyarthasyāpi lābhāt prathamāntārthakarttaryatiprasaṅgaḥ syādatastadupāttam|
yadyapi `gaurbrāhmaṇasya bhavatu' itīcchāviśeṣyatayā gāmevāvagāhate tathāpi sūtrasya phalitārthaparatvānna doṣaḥ| tathāca karmajanyatad‌vṛttiphalabhāgitvaprakārako yadviśeṣayako'bhiprāyaḥ sa sampradānamiti sūtrārthaḥ| tādṛśaphalasambandhitvaprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣyatāśraya iti yāvat|
`viprāya gāṃ tatādi' ityādau parasvatvajanakasvasvatvadhvaṃsāvacchinnecchātmakastyāgo dādhātvarthaḥ| uddeśyatvaṃ caturthyarthaḥ| (nirukvaviśeṣyatāśrayarūpasya tasya svavṛttiviśeṣyatānirūpakatvasambandhena tyāge'nvayaḥ) gorūpakarmaṇaścā''śrayatvena svatvarūpaphale| tathāca govṛttisvatvajanakasvasvatvadhvaṃsānukūlo viprābhinnoddeśyakastyāga iti vaiyākaraṇamate bodhaḥ| uddeśyatvaṃ ca vipre gojanyasvatvabhāgitayecchāviṣayatvameva, evaṃ vṛkṣāyodakamāsiñcatītyatrāpyudakaniṣṭhakriyājanyasaṃyogaphalabhāgitayecchāviṣayatvāt sampradānatvamūhyam|
naiyāyikāstu-parasvatvecchādhīnasvasvatvadhvaṃsecchātyāga iti mate svasvatvadhvaṃsāvacchinnatyāgo dadātyartha-| viśeṣyatvaṃ caturthyarthaḥ| tasya svanirūpakaniruktecchājanyatvasambandhena dadātyarthatyāge gorūpakarmaṇastvādheyatayā svasvatvadhvaṃsarūpaphale| tathāca govṛttisvasvatvadhvaṃsajanako niruktasambandena vipraniṣṭhaviśeṣyatāviśiṣṭo yastyāgastadāśrayaścai iti `viprāya gāṃ dadāti caitraḥ' vākyād bodhaḥ|
yadi tu gaurviprasya bhavatu mametīcchaiva tyāgaḥ, saca gavi svasvatvadhvaṃsamiva parasvatvamapi avagāhate tadā svasvatvadhvaṃsapūrvakaparasvatvotpattyavacchinnatyāgau dādhātvarthaḥ| eva़ñca uktasthale nirūpitatvenecchāviṣayatvaṃ caturtharyarthaḥ| tasya ca svasvatvadhvaṃse'nvayād vipranirūpitatvena icchāviṣayagovṛttisvasvatvadhvaṃsapūrvakaparasvatvotpattyavacchinnatyāgā''śraya iti bodhaḥ| janyasvatvasya govṛttitve'vagate'rthāttasmiṃstajjanyatvaṃ labhyate eveti gojanyasvatvanirūpakatvenecchāviṣayatvarūpamuddeśyatvaṃ viprasyākṣatam| vṛkṣāyodakamāsiñcatītyatra tu saṃyogāvacchinnadravadravyakriyāvacchinnavyāpāro dhātvarthaḥ. vṛttittvecchāviṣayatvaṃ caturthyarthaḥ| sa ca dhātvarthatāvacchedakasaṃyogānvayī| dvitīyārthavṛttitvaṃ tu dravadravyakriyānvayi| tathāca vṛkṣavṛttitvenecchāviṣayo yaḥ saṃyogastadavacchinnā yā udakābhinnadravyāvṛttikriyā tadavacchinnavyāpārānukūlakṛtimāniti bodhaḥ|
nacaivaṃ sicadhātvarthatvarthatāvacchedakadravadravyakriyāyāṃ dvitīyārthavṛttitvānyavaye payasā vṛkṣaṃ siñcatīti na syāt, tādṛśakriyāyā vṛkṣāvṛttitvāditi vācyam| tatra saṃyogāvacchinnadravyākriyāyā eva dhātvarthatayā tadavacchedakībhūtasaṃyoga eva vṛkṣavṛttitvānvayānnānupapattirityāhuḥ|
putṛbhyo dadyādityādau tu rudrāya gāṃ dadātītivat sampradānatvamastyeva| tyāge baijātyaniveśācca na kretuḥ sampradānatvam| vṛkṣāyodakamāsiñcatītyādau tu caturthyarthe gauṇasampradānatvam| tatra sicerdravyavṛttisaṃyogānukūlā kriyā| udakādipadasamabhivyāhāre ca saṃyogānukūlakriyāmātramarthaḥ| caturthyastu vṛttitvam| ittthañca vṛkṣattyudakīyasaṃyogāvacchinnakiyetyādibodhaḥ|
atra cutarthodvitīyābhyāṃ tulyabodhajananttatra dvitīyā mā bhūditi sampradānasaṃjñānuśiṣyate| vṛkṣaṃ payasā siñcatītyādau tu vṛkṣaśabdāt dvitīyā bhavatyeva| tatra tṛtīyārtho'bhedastasya dravadravye'nvayaḥ| dvitīyārthavṛttitvasya tu saṃyoge'nvayaḥ| tathāca vṛkṣavṛttisaṃyogānukūlodakābhinnadravyākriyeti bodhaḥ|
`śatrave'straṃ muñcati' atra duḥkhoddeśyakatvaṃ cuturthyarthaḥ| mocanaṃ vibhāgāvacchinnakriyā, kriyānukūlavyāpāro vā| prakṛtyarthasya vṛttitvena caturthyarthaikadese duḥkhe'nvayaḥ| evaṃ mitrāya dūtaṃ preṣayatītyatra vārttādānoddeśyakatvam| putrāya dhanaṃ preṣayatītyatra prītyuddeśyakatvaṃ caturthyartha ityādyūhyamityāhuḥ|
tanna samyak, sampradāne hi caturthi| naca bhavaduktasampradānatve kiñcinmānam yena vṛkṣāyetyatra caturtho syāt| kiñca saṃyogarūpadhātvarthānvayitvāviśeṣādudakapadādapi caturthyāpattiḥ| bādhakaṃ karmatvaṃ tu vṛkṣe'pyakṣatamiti na kiñcidetat| idameveti niruktoddeśyatvamevetyarthaḥ| pūrvatantre mīmāṃsāyām| ata evetati śeṣitvasyārthakarmaprayojakatvādevetyarthaḥ|
prāsanavaditi "prāsanavanmaitrāvaruṇāya daṇḍapradānaṃ kṛtārthatvāt" ityādhikaraṇe ityarthaḥ| prāsanadṛṣṭāntaścaivam jyodiṣṭome śrūyate "nītāsu dakṣiṇāsu cātvāle kṛṣṇaviṣāṇaṃ prāsyati" iti| yadā yajamānena dattā dakṣiṇā ṛtvigbhirnātāstadā yajamānaḥ svahaste dhṛtaṃ kṛṣṇamṛgaśrṛṅgaṃ cātvālanāmakagartte prakṣipediti tadarthaḥ| tatraivaṃ sandihyate-kimetat prāsanamarthakarma, pratipattikarmeti vā? arthakarmaiva bhavitumarhati, saprayojanatvāt| pratipattitāyāṃ tu apūrvābhāve nirarthakameva syāditi pūrvāpakṣe-"kṛṣṇaviṣāṇayā kaṇḍuyati" iti śrutyā kṛtārthasya pratipattyapekṣatvāt pratipattikarmataiva yuktā| nacānarthakyam, prāsanakriyāprayuktāpūrvābhāve'pi cātvāla eveti niyamāpūrvasadbhāvāditi siddhantitam| tadvuddaṇḍadānaṃ na pratipattiriti yojanā| ityadhikaraṇa iti caturthādhyāya iti śeṣaḥ|
jyotiṣṭomaṃ prakṛtyaiva some dīkṣitāya daṇḍaṃ prayacchatīti daṇḍasya dīkṣābhivyāñjakatvaṃ vidhāyakrīye some " maitrāvarūṇāya daṇḍaṃ prayacchati" iti śrūyate| tū daṇḍadānasyārthapratipattikarmabhāvasandehe'dhyavaryuṇā dīkṣāsiddhayai dattadaṇḍasyāsomakrayādyajamānadhāraṇena kṛtārthatvādupayuktasya tasya dānaṃ pratipattikarmaiveti pūrvapakṣe, " daṇḍī praiṣānanvāha" iti śravaṇānmaitrāvaruṇasya praiṣānuvāde'valambanāya daṇḍopekṣitaḥ. eva़ñca bhaviṣyadupayogasadbhāvāt dānavyāpyamānadaṇḍayāpyamānatvena maitrāvaruṇasya prādhānyācca daṇḍadānasya pratipattikarmaṇa upayuktasaṃskārarūpādarthakammaivopayokṣyamāṇasaṃskārarūpatvena prāśastyāccārthakarmataivocitā,upayojayitumeva sarvatra saṃskārapravṛtteḥ| upayukte tu pratipattirūpasaṃskārasya satkāramātraparyyavasāyitvena kāryaparyāvasānābhāvādapraśastatvamato maitrāvaruṇasaṃskārāya taddānamarthakarma| tathā sati nirūḍhapaśau asatyapi dīkṣite daṇḍasampādanasyaivaitad dānaprayojakamiti siddhāntitam|
arthakarmatve ca śeṣitvaṃ prayojakam| tacca caturthiśrutyā maitrāvaruṇasya bodhyata ityāha-caturthiśrutyeti nanu yatra dādhāturadhīnīkaraṇādyarthakastatrādhinatvādirūpaphalanirūpakatayecchāviṣayatvarūpasampradānatvasya rajākadāvapi sambhavāt "rajakāya vastraṃ dadāti" "hotre uṣṭraṃ dadāti" ityādiprayogāḥ prasajjyerannityāśaṅkāmiṣṭāpattyā pariharati-rajakāyeti| tatra pramāṇamupanyasyati-khaṇḍikopādhyāya iti| dadātīti dādhātvarthasaṃyogasambandhitvenecchāviṣayatvarūpoddesyatvasya tatra sattvāditi tadbhāvaḥ| asmin prayoge dadātiḥ saṃyogānukūlavyāpārārthakaḥ| capeṭāpadārthastu prasṛtakaratalam| " na śūdrāya matiṃ dadyāt" ityatra dadātirbodhanārthakaḥ| matipadārthaśca tajjnakaṃ vedādiśāstramiti bodhyam|
svasvatvanivattiti parasvatvotpattyavacchinnasvasvatvadhvaṃsecchārūpaṃ vyāpāram| artham dādhātvarthamityarthaḥ| tathācoktarāthakadādhātvarthakarmaniṣṭhaphalabhāgitayecchāviṣayatvameva sampradānatvam| anvarthamahāsaṃjñākaraṇasāmarthyāduktasthale ca dadātestadarthakatvābhāvena rajakasya vastraṃ dadātītyeveti teṣāmāśayaḥ| uktabhāṣyaprāmāṇyādanvarthatvamevāsiddham| mahāsaṃjñākaraṇaṃ tu prācāmanurodhādeva, sarvanāmasthānasaṃjñāvadityasvarasādāha  āhuriti|
nanvajāṃ grāmaṃ nayatītyatrājāyāṃ niruktasampradānasya sattvāttadvācakāccaturthyāpattiriti cet syādeva yadi parayā karmmasaṃjñāyā sampradānatvasya na bādha iti| `yūpāya dāru' ityādau na sampradānacaturtho, api tu tādarthye| tādarthyañca-sa evārthaḥ prayojanamasya tattvām| samabhivyāhṛtārthe tādarthyavivakṣāyāṃ tadvācakāccaturthiti `tādarthye caturthī vācyā' iti vārttikārthaḥ|
prayojanatvañca na janyatvam, duḥkhāya pāpamityasyāpyāpatteḥ| nāpi janyatayecchāviṣayatvam, svargādeḥ puṇyādijanyatayecchāviṣayatvena svargāya puṇyamityāpatteḥ| naceṣṭāpattiḥ, tathā sati paktuṃ vrajatītyrathe `pākāya vrajati' iti prayoganirvāhāya "tumarthācca"  itisūtrapraṇayanavaiyartyāpatteḥ| pākāderniruktavrajanādyarthatayaiva tadvācakādvārttikena caturthyupapatteḥ| api tu samabhivyāhṛtapadār‌thaniṣṭhavyāpārecchānusūlecchāviṣayatvam| tatprayojanakatvarūpaṃ tādarthyaṃ tu tadicchādhīnecchāviṣayavyāpārāśrayatvam| dāruṇo yūpecchādhīnecchāviṣayatakṣaṇādirūpavyāpāravattaya yūpārthatvāttadvivakṣayā vūpapādaccaturthī| tadarthaścecchādhīnecchāviṣayavyāpārāśrayatvam| prathamecchāyāṃ yūpādeḥ pravṛtyarthasya viṣayitvenānvayaḥ| tasyā''śrayatayā dārvādau, evaṃ randhanāya sthālītyādāvapyahyamiti kecit,
nanna manoramam, tadārthyasyoktarūpatve sukkaye hariṃ bhajatītyatra caturthyanupapatteḥ| nahi bhajanasya muktīcchādhīnecchāviṣayavyāpāravattā nāpi mukteḥ sampradānatā, karmajanyaphalabhāgitayānuddśyatvāt| tasmāttadicchādhīnecchāviṣayatvameva tattādarthyam| svārgāya puṇyamityāstviṣyanta eva| vārttikaṃ dṛṣṭvā sūtrakṛto'pravṛtterna "tumarthāt" iti sūtrānupapattirapīti| namaḥsvastyādiśabdayoge'pi tadarthaviseṣavācakāt "namaḥ svasti"  sūtreṇa caturthī vidhīyate| tatra namaḥpadārthastyāgo natiśca| tatra natiḥ svāpakarṣabodhanānukūlo vyāpāraḥ| `haraye nāmaḥ `eṣo'rghaḥ śivāya namaḥ" ityādau tyāgaḥ| caturthyā ca tatroddeśyayatvaṃ pratyāyyate| tyāgaśca yadi śivasyāyaṃ bhavatviti phalecchādhīno na mametyākārakaḥ svasvatvecchābhāvarūpastadā taduddeśyatvaṃ tyāgajanakeccāyāḥ svatvabhāgitayā viṣayatvam| yadi tyāgarūpecchaivānyadīyatvena tyajyamānagataṃ svatvamapi viṣayīkaroti tadā svatvabhāgitayā viṣayatvameva taduddeśyatvam, natu svīkārāśrayatvam, śivādervigrahavato'pi svīkārābhāvāttadākārakatayā dhyātasya mantrasya tyāgoddaśyatāmate tadabhāvācca|
tyajyamānopacārādau śivādinirūpitasvatvābhāve'pi tadīyatvena tatra dravye svatvāvagāhinicchāvisaṃvādirūpaiva| anyadīyasvatve bādhitaṃ śivādinirūpitatvaṃ śivādīnirūpitaprasiddhasvatvāntare vā bādhinaṃ tad‌dravyāsambandhamavagāhate|
tatra prathame svatvaprakārakecchājanyatvaṃ janyatāsambandhena tādṛśeccāvattvaṃ vā caturthyarthaḥ| tatra prakṛtyarthasya śivāderviṣayatāviśeṣasambandhenānvayaḥ| dvitīye viṣayatāviśeṣa eva saḥ| tatra ca prakṛtyarthaśivādernirūpitatvasambandhenānvayaḥ| namaḥ-padārthatyāgasya vi,yatāviseṣasambandhenārghādidravye'ḍanvayaḥ| dravyasyaiva vā viśeṣyatayā bhāsamānatyāge'nvayaḥ| śivāyotsṛjatītyādāviva nātrānirākarttṛsampradānatā, namaḥpadasyādhātutvāt|
namaḥpadena ca svaprayoktṛpuruṣakarttṛkatvoparāgeṇaiva tyāgo bodhyate, anyoccaritanamaḥpadenānyadīyatyāgābodhanāt, tatpuruṣoccāritanamaḥpadāttyāgabodhe tyāge tyajyamānadravye ca tadīyatvasaṃśayānudayāt| ata eva tadīyatvabodhanāyāhaṃ dade itivat, `ahaṃ namaḥ' ityādi na prayujyate| kaṝtvijā ca yajamānarūparuruṣāntarīyadravyatyāge svīyatvamāropyaiva pūjāyāṃ namaḥpadaṃ prayujyate| sa cāropastasmin viśeṣadarśanadaśāyāmāhāryatayopapadyate| pratinidhiprayuktadānavākyasyeva pūjāyāṃ namaḥ-padaghaṭitavākyasya bādhitārthakatve'pyadṛṣṭajanakatvaṃ na virūddham, śāstraprāmāṇyādityādyanyatra vistaraḥ|
tad vijate idamiti "karmaṇā yamabhipraiti"  iti saṅketitaṃ sampradānaṃ cetyarthaḥ| trividhamityanenānvitam| tyāgāṅgaṃ karmaṇepsitaṃ karturanirākaraṇāditribhyaḥ sampradānavyapadeśaṃ labhata iti yojanā| svaniṣṭhoddeśyatānirūpakatvasambandhena tyāge samabhivyāhṛtadhātvarthe viśeṣaṇatayā pratīyamānatvaṃ tadaṅgatvam| tena vṛkṣāyodakamāsiñcatītyādau na vṛkṣāderādhikyam| vastutastu vṛttikṛnmate nāyaṃ vibhāgaḥ| tena vṛkṣāderasaṃgrahe'pi na kṣatiḥ| tathāca samabhivyāhṛtadhātvarthe iti na vyākhyeyam|
idaṃ ca viśeṣyaṃ tadbhedaniyāmakamāha-anirākaramāditi pravṛttivṛttyatarānukūlavyāpāraśūnyatvādityarthaḥ| karmaṇepsitamiti karmajanyaphalabhāgitayecchāviṣayaḥ| preraṇeti apravṛttasya karttustyāgādāviṣṭasādhanatābodhanaṃ preraṇā, jāteṣṭasādhanatābodhe aprāmāṇyaśaṅkayā śithilasya kartustādṛśaśaṅkāvidhūnanakṣamapramāṇopanyāsena pravarttanaṃ karmaniṣṭasvatvādiphalābhyupagamamātrānukūlavyāpāravattvaṃ vānumatistābhyāmityarthaḥ| `prakṛtadhātvarthakarmajanyaphalabhāgitayecchāviṣayatve sati karttṛpravṛttinivṛttyanyatarānukūlavyāpārābhāvavattvam' anirākarttṛtavam| niruktaviṣayavattve sati karttṛsambandhityāgādiviṣayakeṣṭasādhanatābodhaupayivyāpāravattvaṃ prerakatvamityādyuhyam|
sūryāyārdhyaṃ dadātīti atra matabhedena dādhātoḥ parasvatvecchādhīnasvasvatvābhāvecchārūpaḥ| parasvatvagocarasvasvatvābhāvecchārūpo vā tyāgo'rthaḥ| anvayabodhastu "namaḥ svasti"  itisūtroktadiśohanīyaḥ| sarvatreti na tatsūtravihitadvitīyādyantaprayoreṣvityarthaḥ| abhedaḥ saṃsarga iti abhedātmakaḥ saṃsargo viśiṣṭabuddhiniyāmako viśeṣa ityarthaḥ| abhedasaṃsargakaprakṛtyarthaprakārakabodhaṃ prati sambandhārthakavibhaktibhinnapratyayajanyopasthiterhetutvāditi bhāvaḥ|
yattu sambandhasya sambandhibhedaniyatatvādabhedasya sambandhatve mānābhāvādabhedasaṃsargaṃ itarasambandhānavacchinna viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāva iti, tanna, viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvasyāpi sambandhaniyatatatvena tadasattve tasyāpyasambhavāt| ata eva bhāsamānavaiśiṣṭayapratiyogitvaṃ viśeṣaṇatvamiti ṭīkākṛtaḥ| kathamanyathā `dviṣṭho'pyasau parārthatvāt' iti viśeṣaṇatvena vivakṣitasya sambandhaṃ vinā viśeṣaṇvāsambhavena tadākāṅkṣitvāditi svayaṃ vyākhyātam|
naca tasya sambandhetve `nīlo ghaṭaḥ' ityādau ṣaṣṭhayāpattiriti vācyam, `rāhoḥ śiraḥ"ityādāvaupādhikabhedasambandhamādāya ṣaṣṭh‌yupapādanaparasyānekāvasthāyuktarāhoḥ "idamekāvastāyuktaṃ śiro'vayavaḥ" iti bhāṣyaprāmāṇyena bhedamūlakasambandha eva ṣaṣṭhītyarthalābhāt abhedasambandhe tadāpādanāsambhavāt,
tādṛśabhāṣyeṇābhedasambandhasya prātipadikārthaviśeṣyatvāvivakṣaṇādvā, sambandhasya sambandhibhedaniyatatvaṃ tvasiddhameva| yadi bhedaniyatatvamevetyāgrahastadā sarvasādhārāṇyāya sambandhitāvacchedakabheda eva niyāmakatvenābhyupeyatām|
naca tatsambandhatve mānābhāvaḥ, sarvasiddhanīlādiviśiṣṭabuddhereva mānatvāt| śābdabodhe tadbhānaniyāmikā ca prakṛtipratyayaviśeṣayoḥ samānādhikaraṇaprātipadikārthayośca samabhivyāhārarūpākāṅkṣā| ata eva "arthavat"  sūtre bhāṣye " eṣāṃ padānāṃ sāmānayeṃ varttamānānāṃ yadviśeṣe paryyavasānaṃ sa vākyarthaḥ" ityuktam|
anvitābhidhānavādimatena cedam, tanmate'nvitasyaiva padārthatvāt| tathāca sāmānyarūpeṇa sanbandhavācakānāṃ padānāṃ yadviśeṣa ityādestattadanuyogipratiyogikatvavaiśiṣṭayabodhakatvam| ākāṅkṣādisamavadhānāt sa eva vākyārtho vākyajanyobodhaviṣaya ityarthaḥ| vākyaśaktayabhiprāyaṇa vā tathābhidhānam| prātipadikārtasūtre bhāṣye "padasāmānādhikaraṇyamupasaṅkhayānamadhikatvāt" ityasya tu 'vīraḥ pūruṣaḥ' ityādau viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvaprayojakaprātipadikārthātiriktābhedasaṃsargasyāpi bhānāt prātipadikārthamātrābhāvāt prathamā na prāpnotītyataḥ prathamāyā upasaṅkhayānamārabdhavyamityatyarthānna tadvirodho'pi|
kaiyaṭenāpi na vākyārthatvāditi tatpratyākhyānāvasare vīraprātipadikādanapekṣitaśabdāntarohitaviśeṣaṇabhāvāt svārthamātraniṣṭhāt prathamā vidhīyate ityatra pratipāditapadamapahāyohitaśabdaṃ prayuñjānena viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvaniyāmakatvamitara sambandasyaivāṅgīkṛtam|
vastutastu prakṛte viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvasya sambandhatānupapannaiva, sambanadhasya sambandhibhedaniyatatvāditi tvadukteḥ| viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇayorbhāva iti vyutpattyā tasya viśeṣyatvaviśeṣaṇatvamātraparyyavasāyitayā pratyekaṃ tayoradviṣṭhatvāccānuyogitvapratiyogitvābhyāṃ dviṣṭhatvopapādane'pyekasyaiva sā syānna samudityordvayoḥ| svasvāmibhāvavat śabdānugamamātrasyārthaparicchedakatvāt sambandāntare ṣaṣṭhī, na viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvasambande ityatra mānābhāvācc| tasmādyathektameva ramaṇīyamiti|
nanu vibhaktināṃ dharmavācakatvasya tantrāntarasiddhatayā kathaṃ tatra prakṛtyarthasyābhedenānvayaḥ, ayogyatvādana āha vibhaktīnāmiti kārakavibhaktīnāmityarthaḥ| dharmivācakatvāt-śaktimacchaktatvādityarthaḥ.
nanvastu śaktirevārtho'ta āha dharmavācakatva iti| sūtrasvarasabhaṅgāpatteriti tad‌ghaṭakakarmādipadānāṃ dharmaparatāṃ vinoktārthalābhāttatparatve tadabhṅgaḥ spaṣṭa evetyarthaḥ| tathārthasya sūtrakārasammatatve, karmatve dvitīyetyādyeva brūyāditi bhāvaḥ|
nanu karmaṇītyādau viṣayasaptamībodhya ityarthaḥ| tathāca vibhaktīnāṃ dharmavācakatve'pi tasyāśrayatayā prakṛtyarthe'nvayena karmabodhāt tatphalānvayini vṛttireva dvitīyārtha itmate'pi tulyavittevedyatayā taṇḍulādīnāṃ phalāśrayakarmatvādinā bhānānna sūtrasvarasabhaṅgo'ta āha karmarthaketi| kṛti-`pakvastaṇḍulaḥ' ityādau| tadvite-`śatyo'śvaḥ' ityādau| ādipadāt `pacyamānastaṇḍulaḥ' ityādau laḍādeśaśānac‌saṃgrahaḥ|
tathādarśanāditi dharmivācakatādarśanādityarthaḥ| tathāca karmaparāmarsakatacchabdaghaṭitayorityādisūtravihitaktādīnāṃ dharmivācakatvavatrāpi tadvācakatvameva nyāyyamiti bhāvaḥ| nanu tatra padārthāntarasāmānādhikaraṇyānupapattyāstu tathātvamatra tūktāpattyabhāvenāstu dharmavācakatvamevetyata āha dvitīyādyarthaketi dvitīyādivibhaktīnāṃ dharmavācakatve tadarthavihitasamāsasyāpi dharmavācakatvāpattyā "prāptodakaḥ" ityādau udakakarttṛkaprāptikarmatvasya samāsārthasya samānavibhaktika padopasthāpyagrāmādāvabhedānvayānupapatteratrāpi jāgarūkatayā dvitīyādividhāyakasūtrasthakarmādipadānāṃ dharmivācakatvamāvaśyakamiti bhāvaḥ|
nanu tvanmate'pi ṣaṣṣṭhayāḥ sambandhārthakatayā tadarthavihitabahuvrīheḥ padārthāntasāmānādhikaraṇyānupapattestaulyena tatra lakṣaṇayā viśiṣṭaśaktyā vā sāmānādhikaraṇyasyopapādyatayānyatrāpi tayaiva rītyā nirvāhe kṛtaṃ supāṃ dharmivācakatvenetyata āha-supāṃ karmādayo'pyarthā iti|
yadi karmatvādidharmo dvitīyādivācyatayā vivakṣito'bhaviṣyat tadā karmatvādayo'pyarthā ityevāvakṣyat| tadanuktyā tu teṣāṃ dvitīyādyarthatvamaprāmāṇikam, minitrayānuktatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
śaktivādapakṣamavalambyāha-āśrayasyāpīti āśrayatvena bhānaṃ tvasiddhameveti bhāvaḥ| āśrayatvamātramiti dvitīyātṛtīyāsaptamīnāmiti śeṣaḥ| mātrapadenāśrayavyāvacchedaḥ|
nanvāśrayatvasya śakyatve āśrayatvatvaṃ śakyatāvacchedakaṃ vācyam, taccāśrayatvetarāvṛttitvaghaṭitatvād gurviti śakyatāvacchedakagauravamevaitasmin kalpe doṣa ityata āha tadeveti āśrayatvamevetyarthaḥ| `eva'kāreṇa niruktāśrayatvatvavyudāsaḥ| tasyākhaṇḍatvasyopapāditatvānnanāvyaktiviśeṣyakaśaktigraha eva svetaradharmasyāvacchedakatvāpekṣaṇāditi bhāvaḥ| karttṛtṛtīyāyā āśraya(tva)mātrārthakatvādāha-karaṇeti| vyāpāro'pītyapināśraya(tva)samuccayaḥ|
vastutastu atrāpi `vyāpāramātramarthaḥ' ityeva vaktumucitam, apiśabdasyāvadhāraṇārthakatvāt|
vibhāgamātramiti mātrapadenāśrayatvavyāvacchedaḥ, vibhāgasyāśrayatayānvayādeva vṛkṣādāvavadhitvalābhāditi bhāvaḥ| vastutastu vṛkṣāderavadhitvarūpasvarūpasambandhena pañcamyarthavibhāge'nvanaya ityuktam| uddeśyatvamātramityanenāpyāśrayatvasya saḥ|  ata eveti karmatvādiśaktestattadvibhaktivācyatvakathanādevetyarthaḥ|
na viruddhayata iti śaktigrahe jātivyāktyorubhayorbhānāviśeṣ'pi jātireva śakyā, `nāgṛhītaviśeṣaṇanyāyāt', `viśeṣyaṃ nābhādhā gacchet' itinyāyācca| vyāktiśaktau vyabhicārāt tāsāmānantyācceti tatra siddhāntitamiti bhāvaḥ| kārakavibhaktyarthaṃ nirūpya prasaṅgāt ṣaṣṭhayathaṃ nirūpayati mūle-sambandha iti| "śeṣe ṣaṣṭhī"  iti sūtrāt kārakaprātipadikārthabhinnaḥ svasvāmibhāvādisambandhaśca tatra śeṣapadārthaḥ| tatrānyavibhaktīnāṃ matabhedena dharmidharmavācakatvavadetasyāstathā bhramo mā bhūdityāśayenāha sāre  sambandhamātramiti pakṣadvaye'piti śeṣaḥ|
sa ca kvacit sambandhatvena sāmānyadharmeṇa vācyaḥ, kvacicca viśeṣarūpeṇaiva| tatra sāmānyarūpeṇa "mātuḥ smarati" ityādau, viśeṣarūpeṇa vivakṣāyāṃ svasvaviṣaye tattadvibhaktīnāṃ prasarāt| ' rājñaḥ pūruṣaḥ" ityādau tu viśeṣarūpeṇaiva anekasambandhasattve'pi sambandhaviśeṣavivakṣayā `nedaṃ caitrasya' iti prayogāt| sambandhaśca sarvatra kriyākārakabhāvamula eva| "ṣaṣṭhī śeṣe" iti sūtre `rājñaḥ pūruṣaḥ' ityatra rājā puruṣāya dadātīti sampratyayādrājā karttā puruṣaḥ sampradīnam, vṛkṣasya śākhetyatra vṛkṣe śākheti laukikaprasiddhayā vṛkṣaḥ śākhāyā adhikaraṇam| "yadetat svaṃ nāma caturbhiḥ prakārairetad‌bhavati-krayaṇādapaharaṇādyāñcāyāḥ parivartanarūpādvinimayādityanena yatrākṣūyamāṇā kriyā tatrāpi tatphalasambandānumitakaraṇatvādiśaktervastutaḥ sattvādityarthakena bhāṣyeṇa śeṣapadārthāpaaisiddhimāsaṅkayaivaṃ tarhi karmādīnāmavivakṣā śeṣa iti śoṣokteḥ"| kriyākārakasambandhasya vastutaḥ sarvatra sattve'pi tannimittasya svasvāmibhāvādeḥ sambandhatvena vivakṣaṇe tasyāvivakṣaṇāt śeṣapadārthasaulabhyamiti tad‌bhāvaḥ|
naca sambandhasyobayanirūpyatvāt `rājñaḥ pūruṣaḥ' ityatra rājapadottaramiva puruṣapadottaraṃ ṣaṣṭhayāpattiriti cenna, yadā rājñaḥ upakārakatvavivakṣā tadā rājapadādeva| yadā tu puruṣasya tadvivakṣā tadā puruṣapadādapi, puruṣasya rājeti| nacaikadobhayatra seti noktātiprasaṅgaḥ| taduktaṃ hariṇā-
dviṣṭho'pyasau parārthatvād guṇeṣu vyatiricyate|
tatrābhidhīyamānaśca pradhāne'pyapacaryyate|| iti|
parārthatvāt-paraṃ prati guṇānāṃ viśeṣaṇatvāt, viśeṣaṇatvena vivakṣitasya sambandhaṃ vinā sambandhaṃ vinā viśeṣaṇatvāsambhavena sambandho vyatiricyate-ud‌bhūtatayā pratiyate iti tatraiva ṣaṣṭhī| viśeṣyasya su padāntarāsamabhivyāhāre svarūpeṇaiva pratīyamānatvānna viśeṣyatāniyamakasambandhākāṅkṣā| tatra-viśeṣaṇe, abhidhīyamānaḥ-ṣaṣṣṭhayā pratīyamānaḥ pradhāne-viśeṣye'pi upayujyate, tasya dviṣṭhatvasvabhāvādrājanirūpitaviśeṣyatāyā rājapadasannidāne pratīyamānāyā upakārako bhavatīti tadarthaḥ|
kārakaṣaṣṭhayā iti "karttṛkarmaṇoḥ kṛti"  ityādisūtravihitaṣaṣṭhayā ityārthaḥ| śeṣavihitaṣaṣṭhayāḥ sambandharūpadharmavācakatayā ṣaṣṭhītvenaitasyā api tadvāācakatvasyaiva nyāyyatvāditi bhāvaḥ| nanu karttṛkarmādikārakavihitatṛtīyādīnāṃ kathaṃ kartrādidharmavācakatvamata āha saptamīpañcamyāviti| tathāca dharmasyāpi kārakatvasya bhāṣyasammatatvena tāsāṃ tadvācakatve na doṣa iti bhāvaḥ|
darśanāditi tatra hi dravyaṃ kārakamiti paśre `adya bhuktvā'yaṃ dvayahe bhoktā' ityādau devadattarūpakarttṛkārakasyaikyānmadhyavyāpadeśāsambhavādaprāptasaptamīpañcamyorupapādanāya pravṛttsya `kriyāmadhya iti vaktavyam' iti vārttikasya khaṇḍanāyoktam| nāntareṇa sādhanaṃ kriyāyāḥ pravṛttirbhavati| kriyāmadhyaṃ ca kārakamadhyamapi bhavati| tatra kārakamadhyamapi bhavati| tatra kārakamadhye ityeva siddhamiti tatra sādhanamityasya śaktimityarthaḥ|
tathāca devadattādirūpakarttṛdravyasyaikye'pi kālabhennabhujikriyānumitakārakapadārthakarttṛtvaśaktyorbhedāt tannimittamdhyavyapadeśābādhādyothokte'nupapattyabhāvāt `kriyāmadhye' iti vārttikaṃ nārambhaṇīyamiti tadbhāvaḥ|
idamupalakṣaṇam-anabhihitasūtrabhāṣyasya| tatra `saṃkhyā vibhaktayarthaḥ' iti pakṣeṇānabhihitādhikāraṃ samarthya dvayoḥ kārakayoranyatarābhidhānaṃ vibhaktyabhāvaprasaṅgaḥ, kva? `prāsāde āste' `śayane āste' kiṃ kāraṇam yadi pratyayenābhihitamadhikaraṇamiti kṛtvā sapmī na prāpnotītyāśaṅkaya, navānyatareṇābhidhānāt, `anabhihite vidhānam'ityādinā prasajjyapratiṣedhe, paryyudāse vā nañarthe dūṣaṇaṃ parihṛtya yadi sādhanaṃ dravyaṃ syāt tadekameva, taccābhihitamiti pūrvoktaparihāro na sambhavati| atha śaktiḥ sādhanaṃ tadā tasyābhedādanabhihitatvasambhavādbhavatyuktaparihāra ityābhipretya kiṃ punardravyaṃ sādhanamāhosvid‌ guṇa ityāśaṅkayoktam, kiṃ punaḥ sādhanaṃ nyāyyam? guṇa ityāha| kathaṃ jñāyate, evaṃ hi kaścit pṛcchati, kva devadatta iti?| sa tasmāyācaṣṭe'sau vṛkṣe iti, katarasmin yastiṣṭhati sa vṛkṣo'dhikaraṇaṃ bhūtvā'nyaśabdenābhisambandhamānaḥ karttā| sampadyate| dravye sādhane yat karma karmaiva syādyat karaṇaṃ karaṇameva yadadhikaraṇamadhikaraṇameva" iti|
kaiyaṭenāpi "yadi dravyaṃ sādhanaṃ syāt tadā tasyaikarūpatvāt tannibandhanābādhitapratyabhijñāviṣayān nānārthakriyā karamanibandanaḥ `ghaṭena jalamāhare' `ghaṭaṃ kūru' `ghaṭo'sti'iti vyāpadeśo na syāt, dṛśyate cāsau| tasmānnānāsaktibhāvāvagamaḥ siddhaḥ" iti tadvivṛtam|
evañca śaktipakṣasyā''karasammatatayā dvitīyādīnāṃ karmatvādau śaktiriti vadatāṃ matamapi samyageveti bhāvaḥ| prātipadikārthetisūtravihitaprathamāyā yathāyathaṃ prātipadikārthaḥ, liṅgam, parimāṇaṃ cārthaḥ|
karmādyādhikye prathamā mā bhūditi tatra mātragrahaṇam| dvandvānte śrūyamāṇasya tasya pratyekaṃ dvandvaghaṭakapadārthe'nvayāt prātipadikārthamātre, prathametyādivākyabhaṅgaḥ| prātipadikārthatvañca-nāmaniṣṭhaśaktayavacchinnajanakatānirūpitajanyatāśrayopasthititvavyāpakaviṣayitāpratiyogitvam| uccairityādyarthaviṣayitāyāstādṛśopasthititvavyāpakatvāt tatra prathamopapattiḥ|
evaṃ kṛṣṇa ityādau kṛṣṇādiviṣayitāyā iva pum̐lliṅgādivaṣayitāyā api tadupasthititvanaiyatyaṃ vyaktameva| yadyapi `dvikaṃ prātipadikārthaḥ' iti pakṣe liṅgasyopasthiviṣayatvaṃ durghaṭam, tathāpyanubhavānurodhena śakyārthopasthiteḥ prāg liṅgodvodhakasamavadhānasya niyamena kalpanāt tasya tattvaṃ bodhyam| evañcāliṅgā niyataliṅgāśca prātipadikārthamātra ityasyodāharaṇāni| taṭādiśabdajanyopasthitau tu pum̐lliṅgādibhānasyānaiyatyātrāprāptaprathamāvidhānāya liṅgagrahaṇam|
`siṃho māṇavakaḥ' ityādau sādṛśyādyadhikārthasya pratītāvapyantaraṅgatvāt svārthamātraniṣṭhāt tasmāt prathamā sulabhaiva| tatra prathamārthaliṅgasya viśeṣaṇatayā prakṛtyarathe'nvayaḥ, yogyatābalādanubhavācca, tathaiva kāryyākāraṇabhāvakalpanāt| evaṃ droṇo vrīhirityādau droṇapratipadikārthātiriktadroṇapadāt parimaṇasyādhikyena bhānāttatra parimāṇagrahaṇam|
naca vrīhyādipadasamabhivyāhārāt tadbhāne'pyantarahgatvāt prātipadikārthamātra eva sā'stviti vācyam, tathā sati droṇapadārthasya parimāṇasya bhedasambandhenānvayānupapapatteḥ| prathamārthasya tasya tu bhedenānvaye na kiñcadi, bādhakamiti sampradāyaḥ|
navyāstu-prātipadikārthaḥ-tattatpadapravṛttinimittam, tadāśrayaśca| ata eva liṅgagrahaṇaṃ caritārtham, anyathā "svamornapuṃsakāt"  iti sūtraprāmaṇyāt tasyāpi prātipadikārthatayā tadupādanavaiyarthyāpatteḥ| āśrayatvaṃ ca vaijñānikamā, śakyatāvacchedakāropa eva lakṣaṇetyanyatra vyāvasthāpitatvāt| gopītyādau prakṛtyarthasya prakṛtyarthasya lākṣaṇikatve'pi na kṣatiḥ| taṭādiśabdānāṃ liṅgaṃ vācyameva| visargādayastad‌dyotakāḥ|
evañca prātipadikārtha ityasyāliṅgā liṅgapravṛttinimittakāvyāyastrīpuṃsādiśabādāścodāharaṇam| itare liṅgamātradyādhi kyasya| droṇatvādipravṛttikadroṇādiśabdānāṃ parimaṇatvena parimāṇamapi liṅgavad‌ viśeṣyānvayyarthaḥ| evañca liṅgavat prātipadikārthatvābhāvāt parimaṇagrahaṇam| "vīraḥ puruṣaḥ" ityādau puruṣādipadasannidhāne viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvasyādhikasya bhāne'pi tatsamabhivyāhārāt pūrvaṃ svārthamātraniṣṭhāttasmāt setyāhuḥ|
"sambodhane ca"  iti vihitaprathamāyāstu sambodhanamarthaḥ| prātipadikārthāpekṣayā tasyādhikyena bhānāt pūrvasūtrāviṣayatvāt tatsūtrārambhaḥ| taccābhimukhībhavanānukūle vyāpāraḥ kriyānvayyeveti pratipāditaṃ prāk| `yathāyathaṃ vibhaktyarthāḥ' ityatratya-yathāyathapadaṃ vyācaṣṭe-uktaprakāreṇeti yadarathabodhe yā vibhaktiḥ samartha tasyāḥ so'rtho'vaseyaḥ| tathaivoktaṃ prāg‌, dvitīyātṛtīyāsaptamīnāmityādineti bhāvaḥ|
atreti supāṃ karmādivācakatva ityarthaḥ| naiyāyikān pratīti prācīnān pratītyarthaḥ| yathā tanmataṃ tathākhyātaśāktinirūpaṇāvasare pratipāditam|
nanu `supāṃ karmādayo'pyarthāḥ' iti bhaṣyāt sidhyatu tu teṣāṃ karmādau śaktiḥ viṣayatvādaulakṣaṇāyāṃ tu kiṃ bādhakam| kriyājanyaphalaśālitvarūpamukhyakarmatvabādha eva tatprasarādata uktarārddhapradarśitaniyamaṃ bādhakatvenopadarśayiṣyannāśayaṃ prakāśayati-ayaṃ bhāva ityādi "svaujasamau"  ityatra kārakavibhaktiṣvādau dvitīyāyā upādānād vārtike'pi tadarthakarmaṇaḥ prāṅ‌nirddeśaḥ| karmādītyādinā kārakāntarasaṃgrahaḥ| apinā śaktiḥ kārakamiti pakṣoktakarmatvādiśaktīnāṃ samuccayaḥ|
tathā tiṅāmiti tadvattiṅāmapi saṅkhayākarmadyartha ityarathaḥ| tatra suptiṅ‌kṣu| prakṛteṣu artheṣvityarthaḥ| vārtikatadbhāṣyeti lokādeva bahutvādiṣu bahuvacanādi bhaviṣyati, kimartham "bahuṣu bahuvacanam"|  ityādisūtramityākṣepe suptiṅ‌ṅāmaviśeṣeṇa vibhānād dṛṣṭaviprayogatvācca niyamārthaṃ vacanamiti samādhāya niyamā''kārapradarśakamidaṃ ślokavārttikam|
athavā prakṛtārthanapekṣya niyamaḥ| ke ca prakṛtāḥ, ekatvādayaḥ ekasminnevaikavacanam, na dvayorna bahuṣu ityādi tadbhāṣyaṃ ca tābhyāmityarthaḥ|
niyamākāramāha-dvitīyā karmaṇyeveti| prakṛtābhiprāyam| niṣkṛṣṭaniyamasya tvekatvaviśiṣṭe karmaṇyeva dvitīyaikavacanamityākāro bodhyaḥ| evamuttaratrāpi| ubhayathāpīti vibhaktyapekṣayā arthāpekṣayā cetyarthaḥ| vibhaktiniyamo'pi dvidhā-arthaviśeṣāpekṣastatsāmānyāpekṣaśca|
tatrādyaḥ-karmārthayogyaprātipadikācced dvitīyā tadā karmaṇyeva, na karaṇādau| karmādiyogye tadrahitaprātipadikārtha eva prathamā, na karmādai| saṃkhyāvadarthayogyaprātipadikāccedekavacanaṃ tadā ekatve eva na dvitvādāvityākārakaḥ| dvitīyastu-prātipadikācced dvitīyā karmaṇyevetyākārakaḥ| arthaniyamastu-karmārthayogyaprātipadikāccet karmaṇi vibhaktiratadā dvitīyaiva| karmādirahite tadyogyaprātipadikārthe prātīpadikātprathamaivetyākāraka iti| siddhetyasyaikavākyatābalenetyādiḥ| tathāca viṣayatvasya niruktakarmatvānātmakatvena tatra dvitīyādeḥ śāstreṇāvidhānāt tadarthatātparyyeṇa taduccāraṇasyānṛtatvādityarthaḥ| ata eveti vibhaktau lakṣaṇāyā aprayojakatvādevetyarthaḥ|
nanu vyākaraṇasya śaktinirūḍhalakṣaṇānyatarapratipādakatvena yatra mukhyārthabādhapratisandhānādi tatra śakyasaghbandhini lakṣaṇāsvīkāre bādhakābhāvaḥ| ghaṭaṃ jānātītyādito ghaṭādiviṣayakajñānabodhasya sarvānubhavasiddhasya dvitīyāyā vṛttiṃ vinānupapannasyaiva lakṣaṇākalpakatvācca "vibhaktau na lakṣaṇā" iti pravādastvekavibhakteraparavibhaktyarthe na lakṣaṇetyetatparo'ta eva na vyatyayānuśāsanavaiyarthyam| `supāṃ karmādayo'pyarthāḥ' ityatra karmādipadasya gauṇamukhyasādāraṇakarmatvaparatayā'pyupapatterna tadvirodho'pītyata āha-digiti|
datarthastu mukhyārthamādāya sāstracāritārthye gauṇe tad‌vayāpāre mānābhāvaḥ| naca ghaṭaṃ jānātīti prasiddhaprayogānurodhena gauṇe'pi tadvayāpārakalpanāvaśiyaketi vācyam, jānāterjñānaviṣayatvāpattyupasarjjanaviṣayatvāpādanārthakatayā mukhyakarmatayaivāsmanmate prayogopapattisambhavāt| svānubhavaikagamye'rthe lakṣaṇābhyupagame'rthanirddeśasya vaiyarthyāpattescetyādiḥ|
atra vadanti-śāstrasya gauṇe pravṛttyanabhyupagame sthādīnāṃ phalatvenābhimate gatyabhāve dhātvarthavyāpārajanyatvābhāvena taddhaṭitamukhyākarmakatvābhāvāttatto bhāve karttari ca pratyayānupapattiḥ, evaṃ vārayateḥ saṃyogānukūlavyāpārābhāvānukūlavyāpārārthakatāyāḥ sarvasammatatayā tasyāpi mukhyasakarmakatvābhāvena tato'pi karttari karmaṇi ca lakārādyanupapattirato gauṇe'pi śāstraviṣayatāvaśyamabhyupetavyeti na ghaṭaṃ jānātītyādīnāṃ viṣayatvalakṣaṇāyāmapyasādhutā|
vastutastu śaktiḥ kārakamiti pakṣasya tanmate tiṅ‌supsu vyāvasthikatvena dvitīyāyā viṣayatvānvayyādheyatvavodhakatve'pi na kṣatiḥ, tādṛśasthale viṣayarūpaphalasya dhātvarthapaphalasya dhātvarthaparatāyāḥ prāgupapādanāt tanmate'pyuktaniyamavirodhasya niṣedhaviṣayatāyāścāsambhavādityalam 
iti śrībhūṣaṇasāradarpaṇe subarthanirṇayaḥ  kramaprāptaṃ nāmārthanirūpaṇamityāśayenāha-nāmārthamiti| jātiriti tatttpadārthāsādhāraṇo dharma ityarthaḥ| tenābhāvatvākāśatvādīnāṃ nityānekasamvetatvarūpajātitvābhāve'pi na kṣatiḥ| etasyaiva sāstre `svārtha' padena vyavahāraḥ| jāteḥ padārthatve mānantu-"jātyākhyāyāmekasmin bahuvacanamanyatarasyām"  iti sūtram, "savarṇe'ṇ‌grahaṇamaparibhāṣyam, ākṛtigrahaṇāt siddham" iti vārttikam, "ākṛtiṃ vājapyāyanaḥ" iti sarūpasūtrasthaṃ bhāṣyañca| yuktimapyāha-lāghaveneti ekāśrayakaśakterekasyā eva sambhavāditi bhāvaḥ|
nanu śaktigrāhakaśiromaṇinā vyavahāreṇa vyāktāveva tatparicchedāt kathaṃ jātau tatsiddhirata āha-anekavyaktīnāmiti pūrvaṃ vyāvahāreṇa vyaktau śaktigrahe'pyānantyāt tāvatīṣu śaktigrahasambhavāttattadāśrayabhedabhinnanānāśaktikalpane gauravāt paścājjatāveva nirddhāryyate ityarthaḥ|
ekavyaktiviṣayakaśaktijñānādaparavyaktiviṣayakaśābdabodhābhyupagame tu govyaktiviṣayakaśaktijñānādaśvaviṣayakabodhāpattyā tadravyaktiviṣayakaśaktijñānasya tadvayaktiviṣayakabodhe hetutāyā avaśyakalpyatayaikaviṣayakajñānādaparavyāktibodhānudayāpattyā sakalavyaktibhānārthaṃ tāvad‌vyaktiṣu śaktikalpanāyāṃ gauravasya sphuṭatayā jātāveva śaktikalpanocitā|
yadyapi `gorgaupadavācyaḥ' iti śaktigraho vyaktiviṣayako'pi, tathāpi `saviśeṣaṇe hi vidhiniṣedhau viśeṣaṇamupasaṃkrāmataḥ sati viśeṣyabādhe' iti nyāyena jātāveva sattāṃ jātāveva sattāṃ paricchinatti| yadvā jātiviśeṣyaka eva śaktigrahaḥ `gaurgopadaśakyaḥ' iti jātiśaktivāda evamevābhilāpāt| vyaktibhānasya tvagre upapādayiṣyamāṇatvānna tadanupapattiriti bhāvaḥ|
vinikamaneti ekatarapakṣapātiyuktirūpetyarthaḥ| tathāceyaṃ vyaktiḥ śakyā, na jātirityevaṃ pratyekavyaktigrahe vinigamakābhāvaḥ| pūrvatra tu vyāktisamudāyasyaiva koṭitayā gauravajñānasyaiva bādhakasya vinigamakatvānna tadabhāva iti bhāvaḥ| tatpakṣe'pi gauravameva bādhakamityāha-evaṃ hītyādi|
nanu uktavinigamanāviraheṇa sarvāsvapi vyaktiṣu śaktisiddhāvaśakyavyakterabhāvena na tadbhānāya vyaktivṛttikalpaneti vyaktiśaktivādapakṣo nirduṣṭo'ta āha-kiñcaivamiti| yadvā nanu gotvādijātyanugatīkṛtayāvad‌vyaktiṣu śaktisvīkārānnānantyādidoṣo'ta āha-kiñaicavamiti evam-viśiṣṭe śaktyabhyupagame| nāgṛhīteti `nāgṛhitaviśeṣaṇā buddhirviśeṣyamadhigacchati' iti nyāyaśarīram| viśeṣaṇāviṣayakabuddhirviśeṣyaṃ nāvagāhata iti darthaḥ, viśeṣyaviṣayakatvasya viśeṣaṇaviṣayakatvaniyatatvamiti yāvat| tathāca viśiṣṭaśaktivāde viśiṣṭaśaktigrahasya viśeṣaviṣayakatvāvaśyakatayā tadubhayaviṣayakatvasya padārthopastityādikāraṇatāvaccedakatvakalpanopekṣayā viśeṣaṇaviṣayakatvamātrasyaiva tadavacchedakatvakalpanā lāghavāducitoti jātāveva śaktiḥ siddhayatīti bhāvaḥ|
nanvevam `gāṃ dadyāt' `vrīhīnavahanti' ityādau dānāvahananakarmatvādyanvayānupapattiḥ, jātestadanvayāyogyatvāditi cedatra kecit-jātyā''kṣiptavyāktāveva dānādikarmatvānvaya iti tatra, `pratyayānām' iti vyutpatteraprakṛtyarthavyaktau tadanvayāsambhavāt| taduktam-
`gāmyamānasya cārthasya naiva dṛṣṭaṃ viśeṣaṇam|
śabdāntarairvibhaktyā vā dhūmo'yaṃ jvalatītivat'|| iti|
kiñcāyamākṣepo'rthāpattiḥ, samānasaṃvitsaṃvedyatvaṃ vā| nādyaḥ, anupapattimantareṇāpi `gaurasti' ityato gotvaviśiṣṭabodhāt| nāntyaḥ, jātiviṣayakaśaktigrahasya viśiṣṭaviṣayakabodhahetutvābhyupagamena jātiviṣayatāyā vyaktiviṣayatānaiyatvarūpo vācyaḥ, anyasyāsambhavāt| sa ca ` gotvamasti' ityatra gotvaviśiṣṭāpratyayād vyabhicarita ityuktānupapattirdurvāraivetyata āha-vyaktivodhastivati| lakṣaṇayeti svaśakyagotvāśrayatvasambandharūpalakṣaṇayetyaparthaḥ| tathāca padavṛttijanyopasthitereva śābdabodhe hetutayā vyakteraśakyatve'pi lakṣaṇāvṛttyupasthitāyāṃ tasyāṃ nāvaghātādikarmatvānvayanupapattiriti bhāvaḥ| evañceti lakṣaṇābhyupagame cetyarthaḥ| tatra lakṣitavyaktau| upapadyata iti anyathā pratyayānāmiti vyutpatteḥ sa nopapadyoteti bhāvaḥ| nanu vyaktilakṣaṇābhyupagame yugapad‌vṛttidvayavirodha iti siddhāntabhaṅgaḥ, gaurastītivākyādanubhūyamānavyaktibodhānupapattiśca, lakṣaṇābijasyānvayānupapattestatrābhāvādata āha-digiti|
tadarthasyu sati tātparyye `gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣamatsyau staḥ' ityatra vṛttidvayasya sarvairevābhyupapagāttādṛśanyāyaḥ, yugapacchaktistātparyāviṣaye lakṣaṇā ca netyevaṃpara iti na tadvirodhaḥ| tātparyānupapattisattvācca na vyāktilakṣaṇā'nupapattiḥ, tadvirodhābhyupagame tu jātivyaktyorabhedapakṣamupagamya vibhaktyarthānvaya upapādyaḥ| taduktam-
`tena tallakṣitavyakteḥ kriyāsambandhacodanāt|
jātivyakt‌yobhedo vā vākyārtheṣu vyavasthita-'|| iti|
vastutastu jātidharmikaśaktigrahasya jātiviśiśṭavyāktiśābdabodhe hetutvakalpanānna vyaktibhānānupapattiḥ, nāpyaśakyabhānā''pattiḥ, tasya tadvaiśiṣṭayābhāvāditi jātiśaktivādaniṣkarṣaḥ| vakṣyate cādhikamupariṣṭāt|
anye tu jātiśiktivādamittthamupapādayāmāsuḥ-mā'stu vyakterbhānam, ānayanādikriyā kevalāyāṃ jātāvasambhavantī vyaṭāktimāhityena niṣpādyate, samāhāre pāṇipādasāhityena vādanakriyāvat| taduktam-"taddhitārthe"  iti sūtre bhāṣye-"ākṛtāvālambhanādīnāṃ sambhavo nāstīti kṛtvā tatsahacarite dravye bhaviṣyati yathā-`agnirānīyatām' ityukte kevalasyāgnerānayanāsambavādacoditamapi pātramānīyate| etadevāgnerānayaṃ yatpātrastasya| tathā''kṛtāvālambhanādīni codyamānāni sāmarthyāt sāhacaryyād dravyamabhiniviśinte| sarva evā''kṛteryogo'navtarbhāvitadravyāyā iti dravyadvārakaḥ sambandhaḥ sampadyate"iti| sāhacaryyam-sambandhaḥ| sarūpasūtre bhāṣye'pi sphuṭo'yamarthaḥ| harirapyāha-
vyaṭakto kāryāṇi saṃsṛṣṭā jātisyu pratipadyate| iti|
gotvādipadānāṃ tu gotvādidharmivṛttidharma eva śaktiriti na tatra goviśeṣyako bodhaḥ|ṣa jāterāśrayāviyoge'pi kevalāyāstasyāḥ śabdavācyatvaṃ nānupapannam, jalādau dṛsyamānasya mukhasya svasthānāviyoge'pi mukhādipadabodhyavat| śakyatāvacchedakatā tu jātigatadharmasyaiveti|
idamapi mataṃ jātyā vyaktyakṣepe paryyapasyati| tacca nacāpadārthe pratyayānvaya iti dūṣaṇagrastam| vināpi saṅketaviṣayatāṃ sāmānyena sambandhaniyamādarthādhyāhāranyāyenopasthitāṃ vyaktiṃ punarjātirviśinaṣṭi, rūpādijñānāpiṣayāṇāṃ cakṣurādīnāṃ cākṣuṣayāmīti pratītyā viṣayīkaraṇavadityapi kecit|
śakyasambandharūpāyā lakṣaṇāyā ekasya lakṣya-lakṣyatāvaccedakayorasambhave'pi lakṣyārtapratītau lakṣyatā'vacchedakabhānavacchakyatāvacchedake śaktyabhāve'pi tādṛśakāryyakāraṇabhāvalādeva gotvādibānanirvāhe kṛtaṃ tatra śaktikalpanayetyāśayenaikapadamanyatā vyācaṣṭe-yadveti vyaktireveti evena jātivyavacchedaḥ|
nanu `savarṇe'ṇ‌grahaṇam' ityādiprāmāṇyādastuktaḥ pakṣaḥ, `vyaktiḥ padārthaḥ' ityatra tu kimmānamata āha-kevalavyaktīti| ekaśeṣasya ceti jātipakṣe ekenaiva śabdena dvayorbahūnāṃ vā pratyāyanasambhavāt tadanarthakaṃ sad‌ vyaktipakṣajñāpakamityarthaḥ| āṇ‌grahaṇasya bhāṣyakṛtā pratyākhyānāt sarūpasūtrasya ca śāstrīyaprakriyāmātropayogitvasyā''kare vyavasthāpanāt kathamuktarthe tayoḥ prāmāṇyamata āha-yuktañcaitaditi sayuktikamapyetadityarthaḥ| apanā `dravyābhidhānaṃ vyāḍiḥ' iti sarūpasūtrasyabhāṣyaprāmāṇmata samuccīyate| yuktimevāha-vyavahāreṇeti|
etattatvaṃ śaktivāde vakṣyate| etatkalpe pūrvoktaṃ saktyā''nantyadoṣaṃ samuddharati-sambandhitāvaccedakasyeti śaktigrahe dharmmitāvacchedakasyetyarthaḥ| sambandhitāvacchedakapadasay liṅgasarvanāmanapuṃsakatvāduddeśyasamarpakatvādvā na tattvānupapatti| tathāca sambanditāvaccedakajātyanugatīkṛtanānāvyaktiṣvekadharmāvacchinnāyā evasyā eva śakterupagamā nna tannānātvaprayuktagauravamiti bhāvaḥ|
evamiti viśiṣṭe śaktitvasvīkāre ityarthaḥ| ghaṭatvāderghaṭādiniṣṭhaśakyatāvacchedakatvābhyupagama iti yāvat tadeva ghaṭatvādyeva vācyam śakyamityarthaḥ| śakyatve hetūpanyasanam-nyāyāditi| akāraṇatve'piti daṇḍatvā''dīnāmanyathāsiddhatvenākāraṇatve'pi tīratvādīnāṃ pravāhādyasambandhitayā| lakṣyatve'pi ca tatra tanniṣṭhāvacchedyatānirūpitasvarūpasambandhā''tmikāyāḥ, viṣayitāviśeṣā''tmikāyā vāvacchedakatāyā abhyupagamavadaśakyasyāpi ghaṭātvādestādṛśakyatāvacchedakatvasambhavāt| śakyatve sati śakyaviśeṣaṇatā''pannasya śakyatāvacchedakatvamiti niyame mānābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ|
ukte'rthe sammatimāha-uktañceti| ānantye'pīti bhāvānām-vyaktīnāṃ nānātvādapariccedyatve'pi, ekam anugatajātyādidharmam upalakṣaṇam aśakyatve'pi śakyavyāvarttakam kṛtvā abhyupāgamya sakyatāvacchedakatayā'bhyupetyeti yāvat śabda gavādyātmkaḥ sukarasambandhaḥ sugrāhyagavādiniṣṭhavācyatāsambandhaḥ| naceti śaktigrahāviṣayavyaktiṃ na bodhayiṣyatīti tadarthaḥ| gotvādisāmānyalakṣaṇayā sarvāsveva vyaktiṣu śaktyavadhāraṇāditi bhāvaḥ| adhikamagre vakṣyate|
vyaktirevātra dravyapadena vyavahriyate, sarvanāmaparāmarsayogyatvāt| uktañca-
`vastūpalakṣaṇaṃ yatra sarvanāma prayujyate|
dravyamityucyate so'rtho bhedyatvena vivakṣitaḥ'| iti|
svabhāvādeva buddhiviṣayatā'vacchedakatvopalakṣitataddharmāvacchinnabodhakaṃ sarvanāma yasman dharmiṇi tādṛśadharmaṃ pravṛttinimittīkṛtya prayujyate| `idam' iti parāmarśayogyamiti yāvat| dravyapadena vyavahriyata ityarthaḥ| bhedyatvena-viśeṣyatvenetyādi tu lakṣaṇāntaraparam, etatpakṣe tu jātyāderapi viśeṣyatvāvivakṣāyāṃ dravyatvamiṣṭameva| tiṅantārthakriyāyāḥ kimādibhiḥ parāmarśāt stokādyarthanirūpitaviśeṣyatāvattvācca dravyatvamastyeva| "vipratiṣiddham"  ityādau ca sāmarthyāttantrāntaraprasiddhadravyagrahaṇam| "cādayo'sattve'  ityatra prathamakalpoktadravyasyaiva paryudāso bodhyaḥ|
śakyatāvacchedakatvañca-śakyaniṣṭhaviśeṣyatā'vacchedakatayā bhāsamānatvam| ghaṭādipade śaktigrahe ca ghaṭatvādereva tattvam na pṛthivītvāderiti na tebhyaḥ pṛthivītvādibodhaḥ| ākāśaśabdasya śabdā'śrayatve śaktyabhāve'pi tatastad‌rūpeṇaiva bodhaḥ| tadviśiṣṭaśaktigrahasya tad‌bodhahetutvāt| yadvā''kāśatvasakhaṇḍopādhiḥ| vṛddhisūtroktarītyā tatpadaṃ vā''kāśaśabdaśakyatāvacchedakamiti na `ākāśaḥ śabdāśrayaḥ' iti prayogānupapattiriti dravyavādiniṣkarṣaḥ|
nanuktapakṣadvayamapi na vicārasaham, tathāhi-jātiśaktivāde gotvādipadāttadvisiṣṭabodhāpattiḥ, gotvaśaktigrahasya tadviśiṣṭabodhaupayikasya sattvāt| naca nirdharmitāvacchedakaśaktigraho gotvaviśiṣṭabodhe heturgotvatvadharmitāvacchedakabādhagrahakāle nirdhārmitāvacchedakaśaktigrahasambhavena gotvaviśiṣṭabodhāpattyā gotvātvadharmitāvaccedakaśaktigrahasya tādṛśabodhahetutāyā āvaśyakatvāt|
yadi ca gotve niravacchinnaiva śaktiḥ-gopadaṃ gaurityākārakaṃ śaktisambandhena gotvaprakārakaṃ gopadatvaviśiṣṭaviṣayakaṃ śaktijñānaṃ gotvaviśiṣṭabodhaheturiti vibhāvyate, tathāpi kadācit samavāyena gotvaviśiṣṭabodhaḥ, kadācit kālikādisambandheneti vyavasthā'nupapattiḥ, gotvaviṣayakaśaktijñānasyāvisiṣṭatvāt| manmate tu samavāyena gotvaviśiṣṭaviṣayakaśābdabuddi prati tena sambandhena gotvaviśiṣṭaśaktigrahasya, evamitarasambandhena tādṛśavisiṣṭaviṣayabodhe tena sambandhena tādṛsaviśiṣṭaśaktijñānasya hetutayā tanniyamopapatteḥ| gopadaṃ gaurityākārakaśaktigrahāt, tādṛsabodhānanubhavācca gotevādipadānāṃ gotvatve śaktirityapi na, prakṛtijanyabodhaprakāre bhāvapratyayavidhānāt| gavetarāsamavetatvavisiṣṭasakalagosamavetatvarūpasya guroḥ śakyatvā'bhyupagame, lāghavasūlakākṛtivādasya mūsaśaithilyāpattasca| vyaktivāde'pi padamātrasyaiva vyaktibodhakatvāviśeṣatvāt| `gauścalo nīlaḥ ityādīnāṃ saha prayogānupapattiḥ|
kiñca hastipakavyaktayantare sambandhagrahe yatra hastipake sambandho na gṛhītastasyāpi hastidarśane smaraṇāpattyā tad‌vyaktiviṣayakasambandhagrahasya tadupasthitiniyāmakatvasyāvaśyakatayā kathamekaviṣayakaśaktijñānāparaviṣayakopasthitiḥ|
yadyapi "ākṛtipadārthasya dravyaṃ na padārtho dravyapadārthakasya cākṛtirna padārtha ityubhayorubhayaṃ padārthaḥ" iti bhāṣyādviśakalitayorevākṛtivyaktayoḥ śaktigrahaviṣayatā tabhyate| tathāpi "pasyacit kiñcid‌guṇabhūtaṃ kiñcit pradhānam" ityuttarabhaṣyaparyālocanayā tata eva viśiṣṭe śaktyavadhāraṇāt tathopādānamaviruddham, anyathā tṛtīyopāttaikatva-karaṇatvayoriva viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvānāpannajāti-vyaktyoḥ śakyatve goryad vyaktiśaceti samūhālambandhabodhāpatteḥ śaktijñānapadārthepasthiti śābdabodhānāṃ samānaprakāratvaniyamāt|
evamevāvayavasaṃyogarūpākṛtau| tathāca sūtritaṃ gautamena "jātyākṛtivyaktayaḥ padārthaḥ" iti triṣvekaiva śaktiriti bodhanāya ca tatraikavacanam, guṇabhūtamiti bhāṣyasya ca svārasikatayeti śeṣaḥ| jāteḥ kadācit prādhānyena bhānaṃ tu tātparyagrahasāpekṣamiti tadāśayaḥ| jāteḥ prādhānye'pi dravyasaṃkhyāmādāya tatra dvivacanādyupapattirbodhyā| taduktaṃ kaiyaṭena-"yathā `bhavataḥ' ityādau kriyāprādhānye'pi sādhanakṛto vacanabhedaḥ, evamākṛtiprādhānye'pi dravyasaṅkhayākṛta ityadoḥ" iti| dravyaprādhānye'pi kvacijjātisaṅkhayāmādāya vacanam, `brāhmaṇaṃ na hanyāt' ityādau yathā| jāteḥ prādhānye ca niravacchinnāyā eva vyakteḥ prakāratāviśeṣyībhūtajātyā ca tadanugamaḥ `nahi prakāribhūtamevānugamakamiti niyamaḥ' iti tatkalpapariṣkāraḥ|
vastutastu jātiviśiṣṭavyaktireva śakyā, gavādipadādroviśiṣṭagotvabodhasyānanubhavenoktakalpāsambhavāt| ata eva tathaiva vyākhyātama| jātyatiriktapadārthasya kiñciddharmāvacchinnatvamiti niyamena niravacchinnāyāstasyā jātyaṃśe bhānāyogāt, gotvasyānvayitāvacchedakarūpeṇānu pasthityā tattatkarmatvādyanvayāsambhavācca| `sampanno vrīhiḥ' ityādau tu svāśrayaprakṛtyarthatāvacchedakavattvasambandena prakṛtyarthavyaktāvevaikatvānvayaḥ| uktasthale ekavacanopasthitāni nānaikatvāni na vrīhiṣvanvīyanta iti tu na sat, sajātīyaniṣṭhabhedapradiyogitānavacchedakaikatvarūpasajātīyadvitīyarahitatvasyaivaikavacanārthatvena tatra tasya bādhāt, saṃkhyārūpaikatvasya dravyamātrasādhāraṇyenārthata eva lābhādanupayogāttasyaikavacanārthatvābhāvācca|
ata eva `paśunā yajeta' ityatra tādṛśaikatvasyaikavacanena vivakṣitatvānnānekapaśukaraṇakayāgādadṛṣṭasiddhiḥ, samabhivyā hṛtapadārthasaṃsargitvaviśṭaprakṛtyarthatā'vacchedakarūpeṇa sājātyasya sājātyasya vivakṣaṇācca nātra `ghaṭo'sti' ityādau ghaṭaniṣṭabhedapratiyogitānavacchedakaikatvavāprasiddhiḥ, etaddeśavidyamānaghaṭaniṣṭhabhedapratiyogitānavacchedakaikatvasyaiva ta6 bodhena tasyoktarītyā prasiddhisambhavāt| evañca vyaktiviśeṣyakabodhābhyupagame kvāpyanupapatterabhāvena tasya caitadviśeṣyakaśaktigrahamantareṇāsambhavādgavādipadānāṃ jātiviśiṣṭavyaktāveva śaktiḥ| vyaktimātraviṣayakasya, jātimātraviṣayakasya vā śaktijñānasya jātiviśiṣṭavyaktiśābdabodhahetutāyāmuktadoṣasya duruddharatvāditi sāraniṣkarṣaḥ|
anye tu-"ubhayorubhayaṃ padārthaḥ" iti bhāṣyājjātau, vyaktau ca gavādipadānāṃ śaktiḥ| parantu vyaktiśābdabodhe'pi jātiviṣayakatvena jātiśaktijñānasyaiva hetutā| vyaktiśaktistu svarūpasatyupayujyate| aśakyasyāpi śābde bhamābhyupagame gavādipadādaśakyagavādivyakterivāśaktaghaṭādīnāmapi bhānāpattyā vyaktiśaktirāvasyakī| ayameva `kubjaśaktivādaḥ' iti vyavahṇiyate, śābdodhakāraṇatāvacchedakakoṭau vyaktiviṣayakatvasyāniveśāt| tathāca gotvaprakārakagoviṣayakaśābdabodhe gotvaviṣayakagopadaśaktimatī gaurityākārakaśaktijñānaṃ kāraṇam, natu gotvavisiṣṭaviṣayakagopadaśaktimatī gaurityākārakam| `gotvamasti' ityādau tādṛśagotvaprakārakaśaktijñānābhāvānna gotvaviśiṣṭabodhapraśaṅga ityāhuḥ|
tadapyāpātamanopamam, gotvaviṣayakagopadaśaktimatī gaurityākārakasaktigrahe kāraṇatāvacchedakakoṭau gotvaviṣayakatvasya praveśe gauravāt|
nanu māstu tadviṣayakatvasyāvaccedakakoṭau niveḥ, gopadaśaktimatī gaurityākārakaśaktigrahasya gotvaprakārakatvena gotvaviśiṣṭaviṣayakaśābdabuddhihetutve vyaktiviṣatāyāḥ kāraṇavacchedakakoṭāvaniveśena lāghavāt| desāntarasthavyaktivisepyakabodhānupapattyā samānaviseṣyakatvena kāryakāraṇabhāvāsambhavena samānaprakārakatvenaiva kāryyakāraṇabhāvopagamāditi cet,
maivam, vyaktiviṣayatāyāḥ kāraṇatāvaccedakakoṭāvaniveśe vyaktiśaktisvīkārasya vaiyarthyāpatteḥ| naca tadasvīkāre gotvaprakārakaśaktijñānatvena hetutvāsambhavaḥ, vyaktau śaktyabhāvena tādaṛśaśaktijñānasya bhramatvāditi vāccayam, jñaktirhi na svarūpasatī śābdabodhahetuḥ, apabhraṃśādapi bodhodayāt, āgṛhītaśaktikācchābdabodhāpatteśca| kintu jñāt| tathāca yadaṃśe śaktijñānahetustadeva śakyamiti jātivad‌ vyaktirapi śakyaiva, evaṃ tad‌vaisiṣṭayamapi tathā| anyatā vyaktāveva śaktiḥ, vyaktiśaktijñāne jāteraprakārakatvāt samānaprakāratvena śaktijñānapadārthopasthitiśābdabuddhīnāṃ kāryyakāraṇabhāvāvāditi śaktirucchidyeta|
naca yadviṣayakatvena śaktijñānaṃ kāraṇaṃ tadavaśyaṃ śakyamiti niyamājjatau śaktiḥ setsyatīti vācyam, tarhi vyaktiviṣayakatvena śaktijñānasya hetutayā vyaktiśaktisvīkārasyāvaśyakatvādukve'rthe bhāṣyopanyasanaṃ tu "kasyacit kiñcit pradhānabhūtaṃ kiñcit guṇabhūtam" ityupasaṃhārabhāṣyānālocanamūlakamevetyāstāṃ tāvat|
nanvevam `ekam' iti pakṣasya nirālambanatāpattirata āha-ekamityasya ceti| `bodhakatvaṃ śaktiḥ' iti mate bodhakatvajñānaviṣayabodhāṃse prakāritayāvacchedakatvaṃ śaktirityarthalābhāttadāśrayajāteraikyācchabdaniṣṭhabodhakatvasyaiva vinigamanāvirahādarthaniṣṭhabodhyatvasyāpi śaktitvamiti mate'pi `asmāt padādetadarthabodho bhavatu' iti śaktijñānaniṣṭhaśābdadhīhetutāyāṃ prakāritayāvacchedikāyā jāterekyādvā tatpapakṣopapattirityarthaḥ| tathāca tādṛśaprakāritāvaccedakatārūpāyāḥ śakyajātimātraniṣṭhatayā `ākṛtireva padārthaḥ' iti pakṣaḥ samarthito bhavati| avacchedakatāyāḥ svarūpasambandarūpāyā ghaṭatvādyātmakatve'pyavacchedyāvacchedakabhāva upahitānupahitabhedāt samrathanīyaḥ|
evameva `ekaṃ vyaktiḥ' iti pakṣo'pi samarthanīyaḥ, tādṛśajñānaniṣṭhaśābdabodhakāraṇatāyāṃ bodhāṃśe viśeṣyatayā vyakteravacchedakatvāt| etatpakṣe cāvaccedyāvacchedakabhāvasya sāmañjasyenopapattirgotvādijātyanugamānna tadānantyamapi| śakyatāvacchedakaprakārakavyaktibodhe vyaktiśaktigrahasyaitatkalpe hetutayā| calatvādinā gavādivyaktibodhārtha tatprayogasyāpyupapattiśceti bodhyam|
nanu `ghaṭo ghaṭapadabodhyaḥ' ityākārakaśaktijñānāt svarūpato ghaṭatvaprakārakaviśi,ṭabuddheriva jātitvena ghaṭatvaviśaṣṭabuddherapyāpattiḥ, tatkāryyatāvaccedakākrāntatvādata āha-tathāceti| ghaṭatvaviśiṣṭabodhe iti| svarūpatastatprakārakabodha ityarthaḥ| kāryyakāraṇabhāva iti tathāca `jātimān ghaṭaḥ' iti bodhasya ghaṭatvāṃśe jātitvaprakārakatvenoktaśaktijñānakāryyatāvacchedakānākrāntatvenoktāpatterasambhavād ghaṭatvāṃśe jātitvaprakārakaghaṭatvaviśiṣṭabuddhiṃ prati tu tadaṃśe jātitvaprakārakasaktijñānasya hetutvāntaraṃ kalpanīyamiti bhāvaḥ|
nanu tadaṃśe'nyāprakārakatvaghaṭitadharmasyārthasamājagrastatayā kathaṃ kāryyatāvacchedakatvamata āha-prapañcitamiti tathāca niravacchinnaprakāratākatadviśiṣṭabuddhiṃ prati niravacchinna(tat)prakāratākasaktijñānasya, sāvacchinnaprakāratākatad‌viśiṣṭhabuddhimprati sāvacchinnatatprakārakaśaktijñānasya hetutvakalpanānnoktāpattiriti tatroktamityarthaḥ| tadabhipretyeti uktarītyā viśiṣṭaśya śakyatvamupagamyetyarthaḥ| jātivyaktī iti viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvāpanne te ityarthaḥ|
pūrvapakṣāditi `ekam' iti pakṣādityarthaḥ| tatpakṣe hi śābdajñānakāraṇatāyāṃ bodhāṃśe prakārityāvacchedakatvaṃ śaktiriti jātireva śakyā| `viśeṣyitayā'vacchedakatvam' iti mate kevalavyaktireva śakyeti siddhāntitam| atra tu viṣayitātvena prakāritāviśeṣyite anugamayya tatra viṣayitayā'vacchedakatvaṃ śaktirityupagamād dviyorapi nāmārthatvasiddhiriti na tayoḥ parasparavirodhaśaṅketi bhāvaḥ| eteṣāṃ matānāmākarārūḍhatve'pi `vyaktiḥ padārthaḥ' iti pakṣa eva kṣodakṣamaḥ| pratyekapadaśaktinirṇayastvanyato'vadhāryyaḥ|
liṅganīti liṅagatvañca-prakṛtaguṇavato dharmaviśeṣaḥ| tadviśeṣañca napuṃsakatvādītyāsayenāha-sattveti| sāmyāvastheti upacayāpacayahāhityenāvasthānamityarthaḥ| upacayaḥ-buddhiḥ, apacayaḥ-tannayūnatvam| tattacchabdaniṣṭhamiti jāti- guṇa-kriyādibhedabhinnaśabdaniṣṭham| tattacchabdavācyañceti svāśrayaśabdavācyañcetyarthaḥ, `pum̐lliṅgaḥ śabdaḥ' iti vyavahārāt, "tasmācchaso naḥ"  "svamornapuṃsakāt"  ityādisūtraprāmāṇyācceti bhāvaḥ| tameveti samānānupūrvokatve'pyupacayādyavasthārūpaṃ parasparasamānādikaraṇaṃ liṅgamādāyaivetyarthaḥ| bhidyante iti pum̐stvādiliṅgaketaravyāvṛttividheyakānumityuddeśyā bhavantītyarthaḥ|
nanu liṅgasyoktāvasthārūpatve tāsāṃ parasparaviruddhānāmekatrāvastānāsambhavena keṣucicchabdeṣvanekaliṅgakatvavyavahāraḥ kiṃnibandhano'ta āha-keṣāñciditi taṭādiśabdānāmityarthaḥ|
vastutasteṣāṃ viruddhatve'pi śabdānāṃ samānānupurvokatvanibandhanābhedādhyāsādanekaliṅgakatvavyavahāropapattirityarthaḥ| arthaniṣṭham-
"stanakeśavatī nārī lomaśaḥ puruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ|"
ityādismṛtilakṣitaṃ liṅgaṃ tu nātrāśrayayituṃ śakyam, `dārān' ityādau śasprakṛtyarthe tadabhāvena natvādyanāpatteḥ| tathāca bhāṣyam-"ekārthaśabdānyatvād dṛṣṭaṃ liṅgānyatvamavayavānyatvācca" iti| ekasminnarthe puṣyaḥ, tārakā, nakṣatram" iti liṅganānātvadarśanāt, `kuṭīraḥ' ityādau rephasyāvayavasyopajane'pi liṅgabhedadarśanāccānyadeva śabdaniṣṭhaṃ liṅgamiti tadarthaḥ| arthaniṣṭhatve tu `pum̐liṅgaḥ śabdaḥ' iti sāmānadikaraṇyānupapattiśceti bhāvaḥ|
evañceti pāribhāṣikaśabdaniṣṭhaliṅgasya vyāvahāraupayikatve cetyartaḥ| vyavahāra iti arthaniṣṭhasya tasya tanniyāmakatve tu strīvyaktirvastupadārthapadābhyāṃ na vyaktiviṣaye, naivaṃ puruṣādirapi| vyaktyādiśabdaiḥ stanakeśādirūpalaukikaliṅgasya khaṭvādāvabhāvena tadvācakāt strīpratyayādyanāpatteśceti bhāvaḥ| taṭastaṭīti arthe laukikaliṅgabhāve'pi parasparāsamānādhikaraṇapāribhāṣikaliṅgavattvena vibhinnānāṃ taṭādiśabdānāṃ tattatkāryabhāktvamupapadyate ityarthaḥ|
nanu śabdaniṣṭhoktaliṅgasyaiva strīpuṃsādivyavahāraupayikatve tasya strīvācakapaśuśabde'pi sattvāt `paśunā yajeta' ityatra pasustrīkaramakayāgādapyadṛṣyadṛṣṭasiddhiḥ syāt, tad‌dravyāttalliṅgasya vyaktyādiśabdoktaliṅgavat strīpuṃsādisādhāraṇyenārthāparicchedakatvādata āha-tacceti śabdaniṣṭhamevetyarthaḥ| artheti arthamapi paricchinattītyarthaḥ| tadevāha-paśvādiśabdoktamiti liṅgamiti śeṣaḥ| yadi strīpaśuryāgāṅgatvena vivakṣito'bhaviṣyattadā astrīvihitanābhāvaṃ vibhaktyanete na prāyokṣyat| tadantapaśuśabdaprayogāttu pumpaśureva yāgāṅmiti nirṇiyate ityarthaḥ|
strīliṅgārthakapaśuśabdāt `āṅo nā'strīyām'  iti vihitanābhāvasya tṛtīyaikavacane'sambhavena tadantapaśuśabdaprayogāt tadarthasya puṃstvāvagateriti bhāvaḥ| nanu vyaktyādiśabdānāmarthaniṣṭhaliṅgānādareṇa pravṛttivatpaśuśabdasya tathā| pravṛttau bādhakābhāvāduktadoṣo durapahnava ityāśaṅkaya nirācaṣṭe-naceti vyaktyādītyādinā vastvādipadasaṅgahaḥ| sādhāraṇyaṃ svāśrayavācyatvasambandhena, strīpuṃvṛttitvaṃ nityastrīliṅgatvenepi, "striyāṃ ktina"  ityanuśāsanāditi bhāvaḥ|
nanu `paśunā yajeta' iti vaidikaprayoga eva tasya nityapuṃstve mānaṃ syādata āha-paśveti tathāca vede paśuśabdasya strīliṅgatvasyāpi darśanena na tasya vyaktyādiśabdasāmyamiti bhāvaḥ| svokte'rthe mīmāṃsakasammatimāha mīmāṃsāyāmiti|
vakṣyamāṇāsvarasādāha-vastutastviti| nanu upratyayāntānāṃ nitpuṃstve paśvetyādistrīliṅgaprayogānupapattirata āha-vedabhāṣye'pīti tathāca vyaktyādiśabdoktaliṅgasyeva paśuśabdoktaliṅgasyāpi strīpuṃsasādharaṇyānna tatsvārthaparicchedakatvamiti bhāvaḥ|
nanu kathaṃ tarhi pumpaśoreva yāgāṅgatvanirṇayo'ta āha-chāgo veti| nyāyanaiva evetyevakāreṇa liṅgasyārthaparicchedakasya vyudāsaḥ| yathā "sārasvatau meṣau" ityatra taddhitaprakṛtiśabdaḥ strīliṅgaḥ pum̐lliṅgo veti sandehe "sandigndhe vākyaśeṣāt" iti nyāyena, "tadvāmadevyamithunam" ityarthavādād dvayorevaikaśeṣeṇa grahaṇaṃ tadvanmantravarṇādatra pumpaśoryāgāṅgatvanirṇaya iti bhāvaḥ| prapañcitamiti laukikaliṅgameva nāmārthaḥ| taccāvayavaviśeṣasaṃsthānarūpam| `dārān' ityādau puṃstvāropeṇaiva tatprayuktakāryam, "rātrāhnāhāḥ puṃsi"  ityādiliṅgānuśāsanasyaivoktārthe mānatvāt| ata eva na paśustriyā yāgamiti| athavā strītvādirjātireva|
`tistro jātaya evaitāḥ keṣāñcit samavasthitāḥ|
