aviruddhā viruddhābhirgomahiṣyādijātibhiḥ'||
ityādi tatroktamiti|
pare tu-
`stanakeśavatī nārī lomasaḥ puruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ|
etayorantaraṃ yacca tadabhāve napuṃsakam'||
iti smṛtyuktalakṣaṇalakṣiliṅgasyāsārvatrikatvena `dārān' ityādau natvānāpattyā `striyām'iti sūtre "avaśyaṃ kaścit svakṛtānta astheyaḥ" ityādinā sārvatrikaguṇāvasthārūpapāribhāṣikaliṅgasya śāstrīyakāryogino bhāṣyakṛtā'bhyupagame'pi gurusaṃjñāvijñānānnatvādiśāstrapravṛttau laukikāliṅgasyāpyāśrayaṇaṃ pumādisaṃjñāyā ivādhikasaṃgrahārtha pravṛttatve'pi satasiddhārthanirāsārthamapravṛtteḥ| ata eva
`āvirābhāvastirobhāvaḥ sthitiścetyapyapāyinaḥ|
dharmā mūrtiṣu sarvāsu liṅgatvena vyavasthitāḥ'||
ityatrāpinā'laukikaliṅgamapi samuccitavān hariḥ| tatra sati sambhave sāmānādhikaraṇyasambandhena laukikapuṃstviśiṣṭaśāstrīyaliṅga eva pumādiśabdānāṃ śaktirna kevalaśāstrīye, nāpi laukike| asambhave tu sāstrīya eva| ata eva `paśunā yajeta' ityādau paśustriyā kṛte yāge vaiguṇyam| tatra laukikapuṃstvaviśiṣṭaśāstrīyapuṃstvasyaiva śabdena pratipādanādityāhuḥ|
atha liṅge śaktigrahaviṣayatāyā abhāvena prātipadikārthatvābhāvāt `trikam' iti pakṣo'nupapanna iti cenna, "svamornapuṃsakāt"  ityādisūtraprāmāṇyena `pūm̐lliṅgādiviśiṣṭo'yaṃ ghaṭo ghaṭapadavāttyaḥ' ityākārakaśakti grahābhyupagamena sāmañjasyāt| `śabdaniṣṭhameva liṅgaṃ natvādipravṛttau nimittam' itimate śabdārthayorabhede liṅgasya sākṣāditarathā svāśrayavācyatvasambandhenārthāṃśe prakārakatvāddharmitāvacchedakatvena dharmitāvacchedakasamānādhikaraṇatvena saktigrahaviṣayatāyā niyāmakatvasya phalabalena kalpanāt|
vastutastu pāribhāṣikaliṅgamapyartha eva svīkarttumucitam| pum̐lliṅgaḥ śabda ityādau pum̐lliṅgādiśabdā arśa ādyajantāḥ| ātmani sarvasyādhyastatvena paramparayā tatrāpi styānādisattvāt| "ātmā brahma" ityādiprayogopapattiḥ| styānaman-tirobhābo'pacaya iti yāvat, upacayādivivakṣāyā eva prayoganiyāmakatvena teṣāmekatra virodhe'pikṣatyabhāvāt| ata eva "sāmānye napuṃmasakam" iti tatra tatroktaṃ saṅgacchate| utkarṣāsattve'pi tadavivakṣāyāmiti tadartha ityādyanyato'vadhārtham|
liṅgāditrayasya nāmārthatvaṃ sādhayituṃ śaṅkate-nanviti|  anvayavyatirekābhyāmiti ṭābādīsamabhivyāhāre aśvādipadāt strītvabodhādabhāve ca tad‌bodhādevaṃrūpābhyāmityarthaḥ| evamanyatrāpyūhyam| tathāca tābhyāṃ liṅgadeḥ pratyayārthatvāvadhāraṇānna liṅgāditrikasya prakṛtyarthatvamityarthaḥ| dadhi paśyatītyādāvityādinā `ayaṃ yāti' `ityamāyāti' ityādiparigrahaḥ| bodhāditi karmatvādibodhādityarthaḥ| tathāca vyatirekavyabhicāreṇānyalabhyatvācca tadarthatvābhāvānnāmārthatvameva teṣāmāvaśyakamiti bhāvaḥ| vakṣyamāṇayuktayā saṅakhayākārakayoḥ pratyayārthatve'pi liṅgasya prakṛtyarthatvameva yuktamityāśayenāha-liṅgeti|
ata eveti prakṛtipratyayayoranyatarasya vācakatve yuktisāmyādevetyarthaḥ nirbandha iti idameva mataṃ mukhyamityabhinivesa ityarthaḥ| tadevāha-pratyayasyaiveti| yuktatvāditi| anantānāṃ prakṛtīnāṃ śaktatvakalpanāpekṣayā katipayasvādināmeva tatkalpane lāghavāditi bhāvaḥ|
kecittu-ṭābādikaṃ vināpi vāgādiśabdebhyaḥ strītvasyāvyutpattipakṣe rāmajñānādiśabdebhyaḥ punnapuṃsakatvayorbodhasya sārvajanīnānubhavasiddhatvālliṅgasya prātipadikārthatvameva| luptavibhaktismaraṇādapi bodāṅgīkāreṇa saṅkhayākārakayorvibhaktivācyataiva| uktañca-
svārtho dravyañca liṅgañca karmādireva ca|
amī pañcaiva nāmārtastrayaḥ keṣāñcidagrimāḥ|| iti|
agrimāḥ-liṅāntā ityāhuḥ|
nanvekavacanatvāderanugatasya durvacatayā kataṃ tena rūpeṇa svādīnāmekatvādivācakatvam| tatāhi-na tāvadekatvādivācakatvaṃ tat, vācakatāyāḥ śaktatārūpatvenātmā''śrayāt, bodhakatārūptave tu dvivacanādīnāmapi saktibhrameṇaikatvādibodhakatayā'tiprasabhaṅgāt, `bodhakatvaṃ śaktiḥ' iti mate ātmāśrayasya durvāratvācca| nāpyekavacanatvādikaṃ jātiḥ, sutvādinā sāṅkaryyāt| naca pāṇinisaṅketasambandenaikavacanādipadavattvaṃ tat, tādṛśasaṅketasya saṅketatvena sambandhatve kasyacidekavacanādipadādaujasādirboddhavya ityākārakasaṅketasyāpi sambhavenātiprasaṅgatādavsthyāt| pāṇinisaṅketatvena tattve tu vyākaraṇāntarapraṇetṛpuruṣāntarasyātapi pāṇinisaṅketatulyasaṅketasambandhena ekavacanādipadavattsya, `ḍitthādipadāt svaujasādirboddhavyaḥ' ityākārakapuruṣaviśeṣīyasaṅketasambandhena ḍitthādipadavattvasya vā vinigamanāviraheṇa śaktatāvacchedakatvāpatteriti cenna,
sutvām‌tvādinaivekavacanasya sakhyatvābhyupagamenoktadoṣanavakāśāt, kāryyatāvaccedakakoṭāvavyavahitottaraniveśācca na parasparajanyopasthitau vyabhicāraḥ| naca `dadhi' `madhu' ityādau vyabhicāraḥ, tatra luptavibhaktyanusandānenaiva bodhābhyupagamāt| kathamanyathā dyotakatāvādimate'pi kevalaprātipadikāt tadupapattiḥ, dyotakasyaivābhāvāt|
nacānekasutvādīnāṃ śaktatvāvacchedakatve gauravāt prātipadikatvasyaikasya tattavamucitam; lāghavāditi śaṅkayam, uktarītyā prātipadikatvasyāpyanugatasya durvacatvāt, tadajñāne'pyekatvādibodasyānubhavasiddhatvāt| padatvena varṇatvena vā śaktatve tu vibhakterapi vācakatāsiddheḥ, ekatvādyupasthiteḥ prāgvarṇatvādyupasthiterapyanāvasyakatvāt tadanurodhena tatkalpane gauravācca| padārthayorviśeṣaṇabhāvenānvaye bhinnapadajanyopasthitestatra tayaikaprakṛtyupasthāpyaghaṭaikatvādyostadbhāvena parasparamananvayāpattiśceti svādīnāṃ saṅkhayādivācakatvameveti bhāvaḥ|
dyotakatāvādināṃ punarayamāsayaḥ-na prakṛtīnāṃ ghaṭaikatvādyorviśakalitayo śaktiryenoktadūṣaṇāvakāśaḥ syāt, kintvekatvāviśiṣṭe ghaṭādau pṛthak‌śaktayanabhyupagamenānvayasyaivābhāvāt, anantaprakṛtīnāmekatvādisaktatākalpanagauravasyāpyabhāvācca, ghaṭapadasyaikatve śaktibhramadaśāyām `ghaṭaḥ prameyaḥ' ityādivākyādekatvaṃ pramaiyamiti bodhasya sarvasiddhatayā datanupodhenaikatvādiviṣayakaśābdhabode ghaṭādipadajanyaikatvādyupasthiterhetutāyā āvasyakatvācca|
kiñca vibhakteḥ saṅkhayāvācakatvamate samānaviṣayakānumitisāmagrīpratibandakatāyāṃ prātipadikajanyaghaṭādyupasthitiḥ svādijanyaikatvādyupasthitiśca niveśyā| tathāca tayorviśeṣaṇabhāve vinigamanāviraheṇa pratibandhayaprartibandhakabhāvabāhulyam| manmate tadu bhayasthalīyaikaikaviśiṣṭavi,yakopasthitistatra niveśyeti lāghavam, viśiṣṭaviṣayakopasthiterapyekatvādiprakāratānirūpitaghaṭādiviśeṣyatāśālitvena, ghaṭādiveśeṣaśyatānirūpitaikatvādiprakāratāśālitvena vā niveśa ityatra vinigamakābhāvāt sāmyamiti tu na, bhavanmate yatra yogyatājñānaghaṭopasthityekatvopasthitīnāṃ tisṛṇāṃ viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāve vinigama nāviraheṇa pratibandhyapratibandakabhāvaṣaṭ‌kam, tatra manmate yogyatājñāne viśiṣṭaviṣayakopasthiteḥ pratyekaṃ viśiṣṭobhayarūpeṇa vaiśiṣṭayaṃ niveśya dvayam, pratyekaṃ tādṛsarūpāvaccinnāyāmupasthitau yogyatājñānasya vaiśiṣṭayaṃ niveśya ca dvayamiti catuṣṭayamiti lāghavāt| atiprasaṅgaparihārastu tattatsaṅkhayābodhe tattadvacanasamabhivyāhārasya hetutvakalpanāt|
evam `kārakaṃ vibhaktayarthaḥ' iti kalpe tattatkārakabodhe svottaratattadvibhaktisamabhivyāhārasya hetutvakalpanāt sa bodhyaḥ| sa ca `am‌ghaṭaḥ' ityāto bodhavāraṇāya vācakatāvādināmapyāvasyakaḥ| evaṃ rītyopasargāṇāmapi dyotakatvaṃ bodyamiti pakṣadvaye'pi harisammatimāha dyotikā vācikā veti|
yadvā saṅkhayāvato'rthasya samudāyo'bhidhāyakaḥ|
iti tvasayottarārddham `ghaṭaḥ' ityādyākārakānamupūrvyavacchinnavibhaktayantasamudāyo vaikatvādivisiṣṭaghaṭādivācaka iti tadarthaḥ| padasphoṭamavalambya cedam| nāmārtha-dhātvarthayoḥ sākṣādabhedāririktasambandhenānvayasyāvyutpannatvādabhidhānānabhidhānavyavasthānupapatteśca kārakavibhaktayarthaḥ|
yadi ca nāmārthadhātvarthayorbhedena parasparānvayāpādanam, taṇḍulakarmakapākaviśeṣyakabodhe dvitīyāntataṇḍulapadasamabhivyāhṛtapacatītyādāvānupūrvoviśeṣajñānasya hetutvakalpanayaiva nirasituṃ śakyam| anabhihitādhikārasyāpi saṅkhayā vibhaktayarthaḥ iti pakṣe evopayogastatpakṣe "karmaṇi dvitīyā"  "dvayekayoḥ"  ityādīnāmekavākyatayaiva pravṛttestatra viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāve kāmacārāt| ekasya yatkarma, karmaṇi yadekatvādanabhihitasūtramarthasampattiḥ prathamakalpe dvitīyādīnām, tiṅādīnāñca karmādivācakatayānyatareṇābhidhānenyatarasya gatārthatvādanabhihitasūtramaphalam| dvitīye tu kṛtā karmaṇo'bhidhāne'pi tad‌gatasaṅkhayāpi anabhidhānāt prāptāṃ dvitīyāṃ vārayitu tadavasaraḥ| sati tasminnanabhihitakarmagatatvābhāvānna dvitīyādiprasaṅgaḥ| saṅkhayāpi yadi prātipadikārthastadoktavākyārthāsambhavāt sūtravaiyarthyameva|
nanvanabhihitasūtrābhāve `bhīṣamaṃ kaṭaṃ karoti' ityādau bhīṣamādipadād dvitīyādyanupapattiḥ, viśiṣṭasvaiva kaṭādeḥ kriyayepsitatamatvāt prātipadikārthamātre prathamāprasaṅgat| tatsūtrapakṣe tvabhidhānasya kṛdādibhiḥ parigaṇanāt kaṭaprakṛtidvitīyayā'bhihite'pi tasmin bhīṣmādipadebhyo dvitīyādivibhakteḥ sūpapādatvāditi cenna,
viśiṣṭasya vidhitsāyāṃ dharmimtreṇa toṣābhāvāddharmāṇāmapīpsitatamatvāt karmatvam, tasya cānyavācakapadottaradvitīyayānabhidhānāt| `kṛtaḥ kaṭaḥ' ityādau tu pratyayasya dhātūttaravarttitvena tena prātipadikārtaviśeṣaniṣṭhakarmatvasyānabhidhānādaviśeṣāddharminiṣṭhatadabhidhānamiti bhīṣmādiviśeṣaṇavācakapadebhyaḥ prathamopapattiriti na kāpi tadanārambhe'nupapattiḥ| taduktaṃ bhāṣye "kaṭo'pi karma bhīṣmādayo'pi" iti, tadā'stu kārakamapi prātipadikārthaḥ, asminnapi pakṣe kārakasya prakṛtyarthavyaktiviśeṣaṇataiva vibhaktidyotyatvāditi kecit|
nacaivaṃ prātipadikārthapraākārakabode subjanyopastiterhetutvaṃ vilīyeteti vāccam, iṣṭatvāt| etatkalpe jātiliṅgasaṅkhayākārakaviśiṣṭadravyasya prātipadikād bodhanoktahetuhetumadbhāvasya gaganakusumāyamānatvād viśeṣyataiva tu kārakasyocitānubhavāt, kriyānvayānurodhācceti pañcakapakṣasyopapattiriti| bahuṣu sthaleṣviti bhāṣyādyākaragrantheṣvityarthaḥ| tatra pañcakacatuṣkapakṣau tatropapadamityatra kaiyaṭenopanyastau, trikamiti pakṣastu vṛttikāreṇa| prādhānyeneti tatra sarveṣāmeva pakṣāṇāṃ bhāṣyakṛtopanyāsāditi bhāvaḥ| sūtrādāvityādinā'nabhihitādisūtrasaṃgrahaḥ  `ghaṭamānaya' ityādiśābdabode ghaṭādiśabdasya bhāne pramāṇābhāvena tasya prātipadikārthatvasādhanamanucitamityāśaṅkāṃ pariharata-śabdastāvaditi| tatra harivākyaṃ pramāṇayati-na so'stīti| pratyayaḥ- śābdabodhātmakaḥ| śabdānugamāditi śabdaviṣayakatvādityarthaḥ|
anuviddhamiti viśiṣṭamityarthaḥ| anuvyavasāyo yathā'rthena viśiṣṭajñānaṃ viṣayīkaroti tathā śabdena viśiṣṭamapītyarthaḥ| vaiśiṣṭhayañcārthadvārakaṃ bodhyam| ghaṭapadajanyabodhānantaram `ghaṭaṃ śābdayāmi' ityevānuvyavasāyānna kalaśaṃ kumbhaṃ vetyata eva "udeti savitā tāmraḥ" (bhānuḥ) ityādau na paunaruktyam, tatpadānupādāne udayāstayoḥ śābdavodhe ekarūpyānirvāhāditi bhāvaḥ|
nanu tāvatā'pi śabdabhānaṃ siddham, na viśeṣyatveneti kathaṃ śabdasya śabdārthatvasiddhirata āha-viṣṇumiti arthoccāraṇāsambhaveneti vadatā lakṣaṇā'vasaro'pi sūcitaḥ| asaṅgatiriti anupapattirityarthaḥ| so'pi-śabdo'pi| prātipadikārtha iti prakārāntareṇopasthitasya viśeṣyatvāsambhavāditi bhāvaḥ|
lakṣaṇayā svavācyavācakatvasambandarūpayā nirvāha iti tāvatā'pi padavāttayatvāsiddheriti bhāvaḥ| nirūḍheti nirūḍhatvañca lakṣaṇāyāmanāditātparyyagrahādhīnatvam, anāditvañca tatra svajanyabodhasamānākārapūrvapūrvabodhadhvaṃsavattvam| śaktyanatirekādityasyāsmanmata ityādiḥ| etattatvamagre vakṣyate| tasyā asārvatrikatvamapyāha- jabagaḍadaśamiti tatra samudāyasya kutrāpyaśaktatvāditi bhāvaḥ| agrahāditi manmate tu tādṛśasamudāyasyaiva tatpadaśakyatayā'nupapattiriti bhāvaḥ|
nanu tatra samudāyasyāśaktatve'pi tad‌ghaṭakajakārādivarṇānāmekākṣarakosādau jaitrādau śaktipratipādanāttattadarthavācakatvarūpalakṣaṇāyāstatra nānupapattirata āha-ajñātāyā iti gaṅgāpadasay vastutaḥ svaśakyapravāhasāmīpyarūpasambandhasyāgrahe lakṣyārthabodhasyānudayena jñātāyā eva tasyāḥ padārthopastityaupayikatvena prakṛte tadagrahāduktānupapattistadavasthaiveti bhāvaḥ|
nanu śābdabodhāvyavahitaprākkāle tādṛśavṛttigrahāsattve'pi yadā kadācijjātatādṛśavṛttigrahādinodabuddhasaṃskāreṇa padārthopastitidvārā tādṛśaśābdabodhopapattiḥ sukaretyata āha-gāvamiti gośabdādyanukaraṇe sādhutvasya sarvairevābhupagamādityarthaḥ| paranaya iti paraḥ naiyāyikastanmate teṣāṃ saktayābhāvena tad‌ghaṭitalakṣaṇāyā apyaprasarād vṛttimatvātmakārthavattvanibandhanaprātipadikatvādyanupapattiriti bhāvaḥ|
ṣoḍhā'pīti pūrvoktapañcakasahitaḥ śabdo'pītyarthaḥ kvacit-anukaraṇasthale| taccānukaraṇaṃ dvividham| dhvervarṇasya ca| tatrādyam-paṭatkarotītyādi| dvitīyam-bhubo vugityādi| bhedavivakṣeti paṭadityādau dhvanīmayatva-varṇamayatvābhyām, bhuvo vugityādāvarthakatva-śabdapadārthakatvābhyāṃ tad‌vivakṣetyarthaḥ| śabdo'pi prātipadikārtha iti tathāca "matau chaḥ sūktasāmnoḥ"  iti sūtre yo'sāvāmnāye'syavāmaśabdaḥ paṭhayate sa chaprakṛterasyavāmīyamityādau padārtho'ta eva prātipadikatvācchasiddharitīti bhāvaḥ| mūle arthe'vabhāsate iti pāṭhe arthe-ghaṭādisvarūpe avabhāsate viśeṣaṇatayā viṣayo bhavatītyarthaḥ|
vastutastu bhūṣaṇaparyyālojanayāsāvartho va bhāsate iti pāṭhaḥ| tatra `va'śabdaḥ sādṛśye, tathāca bhedapakṣe yathā'nukāryyarūpo'rtho viśeṣyatayā śābdabodhe bhāsate tathā'bhedapakṣe'nukāryyābhinnānukaraṇaśabdo'pītyarthaḥ| tatrānupasthitasya kathaṃ bhāvamityākāṅkṣāyāmuktam-śrotrādibhiriti ādinā-vṛttijñānatajjanyasaṃskārasaṅgraha iti kecit| vastutastu-lipyādyanusaṃhitasya tasya śābde bhānasampattaye-ādīti| `padopasthitameva śābdabodhe bhāsate' iti tvasārvatrikamiti bhāvaḥ| ata eva sārakṛtā `arthavadbhāsate' iti tadvivṛtam| etenābhedapakṣe|़nukaraṇasyārthabhāvena tadaṃśe bhānavarṇanamasambhavaduktikamityāśaṅkā samāhitā| nanvabhedapabhepyanukāryyasya śābde kathaṃ bhānam, padavṛttijanyopasthityabhāvādata āha-ayambhāva iti `padānupasthitatvāt' ityasya śābdabodhe bhānānupapatteyati seṣaḥ| tatsiddhaye śābdabodhajanakapadajanyopasthitaye ityarthaḥ| śaktiriti `asambaddhasya tatsmṛtyajanakatvā'diti śeṣaḥ| tathā padavṛttijñānajanyopastitisadbhāvāttasya śābdaviṣayatā sulabheti| abhede iti abhedavivakṣāyāmityarthaḥ, tatpakṣe nirūpyanirūpakatvayorbhedanibandanatvenānukaraṇe tad‌vṛttyasambhavāditi bhāvaḥ| hetutvāditi upastatiniṣṭhajanyatāyāmanveti|
svapratyakṣeti8 anukaraṇapratyakṣarūpāmityarthaḥ|  śābdabodhaviṣayatopapattiriti ayamāśayaḥ-abhedapakṣe svapratya7rūpāmupastitimādāya yathā śābde śabdasya bhānam, tathaiva `ghaṭamānaya' ityādau ghaṭādipadānāmapi tāṃ sāmagrīmādāya bhānasya durvāratayā śābdabodhamātrasya śabādaviṣayakatvasiddhiḥ, taduktaṃ `svaṃ rūpam' iti sūtre bhāṣye-"naitadvaktavyam, kiṃ kāraṇam? śabdapūrvako hyarthe sampratyayaḥ, ātaśca śabdapūrvaḥ' yo'pi hyasau nāmnā''hūyate nāma ca tena yadā nopalabdhaṃ tadā pṛcchati-kiṃ bhavānāheti, śabdapūrvakaścārthasya sampratyayaḥ iha śāstre sabde sambhavādarthasya nivṛttiḥ' iti| tatra-`śabdapūrvako hi' ityādinā śabdavṛttijñānasya padārthopasthityādāvanvayavyatirekau pradarśya `śabdapūrvaka' ityādigrantena prakārāntaraṃ pradarśyate, sabdaviśeṣaṇaka ityarthaḥ| tenārthe asambhave'rthaviśeṣaṇatayopasthite śabde kāryyamityarthaḥ| tathācoktabhāṣyacchabdhatadarthayorubhayoreva śabdādbhānam| yatra ca loke śabde itarapadārthānvayavādhastatrārthaṃśe śabdasya viśeṣaṇatā| yatra tvarthe "viṣṇumuccāraya" "agnerḍhak"  ityādau tatra śabdāṃse'rthasya viseṣaṇateti spaṣṭatipatteḥ, viṣayatvamanādṛtyeti vākyapadīyācceti| atiprasaṅgeti-ghaṭo'stītyataḥ samavāyenaikajñānaviṣayatvādinā vopasthitasyākāśādeḥ śābde bhānāpattivāraṇāyetyarthaḥ|
vṛttijanyeti vṛttijñānajanyetyarthaḥ| padopasthitiriti ghaṭapadaṃma śrṛṇvivatyādau tādṛśapadopasthiterhetutāyā darśanāt prakṛte ca tadabhāve'nukaraṇasya śābdaviṣayatvaṃ durghaṭamiti bhāvaḥ|
āśrayatayeti idañca `bodhakatvaṃ śaktiḥ' ityabhyupetya| `bodhyatvaṃ sā' iti pakṣe tu nirūpakatayeti bodhyam| nanvāśrayatāsaṃsargeṇa vṛttimattavasyaiva śābdabodhaviṣayatve tantratve nirūpakatāsaṃsargeṇa vṛttimataḥ śābde bhānānupapattirata āha-nirūpakateti pramāṇikagoravasyādoṣatvāditi bhāvaḥ|  anavadyamiti vṛttinirūpakārthavodhakatvasyaiva prātipadikatve tantratvānnābedapakṣe tatsaṃjñāprasaktiriti bhāvaḥ|
nanu svapratiyogikatvaviśiṣṭasambandhā''śrayatāyāḥ svasminnanabhyupagamena kathamanukaraṇasya vṛttimattetyata āha-sambandhasyeti| tad‌bodhakatveneti pratyakṣā''tamakaśābdabodhajanakatvenetyarthaḥ| tathāca haryyādivākyaprāmāṇyāt prakṛte svasminnapi ādṛśasambandhāśrayatā'bhyupagamānna tasya śābde bhāvanānupapattirityanayaiva rītyā śabdamātrasya śābde bhānaṃ nirvāhyamiti bhāvaḥ| nanu svaniṣṭhabodhakatvasya svanirūpyatve padāntarasyevānukaraṇasyāpi prātipadikatvaṃ durvāram, kiñca svīyapratyakṣa''tmakabodhajanakatvasya vṛttitvaṃ durupapādam, śābdabodhajanakapadārthopasthitijanakajñānaviṣayatā'vaccedakasambandhasyaiva tattvāt| nacaitādṛśajñānena śābdabodhāt prāk‌ śabdarūpapadārthopastitirjanyate| nāpi bodhakatvavaditi jñānaṃ tadānīmastīta yena sā'pi sambhāvyeta, ghaṭādāvagṛhītavṛttikasya ghaṭapadaṃ svapratyakṣajanakamiti jñānād ghaṭapadaśābdabodhānanubhavāccatasmāt, kathametaditi cet,
atra vadanti-sambandasyetyādigrantasya padavṛttyupasthitenārthena sambandāviśeṣācchabdopasthitistataḥ śabdarthayorubhayorapi śābde bhānaṃ śabdavṛttiprayojyatvasyopasthitidvaye sattvādityarthaparatvānnoktānupapattiḥ, tatrārthasyevetyanantaraṃ bodhyatvāditi śeṣaḥ| tad‌bodhakatvenetyatra tadā'vyahitaprāgupastitārthapadārthaparāmarśasyaivocityādarthabodakatvenetyarthāt| taduktam-
yatsaṃjñāsmaraṇaṃ tatra na tadapyanyahetukam|
piṇḍa eva hi dṛṣṭaḥ san saṃjñā smārayituṃ kṣamaḥ|| iti|
dṛṣṭaḥ-upasthitaḥ śābdabodhastu vakṣyamāṇaharyyādivākyaprāmāṇyāt kṣaṇavilambenaivābhyupagantavyaḥ| anukāryyasyā'bhedapakṣe bhānāya tu prakārāntaramevānusarttavyamiti|
paretu-uktamate padārthopasthiteḥ śābdabodhahetutāyāṃ tadavacchedakakoṭau tatta tsambandhāvacchinnavattijñānajanyatvaniveśāvaśyakatvena vṛtterdvedhā praveśe gauravam| anyataratvena sambandhaniveśe tvanyataratvaghaṭakabhedayorviśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāve vinigamanāvirahāttattādavasthyameva| tadapekṣyābhedapakṣe sādṛśyasambandhenānukaraṇādanukāryyopasthitistatathopasthitasyāpi tātparyyavaśācchābde bhānaṃ vṛttijñānajanyatāvacchedakakoṭau sādṛśyajñānājanyatvaniveśānna taddhetutāyāṃ vyabhicāraḥ| ata eva gavityayamāhetyādau na vibhaktyutpattiḥ, vṛttyārthabodhakatvarūpārthavattvābhāvenāprātipadikatvāt| spaṣṭaścāyamarthaḥ "matau chaḥ sūktasāmnoḥ"  ityatra bhāṣyakaiyaṭayorityāhuḥ|
svapratiyogikatvaviśiṣṭasambandhasya svasmin sattāṃ tejodṛṣṭāntavyājena darśayan svokte harisammatimāha-uktañceti| grāhyatvam-bodhyatvam| grāhakatvam-bodhakatvam| ete-grāhyatva-grāhakatve| pṛthagavasthite ityanena tayorasamanaiyatyaṃ darśayati| dīpādirūpatejaso viṣayasannidhāne śaktidvayaṃ loke pratyakṣasiddham| tadasamavadhāne tu grāhyatvameva yathā, tathā śabdānamarthāvādhe uktaśaktidvayam| tadvādhe tu grāhyatvameveti tadarthaḥ|
svaṃ rūpamiti sūtrabhāṣyādapyuktārthasyaiva lābhāditi tasyaiva padyāntaramāha-viṣayatvamiti svasya viṣayatāmasampādya śabādairtho na bodhyate ityarthaḥ, svajanyārthabodhatvasya svaviṣayakatvavyāpyatvāditi yāvat|
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ-śabdārthayorabhedamate tanmūlakādhyāsācchābdādarthā''kāravṛttau jāyamānāyaṃ śabdenāpi tasyāṃ svākārasamarpaṇamiti śābdabodhasya śabdaviṣayakatvasiddhiriti|
atra vadanti-"na so'sti pratyayo loke" ityuktatvāt kiṃ bodhatvāvacchedena śabdaviṣayakatvaṃ pratijñāyate, uta grāhyatvamiti padyadvayācchabdatvāvacchedena tattvam| kiṃ vā śābdatvasāmānādhikaraṇyena| nādyaḥ, ghaṭādipratyakṣāvyavahitaprākkāle ghaṭādipadopastiteranaiyatyena tādṛśapratyakṣasya ghaṭādipadaviṣayakatvāsambhāvat| evaṃ ghaṭādyanumitāva pi tadbhāvaṃ na sambhavati, vyāpakatayā tadavacchedakatayā vā vyāptigrahe tadabhānāt, tadanantaram `ghaṭapadaṃ sākṣātkaromi' `ghaṭapadamanuminomi' ityanuvyavasāyābhāvācca| na dvitīyaḥ, ghaṭapadajñānajanyopastitestadvācakapade'bhāvena tasya śābdaviṣayatvāsambhavāt, padajñānasya sambandhijñānavidhayā'rthasmārakatvena hastipakadarśanajanyahastyupasthitau hastipakasyaiva tajjanyārthopasthitāvuddhodhakatāvacchedakapadasya viṣayatvāsambhavācca| `ghaṭo na ghaṭaśabdaḥ'
iti sārvajanīnapratītyā śabdarthayostādātmye mānābhāvena nārthākāravṛtteḥ śabdā''kāratāsambhavo'pi ghaṭaśabdabodhānantaraṃ ghaṭapadaṃ śābdayāmīti pratīteḥ sārvajanīnatve kṣaṇavilambasahiṣṇutā sambhavedapi naca tasyāstathātvamiti na kiñcidetat|
apica bhinne viṣaye'numitisāmagnayābalavattvāt yatra tādṛśānumitisāmagrīkāle kevalaghaṭatvaviśiṣṭaviṣayikāśābdadhīrjāyatāmiticchābalād ghaṭaśābdadhīstatra ghaṭāṃśe tadbhānaṃ durupapādameva| ghaṭaśabdaśābdecchāvirahaviśiṣṭabhinnaviṣayakā numitisāmagnyāḥ pratibāndhikāyāḥ sattvāt| nāpi tṛtīyaḥ, siddhasādhanāt, `viṣṇumuccāraya' ityādau viṣṇvādipadānāṃ lakṣaṇayā śābdaviṣayatāyāḥ parairabhyupagamāt, uktabhāṣyādapi kvacicchābdabodhe śabdo viṣaya ityarthasyaiva lābhāt, sati tātparyye kvacidanyatrāpi viśeṣyāṃ'śe tadbhānābhyupagame kṣativirahācca| ata eva nalādīnāṃ viśiṣya tattadrūpeṇānupasthitatve'pi nalapadavācyaḥ kaścidāsīditi sāmānyaśaktigrahāttatpadavācyatvena teṣāṃ bodhaḥ prācīnasammata upapadyate| abhede śabdabhānaṃ tu pare tvityuktabhāṣyasamatamatenaiva| ata eva tasmādavikṛtasyaiva bodhaḥ| harivacanāni tu praṇavabrahmaṇorabhedepāsanaśraddhā'tiśayabodhakāni bhavantīti na tairvyāvahārikārthasya siddhistasmācchābdabodhamātrasya śabdaviṣayakata na vicārasahamadhikamagre vyaktībhaviṣyatīti  anyatheti bhedapakṣasyaivābhyupagame ityarthaḥ| pratyayaḥ paraśceti niyamaśāstrāṇāṃ niṣedhamukhenāpi pravṛtteḥ `pratyayaparaiva prakṛtiḥ prayoktavyā na kevalā'iti niyamamūlakasya "apadaṃ na prayuñjīta" iti niṣedhavideḥ pratyayayogyasya pratyayaṃ vinā prayoge'tikramaṇādasādhutāpatterityarthaḥ| asmanmate tu tasya pratyayayogyatvābhāvānniṣedhaviṣayatāyā evābhāvattadatikramaṇā prasakteḥ sādhutvādeva ca tatrāvādeśādirapi| anyathā bhāṣāśabdeṣviva na tatpravṛttiḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ 
iti śrībhūṣaṇasāradarpaṇe nāmārtanirūpaṇam || 4 ||

 kluptaśaktayaiva nirvāhānna nāmasamudāyātmakasamāse'tiriktā śaktiriti mataṃ dūṣayiṣyan prasaṅgātsamāsārthaṃ nirūpayitumādau tadvibhāgapradarśanamityāśayenāha-samāsāniti samāsatvañca śaktisambandhena samāsapadavattvam, ekārthobhāvāpan napadasamudāyaviśeṣo vā| ekārthobhāve mānañca "kiṃ punaḥ samarthannāma? pṛthagārthānamekārthobhāvaḥ samarthavacanam, kva punaḥ pṛthagarthani, kvaikārthāni| vākye pṛthagārthāni-`rāpūruṣaḥ" iti samarthasūtrasthaṃ bhāṣyam, tanmūlikā vakṣyamāṇā yuktayadaśca|
tatrekārthatvamekopasthitiviṣayārthakatvam| natvekārthasaktatvam, ghaṭādipadānāmapi ghaṭaghaṭatvatatsambandharūpanānārthaśaktatvenaikārtatānāpatteḥ| nānopasthitiviṣayārthakatvaṃ pṛthagarthakatvam| yathā vākye pṛthak‌padārthānākāṅkṣādisahakārema visiṣṭārthamavagāhate, "saṃspṛṣṭārthaṃ samartham" iti bhāṣyāt| rājapūruṣādipadam-`rājasambandhavatpuruṣādau śaktam' ityākārakaḥ śaktigrahaḥ| tataśca prāptāprāptavivekena viśiṣṭaśaktiḥ ṣaṣṭhayādyarthe paryavasyati| ata eva na tatra rājādau sundarādipadārthānvayaḥ, ekadeśatvāt| `devadattasya gurukulam' ityādau tu gamakatvāt sa iti vakṣyate| jahatsvārthāvṛttiḥ padānāmānarthakyamiti tu na samyak, `mahābāhuḥ' ityādāvātvādyanāpatteḥ| bhūtapūrvagatyāśrayaṇantu satyāṃ gatāvanucitam| tudaktam-
jahatsvārthā tu tatraiva yatra rūḍhirvirodhinā| iti|
avayavārthaviruddho yatra samudāyārthastatraiva sā, yathā| kṛṣṇāsarpādāviti tadarthaḥ| ayameva pakṣaḥ-
samāse khalu bhinnaiva śaktiḥ paṅkajaśabdavat|
itivadato mūlakṛtaḥ sammataḥ, tathā sati paṅkajaśabdādau parairyogaruḍhayabhyupagamena tadadṛṣṭāntopanyāsasārthakyāt| jahatsvārthā vṛttiriti pakṣasya tatsammatatve svamate tasya pakṣatayā paraistatra tadanabhyupagamena tadupanyāsānupapatteḥ spaṣṭatvāt| adhikamagre vakṣyata iti|
gatimatodāttavateti upalakṣaṇamidam-`saha supeti yogavibhāgasyāpi| `anuvyācalat' ityatrānorvyacaladityanena, veścācaladityanena yugapatsamāsaḥ, anviti subandena vyacaladiti samudāyasya samarthatvāt| satayāṃ samāsasaṃjñāyāṃ śākalapratidhādyaṇādeśaḥ, samāsāntodāttatvañca bhavati| tiṅaikatvasyoktatvāt prakṛtyarthavyāpārasya saṅkhyānvayāyogyatvādvā na tataḥ subutpattiḥ| samyaparipālakautsargikaikavacanasya tu nāvasaraḥ, chandasi tadāpādanāsambhavāditi bhāvaḥ| tathā subantasyānabhidhānādevāpravṛttiriti bhāvaḥ|
anyatheti subutpatteḥ prāksamāsasaṃjñā'bhyupagama ityarthaḥ| nalopatyasyāpadatvādityādiḥ| yadyapi `upapadam'  iti sūtre bhāṣye'nayoryornivṛttaṃ supsupetyuktam, tathāpyupadamiti mahāsaṃjñābalādeva pūrvapadasya subutpattyanantaram(eva) samāsa iti bhāvaḥ| `prāksubutpatteḥ' ityekadeśaprayojanapradarśanamiṣeṇa paribhāṣāyāḥ prayojanamapi darśitam| subutpatteḥ paścāt samāse hi svārtha-dravya-liṅga-saṅkhayā-kārakāṇāṃ kramikatvasyāntaraṅga bahiraṅgabhāvamūlakasya "kutsite"  iti sūtrabhāṣyasammatatvenāntaraṅgavāt subutpatteḥ pūrvaṃ ṭāpyanadantatvāt "krītāt karaṇapūrvāt"  iti ṅīṣo'nupapateriti dik|
bhāṣye vyutpādita iti tatra hi pratyekaṃ samāsasaṃjñāvāraṇarūpaṃ sahaśabdaprayojanaṃ samudāye vākyaparisamāptityāyena parihṛtya "evaṃ tarhi siddhe sati yat sahagrahaṇaṃ karoti tasyaitat prayojanaṃ yogāṅgaṃ yathā vijñāyeta, sati yogāṅge yogavibhāgaḥ kariṣyate| saha sūp samasyate| kena saha samarthena, anuvyacalat `anuprāviśate'| tataḥ supā, ca saha sup samasyate| adhikāraśca lakṣaṇa़ñca, yasya samāsasya anyallakṣaṇaṃ nāsti idaṃ tasya lakṣaṇaṃ bhaviṣyati" ityuktam| tatrādhikāraprayojanam-devadattaḥ pacatītyādau samānādhikaraṇena samānasaṃjñāvāraṇaṃ bodhyam  bhedamiti ṣāḍ‌vidhyarūpamītyarthaḥ prācīnetyādi| yadyapi "avyayaṃ vibhakti"  iti sūtre bhāṣye samudāyasya saṃskāraryyatvāt prādhānyāttasyaiva samṛddhayādayo viśeṣaṇānīti samudāyāt-samṛddhayādīnāṃ gamyamānatvādatrā'pyavyayībhāvāpattirityabhipratyeṇaha-`iha kasmānna bhavati sumadrāḥ, sacchātra iti' praśne `naiṣa doṣaḥ, iha kaścit samāsaḥ pūrvapadārthapradhānaḥ' ityādisamāsalakṣaṇānyuktvā `nacātraṃpūrvapadārthaprādhānyaṃ gamyate' iti samāhitam| tathāpi `athavā' `neme samāsārthā nirdiśyante' ityādinā pakṣāntaropanyāśenoktalakṣaṇeṣnirbharasūcanādadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ|
lakṣaṇāsyeti tattavañcāsādhāraṇadharmavattavam| dharme'sādhāraṇñca-taditarāvṛttitve sati sakalatad‌vṛttitvam| yathā-sāsnāśrṛṅgavattvaṃ gorlakṣaṇam| ta6 śrṛṅgavattvaṃ gaganakṛṣṇarūpādīnāṃ lakṣaṇatvavāraṇāya satyantavṛttitvasākalyānāṃniveśaḥ| tuktam-"lakṣaṇe tānyeva padāni deyāni yairavyāptyativyāptyasambhavadoṣā vārtyyante" iti| tatra lakṣye lakṣaṇāsattvamavyāptiḥ| alakṣye tatsattvamatīvyāptiḥ| lakṣyamātrāvarttanamasambhavaḥ| udāharaṇāni vakṣyante| aśādhāraṇadharmavattājñānāttaddharmāśraya itarabhedānumitivyavahāraśca phalam| yasya tu dharmasya kevalānvayitvaṃ taddharmajñānasyantu vyavahāramātraṃ tat| taduktam-
vyāvṛttirvyavahāro vā lakṣaṇasya prayojanam| iti|
prāyovāda iti prāyaśasteṣāmupalabhyamānatve'pi anyeṣāmapi keṣāñcidupalabdherityarthastadeva viśadayati-
bhūtapūrva ityādi eṣāṃ vakṣyamāṇayatkiñcillakṣaṇākrāntatve'pi tattadadhikārīyasūtrānanuśiṣṭatvādasaṅgraho bodhyaḥ, samāsādhikārīyasūtrānuṣṭhatatvādvibhāge tadaṅgrahe iṣṭhāpatterayogāt|
pūrvapadārthetyādi prādhānyañca samāsaghaṭakapadārthaprakāratānirūpitamukhyaviśeṣyatvam| tathāca pūrvapadārthaviśeṣyakabodhajanakatvamavyayībhāvatvamityarthaḥ| evamagre'pi| `upakumbham' ityatra kumnirūpitasāmīpyasya viśeṣyatayā bodhāt `rājapuruṣaḥ' ityādau rājasambandipuruṣasyottarapadārthasya, `pītāmbaraḥ' ityatra pītābhinnambarasambandino'nyapadārthasya, `dhavakhadirau' ityatra sāhityāpannadhavakhadirayoḥ pūrvottarapadārthayorviśeṣyatayā bhānād yathāyathaṃ lakṣaṇasamanvayaḥ|
vyabhicārāditi yadyapi vyabhicāro'tīvyāptireva tathāpi prakṛte doṣanvena vyabhicārapadenobhyaṃ lakṣyate| yadvakṣyati-avyāptiriti tamevāminayena pradarśayati-tathāhītyādi pūrvapadārtheti unmattādipadārthetyarthaḥ| evamagre'pi| avyayībāvalakṣaṇeti pūrvapadārthaviśeṣyakabodhajanakatvarūpatallakṣaṇāsattvarūpetyarthaḥ| bahuvrīhīti tallakṣaṇetyarthaḥ| evamagre'pi|
nanu tarhi kānyavyayībhāvādilakṣaṇanītyata āha-siddhānte tviti lakṣyatā'vacchedakantu śaktisambandenāvyayībhāvādipadavattvam| bhūtapūrvādīnāṃ lakṣyatā'vchedakānākrāntatvāt tadasaṅgraho na doṣāyeti bhāvaḥ| jahatsvārthavṛttipakṣama valambya mūlamavatārayati-asambhavaścaiṣāmiti asya jahatsvārthavṛttipakṣa ityādiḥ  eṣām-lakṣaṇānām| asambhavaḥ-lakṣyāmātrā'varttanamityarthaḥ| tatpakṣe pūrvottarapadayostattvavena kutrāpi vṛtterabhāvena ladarthaghaṭitānāṃ teṣāmupakumbādāvasambhavāditi bhāvaḥ|
mūle bhauteti śabdanityatāvādimate yathāśrutamautapūrvyasyāpyavyayībhāvādāvasambhavādāha-so'pīti vyavahāro'pītyarthaḥ| rekheti| anyasya kathamanyadharmabodhakatvamata āśayaṃ pa3kāsayati sāre-rekhāgavayetyādi| tadvadeveti rekhāgavayādivadevetyarthaḥ| yathā gavayādāvavidyamānamapi rekākavayādiniṣṭhaṃ rekhākṛtipucchaṃ gavayādipucche sādṛśyamūlakābhedādhyavasāyād gavayādibodhakam, tathā samāse'vidyamānamapi vigrahavākyaniṣṭhapūrvapadārthādiprādhānyaṃ sādṛsayād avyayībhāvādibodakamityarthaḥ 
ajahatsvārthavṛttipakṣe tu pūrvapadārthaprādhānyavyavahāro na punaḥ durupapāda ityāśayena mūlamavatārayati-nanviti| anekadhoktaṣviti "atha teṣāṃ bruvatāṃ kiṃ jahatsvārthā vṛttirāhosvidajahat‌svārthā" ityādinetyādiḥ| ekārthobhāvavyapekṣeti ekārthobhāvastuktaḥ| vyapekṣā tu svārthaparyavasayināṃ padānāmākāṅkṣāvaśātparasparārthānvayabodhakatvam| paryyavasānaṃ labhyate iti samarthasūtrabhāṣyaparyyālocanayā tu ekārthabhāvaḥ sāmarthyamiti pakṣa eva vṛttidvayasvīkāro bod‌dhṛbhedena, kintu, atha ye vṛttiṃ varttayanti kiṃ te āhurityādibhāṣyād vākyādeveyaṃ vṛttirniṣpanneti matamavalambyāyaṃ vicāra iti labhyate| samāsaśaktayaiva ityevakāreṇāvayavaśiktivyavacchedaḥ| dṛṣṭāntamāha-vṛṣādipadānāmiveti|
ānupūrvyantaraghaṭakatvābhāvaviśiṣṭānupūrvyā eva śaktatāvacchedakatvena vṛṣabhādipadāntargatavṛṣādisamavetānupūrvyāḥ śaktatā'navaccedakatvavat samāsāntargatarājānupūrvyāḥ śaktatāvaccedakatvāsambhavādityarthaḥ| anyathā rāmādipadādagnyādīnāmavayavārthānāmapi padārthopasthitiśābdabodharorāpattiḥ, avāntakasubutpattyāpattiḥ, dhanaṃ vanamityādau jaśvā''pattiśca| vākye tu padānyarthavantyeva| `rājasambandhī pūruṣaḥ' ityādyarthe rājñaḥ pūruṣaḥ `puruṣo rājñaḥ' ityādiviśiṣṭānupūrvodvayasya śakyatāvaccheda katvakalpanā'pekṣayā padaśaktikalpanāyā eva laghīyastvāditi bhāvaḥ|
nacaitatkalpe viśiṣ‍rūpopādānavihitatvā'nāpattayā, mahābāhuḥ, supanthāḥ, ityādau doṣa, anarthakatvāt| evamarthavattvanibandhanaprātipadikatvābhāvena nalopādyanāpattiśceti vācyam, vṛttipragbhāvinamarthavattvamāropya tattakāryasya sūpapādatvāt| idañca "pratyayottarapadayośca"  iti sūtrabhāṣye spaṣṭam|
nacaivam `vāpyaśvaḥ' ityādau na doṣaḥ jahatsvārthāyāṃ vṛttāvānarthakyāditi "īdūtau ca saptamyarthe"  iti sūtrasthabhāṣyavirodho duṣpariharaḥ, āropitā'rthavattvasya samāsā'ntargatapadeṣu svīkārāditi vācyam, arthagrahamasāmarthyenāropitārthasya tatrāgrahaṇādityāśayāt| ekasyaiva padasya vṛttitatprākkālayorarthavattvānarthavattve ca prayogānurodha eva śaraṇam| yogarūḍhisthale tu yogāntarbhāveṇa śaktayabhyupagamānna taducchedo'pi|
nacātra pakṣe rājādipadaiḥ sarvathā svārthatyāge tadbhānaṃ na syāttasmāccaikapadārtho'parapadārthe viśeṣaṇamiti vyavahāra iti vācyam, viśiṣṭonaiva rājādiviśiṣṭapuruṣādyupasthāpanenā''dyadoṣābhāvāt| athavā` anvayād viśeṣaṇaṃ bhavati' iti bhāṣyeṇa dvitīyasyāpi samādhānāt| niṣkīrṇāsvapi sumanaḥsvāmodānvayamātreṇa yathā campakapuṭavyāvahārastathā sadṛśārthānvayamātreṇa rājādipadārthaḥ puruṣe viśeṣaṇamiti vyavahāra iti tadartha iti jahatsvārthavṛttipakṣopapattiḥ| parantvevaṃ hi dṛśyate loke pūruṣo'yaṃ parakarmaṇi puvarttamānaḥsvaṃ karma jahāti, tadyathā-takṣā rājakarmaṇi pravarttamānaḥ svaṃ karma jahāti| evaṃ yuktaṃ yadrājā pūruṣārthe pravarttamānaḥ svamarthaṃ jahyādupaguścāpatyārthe varttemānaḥ svamarthaṃ jahyāditi bhāṣyādetatkalpe'pi pūrvapadārtha eva jahatsvārthatvaṃ labhyate iti|
anyatheti vṛttau vyapekṣārūpasāmartyābhyupagame ityarthaḥ| ityasyāpi rājñaḥ pūruṣo bhāryāyāścetyekatra dvayamiti viṣayatāśālibodhajanakasya samuccayaḥ| āpatteriti asmanmate tu padārthaikadeśatvādbhede'pi śakt‌yabhyupagamena (a) rājakīyabhinnatvenāgate viseṣaṇāntarāyogācca na seti bhāvaḥ| ajahatsvārthavṛttipakṣasya bhāṣyānusāreṇā'nyathaivopapādanād `vyapekṣāyāṃ sāmarthyameke' ityekīyamatāśritāmanusṛtyāha-ayamabhiprāya iti| nātirikteti klṛptapratyekavṛttyatiriktetyarthaḥ| kalpakābhāvāditi kalpakasyānyathānupapattirūpapramāṇasyābhāvādityarthaḥ|
nanu viśiṣṭārtānupapattireva tatkalpikā bhaviṣyatītyata āha-klṛptarājādīti tat‌kalpanasya-viśiṣṭaśaktikalpanasyetyarthaḥ| nanu syādevaṃ yadyetatpakṣe pa3tyekapadaśaktikalpanā, saiva netikva goravāvasaro'ta āha-klṛptaśaktiti vṛttiprākkāle klṛptāyāḥ pratyekapadaśaktistatparityāga ityarthaḥ| kālabhedenāpyekasyaiva śabdasyārtavattvānarthakatvayorviruddhatvāt, nalopādivyavasyā'nupapattyā ca vṛttidaśāyāṃ tatsvīkārasyaivocitatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
nanu pratyekaśaktayā tattatpadārthopasthitāvapi viśiṣṭe śaktayanaṅgīkārema tadanupasthityā kathaṃ tasya śābdaviṣayatetyata āha-tathāceti| ākāṅkṣeti| ādipadādyogyatā''diparigrahaḥ, rājapadāvyavahitapūruṣapadattvādirūpakāraṇasamavadhānādityarthaḥ| tathāca pratyekapadārtho'pasthitisahakṛtā''kāṅkṣā''dibhireva viśiṣṭabodasambhavena tat‌pūrvaṃ viśiṣṭopasthityapekṣā yasyai sā kalpyeteti bhāvaḥ| ayameveti ayamabhiprāya ityādinokta evetyarthaḥ| bhāṣyakārairityasya `parasparavyapekṣāṃ sāmarthyameke' ityādineti śeṣaḥ| uktāpattisuddharati-nacātreti| anvayāpattiriti yathāśrutābhiprāyeṇa sambandhilakṣaṇāyāmāpatterabhāvasya vakṣyamāṇatvāt|
prasaṅgāditi smṛtikālāvacchinnopakṣatānarhatā'vacchedakadharmavattvādityarthaḥ| vṛttibhedamiti vṛtteḥ-samāptādivṛtterbhedam bhedasaṃsargādirūpārthakṛtamityarthaḥ| taduktaṃ bhāṣye-"sāmarthyannāma bhedaḥ, saṃsargo sā| apara āha--bhedasaṃsargau vā sāmarthyam" iti| yadi vṛttau bhedasaṃsargau na syātāṃ tadā sāmartyameva na syāttadātmakatvātsāmarthyasya" ityatastadarthakatvaṃ tasyeti tad‌bhāvaḥ|  bheda iti saṃsargāvinābhāvityādanumīyamāna (saṃsargaḥ) tādātmyasambandāvaccinnapratiyogitākābhāva ityarthaḥ| tadevāha anyonyābhāva iti|
saṃsarga iti bhādāvinābhāvitvādanuṃmīyamānabhedaḥ-bhedamūlakaḥ saṃsarga ityarthaḥ|
vastutastu "bhedasaṃsargo vā sāmārthyam" iti bhāṣyasya rājādipadasamabhivyāhṛtapuruṣādipadena rājādiviśiṣṭapuruṣādirupaviśiṣṭārthasyaiva bodha ityabhiprāyaḥ, natu bhede pṛthak‌śāktibodhakaṃ tat, tādṛśabodhānanubhavāt, `rāmakṛṣṇau' ityādidva dve bhedasya vācyatāyā durupapādatvācceti  vyapekṣāvādasyeti avyavahitoktavidhayā varṇitasyeti śeṣaḥ| uktājahatsvārthapakṣasya tu nirddiṣṭatvameveti bhāvaḥ| evaṃ yuktīti uktavakṣyamāṇetyarthaḥ| bhāṣyeta "nānākārakānnighātayuṣmadasmadādeśapratiṣedhaḥ" ityādibhāṣyadūṣitatvādityarthaḥ|
vṛttimātreti mātrapadaṃ kṛtsnārthakam| ajahatsvāthalakṣaṇayā vṛttipañcakopasthāpakamityarthaḥ| vākyārthābhidhāna iti tathābhidhāne ityarthaḥ| bhāṣyādityasya ` samarthānāṃ padavacanam' ityādiḥ|
mūle paṅkajaśabdavaditi saptamayantādvatīḥ, upameye saptamīdarśanāt| paṅkajaśabde yathā'vayavaśaktayatiriktaśaktistadvatsamāse'pītyarthaḥ| naca paṅkajapade rūḍhisvīkāre mānābhāvaḥ, samudāyāt padmatvaviśiṣṭapratītereva mānatvāt, padānniyatopasthite rūḍhayekasādyatvāt paṅkajapadād yogena kumudasyāpi bodaprasaṅgācca| rūḍhisvīkārepyudbhitpadena vṛbhāderiva yogena paṅkajapadena kumudamapi bodhyate iti smṛtaṃ padmameva karttṛvyaktiśaktaḍapratyayena paṅkajanikarttṛtayā bodhyate| "bādhakaṃ vinā' iti|
tathāca padme tātparyyasattvena tadeva yogenāpi pratīyate, na kumudama| prathamakalpastu rūḍhijñānasya grāhyābhāvānavagāhitvāt, janakajñānavighaṭakatvāccha pratibandhakatvāsambhavānna kṣodakṣamaḥ, siddheranāyatyā `maṇimantrādi' nyāyena pratibandhakatvābhyupagame'pyanyatra tathākalpena mānābhāvāt| itthañca padmānubavasāmagrīsattvāt tasyaiva bodhaḥ, na kumudasya| satyāṃ hi sāmāgrayāṃ palānutpāde pratibandhakatvāpekṣaṇāditi tārkiṃkāḥ| tad‌dṛṣṭāntena vṛttisāmānye samudāye saktirāvasyakīti kathane pūrvoktārtha eva paryyavasyatīti|
nanvajahatsvārthavṛttikalpe viśiṣṭaśakte sambanandhāṃśe virāmasyoktatvādvākye'pi sambandhāṃśe eva taiḥ svīkārāt ko vṛtti-vākyayorvaiyākaramanaye viśeṣa iti cedatrāhuḥ-tatra hi phalabalāt padārthopasthitikāle niyamataḥ sambandhāṃśa ud‌bodha kāsamavadhānakalpanena pṛthagupasthitayoḥ padārthayoḥ saṃsargabodhaḥ| vṛttau padārthopasthitisamakālameva niyamataḥ saṃsargāśeṃ ud‌bodhakasamavadhānakalpanād viśiṣṭārthopasthitiriti vailakṣaṇyopapatteḥ| ata eva prāptodakādau viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇa bhāvavaiparītye tu na bodhaḥ, tatrāprayujyamānadvitīyārthaḥ sambandhaścobhayaṃ samudāyaśaktisādhyam, ananyalabhyatvāt, klṛptaśaktayaiva rājādipadādrājāderupasthitisambhavena tatra samudāyaśaktikalpane gauravācca| `nāmārthayorabhedānvayaḥ' ityādivyupattayaśca pṛthagupasthitanāmārthaviṣayā iti|
jahatsvārthavṛttipakṣe paṅkajaśabdasya pūrvoktarītyā dṛṣṭāntatvāsambhavenā'nyathā mūlamavatārayati-viśiṣṭaśaktayasvīkartṝṇāmiti| sādhakamiti viśiṣṭaśaktisādhakamevetyartha-. vyapekṣāvādinaye `pājapuruṣaḥ' ityādau pratyekapadavṛttyā padārthopastitāvākāṅkṣādivaśāt viśiṣṭārthabodhavat paṅkajapadādapi tathaiva viśiṣṭārthopastityā rūḍherucchedāpattiḥ, na ca sā taveṣṭetyāha-paṅkajanikartturapīti tena rūpeṇa padmasyāpītyarthaḥ| yogādeveti avayavasakterevetyarthaḥ| tatrāpi paṅkajādipade'pi| tatra tatsvīkāre tu kimaparāddhaṃ samāse viśiṣṭaśaktayeti bhāvaḥ| sā samudāyaśaktiḥ|
tatkalpanasya-rūḍhikalpanasyetyarthaḥ| tathopasthitiriti svāmitvādinā svamyādyupasthitirityarthaḥ| suvaceti padmatvādinopasthitirapi lakṣaṇayeti vaktuṃ śakyamityarthaḥ| rathakārādhikaraṇavirodhamapi tatrāha-evamiti|
lakṣaṇāmiti nirūñalakṣaṇāmityarthaḥ, tasyāḥ śaktisamākakṣatvāditi bhāvaḥ| yogeneti pratyekapadavṛttyupasthāpyarathakarttṛtvenetyarthaḥ| upapattāviti śāstrasaṅkocamantareṇāpyanuṣṭhānanirvāha ityarthaḥ| tatkalpanāmityādi rūḍhikalpanāmityarthaḥ| (pratilomajātaye) rūḍhiṃ tāṃ kalpayitveti yāvat| jātiviśeṣasyeti
rathakārastu māhiṣyāt karaṇyāṃ yasya sambhavaḥ|
iti smṛtisaṅketitajātiviśiṣṭasyetyarthaḥ| ayuktamiti, vinaiva śāstrasaṅkocaṃ vidheścaritārthyāditi bhāvaḥ|
nanu neyaṃ yuktirviśiṣṭaśaktisādhikā, pratibanderanuttaratvāt, taduddhārasya svayameva varṇayiṣyamāṇatvācca| kiñca paṅkajapade lakṣaṇā'pi na suvacā| tathāhi-sā kiṃ paṅkajapade,uta tadavayavaḍapratyaye| na tāvadādyaḥ, samudāye śaktyabhāvena tad‌ghaṭitalakṣaṇāyā evāsambhavāt| kiñca jahatsvārthāyāstasyāstatrāsambhava eva, avayavārthavirodhinyā rūḍherabhāvāt|
jahatsvārthā tu tatraiva yatra rūḍhirvirodhini|
ityabhiyuktokteḥ| yathā `maṇḍapaṃ bhojaya' ityatra maṇḍapapadasya gṛhaviśeṣe rūḍhiryogavirodhini, rūḍhayarthagrahe maṇ‍ḍapānakarttari rūpayogārthasya bādhāttatra "rūḍheryogāpahāratā" iti pravādādeva śaktyā maṇḍapānakarturbodhāsambhavānmaṇḍapapadasya maṇḍapānarttari lakṣaṇā| tathāca tatra rūḍhiyarthagrahasambandhitāvacchedakatayā gṛhītacatratvādinaiva tad‌bodho na maṇḍapānakarttṛtvena| naca tathā prakṛte, padmatvena lakṣaṇāyāmavayavārthasyābhāvenājahatsvārthāyā apyasambhavācca| jahatsvārthā tvāvavārthapratītyanupapattyā na vaktuṃ śakyā| nāntyaḥ, prakṛtyarthasyānanvayāpatteḥ, tadarthe pratyayasyāsādhutāpatteśceti lakṣaṇānavasarādanāyattyā tatra vuśiṣṭaśaktyamyupagamaḥ| "rājapuruṣaḥ" ityādau tu pratyekapadavṛttyā'pi viśiṣṭārthabodhanirvāhe samudāyaśaktikalpanaṃ tatrānucitameva| nāpi rathakārādhikaraṇavirodhaḥ, yaugikāryarathakaraṇanimitte varṣābhidhānasya saṅkarasya viśiṣṭādhānāpekṣayā lavavutve'pi ṛbhūṇāṃ tvā devānāmiti mantravarṇādṛbhusaudhanvanparyāyapratilomajātirathakārasyaiva tadadhikārāvagatyā lāghavānādareṇa rūḍhārthe tatra viśiṣṭādhānakalpanāt|
nacāpūrvavidyādhyayanakalpanāgauravam, phalamukhatvāt| prakṛte rājapūruṣādisamudāye śaktiprāhakaṃ na tādṛśaṃ kosādi, yena tad‌balādavayavārthaḥ parityajyetetyaparitoṣānmūlamavatārayati-sādhakāntaramiti liṅgoti `kukkuṭāṇḍam' ityādau kukkuṭapadārthe strītvasyāpratīteriti bhāvaḥ saṅkhayeti rājño rājñāṃ vā puruṣa iti vākye uphapasarjanapadāni saṅkhayāviśeṣayuktaṃ svārthamabhidadhati samāse tvantarbhūtasvārthaṃ pradhānārthamabhidadhatīti bhāṣyāttatra saṅkhayāviśeṣasyāpratīteriti bhāvaḥ| saṅkhayādītyādipadāt "ime tarhyekārthimāvakṛtā viśeṣaḥ, saṅkhayāviśeṣo vyaktābhidhānamuvapasarjjanaviśeṣaṇāñ cāyogo" iti bhāṣyoktasya `rājño rāvaśvapuruṣāḥ' devadattayajñadattaviṣṇumitrāṇāṃ gauḥ" ityādau cāyogasya ca saṅgrahaḥ| vacanairiti "saviśeṣaṇānāṃ vṛttirna" ityādibhirityarthaḥ| uktārthameva prakaṭayati-
ayambhāva iti| nyāyasiddhimeva sūtramiti iha vyapekṣāyāṃ samāso na bhavatyekārthimāve vākyaṃ na bhavati, vibhinnaviṣayatvena vādhyavādhakabhāvāsambhavāditi nyāyāsiddhavibhāṣāvacanaṃ nāpūrvamityarthaḥ| taduktaṃ bhāṣye-"nasaṃjñāyā bhāvābhāvāviṣyete" iti| ekārthānāṃ vikalpanānnārtho vikalpeneti tadbhāvaḥ|
anvayāsambhavāditi vṛttau hyupasarjjanapadena pradhānārthābhidhāyinā bhāvyam, svasya viśeṣaṇā'kāṅkṣāyāñca prādhānyamiti kathamekadā prādhānyamekārthobhāvaścāsya, yugapad‌guṇapradhānabhāvavirodhāditi nyāyasiddhamevetyarthaḥ| ata eveti vyapekṣāvādasyā'pātaramaṇīyatvādevetyarthaḥ| dūṣaṇāmapyuktamiti asya nānākārakānnighātayuṣmadādeśapratiṣedhaḥ| `ayaṃ ḍhaṇḍo harānena' ityādineti śeṣaḥ kauyaṭoktaṃ doṣasaṅghātamāha-tathetyādi| iti duṣaṇāntaramiti ityākārakaṃ duṣaṇāntaramityar‌thaḥ| pūrvoktānāmiti `niṣkauśānbiḥ' ityādau krāntādyarthavācakānāṃ lopavacanamityādikānāmityarthaḥ| etad‌dūṣaṇoddhārasya vakṣyamāṇatvādāha-dūṣaṇāntaramiti|
vyutpattibhaṅgamāviṣkarttu māha-ayamāśaya iti| lakṣeṇeti vākye iti śeṣaḥ|
`citraguḥ' ityādau vākyasya gosvāmilakṣakatve'bhāṣsitabodhalābhasammavādāha-prāptodako grāma iti| lakṣaṇāyāmapīti idañca mīmāsakamatena| svavādhyasambandhasyaiva lairlakṣaṇātvā'bhyupagamāt| bodhottaram ityasyānupapattiprātasandhāna satāta śeṣaḥ|  lakṣaṇāyāmati ityapinā tatra lakṣaṇāyā asambhavaṃ sūcayāta| arthalābhāditi prāptakartrābhannodakasambandhino lābha'pyanubhūyamānoktārthālābhādityarthaḥ| tarhi samānādhikaraṇeti sāmānādhikaraṇyañcātra samānābibhaktakatvam| anyathā karmalābhāṇakaktāntārthodakapadārthayorabhedānvayānabhyupagame ityarthaḥ| baṅgāpatteriti tādṛśavyutpattaratraiva vyabhicārāditi bhāvaḥ| nanu tad‌vyutpattau samānādhikaraṇasyaikārtābhidhāyitvasyaiva niveśena rnedaṃ tad‌āvaṣayā'ta āha-prāpterati| anvayāsambhavāditi nāmārthadhātvarthayoḥ sākṣādbhedenānvayasyāvyutparannatvāt' iti bhāvaḥ| anyathā devadatta iti anyathā-uktavyatpattyanabhyupagama ityarthaḥ|
grāmo lakṣyata iti| vākyeneti śeṣaḥ| uktabodhasya sambhavaduktikatve kathañcillakṣaṇā'pi kalyeta, tadeva nāstītyāha-prāpterdhātvarthatayeti "prakṛtipratyayayoḥ" iti nyāyasya saṅkhayādau vyabhicaritatvaṃ sambhāvyāha-prāptapadeiti| nāmārthatveneti| nāmajanyapratītiviśeṣyasyaiva nāmārthatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
abhedānvayāpatteśceti samānābhaktikanāmārthayoriti bhāvaḥ|draṣṭavyamiti| bhavanmate `vahanakarmābhinno rathaḥ' iti bodhottaraṃ sambandhini lakṣaṇāyāmapi `rathakarmakavahanakarttada'naḍ‌vān' itīpsitārthasyālābha eva| karmārthekattapratyayasya karttāre lakṣaṇāyāṃ tu dhātvarthavahane rathasya karmatayā bhedasambandenānvayo durlabha eva, uktavyutpattivirodhāditi pūrvoktadiśā'vaseyamityartha-| atra hīti bodhābhyupagamādityenanānvayi| atra `ūḍharathaḥ' ityādisamse viśiṣ‌ṭaśattayabhyupagame tu na kasyā api vyutpattervirodha ityāha-atirikteti ṣaṣṭhayarthabahuvrīhiparyudāse bījaṃ pradaraśayati-ayambhāva iti| pavanā śaktimiti| samudāyaśaktimityarthaḥ| naca tatreti tādṛśasamudāyakhṛkaṃpūrvottarapadayorityarthaḥ| citrasvāmīti `samānādhikaraṇanāmārthayoḥ' iti vyutpatteriti bhāvaḥ| nanu gopadasya gomati lakṣaṇā| gavi citrasyabhedānvaye citrābhinnagosvāmītadi bodho nānupapanno'ta āha-citrādimātrasyeti ādinā gavādiparigrahaḥ| prāthamyāt pūrvapade lakṣaṇetyāśyena citrapadopādānam|
lakṣyaikadeśatveneti lakṣyagosvāmirūpapadārthaikadeśatvenetyarthaḥ| anvayāyogāditi `ekapadārthaprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣyatāsambandhena śābdabuddhiṃ prati viśeṣyatāsambandenāparapadajanyopasthiterhetutā' ityarthaparyyavasitāyāḥ "padārthaḥ padārthenānveti" ityādivyutpatterviṃrodhāduktānvayāsambhavāditi bhāvaḥ| kasyacinmatamāsaṅkaya nirācaṣṭe-naceti sākṣāditi godvāraka ityarthaḥ| tathāca ṣaṣṭhayarthabahuvrīho na vyutpattyantarakalpanamiti bhāvaḥ| padadvaya iti vinigamakābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ naiyāyiketi asya prācīnetyādiḥ| ekadeśānvayapakṣe hi tairittthamuktam bodhāvṛttīti bodhyamānasya svamīno dvedhā bhānāpatterityarthaḥ|
vinigamanāviraheti citrapadaṃ tātparyyagrāhakamuta gopadamitadyekatarapakṣapātiyuktivirahatādavasthyenetyarthaḥ| asambhavāditi ekatarapade lakṣaṇāyā asambhavādityarthaḥ| padadavye lakṣaṇāyāntu uktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ| pratyayārthānvayānupapattireva uttarapade lakṣaṇāyāṃ vinigamikā bhaviṣyatītyāśaṅkaya nirāvaṣṭe-naceti evaṃ hīti uttarapadasyaivānyapadārthalakṣakatve ityarthaḥ| bahuvrīhyasambhave hetumāha-anekamiti tadavidhānāditi bahuvrīhividhaānādityarthaḥ|
ayambhāvaḥ| vṛtterviśiṣṭārthakatvaṃ na śāstraikagamyam| "acatura" ityādāvarthānādesanāt| kintu laukikavyavahāragamyam| "svabhāvata eva teṣāṃ śabdānāmeṣvartheṣvabhinīviṣṭānāṃ nimittatvenānvākhyānaṃ kriyate" iti samarthasūtrabhāṣyāt| "anekamanyapadārthe"  "cārthe dvandvaḥ"  ityādīnāmanekaprathamāntamanyārthapratipādakaṃ bahubrīhisaṃjñakaṃ bhavati, cārthe varttate yaḥ prathamāntasamudāyaḥ sa dvandva saṃjñako bhavatīti krameṇa lokasiddhārthā'nuvādena bahubrīhyadisaṃjñāvidhāyakatvamātram, arthasyāpi vidhāne vākyabhedāpatteḥ| svasamānārthakavākyānivṛttaye vibhāṣā'dhikāro'pi vidheyaḥ syāt| evañca subantasamudāyasyānyārthapratipādakatve kathaṃ bahubrīhi syāditi| nanvekapadasyānyārthapratipādakatve tātparyyagrāhakatayā'parapadasyapyupayogitvena samudāyasya viśiṣṭārthapratipādakatvamivikalamata āha-kañcaivamiti samāsasthacaramapadasyaiva lakṣakatve satītyarthaḥ| iṣṭāpattāvāha-evaṃ satīti caramavarṇasya vācakatve satītyarthaḥ atrāpi prāptodakādāvapītyarthaḥ āpattiriti āpādyavyatirekanirṇayakālikā''pādyavyāpyā''pādakavattājñānasattvāditi bhāvaḥ|
nanūdakapadamātraśravaṇānna lakṣyārthabodhaḥ, tadānīṃ lakṣaṇākalpikāyāstātparyyagrāhakarpūvapadopasthiterabhāvādata āha-ānyatreti bhūṣaṇe ityarthaḥ| vistara iti uttarapadalakṣyārthe prakṛtyarthatvābhāvena tatra pratyayārthānanvayā'pattirityādi tatroktam| vyutpattīti vyutpattityāgaḥ prakṛte pratijñāhānireva| vyapekṣāvādina iti samarthasūtre "parasparānvayayogyatvarūpasāmarthyameva vyapekṣā" itivādina ityarthaḥ| na samāse śaktiriti| samāse-rājapūruṣadisamudāye, viśiṣṭaśaktirnetyarthaḥ| samāsasaṃjñāprayojakatvaṃ klṛptapratyekapadavṛttayatarikte nāstīti yāvat| saṃsṛṣṭārtha samarthamiti bhāṣyakārokteḥ, saṃsargasya pṛthagupasthitinibandhanatvāt parasparavyapekṣaiva sāmarthyam| "isusoḥ sāmarthye"  ityatra samartapadasya tādṛśārthakatvasya sarvairevāṅgīkārāt, parāṅgavadbhāvānurodhāccha| anyathā samāsādāvekārthobhāva; parāṅgavadbhāvādau vyapekṣeti vākyabhedāpatteḥ| tatra vyapekṣā'nādare tu "ṛtena mitrāvaruṇāvṛtāvṛdhāvṛtaspṛśau" ityādāvṛtenetyādeḥ parāṅgavdbhāve sati "āmantritasya ca"  ityādyudāttatā''pattiḥ|
naca "tannimittagrahaṇaṃ karttavyam" iti vārtikenā'mantritārtasya yannimittaṃ tad‌vācakameva parāṅgavadityarthakena niyamitatvāt parāṅgavadbhāvāprasaktiriti vācyam, samarthaparibhāṣāmāśritya tatpratyākhyānasyaivaṃ nyāyyatvāt| "ṛtena mitrāvaruṇo" ityetayoḥ samabhivyāhṛtakriyānvayitvena parasparamasāmarthyāt "mitrāvaruṇāvṛtaspṛśo
etayostu pārṣṭhikānvayabodhasattvādastayeva sāmārthayam, spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ vedabhāṣye| ata evānanatarapāṭho'pi caritārthaḥ| `pūtro rājñaḥ puruṣo devadattasya' ityādau niruktavyapekṣārūpasāmarthyābhāvādeva samāsāprasakteḥ|
samarthasūtrārthastu padasambandī samāsādividhiḥ sākṣātparamparayā vā svaprayojyaviṣayatānirūpivaviṣayatāprayojakatvarūpavyapekṣā'paranāmakasāmarthyavatpadā'śrito bodhya iti bahuvrīhau citra'diviṣayatāyāḥ sākṣātpratyojakatvābhāvātparamparayeti|
kiñca vṛttitvāvacchedanaikārthobhāvavādimate svavayavārthātiriktārthābhidhāyakatvarūpavṛttilakṣaṇasya karmadhāraye'vyāptiḥ, tatrāvayavārthātiriktārthavattvābhāvāt| vṛttitvasāmānādikaraṇyena tu rathantarā'diśuddharūḍhe paṅkajādiyogarūḍhe ca kośā'disiddhāyāḥ samudāyaśakterasmābirapyabhyupagamena siddhasādhanāt| śuddhayogikacitragvādisamudāye śaktayabhyupagamastu kośā''dyanuktatvātsamāsaghaṭakapratyekapadavṛttyaivopapatteśca heya iti tadabhisandhiḥ| śaktikalpanānupapattyabhāvaṃ tatprayojanatayā darśayati-rājapūruṣa ityādāviti atra rājapūruṣapadamaśrūyamāṇavibhaktipūrvapadakaṣaṣṭhīsamāsaparam, tena `dāsyāḥ putraḥ' ityādyaluk‌samāse na doṣaḥ|
naca sambandha eva lakṣyo'stvevañca vyāsasamāsayoḥ samānākārabodhakatvapravādo'pyupapatsyate| rājā''disambandharūpalakṣyārthasyottarapadārthe āśrayatayā'nvayenaivopapattau sambandini lakṣaṇā'bhyupagame prayojanābhāvaśceti vācyam, `rājā pūruṣaḥ' ityādāvapi rājādipadārthasya svasvāmibhāvasambandhena śābdabuddhiṃ prati viśeṣyatāsambandena pratyayanipātānyatarajanyopastiterhetutvāsyāvaśyakatayā prakṛte rājasambandarūpanāmārthasya pratyayanipātānyatarānupasthite pūruṣādāvanvayāsambhavāt nipātātiriktatvaviśeṣaṇāte `candra iva mukham' ityādāvivapadārthasādṛśyasya nāmārthasya pratyayajanyāpasthityaviṣete mukhe nipātātiriktanāmārthacandrasya tathopasthityaviṣaye sādṛśye'pi na kṣatiḥ| abhedātiriktasambandāvacchinnatvasya prakāratāyāṃ niveśāccha `rājā puruṣaḥ' ityādāvabhedānvaye'pi na sā|
naca samasyamānātiriktatvenāpi nāmno viśeṣaṇīyatvānna prakṛte'nupapattiriti vācyam, gauravāt, kvacidagatyā saṅkoce'pi sarvatra saṅkocasayānyāyyatvācca, prakṛte sambandhilakṣaṇā'bhyupagamarūpagatisattvāta, nipātānāṃ dyotakatvanaye ghaṭādipadānāṃ ghaṭādipratiyokikalākṣiṇakatayaivopapattau vyatpattau nipātātiriktatvasyāpraveśācceti bhāvaḥ|
ata eveti pūrvapadasya sambandhilākṣaṇikatvādevetyarthaḥ| (na)viśeṣaṇānvaya iti "padarthaḥ padārthenānveti" iti vyutpatteriti bhāvaḥ| nanu `gabhīrāyāṃ nadyāṃ ghoṣaḥ' ityādau nadīpadalakṣyanadīdīrārthaikadeśanadyādau gabhīrādyanvayavadatrāpyekadeśānvayo durvāra iti cenna, dṛṣṭāntāsiddheḥ| tatrāpyetanmate nadīpadasya gabhīranadīdīralakṣakatāyā gabhīrapadasya tātparyyagrāhakatāyāśca vakṣyamāṇatayā adoṣāt tādṛśavyutpatteḥ sasambandhivyatiriktaviṣayatayā caitrasya naptetyādāvekadeśānvaye'pi kṣativirahāditi| uktārthatayeti tatra nirādīnaṃ dyotakatayā kauśāmbyādipadasyaiva kauśāmbyavadhikaniṣkramaṇakartrādyarthe lakṣaṇā'bhyupagamenaivepsitārthabodhopapattau krāntādiśabdanāmupādānāsambhavādityarthaḥ| āvaśyakatvamiti tena pārāyaṇādāvadṛśṭārthakatayā spaṣṭhapratipattyarthakatayā vā tadupayoge'pi na kṣatiriti bhāvaḥ|
pratibandīti paṅkajādipade padmatvādiviśiṣṭaśaktayabhyupagamarūpetyarthaḥ| avayavaśaktimajānato'pi ityāpinā dṛṣṭanta-dārṣṭantikayārvaiṣamyaṃ sūcyate| śaktyagrahe avayavaśaktigrahābhāve| tebhyaḥ paṅkajādipadaghaṭakāvayavebhyaḥ| viśiṣṭarthapratyaya iti padmatvādirūpeṇa padmādiboda ityarthaḥ| lakṣaṇāyāḥ śaktighaṭitatvāt, kintu samudāyaśaktyupastite tasminnavayavaśaktyupasthāpitapaṅkajanikarturabhedānvayopagama eveti bhāvaḥ| ata eveti| tatpadīyalakṣaṇagrahe tatpadaśaktijñānasya hetutvādevetyarthaḥ uttarapadalakṣaṇāyāṃ vinigamakamāha-yaugikatveneti| vilambitatvāt ityanena śīghropasthitikatvamuttarapadalakṣaṇāyāṃ vinigamakamiti sūcitam|
nanu vyutpattipakṣe yaugikatvamudakādipadānāmapi samamata āha-pratyayānāmiti| yathāśrutaprakṛtyarthatvasyodakādau bādhāt prakṛtyarthapadamanyathā vyācaṣṭe-sannihiteti `daṇḍinamānaya' ityādau vyavahitapadārthe-daṇḍādau karmatvādyanvayavāraṇāya sannihiteti| tathāca "aṇurapi viśeṣo|ṭadhyavasāyakaraḥ" iti nyāyenottarapade eva lakṣaṇeti na bodhā'vṛttirūpadūṣaṇamapīti bhāvaḥ| pūrvaktadūṣaṇāntaramuddharati-
ghaṭādipade ceti| kalpyamānā ityuktayā aklṛptatvaṃ sūcayati-viśiṣṭasyaiveti vyavahārādinā tatraiva tannidvārāṇādāta bhāvaḥ| asattvāt ityasya yena tatrāpi vinigamakagaveṣaṇā syāditi śeṣaḥ| uttarapada iti prāptādakādighaṭakodakādipade ityarthaḥ| sā lakṣaṇā| viśeṣa iti ghaṭādipadāt samāsasya lakṣaṇāyāṃ vaiṣamyamityarthaḥ|
atredaṃ bodhyam-uktayuktayopakumbhamityādāvuttarapadasyaiva samīpādyarthe lakṣaṇā, pūrvapadaṃ tātparyyagrāhakam| tatpuruṣe'pi kvacidarddhapippaṃlītyādyekadeśini caramapadasyaiva sā pippalyarddādyarthe, itarad dyotakam| nīloppalamityādikarmaghāraye tu na kvā'pi sā, pratyekapadaśaktyupasthitayorabedānvayasaulabhyāt| bahuvrīhau tūttarapada eva seti sāra eva vyaktam| pāṇipādamityādidvandve sāhatyāpannapratyekapadārthayāruttarapadasyaiva sā| ata eva dvivacanādyarthadvitvādyanvayastatra| samāhāradvandve sāhityaṃ viśeṣyam, atra tu viśeṣaṇamiti bhedaḥ|
yadvā samāhāradvandve'pi sāhityāpratīterna ta6 lakṣaṇā, kintu pratyekapadaśaktyupasthāpyayoreva sākṣād vādanakarmatvānvayaḥ| `dvandvasca prāṇi'  itiprakaraṇātidiṣṭaikatvasamāsasyaiva mukhyaṃ samāhārapadavyapadesyatvam| itaratra tu tadyavahāra aupacārikaḥ| nāta itaretarayoge tatprasaṅgaḥ| evamitaretarayoge'pi na lakṣaṇā, ekasmṛtyārūḍhapadadvayāt pratyekaśaktyupasthitārthayordvitvānvayasambhavāt, ekaktriyā'nvayitvarūpasya ekabuddhayavacchinnatvarūpasya vā sāhityasya paścādevāvagameva pūrvaṃ tatra lakṣaṇāgrahāsambhavācca| `cārthe dvandvaḥ' ityasya cārthānvayayogya ityarthātanna tad‌virodho'pi|
yuktatrcaitat, dvandvatvāvacchinnasya pradhānībhūtārthadvayapratipādakatve eva "dvandvaḥ sāmāsikasya ca" (gītā) ityanena samāsasamūhamadhye dvandvasya vibhūtitayā parigaṇanasya bhagavatkṛtasya saṅgateḥ| viśiṣṭaśaktyā, lakṣaṇāyā vā tasya pradhānībhūtasamāhārapratipādakatve tu samāsāntarād dvandvasyāviśeṣāttadasaṅgatiḥ spaṣṭaiva|
yattu bhāratabhāvadīpe-samamekatrā''sanaṃ samāso viduṣāṃ guruśiṣyāṇāṃ vā māntrārthe kathārthaṃ vaikatrāvasthānam| tatra viditamarthajāta sāmāsikam, cāturarthikaṣṭhak tasya "ṭhasyekaḥ"  itīkādeśaḥ, "yasyeti ca'  ityalopaḥ| tasya madhye dvandvaḥ-rahasyo'ham "dvandvaṃ rahasya"  iti sūtre dvandvaśabdasya rahasyavācitvaṃ śāvdikaprasiddhamiti vyākhyātam|
tattu viditārthasya caturthe caturarthyāñca adarśanādrathantarādhikaraṇānyāyena saṅketitasamāsaśabdāt pāribhāṣikasamāsarūpārthasyaivopasthiterucitatvena prāptasya sāmūhikaṭhakaḥ samāsaśabdāttadanabhidhānāt| "dvandvaṃ rahasya"  ityanena rahasye vyutpāditadvandvasya nityapuṃsakatvena pum̐lliṅgasya tadvācitvāsambhavāt| samāsavācinaśca tasya pūm̐lliṅgatāyāḥ "cārthe dvandvaḥ"  ityādau dṛsṭatvāt pāribhāṣikatvāccha tena samāsasyaiva grahaṇasyocitatvācca cintyam|
naca sāhityābhāne'nyatarasya kriyā'nvayavivakṣāyām `paśya dhavaṃ khadiraṃ chindhi' itivākyavat `paśya dhavakhadirau chindhi' iti prayogāpattiriti vācyam, pratyayārthadvitvāvacchinnadharmikasyaiva padārthāntarānvayavodhasyānyatra darśanenātrāpi tadavacchedenānvayasyaivocitatvāt|
naca dvitvānvayayogyatāvacchedakasāhityabhānamantarema dvitvānvaya eva durlabha iti vācyam, yogyatā'vacchedakopasthiteranapekṣaṇāt, upasthireryogya eva tadanvayāt| ata eva `ghaṭena jalamānaya' ityatra chidretarasyaiva yogyatābalenānvayaḥ, natu chidretaratvaprakārakaḥ śābdabodhaḥ, tatra prakṛtyarthatāvacchedakasyaiva dvitvānvayayogyatāvacchedakatve'pi kṣativirahācca| vivecitaṃ cedamadhikamanyatra|
naca `kṣaumavasanāvagnimādadhīyātām' ityatra samuccayasāhityalābho na syāditi vācyam, "yat karttavyaṃ tadanayā saha" iti vacanāntarāttatsiddheḥ| yadyapi dvandve parasparānvayitvarūpavyapekṣā durghaṭā, tathāpi "cārthe dvandvaḥ"  iti vidhisāmarthyādeva tatra samāsaḥ, datarthastūktaḥ, samuccayānvācayayostvanabhidhānādeva na dvandva iti spaṣṭaṃ bhāṣye| yattvekasyāṃ kriyāyāmekarūpeṇānvayitvameva sāmarthyamiti, tanna, kvacidetasya kvacit pūrvāktasya sāmarthyasyaśrayaṇe samarthasūtre vākyabhedāpatteḥ, ekakriyānvayitvasya tato'nanubhavācceti pūrvoktāpattimiṣṭatvenāpi pariharati-svīkṛtamiti|
caramavarṇāsyaiveti pūrvapūrvavarṇānubhavajanyodubuddhasaṃskārasahitāntimavarṇasyetyarthaḥ| nātaḥ kevalacaramavarṇārthabodhāpattiḥ| adhikamagre vakṣyate| "vyapekṣāyāṃ sāmarthye samāsa eko'saṅ‌gṛhīto bhavati" iti bhāṣyāśayaṃ prakāśayannaiyāyikamataṃ dūṣayati-atrocyate iti| saṃjñādītyādinā padatvādiparigrahaḥ|
bhāṣyasiddhāyā iti "samāsagrahaṇamarthavatsamudāyānāṃ niyamārtham" iti bhāṣyasiddāyā ityarthaḥ|
spaṣṭamiti vākyasyāpyarthavattvena prātipadikatvaprasaktau tad‌vyāvṛttaye vacanārambhamāśaṅkaya niyamārthena samāsagrahaṇena samāhitam| samāse śaktyanabhyupagame tu tasya vṛttyārthabodhakatvarūpārthavattvābhāvenāprāptasaṃjñāviṣaye tasyā''vaśyakatayā viyamārthatvavyāghātaḥ spaṣṭa eva| tasmādetadbhāṣyamapi samāse viśiṣṭaśaktaumānamiti tatroktatvādityarthaḥ|
naca arthavatpadasāmarthyanārthavācakapadaghaṭite'pi prātipadikasaṃjñāsiddhiranyatā dhātvādiparyyudāsenaivā'rthavattve labdhe tadupādānavaiyarthyaṃ spaṣṭameva| daśadāḍimānītyādyanarthakasamudāyasya tu niyamena vāraṇīyatvāditi vācyam, arthavad‌grahaṇasyottarārthatāyāḥ siddhāntasiddhatve tatptāmarthyābhāvāt, "arthavad‌grahaṇaṃ saṃjñivyapadeśārtham" iti bhāṣyeṇārthavattvena sādṛśyabodhanārthaṃ tadupayogadaśanācceti viśiṣṭaśaktyabhyupagamamantareṇa samāse prātipadikasaṃjñādaubhikṣyameveti bhāvaḥ|
nanu citragurityādau viśiṣṭe śaktyasvīkāre'pi nārthavattvanibandhaprātipadikasaṃjñā'nupapattiḥ| tathāhi-na śakyasambandho lakṣaṇā, `gabhīrāyāṃ nadyāṃ ghoṣaḥ' ityādāvasambhavāt| tatra tāvanna gabhīrapadaṃ tīralakṣakam, nadyāmityasyānanvayāpatteḥ| nahi tīraṃ nadī, ata eva nīdīpadamapi na tathā, gabhīrapadārthasyānanvayāpatteḥ| naca padadvaye pratyekaṃ sā, viśiṣṭanadītīrābhānaprasaṅgāt| naca nadīpadenaiva gabhīranadītīraṃ lakṣyate, gabhīrādipadaṃ tātpartyagrāhakamiti vācyam, gabhīrapadasya tātparyyagrāhakatvamuta nadīpadasyeti vinigamanāvirahāt|
naca nadīpadasya dravyavācakatayā sākṣātsambandha eva lakṣaṇāyāṃ vinigamaka iti vācyam, gabhīrapadasyāpi nityaṃ guṇivācakatayā tadarthasyāpi sākṣātsambandhāviśeṣāt| tasmāt padadvayalakṣaṇāyāṃ gauravāt samudāya eva sā'ṅgīkāryyā| ata evā'rthavādavākyānāṃ prāśastye lakṣaṇeti saṅgacchete| kintu svabodhyarūpaiva sā| asti ca gabhīrāyāṃ nadyāmityatra vākyajñāpyā gabhīrābhinnanadī tatsambandhastīre iti| evañca citragurityādisamudāyasya lakṣaṇayā citrābhinnagosvīmirūpārthabodakatvarūpārthavattvāt prātipadikasaṃjñā nānupapannetyata āha-samāsavākya iti vākya ityuktyā kvacittatsambhave'pi sarvatra rājapuruṣādisamāse tadasambhavaṃ sūcayati|
ayamāśayaḥ-svajñāpyasambandhasya na lakṣaṇātvam, apabhreśe'pitatprasaṅgāt| kiñca `svajñāpya' ityasya svaniṣṭhajñāpakatānirūpakajñāpyatāvadarthasambanda ityartho vācyaḥ| tatra jñāpakatvaṃ vṛtyā bodhakatvam, uta jñānajanakajñānaviṣayatvamātram| nādyaḥ, vākye lakṣaṇā'bhāvaprasaṅgāt| tadarthasya gambhīrābhinnasya vṛttyabodhyatvāt| nāntyaḥ, pratyekaṃ varṇānāmarthavattvāpattyā vibhaktyāpatteriti svaśakyasambandho lakṣaṇetyevāṅgīkaraṇīyam| tathāca samāse tadasambhavena prātipadikasaṃjñā'nupapattistadavasteti tadarthaṃ viśiṣṭaśaktisvīkāra āvaśyaka iti|
bhāṣyasiddha iti "tibekādeśe pratiṣedho'ntavattvāt" ityādestatrābhidhānāditi bhāvaḥ|
sūtryatāmiti "atipsamāsaśca prātipadikam" ityeva sūtrayatāmityarthaḥ| tathāca tiṅsubantabhinnaṃ yattatprātipadikamityarthena samāsasya prātipadikatvalābhādatiricyamānaṃ samāsagrahaṇaṃ niyamārthamastviratyarthaḥ| pratyekaṃ varṇeṣviti varṇānāmānarthakyapakṣe prātipadikasaṃjñāyāṃ prakāśanīyārthābhāvādaviśeṣeṇa prativarṇe vibhaktisambhavādavyayādiveti bhāvaḥ| arthavattveti| arthavad‌grahaṇasyāvaśyakatvenetyarthaḥ||
nacārthavatsamudāyaghaṭakavarṇānāmarthavattvapakṣasyāpi bhāṣye darśanāttad‌grahaṇe'pi vibhaktirdurvāreti vācyam, tatpakṣe'pi, "saṅghātasyaikārthyāt subobhāvo varṇāt" iti bhāṣyāttantreṇaikaiva vibhaktiḥ samudāyāvayavārthagatamekatvaṃ bodhayet| ekameva tadekatvaṃ taccaikena supā pratyāyitamiti bodhanīyābhāvāt vibhakteḥ pratyekavarṇādanutpatteriti bhāvaḥ|
nanu samāsavākyasyārthavattvā'bhāve'pi na prātipadikasaṃjñānupapattiḥ, "kṛttaddhita"  iti sūtreṇaiva tatsambhavāt| sambhavati vidhitve niyamakalpanāyā anyāyyatvāt| samāsagrahaṇameva ca samāsa ekārthobhāvābhāve mānam| vākyasya tu na pūrvasūtreṇa tatprasaktiḥ, artavattvābhāvāt| saṃsargasyāśakyave'pyākāṅkṣādivaśādeva tadbhānasya sūpapādatvāt| evaṃ prakṛtipratyayasamudāyasyāpyanarthakatvenāprāptasaṃjñāvidhānārthaṃ kṛttaddhitagrahaṇamiti samastasyaiva sūtrasya vidhāyakatvam| ādyasūtrasya tu avyutpannasādhuśabdasvarūpameva, natu bahupaṭurityādisamudāyo'pyuttarapadalākṣaṇikatādṛśasamudāyasyārtavattvābhāvāt| tṛdantānāṃ keṣāñcidarthavattve'pi dhātutvāt, evaṃ taddhitānāmapi `i' `iyān' ityādīnāṃ pratyayatvena tādṛśasamudāyasya tvanarthakatvena pūrvasūtrāviṣayatvena tatra tatra prātipadikatvavidhānārtha sūtrakārema dvitīyaṃ sūtraṃ praṇītam| ata evātrārthavaditi nānuvarttate, asambhavātprayojanābhāvācca| tatra kṛcchabdena pratyayaparibhāṣayā tadantagrahaṇaṃ saṃjñāvidhāvityasya prāyikatvāt, ḍatarādivatkelakṛtāmaprayogeṇa tadupādānavaiyarthyācca| yadyapyuktayuktyā taddhitapadasya taddhitāntaparataiva labhyate tathāpi lakṣyatāvacchedakākrāntabahupaṭ‌vādisaṅgrahāya tatsvāvyavahitottaratvasambandhena taddhitaviśiṣṭaprakṛtighaṭitasamudāyataddhitāntānyataraparatayā vyākhyeyam|
prakṛtitvañca pratyayavidhānāvadhitvam| `iḥ' `ityān' ityādau tu pratyayasyaiva tadantatvānna doṣaḥ| pacatakyādisamudāyāntargatataddhitaprakṛterniruktataddhitavaiśiṣṭayābhāvānna tatrātiprasaṅgaḥ, kṛd‌grahaṇaparibhāṣāyā anantaragrahaṇajñāpitāyā asārvatrikatvācca na mūlakenopadaṃśamityādāvatadiprasaktiḥ| evaṃ samāsagrahaṇenāpyapraptaprātipadikasaṃjñāvidhānādrājapuruṣa ityādisamudāyāt svādyutpattiḥ sulabheti tatra viśiṣṭaśaktyabhyupagamo niṣpramāṇakaḥ| vākyasya sā netyuktameva, tatra kosādinā śaktyaricchedāt|
yatra tu kosādikaṃ ricchedakaṃ tatra samāse paṅkajādau tāṃ nivārayāmaḥ| evañca vākyasyārthavattvābhāvena prātipadikasaṃjñā'prasaktestad‌vayāvṛttirūlābhāvena samāsagrahaṇasya niyamārthatvavarṇanamasāmpratam| bhāṣye tasya niyamaparatayā varṇanaṃ tu saṃsargasya śakyatvamityekadeśimatamanusṛtyaivaityarucerāha-kiñcaivamiti| evam-samāse viśiṣṭaśaktayasvīkāre| karmatvādyananvayāpattirityasya gavādipadārtha iti śeṣaḥ| tatra hetumāha-pratyayānāmiti tathāca prakṛtitvam pratyayavidhānāvadhitvam, na tu pratyayāvyavahitapūrvatvam, bahupaṭurityādau paṭ‌vādisabde'vyāpteḥ|
naca "tāveva suptiṅau yau tataḥ parau, saiva ca prakṛtirādyā" iti bhāṣyāt pratyayāvyavahitapūrvasyaiva prakṛtitvalābha iti vācyam, tadarthānavabodhāt| yau tato vidhānāvadheairasati viśaṣānuśāsane parau tāveva suptiṅāvityupalakṣaṇam, pratyayāntarasyāpi, yato vihitau saiva prakṛtiriti tadarthāt| ādyetyabhyāsābhiprāyeṇa| tathāca samāsottarapadasya vidhānāvadhitvarupaprakṛtitvābhāvena tadarthe na karmatvā'dyanvayaḥ sambhavati, uktavyutpattivirodhāditi bhāvaḥ|
pratyayānāmiti vyutpattirhi daṇḍanamānayetyādau daṇḍādau karmatvānvayavāraṇāya svīkriyate| tadarthaśca `prakṛtyarthaniṣṭhaviṣayatānirūpitaviṣayatāsambandhena śābdabuddhiṃ prati viśeṣyatāsambandhena pratyayajanyopasthititvena hetutā' iti tatra prakṛte sannihitatvenaiva niveśastāvataivoktasthale vyabhicāravāraṇāt, na tu prakṛtitvena, gauravāditi mataṃ dū,yitumupanyasyati-yattviti|
sannihiteti sannihitatvañcāvyavahitapūrvatvenānusandīyamānatvam| tasya ca citragvādipade gavādau sattvānnoktānupapattiriti bhāvaḥ| vyutpattirevetyevakāreṇa pūrvoktaprakṛtitvaghaṭivyutpattivyavacchedaḥ| upakumbhādisamāse'pītyarthaḥ|
ānuśāsaniketi sannihitatvaṃ nāvyavahitapūrvatvam| tattvenānusandhīyamānatvaṃ vā, dhavakhadirāvityādau dhavādipadārthe vibhāktyarthānanvayaprasaṅgat, kintu sannihitatvenānuśāsanabodhitatvam| tacca dhavādipadārthānamivopādipadārthānāmastītyāśayavānāha-tathāceti| samasyamānapadottaramiti samāsasyānekapadasamudāyātsakatayā samudāyasya pratyekānatiriktatayā samāsarūpaprātipadikād vihitavibhaktestattatpadasannihitatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
sambhavedevaṃ yadi samāsagrahaṇanibandhanā prātipadikasaṃjñā, kintu niyāmakatayā pūrvasūtreṇaiva sā vācyā| tathācoktaprakārāsambhava ityāśayenāha-arthavatsūtreṇeti tathāca śāstrabodhitasannihitatvamapi viśiṣṭasyaiva, na kebalapūrvapadasyetyuktasthale vyabhicāro duruddhara iti bhāvaḥ| prakṛtitvasya paryyāptyākhyavilakṣaṇasambandhena samudāyamātraviśrāntatve'pi kevalāśrayatayā tadttvasya pratyekadeśe'pi sambhavāduktavyutpattiraśarīre āśrayataiva niveśanīyetyāśayena śaṅkate-atheti| prakṛtitvāśraye-prakṛtitvāśrayārthe| asannihitaprakṛtyarthe dūṣaṇamudbhāvya sannihite'pi tadāha-aghaṭamiti pūrvapadārthaprādhānyamatretyāsayena dūṣaṇodbhāvanam| tattvañcā'gre vakṣyate| viśeṣaṇatayeti ekatra viśeṣaṇatayā'varuddhasyāparatra tadanvaye nairākāṅkṣayāditi bhāvaḥ| yatra kvapi viśeṣaṇenāparānvaya iti yathāśrutaṃ dūṣayati-
pākānnīla ityādi nīlatva-sukhayoḥ svāśraye viseṣaṇatayā'nvitayo- pākādityādyantargatapañcamyarthahetutvasyānanvavāpatterityarthaḥ| tajjanyajñānaviṣayatveti tatpadārthaḥ prakṛtiḥ| mātrapadena vṛttipratipādyatvasya vyavacchedaḥ| atra citragvādisamāsāntargatagavādipadārthe ityarthaḥ| aviruddhamiti abādhitamityarthaḥ| `ghaṭaṃ paśya' ityatra dṛśirjñānasāmānyārthaḥ| ākāśavāraṇāyeti samvāyenopasthitākāśe dṛśyarthakarmatvādyanvayavāraṇāyetyarthaḥ| vṛttyā prakṛtyarthatvasyeti vṛttijanyopasthitiviṣayatvasyetyarthaḥ|
`vādivākye yāvanto'rthāḥ sambhavanti' iti nyāyenoktavyutpattiṃ dūṣayitumarthāntararatayā vyācaṣṭe-prasaṅgāditi pratyayaprāgvarttipadajanyopasthitiviṣayatāyāḥ kṛṣṇe sattvāditi bhāvaḥ| `pratyayānām' iti vyutptterekārthobhāvavādimate sattve'pi nāsmanmate sā sārvatrikī, samāsa evāsambhavāt| kintu prakṛte vyuttpattyantarameva kalpyate ityabhipretyāha-atheti|
aklṛptakalpanāmiti samasyamānapadārthaviṣayatānirūpitaviṣayatāsambandhena śābdaṃ prati viśṣyatāsambandhena pratyayajanyopasthiteḥ kāraṇatvāntarakalpanāmityarthaḥ|
nanu pratyayānāṃ sannihitapadārthagatasvārthabodhakatvarūpā vyāsasamāsasādhāraṇyekaiva vyutpattiḥ| sannihitatvañca padatvasya prakṛtitvaghaṭitasya vyutpattau niveśe gauravamityādidigarthaḥ|
nanu pratyayānāmiti vyutpatteḥ prakṛtipratyayaghaṭitatayā tatra prakṛtitvapratyayatvayoranutayordurvacatvena tattadvayaktibhedena kāryyakāraṇabhāvo vācyaḥ| tathāca samāsasthale samāsyamānapadārthasya pratyayārthānvayābhyupagame bādhakābhāvo'ta āha-api ceti| yadvā'nekasamāseṣu viśiṣṭaśaktikalpanāpekṣayā klṛptavyutpattityāgasahakṛtā vyutpattyantarakalpanaiva laghīyasī ityata āha-api ceti| ananvayaprasaṅga iti nāmārthayoriti vyutpatteriti bhāvaḥ|
ananvayamevāha-rājapadāderiti| lakṣaṇāyamapītyapinā sambandhalakṣaṇāyāṃ nitarāmanvayābhāvaḥ sūcyate| nacoktavyutpatteḥ `śubhraḥ" ityādau vyabhicāro'ta āha-taṇḍulaḥ śūbhra iti|
tathāca taṇḍulānvayini prātipadikārthe vibhaktijanyopasthiteḥ sattvānna sattvānna tatra vyabhicāra iti bhāvaḥ| śubhreṇa taṇḍulena ityādau vyabhicāramāśaṅkaya nirācaṣṭe-abhedārthiketi athavā kaṭa iva tatsamānādhikaraṇabhīṣmā''dibhyo dvitīyā bhaviṣyatīti dvitīyādivibhakteranabhihitasutre bhāṣyakṛtā'bhidhānāt sāmānādhikaraṇyasya samānādhikaraṇayorviśeṇaṣayorbhāvasyābhedasya viśeṣaṇapadottaravibhaktyarthalābhāt tatrāpi śubhreṇeti tṛtīyājanyābhedopasthitisattvānna vyabhicāra iti bhāvaḥ|
nanvatra abhedo yadi bhedatvāvacchinnābhāvastadā'prasiddhiḥ, bhedasya kevalānvayitvāt| yadi bhedapratiyogikābhāvastadā `nīlo vahniḥ' iti vākyasya prāmāṇyāpattiḥ dvitvādinā nīlabhedābhāvasya vahnau sattvād‌ viśeṣaṇavibhaktyarthaikadeśe bhede prakṛtyarthasyānanvayāpattiśca|
naca bhede'bhāve ca dvitīyādeḥ śaktiḥ| bhede ca prakṛtyarthasya prakṛtyarthatā'vacchedakāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvenānvayaḥ, uktasambandhena prakṛtyarthaviṣṭabhedasya tu tattvāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvenābhāve'nvayopagamānnoktāpattyādiriti vācyam| `prameyo ghaṭaḥ' ityādau prameyatvācchinnabhedāprasiddhayoktaprakārāsambhavāt, nīlāpaṭādiparanīlapadaghaṭitanīlo ghaṭa iti vākyasya prāmaṇyā''patteśca| nīlatvādinā nīlabhedābhāvasya ghaṭe'bādhāt|
naca tad‌vyaktitvāvacchinnābheda eva vibhaktyarthaḥ| tathā satyapūrvavyaktiniṣṭhatad‌vyaktitvasya bānāsambhavena tadavacchinnapratiyogitākābhāve śaktigrahāsambhavenāpūrvavyaktyabedānvayā'nupapatteḥ| abhāvabuddherviṃśiṣṭavaiśiṣṭayā'vagāhitvānupapattyā ekadharmā'vacchinnapratiyogitākābhāve'paradharmā'vacchinnapratiyogitāyāḥ sambandatayā bhānāsambavācceti| cenna, abhedastādātmyam, tacca svavṛttyasādhāraṇo dharmmaḥ| asādhāraṇyañca svapratiyogivṛttitva-svasābhānādhikaraṇyobhayasambandhena bhedaviśiṣṭaṃ yattadanyatvamityekamātravṛttidharma eva, tasminnādheyatayā prakṛtyarthā'nvayaḥ| apūrvavyaktiniṣṭhatad‌vyaktitvasya viśiṣyaṃjñātumaśakyatve'pyekamātradharmatvena sāmānyapratyāsattyā sugrahāt sarvaṃ susthamiti| pārṣṭhika iti| śābdabodhottarakālika ityarthaḥ| anvaya iti tadvodha ityarthaḥ| aruṇādhikaraṇarītyā sarveṣāṃ vibhaktyarthānāṃ prathamaṃ kriyāyāmevānvayena tatra śubhrādipadārthānvaye vibhaktijanyopasthitisattvāttatpakṣe'pi noktavyutpattervyabhicāra iti bhāvaḥ|
nanu nāmārthayoriti vyutpatte rājā pūruṣastaṇḍulaḥ catītyādau rājñaḥ svasvāmibhāvasambandhena puruṣe, taṇḍulasya karmatāsambandhena dhātvarthapāke'nvayavāraṇāyā'vasyakatve'pi tatra rājā puruṣaḥ stokaṃ pacatītyatrābedasambandhenānvayād vyabhicāravāraṇāyā bhedātiriktasambandhāvacchinnatvaṃ nāmārthaniṣṭhaprakāratāyāṃ niveśanīyam|
naca viśeṣaṇavibhakterabhedārthakatayā nirvāhaḥ, kaṭa eva karmeti bhāṣyavyākhyāvasare bhīṣmādīnāṃ svayamakarmakatve'pi viśeṣyasambandhivibhaktyaiva bhīṣyamādipadottare bhavitavyam, tadekayogakṣematvāt| kevalānāṃ ca prātipadikānām "pratyayaḥ" "paraśca" iti niyamena prayogānarhatvāt| yatheśvarasuhṛdraḥ svayaṃ nirdanā api tadīyadhanenaiva tatphalabhājaḥ, evaṃ guṇā apīti "anabhihita" sūtre vadatā kaiyaṭena viśeṣaṇavibhakteḥ sādhutvamātrārthakatvaṃ spaṣṭamevoktam| vibhaktyarthaprātipadikārthaviśeṣyakanāmā(puruṣā)diprakārakabodhasya samānādhikaramaviśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvasthale'nanubhavācca| ata eva vyutpattau bhedeneti paṭhayate| tathāca tatkṣe lakṣyārtharājasambandhinaḥ puruṣādāvanvayo nānupapanno'ta āha-kiñceti| vivaraṇavirodhāditi|
atra vadanti-vigrahasya vivriyamāṇasamānā'ḍarthakatvam, nāvivriyamāṇajanyabodhe yadyad‌viśeṣyakatve sati yadyatprakārakabodhajanakatvaṃ tattad‌viśeṣyakatattatprakārakabodhajanakatvam, ityādikṛttiddhittāntavigrahatvānāpatteśca, tatra vigrahavigṛhyamāṇajabodhaviṣayapadārthānāṃ viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvavaiparītyāt| nāpi tajjanyabodhīyayāvad‌viṣayakabodhajanakatvam, puruṣasya rājetyasyāpi rājapuruṣādivigrahatvāpatteḥ| kintu vivriyamāṇajabodhajanakatvaṃ tad‌ vācyam| prāptamudakaṃ yamityādeḥ prāptodakādisamāsajabodhaviṣayāḥ parasparasambandhatātparyyaviṣayībhūtāśca yāvantaḥ padārthā udakādayastāvad‌viśayakabodhajanakatvam| ghaṭakī karmatetyetāvanmātrasya ghaṭakarmakānāyanakṛtiriti vigrahatvāvāraṇāya yāvaditi| rājñaḥ sundara ityādhikā'vagāhino rājapuruṣa iti vigrahatvamiṣṭameva|
vastutastu śaktigrahaṇārthaṃ prayujyamāne tasminnatātparyyaviṣayārthakapadaprayogadaurlabhyameva| pūruṣasya rājetyādesyu vigrahatvavāraṇāya tātparyyaviṣayatvaṃ viśeṣaṇam| tatra puruṣaḥ pratiyogī na, ghaṭaḥ karmatvamityādisamudāyasya ghaṭakarmakānayetyādivigrahatvam| kumbhaṃ karotītyādīnāṃ tu karttṛlākṣaṇikānāmeva nyāyanaye kumbhakārādivigrahatvam| prakṛte ca rājapūruṣa ityāditvasya rājñaḥ puruṣa iti vākye akṣatatvānna tasya vigrahatvānupapattiriti|
anyatheti samānā'rthakatvābhāve ityarthaḥ| tasmāt vigrahavākyāt| na syāditi tatsamānā'rthakavākyasyaiva tacchaktinirṇāyakatvāditi bhāvaḥ| sambandalakṣaṇākalpaṃ dūṣayati-nāntya iti nīlotpalādisamāsa saṃgrahāya samānavibhaktiketyapahāyoktam-viruddhavibhaktirahiteti viruddhavibhaktikadhātuprātipadikārthayoḥ `stokaṃ pacati' ityādāvabhedā'nvayadarśanādāha-pratipadikārthayoriti tathācābhedasaṃsargāvacchinnaprātipadikārtaniṣṭhaprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣyatāsambandhena śābdabuddhiṃ prati viruddhavibhaktirahitapadajanyopasthiterhetutayā tasyā rājapuruṣa ityādisamāsottarapadārthe puruṣādau sattvādabhedā'nvayo durvāra iti bhāvaḥ|
nanu viluddhavibhaktirāhityaṃ vyāsasamāsasādhāraṇam, na viśeṣyavācakapadasya viśeṣaṇavācakapadā'prakṛtikavibhaktya prakṛtitvam, nalighaṭamānayetyādāvabhāvāt| nāpi viśeṣaṇavācakapadasya viśeṣyavācakapadāprakṛtikavavibhaktyaprakṛtitvam, viśeṣyapadāprakṛtikatvasya nadanuttaratvasya taduttaratvenā'pratisandhīyamānatvasya vā nīlo ghaṭa ityādau nīlapadottaravibhaktau ghaṭādipadottaravibhaktibhinnāyāṃ sattvena tatrābhedānvayānupapatteriti cet,
atra kecit-viśeṣaṇapade viśeṣyavācakapadaprakṛtikavibhaktasajādīyabhinnavibhaktyaprakṛtikatvameva viruddhavibhaktirāhityam| sājātyañca prathamātvādinā sutvādinā vā, nīlo ghaṭa ityādau viśeṣaṇavibhakterviśeṣyavibhaktibhinnatve'pi tatsajātīyabhinnatvā bhāvenoktaviruddhavibhaktirāhityasya tatrākṣatatvāditi|
vastutastu viśeṣaṇavibhakteḥ sādhutvāmātrartakatvamiti mate viśeṣayavācakapadottaravibhaktiviruddhatvaṃ svārthabhinnārthakatvam| nīlo ghaṭa ityādau viśeṣaṇavibhakternirarthakatayā tatra viśeṣyavibhaktiviruddhārthakavibhaktirdvitīyādirūpaiva, tadaprakṛtitvasya, viśeṣaṇavācakapade sattvānna tadasaṃgrahaḥ| evaṃ nīlaghaṭamānayetyādisamstasthale viśeṣaṇavibhakterevābhāvena tādṛśavibhaktyaprakṛtitvaṃ viśeṣaṇavācakapadasyāvyāhatam| ata eva caitrasya sutasya dhanamityādau nābhedānvayā''kāṅkṣā| tatra viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyavibhaktyorvibhinnasambandhārthakatvāt| nāpi stokamattītyādyaṅagrahaḥ| pūrvakalpe tu viśeṣaṇavācakavibhaktestiṅ‌vijātīyatvenoktākāṅkṣāyāstatrāsattvena tadasaṅagrahaḥ spaṣṭa iva|
sāre, prātipadikārthayoriti prakṛtābhiprāyeṇaiva| tathācoktaviruddhavibhaktyaprakṛtiprātipadikārthaniṣṭhābhedasaṃsargāvacchinnaprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣyatāsambandena śābdebodhe dhātuprātipadikānyatarajanyopasthitiḥ kāraṇamityarathaḥ phalita iti|
nanu pūrvanuruktaviruddhavibhaktirāhityasyābhedānvayabodhākāṅkṣatve caitrasya sutasya dhanamityādau tadāpattirata āha-prapañcitamiti vibhaktyarthamantarbhāvya nāmārthānvaya evoktavyutpattiriti tatroktam| tasyāpi sārthakavibhaktyaprakṛtitvamapi viśeṣaṇavācakapade niveśanīyamatra tātparyyam| mīmāṃsakairapi viśiṣṭaśaktipakṣā'bhyupeya ityāha-ata evaneti samāse ekārthibhāvasya sarvasammatatvādetyarthaḥ| bhakṣamuddiśyeti "ṛtvijo haviḥśeṣān bhakṣayanti" iti bihatabhakṣaṇabhanūdyetyarthaḥ|
ekāprasaratābhaṅgāpatterita uddeśyavidheyabhāvenānvaye pṛthagupatthiterniṃyāmakatvena tadanurodhena tadabhyupagame viśiṣṭa'rthopasthitijanakatvarūpaikaprasaratābhaṅgāpatterityarthaḥ| tairviṃśiṣṭaśaktyanabhyupagame'pi viśiṣṭalakṣaṇāyā abhyugamenaikaprasaratāyāstanmatasiddhatvāt| nacoddeśyatayā'nvaye yacchabdayogaprāthamyādīnām, vidheyatayāvaye ca tacchabdayogādīnāṃ tantratayā tatra tadabhāvāt kathaṃ tadāpādānamiti vācyam, sati tātparyye pa3thamo bhakṣaḥ, paṇḍito brāhmaṇa ityādāvuddeśyavidheyabhāvena buddherānubhavikatayā yacchabdayogādīnāṃ prāyikatvāt| tādṛśaśābdabodhe asamastapadajanyopasthiterhetutā tu na, dāmodaraḥ pūjyaḥ, rājapuruṣaḥ sunādara ityāditastathābodhānupapatteḥ, tatra ca prāthamyaviśiṣṭaṃ bhakṣaṇāntarameva vidhīyate śabdavṛttyā, tataśca prāptāprāptavivekena prāthamyavidhiḥ paryavasyatīsi siddhāntāditi bhāvaḥ|
tryaṅgairiti| tatra hītyāśeṣabhūtatrittvaviśiṣṭayatkiñcidaṅgavidhānam, kiṃ vā tritvaviśiṣṭaśeṣāṅgavidhānamiti saṃśaye prakṛtāvijyāseṣeṇa puroḍāśena sviṣṭakṛto hutatvādatrāpi śeṣabhūtatritvaviśiṣṭaṅgaireva rivaṣṭakṛd‌havanaṃ yuktamiti pūrvapakṣe, śeṣabhūtāni kānicadaṅgānyanudya tritvavidhāne ekaprasaratābhaṅgapattistasmāt kalpasūtroktatritvaviśiṣṭāṅgāntaravidhānamiti nirṇitaṃ daśamādhyāyasya tṛtīye pāde ityarthaḥ| kalpasūtroktāni hṛdayādyekādasāṅgāni tu-hṛdayam 1, jihvā 2, vakṣaḥ 3, yakṛt 4, vukṇau 5, savyaṃ doḥ 6 7, ubhe pārśve 8|9 dakṣiṇā śroṇiḥ 10, gudam 11, iti| tatra trīṇi-dakṣiṇoṃ'saḥ, savyā śroṇiḥ, gudaṃ tṛtīyamiti|
nanvekaprasaratvam-na viśiṣṭārthopasthitijanakatvam, kuntu samāsaghaṭakapadayoruddeśyavidheyabhāvenānvayābodhakatvamiti vyutpattistadbhaṅgāpatteriti tadarthaḥ, natu viśiṣṭārthopasthitijanakatvābhaṅgāpattiriti| tādṛśavyutpattiśarīraṃ tu samāsaghaṭakapaḍhārthaviṣṭhavidheyatānirūpitoddeśyatāsambandhena śābdabuddhiṃ prati samāsaghaṭakapadajanyopasthitiḥ pratibandhiketi|
naca tādṛśapratibadhyapratibandhakabhāvakalpane gauravamiti vācyama, sarvatra viśiṣṭaśaktikalpanāpekṣayā kvacit pratibadhyapratibandakabhāvakalpane eva lāghavāt|
vastutastu yatroddeśyavidheyabhāvenānvayabodhastatpadārthopasthitisamavahitatattadānupūrvovirahādeva tathānvayāsambhavānnoktapratibadhyapratibandhakakalpanā'pi| ekaprasaratā tu prathamapadārthanvitabhakṣaviśeṣyakabodhā'nubhava eveti noktayuktyā mīmāṃsakairviśiṣṭaśaktirabhyupeyetyata āha-saṅgacchate ceti| vākyād bhedeti naca tasya vākyapadena prakaraṇe niveśo'pi sambhavati| āmūrttatvena kriyabhiḥ kārakatvena dravyaiḥ samamapi sambandhāsambhavāt| naca tṛtīyayā jyotiṣṭomakaraṇībhūtaprākaraṇikadravyāṇyanūdya yat karttavyaṃ tadārūṇyaguṇakeneti paricchedakatvena tadanvayo nāsambhāvita iti vācyam, tathā sati `śaktiḥ `kārakam' iti siddhāntabhaṅgapatterityapi bodhyam| tadevāha-prapāñcitamiti| upasaṃharati-tasmāditi| jaiminiyairiti vadatā naiyāyikamatasya sayuktikatvaṃ dhvanyate  samānārthatvānurodhāditi samānārthatvatrca vyāsa-samāsayoḥ samānaviśeṣyaprakārakabodhajanakatvaṃ tadanurodhādityarthaḥ| uktavigrahavākyāntargatarājaśabdasya sambandhini lākṣaṇikatvānna vigrahatvakṣatiḥ sambandhārthakaṣaṣṭhayantasya tu sambandhaprakārakabodhajanakatvānna vigrahatvameva citrā gāvo yasyetyāderapītyāśayaṃ prakāśayati-yadyapītyādi mīmāṃsakā ityantena| sūtrāllabhyate iti tena hi "śeṣo bahuvrīhiḥ  ityadhikārāt prathamātrikasya dvitīyāśritetyādinā'nuktasya samāso vidhīyate| yadyapi "viśeṣaṇaṃ viśeṣyeṇa  ityādinā tasyāpi samāsa ukta eva tathāpi nāsau prathamāśabdenollikhita iti tattrikasya śeṣatvamavikalamiti bhāvaḥ|
uktasyaiveti| citrāṇāṃ gavāmayamityāderevetyarthaḥ| evakāreṇa prathamāntacitrādipadaghaṭitasya vyavacchedaḥ| upadarśakapadasya vivaraṇam-arthabodhakamiti| ākhyātam-tiṅantam| tatra tiṅantaghaṭite vivaraṇe ityarthaḥ| vyatyāsamevāha-tathāhityādi|
nanu tatrāpi tipaḥ karttṛlakṣakatayā'kṣakaraṇavyāpārāśraya iti bodhanna samānārthakatvabhaṅgo'ta āha-kṛtpratyaye iti| kārakāṇām-kartrādīnām| prādhānyam mukhyaviśeṣyatvam| kārakāṇāṃ prādhānyam, "prakṛtipratyayārthayoḥ" iti nyāyena, bhāvanāyāstu "bhāvapradhānamākhyātam" iti nirūktāditi bhāvaḥ  śaktimattvāviśeṣāditi śaktimattvamekārthobhāvaḥ| siddhāntasiddhiriti siddhāntasya-mīmāṃsitārthasya siddhiḥ-anuṣṭhānopayogārhatvamityarthaḥ| tathāhi-"niṣādasthapatiṃ yājayet" iti śruyate| tatra niṣādasthapatiśabdaḥ kiṃ ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣa, bahuvrīhirvā, uta karmadhāraya iti saṃśaye, karmadhāraye'pūrvavidyādhyayanakalpanāgauravād bahuvrīhau vākyalakṣaṇāyāṃ gauravāllāghavāt ṣaṣṭhītatpūruṣa eveti pūrvapakṣe, tatpuruṣasyāpi pūrvapadalākṣaṇikatayā karmadhārayā'pekṣayā gauravāllāghavāt karmadhāraya eva| apūrvavidyādhyayanasyottarakālakalpyatvena tādṛśagauravasya phalamukhatayā adoṣatvāditi siddhāntitam, tad virudhdyeta, bhavanmate tatpuruṣasvīkāre'pi gauravānavakāśāditi bhāvaḥ|
tadānīntaneti tādṛśavākyajanyaśābdabodhāvyavahitaprāk‌kṣaṇavṛttiśaktigrahaniṣṭhāntaraṅgavahiraṅgabhāvacintanaprayuktalāghavamādāyetyarthaḥ| niṣādasambandisthapatāvityarthaḥ| tatra ṣaṣṭhayarthasambandhāntarbhāveṇaikārthībhāvābyupagamāt| niṣādasvāmika iti sthapatiśabdasya svāmyarthakatayā niṣādābhinnasvāmike puṃsītyarthaḥ| tatrānyapadārtantarbhāveṇa samudāyaśaktisvīkārāt `nānārtha' ityasya vṛttyānekārthopasthitau jātāyāmiti śeṣaḥ| `nyāyena' ityasya kva tātparyyamiti mandehe sati| tatkalpanāyām-tātparyyakalpanāyām| pūrvopasthitārtha eveti| samāsaghaṭakapratyekapadaśaktyupastāpitārthamātraviṣayakakarmadārayīyasaktigrahasyāntaraṅgatvena prathamopasthitārtha evopasthitirlāghavānniṣādasthapatiśabdasya tātparyyamavasīyate tena viśeṣāvagatiḥ| natu bahurbhūtapadārthaviṣayakaśaktigrahopasthāpite, tadupastirlāmbitatvādityākhaṇḍalārthaḥ|
niruktavyapekṣeva sāmarthyaṃ mulakṛdabhipretamityarthe idamapi pramāṇam| anyathā jahatsvārthavṛttipakṣe padānāmānarthakyāt pratyekapadavṛttyā'rthopasthiterasambhavena kva tanmūlakaśaktigraha iti tasyāntaraṅgatvakathanamasaṅgatameva syāditi bodhyam| tat tātparyam| kalpyate-avadhāryyate|
nanu "saṃyogo viprayogaśca" ityādinā hariṇā saṃyogādīnāṃ viśeṣāvagatihetutvaṃ bodhyate, tat kathaṃ tātparyyasya viśeṣaṇavagatihetutvamiti cenna, nānāsaktyupasthitānekeṣu saṃyogādinaikastimannavadhārite tātparyya viśeṣāvagatiriti sabdārthasyānavaccheda iti bhāvārthasya vakṣyamāṇatvāt| saṃyogādīnāṃ tātparyyagrāhakatāyāmevopayogena tātparyyasya viseṣavagatihetutvaṃ sarvasammatameveti bhāvaḥ|
nanu pratyekapadavṛttipakṣe'nāyāsenaivādhikaraṇasiddhāntāpapattau viśiṣṭaśaktipakṣe tādṛśasidvāntasamarthane kleśo duṣparihara evetyata āha-pareṣāmapīti| praveśayetivaditi tātparyyasyaiva lakṣaṇāniyāmakatayā tatsattve vākyalakṣaṇayā samāsāntarabodhasya durvāratayā tātparyyābhāvenaiva tadavagatervāraṇīyatvādityarthaḥ|
nanu "niṣādasthapatiṃ yājayet" ityatra sthapatiśabdaḥ svāmiparyyāyaḥ, śreṣṭhaparyāyo vā| ubhayathā'pyasau guṇavacanaḥ| tasya ca jātivacananiṣādapadena karmadhāraye tatra guṇavacanasyaiva pūrvanipātaniyamena sthapatiniṣādamiti prasajjayeta, natu niṣādasthapatimiti syāt| kiñca ubhayathāpi sthapatiśabdārthasya nityasambandhisāpekṣatvena ṣaṣṭhīsamāsa eva nayāyyaḥ, natu karmadhāraya iti kathamuktasiddhāntasaṅgatiḥ prakṛte'pītyata āha-digiti tadarathastvata evāsvarasāt "liṅgadarśanācca" iti sūtraṃ hetvantaropasaṅgrāhakaṃ praṇītaṃ maharṣiṇā| tacca śabarasvāminā, `kūṭaṃ hi niṣādānāmevopakārakam, nāryāṇām evañca tanniṣādānāmeva svam" iti bhāṣye vyākhyātam| tathāca kūṭadakṣiṇāliṅgat karmadhārayatve nirṇite sthapatiśabdasya rājadantāditvāt paranipāto bodhyaḥ, sambandhākāṅkṣānivṛttirapyarthato niṣādānāṃ svatbalābhādupapadyate| evaṃ lāghavādivicārastu kathañcit sambhavamātreṇeti sarvamanavadyam 
iti vaiyākaraṇabhūṣaṇasāradarpaṇe samāsaśaktinirūpaṇam|

 prasaṅgāditi "samāse khalu bhinnaiva śaktiḥ" iti grantena smṛtāyaḥ śaktoranupekṣaṇīyatvāditi bhāvaḥ| agṛhītavṛttikāt padāt padārthopasthiteḥ śābdabodhasya cādarśanāttaddharmāvacchinnaviṣayakaśābdabuddhiṃ prati taddharmāvacchinnārthanirūpitavṛttiviṣayakapadajñānaṃ karaṇam, vṛttyā padajanyapadārthotirvyāpāraḥ, tāṃ vinā tadanutpatteḥ natu vṛttimattvena jñānaṃ padam, atītānāgataśabdānusandhānācchābdabodhānudayaprasaṅgāt|
vṛttiśca-śaktilakṣaṇābhedād dvividhā| tatrārthe padasaṅketaḥ śaktiḥ| sa cedaṃpadamimamartha bādhayatu iti padaviśaṣyakeśvarecchātmakaḥ| `asmāt padādayamartho bodhyaḥ' ityākārapadārthaviśeṣyakeśvarecchārūpo vā, vinigamakābhāvāt| tayā cārthabādhakaṃ padaṃ pācakamiti vyavahriyate| śāstrakārādisaṅketaḥ paribhāṣā| tayā cārthabodhakaṃ padaṃ pāribhāṣikamiti vyavahriyate, yathā-nadīvṛddhayādipadam| labhaṇā tu śakyasambandhaḥ| tatkalpikā ca mukhyārthabādhonnītatātparyānupapattiḥ|
tathāhi-`gaṅāgāyāṃ ghoṣaḥ' ityatra gaṅgāpadasya ghoṣābhāvavatvāhārthaktavena tīratātparyyakatve unnite tātparyaviṣayatīrasmṛtiṃ vinā'nupapadyamānena tīraśābdabodhena tatsmṛtirākṣipyate| sā ca gaṅgāpadavṛttiṃ vinānupapadyamānā tajjñānamākṣipati| tadviṣayo gaṅgāpadanirūpitā vṛttirna śaktiḥ, tasyātrābhāvādatastīrasmārikā kācidanyaiva vṛttiḥ kalpyate| sā ca gaṅgāpadaśakyasāmīpyarūpaiva tena rūpeṇa gṛhyamāṇā tīraṃ smārayatyanubhāvayacati ca| śakyādaśakyopastitistu na lakṣaṇā, upasthitihetostasyāstattvāsambhavāt| nāpi svabodhyasambandhaḥ, samāsaśaktinirūpaṇoktayukteḥ|
