nanvīśvarecchāyāḥ śaktitvāsambhavaḥ, ghaṭapadasyāpi paṭe śaktatvaprasaṅgāt `ghaṭapadāt paṭo boddhavyaḥ' itīcchāyā viṣayatayā paṭe'pi sattvāt| yadi ceśvarecchīyaghaṭapadajanyobodhaviṣayatvaprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣyatvaṃ śaktiriti na paṭapade'tiprasaṅgaḥ, tadviṣayatve'pi tādṛśavi,yaṭatvaniṣṭhaprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣyatvasya tatrānabhyupagamādityucyate, tadāpi gaṅgāpadāt tīrabodhasyāpi jāyamānatvena sanmātraviṣayakeśverecchīyagaṅgāpadajanyabodhaviṣayatvaprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣyatvasya tīre sattvācchaktyaiva nirvāhe kṛtaṃ lakṣaṇayeti cenna, gaṅgapadavācyatvavyavahārasya tīrādāvasattvena gaṅgāpadajanyabodhaviṣayatātvāvacchinnaprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣyatvasambandhena īśvarecchīyagaṅgādipadānāṃ pravāhādau śaktiḥ, tīrādiniṣṭhaviśeṣṭhaviśeṣyatāyāśca gaṅgādipadajanyabodhaviṣayatvaprakāratānirūpyatve'pi na tasyāḥ tādṛśaviṣayatātvena tādaṛsaprakāratānirūpitatvam, āpi tu śuddhaviṣayatātvenaivetyabhyupagamānna tayā lakṣaṇā'nyathāsiddhiḥ, nāpi viṣayatvalakṣaṇasambandhasya pade janyajanakabhāve ca sattve'pi tatrātiprasaṅgaḥ|
tatra lakṣyopasthitiniyāmakaḥ sādṛsyātmakaḥ sambandho gauṇī, tadatiriktastu śuddhalakṣaṇeti vyavahriyate| śuddhāpyupādānalakṣaṇabhedena dvividhā| tatra mukhyasaṃsṛṣṭā yārthopasthāpakatvamupādānatvam| śakyāviṣayakalakṣyārthopasthitijanakatvaṃ lakṣaṇatvam| `yaṣṭīḥ praveśya,'gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣaḥ' ityacādike yathāyathamudāharaṇe| tatrāpyādyā'jahatsvārthā, antyā ca jahatsvārthetyucyate| tadbhedāntarāṇi tvanyato'badhāryyāṇīti naiyāyikā āhuḥ|
mīmāmakāstu uktayuktyā lākṣaṇīka padasya vācakatvaparihāre'pi neśvarecchāyāḥ śaktitvam, īśvarasyaivānabhyupagamāt| nanvicchāyā īśvarīyatva nupādeyamevañcā''dhunikasaṅketajñānādapi śābdavodhodayena tādṛśasaṅketasya vṛttitānirvāhaḥ| ādhunikasaṅkenatvena tātparyyasyā'pi vṛttitvaprasaṅgastu na, padārthopasthitiniyāmakasaṅketasyaiva vṛttitvena tasyā'tattvāt| tajjñānasya sākṣādeva śābdadhījanakatayā padārthopasthityātamakavyāpārānapekṣaṇād vṛttyupastāpitārtha eva prakaraṇādinā tadavadhāraṇāt|
ādhunikasaṅketitaḍitthādipadeṣu tvādhunikasaṅketasya prāgavadhāritasya ḍitthādivyaṭaktiśābdadhījanakapadārthopasthitau niyāmakatvena tad‌vṛttitvasya padārthānupastāpakatvād vṛttitvānirvāhāt| tasmāt padapadārthayorvācyavācakabhāvaniyāmakaḥ sambandhaḥ padārthāntarameva| nāpyasau samavetaḥ, abhāvādāvapi sattvāt| kintu vaiśiṣṭaḍayākhyena sambandantareṇa varttate ityāhuḥ|
vācyatvaṃ yathā na samavāyi tathā vakṣyata iti vadatā dīdhitikṛtāpi tanmatamevānusṛtam| tadetannānāmatāni nirākarttumādau tatsvarūpapradarśanamityāśayena mūlamavatārayati-tasyāḥ svarūpamiti tasyā-śakteḥ| nirviṣayāṇāṃ teṣāṃ kathaṃ ghaṭādirviṣayo'ta āha-cākṣuṣeti tathācaupacārikaviṣayatvaṃ na viruddhamiti bhāvaḥ| cākṣuṣapadaṃ tvācādipratyakṣasyāpyupalakṣaṇām| indriyaniṣṭacākṣuṣādikāraṇatāyāmanāditvam-indriyajanyapūrvapūrvabodadhvaṃsakālikatvam| cakṣuṣṭvādivyāpakatvaṃ puruṣapratyatnānapekṣatvaṃ vā, na tvajanyatvam, avyavarttakatvāt| śabdaniṣṭhabodhakāramatāyāṃ tu svājanyabodhasamānākārapūrvabodhadhvaṃsavattvam| tad‌bodhakāraṇatā-arthabodhakatvam|
jñāyamānaṃ padaṃ karaṇamabhipretyedam| anyathā viṣayatāyāṃ tādṛśahetutā'vacchedakatetyartho bodhyaḥ| saṅkejñānaṃ vinoktaśaktigrahāsambhavenāvaśyāpekṣaṇīyatayā tasyaiva śaktitvamastu, "taddheroreva" iti nyāyādityāśaṅkete-nanviti tasyā śaktitve heturādhuniketyādi| ādhunike-etatkālikasaṅketaviśiṣṭe| tadabhāvāt-niruktānāditvaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭabodhakāraṇatvābhāvādityarthaḥ| viśeṣyasattve'pi prakṛte viśeṣaṇābhāvaprayuktaviśiṣṭābhāvasattvena tadapratiyogitvarūpakāraṇātvaṃ na sambhavatītyarthaḥ|
nanvanādibhūtetiprasiddhipradarśanamātrārthakam, natu tacchaktiśarīre praviṣṭaṃ yena tadrūpeṇa tasya hetutā sambhāvyeta| kintu bodhakāraṇatvatvenaiva prakṛte tena rūpeṇa tajjñāne bādakābhāva iti cetsyādevaṃ yadi saṅketagrahamantareṇaiva tadraho bhavenna tat sambhavatītyāha-anyatheti saṅketanirapekṣasyaiva tasya śābdabodhahetutve ityarthaḥ| lākṣaṇiketi tatrāpyanādibhūtasyāpi bodhajanakatvastha sattvāditi bhāvaḥ| āpaśyakatvāditi avaśyā'pekṣaṇīyajñānaviṣayatvādityarthaḥ| sa eva-saṅketa eva| evakāreṇa bodhakatvavyapacchedaḥ, saṅketasya svarūpataḥ kāraṇatvaṃ jñātasya vā, tasyāpi sāmānyato viśeṣarūpeṇa vā hetutvamabhimatamiti vikalpaṃ dūṣayati-ucyata iti| svarūpeṇeti svasya yadrūpamiti vyutpattyā svāsādhāraṇadharmeṇa saṅketatvenetyarthaḥ| daṇḍatvena ghaṭe daṇḍa iva jñānāviṣya eva saṃketaḥ saṅketatvena heturiti yāvat| agṛhītaśaktikāditi ajñātasaṅketādityarthaḥ| arthabodhaprasaṅgāditi saṅketasya tatra vidyamānatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
sāmānyata iti janakājanakasādhāraṇarūpeṇetyarthaḥ| saṅketatveneti īśvarīyasaṅketatvādinā viseṣadharmeṇetyarthaḥ| tajjñānaśīnyānāmiti saṅkete īśvarīyatvamaviduṣāmityarthaḥ| laukikamīmāsakānāmiti vaidikānāṃ teṣāṃ kathañcidīśvarabodhasambhavāduktam-laukiketi| athavā laukikānāṃ vyāvahārikapadārthamātraviṣayakajñānavatāṃ pāmarāṇāmiti yāvat, mīmāṃsakānāṃ cetyarthaḥ| bodhodayeneti saṅketatvena tajjñānamātrād gabādyarthabodhodayenetyarthaḥ|
yadyapi saṅketatvena saṅketajñānakāraṇatāvādimate neśvarīyatvāderniveśastathāpi gavādāviśvarasyeti tad‌granthālocanāyeśvarīyatvāderniveśastadabhimata ityabhipretya dūṣitamiti dhyem| tatdarthadhījanakatvagrahavatāmevetyevakāreṇa niruktasaṅketajñānavattvavyavacchedaḥ| sa ca tajjñānaśūnyānāmityasyaivānuvādaḥ| evaśūnyapāṭhastu sugamaḥ| idañca śaktigraharūpakāraṇapradarśakam| tajjñānam-saṅketajñānam tathā-hetuḥ| saṅketitapadayaivārthadhījanakatvena padaniṣṭhajanakatāyāṃ saṅketasyāvacchedakatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
tato'pīti arthadhījanakatānirūpitaviṣayatāsambandhā'vacchinna'vacchedakalvasyā'vacchedakatvā'pekṣayārthadhijanakatātvena hetutāyā aucityena vaiyākaraṇeṣṭasiddhirityarthaḥ, saṅketajñānasyedaṃ padamarthadhījanakatā'vacchedakadityākāṃrakasyā'rthadhījanakatā'vacchedakatvā'vacchinnaprakāratākajñānatvena hetutve gauravādidaṃ padamarthadhījanakamiti jñānasyaivārthadhījanakatātvāvacchinnaprakāratākajñānatvena hetutvamucitam, lāghavāditi bhāvaḥ|
vastutastu tanmate saṅketatvenaiva hetuteti noktavikalpāvasara iti bodhyam| saṅketajñānādevetyevenā'rthadhījanakatvagrahavyudāsaḥ| asya-bodhakatvagrahasya, vyabhicāro vyatireke| tatrāpi-saṅketamātrādarthabodhasthale'pītyarthaḥ| icchāgraha iti idampadaviśeṣyakaitadarthabodhakatvaprakārakecchāgrahe ityarthaḥ| avagāhaneneti viṣayīkaraṇenetyarthaḥ| jñānecchādiviṣayakajñānasya tad‌viṣayakatvaniyamāditi bhāvaḥ| svātantryeti itarāviseṣamatvenetyarthaḥ| prakṛte cecchāṃśe viṣayitayā viśeṣaṇībhūtabodhakasvasyaiva tajjñānena viṣayīkaraṇāditi bhāvaḥ|
anyatheti pāratantryeṇāpi bodakatājñānasya sābdadhīhetutva ityarthaḥ| iṣṭāpattinirāsāyāha-nedaṃ taddhījanakamitīti| asmācchabdāditi etacchabdajanyaitadāśayakabodhaviṣayo'yamartha ityākārakavarttamānagrahavata ityarthaḥ| padaviśeṣyakārthadhījanakatvabodhagrahe vidyamāne tadaṃśe'rthadhījanaktavāvagāhinaḥ `idaṃ padametadarthadhījanakatvena buddhamanena' iti grahasyāsambhavāttadapahāyāsmādityākārakabodhagraha uktaḥ| etasmiṃstu na tasya pratibandhakatā, grāhyabhāvānavagāhitvāditi bhāvaḥ|
tad‌grahāpatteriti ghaṭādiśābdabodhāpatterityarthaḥ| uktajñānasya viṣayaviṣayakatve'pi na tena padāṃśe'rthabodakatvaṃ viṣayīkriyate yena tasya śaktigrahatvaṃ syāt| kintu tatpadajanyatvasyārthaśe tādṛśabodaviṣayatvabheva tathā, tena tulghavittivedyatayā padāṃśe'rthabodhakatvamapyavagāhyata iti tu durvacam, tulyavittivedyatve mānābhāvāt, tatpratibandhakasya grāhyābhāvāvagāhino nedaṃ taddhījanakamiti jñānasya jāgarūkatvācca|
naca padananiṣṭhārthadhījanakatvavadarthaniṣṭhapadajanyabodhaviṣayatvamapi śaktirvinigamanāvirahāt, ubhayavidhajñānācchābdabodhasya sarvānubhasiddhatvācca, kāryyatāvacchedakakoṭāvavyavahitottaratvanivesācca na parasparajanyaśābdabodhe vyabhicāraḥ| evañca dvitīyaśaktigraharūpāt tasmāccābdabodhāpattiḥ sukareti vācyam, tathā sati nedamarthadhījanakamiti ga
rāhyābhāvānavagāhino'pratibandhakagrahasyopanyāsavaiyarthyāt| grāhyābhāvānavagāhitvepyubhayavidhaśaktiṃ pratyanvayavyatirekābhyāṃ pratibandhakatvopagamena tadupanyāsasya sārthakyasampādane tuktajñānasyāsambhava evetyāśayena dūṣayati-nedaṃ taddhījanakamiti-arthaviśeṣyakapadajanyabodaviṣayatvagrahasambhavādityarthaḥ| anyatheti bādhagrahe'pi jāyamānasya rajataṃ buddhamaneneti grahasya, idaṃ viśeṣyakarajataprakārakagrahatve| `bhrantijñāsya' ityanena doṣābhāvavattvamapi sūcyate bhrāntatvāpatteriti tadabhāvavati tadavagāhijñānavata eva tattvāditi bhāvaḥ|
atredañcintyam-yatredaṃ padametadartha bodhayatviti saṅketajñānamātrācchābdabodhastatra bodhakatāśkatijñānasya hetutāvādimate vyabhicāro duruddhara eva| naca tatra saṅketajñānameva bodhakatājñāmityuktameveti vācyam, saviṣayakajñānasya tadviṣayaviṣayakatve'pi tadviśeṣyā'śe tatprakāro na tena viṣayīkriyate| `ghaṭamahaṃ jānāmi' ityanuvyavasāyena jñānāṃśe ghaṭatvaprakārakatvaghaṭaviśeṣyakatvayoreva viṣayīkaraṇāt| anyathā bhrāntijñasya bhrantatvāpattiranivāryyaiva syāt|
ata eva śabdacintāmaṇau tātparyyaviṣayasaṃsargajñānapūrvakatvasya padapakṣakānumānena siddhāvapi netarapadārthe'bhimatāparapadārthasaṃsargasiddhiḥ, tādṛśasaṃsargajñānapūrvakatvānumānena tatpadārthe itapadārthatātparyyaviṣayasaṃsargasyāviṣayīkaraṇādityasvarasāt padārthapakṣakānumānamavatāritaṃ pabhadharamiśrairiti|
śaktigrahakamāha-ida़ñceti śaktitvenābhyupetaṃ bodhakatvatrcetyartha-. pitrādityādināptaparigrahaḥ| gṛhyata iti kāryatāvacchedakakoṭau svātantryaniveśānna saṃketajñānājanye saṃketajñānātmakavādhakatājñāne vyabhicāra iti bhāvaḥ| pūrvoktāṃ lākṣaṇikocchedāpattimiṣṭatvena pariharati-nāpīti
śaktigrāhaketi śakya śaktigrāhakavyavahārāderlakṣye'pi tulyatvādityarthaḥ|
nanu śakyalakṣayavyavahārānupapattyā lakṣayavyāvṛttaiva sā vācyā| tathāca lakṣyopasthitaye lakṣaṇāvṛtterāvaśyakatvena kathamuktāpatteriṣṭatvamata āha-kiñceti śābdabodhānaṅgatvāditi ghaṭādipade'gṛhītaśaktikasya pratyakṣādyupasthitaghaṭāderghaṭādipadācchābdabodhānudayādanvayavyabhicāreṇa pratyakṣādyupasthiteḥ śābdabodhānakatvādityarthaḥ|
nanvanāyattyā tādṛśagauravaṃ sauḍhavyamata āha-apiceti| vyabhicāro vyatireka bodhyaḥ| tamevopapādayati-śaktijanyopasthitimiti| avyavahirottaratveti tacca-svadhvaṃsādhikaraṇakṣaṇānutpattikatve sati svādhikaraṇadhvaṃśādikaraṇakṣaṇotpattikatvam| atra svapadam-śaktijñānajanyopasthitiparam| lakṣaṇājñānajanyapadārthopasthitiprāgvarttiśaktijñānajanyapadārthopasthijijanyaśābdabode vyabhicāravāraṇāya viśeṣyam| śakyaśābdabodhānantarabhāvilakṣaṇājñānajanyopasthiti janyaśābdabodhe vyabhicāravāraṇāya satyantam| prakṛte lakṣa
ṇājñānajanyopasthitijanyaśābdabodhe uktasambandhena śaktijñānajanyopasthitivaiśiṣṭayābhāvena tatkāryyatāvacchedakākrāntatvānna vyabhicāra iti bhāvaḥ|
ananteti svatvasya tattad‌vyaktiviśrāntatvenānanugatatāyā tad‌ghaṭitāvyavahitottaratvasambandenopasthitimattvasya kāryyatāvacchedakakoṭau niveśe kāryyakāraṇabhāvānantyamiti bhāvaḥ| nyāyasāmyādāha-kiñceti prāgvadeveti śaktijñānaṃ vinā'pilakṣaṇājñānāt padārthopasthiteḥ, evaṃ lakṣaṇājñānaṃ vinā'pi śaktijñānātpadārthopasthitestayorupasthitihetutāyāṃ parasparaṃ vyabhicāraḥ, avyavahitottaratvasambandhena tattatkāraṇavaiśiṣṭayasya kāryyatā'vacchedakako niveśe ca gauravaṃ tadvadevetyarthaḥ|
nanu bodhakatvaśaktivādino'pi gaṅgāpadaṃ pravāhabodakamiti śaktigrahāllakṣyārthopasthiti-śābdabodhadhayorāpattivāraṇāya kāramatāvacchedakakukṣau tattadarthaniveśa āvaśyakaḥ, tathāca lakṣaṇāvādimate kva gauravamityāśaṅkaya nirācaṣṭe-naceti| paraspareti gaṅgāpadaniṣṭhatīrabodhakatvagrahaṃ vinā'pi tīrādipadaniṣṭhatādṛśaśaktigraheṇa tīrabodhāttayoḥ parasparajanyabodhe vyabhicārapratirodhāyetyarthaḥ|
śaktibhramānurodheneti gaṅgāpadasya tīra śaktibhramadaśāyāṃ tīropastityādeḥ sarvamatasiddhatayā tavāpi tattatkāraṇatāvacchedakakoṭau tattatpadattadatarthaniveśasyāvaśyakatvādityarthaḥ|
taveti lakṣaṇāsvīkarturityarthaḥ| śaktijñānakāraṇatāyāṃ śakteśśaktitvena niveśe syādevoktadoṣaḥ kintu vṛttitveneti na tatsambhāvanetyāśaṅkete-atheti| vācyamiti śaktijanyopasthitāviva lakṣaṇājanyopasthitāvapi śaktilakṣaṇāsādhāraṇavṛttitvāvacchinnajanyatvasattvāditi bhāvaḥ|
śaktilakṣaṇeti śaktibhinnatve sati lakṣaṇābhinnaṃ yattadbhitvasyetyartaḥ| śābdabodheti śābdabodhajanikā yā padārthopasthitiḥ padavṛttijñānajyopasthitistadanukūletyarthaḥ, kāraṇatā'vacchedakatve ubhayasādhāraṇaṃ dūṣaṇamāha-guruvāditi asmadabhimataśaktitvamapekṣya niruktānyataratvādegurrūtvādityarthaḥ| dvitīye dūṣaṇāntaramāha-śābdabodhahetviti| śābdabodhānukūlatvasya tajjanakatvasya kāraṇātā'vacchedakopasthityadhīnatvena tasya ca padavṛttijñānajyopasthititvātmakatayā tajjñānasya svaghaṭakavattitvajñānaṃ vinā asambhavena kāryyakāraṇabhāvagraha eva durśaṭa ityarthaḥ| tathāca vṛttitvagrahe śābdavodhapadārthopasthityoḥ kāryyakāraṇabhāvagrahastad‌grahe ca niruktapadapadārthasambandhatvarūpavṛttitvagraha ityanyonyāśrayeṇoktasya vṛttitvāsambhava iti bhāvaḥ|
vastutastu vijātīyapadārthopasthitijanakapadapadārthayoḥ sambandhaḥ śaktiḥ| vaijātyasya niveśācca nākāśopasthitijanakasamavāyādāvattivyāptiḥ| tādṛśavai3jātyaparicāyakañca śābdabodhānukūleti| natu tena lakṣaṇājñānasyāpi śaktijñānatvenaiva hetutvena lakṣaṇājñānajanyalakṣyārthopasthityādau śaktijñānasya hetutāyāṃ vyabhicāra ityar‌thaḥ| viṣayamaniveśyaiva yadi śaktijñānapadārthopasthityoḥ kāryyakāraṇabhāvaḥ sambhavet tadā syādapyuktaprakāraḥ, sa eva na sambhavatītyāha-śaktijñāneti| āvaśyakatvāditi tadarthaviṣayakopasthitiṃ prati tadarthanirūpitatatpadaniṣṭhaśaktijñānatvena hetutāyā avaśyakalpyatvādityarthaḥ| āvasyakatvamevāha-anyatheti śaktijñānapadārthopasthityoḥ kāryyakāraṇabhāve samānaviṣayatvāniveśe ityarthaḥ| sambandhāgrahavata ityuttayā lakṣaṇājñānāsattvaṃ bodhitam|
gaṅgāpadeti pravāharūpārtanirūpitabodhakatvaśaktiṃ gaṅgāpade jānata ityarthaḥ tīrabodhaprasaṅga iti tīropasthitestad‌dvārakaśābdabodhasya cāpattirityarthaḥ|
nanūktāpattivāraṇāya vṛttijñānajanyopasthitiśābdabodhayoreva kāryyakāraṇabhāve samānaviṣayakatvaṃ niveśyatām, kṛtaṃ vṛttijñāna-padārthopasthityoḥ kāryyakāraṇabhāve tanniveśenetyata āha-apiceti hastipakasmṛtekvṛttijñānajanyatvasampattaye hastino'pi vākyāt smṛtīviṣayatā darśitā| samūhālampanetyatrālambanapadaṃ viṣayaparam| vṛttijanyopasthitiriti tadviṣayakatvāvacchinnavṛttijñānaniṣṭhakāraṇatānirūpitakāryyatāvacchedakadharmavacchinnopasthitirityarthaḥ| uktasthale hastipakopasthitervṛttijñānajanyatve'pi na tanniṣṭhā janyatā hastipakaviṣayatvenā'vacchidyate| nāpi vṛttijñānaniṣṭhajanakatāyāṃ hastī viṣayatvenāvacchedakaḥ, vṛttijñāne dharmitvenābhānāditi noktāpattiriti bhāvaḥ| prakṛtopayoginamarthamāha-evañceti vṛttijñānopasthitiśābodhānāṃ samānaviṣayakatvena hetuhetumadbhāvaḥ phalatītī bhāvaḥ| asambhavaduktikamiti lakṣaṇājñānasya śaktiviṣayakatve'pi tīraviṣayakaśaktigrahātmakatvasyābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ|
eteneti dvividhavṛttijñānasyaikarūpeṇa hetutvāsambhavena, vakṣyamāṇadūṣaṇena vetyarthaḥ| asambhavaduktikamiti lakṣaṇājñānasya śaktijñānādhīnatvena tajjanyosthitau śaktiprayojyatvasattvānna lakṣaṇāvādimate hetvantarakalpanotyarthaḥ| aṃnatiprasaktsyeti| janyajanyatvasya lakṣaṇājñānajanyopasthitāviva viśeṣaṇatāvacchedakaprakārakanirṇayavidhayā śaktijñānajanyaghaṭapadaśakyavān deśa ityākārakaviśiṣṭavaiśiṣṭayāvagāhyanuvyavasāyātmakopasthitāvapi sattvāttadviṣayasyāpi śābdabodhāpatti, sākṣājjanyopasthiterasaṃgrahaśca, sākṣājjanyatvasya tattve tu lakṣaṇājñānajanyopasthityasaṃgrahaḥ, ityubhayasādhāraṇasya tasya nirvaktumaśakyatvāt svarūpasambandharūpasya tasyānanugatasyobhayasādhāraṇyaṃ tu sutarāmasambhāvitamiti bhāvaḥ| anthāsiddhinirūpakatānavacchedakaśaktijñānaniyatottaravartitāvacchedakadharmavattvarūpaṃ śaktiprayojyatvamubhayasādhāraṇaṃ suvacamityapi kecit| adhikamagre vakṣyate|
atra vadanti-artabodhajanakatvasya vastutaḥ padaniṣṭhasya vācyavācakavyavahāraniyāmakaśaktitvasambhava evoktakāryyakāraṇabhāve gauravalāghavādicarccā, natu tasya tadrūpatvasambhavaḥ| tathāhi-śābdabodhaṃ prati vṛttijñānasya hetuteti nirvivādam| sā ca bhavaduktārthadhījanakatvasya vṛttitve na sambhavati, tadrūpāyāstasyāḥ jñānaniṣṭhaśābdadhījanakatāyāmanavacchedakatvāt| ātmāśrayeṇa svasyaiva svāvaccedakatvāsambhavāt|
kiñca prayojyaprayojakavyavahāradraṣṭṛprayojyapravṛttyanumite prayojyaghaṭānayanādijñāne upasthitatvācchābdasyaiva hetutāyāmavadhāryyamāṇāyāmasambaddasya kāraṇatvānupapattyā kalpyamānasambandhasya janakatvātmakatvāsambhavaḥ, svayamanupapadyamānatvena gṛhītasya grahītumaśakyatvāt| tasmānna `bodhakatvaṃ śaktiḥ'| evaṃ nyāyamatābhyupetāsmāt padādetadarathaviśeṣyakavodho bhavatvityākārakaśvarecchāviṣayatvasyāpi saṅketīyagaṅgāpadajanyatvaprakāratānirūpitabodhatvāvacchinnoddeśyatākanirūpitaviśeṣyatvavṛttyavacchedakatādheyatāsambandhāvacchinnāvacchedakatvaparyyavasitasya na śaktitvam, lakṣaṇocchedāpatteḥ|
gaṅgāpadāttīrabodhedayena gaṅgāpadāt tīraviśeṣyakabodho bhavatvitīcchāyā api bhāgavatyā sambhavāt, tādṛśecchīyaniruktāvacchedakatāyā lakṣye tīre'pi sambhavāt tādṛśāvacchedakatātvena śaktijñānasya śābdabodhe kāraṇatākalpane gauravācca| tīrādiniṣṭhāvacchedakatāyāḥ pūrvoktaviśeṣyatvavṛttyavacchedakatānirūpitatve'pi na viśeṣyavṛttyavacchedakatātvena tannirūpyatvam, kintu śuddhāvacchedakatāmātreṇeti na tādṛśaśaktistīre iti tu śiṣyapratāraṇāmātram| icchāviṣayatvam, jñānaviṣayatvaṃ vā śaktiriti vinigamanāvirahāccetyadhikamagre vakṣyate| tasmāt padapadārthayorvācyavācakabhāvavyavahāraniyāmaka sambandhaḥ padārthāntarameva|
tasya ca lakṣyavyāvṛttasyaiva dharmigrāhakamānasiddhatvāllakṣyabodhārthaṃ sakyasambandharapalakṣaṇāvṛtterapi svīkāra āvaśyakaḥ| tajjñānasyaṭa śābdabodhe hetutvāntarakalpanaprayuktagauravantu phalamukhatvānna doṣāvaham, anyathā'nupapatteśca| ata eva śakyalakṣyavyavahāraḥ svarasataḥ saṅgacchate|
athavā śaktilakṣaṇājñānayoḥ śābdarūpaṃ pratyekarūpeṇaiva hetutā| tathāhi-tacchādvaṃ prati śaktitvāvacchinnasāṃsargikaviṣayatānirūpipadaniṣṭhaprakāratānirūpitatadarthaviśeṣyatākajñānatvena hetutā| śaktisambandhena, śakyasambandhena vā padaprakārakajñāne śaktaḥ saṃsargatvāt| śaktitvaniṣṭhāvācchedakatāyāṃ paryyāpteraniveśena śakyasambanadhaprakārakajñānasyāpi tena kroṅīkaraṇāt| parantu padaniṣṭhaprakāratānirūpitatādṛśasāṃsargikaviṣayatāyāmarthaviśeṣyatānirūpitatvānabhyupagamānna śaktisambandhena padābhāvaprakārakabuddhipratibadhyatvaṃ lakṣaṇājñānasya|
"indriyām" ityādiripadyasya tvayamarthaḥ-indriyāṇāṃ cākṣuṣādiviṣayeṣu-ghaṭādiṣu puruṣaprayatnānapekṣā yathā yogyatā-tadīyacākṣuṣādikāraṇātāvacchedakarūpā'sti tathā'rthaiḥ sākamapi padānāṃ sambandho yogyatā-tacchābdabodhajanakatā'vacchedakadharmaḥ, natu janakatvameva, uktayukteḥ, iti na tad‌virodho'pīti sarvamanavadyam| tad‌grāhakañca-vyākaraṇasmṛtyādi| taduktam-
śaktigrahaṃ vyākaramopamānakośāptavākyād vyavahārataśca|
vākyasya śeṣādvivṛtervadanti sānnidhyataḥ siddhapadasya vṛddhāḥ|| iti|
tatra vyākaramādikaṃ lāghavasahakṛtameva śaktigrāhakamiti naiyāyikamatam| yato lāghavasahakṛtena "laḥ karmaṇi"  iti sūtreṇa kṛtāveva śaktiḥ paricchidyate, natu karttarīti yatthaitattathoktam| "guṇe śuklādayaḥ" iti kośenāpi lāghavasahakṛtena guṇe eva śaktirnardhāryyate, natu guṇini, gauravāt| guṇibodhastu śakladapadebhyo nirūḍhalakṣaṇaiveti ca tadukteḥ| nirūḍhatvāñca prāṅ‌niruktam|
upamānasya tu sādṛśyajñānatmakasyātideśavākyārthasmṛtīdvārā gavayādirgavayādipadavācca iti śaktiparicchedakatvam| idametadvācakamityākārā''ptavākyādapi tad‌grahaḥ| āptatvatrca-tadarthaviṣayakayathārthajñānavattvam| vyāvahārāt-yathā prayojakavṛddhena ghaṭamānayetyukte prayojyavṛddhenā'nīte ghaṭe pārśvastho ghaṭakarmakānayanādikārtha ghaṭakarmakānayanādijñānasdhyamityanumimīte, vyāvahāre vyavaharttavyajñānasya hetutvāt| antaraṃ taddhetujijñāsāyāmupasthitaṃ tadvākyameva taddhetutvenāvadhārayati| tasya cārthāsambaddhasya taddhetutvānupapattyā'rthena sākaṃ paścād vākyasya sambandhamavadhārayati| saiva śaktiriti vyaṭavahriyate|
yadapi prathamaṃ tena vākyasyaiva vākyārthe'vadhāritaḥ saḥ, tathāpi ghaṭaṃ naya' `gāmānaya' ityāvāpodvāpābhyāṃ paścājjātapratyekapadaśaktigraheṇa prāg‌jātavākyaśaktigrahe candrāderjyotiḥśāstrādhyayanajanyaśatayojanādiparimāṇagraheṇa pāścātyena prāthamikālpaparimāṇagraha ivāprāmāṇyaṃ gṛhyate iti naiyāyikāḥ| pratyekapadaśaktigraheṇaivākāṅkṣādisahakṛtena vākyārthabodhopapatteriti tadāśayaḥ|
anye tu vākyārthaśaktigrahasyaṭa pratyekapadaśaktyupajīvyatvāttenaitad‌bādhāsambhavena prāg‌jātaśaktigrahasyaiva balavattvamityāhuḥ| vākyaśeṣāttu-"yavairjuhoti"ityatra yavapadasya dīrghaśūkaviśiṣṭe āryāṇāṃ prayogāt kaṅgau mlcchānāṃ prayogāt kva śaktiriti sandehe "yatrānyā oṣadhayo mlāyante athaite modamānā ivottiṣṭhaniti" iti vākyaśeṣeṇa dīrghaśūkaviśiṣṭe tannirṇayaḥ| vivaraṇasya yathā śaktigrāhakatvaṃ tathā dhātvarthanirūpaṇe uktam| "iha sahakāratarau madhuraṃ piko rauti" ityādau prasiddhasahakārādipadasannidhānāt pikādipadānāṃ kokilādau śaktiparicchittiriti| sauṣā śaktirnānārteṣu saṃ yogādinā niyamyate| taduktaṃ kāvyapa3kāśe-
saṃyogo viprayogaśca sāhacartya virodhitā|
arthaḥ prakāraṇaṃ liṅgaṃ śabdasyānyasya sannidhiḥ||
sāmārthyamauciti deśaḥ kālo vyaktiḥ svarādayaḥ|
śabdārthasyānavacchede viśeṣasmṛtihetavaḥ || iti||
śabdārthasyānavacchede-sandehe tannirāsadvārā viśeṣanirṇayasya janakāḥ saṃyogādaya iti tadarthaḥ| śakyopasthitānāmanekeṣāmekataramātrārthatātparynirṇayadvārā tattadarthabodhajanakatvaṃ teṣāmiti bhāvaḥ|
tatra savatsā dhenuravatsā dhenuriti krameṇa saṃyogaviprayogayorudāharaṇe| sāhacaryam-sādṛśyam, sadṛśayoreva prāyaśaḥ saha prayogāt, evañca `rāmalakṣmaṇau ityādāvubhayornāmārthayoryugapadeva sāhacaryyagrahādhīnatātparyagraheṇa viśṣāvagatiriti nānyonyāśrayaḥ|`rāmārjunagatistayoḥ' ityādau virodhena sā| `añjalinā juhoti' `añcalinā'dityamupattiṣṭhate' ityādau `tejo vai ghṛtam' ityādistutirūpālliṅgādaktapadasya ghṛtasādhanāñjane tātparyāvagatiḥ| `rāmo jāmadagnyaḥ' ityādau jāmadagnyapadasannidhānena rāmapadasya paraśurāmaparatā| evam `abhirūpāya kanyā deyā' ityatrābirūpataryeti sāmarthyādavagamyate| `yaccanimbaṃ paraśunā' itayatraucityāt paraśupadasya chedanārthatvam| `bhatyatra parameśvaraḥ' ityatra rājadhānīrūpadeśāt parameśvarapadasya rājaparatvam|
`citrabhānurbhāti' ityatra citrabhānupadaṃ rātrau vahnerdivā sūryyasya bodhakam| `mitro bhāti' ityādau vyakteḥ-liṅgādādye suhṛdo'ntye sūryyasya bodhakaṃ mitrapadam| `sthulapṛṣatīmanaḍ‌vāhīm' ityatra svarāt tatpuruṣabahuvrīhyarthanirṇayaḥ| ādipadāt ṣatvāṇakhādi, yathā-`susiktam' ityatra suśabdasya karmapravacanīyasya pūjārthakatvam suṣiktamityatropasargasyānyārthakatvam| evam-`praṇayakaḥ' ityatra ṇatvāt praṇayanakarttṛbodhaḥ| `pranāyakaḥ' ityatra tadabhāvāt pragatanāyakabodha ityādyūhyam|
yattu-padapadārthayoryo'rthaḥ sa śabdo ya śabdaḥ so'rtha itītaratarādhyāsamūlakaṃ tādātmyaṃ bhedavyāpṛktamasti| tasya vācakaḥ praṇavādi| `omityekāśraraṃ brahma' iti bhedābhedayordarśanāt| ādye bhedasyod‌bhūtvavivakṣayā ṣaṣṭhī, abhedasyod‌bhūtatvavivakṣayā tvantye prātipadikārthe pratham| sa eva sambandhaḥ padatadarthayorvācyavācakabhāvyavahāraniyāmakaḥ|
śaktistu saṅketaḥ, padārthāntaraṃ veti bhaṭṭānuyāyimatam| tattu na sādhīyaḥ, adhyāsamūlakasyāsatastasya sambandhatvāsambhavāt, bhedābhedayoḥ parasparābhāvarūpatayā viruddhatvenaikatrāsambhavācca kasyacid tathā'dhyāsasambhave'pi sarvaiṣāṃ tathā'dhyāse mānābhāvāt, ghaṭapadanāśe'pi ghaṭasyopalabhyamānatayā tayostādātmyasyāsambhavaduktikatvācca|
naca pākarakte prāk śayame'pi pākottaram `ayamidānīṃ na śayāmaṃ' iti pratīterbedābhedau na viruddhāviti vācyam, avyāpyavṛttīnāṃ teṣāmekāvacchedena virodhānapāyāt| ata eva tatra deśakālāvacchedakabhedenaiva `ayabhidānīṃ na śayāmaṃ' `vṛkṣo mūle na kapisaṃyoga,' `iti, natu tadanavagāhinī ghaṭo'yaṃ naśyāmo vṛkṣo na kapisaṃyogīti pratītiḥ, kintvabhedavāde ghaṭapadanāśe ghaṭanāśāpattirevaṃ śabdavadarthasyāpi śrotrendriyagrāhyatāyāḥ śabdasya cakṣurādīndriyagrāhyatāyāścāpattiriti bahuvyākopaḥ| śabdasya yathā na vityatvaṃ tathā vakṣyate|
nacātadyantabhede ghaṭaḥ paṭo'tya tā'bhede ghaṭo ghaṭa iti sāmānādhikaraṇyādaraśanād bhedābhedavādopapattiriti śaṅkayam' ityādau tvakṣarapadasya tatpratipādye nirūḍhalakṣaṇayā sāmānādhikaraṇyopapattestadvākyasyopāsanākāṇḍasthatayā bhede'pyabhedopāsanaparatayopapattescetyanyatra vistaraḥ|
sā ca śaktistridhā-yogaḥ, rūḍhiḥ, yogarūḍhiśceti| prakṛtipratyayanirūpitā tattadarthe yā śaktiḥ sa yogastanmātreṇārthabodhakaṃ padaṃ yaugikamiti vyavahriyate| yathā pācakādipadām, taddhaṭakadhātupratyayoḥ pākādau, kartrādikārake ca vyutpannatvāt| avayavārthāpratītau kevalasamudāyasya tattadarthe yā śaktiḥ sā rūḍhiḥ, tayā'rthabodhakaṃ padaṃ rūḍham| yathā-maṇīnūpurādi| aśvagandhādipadāttu kadācidoṣadhiniṣṭhasamudāyacanirūpitaśaktyoṣadhibodhaḥ| kadācittu aśvasya gandho'syāmiti vyutpattyā vājiśālādibodho'vayavaśakyeti yaugikūḍhiḥ pṛthaṅ‌noktā, uktayorevāntarbhāvāt|
"rūḍhiryogāpahāriṇī" iti tu prakāraṇādyabhāve| lākṣaṇikaṃ tad‌ vājiśālābodhe ityapare| yatrāvayavārthasahakṛtā samudāyaśaktirarthabodhikā sā yogarūḍhistayā'rthabodhakaṃ padaṃ yogarūḍham| yathā-paṅkajādipadam| tatra paṅgadhikaraṇakotpattyāśrayapadmtavāviśiṣṭabodhād anupapattipratisandhānaṃ vināpi padmabodhānna tatra lakṣaṇāvasara ityādi prapañcitaṃ pūrvameva|
`gaṅgāyaṃ ghoṣa' ityādau gaṅgāpadasya tīre śakyasāmīpyapūpalakṣaṇāgrahe tadā''hītasaṃskārajanyatīrādismṛtyā tīrādhikaraṇakaghoṣaśābdavuddhiḥ| lakṣyavyavahārānyathānupapattyā lakṣaṇāyā apyavaśyambhyupeyatvāt| anvayā'nupapattipratisandhānaṃ ca tatkalpane bījam| yathokta kvacinnirāgāṅakṣatvampi tadvījam| yathā "vadanaṃ tasyā vadanaṃ mukhamamukhaṃ bhāti cānyāsām" ityatra tādātmyambandhāvacchinnavadanatvāvacchinnapa3kāratānirūpitavadanatvāvcacinnaviseṣyatāko bodho na sambhavati, tādātmyasambandhāvacchinnatādṛśaprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣyatāvacchedakatāsambandhena śābdabuddhau vidheyatā'vacchedakabhedasya tantratvāt, iti vadanatvāvacchinnārthakavadanapadanirūpitākāṅkṣā na vadanapade'tastatra padanapadaṃ lakṣaṇayā''hlādakaparam| tadupasthitāhlādakatvāvacchinnasyābhedānvayo vadane anuśāsanabodhitanairākāṅkṣayamapi kvacittathā| yathā-
kṛtasītāparityāgaḥ sa ratnākaramekhalām|
bubhuje pṛthivīpālaḥ pṛtivīmeva kevalām||
ityatra bujidhātoravanavācakasyātmanepadanirākāṅkṣatayā vācyarthabuddhau pratibaddhāyāṃ taddhātunā svārthapālanasanbandhiśāsanaṃ lakṣyate| ātmanepadasamabhivyāhṛtabhujitvāvcacedenā''khyātavodhyakartranvitapālanabodhasvarūpāyogyatvarūpanaikāṅkṣayasya, "bhujo'navane"  iti sūtreṇa bodhanāt| tatra kevalapadena tatkālikarāmabhinnapṛthivīpālakarttakabhogakarmaniṣṭhānyonyābhāvapratiyogitā'navacchedakaikatvayoginī vyaktirabhīdhīyate| evakāreṇa pṛthivyanyayogavyavacchedaḥ|
evañca tatkālikarāmābhinnavṛthivīpālakarttṛkabhogakarmaniṣṭhānyonyābhāvapratīyogitānavaccedakaikatvaviśiṣṭābhinnavṛthivīkarmakabhogakartta vṛthivyanyastrīkarmakabhogākarttā rāmābhinnaḥ pṛthivīpāla iti nyāyanaye vākyārthabodhaḥ| `dvirepho madhuraṃ rauti,' `pikaḥ kūjati kānane' ityādau tu mukhyārthabādha eva lakṣaṇāvījam| kintu sā lakṣitalakṣaṇetyucyate, lakṣyasamāsārtharephadvayaviśiṣṭaghaṭitatvāttasyāḥ|
tathāhṛyena puruṣema dvirephādipadasya prāṇiviśe, rūḍhirna gṛhītā, kintu rephau yatreti vyatpatadtadyā rephadvayasambandhibhramarapade śakyasambandharūpalakṣaṇā gṛhītā tasya prāṇiviśeṣaviśeṣyakamadhurārāvakartṛtvaśābdabodhasya tātparyyaviṣayasyānupapattyādvirephapadāt svalakṣyarephadvayaghaṭitapadavācyatvalakṣaṇayopasthite prāṇiśeṣa madhura''rāvakartṛtvānvaya iti dvirephapadena bhramarapadamupasthāpyate| tena ca prāṇiviśeṣopasthiriti tu na samyak, `pratyayānām' iti vyutadpattestatra pratyayārthānvayāsambhavāt| bhramarapade lakṣaṇāyāmapi na tena tadupastitiḥ, uccarītasyaivārthepratyāyakatvādityapayāhuḥ| evam "pikaḥ kūjati kānane" ityādāvapi bodhyam|
evam-
gaṅgā harati vai pusāṃ dṛṣṭā pītā'vagāhitā|
ityatra pītā gaṅgā pāpaṃ haratīti vākye gaṅgāpadāt svaśakyapravāhāvayavāvayavibhāvasambandharūpalakṣaṇāgrahā'hitasaṃskārasahakṛtādupasthitod‌dhṛtajale pānakarmatvā'navayaḥ| tātparyyanupapattireva vā sarvatra lakṣaṇābījam| tadunnāyakatvenaivoktanairākāṅakṣayādīnāmupayogaḥ| ata eva `yaṣṭīḥ paiveśaya' ityādau yaṣaṭayādipadānāṃ taddhāraṇakarttari lakṣaṇā, yaṣṭiṣu praveśakarmatvānvayānupapattyabhāve'pi bhojanādiprakāraṇonnītatātparyyaviṣayabhojanakapraveśakarmatvānupapattestatra jāgarūkatvāt| evam `kuntāḥ pravīśanti' `chatriṇo yānti' ityādīṣvapyūhyam|
nanu chatriśabdasya padadvayaghaṭitatvena vākyatayā nyāyanaye kathaṃ tasya lakṣaṇā, śakyāprasiddheḥ| kiñcātra naikasārthagantṛtvaṃ lakṣyatā'bacchedakam, `yānti' ityasyānanvayāpatteḥ, gantṛtvenopasthitasya gamanākāṅ‌kṣāvirahāditi cet satyam, sambandiśaktamatupa eva tatraikasārthasambandhilakṣyakatvābhyupagamenoktadoṣadvayasyāpraskteḥ| naca chatrapadavaiyarthyam, tasya tātparyyagrāhakatvena sārthakyāt| `lakṣiṇakaṃ padaṃ nānubāvakam' iti sampradāyaḥ| svaśakyānubhavaṃ pratyeva padānāṃ kāraṇatvam| itarapadārthena saha svaśakyānubhākatvena kalṛptād‌ghoṣādipadādeva tīrānubhavopapatterna śakyādaśakyopasthilirlakṣaṇetyabhyupagacchatāṃ mate svajanyopasthitisambandhenaiva padasyānubhāvakatayā tasyāśca tīrādāvabhāvāttasyānanubhāvakatvamiti tanmūlam| naca tatsambhavati, śakyopasthitāccīrāderaprakṛtyarthatayā tatra pratyayārtānvayāsambhavāt|
vastutastu lākṣaṇikaṃ padamuddeśyavideyabhāvenānvayājanakamityeva tadarthaḥ, uddeśyavidheyabhāvenānvaye śaktimattvasyaiva tantratvāt| ata eva "na vidho paraḥ śabdārthaḥ" iti saṅgacchate| kiñca tantusaṃyogasya paṭasaṃyogahetutvatātparyyake `tantuḥ paṭaṃ janayati' iti sarvalakṣaṇikavākye'nvayānubhavāpalāpā'patterlākṣaṇikapadasyānubhāvakatvameva nyāyyam gaṅgāpadāttīramanubhavāmi' iti pratīteśca tādṛśapravāde nābhiniveṣṭavyam|
nanu `svāyatte śabdaprayogo kimityavācakaṃ padaṃ prayokṣyāmahe' iti nyāyāttīradyavācakagaṅgādipadābhidhānamanucitam| prayojanajijñāsāyāṃ pāvanatvādipratipattaye tadīti yadi, tarhi vṛttiṃ vinā tasya śābde bhānāsambhavāttadartha vyañjanāvṛttirapi tṛtīyā'ṅagīkaraṇīyā| ata eva--
ityatra aśaktānāṃ śriyamapadṛtya puṃścalyo jalavāhakaiḥ sākaṃ ramante ityātātparyyaviṣayarūḍhayarthānāliṅgitārthāntarasyāpi pratītiḥ| sā cārthadvārikā'pi-
"bhrama dhārmika vistrabdhaṃ so'vayaśvā māritastena|
godātīranikuñcapratiṣṭhitanisargadṛptasiṃhena||"
ityādau pratibhājuṣāṃ sahṛdayānāṃ vaktuboddhavyavaiśiṣṭayasphūrtau siṃhapratītyanantaraṃ godātīre śvabhīrubhramaṇāyogyatvapratyayāditi ityādau lūḍhayarthānāliṅgatārthasya kevalayogena pratītyasambhave'pi lakṣaṇayā tasyāḥ sūpapādatvāt| pāvanatvādipratītirviśiṣṭalakṣaṇayā'pi suddhalakṣaṇayaivānupapattiparihāre viśiṣṭa tadabhyupagame bījābhāva iti tu na, `naṣṭīḥ praveśaya' ityatrāpi tadanāpatteḥ, vācyārthavaiyañjanikabuddharenumānenāpi gātārthatvācca|
vastutastu gālipradānāderiveto'pitathā tātparyyamebonnīyate,na tvānvayabodhaḥ, anumitirvā, bodhe vbhicārapakṣadharmatayoḥ, sandehe vā tayorasambhavāttatraiva tairvyaḍhjanollāsābhyupagamādityanyatra vistaraḥ  yadi punarānubhāviko lokānāṃ svarasavāhī śabdādamumartha pratyemiti pratyayastadā vyañjanāvaḍattirapratyūhaiveti svoktaniṣkṛṣṭamatamavalambya mamūlamavatārayati-nanvisi evam-bodhakatvasyaiva saktitve tatrāpi-aparbhraśātmakabhāṣāyāmapi| syāditi tasyā api bodhakatvāt| īśverecchādīnāṃ śaktitvavādināṃ tu nāyamatiprasaṅga iti bhāvaḥ| iṣṭāpattāvāha-tathāceti| "nāpabhraṃśitavai" ityādiniṣadhavidhiviṣayatvānāpattyeṣṭāpatteḥ kartumaśakyatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
dvidheti apabhraṃśānāmuktaśaktimattvābhāvavyavasthāpanena sādhutvasyā'nyathānirvācanena cetyartha| mūle anumānena vācaka iti anumānamatra smṛtiḥ| anu-paścānmānam-mitiriti vyutpatteḥ, natu vyāptijñānam, tacchūnyānāmapi śābdabodhadarśanāt| gāvyādyapabhraṃśānāṃ gavādiśabdavikṛtitvenod‌bodhakavidhayā jhaṭiti prakṛtismārakatvādityāśayena vyācaṣṭa-anumānena sādhuśabdamiti| anumāyeti smṛtvetyarthaḥ| vakṣyati ca -`teṣāṃ sādhusmaraṇe evopayoga' iti| apabhraṃśānaṃ sākṣād bodhakatā tu na, śabdajanakasādhutvasyābhāvādasādhutvajñānasya pratibandhakatvādveti bhāvaḥ| tatra harisammatimāha-uktaṃ hīti| te-apabhraṃśāḥ| `sādhuṣu iti viṣayasaptamī, sādhuviṣayakasmaraṇenetyarthaḥ| pratyayeti gavādyarthabodhakāraṇānītyarthaḥ| pakṣāntaramāha-tādātmyamiti sādhugavādiśabdatādātmyaprakārakajñānaviṣayatāmāpādya vetyarthaḥ| evakāro vā'rthe| gopade uccāraṇīye kāraṇāpāṭavena gāvītyuccāritam, vastuto gopadamevedamiti tādātmyena bhāsamānā gāvyādiśabdā gāvādirūpārthasya prakāśakā ityarthaḥ| naca vakṣyamāṇakalpābhedaḥ, tatrāpabhraṃśagatānupūrvyā eva śaktatāvacchedakatvāt| atra tu sādhugatāyāstasyāstattvamiti bhedāditi bhāvaḥ|
yattu-śabdārthayostādātmyaṃ śaktiriti svoktārtheṃ tādātmyamityādiharikārikārddhasyopaṣṭambhakatayopanyasanam, tanmudhaiva, tādātmyasya śaktitve'pi śabdaniṣṭhatādātmyajñānasyārthābodhakatvena tasya śaktitvāsambhavāt| upakramopasaṃhāraparyyālocanayā sādhuśabdatādātmyabhrāntyā'pabhraṃśānāmarthabodhakajanakatvamiti pakṣāntaraparatayaiva tad‌vayākhyānasyaucityāditi|
avācakatve yuktimāha-na śiṣṭairiti yataste'pabhraṃśāḥ sādhavaḥ paryyāyā iva śiṣṭaiḥ smṛtyādinā na pratipādyante'to na vācakā ityarthaḥ| te sādhuṣvityādyuktamartha| tena sādhuvikṛtyapabhraṃśaśravaṇena| vyakte apabhraṃśaprakṛtisādhuśabde| asya smṛte satīti śeṣaḥ| nirṇāyaḥ tadarthaśābdabodho bhavatītyartha-| evamiti ittthamevetyarthaḥ sādhauśa-gavādiśabde| apabhraṃśaḥ-gāvyādiḥ|
tena-asādhuśabdena| sādhuvyavahitaḥ-sādhusmṛtidvārakaḥ| kiścit-prasiddho'mbādirūpaḥ| ekatvāditi ekajātīyatvādityarthaḥ| tathā ekajātīyatvam, yena tatrāpi vinigamakābhāvācchaktikalpanā syāditi bhāvaḥ|
anyatheti ekajātīyatvasya śaktikalpanāyāmatantratve śaktimattvāviśeṣāt sādhunāmivāpabhraṃśānamapi kośādau pratīpādanaṃ prasajyetetyarthaḥ| tanmatenaivoti te sādhuṣvanumānenetyādyuktaharimatenaivetyarthaḥ| apabhraṃśād bodhasyoktarītyopapāditatvāt tatra śaktikalpanaṃ svāgrāhakamūlakamityāśaṅkāṃ nirācikīrṣustatroktaprakārāsambhavaṃ darśayati-ayambhāva iti| tāniti sādhuśabdānityarthaḥ| ajñāne hetumāha- pāmarāṇāmiti| bodhāditi vyāvahāreṇa teṣāṃ bodhānumānādityarthaḥ|
tathācānadhītaśāstrāṇāṃ teṣāṃ sādhutpaprakārakaśabdajñānāsambhavena na tatra sādhuśabdasmṛtikalpanayā śābdabuddhirupapādayituṃ śakyeti bhāvaḥ| kiñca sādhuśabdasmṛtikalpanā kathañcit sambhavedapi yadi pratyakṣaśrutasādhuśabdātteṣāṃ bodhodayaḥ syāt| sa eva nāstītyāha- teṣāṃ sādhoriti| śaktibhramāditi arthanirūpitāyāḥ sādhuśabdaniṣṭhāyāḥ śakterapabhraṃśebhramādityarthaḥ|
kiṃ bodhakatvarūpaśaktibhramāt tatra bodha upapadyate, uteśvarecchārūpaśaktibhramāt| tatra nādyaḥ kalpa ityāha-bodhakatvasyeti| dvitīye tvāha- īśvareccheti|sanmātreti mātrapadaṃ kṛtsnārthakam| apabhraṃśād bodhasya sarvānubhavacasiddhatvena bodakatveneśvarecchāyā api tatrābādhena tāmādāyāpi na śaktijñānasya bhramatvasampattiḥ| pratīterbhmatve viṣayabādhasyaiva tantratvāditi bhāvaḥ|
nanu lākṣaṇika ivāpabhraṃśe'pi vācakavyavahārābhāvena tad‌vyāvṛttyeśvarecchārūpaśaklestatra bhrame bādhakābhāvo'ta āha--śakteriti|  bhramāsambhavācceti tatpadaviśeṣyakatadarthabodhajanakatvaprakārakecchāyāstatpadaviśeṣyakatvena gṛhītāyā padāntaraviśeṣyakatvāsambhavena tad‌bhramamāsambhavādityarthaḥ| yathāśrutābhiprāyeṇedam| pariṣkṛtaviśeṣyatvarūpāyāstatsambandhāvacchinnāvacchedakatvarūpāyāḥ padārthāntararūpāyā vā śaktestatra bhrame bādhakābhāvāditi bodhyam| tathāca bhrameṇāpi bodhasya durūpapādatvena bhāṣāyāṃ śaktirāvaśiyaketi bhāvaḥ|
asminnapi pakṣe harisammatimādarsayati-uktañceti| pāramparyyāditi svārthe ṣyañ‌| viguṇeṣu iti hetugarbha viśeṣaṇam| vaiguṇyañca karaṇāpādavarūpam, abhidhātṛvaiguṇayasyāpabhraṃśaniyāmakatvāt| tathāca viguṇeṣvabhidhātṛṣu satsuparamparayā yeṣvartheṣu prasiddhimāgatā ityarthaḥ| daivīti saṃskṛtarūpetyarthaḥ| aśakteḥ-karaṇāpāṭavavadbhiḥ vyavakīrṇā-ucchinnānupūrvokā kṛtetyarthaḥ| anityadarśinām śabdanityatvavādinām| asmin‌vāde-śaktivivāde ityarthaḥ|
nanu `teṣāṃ sādhuravācakaḥ' ityanupapannam, yasminnarthe'pabhraṃśāḥ prayujyante tadvācakatvasya sādhuṣu sarvasammatatvādato vyācaṣṭe-abodaka iti vyavahitaḥ sadhurna tatra bodhakaḥ, kintu sākṣādapabhraṃśā eva tattadānupūrvosattvena vācakā iti bhāvaḥ|
nanviya apabhraṃśarūpā vāg‌ abhidhātṛbaiguṇyenocchinnānupūrvokā'pi dairva saṃskṛtarūpaiva| naiyāyikānāṃ tvasyā abodha katve bhrama eva| pāramparyyādityupapādanāt sādhutādātmyabhrāntimūlakaśaktibhrameṇa bodhakatvasambhavādityrathakam `daivīvāgavyabakīrṇeyam, ityapi pūrvoktāntimakalpasyaivopodvalakam| nacaivaṃ `teṣāṃ sādhuravācak' ityanupapannam, tatkalpe āropitasādhutvākādyapabhraṃśānāṃ bodhakatvena sādhusmaraṇād bodhānabhyupagamāt| teṣāmityādeḥ sādhutādātmyāropaviṣayāsta eva prasiddhārthānāṃ bodhakāḥ, natu tattadānupūrvomattvena bodhakā ityarthāttasmānnoktayuktyā'pabhraṃśānāṃ tattvena vācakatāsiddhirata āha-kiñceti| tāsām-bhāṣāṇām| doṣa iti tathāca gauravāt tatra śaktirityarthaḥ|
sakaladeśāśiṣṭaparigṛhītatvameva vinigamakamata āha-avacchediketi bodhajanakatā'vacchediketyarthaḥ tayoriti sādhvāpabhraṃśayorityarthaḥ| sakalaśiṣṭaparigṛhītatvantu na vinigamakam, "śavatirgatikarmā kambojeṣu prayujyate| vikāre evainamāryyā bhāṣānte" iti bhāṣyāttattaddeśaniyatasaṃskṛteṣu śaktisiddhayānāpatteḥ, vācakasya vyañjakatāyāmālaṅkārikāṇāṃ prākṛtabhāṣodāharaṇasyāsaṅgatatvāpatteśca| ata eva tattaddeśīyaśiṣṭānāṃ tattadbhāṣāsu nissandigdhaśaktatvapratyayaḥ saṅgacchate| nacāsau bhramaḥ, bodhakābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ| upasaṃharati-tathāceti| evam-apabhraṃśānāmapi śaktatve teṣām-apabhraṃśānām| sādhutā syādati śaktatvasyaiva sādhutvāditi bhāvaḥ| sādhubhirbhāṣitatvamiti sādhūneva prayuñjatetyādirūpaścetyrathaḥ| nāsādhubhiriti "nāpamraṃśitavai na mlecchitavai" ityādiniṣedhāvidhirityartha-| yogyatvam-janakatāvcachedakadharmavattvam| tatra-sāduśabde|jātiriti
tatra pramāṇantu "ekaḥ śabdaḥ sāmyagā jñātaḥ śāstrānvitaḥ suprayuktaḥ svarge loke ca kāmadhug‌ bhavati" iti "eka pūrvaparayoḥ"  ityatra bhāṣyapaṭhītaśrutiḥ| samyag‌ jñātaḥ-sādhutvena jñātaḥ| `yadiha pariniṣṭhinataṃ tat sādhu' ityarthāpattilabhyavākyena ca tat| śāstrānvitiḥ-śāstravyutpādanamārgeṇabhisaṃhitaḥ| suprayukta--śikṣadyuktamārgeṇa prayukta iti tadarthaḥ, "te he'laya iti kurvantaḥ parābabhūvuḥ" iti śrutiśca| yadi ca katvādinā sāṅkaryyānna tasya jātitvamiti vibhāvyate tadāstupādhiriti bhāvaḥ|
tajjñāpakamiti tadabhivyañjakamityarthaḥ| yathaitattathoktam| vyākaraṇādipadācchiṣṭaprayuktatvaparigrahaḥ| evameveti tattadarthapūraskārema tattacchabdānāṃ sādhutvaryyavasānalābhenaivetyarthaḥ| phalājanakatvavaditi kṣatriyādhikārikṛtasyaiva tasya phalaśravaṇāditi bhāvaḥ| sādhutvamiti gavādyarthapūraskāreṇaiva gavādiśabdānāṃ prayoge puṇyanajakatvarūpaṃ sādhutvamityarthaḥ| nanvādhunikadevadattādyarthapūraskāreṇadevadattādiśabdānāṃ śāstre'vyutpādanāt teṣāṃ devadattādau sādhutā na syādata āha-ādhuniketi| dvayakṣaramiti "ghoṣavadādyantarantaḥsthaṃ dvayakṣaraṃ caturakṣaraṃ vā pūruṣasya nāma kṛtaṃ kuryyānnataddhitam' iti bhāṣyasthasmṛtyetyarthaḥ| tattaddeśabhāṣā'nusāreṇa kriyamāṇānāṃ nāmnāṃ tu śāstrāvyutpannatvādasādhutvameveti bhāvaḥ| gauṇānāmiti śuklādiśabdānāmityarthaḥ| guṇe-svaśakye śuklādirūpe|
vyatpādanāditi "guṇavacanebhyo matupo luk‌" iti smṛtyā "guṇe śuklādayaḥ puṃsi" ityādikośena cetyatyādiḥ| tatpuraskāreṇa-nīlādyarthapuraskāremetyarthaḥ pravṛttau-guṇini prayoge| śakyārtasya bādhe anvayānupapattipratisandhānāttairlakṣyārthopasthāpane'pi śakyārthamādāya teṣāṃ sādutvaṃ nānupapannam| śakyārthapuraskāreṇa taṣāṃ śāstravyuttpannatvādilyarthaḥ| gauṇapadasya nirūḍhalākṣaṇikapadopalakṣaṇatvācca na gaṅgāyaṃ ghoṣa ityādau gaṅgādipadānāmasādhutvamiti bhāvaḥ| ādhunikānāmiti ecchikayatkiñcitsambandhena śāstrakṛt‌tātparyāviṣaye prayuktānāmityarthaḥ  nanu śaktigrāhakayoḥ kośavyākaraṇayārvidyamānatvācchaktigrahopāyakathanaṃ vyarthamata āha atirikteti kośavyākaraṇābhyāmatirikto yaḥ śaktigrahopāyastamityarthaḥ| tathācānadhītavyākaraṇānāṃ tena śaktigrahasambhavāt tadarthaṃ vyavahārarūpamapyupāyamāhetiparyyasānānna vaiyarthyamiti bhāvaḥ| etena granthakṛtātiriktaśakteranaṅgīkāreṇa tad‌grahopāyapradarśanaṃ vyarthamityāśaṅkāpi samāhitā| viṣayapadādhyāhāreṇa mūlaṃ vyācaṣṭe-sambando viṣaya iti|
tathācāyametatsambandhīti vyavahāre sambandho viṣayaḥ| yogyatāṃ prati--idamatra yogyamiti yogyatāvyavahāre yogyatāviṣayoḥ yato'taḥ samayāt tad‌vyavahārādyogyatāyā bodhakatārūpaśakteḥ saṃvit‌-jñānaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ|
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥmātāpūtrādiyogavaditi yathā'yametat sambandhīti vyavahārastayorjanyajanakabhāvaniścāyakastadvadityarthaḥ|
anye tvidaṃ haripadyamittthaṃ vyācakṣate-sambandiśabde-ayametat‌sambandhīti vyavahāre| yogyatāṃmiti-arthabodhajanakatā'vacchedakadharmavattvarūpayogyatāvyavahāranirūpito yastādātmyalakṣaṇo'nyo vā sambandhaḥ sa eva yogyatā'paraparyyāyo viṣaya iti śeṣaḥ| sa cānādiḥ, vṛddhavyavahārāparaparyyāyo'smadādisamayānniścīyate, natu bodajanakatvameva śaktiḥ, etad‌bodajanane'yaṃ yogya iti vyavahārāt| ata evaitaccheṣe-
sati pratyayahetutvaṃ sambandha upapadyate|
śabdasyārthe yatastasmāt sambandho'stīti gamyate||
ityantenoklārthaḥ spaṣṭīkṛta iti| adhikamanyato'vadhārtham 
iti vaiyākaraṇa bhūṣaṇasāradarpaṇe śaktisvarūpanirūpaṇam|| 6 ||
 `samāse khalu bhinnaiva' ityatra paṅkajaśabdavaditi vadatā samse tad‌ghaṭakapratyekaśaktisahakṛtasamudāyaśaktervyapekṣātvamaṅgīkṛtam| taccānupapannam, yatra samāsaghaṭakapratyekapadasyānarthakatvaṃ tatra pratyekaśaktisācivyāsambhavāt| yathā nañcasamāsaghaṭakanañaḥ|
tayorarthābhidhāne hi vyāpāro naiva vidyate| ityukteḥ|
evañca jahatsvārthāvāda ivedṛsasthale uttarapadārthādiprādhānyasya svarasato'nupapattirapītīmāṃ śaṅkāṃ pariharttu nañarthanisūpaṇamityāha-nañarthamiti| mule nañ‌samāsa iti|
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ-`ghaṭo na paṭaḥ, `ghaṭo nāsti' ityādāvabhāvaśābdabodhasya sārvajanīnaprasiddhasiddhitvānānaño'bhāvārthakatvamāvasyakam| tathācottarapādthaprādhānyadivyavasthā sūbhā| yadyapi `ghaṭo na' ityādāvabhāvaviśeṣyaka eva bodhaḥ, tathāpi `asarvaḥ' ityādyanurodenāropitatvārthakatvamapi naño'ṅgīkaraṇīyamiti śeṣapūraṇena vyācṣṭe sāre- sidhyatīti ata eveti prādhānyānupapatterevetyarthaḥ|
nanu nañastatrābhāvārthakatve kathaṃ prādhānyanupapattirityatobhāvārthamāha-ayambhāva iti| anyathā āropitatvārthakatvānabhyupagame, bhedārthakatva iti yāvat| sā-sarvanāmasaṃjñā| na syāditi `asarvaḥ' ityatra naño'bhedārthakatve tatra pratiyogitayā sarvapadārthasyopasarjanatvena saṃjñopasarjanānāṃ sarvādibahirbhūtatvena atisarva' ityādāviva tadapravṛtteriti bhāvaḥ| nanu `bhedapratiyogī sarvaḥ' iti tatra bodhābhyugapamānnottarapadārthasya prādhānyahānirata āha-ghaṭo nāstītyādāviti tasya nañarthasya| nañarthābhāvavattvasya viśeṣyatāyāṃ tantrātvāditi bhāvaḥ|
nanu tavāpi `aghaṭaḥ paṭaḥ' ityato `ghaṭabhinna paṭaḥ' iti sarvasiddhapratītyanupapattirata āha-asmadrītyeti saḥ-bhedabodhaḥ| ārtha iti arthāt‌-upasthitiviṣayādāgata ityartha-| mānasa iti tatra śābtvapratyayastvasiddha eveti bhāvaḥ| nanvasarvaśabdasya sarvanāmatvābhāve kā kṣatirata āha-tathāceti atra ceti āropitatvaṃ nañvācyamiti kalpe cetyarthaḥ| niṣakarṣa iti viṣayatvasya saṃsargamaryyādayaiva lābhādananyalabhyāropasyaiva nañarthatvam, natvāropaviṣayatvasya, gauravāditi bhāvaḥ| nanvāropasya vācyatvabhyupagame dyatakatāpratipādakamūlavirodho'ta āha-dyotyatvoktiriti vācyasyāpi tasya viśeṣaṇataiva, nābhāvavadviśeṣyateti sūcayitumeva tathoktiriti bhāvaḥ  ghaṭo nāstīti yadyapi samāsayogyanaña evāropārthakatvaṃma mūle uktam, tathāpi `ghaṭo nāsti' ityuktiratreva tatrāpyāropānanubhava iti dṛṣṭāntārthetvādheyam| `nañadyotyam' ityatra naño viśeṣaṇatayā vyavadhānena copasthitatve'pi tyadādīnāṃ buddhisthaparāmarśakatvāttadā tatparāmarśo nānucita ityāha-tadartha iti| sādhāraṇyeneti prakṛte'rthapadasya tajjanyabodhaviṣayaparatayā pakṣadvayasya saṃgrahaḥ| tatrāsati bādhake vācakatvameva| sati tu tasmin dyotakatā'pīti bhāvaḥ| nañavācyatvāditi
yadyapyuttarapadārthaprādhānyena nañ‌tatpuruṣasya, brāhmaṇasya'nayanaṃ prāpnoti| nañaḥ prayogānnañarthaviśiṣṭasyānayanaṃ bhaviṣyati| kaḥ punasau naña rtho? nivṛttapadārthakaḥ| brāhmaṇadṛṣṭasya gauratvāderdarśanād dṛṣṭopadeśādvā pūrvaṃ brāhmaṇo'yamityadhyavasyati| tataḥ paścādupalabhate-`nāyaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ' iti tato'brahmaṇo'yamiti prayuṅ‌kto| ātaśca tad‌dṛṣṭaguṇadarsanād dṛṣṭopadeśādvā brahmaṇatvādyadhyavasāyapūrvakameva nāyaṃ brāhmaṇa iti jñānam| nahyayaṃ kālavarṇamāpaṇedṛṣṭvā'dhyavasyati brāhmaṇo'yamiti| yathāsthitaṃ vastutattvaṃ tasya nirjñātaṃ bhavati" iti nañsūtrabhāṣyaparyyalocanayā kaiyaṭoktārthasyaiva sādhutvaṃ labhyate| tathāpi tadbhāṣyasya sedapratyakṣe kvacid yo yānupalabdherhetutvapradarśanaparatayaivopapatternaña āropavācakatve tadupaṣṭambhakatvābhāvāt|
anyathā'tyantāsadṛśe uktayuktyā tadāropāsambhavena tatra nañarthastadarthakatvāsambhavāt `aghaṭaḥ padaḥ' ityanāpatteḥ| `pratiyogyabhāvānvayau tulyayogakṣemau' iti tāntrikokte nīlo ghaṭaḥ' ityādau tādātmyena ghaṭāṃśo nīlapadārthabhānānnañ‌samabhivyāhāre tādātmyasambandhāvacchinnanīlābhāvavodhasya sarvānubhavasiddhasya ऩño'bhāvārthakatvaṃ vinā'nupapatternedaṃ rajatamidaṃ rajatamiti vākyajanyabodhayostattdabhāvānavagāhitayā parasparapratibandyapratibandhakabhāvānāpatteśca naño bhedārthakatvamāvasyakam|
nacaṃ naño'bhedārthakatve `abrāhmaṇamānaya' ityādito brāhmaṇabhinnaloṣṭāderapi bodhā''pattiḥ| naceṣṭāpattiḥ, nahmabrāhmaṇamānayetyukte loṣṭamānīya kṛtī bhavati' iti bhāṣyavirodhāditi vātyam, ghaṭatvena ghaṭārthakaghaṭapadāt sannihitavyaktibodhavat prakāraṇādinā brāhmaṇabhinnamātrārthakād brāhmaṇapadāt kṣatriyādereva bodhasambhavāt| tasmādaghaṭo na pada ityādyasamste naño bhede eva śaktiḥ, bhedatvasyākhaṇḍopādhitayā tasya śakyatā'vacchedakatve lāghavāt| samāsaghaṭakasya tasya tu uttarapadasya tadbhinne lakṣaṇāyāṃ tātparyyagrāhakatvameva| naca "na brāhmaṇairetairupadrūtam" ityādau naño bhedārthakatve tatpratiyogyanuyogivācakapadayoḥ samānavibhiktikatvā'nupapattiḥ, sāmānādhikaraṇyābhāvāditi vācyam, pratiyogyanuyoginorabhedānvayabodhaupayikākāṅkṣāyāṃ satyāmeva nañā bhedabodhanena tayoḥ samānavibhaktikatvasya tadupajīvyatvāt| evañca nañā bhedabodhane pratiyagyanuyogivācakapadayoḥ samānavibhiktivacanatvaṃ niyāmakamata eva `bhūtale na ghaṭaḥ' ityādau na bhedabodhaḥ|
dṛśyate ca-"yajatiṣu ye yajāmahaṃ karāti nānuyājeṣu" ityatra nañā paryyudāsabodhaḥ| uktavākye yajatiśabdo yagasvarūpavacanaḥ| tathāca anuyābhinneṣu yāgeṣu ye yajāmahaśabdavanmantraṃ kacoti-uccārayatītyarthaḥ| ata evātrānuyājabhinneṣu yajatiṣu ye yajāmahapadavanmantraprayogarūpaikārthavidhānādekaviveyārthakatvarūpaikavākyatvamupapadyate| anyatā yāgeṣu ye yajāmahapadavanmantravidhaānamanuyājeṣa niṣadhavidhānamiti vidheradvayārthakatvarūpavākyabhedāpatteḥ, ye yajāmahapadānuṣaṅgakalpane gauravācceti bhāvaḥ|
anye tu-śāstravihitapratiṣiddhatvāt, ṣoḍaśigrahaṇāgrahaṇavadvikalpāpatternānuyājeṣvityatra nañaḥ pratiṣedhārthakatvam| tathāhi-kriyāyāḥ kārakasākāṅ‌kṣatvād‌ vinigamanāviraheṇa sakalakārakaviśiṣṭāṃ pratiyogibhūtāṃ bhāvanāmuddiśyabhāvavidhistatra vācyaḥ| evañcoddeśyasya prasidhdyarthe tatprāptirapekṣitā| prāptisāpekṣātvāt pratiṣedhasya| prāptiśca kvacidrāgataḥ, yatā-"na kalañjaṃ bhakṣayet" iti| tatra kalañjabhakṣaṇāderāgata iṣṭasādhanatvāvagatyā prasaktāyāḥ kalañjabhakṣaṇabhāvanāyā niṣedhaśāstreṇa nivṛttiniścayatve'vagate'rthāt tadvi,yakalañjabhakṣaṇasyāniṣṭasādhanatvākṣepāt tato nivṛttiḥ| prakṛte tu ye yajāmahaṅkarotīti śāstrādeva sā| evañca niṣedhasya śāstraviṣayatvena prābalyavad vidhiśāstrasyāpyupajīvyatvena prābalyānniṣedhena vidheratya nyabādhāyogād vikalpa eva prāpnoti|
vikalpe ca "vrīhibhirjuhoti" "yavairvā juhoti" ityādau prathamaṃ vrīhyanuṣṭhāne vedasya viparyyāsamiti janakatvāsambhavādyavaśāstrasyāprāptaprāmāṇyasvīkāraḥ, pratītaprāmāṇyaparityāgaśceti doṣadvayam| prayogāntare yavopādāne yavaśāstrasya svīkṛtāprāmāṇaayaparityāgaḥ, parityaktaprāmāṇyopādānañceti dīṣaṃdvayam| caturthe yavopādāne vrīhiśāstrasyāsvīkṛtāprāmāṇyasvīkāraḥ, svīkṛtāprāmāṇyatyāgaśceti doṣadvayamiti catvāro'nye'pītyaṣṭadoṣavikalpāpattyā nañanuyājapadābhyāmanūyājabhedo bodhyate| so'yaṃ paryyudāsaḥ| asmiṃśca pakṣe ye yajāmaho na karttavyatayā pratīyate, `ya-tiṣu ye yajāmahaṅkaroti' ityanenaiva tadvidhānāt kintu sāmānyaśāstravihitayeyajāmahamanūdya tasyānuyājavyatiriktaviṣayatā bodhyate|
tathāca sāmānyaśāstrasay viseṣavidhisāpekṣatvānnānuyājeṣvityanenānuyājavyatiriktaviṣasamarpaṇādanuyājavyatirikteṣu ye yajāmahaḥ karttavyatayā pratīyate'nuyājeṣu tu naca karttavyatayā prāptaḥ, nāpi pratiṣiddhaḥ sa iti na vikalpaḥ| naca nañā niṣedhabodhane'pi padaśāstreṇā''havanīyasāstrasyeva nānuyājeṣviti viśeṣaśāstreṇa yajatiṣu ye yajāmahaṅkarotīti sāmānyaśāstrasya bādhānna vikalpa iti vācyam,
parasparanirapekṣatorhi bādhyabādakabhāvaḥ padaśāstreṇ''havanīyaśāstrasyānapekṣaṇāt sa ucitaḥ| niṣeśāstrasya tu pratiyogiprasiddhayarthamastyapekṣā vidhyarthasya| "prasaktaṃ hi pratiṣiddhayate' iti nyāyena na tad‌bādhaḥ, asambhavāt| kintūktayuktayā vikalpa eva| tathāca dviradṛṣkalpanāprasaṅgo'pi| vidherhyevaṃ jñāyate| yadanuyājeṣu ye yajāmahakaraṇe na kaścanopakāro bhavati niṣedhācca tadakaraṇādanṛtavadanākaraṇādiva darśapūrṇamāsayoḥ sa copakāro'dṛṣṭarūpa evetyato'pi vikalpo na yuktaṃ ityāhuḥ|
atra kecit-niṣedhaśāstrānālocanena sāmānyaśāstrālocanamātreṇa bhramātmakapratiyogiprasiddhayābhāvavodhanasambhavena vikalpāprasakteḥ, viśṣaniṣedhe sāmānyavidhestaditaraparatvasya `brāhmaṇebhyo dadhi dātavyam' kauṇjinyāya na dātavyam' ityādau vyutpattisiddhatvācca|
naca tatrāpi paryyudāsa eva nañā bodhyate, nañā paryyudāsabodhena viśiṣyaviśeṣaṇavācakadayoḥ samānavibhaktitvāsyeva tayoḥ samānavacanasyapi tantratvāt, tasya prakṛte'bhāvāt| anyathā na mahato rājña ityādāviva, na mahatāṃ rājña ityādāvapi mahadbhedapratīyāpattiḥ| eko na dvāvitivat kathañcit kathañcit samarthane'pi dvitīyasya dātavyamityasya nairarthakyaprasaṅgaśca, `bāhmaṇebhyo dadhi dātavyaṃ kauṇiḍinyabhinnabrāhmaṇoddeśyakadadhidānabodhasambhavādityāhuḥ|
vikalpaprasaktirūpadoṣabhāve'pi vākyabhedā''patterjāgarūkatayā samānavibhaktikatvādestad‌bodhaniyāmakasya satt‌ve, ca nañaḥ paryyudāsādthakatvameva, nānuyājeṣvityatrocitamiti tu pare|
prakṛtamanusarāmaḥ-anyatheti lāghavānādareṇā''ropitatvasya nañ‌vācyatvābhyupagame ityarthaḥ| sādṛśyādeḥ ityādinā paṭhiṣyamāṇālpatvādeḥ saṅgrahaḥ| `abrāhmaṇasadṛśaḥ, pāpābhāvaḥ, aśvabhinna, alpodarā, gavāśvetarāḥ paśavo'praśastāḥ, dharmaviruddha ityādikrameṇārthaḥ| nanu sādṛśayāderavācyatve kathaṃ tatastad‌bodho'ta āha-tattviti tatpaṭhanaṃ tvityarthaḥ| abhipretyeti bhedapratiyogitvaprakārakabrāhmaṇādibodhānantarabhāvimānasabodhamabhipretyetyarthaḥ|
brāhmaṇādibodhasya tattatpadādeva sambhavādbhedapratiyogitvena brāhmaṇādibodhanāya yadabrāhmaṇādipadopādānaṃ tatsādṛśyādinā kṣatriyādibodanārthamityūhamūlakakṣatriyādibodhasya bhedapratiyogī brāhmaṇa ityādiśakyārthabodhottarameva sambhavāditi bhāvaḥ| tad‌bodhe śābdatvapratyayasyānubhāvāllakṣaṇayaiva sādṛśyādinā brāhmaṇādibodha iti tvanye| viśeṣaṇasya viśeṣyasākāṅkṣatvādāha-pratiyoginīti evañca `abrāhyaṇaḥ' ityādaiḥ pratiyogitayā bhedaviśiṣṭo brāhmaṇa ityādyākārako bodha iti bhāvaḥ| bhedasya pratiyogitayottarapadārthānvaye upapattimāha-tathāceti|
sarvanāmatā cetyādi-prathamāntacuṣṭayasya `upapadyate' ityanenānvayaḥ| sarvanāmatā-sarvanāmasaṃjñā| tadantavidhineti śeṣaḥ| anyathā saṃjñopasarjanānāṃ sarvaditvābhāve tadrathasya nañrathaviśeṣaṇatve tatasaṃjñānupapattiriti bhāvaḥ| anekam ityādāvekabhinnamiti bodhe svīkriyamāṇe ekapratiyogikabhedavati dvitvasya bahutvasya vā'vaśyambhāvena tatastad‌bodhakadvivacanabahuvacanayorāpattiḥ, ekatvārthas tasya tatra bādhāttad‌bodhakaikavacanasyānupapattiśceti bhāvaḥ|
tatpuruṣatvamiti bhedeviśeṣaṇatve tadanupapattiḥ syāt| pūruṣeti tavibāditrikasaṅketitapuruṣetyartha-| vacaneti ekavacanatvādirūpetyarthaḥ| "ayuvāṃ bhavatha" ityādau yuṣmadasmadarthābhāvaviśeṣyakabodhe abhave dvitvādyarthasya bādhād dvivacanādyanupapattiḥ| `bhāvanā|ṭanvayinyeva saṅkhayā'nvayaḥ' iti tvadabhyupakamenānyārthagatasaṅkhayāmādāya dvivacanādyupapādanasya kartumaśakyatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
anyatheti nañarthābhāvasyottarapadārthaviśeṣaṇatve tadviśeṣyatva iti yāvat| pūruṣavyavastheti "yuṣmdyupapade"  iti sūtradvayāviṣayatayā "śeṣe prathamaḥ" itayasya pravṛttyā prathamapurūṣasyaivāpatterityarthaḥ| nānupapannamiti| tathācoktavyavasthā sulabheti bhāvaḥ| nanu nañarthabhedasya pratiyogitayottarapadārthānvaye 'aghaṭamānaya' ityātra svabhedapratiyogitvasya ghaṭe sattvāttad‌bodhānantaraṃ ghaṭānayasyāpyāpattirata āha-mūle-viśeṣyo vetiḥ| viśeṣaṇākāṅkṣāyāmāha-sāre pratiyoginīti uttarapadārtharūpapratiyoginītyarthaḥ|
nirūpitatvaṃ saptamyraḥ| uttarapādārtharūpapratiyoginiṣṭhaviśesaṇatānirūpitaviśeṣyatāśrayo nañartha iti yāvat| nanu tarhi uttarapadārthāprādhānye pūrvoktadūṣaṇagaṇo duṣpariharo'pi āha-ayambhāva iti| gauṇatve'pīti nañ‌samāsaghaṭakaitadādyarthasya nañarthaviśeṣaṇatve'pītyarthaḥ| jñāpakāditi| tatsūtrasthānañsamāsagrahaṇājjñāpakādityrathaḥ| jñāpakatāmeva prapañcayati-asaḥ caśiva iti|
"etattadoḥ"  ityanena parasparasāhacaryyāt sarvanāmaitattadbhāyāṃ parasya sorhali lopo vidhīyate| nañtatpuruṣānātargataitattadorupasarjanatayā sarvanāmatvābhāvādenānatiprasaṅge vyarthaṃ sadanañsamāsagrahaṇaṃ nañsamāse guṇībhītārthasyāpi sarvādeḥ sarvanāmatājñāpanena caritārthamiti bhāvaḥ| sāhacaryyajñāpakatvasyāsārvatrikatvaṃ sūcayan sūtre sarvanāmnoreva grahaṇe mānāntaramāha-
akoriti asarvanāgnaḥ sarvanāmavihitākajasambhavena tatparyyudāsāt sarvanāmnoreva tayorgrahaṇaṃ niścīyata ityarthaḥ| etatkalpe etattaduttarasya sorhali iti sūtrārthaḥ| arthadvārakasambandhāśrayaṇena vyākhyānaṃ tu pūrvakalpābhiprāyoṇottarapadalakṣaṇābhiprāyeṇa veti bhāvaḥ| viśeṣyānurodhāditi| asati viśeṣyanuśāsane viśeṣaṇavācakapadasya viśeṣyavācakapadasamānavacanatvaniyamāditi bhāvaḥ|
nanu viśeṣaṇasya viśeṣyasaṅkhayāviruddhasaṃkhyāvattve'pi viśeṣaṇavācakapadāttadvacanamityatra pramāṇābhāvo'ta āha-kiñceti| nanvenekaśabdopādānādeva dvayorbahūnāṃ vā tatsiddherddhiva canabahuvacanayorapi samayaparipālanārthatvasambhavādekavacanopādānaṃ vyarathamevetyata āha-autsagikaṃ veti| "ekavacanamutsargataḥ kariṣyate" iti, "na kevalāḥ" iti niyamamūlavacanavihitamityarthaḥ| sati tātparye kvacita, sārthakamapyekavacanamiṣyata evetyāha-pratyekamiti ekabhinnatvenopasthitāsu yoṣāsu pratyekaṃ sevanānvayabodhāyetyarthaḥ| upasaṃharati-natviti| ata eveti anekaśabdasyaikabhinnārthakatvādetyarthaḥ|
sūpapādamiti| bhedapratiyogyekārthakatve tu durūpapādaṃ syāt, ekaśeṣeṇa samarthanaṃ tvagatikagatiriti bhāvaḥ| bhāṣyodāhṛte "naikastiṣṭhati' iti prayoge `janaḥ' ityadhyāhāryyam| pratyekaṃ sthityanvayabodhārthañca tatraikavacanam| `anekojanaḥ' iti tvasādhveva| uttarapadārthaprādānyādivyapadeśastu prāyika ityādyapi bodhyam| `atvaṃ bhavasi' ityādinā `anahambhavāmi' ityādirhṛhyate| yuṣmada ityasmado|ṭapyupalakṣaṇam| evam `tvadbhinnaḥ' ityatrāpi lakṣaṇoti tu naiyāyikādyanusāreṇa, svamate tadanabhyupagamāt| `atvambhavasi' ityādāvāropādibodhasya sarvānubavaviruddhatvānnatrsamāse sarvatrottarapadasya svār‌thabhinnalākṣaṇikatvameva yuktamiti bhāvaḥ|
prasajyapratiṣedhārthakanaño vyavasthāmāha-evaṃ na tvaṃ pacasītyādi natrarthe yuṣamadarthānvaye tu prakṛte puruṣavyavasthā na sid‌dhyediti sūcayituṃ pacasīti madhyamapūruṣanirddeśaḥ| adhikaṃ tvādāvevokm| idamupalakṣaṇam-`ghaṭau nastaḥ' ityādau dvivacanādyanupapatte| kriyāpratiyogikābhāvaviśeṣyakabodhe hetumāha-asamasteti vyākhyānāt nañaḥ tadarthasya prasajyapratiṣedhasya tādṛśanañarthābhāvaniṣṭhaviśeṣyatānirūpitaprakāratāsambandhena bodhe dhātujanyopasthiterbhāvanātvāvacchinnaviśeṣyatāsambandena hetutvasya pūrvamuktatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
nanvabhāvasya kena rūpeṇa śakyatā| na tāvadabhāvatvena| `caitro na pacati' bhedabodhāpatteḥ| prasajyapratiṣedhatvena śakyatve'pi sa eva doṣaḥ, bhedasyāpi yogyānupalabdhijanyapratyakṣaviṣayatvāt| tadanupasthitāvapi naño'bhāvabodhāccetyata āha-sa ceti abhāvaścetyarthaḥ| tathāca naño'nyonyābhāvatvenātyantābhāvatvena vā'bhāve śaktikalpanānna doṣaḥ|
atyantābhāvatveti antam-svapratiyoginiṣṭhābhāvapratiyogitvamatikrāntaḥ-vyabhicarito'tyantaḥ| sa cāsāvabhāvaśceti vigrahaḥ| tasya bhāvastattvam| svapratiyoginiṣṭhasvasadṛśābhāvapratiyogitvavyabhicāryabhāvatvamiti yāvat| `anto jaghanyaṃ caramam' ityamareṇāntaśabdasya jaghanye vṛttibodhanāt jaghanyatvañcoktārthasya jaghanavṛttibodhyatvāt| ghaṭātyantābhāvasya svapratiyoginiṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitvābhāvavati ghaṭavṛttidharme sattvāttattvaṃ tatrākṣatam| ghaṭānyonyābhāvasya tu svapratiyoginiṣṭhānyonyābhāvapratiyogitvavyāpakatvāttad‌vyāvṛttiḥ| vyutpattipradarśanaṃ cedam| lakṣaṇaṃ tvatyantābhāvasya tādātmyetarasambandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākābhāvatvam| anyonyābhāveti anyonyasmim̐stādātmyenābhāvaḥ-abhavanamiti vyutpattyā tādātmyasambandāvacichinnapratiyogitākābhāvatvamanyonyābhāvatvam| tābhyāṃ śakya ityarthaḥ|
vastutastu atyantābāvasyābhāvatvenaiva śakyatā, natūktarūpeṇa, gauravāt, tena rūpeṇa bodhasyānanubhavācca| ata eva "tatsādṛśyamabhāvaśca" ityatrābhāvatvenaivātyantābhāvopādānam|
naca naña saṃyogādisambandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākābhāvatvādinā'bhāve śaktayanabhyupagame `bhūtale na ghaṭaḥ" iti vākyajabodhasya saṃyogasambandhena bhūtalaviśeṣyakaghaṭaviśiṣṭabuddheḥvirodhitvānāpattistatsaṃsargāvacchinnātadviśiṣṭabuddhau abhāvāṃśe tatsaṃsargāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvāvagāhinyā eva tadabhāvabuddheḥ pratibandakatvāditi vācyam,
nirūktābhāvatvena śaktāvapi saṃyogādīnāṃ lakṣaṇe tādātmyetarasambandhatvenānugataparūpaṇaiva niveśnīyatayā nañpadaśaktayā'pyabhāve viśiṣyasaṃyogāvacichinnapratiyogitākatvabhānāsambhavāt, pratibandhakatvānupapattitādavasthyāt| tādṛśaviśiṣṭabuddhivirodhitvānurodhenākāṅśrābalādeva tādṛśapratiyogitākatvasya viśiṣyapratiyogyabhāvayoḥ sambandhavidhayā bhānasyopagantavyatvāt|
nanvanyonyābhāvasādhāraṇābhāvatvasya nañpadapravṛttinimittatve'nyonyābhāve ta6 pṛthak‌śaktikalpanānarthakyam| nacā'nyonyābhāvatvaprakārakabodhānurodhāttasyāpi śakyatāvacchedakatvakalpanamāvaśyakam, anyathā tādātmyasambandhena pratiyogibuddhivirodhitvāt| ghaṭābhāvavāniti buddherapi tad‌virodhitvāpatteśceti vācyam,
tatrābhāvatvasya nañpadāśaktyā tādātmyasambandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvasya samānavibhaktikapratiyogyanuyogivācakapadayoḥ samabhivyāhārarūpākāṅśrābalena lābhena tādātmyasambandhāvacchinnapratigitākābhāvatvarūpānyo'nyābhāvatvāvagāhitayaiva tattad‌buddheḥ pratibandhakatvopapattau tena rūpeṇa śaktikalpanasyākiñcitkaratvāt|
naca nāstyeva tadavacchinne śaktiḥ, pṛthagupādānaṃ tvabhāvasāmānyasya nañarthatvālābhāyaiveti vācyam, tathā sati "nañarthāḥ ṣaṭ‌ prakirtitāḥ" ityasyāsaṅgatyāpatteriti cet, syādeva, yadyanyonyābhāvatvam-tādātmyasambandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākābhāvatvarūpaṃ nirvaktuṃ śakyeta, tadeva na, saṃyogitādātmyasya saṃyogarūpatayā saṃyogātyantābhāve'tivyāpteḥ| sambandhavidhayā tādātmyaniṣṭhāvacchedakatāniveśe'pi saṃyogasambandhāvacchinnasaṃyogadharmavacchinnapratiyogitākātyantābhāvātivyāptitādavasthāt| tādātmyatvāvacchinnatādātmyasaṃsargatāṃ niveśya pariṣkṛtasya tasya vācyatāvacchedakatve tu `ghaṭastādātmyena na paṭaḥ' iti prayogānupapattiḥ| evañca tādātmyasyānyonyābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakasambanadhatvamapi prāmāṇikam| iha saṃyogena ghaṭo nāstītivat `ayaṃ tādātmyenana ghaṭaḥ ityādipratyayāt| evañca tādṛśānuyogitāviśeṣaprakāratākatvamevābhedasambandhena pratiyogiprakārakadhīvirodhitve tantramiti tadanurodhana nañaḥ pṛthaganyonyābhāve śaktirāvaśiyaketi|
nanu naño'viśeṣaṇātyantābhāvabodhakatve `bhetale na ghaṭaḥ' ityādivākyajabodhe'tyantābhāvabhānāpattiḥ| naca nañpadajanyātyantābhāvabodhe'nuyogivācakapadottarasaptamīsamabhivāhārajñānasya hetutvānnoktāpattiriti vācyam, `caitro na pacati' ityādivākyajabhāvanātyantabhāvabodhe vyabhicāreṇoktakāryakāraṇabhāvasyaivāsambhavāditi cet,
atra vadan‌ti-yatra nañsamabhivyāhāre pratiyogyanuyoginorādhārādheyabhāvacasaṃsargabodhaupayikākāṅkṣā tatraiva naño'tyantābhāvārthakatvopagamena noktastale tad‌bodhāpattiḥ, ta6 nañsamabhivyāhāre tādṛśabodhaupayikākāṅśrāvirahāt| `na pacati caitraḥ, `nedaṃ caitrasya' ityādau nañsamabhivyāhāre tiṅarthasya kṛtyādeḥ prathamāntapadopasthāpitacaitrādau ṣaṣṭhayarthasvatvasyedaṃpadārthe ādhārādheyabhāvabodhaupayikaprathamāntapadādisambhivyāhārarūpākāṅkṣāyāḥ sattvena tatra nañā kṛtyādyabhāvabodhanasambhavāt|
evañca yatra pratiyogyanuyoginorādhārādheyabhāvabodhaḥ saptamīṃ vinā'nupapannastatrānuyogivācakapadāt saptamyapyapekṣyate| yathā-`bhūlate na ghaṭaḥ' ityādau| taduktaṃ dīdhitikṛtā-"vastuto na pacati caitro nedaṃ maitrasyetyādau vibhaktayarthakṛtisambandhādebhāvasya bodhane nañā sapmī nāpekṣyate, apekṣyate ca prātipadikārthasyeti" iti|
naca caitro na pacatītyādau nañākhyātārthabhāvanābhāvabodhane caitrau na pacataścaitrā na pacantītyādau dvivacanād‌yanupapattiḥ, bhavanmate bhāvanā'nvayini saṅkhayānvayaniyamena bhāvanānvayinyabhāve dvitvād‌yabhāvena dvivacanādinā tasya bodhayitumaśakyatvāt| vaiyākaraṇamate tu tatra dhātvarthakriyābhāvasyaiva nañā bodhanena dvitvādiviśiṣṭacaitrakartṛkakriyābhāva iti bodhābhyupagamena tādṛśānupapattyabhāvāditi vācyam,
nañsamabhivyāhāre bhāvanābhāvānvayini tiṅarthasaṅkhayānvaya iti vyutpattyantarābhyupagamena dvivacanād‌yupapatteḥ| tvanmate'pi yaccaitrakartṛkaḥ pāko'prasiddhastatra caitro na pacatīti prayogānupapattyā kriyāpratiyogikābhāvasya nañā pratyayitumaśakyatvācca|
naca tatra nañā bheda eva bodhyate| pratiyogitayā caitrādiviśiṣṭasya tasyā''khyātārthakarttaryyevānvayānna tādṛsaprayogānupapattiriti vācyam, tathā sati pratiyogitāsambandhāvacchinnadhātvrathaprakārakabodhe asamastanañpadajanyopasthiterhetutvasyātraiva vyabhicāreṇa tvatsiddhāntavyāghātāt| nañsamabhivyāhāre ākhyātārthabhāvanānvayyarthakatvavat tatsamabhivyāhāre bhāvanānvayyarthakatvarūpasāmānādhikaraṇyasya yuṣmadādau sattvānna na tvaṃ pacasītyādau madhyamādipuruṣānupapattiḥ|
yadvā nañsamabhivyāhāre svabodhakartṛtvabodhakatvam| tatsamabhivyāhāre tu bhūtapūrvaṃ tadādāya puruṣopapattiriti| idaṃ tvavadheyam-`nañsamabhivyāhāre pratiyogyanuyoginorthaḥ sambandho bhāsate tatsamabhivyāhāre tatsambandhāvacchinnatadabhāvastadanuyogini bhāsate' iti vyutpatterna bhūtale na ghaṭa ityādāvanyonyābhāvabodhastanniyāmakaṃ tvādāvevoktam| parantu ghaṭatvādiviśiṣṭopasthāpakapadasamabhivyāhṛtanañādipadasya ghaṭatvāvacchinnapratiyogitāsambandhena ghaṭaprakārakābhāvaśābdatvasya, tathā nīlādisākāṅśraghaṭād‌yupasthāpakapadasamabhivyāhṛtanañpadasya nīlaghaṭatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvasambandhena nīlaghaṭādiprakārakaśābdavuddhitvasya ca kāryyatāvacchedakatvopagamānna nīlaghaṭo na ghaṭa ityādiprayogaprasaktiḥ, bhavati ca pīto ghaṭo na nīlaghaṭa ityādiprayogopapattirityād‌yanyato'vadhāryyamityāśayavānāha-ityādyanyatra iti 
iti vaiyākaraṇabhūṣaṇasāradarpaṇe nañarthanirūpaṇam || 7 ||

 prasaṅgikaṃ nīpātopasargārthanirūpaṇamityāśayena mūlamavatārayati-prādayo dyotakā iti dyotakatārūpaśaktimanta ityarthaḥ| ata eva `prajati' ityādau na prakarṣadyarthasya viśeṣyateti bhāvaḥ| matāntare tu tātparyyagrāhakatvaṃ dyotakatvaṃ vakṣyati| tatra yenetyatra yacchabdo nottarārddhapaṭhitahetumātraparāmarśakaḥ, kintu hetvantaraparāmarśako'pi ityāha-ayaṃ bhāva iti| na dhātvartha iti na tatprayogāntargatadhātuśakya ityarthaḥ| āpatteriti anubhavādipratītyāpatterityarthaḥ| nopasargārtha iti na taddhātusamabhivyāhṛtaprādiśakya ityarthaḥ| ananvayāpatteriti etacchānubhavādimātrapratītimabhyupetya| vastutastathāsvīkāre'kriyārthakatvena anubhavāderdhātutvānāpatteriti bodhyam|
nanvanubhavārthakatve'pi tasya phalasthānīyatvena tadanvayivyāpārasāmānyārthakānvāderna dhātutvānupapattirata āha-anugacchatītyādāviti atrānoranūbhavārthakatve tadanurodhena gamerapi vyāpārasāmānyārthakatāyā evābhyupeyatayā tathā pratītirthurvārā syādityarthaḥ|
vastutastu saṃyogāvaruddhavyāpāre'nvartānubhavasya bādhenānvayāsambhavānnānoranubhavārthakatvasambhāvaneti bhāvaḥ| gauravāditi viśiṣṭānupūrvyāḥ śaktātāvacchedakatve gauravādityarthaḥ| tathāceti dhātūpasargaviśiṣṭānāṃ pratyekaṃ pratīyamānānubhavārthapratipādakatvāsambhavādatiti samuditārthaḥ| vid‌yamānatvādītyādinotpattirgṛhyate| lakṣametyasyānubhavādirūpe'rthe iti śeṣaḥ| lakṣaṇākalpikāyāstatparyyānupapattestātparyyagrahādhīnatayā tasya copasargasamabhivyāhārād‌yāyatatvādityarthaḥ|
nanvetāvatopasargasya tātparyyagrāhakatvameva samāyātam, na d‌yotakatvamata āha-tathāceti tātparyyagrāhakatvamevetyevakārema d‌yotakatvasya vṛttyantaratvavyavacchedaḥ| anubhavādiphale dhātostātparyyagrāhakatvasyopasargasamabhivyāhārādhīnatvāt "upasargāḥ kriyāyoge"  ityatrāpi kriyāviśeṣaṇībhūtārthatātparyyagrāhakatvamevopasargāḥ kriyāyoge ityanena vivikṣitamiti bhāvaḥ| etattattvamagre vakṣyate| tatreti d‌yotakatva ityarthaḥ| sakarmakatvasyetyanāpatterityanenānvitam|
nanu māstu sakarmakatvamata āha-tata iti| upopasṛṣṭās‌dhātorityarthaḥ| tathācopāsyate rahirityādiprayogānupapattiriti bhāvaḥ| tṛtīye tviti dhātumātrārtha iti kalpe tvityarthaḥ. āgatamiti kartṛktāntam| upād‌yasamabhivyāhāre vinā śaktiṃ gam‌dhātostādṛsabodhādarśanāttsyetarārthaparatāyāmupasargasya niyāmakatvalābhāditi bhāvaḥ  taccādiṣviti tata-dyotakatvam| tattadartheti tattadarthabodakatvamityarthaḥ|
amīṣāmiti cādinipātānāmapītyarthaḥ| astyeveti `ghaṭa kriyate' ityādau karmaṇi lakāradarśanāt kṛñaḥ na sakarmakatvānupapattyā sākṣātkāre lakṣaṇeti bhāvaḥ|eṣviti sākṣātkārādirūpārtheṣvityarthaḥ| anyatheti kvacidarthāntaramādāya kṛñaḥ sakarmakasya sarvatra tattvāṅgīkāre ityarthaḥ|
vāyurvikuruta iti vikārānukūlavyāpārāvācakasyāpi sakarmakatāpattyā "akarmakācca'  iti vihitātmanepadānupapattiriti bhāvaḥ| upāsyeta gururityādāvupād‌yupasargāṇāmupāsanād‌yarthavācakatve'pi pūrvoktasakārmakatvānupapatti pariharati-atheti| idamiti sakarmakatvānupapattirūpamityarthaḥ| sādhakam-d‌yotakatvasādhakam| vyutpattau nipātātiriktatvariśeṣaṇānneyamāpattirata āha-kiñceti| ityapi syāditi abhyuccayavādo'yaṃ cādyupasthāpyasamuccayasya nityaṃ samucceyasākāṅkṣatayā samuccayārthakapadaprayogaṃ vinā cādiprayogāsambhavāt| śabdaśaktisvābhāvyena nityaṃ dhātuparatantropasargai svārthasya dhātvarthaviśeṣaṇatayaiva bodhanena datasamabhivyāhāre tatprayogasyāpyasambhavāt| ata eva vakṣayati-tulyasamādheyatvāditi|
nanu nipātānāṃ vācakatve "cārthe dvandvaḥ"  ityādisūtrameva mānam| prādināṃ vācakatve tu mānānupalambhād dyotakataivociteti cet, tat kim `īṣad‌duḥ suṣu kṛcchākacchrārtheṣu khal"  ityādisūtrātmakaṃ mānaṃ pāṇipihitamiti vibhāvaya| ṣaṣṭhayādikamityādipadena tadarthavihitadvitīyādiparigrahaḥ| abhedātiriktasambandhāvacchinnaprātipadikārthaprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣyatāsambandhena śābdatvāvacchinnaṃ prati viśeṣyatayā vibhaktijanyopasthiterhetutayā prakṛti tadabhāvena bhedānvayāsambhava ityarthaḥ| anyathā-uktakāryakāraṇabhāvānabhyupagame| āpatteriti| bhedenānvayabodhāpatterityarthaḥ|ādīti bhedānvayasaṅgrāhakamādipadamityarthaḥ  bhedānvayeti| `prātipadikārthayorabhedānvayaḥ' iti pāṭhastu tādṛsānvayabodhe vibhaktijanyopasthiterhetutvasya kenā'pyanabhyupagamādaprakāntatvācca cintyaḥ| ni pātātirikteti| tathāca samuccayādyarthasya nipātārthatvena na tena sākaṃ padārthāntarasya bhedānvayānupapattirityarthaḥ| sadṛk‌śabdasya samānā dṛgitivyutpannasyopādāne'nanvayāpattyā prakṛte tulyarūḍhasya, "tamivemaṃ paśyanti janāḥ" iti bhāṣye kartṛkarmavyutpannasya vā tasya grahaṇamityabhipretya vyācaṣṭe-sadṛśeneti tulyenetyarthaḥ|
nanu viruddhavibhaktikasyāpi nāmatvādinā tulyatvāt tenābhedenānvayāpattirata āha-samānādhikaraṇeneti tathāca samānavibhaktikapadopasthāpyatvena sādṛśyasya vivikṣaṇānnoktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ| evañca mūle'nvetītyantaramabhededanetīti śeṣaḥ. `stokampacati' ityādau vibhinnavibhaktipadopasthāpyatvādasadṛśenāpyabhedānvayādāha-asamānādhikāraṇeneti viśeṣavācakapadaprakṛtikavibhaktiviruddhārthakavibhaktiprakatyupasthāpyenetyarthaḥ|
nanu vācakatāvādinoktavyutpatternipātātiriktatvena saṅkocanīyetyuktyaivākṣepavrauvye mūle samānādhikaraṇetyādivyutpattikathane'rthāntaramata āha-ayamartha iti sa cettham-"vibhāgena kadācana" ityanena vyadhikaraṇayorabhedānvayaṃ vyapacchindatā nāmārthayorbhedanānvaya iti vyutpattireva prādhānyena pradarśyate, natu samānādhikaraṇayoriti vyutpattyantaraṃ tathā| kitu dṛṣṭāntavidhayā tadupanyasanamiti noktadoṣaḥ|
tathāca pradhānībhūtavyutpattāveva nipātetarasaṅkocotkīrttanamiti sarva sustham|
nanu nāmārthayorabhedānvayabodhe samānavibhaktikapadajanyopasthitihetutāyāṃ padāṃśe nipātātiriktatvaṃ niveśānīyamiti noktadoṣa ityavataraṇaṃ tu sadhrīcīnam| naca tādṛśabyutpateraprakrāntatvāt kathametaditi vācyam, mūloktādipadenaiva ṣaṣṭhayādikamantareṇa bhedenānvayāsambhavaṃ pratipādayatā samānavibhaktitvena dhavaścetyādāvabedānvayāpādānasyāpi kukṣikṛtatvāttadeva ca sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ vyutpattau niviṣṭamiti yathākṛtamūlasaṅgamanāditi|
nanu tadaṃnvayānupapattireva mānamata āha-gauravañceti| nanu tavāpīdaṃ tūṣaṇaṃ samānamata āha-asmākamiti dyotakatāvādināmityarthaḥ| dyotakatvāditi cādīnāṃ samabhivyāhṛtapadasamucitatattadartharūpalakṣyārthe tātparyyagrahakatvādityarthaḥ| nāstīti samuccayādestannirūpakatvādeśca samabhivyāhṛtapadenaiva lābhāttasya pṛthagupasthityabhāvenānvayasyaivābhāvenoktasthalasya tādṛśavyutpattyaviṣayatvāditi bhāvaḥ| ata eveti| vyutpattau nipātātiriktatvaviśeṣaṇapraveśādevetyarthaḥ| lākṣaṇikamiti| vyutpattestad‌ghaṭitatve tu tatra lakṣaṇānusaraṇaṃ vyarthameva syāditi bhāvaḥ|
naiyāyikāstu naño vācakatāyā bhāṣyasammatatvāt `ghaṭo na paṭaḥ' caitro na pacati' ityādau nañarthabhāvasya viśeṣaṇatayā ghaṭacaaitrādiṣvanvayānurodhāduktavyutpattau vyabhicāravāraṇāya nipātātiriktatvaviśeṣaṇamāvaśyakam| evañca ghaṭaśca paṭaścetyādau vibhaktijanyopasthiti vinā'pi cārthasya ghaṭādiṣvanvayaḥ sulabhaḥ|
naca ghaṭo netyatra ghaṭasya nāmārthatvādvibhaktijanyopasthitiṃ vinā tatprakārakapratiyogitāsaṃsargakanañarthaciśeṣyavodhānupapattirduṣpariharaiveti vācyam, etadbhiyaiva ghaṭādipadānāṃ tatpratiyogike lakṣaṇā'bhyupagamāt nipātātiriktanāmārthayoḥ sākṣādbhedenānvayo'vyutpanna ityatra dvivacanasvārasyena nipātātiriktanāmārthaniṣṭhabhedasambandhāvacchinnaprakāratānirūpitanipātārthāvṛttiviśeṣyatāsambandena bodhe, viśeṣyatāsambandena vibhaktijanyopasthiterhetutvakalpanādvā sāmañjasyādityāhuḥ  uktavyutpatternipātātiriktaviṣayatvena pūrvoktadoṣaparihāre'pi sthalāntare tanmate'nvayānupapattirdurvāraivetyāśayena mūlamavatārayati-apiceti| ityartha iti ityanvayabodhaḥ sarvasammata ityarthaḥ| ayañceti uktārthānvayabodhaścetyarthaḥ| anyatheti nipātānāṃ vācakatvābhyupagama ityarthaḥ| vyutpattivirodaṃ vyaktīkaroti-(ata-udīcoddharaṇe) tathāhītyādi|
nanvivaśabdottara laptatīyārtakaraṇa evevārthānvayaḥ sulabho'ta āha-ivaśabdasyeti| asattvārthakatayeti| liṅgadyananvayyarthakatayetyarthaḥ| nipātatvāditi yāvat| eva़ñca kārakānanvayitāvacchedakarūpeṇopasthite'rthe nairākāṅkṣayāt kārakānvayāsambhaveṃna tadarthakata़tīyāyā asambhavāditi bhāvaḥ| sambhave veti| vākāro'nāsthāyām| avyaye vaśabdasya kvāpyadṛṣṭatvāttadabhyupagame tu tato vibhaktestacchravaṇasya ca durnivāryyataiva syāditi dūṣaṇāntaramāha-ustrapadottareti| ananvayaprasaṅgāditi vācakatāvādimata iti śeṣa-|
idamupalakṣaṇam, tṛtīyā'nupapatteḥ| ustrasyevārthasadṛśānvaye uddharaṇakaraṇatvasya tatrābhāvāt| asmanmate tūstrasadṛśarūpostrārthasya karaṇatvānna tadanupapattiriti bhāvaḥ| taduttaraṃ subvyatiriktapratyayaśravaṇatvāpādyatvāsambhavena samucceyāsambhavāmāśahkaya cakārasyānyatrāpakarṣa ityāha-supāṃ śravaṇañceti| cakāramamucyaṃ darśayati-ustrapadottaratṛtīyeti| tṛtīyāyāstadarthasya ananvayameva spaṣṭayati-ustrasadṛsetyādi-tattatpadārthānāmityarthaḥ| bhedenānvayeti sāmānādikaraṇyaṃ nāmārthayorityuktadavyutpatteriti bhāvaḥ|
nanu tarhyabhedānvayastatra syādata āha-bādhāditi sādṛśyasya bhedaghaṭitatvāditi bhāvaḥ| atredaṃ bodhyam-vācakatāvādinā ivādinā sādṛśyādibodhene paryyudāsamañivānuyogipratiyogivācakapadayoḥ samānavibhaktikatvasya-
liṅgaṅkhayāvibhede'pyastyupamānopameyatā|
vibhaktiḥ punarekaivamupamānopameyayoḥ||
iti anuśāsanasiddhāyā niyāmakatāyā abhyupagamena prakṛte ustranirūpitasādṛśyāśrayaśarakaraṇakoddharaṇabodhaḥ sūpapādaḥ| bhedābhedayorvikalpasyopanyāsastu sārakāroktaścintya eveti  uttarapadārtaprādhānyābhāvāditi prādhānyasyānyanirūpitatvaniyamāt| dyotyārtamiti dyotakatāvṛttyupasthāpitārthamityarthaḥ| tameveti| dyotyamevetyarthaḥ| arthavattvāditi ayaṃ bhāvaḥ-dyotakatāyā api vṛttitvena tayā'rthavattvasya dyotake'pyakṣatatvānna tannibandanaprātipadikasaṃjñānupapattiḥ| nahi śaktilakṣaṇānyataradevārthepasthāpakamiti vacanamastīti|
nanu śabdādupastitasāmānyasya viśeṣe paryyavasthāpakatvaṃ dyotakatvam, natu tadupasthāpakatvameveti na tadādāyārthavattvasambhavo'ta āha-vastutastviti| jñāpakāditi dyotakatāvāde teṣāmanarthakatvādeva subapāve lugvighānamanarthakaṃ saduktārthe jñāpakamiti bhāvaḥ|
nanu svārādīnāṃ sattvāvacanānāmapi sattvāttadvihitasublugvidhānena caritārthasya tasya na jñāpakatvamata āha-nipātasyeti| tadvārttikapratyākhyāne tvāha-kṛttaddhiteti| saṃgraha iti anarthakasyāpi nipātasya prātipadikatvamityarthaḥ| upasaṃharati-tasmāditi| etatkalpe mīmāṃsakasammatimāha-uktañceti| caturvidha iti nāmākhyātopasarganipātabhedena catuḥprakāra ityarthaḥ| pade-suptiṅantarūpe| dvividhasya-nāmākhyātasvarūpasya| arthanirupaṇe hetumāha-saṃśayotpatteriti| nāmārtho jātiḥ, kiṃ vā vyaktiḥ| dhātvartho vyāpāra uta phalam, kiṃ vobhayamiti sandehe tannirūpamasyocityāditi bhāvaḥ| tayoḥ-nipātopasargayoḥ| vyāpāraḥ-śaktilakṣaṇānyatararūpaḥ| tau-upasarganipātau| yadarthadyotakau-yadarthaviṣayakatātparyagrahajanakau| sa-nāmākhyātātmakaḥ śabdaḥ| tasya ca tiṅkṛttiddhitaghaṭitatvāttadartho'pi vicāryyata ityarthaḥ|
yadyapi svādyantatvanibandhanaṃ nāmātvamupasarganipātayorapīti tayoḥ pārthakyena nirddeso'nucitaḥ| tathāpi liṅgasaṅkhyāniṣṭhaprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣyatāśālyarthakatvarūpapāribhāṣikanāmatvamādāya tathoktam| upasargāṇāṃ nipātatve'pi tadupādānantu `brāhmaṇavasiṣṭha' nyāyeneti bodhyam| nanu `upasargeṇa dhātvarthaḥ' ityatra nipātatvavyāpyopasargatvena prādīnāmupādānāt kathaṃ taccādīnāṃ dyotakatve upaṣṭambhakamata āha-upasargapadamiti| padāntareti`--candra iva mukham' ityādāvarthavattvena candrādināmopalakṣakamityarthaḥ| evañca na tadūlānnipātānāṃ vācakatvamiti bhramitavyamiti bhāvaḥ  idānīṃ vācakatāpakṣaṃ pariṣkaroti-nanviti| anvayavyatirekābhyāmiti| candra iva mukam' ityādāvivādinipātasattve sādṛśyādyarthapratītestadasattve tadapratīteśca nipātānāṃ ghaṭādipadavadvācakatvameva yuktamityarthaḥ| nanvicchādirūpaśakterabhāvāt kathaṃ teṣāṃ vācakatvamata āha-bodhakatārūpeti tathāca tadarthabodhakatvameva tadvācakatvamiti teṣāmapi ghaṭādipadavadvācakatvamevetyarthaḥ|
nanvanvayavyatirekayostadarthatātparyyagrahe upayogasya prāgābhihitatvānna tayorvācakatāprayojakatvamata āha-kiñceti| uktarītyeti ananyalaṅyasyaiva śabdārthatayā `prajayati' ityādau prakṛṣṭajayasya lakṣaṇayā jidhātorevopasthitau na padīnāṃ prakarṣādyarthakatvam, teṣāṃ tātparyyagrāhakatayaivopayogādityuktarītyetyarthaḥ| tiṅādeḥ syāditi tatrānvayavyatirekābhyāṃ kartrādivācakatvāvadhāraṇe tu prādīnāmapi vācakatvavidhṛtiriti bhāvaḥ| pare tu "ajarghāḥ" "acakāt" ityādau dhātumātrāt kartrādibodhena tiṅā dyotakatve iṣṭaivāpattiḥ|
naca tatra luptavibhaktismaraṇād bodhaḥ| lopamajānato'pi bodhāt pratyayalakṣaṇasya lopaphalatvaṃ vadatā bhāṣyakṛtā luptavibhaktismaraṇād bodhasyānabhyupagamācca| yatra pratyayasya vācakatā tatraiva prakṛtipratyayārthayoḥ, iti niyamāvakāśaḥ| yathā-`pācakaḥ' ityādikṛdante| "la karmaṇi"  ityādau karmakartṛviṣayāddhātorityarthānna tadvirodhaḥ, "bhāve" ityasya sarvaistarthaiva vyākhyānāt|
naca sarvatraiva dhātorbhāvanāviṣayatvāttatraiva lakāraḥ syāditi śaṅkayam, `bhāvaviṣayāt' ityasya kevalakriyāmātrabodhakādityarthenādoṣāt| ata eva "bhāvakarmaṇoḥ"  "śeṣāt karttari parasmaidam"  "dvayekayoḥ"  ityādīnāṃ niyama iti bhāṣyakṛduktisaṅgatiḥ| teṣāmanirdiṣṭārthatvena " anirdiṣṭārthāḥ svārthe bhavanti" iti nyāyena sarvatra siddhatvādityāhuḥ|
taccintyam, `vyatise' ityādau dhātuṃ vināpi kartṛbodhenādyayukteḥ śaithilyāt, tulyayuktyā supāmapi dyātakatāpatterddhātordvivacanādyanupapatteśca, pratyayalakṣaṇasya lopaphalatve'pi luptavibhaktismaraṇajanyabodhasya phalātvābhyupagame bādhakābhāvācca| svottaratiṅ‌vācyakartrādigatadvitvāropeṇa dvivacanādiriti svoktivirodhācca|
bhavaduktarītyaiva karttṛviśiṣṭabhāvanāvācakāt, karmaviśiṣṭabhāvānāvācakāt, kevalabhāvanāvācakācca dhātorlakārā bhavantītyarthe laḥ karmaṇītyarthaparyyavasānena `anirdiṣṭārthāḥ' iti nyāyenāpi tattatkārakādiviśiṣṭabhāvanārthakānāṃ tiṅāṃ sarvatra sidhdyasambhavena niyamasyānyathaiva saṅgamanīyatvādityanyatra vistaraḥ|
nanu nipātānāṃ vācakatve `ghaṭo na paṭaḥ' ityādauda ghaṭādeḥ pratiyogitayā nañarthābhāve tasya cā'śrayatayā ghaṭādāvanvayo'nupapannaḥ| bhedasambandhena prātipadikārthaprakārakabodhe viśeṣyatayā pratyayajanyopasthiterhetutāyām "nāmārthayorbhedanānvayaḥ" iti vyutpattisiddhatvāt, evaṃ "samānādhikaraṇanāmārtayoḥ" iti vyutpatterabhedānvayāpattiścetyata āha-evañceti| vidhivaiyarthyamiti tādṛśavārttikena prātipadikasaṃjñāvidhānavaiyamityarthaḥ|
(anarthakasyeti) vyarthamiti vyabhicārābhāvena tasya viśeṣaṇatvāyogāditi bhāvaḥ| pratyākhyātatvāditi "kṛttaddhita"  ityatra caśabdenaiva tadarthasya samuccitatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
vakṣyamāṇāsvarasādāha-kiñceti prakaraṇādityanena dyotakatāyā vṛttitvābhāvo dhvanyate| asmākamiti pakṣadvayābhyupagandṝṇāmityarthaḥ| kāryyakāraṇabhāva iti samuccitaghaṭādiśābdabodhe ghaṭapadanirūpitalakṣaṇājñānatvena hetutetyākāraka ityarthaḥ| śaktigrahasyāpīti ghaṭādyarthabodhe ghaṭadyarthabodhe ghaṭaśāktijñānasya, samuccayādibodhe tacchaktijñānasya hetutetyākārakaḥ kāryyakārabhāvo'pyāvaśyaka ityarthaḥ|
kalpyāntarābhāveneti dyotakatāpakṣe vācakatāpakṣe'pi tattatpakṣaklaptādhikakalpanīyābhāvenetyarthaḥ|
ata eveti kalpyapakṣadvaye yuktisāmyādevetyarthaḥ sa vācaka iti upasarga prakramyedam, `pratiṣṭhate' ityatra "gatinivṛttivācakaḥ sthādhātuḥ, upasargastadabhāvarūpagativācakaḥ" iti puñjarājena tadarthasya kathanāt| `vācakatvameva ityasya nipātānāmityādiḥ, evaṃ `dyotakatvameva' ityasyopasargāṇāmityādiḥ| matamevetīti| vā-athavā, anvayavyatirekato nipātānāṃ vacakatvaṃ yuktam| naḥ-asmākaṃ mate pareṣāṃ yadekasya vācakatvamaparasya dyotakatvamiti matam, tadeva tu na yuktamiti mūlayojanā  nanu "cādayo'sattve"  ityatra `asattve' iti prasajyapratiṣedhāśrayaṇe'samarthasamāsāpattiḥ, vākyabhedāpattiśceti paryyudāsa evāśrayaṇīyaḥ| tathācoktalakṣaṇasyāśrayaṇe'narthake tasminnavyaptiḥ| tāvadanyatamatvasya niveśetu gauravamata āha-śaktisambandheneti| tasya lakṣyatā'vacchedakamabhipretyāha-jātiriti| nipātapadaśakyatāvacchedakatayā ca tasmiddhiriti bhāvaḥ|
nanu hatvādinā sāṅkaryyānna nipātatvaṃ jātiḥ. tathāhi-nipātvābhāvavati hatvam, dhātvādihakāre hatvābhāvavati nipātatvam nipātātmakacakāre tayorekatra hakāre samveśāt| tad‌dūṣakatābījaṃ tu svasāmānādhikaraṇya-svabhāvasāmānādhikaraṇyobhayasambanadhena jātivisiṣṭajātitvāvacchedena svasamānādikaraṇābhāvāpratiyogitvaniyamabhaṅga eva| nirupādhisahacāragrahaśca tādṛsaniyame'nukūlastarkaḥ| anyathā tanmātrabalapravṛttānāṃ `sāttāvān jāteḥ' ityādyanumānānāṃ vilayaprasaṅgat| ata eva `bhūtatvaṃ na jātiḥ' iti sāmpradāyikā|
hatvādivyāpyanipātatvasya nānātvābhyupagamena sāṅkaryyasya parihāre'pi sakalacādisādharaṇyābhāvenānanugatatayā tasyaivāsiddhirniṣpramāṇatvāt‌| kiñca kāryatāvacchedakatvādīnāṃ jātitvasādhakānāṃ tatrābhāvādapi na sā jītiḥ| śakyatāvacchedakatvaṃ tu na tatsādhakam, abhāvatvavibhutvādīnāmapi jātitvāpatteḥ| ata eva guṇadīdhitau guṇatvasya guṇatvasya jātitvaṃ dīdhitikṛtā khaṇḍitamata āha-upādirveti| sāmānye-vyāpakadharme| ityarthaḥ|
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ-upasargeṣūbhayamate'pi dyotakatve, vācakatve vā kalpanīye ta rmivṛttitayopastitopasargatvanipātatvāyorubhayordharmayormadhye dyotakatvādikamupasargatvāvacchedena kalpyam, uta nipātatvācchedeneti saṃśaye avacchedakanirṇinīṣāyāṃ vyāpakatayā nipātatvāvacchedanaiva tat kalpyate, saṅgrahakalāghavāt| ata eveśvarānumāne kartṛjanyatvaṃ ghaṭatvādinā'vacchidyate, uta kāryatvena, ityavacchedakajijñāsāyāmuktalāghavāt kāryyatvemeva tadavacchedakam, natu ghaṭatvādikamiti nyāyasiddhānta iti|
atra vadanti-pramāṇānāṃ sāmānyadharmasyāvacchedakatāyāṃ tatra pakṣapātaḥ, yatra ca bahūnāṃ dharmāmāmavacchedakatvakalpanāprayuktaṃ gauravam| yathā karttṛjanyatvānumāne kṛtijanyatāyāṃ ghaṭatvādyapekṣayā kāryyatvasya| yatra tu viśeṣo'pyeka eva tatroktayuktivirahāt, tasyaivāvacchedakatvam| ata eva vyāpārasyādhikasaṃgrāhakatve'pi kṛtitvasyaivākhyātaśakyatāvacchedakatvaṃ svīkṛtatryaca mīmāṃsakairapi| dravyapratyakṣatvaṃ vihāya dravyacākṣuṣatvasyaiva rūpakāryyatāvacchedakatvam| prakṛte vyāpakatvānnipātatvādhikasaṃgrahakatve'pi vyāpakopasargatvasyāpyekatvena tasyaivāvacchedakatvamucitam| nipātatvāsyādyotake'vācake pādapūraṇamātrārthake vṛttitvenātiprasaktatvāt, prādayo dyotakāḥ, upasargatvāt' ityanumānācca|
naca prādīnāṃ dyotakatā nipātatvaliṅgenaiva setsyatīti vācyam, tasyopasargatvarūpodhimattatayā tadasādhakatvāt| naca ceṣṭādau sādhyāvyāpakatvānna tasyopādhitvamiti vācyam, śuddhasādhyayāvyāpakatvasambhavāt| asti hi nipātatvāvacchinnaṃ dyotakatvaṃ yatra tatropasargatvam, yatra nipātatvaṃ nañādau tatra tadabhāvāt| nanu prakarṣadyarthaḥ, tadanvayavyatirekānuvidhāyiśābdadhīviṣayatvāt| yatra yadanvayavyatirekānuvidhayisābdadhīviṣayaḥ sa tadvācya iti sāmānyavyāptau ghaṭādidṛṣṭāntaḥ|
nacānanyalabhyatvamupādhiḥ, tasya vācyatvarūpasādhyavyāpakatvāt kartrādau ākhyātānvayavyatirekānuvidhāyiśābdadhīviṣaye sādhanāvyāpakatvāditi vācyam, uktahetughaṭakaśābdabuddherākṣepādyajanyatvena viśeṣaṇenānanyalabhyatvasya sādhanavyāpakatvenopādhitvāyogāt|
naca svarūpāsiddhiḥ, dhātoreva prakṛṣṭajayārathakatvenopasargasya tātparyamātragrāhakatvāditi vācyam, dhātoḥ prakṛṣṭajayārthakatvasya granthakṛtaiva khaṇḍanāt| nāpi lakṣyatā tasya, prottaratvāderlakṣamatāvacchedakakoṭipraveśe gauravāt| tasmājjayatyādi dhātoḥ śakyatāvacchedakam, prakarṣatvādikaṃ tūpasargasyeti vācyam| tathāca kva detvasiddhiḥ| kiñcārthasya dhātulakṣyatve jipūrvapratvenāpi vinikamanāvirahāllakṣakatāvacchedakakoṭau niveśāpattiḥ| nāpi tadarthasya dhātvarthena sākamananvayaprasaṅgaḥ, nipātātiriktatvasyoktavyutpattau viśeṣaṇāt| evañca `pratiṣṭhate' ityādau dhātorgatyabhāvo'rthaḥ, tadabhāvaścopasargārthaḥ|
tathāca tato gatyabhāvābhāvavāniti dhīḥ, gamanatvaprakārakabodhastūttarakāliko mānasa eveti cenna,`pratyayānām' iti vyutpatterjāgarūkatayā gamanābhāvavāniti bodhasyāpi gaganakusumāyamānatvāt|
kiñca tathābodhāṅgīkāre tādṛśasthale prādīnāmupasargatvamapiṃ durghaṭam, kriyāyā aviśeṣakatvāt| kriyā hyatropasargābhāvaviśeṣikā, natūsargārtho kriyābhāvaviśeṣakaḥ| svādīnāṃ dyotakatāvyavasthāpanoktarītyāpyupasargāṇāṃ sā'vaseyeti| nipātānāntu keṣāñcid vācakatvam| tatra nañartho nirūpitaḥ| cārthasca samuccayādibhedena caturvidha iti "cārthe dvandvaḥ"  ityatrākareṣu vyaktameva|
