ivārthastu sādṛśyam| tacca tadbhinnatve sati tadratadharmavatvam| yattu-sādṛśyaṃ na bedaghaṭitamiti, tanna tāvatapi "nañivayuktam" iti paribhāṣaṇena tasyevārthatvānapāyāt| tad‌bodhakānuyogipratiyogivācakapadayoḥ samānavibhaktikatvaṃ niyāmakam| nātaścandra iva mukhamityarthe candreṇeva mukhamiti sādhu|
evakārasya tvanyayogavyavacchedaḥ, ayogavyavacchedaḥ, atyantāyogavyavacchedaścārthaḥ| tatra viśeṣyagataivasthale-`pārtha eva dhanurddharaḥ' ityādāvanyatādātmyavyavacchedo'rthaḥ| anyatvañca-samabhivyāhṛtapadārthāpekṣikam| tathāca pārthānyatādātmyābhāvavaddhanurddharābhinna, pārtha iti bodhaḥ| `pṛthivyāmeva gandhaḥ' ityatra pṛtivyanyasamavetatvābhāvavān pṛthivīsamavetasca gandhaḥ' iti bodhāttādṛśabodhe saptamīrūpaviśe,ṇavibhaktisamabhivyāhāro'pi niyāmakaḥ| atra bhāvānvayabodhānupagame pṛthivyāmevākrāsamityapi prasajyeta| evañcaivakāreṇānyayogavyavacchedabodhe viśeṣyavācakapadaviśeṣaṇavācakasaptamyantānyatarāvyavahitottaraivaśabdo hetuḥ| viśeṣaṇasaṅgataivasthale'yogavyavacchedaḥ| `śaṅkha pāṇḍura eva' ityādau śaṅkhatvāvacchedana pāṇḍuravattvasamāvāyābhāvavyavacchedabodhāt‌| `nīlaṃ sarojaṃ bhavatvayeva' ityādau kriyāsaṅgataivasthale'tyantāyogavyavacchedo'rthaḥ|
atra kecit-ayoge ātyantikatvaṃ vyavacchidyate| taccānvayitāvacchedakavyāpakatvam| khaṇḍaśaśca tatra śaktiḥ| tathācoktasthale sarojaniṣṭhābhāvapratiyoginīlabhavanābhāva iti bodha iti vadanti|
vastutastu anyayogavyavacchedāyogavyacavcachedayorevaivakārasya śaktiḥ| anvayitāvacchedakāvacchedenāyogavyavacchedabodhagrahaṇādhyayanārthakadhānusamabhivyāhārasyāpi niyāmakatvāt `jñānamartha gṛhṇātyeva' itivanna jñānaṃ rajataṃ gṛhṇātyevetyādiprayogaḥ| kriyāntarasamabhivyāhāre tu prāyaḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyena tadbhānam| itthañcānvayitāvacchedakasāmānādhikaraṇyenānyayogavyacchedo'tyantāyogavyavacchedo'nvayitāvacchedena tvayogavyavaccheda iti vyavahriyate| vistatarastvanyato'vadhāryyaḥ|
`kveva bhokṣyase' ityādāvasambhāvanā'pi tadarthaḥ| evamanyeṣāmapi sārthakānāṃ nipātānāmarthā ūhyāḥ| vaiyākaraṇamate tvevakārasya vācakatvāsambhavādavadhāraṇātmakajñānaviśeṣadyatakatvemeva| sa ca samabhivyāhṛtapadārthānurodhādanekavidhaḥ| yathā jñānamarthaṃ gṛhṇātyeva, ityatrāvacchedakāvacchedenārthagrahaṇābhāvānadhikaraṇajñānaprakārakajñānaviṣayo jñānakartṛkamarthakarmakaṃ grahaṇamiti bodhāduktapadārthaghaṭitaḥ saḥ| parantvanekaghātvādīnāṃ tattadarthe vṛttikalpanāpekṣayaivakārasyaiva socitetyanye|
yadyapyupasarga prakamya-
sa vācako viśeṣaṇāṃ sambhavād dyotako'pi ca|
iti harimā pakṣadvayamaviśeṣeṇoktam| granthakṛtā ca tadevānusutam| tathā'pi
kvacit sambhavino bhedāḥ kevalairanidarśitāḥ|
upasargema sambandhe vyajyante prasarādinā||
iti taduktopasaṃhṛtiparyyālocanayopasargāṇāṃ dyotakatvamevāvasīyate|
atredaṃ bodhyam-upasargatvādyajānato'pi `prajayati' ityādau prakṛṣṭajayabodhāt naiva teṣāṃ dyotakatvam| nipātānāmapi catvādinaiva vācakatā, yatra na vācakatāmantarema nirvāhaḥ| kvacid dyotakatvamapyuktayuktayanabhyupagame| yathā "śarairustraiḥ" ityādau| kvacinnobhayam| yathā pādapūraṇamātrārthake tvādau| taduktam-
nipātā dyotakāḥ kecit pṛthagarthābhidhāyinaḥ|
āgamā iva ke'pi syuḥ sambhūyārthasya vācakāḥ|| iti|
kecidityubhayānvayī| natūpasargatvena prādīnām, nipātatvena cādīnāṃ dyotakatā vācakatā ceti sthite; upasargatvāvacchedana dyotakatā, na nipātatvāvacchedeneti vivādaḥ siddhāntānālocanamūlaka eva| karmapravacanīyānāntu nopasargavat kriyāviśeṣakatvam, kintu kriyāgatasambandaviśeṣakatvam, karma-kriyāṃ proktavantaḥ `karmapravacanīyāḥ' ityanvarthasaṃjñānāt| adhiparyyostu sūtrārambhasārthyādeva sā| `japamanuprāvarṣat' ityatra hetubhūtajapanirūpitalakṣyalakṣaṇabhāvasambandhasya varṣaṇakriyāniṣṭhasyānunā dyotanāt| evamanyatrāpi jñeyam| ata eva na teṣāmupasargātvam| kriyaḥgataviśeṣadyotako hyupasargaḥ| uttañca hariṇā-
kriyāyā dyotako nāṃ sambandhasya na vācakaḥ|
nāpi kriyāpadā''kṣepī sambandhasya tu bhedakaḥ|| iti|
tadarthastu-karmapravacanīyasaṃjñako'nvādiḥ, `prajayati' ityādāvupasargavanna kriyāgataviśeṣasya dyotakaḥ| vibhaktyevoktatvācca na sambandhasya vācakaḥ| nāpi `prādeśaṃ vilikhati' ityādau viśabdavat kriyākṣepakaḥ, tatra vinā mānakriyā kṣepāt| tathā sati tattatk‌riyānibandhanā kārakavibhaktireva syāt| kintu viśiṣṭakriyājanito yaḥ sambandho vibhaktyartha iti tad‌dyotakaḥ| `prādeśaṃ vimāya likhati' ityarthapratīterityadhikamanyato'vadhāryyam 
iti baiyākaraṇabhūṣaṇasāradarppaṇe upasarganipātārthanirūpaṇam || 8 ||

 prakrānte nāmārthanirūpaṇe nāmaghaṭakakṛttaddhitānāmarthamupoddhātasaṅgatyā nirūpayiṣyannādau prakṛtyarthaprakāratvādbhāvapratyayārthanirūpaṇamityāśayenāha-bhāveti ṣyañ‌vuñāderapi sambandharūpabhāvabodhakatvāt tatsaṃgrahārthamāha-tvataloriti| bhāvapratyayeti bhāvavihitataddhitapratyayesyarthaḥ| rahivākyaṃ vivṛṇoti-ayamartha ityādinā| `ityuktam' ityasya samāsaśaktinirūpaṇāvasara iti śeṣaḥ| sambandhamiti uttarapadārthe pūrvapadārthasambandhasyaiva dharmatvena bhāsamānatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
bhedapakṣa iti tatpakṣe hi `rājapūruṣaḥ' itayatra `arājakīyabhinnaḥ puruṣaḥ' iti bodhena puruṣāṃśe rājasambandhābhānāditi bhāvaḥ| upagvapatyasambandha iti upagvapatyayorjanyajanakabhāvātmakaḥ sbanda ityarthaḥ| anvayabodha iti tattatpadottarabhāvapratyayasya tattatsambandhavācakatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
avyabhicaritasambandhe tviti avyabicaritasambandharūpajātyaiva bhānopagame tvityarthaḥ| tuśabdasūcitārucibījantu "tasyāpatyam'  ityanena ṣaṣṭhīprakṛtyarthopagvādisambandhe'patye'ṇādīnāṃ vidhānena ṣaṣṭhayarthasambandāntarbhāveṇaiva tatraikārthobhāvasyopagantavyatayā tatrāpatyādyaṃśe saṃsargasyaiva prakāratayā tasyaiva taduttarabhāvapratyayārthatvena jāteratadvācyatvāsambhavāditi| arthāntaravṛttiriti dhātvarthaghaṭitārthavṛttirākṣikādyantargataṣṭhagādirityarthaḥ| tadarthekartrādau devanādirūpakriyāyāḥ kriyākārakabhāvasambandhasya prakāratayā bhānādityāśayaḥ|
 rūḍheriti kathamapyavayavayogādarthaviśeṣaprasiddhādityarthaḥ| abhinnarūpāditi pratyayāntatve'pi pratyayalukā prakṛtisamānā''kārāt `śiṣyamāṇaṃ lupyamānārthābhidhāyi' iti nyāyena prakṛtyarthānvitapratyayārthābhidhāyina ityarthaḥ| avyabhicaritasambandhebhya iti avyabhicarito vyaktiniṣṭhātyantābhāvāpratiyogī sambandhaḥ samavāyātmako yasya jātyādestadviśiṣṭavācakebhya ityarthaḥ| nātaḥ pākādinā nāśena svāśrayaniṣṭhābhāvapratiyogināṃ tattvam| ukteti tadvitāntatve'pītyapinā taduttarabhāvapratyayena sambandhābhidhānaṃ prāpnotīti sūcitam| abhedapratyayāditi idañca ṣaṣṭhayarthe vihitasya matupaḥ sambandhārthakatvāśaṅkānirāsāya| `tṛtīyam' ityasyodāharaṇaṃ yatheti śeṣaḥ|
sata iti  sacchadbajanyabodhe satrarthakartraśe sattāyāḥ prakāratayā bhānāttaduttarabhāvapratyayenāpi saivābhidhīyate ityarthaḥ| tathācoktasthaleṣu bhāvapratyayāntāt sambandhābhānāduktavākye anyatretyupāttamiti bhāvaḥ|
nanu `śuklatvaṃ sattvam' sato'pi śauklyādisambandhasya bhāvapratyayenānabhidhānavat `rājapurupaṣaḥ" ityatrāpi tadanabhidhānaṃ syāt, tatrāpi rājādīnāṃ puruṣādipadārthe svatvādisambandhena prakāratetyasyāpi śakyatvādityata āha-digiti tadarthastu nirvakṣyamāṇabhāve "tasya bhāvaḥ"  iti sūtreṇa tvatalādayo vidhīyante|
nanu jātitvādinā jātāveva sambandha eveti niyamaḥ| tathāca `yatprakṛteryaddharmaprakārabodhaḥ śāstrakṛtsammataḥ sa eva dharmastvādyabhidheyaḥ' iti ruḍhayādibhinnaprakṛteḥ kṛdantādito'pi sambandhaprakārakabodhasya bhāṣyasammatatvāt tasyaiva bhāvapratyayenābhidhānamucitamiti nokteṣu bhāvapratyayasya jātyādyabhidhāyakatvamiti  `prakṛtyarthaviśiṣṭadravyamātravacanatā' iti pāṭhaḥ| mātrapadena sambandhavyavacchedaḥ| taddhitasyaiti inyāderityarthaḥ| sambandhānabhidhāyakatveneti prakṛtijanyabodhe tasyāprakāratvāditi bhāvaḥ|
nanu prakṛtījanyabodaviṣayatve sati prakṛtyarthavṛttitvaṃ bhāvatvam| tacca daṇḍasambandhasyākṣamiti bhāvapratyayena tadabhidhānaṃ bhaviṣyatītyata āha-prakṛtijanyabodha iti uktārthasya bhāvatve tu `dāmodaratvam' ityādau jātiguṇasambandasyāpi bhāvārthatā prasajyeteti bhāvaḥ| krameṇadaladvayavyāvarttyamāha-anyatheti ekataradalamātropādāne ityarthaḥ| taduttareti ghaṭadaṇḍipadottaretyarthaḥ. tathāca `ghaṭatvam' ityādau dravyatvasyeva tvanmate daṇḍītyādau daṇḍasambandhasyāpyapadārthatayā'prakāratvādabhāvatvena na tasya pratyayārthatvasambhava iti bhāvaḥ|
vastutastvantyadalopādānaṃ vyartam, matubādīnāṃ sambanadhyarthakatayā daṇḍītyādivākyajanyabodhe sambandhasyaiva prakāratvena daṇḍādīnāṃ prakṛtyarthe'prakāratvādevānatiprasaṅgāt| mīmāṃsakaikadeśimate matvarthoyānāṃ sambandhavācakatve'pi tadākṣiptāśrayanīyam| sambandhaḥ prakāra iti asya yena tasya tvādipratyayavācyatā syāditi śeṣaḥ|
mīmāṃsakamatamupanyasyati-yattviti|  atra daṇiḍitvamityādau| svasamavetaḥ--tvādiprakṛtyarthasamavetaḥ| nīlatvāmityādau nīlādiguṇavanna sambhavati yato'taḥ sambandhasyaiva bhāvapratyayavācyatvamāśritamityarthaḥ| daṇḍādeḥ prakṛtyarthāśe prakāratve'pi prakṛtyarthāsamavetatvenābhāvatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
sambandhivācakatveneti "tadasyāstyasmin"  matupaḥ sambandhe vidhāne'pi, 'daṇḍī puruṣaḥ" iti sāmānādhikaraṇyānupodhāt sambandhivācakatvasyāvaśyakatvāditi bhāvaḥ| gatyabhāvābhāvāditi sambandhasyaiva bhāvatvasambhavāditi bhāvaḥ| ādāveveti evam `vaiśvadevī' ityādisāmānādhikaraṇyasyoktarītyaivopapatterityantagranthena bhūṣaṇe ityarthaḥ| tavāpīti vaiyākaraṇasyāpītyarthaḥ| idam-kvacijjātyādivācakatvam kvacit sambandhavācaktavamityevaṃ rūpaṃ vaiṣaṃmyamityarthaḥ|
nanu prakṛtijanyobodhaprakārakatvenaivopādheḥ saṃgrahe tasya pṛthagupādānaṃ vyrathamta āha-prakṛtijanyoti| sa ca prakṛtyarthavṛttirasādhāraṇo dharmastasminnasādhāraṇatvam-taditarāvṛttitve sati sakalatad‌vṛttitvam| satyantopādāne dravyatvāderghaṭāsādhāraṇyam, viśeṣyopādānācca tadvayaktitvādinirāsaḥ| tena rūpeṇa dharmo bhāvapratyayavācyaḥ| tatra tatpadārthasya ghaṭādestvādiprakṛtyaiva lābhādananyalabhya itarāvṛttitvādau bhāvapratyayasya śaktiḥ| tatrāpītaravṛttitvamabhāvaḥ| sākalyaṃ vṛttimacca viśakalitamarthaḥ| itaravṛttitvapratiyogikābhāvasyātiprasakhtatvāditaravṛttitvatvavacchinnapratiyogitākābhāvasyāprasiddhatvād ghaṭatvādau bādhācca viśiṣṭasya śakytavāsambhavāt| atra ca prakṛtijanyobode yena sambandhena pratipādyatāvacchedakadharmasya prakāratā tena sambandhenetarāvṛttitvaṃ vṛttimattvañca bodhyam| nātaḥ kālādau kālikādisbandhena ghaṭatvasya vṛttāvapi kṣatiḥ| navā ghaṭatvamityādau kambugrīvādimattvasya bhānam| prameyatvamityādau tvatārāvṛttitvāṃśo na bhāsate, aprasiddhatvāt, kintu sakalaprameyavṛttitvamātr| evam `ekatvam' ityatra sākālyāṃśo'pi| naca viśiṣṭasyānatiriktatvamate guṇānyatvaviśiṣṭasattvamityādau viśiṣṭasattve viśiṣṭasaditarāvṛttitvasya mahattvāmityādau sakalamahad‌vṛttitvasya mahatparimāṇādau cāsambhava iti vācyam, taditaratvavyāpakātyantābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakatad‌vṛttyatyantābhāvapratiyogitānavacchedakadharmavattve daladvayatātparyye ṇādoṣāt| tatrādyadalaṃ ghaṭatvamityādau dravyatvāderasādhāraṇyavāraṇāyeti bhāvaḥ  prayoga iti śabdapravṛttāvityarthaḥ| nimittamiti yaddharmavattvena jñāte'rthe yaśśabdaḥ prayujyate sa dharmastacchabdapravṛttau nimittamityarthaḥ| sa dāmodaratvamityādau jātiḥ, śuklatvamityādau guma eveti|  prakāratvāditi ghaṭatvāsādhāraṇavarmatvādityartha| laghugurviti ayaṃ bhāvaḥ-dharmasyāsādhāraṇadharmatvena vācyatve'pyekatvamityādau parimāṇāderbhānāpattiḥ, uktāsādhāraṇyasya parimāṇe sattvāt| naca prakṛtyarthatāvacchedakanirūpitatvaviśiṣṭasamāvāyasambandhena taditarāvṛttitvaṃ tad‌vṛttitvañca vivakṣita| naca tena sambandhena parimāṇaṃ tatra vṛttīti noktadoṣa iti vācyam, tathā satyanantaśaktikalpanāvaśyakatve tattaddharmatvenaiva dharme śaktirastu, kimitarāvṛttitvādigauravasahiṣṇutayā|
taduktaṃ gumadīdhitau-pṛthaktvamityādau prakṛtyarthatāvacchedakaguṇāna bhāvapratyayaḥ prapipādayati| pratītustu tādrupyeṇa dharmatvena vetyabhipretyāha mūle-dharmamātramiti-asya tattad‌rūpeṇa, dharmatvena veti śeṣaḥ| etena bhāvaśaktatvādipratyayena kambugrīvādimattvamityādau gurorapi dharmasya kambugrīvādimattvasya bādhanena tadvad‌ ghātvamityādāvapi gurordharmasya tvapratyayena bodhanasambhavādāśaṅkā niḥsāretyapāstam| dharmasya bhāvapratyayavācyatve sammatimāha-tattad‌vyaktīti tattad‌vyaktyupalakṣitetyarthaḥ| ghaṭatvaghaṭatvatvādirūpatvāt ghaṭaghaṭatvapadottaratvapratyayārthatvādityarthaḥ| jāteruktasattātmakatve bhāvapratyayārthatve ca mānamāha-sambandhibedāditi| `jāti padārthaḥ' iti kalpe sādhakamidam| smabandhibhedāt-anuyogibhedāt, bhidyamānā-gavetarāsamavetatve sati sakalagosamavetatvādinā tad‌vyaktyuparāgeṇa vā pārthakyena pratīyamānāṃ gavādiniṃṣṭhā brāhmasattaiva jātirityucyate| tasyāṃ sarve śabdā vācakatvena vyavasthitā ityarthaḥ| tadeva vyaktīkaroti-tāmiti tāṃ tattad‌vyaktyuparaktāṃ sattāmityarthaḥ|
dhātvarthamiti ākṛtyādhikaraṇanyāyena, "jātimanye kriyāmāhuḥ" iti pakṣābhiprāyeṇa vā| mahānityātmaviśeṣaṇam| kvacijjātyādiḥ, kvacit sambandārta ityuktārthe vārttikaṃ pramāṇayati-uktañceti guṇaśabdasya vaiśeṣikāṇāṃ rūpādiṣveva prasiddheḥ| prakṛtopayuktārthaparatāmāha-viśeṣaṇatayeti| rūpādītyādinā rasādeḥ, guṇaparaśuklādeśca saṅgrahaḥ| bodhajanakatva iti bodhajanakatvamata ityarthaḥ| vyapekṣāvāde kriyākārakabhāvasambhandhena kṛdarkhakartrādau dhātvarthasyaiva prakāratayā bhānopagamāditi bhāvaḥ|
svasiddhāntamanusutyāha-saṃsargaprakāraketi| sūpapādeti bhāvapratyayo jātāvevetyādyaniyamāditi bhāvaḥ| tatreti jāṃtyādivācakabhāvapratyayeṣu madhye ityarthaḥ| jātivācakānāmiti arthaniṣṭhaśakteriti śeṣaḥ|
guṇādyatmakabhāvavananānāṃ śuklatvādiśabdānāṃ jāteḥ śakyatāvacchedakatvasambhavādāha-jātivācakānāmitibhāvapratyayānāmiti śeṣaḥ| avacchedikā iti upasthitatvād ghaṭatvādijātiniṣṭhaśakteḥ svetarāvṛttitvāvacchinnanikhilasvavṛttitvarūpādheyatvādisambandhenāvacchedikā ityarthaḥ| tena na dravyatvādijātiniṣṭhaśakterghaṭāvaccachinnatvam| avacchedakānugamakañca ghaṭtavādikameveti bhāvaḥ| paryyavasitabodhamāha-ghaṭavṛttiriti| pakṣāntaramiti tattacchabda eva bhāvapratyayavācya iti matāntaramityarthaḥ| "tatsūcanaprayojanako'pīti yadvā śabdaghaṭitoktavārtikasūcanaprayojanako'pītyarthaḥ| apinā pakṣāntarasaṅgrahaḥ| vārttike "svenārthena bhavantītyatra svaśabdasyātmīyavacanatayā ātmīyārthena jātyādina pravartate ityarthāt kathaṃ pakṣāntaraparatā, kathaṃ vārthāvṛtterdharmasya śabdasya bhāvatvañcetyasya āśayaṃ prakāśayati-
ayaṃ bhāva iti| jātyādyarthasya dravyāṃśe viśeṣaṇatāyāḥ prasiddhatvāttad‌dṛṣṭāntena śabdasyātapi tāṃ sādhayati-arthavaditi jātyādivadityarthaḥ| śabdasyārthaviśeṣatāyāmasādhāraṇasthalamāha-harihareti| `kaścidāsīt' ityanena jātyādiprakārakabodhāsmabhavo dhvanyate| haryyādau sannikarṣābhāvenānupasthitaharitvādyavacchedena| śaktigrahābhāvena taddharmaprakārakaśābdabodāsambhavādupasthitapadasyaiva viśeṣaṇatvamabhyupagamya śābdabodha upapādanīyaḥ| yathāca vṛttyā tadupasthitistathopapāditaṃ nāmārthanirūpaṇa iti bhāvaḥ|
anyatheti arthāṃśe śabdasya prakāratvānabyupagam ityarthaḥ| āraṇyakānāṃ sannikarṣeṇa jātidharmiṃtāvacchedakaśaktigrahasambhavāduktam-nāgarikāniti| evameveti padaprakāreṇaivetyarthaḥ| aprasiddheti| agṛhītajātitvādiviśiṣṭaśaktikapadeṣvityarthaḥ| jātyādibhedeneti tadviseṣeṇetyarthaḥ| kvacittu jātyādirūpeṇaivetyeva pāṭhaḥ| ubhayamiti| jātyādirūpaṃ padañcetyarthaḥ| avacchedakamiti śaktyavācchedakamityarthaḥ|
nanu jātyādyanupasthitidaśāyām `ayamartha etatpadavān' iti śaktigraho'pyasambhavaduktiko'ta āha-padāprakāraka iti| viśiṣyeti ghaṭapadatvādirūpeṇetyarthaḥ| yadyapi `sarve bhāvāḥ' iti sarvaśabdopādānādetatkalpe śabdasyeva sarvaśabdapravṛttinimittatvamavagamyate| tathāpi ghaṭatvādinā gṛhītaśaktikaghaṭādipadād‌ ghaṭatvaprakārakavodhasya sārvajanīnaprasiddhisiddhasyāpahnotumaśakyatayā prāyaśaḥ prasiddhārthakasthale śabdāprakārabodhasyānanubhavena taitatkalpe'pi jātyādīnāṃ śabdapravṛttinimittatvamaṅagīkaraṇīyameva|
vārttikaṃntu bhāvaśabdasyāprasiddhārthakaśabdaparatayā yojyam, tatra nimittāntarābhāvena vārtikabalācchabdasyaiva śabdapravṛttinimittatvāvadhāraṇāt| yathā-kādipañcakaśaktakupade| ata eva "anudāttaṅiṅataḥ"  iti sūtre'nudāttaṅitau dvāvarthaḥ" iti tatra "saṅkhayātānudeśaḥ prāpnoti" iti yathāsaṅkhayasūtre bhāṣyakṛduktaṃ saṅgacchate| anyathā navānāṃ taṅarthatvenātmanepadavattvena bodhe sarveṣāmekatvena tatra ca dvāvityasyānupapattiḥ spaṣṭaiva| ata eva sārakṛtā'pi `ubhayamavacchedakam' ityupasaṃhṛtam-kvacicchabdo'pīti tadāśaya iti| kathaṃ-tarhi tatra śaktigraho'ta āha-kittviti| kvacicchaktamiti kiñcinnaṣṭhaśaktinirūpakamityarthaḥ| tathāca nṛpādivarmanaprastāve nṛpatvādinopasthitanalādīnāṃ saktyaṃśe bhāne upasthitatvānnalādipadānāmapi tadarthe bhānamaviruddham' idametacchakyam' ityākārakārthaśaktigraha eva viśeṣarūpeṇopasthitestantratvāditi bhāvaḥ| tathaiva bodha iti padavācyatvaprakāraka evetyarthaḥ| evakāreṇa jātyādiprakārakabodhavyavacchedaḥ| idañca kaścidartho nalādipadāśakyaḥ, arthatvādityānumānikaśaktigrahābhiprāyem̐ṇa| uktopanītapadaśaktigrahe tvarthaviśeṣyakabodhānupapattireva| śaktijñānapadārthopasthitiśābdabodhānāṃ samānaprakārakatvaniyamādityavadheyam|
naiyāyikāstu-nalādipadānāmapi viśiṣya śaktigraho nānupapannaḥ, yatra nalatvādisambandhena yatkiñciddharmavattvadhīstadviṣayībhūtanalatvādeḥ śaktigrahe dharmitāvacchedakatayā bhāne bādhakābhāvāt| yadvā yatra `prameyatvena prameyavān' ityākārānalatvādivisiṣ‍buddhiḥ| tataśca prameyatvāṃśe moṣadaśāyāṃ svarūpato nalatvādiprakārakanalaviśeṣyakasmaraṇe bādhakābhāvāttadviṣayanalatvādinā viśiṣya nalādau nalādipadaśaktigrahema tattadrupeṇa nalādipadānnalādibodhasya sulabhatvāt| naca prameyatvāṃśe moṣakalpane mānābhāvaḥ, anubhūyamānatattaddharmaprakārakaśābdabodhānupapattereva mānatvāt| kathamanyathā'nubhavātmakasamūhālambanādekapadārthodvodhakāsamavadhāne'nyapadārthasmṛtiḥ| aklṛptasyārthāṃśe padaviśeṣaṇatvasya kalpanāpekṣayā tādṛsasmṛtau prameyatvāṃśe moṣakalpanasyaiva nyāyyatvāt, padasyārthadarmatvābhāvena tasya padāṃśe viśeṣaṇatvasambhavācca, tasya padasya tasmin pade śaktirityarthasyālaukikatvena padaprakārakabodhasya sārvatrikatvāsambhavāccetyāhuḥ 
iti vaiyākaraṇabhūṣaṇasāradarpaṇe bhāvapratyayārthanirūpaṇam || 9 ||

 nanu devatāyā indrādipadāllābhena tadantarbhāveṇa taddhitaśaktikalpanamanarthakamata āha-devatātvopasthāpaketi indratvena rūpeṇa devatopasthitāvapi devatātvena rūpeṇa tadupasthāpakābhāvādityarthaḥ| tena rūpeṇeti-devatātvena rūpeṇendropasthitaye ityarthaḥ| anyathā-
tadvitena caturthyā vā mantraliṅgena vā punaḥ|
devatāsaṅgatistatra durbalaṃ ca paraṃ param||
iti mīmāṃsakasidadhāntāsaṅgatiriti bhāvaḥ| dravyasya devatāsambandhitvañca taduddeśyakatyāgākarmatvaṃ tyāgeddeśyatvamapi vedabodhitābādhitadravyasvāmitprakāreṇecchāviṣayatvam| tadeva ca devatātvam| nāto ghṛtādisampradānabrahmaṇasya tyāgoddeśyatve'pi devatātvam| tasya tatsvāmitvābādhāt `endro mantraḥ' ityādau mantrasya taddevatākatvañca na taduddeśyakatyāgakaraṇatvam| vinā'pi mantramicchāviśeṣa-mṛkatyāgotpattyā'vyabhicārāt| kintu tyāgāhgoccārayaṇakatvam| vañca devatāsambandhī pratyayārthaḥ, natu taddevatākatvam, `aindraṃ haviḥ' iti sāmānādhikaraṇyānupapatteḥ| sa cāmikṣādipadasambhivyāhāre tattd‌dravyema pratīyate| `deyaṃ pratyayārthaḥ' iti tu tasyāmikṣādipadasamabhivyāhāre dānakarmatvenāpi pratīterityabhipretyoktamiti bhāvaḥ| uktārthe sammatimāha-ata eveti devatāsambandhinaḥ pratyayārthatvādevetyarthaḥ| sānnidhyāditi sā-āmikṣā vaiśvadevītyatrāmikṣāpadasamabhivyāhārādityarthaḥ| viṣayārpaṇamiti āmikṣātmakadravyasyaiva devatoddeśyakatyāgakarmatvapratītirityarthaḥ| kevalāditi somādipadāsamabhivyāhṛtāt| agneḥ-agniśabdāt, samuccaran-pratyayatvāt agniśabdāt puraḥ prādurbhavat| 'agneyo'ṣṭākapālo bhavatyamāvāsyāyām" ityādau| nānyeti somādisāhityāpannāgnidevatoddeśyakatyāgaviṣayaṃ dravyaṃ pratipādayituṃ kintu taddevatākameveti tadbhāvaḥ| tathāca devatāviśiṣṭadeyasya taddhitārthatvaṃ teṣāmapi sammatamiti bhāvaḥ|
prakṛtyartheti `padārthaḥ padārtenānveti' iti vyutpatteriti bhāvaḥ| asmin kalpe ca prakṛtyarthasyābhedasambandhena devatārūpataddhitārthe'nvayaḥ| tasya ca svoddeśyakatyāgakarmatvasambandhena dravyarūpataddhitāparapadārthe iti bodhyam  dyotakatvanaya iti vācakatvanaye prakarṣasyopasargārthatvena tadviśiṣṭe dhātorlakṣaṇāyā akalpanena dṛṣṭhāntatvāsambhavāditi bhāvaḥ| prakṛṣṭajayavaditi prakṛtyaṃśamātramādāya dṛṣṭāntatvaṃ bodhyam|
lābhāditi samabhivyāhṛtadadhyādipadena devatārūpasambandibhānādityarthaḥ| kutaḥ punariti " ananyalabhyo hi śabdārthaḥ" iti nyāyāt| kintu sambandhārthakatvamevocitamiti bhāvaḥ| tatpratīteriti devatāsambandhipratīterityarthaḥ| tathāca tadanupapattyā taddhitasya tadarthakatvamāvaśyakamiti bhāvaḥ|
padāntarāśravaṇe sambandhipratīterapyarthanirṇeyatvādāha-endraṃ dadhīti sambandhamātrārthakatve sāmānādhikaraṇyānupapattirityarthaḥ| anyatheti uktānupapattstadasādhakatve ityarthaḥ| `pacyate taṇḍulaḥ' ityātra sāmānādhikaraṇyānupapattirhi kartrādivācitvasādhiketi bhāvaḥ| vaiyākaraṇān pratīyamāpattiḥ| mīmāṃsakān kaṭākṣīkṛtyāha-mīmāsakānāmiti tanmate tattaddvatākatvarūpapratyayārthena dharmiṇa ākṣepāllābhena sāmānādhikaraṇyopapattiriti bhāvaḥ| `padāntarāt' ityākṣepasyā'pyupalakṣaṇam| vācyaṃ māstviti ākhyātasya karttevaṃ taddhitasya dravyaṃ vācyaṃ māstvityarthaḥ| iṣṭhāpattau `āmikṣāṃ devatāyuktām' iti tvatsiddhāntabhaṅga iti bhāvaḥ|
nanu teṣāṃ karturananyalabhyatvānna pratyayavācyam, kintu prādhānyāpattereva tattvamata āha-digiti| darthastu "prakṛtipratyayārthayoḥ" iti nyāsya saṅkhayādau vyabhicāreṇa tena prādhānyāpattyabhāvāt| "bhāvapradhānamākhyātam" taddhitena curthyā vā" iti nyāyasya sarvasiddhasyānupapattiḥ| naca taddhitasya tadarthakatve'pi kathaṃ tadapekṣayā caturthyā jaghanyatvam, tyāgoddaśyakarūpadevatābodhakatvasyobhayatrāviśiṣṭatvāditi vācyam, nahi detāsambandhideyamātraṃ taddhitārthaḥ| kintu vedabodhyadevatātvaghaṭitaḥ| evañca taddhitārthena yāgasya devatātvāṃśe vedabodhyatvāvagāhitvena tadaṃśe apramāṇyaśaṅkāyā anudayāttaditi viniyojakatayā balavattvaṃ caturthyā tu tadaṃśe vedabodhyatvānavagāhivodhasyaiva jananena tatrāprāmāṇyaśaṅkānirāsārthamupāyāntara syāpekṣaṇājjaghanyatavam|
yadvā taddhitena devatātvena rūpema devatābodhe "sā'sya devatā'  smāraṇamasti| caturthyāstu devatātve na smaraṇam, "caturtho sampradāne" iti  iti sampradāna eva tatsmaraṇāt| sampradānatvañca-tyajyamānadravyoddeśyakatve sati pratigrahītṛtvam| evañca tataḥ sampradānatvaghaṭakatayā devatātvapratītāvapi taddhitādiva mukhyatayā devatātvānadhigamāt tasyāstaddhitāpekṣayā jaghanyatvam| evaṃ mantravarṇādadhiṣṭhānapratītāvapi devatātvasya sākṣādapratītermantravarṇasya caturthyapekṣayā jaghanyatvam| evaṃ mantravarṇādadhiṣṭhānapratītāvapi devatātvasya sākṣādapratītermantravarmasya caturthyapekṣayā jaghanyatvaṃ bodhayam| taddhitasya devatārthakatvānabhyupagame tu tadasaṅtiḥ spaṣṭaiveti|
nanvindrādipadācchaktyaiva devatālābhe ta6 lakṣaṇā'bhyupagamo vyartho'ta āha-devatātvena rūpeṇeti| tathāca "śakyādanyena rūpeṇa jñāne bhavati lakṣaṇā" iti vṛddhokterdevatāyā indrasvarūpāyāḥ śakyatve'pīndrabhinnadevatātvena tadbhānārthaṃ socitaiveti bhāvaḥ| anupapattijñānapūrvokāyāmapi tīraniṣṭhagaṅgāpadalakṣaṇāyāṃ nirūḍhatvavyavahārādāha-anāditi etattattvamabhihitaṃ prāk‌  ekadeśānvayaprasaṅgamāśaṅ‌kyāha-praharaṇakrīḍeti vyutpattivaicitryādekapadopasthitayorapi tayoḥ parasparamanvaya iti bhāvaḥ| praharamasyāpi prakṛtyaiva lābhamabhipretyāha-kraḍāmātrañceti krīḍārūpasambandhyevetyarthaḥ| mātrapadena tatpraharaṇasya taddhitārthatvavyavacchedaḥ| tathāca daṇḍābhinnapraharasādhanaviśiṣṭā krīḍeti bodhaḥ| so'syeti etatsūtravihitapratyayasya nivāsaḥ sambandhī cārthaḥ| atra nivāsaśabdo'dhikaraṇaghañantaḥ-niśabdena ca vāse nairantaryyarūpātiśayaḥ pratyāyyate| sa ca prakaraṇādinā tattatkālaghaṭito grahyaḥ| tathāca stru dhnābhinnanivāsādhikaraṇasambandhīti bodhaḥ| nivāsaśca pratyāsattyā pratyayārthasambandhikartṛka eva| ata eva kādācitkastru dhne vāsakartari na tathāprayogaḥ|
sā'sminniti sūtravihitataddhitasya tu paurṇamāsīghaṭaṭitatvāvacchinno'rtha-| nakṣatranuktakālavihitāṇādyantapauṣyādiśabdānāṃ puṣyādyabhinnanakṣatrakarmakaśaśibhogāśrayakālo'rthaḥ| tasyoktārthaikadeśapaurṇamāsyāmabhedenānvayaḥ| tathāca `pauṣomāsaḥ' ityādau puṣyādyabhinnanakṣatrakarmakaśaśibhogāśrayakālābhinnapaurṇamāsīghaṭito māsa ityādibodhaḥ| pauṣādipadañca na kevalayogikam, `pauṣaḥ paśraḥ' iti vyavahāravirahāt, kintu rūḍhamapi| etad‌bodhanāyaiva sūtre saṃjñāgrahaṇam| rūḍhinirūpakatā'vacchedakañca na triṃśattithisamudāyatvarūpamāsatvamātram| yatkiñcittithyavadhikatriṃśaktithisamudāye māsādivyavahāre'pi cādrasaurapauṣādibahirbhūtapauṣyādighaṭitatraṃśattithyantargatatithiṣu pauṣādivyavahāravirahāt, kintu śuklapratipadādirśāntatithisamudāyatvamato noktadoṣa ityādyanyatra vistaraḥ|
"tadasyāstyasmin"  iti sūtreṇa prathamāntādastyarthopādhikāt sambandinyādhikaraṇe ca matvarthoyo vidhīyate| yadyapyasyetyukteḥ ṣaṣṭhayarthasambanda eva manubādividhānamābhāti tathā'pi `gomām̐ścaitraḥ' iti sāmānādhikaraṇyānurodhāt sambandhivācakataivetyuktaṃ prāk‌| evañca `gomān' ityādau gonirupitasvāmitvasambandhavānityādyanvayabodhaḥ| naca-
bhūmanindāpraśaṃsāsu nityayoge'taśāyane|
sambandhe'stivivakṣāyāṃ bhavanti matubādayaḥ|
iti sambandhasamakakṣatayā bhūmādyarthānāmapyupādānāt teṣāmapi matubādiśakyatvāmiti śaṅkayam, syādevaṃ yadi teṣvartheṣu matuvādīnāṃ vidhāyakaṃ sūtrādikamupalabhyate, kintu prayogopavitvameva teṣām, astyarthavat, gāvādipadānāṃ bahutvādiviśiṣṭalākṣaṇikatayaiva bhūmādipratītisaulabhyācca| anye tvastiśabdānmatubarthaṃ tad‌grahamamityāhurityanyatra vistaraḥ|
ityādikamityādipadāt `tasyāpatyam' "tena raktaṃ rāgāt" "saṃrakṛtaṃ bhakṣāḥ" ityādisaṅgrahaḥ| tatra 'gārgiḥ' ityādau taddhitārthāpatyasya janyapuruṣasyaikadeśe janyatve-prakṛtyarthagargādernirūpakatayā'nvayāt 'garganirūpitajanyatāśrayaḥ pumān' ityādibodhaḥ| `gārgyaḥ' ityādau tu janyapuruṣajanyapuruṣādyātmakagotrāpatyarthaikadeśe janyatve prakṛtyarthānvayaḥ| `gārgyāyaṇaḥ' ityādau taddhitārthayuvāpatyaikadeśe gotrapratyayāntārthasya nirūpakatayā'nvayaḥ| parantu "jīvati tu vaṃsye yuva"| iti paribhāṣaṇād gargajīvanasamānakālikatvamapi pradhānapratyayārtha viśeṣaṇam, tasyāpi pratyayaśakyatvāt| evañca, gargajīvanasamānakāliko gargagotrāpatyanirūpitajanyatāvānayaṃ pumāniti bodhaḥ|
"ne raktam" iti vihitapratyayasya tatsampabandhādhīnatadīyarūpāropaviṣayo'rthaḥ| tādṛśāropaviṣayatvamva tena raktatvam| `śaṅkhaḥ pīta.' ityāropamādāya śaṅkhāderāropyapītimāśrayaharitālādinā raktatvavāraṇāyādhīnāntamāropraviseṣaṇam| māñjiṣṭhapaṭādeścakṣurādinā raktatvavāraṇāya tadīyatvarūpaviśeṣaṇam| atra ca rāgakaraṇasya mañjiṣṭhādeḥ prakṛtyarthasya taddhitārtha syaikadeśasambandhe pratiyogitayā rūpe cādheyatayā'nvayaḥ| tathāca mañjiṣṭhāsambandhādhīno yo mañjiṣṭhāniṣṭharūpāropastadviṣayatāśraya iti `māñjiṣṭhaḥ' ityato bodha-. "śūlyam," "ukhyam" ityādau "saskṛtaṃ bhakṣāḥ"  iti sūtravihitataddhitārthaikadeśe pākādisaṃskāre śūlokhādeḥ prakṛtyarthasyādhikaraṇatvenānvayaḥ| anayaiva rītyā taddhitāntarād bādha ūhanīyaḥ|
taddhitānāṃ kvacitprakṛtyarthe svārthaikadeśasya, kvacit svārthesyaivānvayabodhakatvāditi jahatsvārthavṛttipakṣamāvalambya mūlamavatārayati vṛttimātra iti tathāca tatpakṣe padānāmānarthak‌yāt‌ tadarthanirūpaṇe siddhāntaprañyutiriti bhāvaḥ|
tadrītyaiveti sāstrāntarīyarītyaivetyarthaḥ| nanu vaiyākaraṇamate jahatsvārthavṛttyāśrayaṇe "na pūjanāt" "tena raktaṃ rāgāt" ityādisūtrāṇāṃ kā gatirata āha-āropiteti| viśiṣṭasaktyaiveti| viśiṣṭārthasyeti śeṣaḥ| vṛttau pakṣadvayābhyupagamena jahatsvārthapakṣe tad‌ghaṭakapadārthapūpaṇasyānupayāge'pyaja tsvārthapakṣe viśiṣṭaśaktigrahasya pratyekaśaktinirūpaṇāpajīvakatayā tannirūpaṇāsyaucityameva| etatsūcanāyaiva mūle'tīvetyupāttamiti tu rahasyam 
iti vaiyākaraṇabhūṣaṇasāradārpaṇe devatāpratyayārthanirūpaṇam || 10 ||

 nanu prakrānte prātipadikaghaṭakapratyayārthanirūpaṇe'bhedaikatvasaṅkhayānirūpaṇamasaṅgatamata āha-pṛttiprasaṅgāditi tathāca vṛttinirūpaṇe kriyamāṇe smṛtipathaprāptataddharmopekṣā'nuciteti prasaṅgasaṅgatisattvānnoktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ| yadyapi vakṣyamāṇaktvādyante'pi kṛd‌vṛttisattvādattadanantarameva saṅkhayānirūpayitumucitā, tathāpi tatprakṛtyarthe saṅkhayāyā abhānādākāṅkṣāvicchedaḥ syādityatraiva tannirūpitamiti bodhyam| abhedaikatvasaṅkhayāpadārtheḥ. matabhedena dvidhā-nirvakti- saṅkhayā viśeṣāṇāmiti ekatvatvādyavacchinnānāmityarthaḥ| avibhāgeta-abhedena-avirodheneti yāvat| uktārthe harivākyaṃ pramāṇayati-uktañceti| āhitaśaktayaḥ āhitā-samarpitā tattaddoṣavibhedanivandhanā virodharūpā śaktiryairityarthaḥ| tyaktavirodhā iti yāvat| ata eva avibhāgena-avirodhenetyarthaḥ| tām-abhedaikatvasaṅkhayām| tādadṛśīṃ-madhuniṣṭharasasthititulyāmityarthaḥ| nanvekatvadvitvādīnāmekatra sattvamanupapannam| nahi dvāvekatvena vyavahaṇiyate, nāpyeko dvāviti, "nahi dviṣutraḥ" iti bhāvaṣyāt| kiñca dṛṣṭānto'pi na samañjasaḥ, tattadrasavadārabdhe'vayavini mākṣike'vayavasajātīyarasāntarasyaibotpādābhyupagamādata āha-parityakteti vāśabdaḥ pakṣāntare| agṛhītīviseṣadharmakaṃ skhyāmātramityarthaḥ| pūrvakalpe uktāsvarasābhisandhikaṃ vākyapadīyaṃ tatpakṣe'pi pramāṇatvenopanyasyati-bhedānāmiti bhedānām-ekatvatvadvitvatvādiviśeṣadharmaṇāṃ parityāgād-agrahāt| idañca vyāpārapadārtheabhedenānvayi| saṃkhyātmā-saṃkhyāsvarūpaḥ| saḥ-ekatvādiḥ| tathāvidhaḥ-abhedaikatvavyapadeśya ityarthaḥ|
tadeva viśadayati- jātibhāgasyeti ekatvatvāderityarthaḥ|
bhedāpoheneti bhedasya viśeṣamasya yo'poho'graharūpo yo vyāpāro viśeṣaṇā'tmā tato varttata ityarthaḥ| "bhedāpoho na varttate" tathācaikatvatvāderjātiviśeṣasya bhedāgraharūpo yo vyāpāro viśeṣaṇā'tmā tato varttata ityarthaḥ| "bhedāpoho na varttate" iti vā pāṭhe tu bhedarūpo yo'poho'tad‌vyāvṛttaṃ sa na varttate-na bhāsate ityartho'vaseyaḥ| padyāntarāvalokanenaitatpāṭhasyaiva harisammatatvamadhyavasīyate| dvitvatvādyagrahakālisaṅkhayātvaprakārakapratītigaucaraḥ saṅkhayāpadārtho'bhedaikatvapadavyapadeśyo bhavatīti samuditārthaḥ| tatra dṛṣṭāntaramāha-agṛhītaviśeṣeṇeti śuklatvādiprakārakagrahāsamānakālikarūpatvaprakārakapratītiviṣayeṇa śuklādinaiva yathā ghaṭādī rūpavāniti pratīyate| śuklāderyā bhedarūpo'pohaḥ śuklatvādiratad‌vyāvṛttirūpaḥ sa tu na pratīyate| tadvadrājapūrūṣa ityādau rājādipadārthajha saṅkhayāvāniti pratīyate, na tatra saṅkhayāṃ'śeekatvatvādi bhāsate ityarthaḥ|
tādṛśasaṅkhayābhānasya parānabhyupetatvāt kathamiyaṃ sthitirataḥ śeṣarūraṇena vyācaṣṭe-vaiyāraṇānāmiti| nanu tādṛśasaṅkayāyā vṛttau bhāne kiṃ pramāṇamata āha-ayaṃ bhāva iti| sāmānyajñānapūrviketi taddhetuketyarthaḥ| āvaśyakīti tāṃ vinoktajijñāsāyā abhāvāditi bhāvaḥ| sā ca saṃkhyāpūrvottarādārthayoḥ sambandhavad vṛttisakyaiva ajahatsvārthāvṛttikṣe pūrvapadasya saṃkhyāviśeṣavācakatve gamakābhāve, tena `dviputraḥ', `maudrikaḥ' `māsajātaḥ' `tāvakīnaḥ' `bhādrirāmavasara' ityādau vṛttāvabhedaikatvasaṃkhyāyā abhāne'pi na kṣatiḥ|
ādyo-saṃkhyāviśaiṣasyaiva pūrvapadārthatvāt| dvitīye-ekamudrasya krayakaraṇāyogyatvād bahutvāvagatiḥ| tṛtīye-viśiṣṭakālāṃvagamāya māsapadaprayogāt saṃkhyā'dhyavasāyaḥ. caturthe-tavakādyādeśādekatvāvagātiḥ| pañcame-prakaraṇād dvitvāvagatiḥ| evam-`kārakamadhye' ityādāvapyūhyam, ekasya madhyāsambhavāt| sati prakāraṇe `rājapūruṣaḥ' ityādāvapi saṃkhyāviśeṣaprakāreṇaiva bhānam| tadā tatrāpi na tasya vṛttyarthatvamiti bhedo bhedasahitā dvitvādisaṃkhyā, tadabhāvasahitamekatvama bhedaikatvamiti vyutpattyā saṃkhyātvenaikatvamevopasarjanapadārthe bhāsate| tathā-"ekavacanamutsargataḥ kariṣyate" iti vacanāt `rājan as‌-citragurityādau, citrā-as-go-as-ityabhipretyottaragranthamavatārayati-tasyā iti vṛttighaṭakapadārthopasarjananavyaktarityartheḥ| pratītāviti saṃkhyātvāvacchinnaikatvaprakāratānirūpitaviseṣyatvena bodha ityarthaḥ| upasarjanapadārthe saṃkhyātvenaikatvameva pratīyate, na dvitvādītyatra dṛṣṭāntamāheti yāvat| gaṇanāyāmiti bahutvaviśiṣṭānekasaṃkhyāne ityarthaḥ| "kapiñjalānālabheta" iti vākyena bahutvavyāpyasaṃkhyāviśiṣṭakapiñjalālambhanaṃ vidhīyate| tatra trayāṇām, caturādīnāṃ vā''lambhamiti saṃśeye bahutvavyāpyatvasya tritvādiṣu sarvaiṣvapyaviśiṣṭatvādyathāprāptamevālabdhavyamiti pūrvasya caturādīnāmālamabhanam, "mā hiṃsyāt" iti sāmānyaśāstravihitaniṣedhasyālabhyatāvad‌vyaktītaraparatvarūpatātparyasaṃkocakalpane gauravād gaṇanāyāṃ prathamopasthitatritvaparatayā'pi vacanasya cāritārthyāllāghavācca bahutvavyāpyatvena tritvameva bahuvacanārtha iti siddhāntitam| tannyāyādatrāpi saṃkhyātvenaikatvasyaiva vṛttyarthatvam, natu dvitvādināmapratīyamānaviśeṣadharmakaikatvamabhedaikatvamiti vyutpatteriti bhāvaḥ| "nijāṃ trayāṇām"  ityādinirddeśairasmin vyākaraṇe vyāptinvāyasyaivāśrayaṇena prakṛte saṃkhyātvenaikatvapratītirhi durupapādetyapi kecit|
nanu jijñāsāyāṃ śābdameva sāmānyadharmaprakārakajñānadarśanamapekṣitamiti na niyamaḥ āmrādiphaladarśanenāpi tadrasā svādanecchāyā vyabhicārāt| nacaivaṃ sāmānyadharmaprakārakajñānasyaiva hetutvaṃ vilīyeteti vācyam, āmradarśanasyod‌bodhakatayā tadrasasmaraṇena tadupapatteḥ| prakṛte tasyā aśakyatve'pi tadavyabhicaritadravyatvenānumānikatad‌bodhasambhavāt tādṛśajijñāsāyā ananubhavācca noktayuktirvṛttirebhedaikatvasakhyārthatvasādhiketyabhipretyāha-vastuta iti jijñāsaiveti-`rājapuruṣaḥ' ityādau pūrvapadārthe ekatva-dvitvādiprakārakajijñāsaivetyarthaḥ| anubhavasiddheti| asya yena tadanurodhena sāmānyadharma prakārakajñānisyāvaśyakatā syāditi śeṣaḥ| `abhedaikatvasaṃkhyāyā vṛttau bhānam' iti mūlaṃ tu prācīnagranthānuvāda eveti bhāvaḥ|
anubhavasiddhatvasya śapathanirṇeyatvaṃ matvā''ha-tathātve veti vāśabdo'nāsthāyām, anubhavasiddhatve vetyarthaḥ| samānaprakārakatvenaiveti| evakāreṇa vibhinnaprakārakatvavyavaccheda| taddharmmaprakārakecchāmprati taddharmmaprakārakatvajñānasya hetutāyā sukhādīcchāsthale kalṛptatvāt| prakṛte viśeṣadharmaprakārakajijñāsāyāḥ saviṣayaviṣayakatvena jñānaprakārībhūtaviśedharmaprakārakatvasyāvaśyakatvena tatpadārthaviśeṣadharmaprakārakajñānakāryyatā'vacchedakākrāntatvāditi māvaḥ|
tattadrūpeṇaiveti ekatvatvādiviśeṣarūpeṇevetyarthaḥ| evakāreṇa saṃkhyāsāmānyadharmmavyavacchedaḥ| iṣṭhāpattistu na, tathā sati śabdādeva viśeṣadharmāvagatau tatra jijñāsāyā asambhavāditi bhāvaḥ|
atra vadanti, taddharmaprakārakajñānasya kāryyatāvacchedakakoṭau svātantryeṇa taddharmaprakārakatvaṃ sāmānyadharmaprakārakajñānasya viśeṣadharmaprakārakajijñāsājanakatvānurodānniveśanīyam| anyathā sāmānyadharmaprakārakajñānasyoktajijñāsāṃ prati hetutvaṃ vilīyate|
naca tatra mānābhāvaḥ, "jāyate ca kāryye kāraṇe vā jñāte kāryaṃ kāraṇaṃ vā" iti dīdhitikṛdvākyasyaiva mānatvāt| prakṛte viśeṣadharmasya pāratantryeṇaivecchāṃ se prakāratvāttaddharmaprakārakajñānaṃ vināpi sāmānyadharmaprakārakajñānat tādṛśajijñāsāyā)ḥ sūpapādatvāt| sāmānyadharmaprakārakajñānaṃ tu viśeṣardhamapuprakārakatvāvacchinnaviśeṣyakecchātvamiti na guṇatvasāmānyajñānāt sukhādīcchāprasaktiradhikamanyatrānusandheyam| tathācātra jijñāsāyāḥ sarvasiddhātve tadupapattaye'bhedaikatve vṛttervṛttirāvaśiyakā| evañca "abhedaikatvasaṃkhyāyā vṛttau" iti mūlasya saṅgatiḥ| etena bhedasya tatsahacaritadvitvāderekatvasya ca yā pratītistadabhāva ityarthaparatayā vyākhyānaṃ parāstamiti 
iti vaiyākaramabhūṣaṇasāradarpaṇe abhedaikatvasaṃkhyānirūpaṇam || 11||
 saṅkhayāvivakṣā'vivakṣānirūpaṇe saṅgatiṃ darśayati-prasaṅgāditi| abhedaikatvasaṅkhayānirūpaṇe `kapiñjalānālabheta' ityatra vidheyaviśeṣaṇatayā tritvasaṅkhayāvivakṣāyāḥsmṛtatvāt upekṣānarhatvāccetyarthaḥ| āvivakṣitamitīti tadvivakṣaṇe yajñīyayāvatpātrāṇāṃ mārjanālābha iti bhāvaḥ| dhātoritīti pratyayavidhau "dhātoḥ"  iti sūtrasthadhātupadārthaviśeṣaṇībhūtaikatvāderetacchāstre avivakṣaṇādityarthaḥ| asyamacchāstre tu uddeśyagataviśeṣaṇasyāvivakṣā mīmāṃsakānāmapi sammatetyāha-utpadyeteti| viśeṣyeteti| tadā sa pratyayārthaḥ| sarvairdhātvarthairviśiṣyetetyarthaḥ|
nanu neyaṃ yuktirvivakṣāsādhikā, tādṛśasamudāyasya kriyāvacanatvābāvena tataḥ pratyayotpattergaganakusumāyamāntavena svata eva vivakṣāsattvādata āha- ārddhadhātukasyeṅ‌valāderitīti| vivakṣitatvācceti| anyathā tadupādānavaiyarthyāpatteriti bhāva-. evamiti anuvādyaviśeṣamavivakṣitameva| niyamānāśrayaṇavādityarthaḥ| `paśunā yajeta' ityatra yajyarthayāgasya karaṇākāṅkṣāyāṃ sādhanattvena paśurvidhīyate| evañca paśuśabdasya vidheyasamarpakatayā tadupasthitānāṃ keṣāñcid, grahaṇe, keṣācchit parityāge mānābhāvena paśutvapaṃustvādivadekatvamapi yāgasādhanatvena vivakṣitam| tena nānekapaśukaraṇayāgādadṛṣṭasiddhiḥ| evamanyatrāpi vidheyaviśeṣaṇaṃ vivakṣitam| atrāvivakṣākāraṇavirahāditi mīmāṃsakapariśīlitā saraṇistadanusaraṇāniyamo nāsmākamityaḥ| etacchāstre tadāśrayaṇe'niṣṭaṃ pradarśayati-radābhyāmiti| vidhānānāpatteriti `na' ityekavacanopasthitaikatvasyāpi vidheyaviśeṣaṇatayā tad‌vavakṣāyāṃ nakāradvayavidhānaṃ na syādityarthaḥ| nanu māstu nadvayavidhānamata āha-tathāceti itañacaikavākyatayā vidhānamamipretya, vākyabhedena vidhānapakṣe tvāha-ādguṇa ityādāviti|  ekātvavivakṣayaiveti vidheyagumagataikatvavivakṣayaivetyarthaḥ| tathācaikagrahaṇamevoktārthejñāpakamiti bhāvaḥ|
nanu paśvekatvādivivakṣāhetūnāṃ jaiminisammatatvānna tadāśrayaṇasyānākaratvamityāśaṅkāmapākariṣyan yojanāpradarśana miṣeṇā'karapādārtha vyācaṣṭe-śabdārthastvityādiḥ| paśvekatvādhikaraṇoktahetūnāmiti `paśunā yajet' ityatra vidheyagataikatvasyāvivakṣaṇe paśvantarālambhe prayogaprāṃśabhāvabādhena yāgavaiguṇyāpattiḥ, guṇānurodhena yāgā''va़ttau pradhānatvabhaṅgāpattiryāvatpasukaraṇakayāgāsambhavaścetvevamādayo dīṣā hetavasteṣāmityarthaḥ| siddhāntasiddhamiti siddhāntārthapratipādakamahābhāṣyādisiddhamityarthaḥ  ādeśabhedavāraṇāyeti| vastutasyu naikagrahaṇaṃ vidheyagaviśeṣaṇāvivakṣāyāṃ tantram, `anyoḥ pūlayoḥ kaṭaṃ kuru' ityādāviva `ād‌ guṇaḥ' ityādāvapi `pūrvaparayoḥ' iti dvandvanirdeśenaikavākyatayaivobhayoḥ sthāne ekavidhānasambhavātsthānibhedānnāneko guṇa ityekagrahaṇasya bhāṣye praṃtyākhyānāditi rahasyat|
vākyabhedādiriti `prājāpatyā navagrahāḥ' iti vākyavihitagrahoddeśena sammārgo vidhaīyate grahaṃ sammārṣṭityanena| tatrānuvādyagrahaikatvavivāyāmutpattivākyagatasaṅkhayāyā virodhād vākyasya yaco bhedo vibhinnabiśeṣyakabodhajanakatvaṃ tādapāyā dītyarthaḥ| anye tu-`grahaṃ samārṣṭi' tañacaikam' ityākārakavākyabhedāpattimāhuḥ| ādipadenotpattivākyagatanavatvasaṅkhayayā grahapadārthasya paricchinnatvātparicchedakāṅ‌kṣābhāvasya `graham' iti dvitīyayā saṃskāryyatvalakṣaṇaprādhānyabodhānāt `pratipradhānaṃ guṇāvṛttiḥ' iti nyāyādvinigamanāvirahāt somāvasekanirākaphalakasammārgasya sarvairgrahaipekṣaṇādvidheyapaśvādiviśeṣaṇavat kriyāṅgatvābhāvena tadvaikalyaprayuktakriyāvaikalyāprasakteśca tadvivakṣāvirahavījasya ca saṃgrahaḥ, anākaramiti pūrvavat| `iḍāgamādeḥ' ityādinā-`udīcāmātaḥ sthāne yakapūrvāyāḥ', iti vihatetvasaṃgrahaḥ| tatrā''kārasthānikā'tamuddisyetvavidhānāt| ata eveṇniṣedhavidhāyakaikājiti sūtre `acaḥ' ityekatvavivakṣādareṇānekāckopadeśavyāvṛttirgranthaḥ kṛdbhirvyākhyātā| vastutastu grahaikatvadṛṣṭāntenātrāpyanuvādaviśeṣaṇanām, saṅ‌khyādīnāṃ kacidavivakṣā'stu, taditaraviśeṣaṇānāṃ vākyādevāvagamenānuṣaṅgikatvādavivakṣāyujyate| prakṛte tu na tatheti bodhyam 
iti vaiyākaraṇabhūṣaṇasāradarpaṇe saṅkhayāvivakṣā'dinirṇayaḥ || 12 ||
 avasarasaṅgati ktvādyarthanirūpaṇe darśayati-ktvāpratyayāderiti| prakṛtyarthetvasya phale'pi sattvādāha-bhāva iti tatrāpi sattvabhūte vyāpāre eva, natu pūrvakālādāvityartha-| `pākaḥ' ityādāviva `paktum' paktvetyādijanyabodhe bhāvāṃśe vaikṣyasyānanubhavāditi bhāvaḥ| vastutastu paktvā ityādijanyobodhe bhāve liṅgasaṅkhayānanvayitvaṃ tadanubhūyata eva| evaṃ `paktum' ityādau phalārthakatumunā sādhyatvena tad‌bodhāccetyuktaprāyamiti|
kā gatiriti tatra `pūrvakāle' ityādyupādānāt pūrvakālādīnāṃ vācyatā pratīyate| bhāvamātrārthakatve tu tadanupapattiriti bhāvaḥ| samānakarttṛkatvādīti| tathāca samānakarttakayordhātvarthayormadhye pūrvakālasambandhī yo'rthastadvācakāt ktvetyarthe'rthākāṅkṣāyām `kartari kṛt' ityasya prasaktau tad‌bādhanārtha vārttikārambheṇa bhāvasyaiva vācyatā nirdhāryyate| karttuḥ pratyayaviśeṣaṇatve tu samānakarttarītyeve brūyāt| sūtropāttānāṃ tu dyotyatvameva, varttamānatvādivaditi bhāvaḥ| vastutastu na dyotyatāyāmapi nirbharaḥ, saṃsargamaryādayaiva tadbhānasambhavādityāha-ayaṃ bhāva iti|
`samānakarttṛkayoḥ' ityādisūtrāṇāṃ dhātusambandhadhikārīyātvāditi bhāvaḥ| anupapanneti viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇatvayoḥ saṃsargaviṣayatānūrūpyatvena sambandhaṃ vinā viśiṣṭabuddherevāsambhavātsambandhabhānaṃ tadvācyatāṃ vināpyupapannamiti bhāvaḥ| anyatheti viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvamantareṇāpyekavākyatābhyupagame ityarthaḥ| sāmānādhikaraṇyamiti ekā'śrayavṛttitvamityarthaḥ| pūrvottarabhāvaḥ-svottarasamyotpattikatvam, svapūrvasamayavṛttitvaṃ vā| vyāpyatvam-avinābhāvitvam| tumunādyante sābdabodhiśamāha-tathāceti|
bhojanajaniketi janakatvasyātra saṃsargateti sphuṭīkarttumiyamuktiḥ| bodhākārastu bhojanaviśiṣṭetyeva, janakatvasyāpadārthatayā'prakāratvāt| kriyayoḥ janyajanakabhāvasyāpi sambandhatve yuktimāha-ata eveti| janyatvasya saṃsaragatvābhyupagamādevetyarthaḥ| `jalapānāntaryasya' ityanena pūrvottaravākyasambandhasattvaṃ bodhyate| tajjanyatvavivakṣāyā natu prayoga iṣṭa eveti bhāvaḥ| sāmānādhikaraṇyasya prakṛtapadavṛttigrahāviṣayatayā saṃsargatābhyupagamastu yatra pūrvāparībhāvāpannabhojanatṛptyorvaiyadhikaraṇyaṃ tatra bhuktvā tṛpyati' itiprayogavāraṇāya| avyavahitottaratvasya tu-dināntaritapūrvakālavṛttisamānādhikaraṇabhojanādikriyāmādāya ktvāvāraṇāya| vyavadhānañca-tātparyavaśāddaṇḍamuhūrttādinā grāhyam, ato bhojanānantaraṃ daṇḍādivyavadhāne'pi na tādṛśaprayogānupapattiriti bodhyam|
nanu pūrvottarabhāvasya sambandatābhyupagame'dhyayanakāle `adhītya tiṣṭhati' iti prayogānupapattiḥ, sthitāvadhyayanānantaryyābhāvāt| evam `mukhaṃ vyādāya svapiti' iti na syāt, oṣṭhapuṭavibhāgārūpavyādānakāle eva svāpādisattve tādṛśaprayogasyeṣṭhatvena vyādānāsya svāpapūrvakatvābhāvādata āha-adhītya tiṣṭhatītyādi| kālaviśeṣeti vibhinnakālikayoḥ kālikasambandhaghaṭitavyāpyavyāpakabhāvābhāvena tatsambandhāvacchinnavyāpyatvasyāpi saṃsargatopagantavyā| na kevalaṃ tatra vyāpyatvasyaiva saṃsargatā, kintu sāmānādhikaparaṇyasyāpītyāha-sāmānādhikaraṇyañceti nāto vibhinnakarttṛkaikakālikādhyayanādikamādāyoktaprayogāpatitiḥ| evañca `samānakartṛkayoḥ' iti sūtravihitaktvāntasthale samānakartṛkatvarūpasāmānādhikaraṇyasaṃsargasya śābdabodhaviṣayatā sārvatriki| janyatvādīnāṃ tu tātparyyavaśāt kvācitkī seti bhāvaḥ|
anyetu-sāmānādhikaraṇyasyaiva pūrvottarabhāvasyāpi kt‌vāntajabodaviṣayatā sārvatrikī, `mukhaṃ vyādāya' ityādau `suptvā mukhaṃ vyādatte' ityatreva vyādānottaramapi svāpānuvṛttestathā paurvāparyyamādāya prayogopapattiḥ| evañcoktasambandhadvayātiriktasambandhabhānasyaiva kvācitktavamityāhuḥ| naca `avajānāsi māṃ yasmādataste na bhaviṣyati| matprasūtimanārādhya prajeti tvāṃ śaśāpasā'|| ityatra matprasūtimanārādhya te prajā na bhaviṣyatītyarthādvibhinnakartṛkādapi ktvādarśanāt samānakartṛkatvasyāpi ktvāntajanyabodhaviṣayatā na sārvatrikīti vācyam, sūtrasvārasāttādṛśasthale sthityādikriyāmadhyāhṛtyā'nārādhya sthitasyaiti bodhasyābhyupagamena sārvatrikatvākṣateriti| anyalabhyatvāditi ākāṅkṣāniyamyatvādvākyasaktilabhyatvādvetyarthaḥ|
kriyayoḥ saṃsarga iti prakṛtyarthaviśeṣakatvarūpasya tasyāsambhave'pi niruktaṃ tadiha bodhyamityarthaḥ| ata eveti ktvaḥ samānakartṛkatvādidyotakatvādevetyarthaḥ| bhāṣyapratīkamiti `iha kasmānna bhavati-`pūrvaṃ bhuṅ‌kte paścād vrahati' iti, svaśabdenopāttatvāt' iti bhāṣyam| tatra samānakartṛkatvena pūrvakālatvena, ca ktva āpādanam| natu viṣaya iti natu prayogaghaṭakayatkiñcitpadabodhya ityarthaḥ, `uktārthānāmaprayogaḥ' iti nyāyāditi bhāvaḥ| tathāca `samānakartṛkayoḥ' iti nirddhāraṇe ṣaṣṭhī, `pūrvakāla' iti ca ṣaṣṭhayarthe bahuvrīhiriti kaiyaṭāśayaḥ| vākyārthasyu pūrvamevoktaḥ| naca pūrvakālasya pūrvādiśabdenābhidhāne ktvo'sādhutve `pūrva bhuktvā paścād `vrajati' ityatra ktvo'nupapattistadavastaiveti vācyam, bhāṣyakṛtaiva tatsamādhānāt| tathāva bhāṣyam-`naitat kriyāpaurvakālyam, kiṃ tarhi kartṛpaurvakālyametat-pūrvamasau bhuktvānyebhyoḥ vrajitṛbhyaḥ paścād vrajatīti| anayebhyo bhoktṛbhyaḥ pūrva bhuktvā'parebhyo gantṛbyaḥ paścād vrajatītyarthe sādhanāntarakriyā'pakṣaṃ paurvakālyamādāya-`vibhāṣā graha' iti ktvaḥ sambhava iti tadāśayaḥ| navyāstu-`pūrvakāla' iti karmadhārayaḥ, bhūta iti tu noktam, paryāyaśabdatvāt, tena rūpeṇa bodhānanubhavācca| tathāca samānakartṛkayorthayormadhye pūrvakāla viśiṣṭārthavṛttidhātoḥ ktaveti sūtrārthaḥ| tathāca pūrvakālaḥ ktvārtho dhātvarthaprakāra eva| ata eva samānakartṛkatvena `pūrvabhuṅ‌kte paścād vrahati' ityatra ktvāpratyayamāśaṅkaya-`svaśabdenopāttatvānna' iti parihṛtaṃ bhāṣye| pūrvaśabdasya pūrvakālo'rthaḥ, natu dhātostatra vṛttiriti tadartho natūktārthānāmaprayoga iti| ata eva iha kasmānna bhavatyāsyate bhoktum' ityagrimaśaṅkāsaṅtiḥ| anyathā phalārthakatumunā pūrvakālatvasyāsikriyāyāṃ dyotanāttadasaṅkati spaṣṭaiva| pūrvakālatvasya tato'pratītau tu kathaṃ ktava āpādanam, vā'sarūpanyāyena laṭā sādhitamityanyat| evañca ṣaṣṭhayāḥ sambandhavācakatvavadamīṣāmapi sambandhavācakatvaṃ suvacamityāhuḥ|
atredaṃ cintyam-natu dhātestatra vṛttirityarthe-`āsyate bhoktum' ityagrimaśaṅkānupapatterupaṣṭhambhaktevanopanyasanaṃ śithitam, yataḥ kaiyaṭamate'pi pratīyamānasyārthe pūrvakālasambandhitvasya kenacidanabhidhānenoktārthanāmaprayoga iti nyāyānavatārādāśaṅkotthānasambhavāt| pratyayavācyasya kṛtpratyayasthale dhātvarthaviśeṣyatāyā navyānāmapi smatatvena `pūrvakālaḥ ktvārtho dhātvarthaprakāra eva' iti pūrvagranthavirodhāśca| naca dyoyatakatvābhyupagamāduktasaṅgatiḥ, ṣaṣṭhāyāḥ sambandhavācakatvavadamīṣāmapi sambanadhavācakatvam' ityuttaragranthavirodhāt| naca tadapi suvacam, samānakartṛkatvādīnāṃ vācyatāyāḥ sūtrādalābhāt| tasmāt karmapacanīyavat kriyāsambandhaviśeṣakatvameva ktvādīnāmiti kaiyaṭoktameva samyagiti|
matāntaraṃ dūṣayitumupanyasyati-yattviti|ktvāvācyamiti `samnakartṛkayoḥ pūrvakāle'ityubhayoḥ padayorbahuvrīhitvāviśeṣeṇa pūrvakālasya vācyatvamitarāthasya dyotyatvamiti vaiṣamye bījābhāvāditi bhāvajha| vaiṣamye bādhakamāha-anyatheti tasya ktāpratyayāvācyatve ityarthaḥ| tṛtīyāprasaṅgaśceti ktvāpratyayena karturanabhidhānāditi bhāvaḥ| nanu `anabhihite' iti sūtre nañaḥ prasajyapratiṣedhārthakatayā prakṛte karturākhyātenābhidhānānna tṛtīyāpattirityāśaṅkayanirācaṣṭe-naceti `anabhihite' iti na prasajhyapratiṣedhaḥ, vākyabhedādidoṣāt| nacānupapattvā tatsvīkāraḥ, vakṣyamāṇabhāṣyavirodhāt| tathācaikenābidhānepyanyenānabhidhānā tṛtīyā durvāretyabhiprāyeṇāha-pāpakriyākarturityādiṃ tadabhāvāditi abhihitabhinnatvādityarthaḥ| ata eveti tatsūtrasthanañaḥ paryudāsārthakatvādevetyarthaḥ| `bhāṣpe' ityasyānabhihitasutrasthe' iti śeṣa| tasyeti samānakartṛkatvasyetyarthaḥ| dvivacanaprakṛtabahuvrīhiṇā samānakartṛkatvasya dhātvarthaviśeṣaṇataiva ityarthaḥ| samāneti tadupādānācca kartṛdvayasyaikyalābhaḥ| nyāsaḥ syāditi `karttari kṛt' itivat| vastutastu na tathā sambhavati, `karttari kṛt' iti sūtreṇaiva gatārthatvāt, `avyayakṛto bhāve' iti vārttikavirodhācceti bhāvaḥ| uktāpattiṃ nirasyati-tṛtīyāpādanaṃ tviti| ākhyātārthakriyāyā iti kriyādvayanirūpitakartṛśaktyoranekatve'pi pradhānatiṅantārthakriyānirūpitakartṛśaktorākhyātenābhidhāne tatra kṛdantārthakriyānirūpitatacchaktaranabhihitāyā apyabhihitatvātideśasya, anabhidhāne tvanabhihitatvātideśasya coktabhāṣyaprāmāṇyenābhyupagamānna tṛtīyāpattiḥ prakṛte iti bhāvaḥ| pradhānetarayeriti guṇapradhānakriyanirūpitaśaktiḥ pradhānakriyānirūpitaśaktidharmamanurūṇaddhītyarthaḥ| uktātidese'pi tatsammatimāha-pradhānaviṣayeti pradhānam-viṣayaḥ-nirūpakaṃ yasyāḥ sā śaktiryadā tiṅā'bhidhīyate| tadā guṇakriyāvācakadhātūttarapratyayenānabhihitāpyabhihitavāt pratīyate ityarthaḥ| idamupalakṣaṇam--abhihitāyā api anabhihitatvapratīteḥ| tatra `prāsāde āste' ityudāhāryyam| avaśyaṃ cetthamabhyupetavyam-anyathā kartuḥ ktvārthatvābhyupagamenoktasthale tṛtīyāpattivārayaṇe'pi `paktvaudano mayā bhujyat' ityatrodanapadottaraṃ dvitīyā''pattirdurvārā| tatra pākakriyānirūpitakarmatvaśakteḥ kṛtānabhidhānāduktarīterasambhavādityāha-kiñceti atredaṃ bodhyam-yadaudane pākakriyānirūpitakarmatvasyaiva śābdaviṣayatvamabhipretam, natu pradhānakriyānirūpitākarmatvasyāpi, tadā `odanaṃ paktvā bhujyate' ityapi sādhu, odanasya bhojanakarmatāyā ārthikatvāt| yadā tūbhayakarmatvasyaiva śābdaviṣayatvamabhipretaṃ tadaudanapadād dvitīyā asādhveva, uktavyavasthiteḥ| parantvākhyātavācyakriyāyāḥ prādhānyāttāṃ pratyeva ktvāntopasthitāḥ sarvāḥ kriyā guṇībhavanti, natu tāsāṃ parasparaṃ viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvaḥ, `guṇānāñca parārthatvāt' iti nyāyāt| evañca `snātvā pītvā bhuktvā vrajati' ityaviśeṣaṇa prayogāḥ| tumunastādarthyarūpamuddsyatvamapi dyotyam `kriyārthāyāṃ kriyāyām' ityukteḥ| tanniṣkarṣasca pūrvavadeva| tumprakṛtyarthopapadārthayostādarthyavatsamānakaratṛkatvādikamapi sambandhaḥ| tathāca,-`hariṃ draṣṭuṃ yāti' ityato harikarmakabhaviṣyaddarśanoddeśyakaṃ dar‌śanakartṛkartṛkaṃ yānamiti sābdadhīḥ| anayaiva diśā `edhānāhārako vrajati' iti bodha ūhanīyaḥ 
iti vaiyākaraṇabhūṣaṇasāradarpaṇe katvādyarthanirūpaṇam
 ittthaṃ vyākaraṇādinā gṛhītaśaktikapadasamudāyādanāsamudāyādanāsannādagṛhītārthatātparyyakādayogyādanākāṅkṣācca śābdabodhādarśanādāsattyādikamapi śaktijñānajanyapādārthapasthityā bodhe jananīye sahakāri| āsattiśca-prakṛtabodhānukūlapadavyavadhānam| asti ca `parvato vahimān' ityatra tādṛśapadāvyavadhānam, natu `girirbhuktaṃ vahrimān devadattena' iti na tato bodhaḥ| ata eva "sthālyāṃ taṇjulaṃ pacati" ityatra taṇḍulakarmakasthālyādhikaraṇakakriyeti bodhe nānāsannatā, tad‌ghaṭakasarveṣāṃ padānāṃ tādṛśabodhānukūlatvāt `sthālyāṃ pacati' ityetāvanmātrabodhe tu tadanāsamnameva| ślokādau tu yojanāvākyādeva bodhaḥ| d‌bhrameṇānāsaṃnnād bodhadharśanāttajjñānameva heturiti sampradāyaḥ| navyāstu-mauniślokādanvayabodhānudayaprasaṅgānnoktāvyavadhānamāsattiḥ, ākāṅkṣādisattve, tātparyyajñāne ca sati vyavahitādavyavahitācca bodhadarśanāttajjñānavilambema śābdāvilambāccha| kintvidaṃ padametatpadena sambhūyānvayabodhaṃ janayatvisyākārakapadatātparyyarūpābhisandhāpayitricchā sā, natu vaktricchā, mauniślokādau doṣatādavasthyāt| śukavākye tu bhagavattātparyyamādāyaiva gatirityāhuḥ| vastutastu satyarthatātparyyajñāne padatātparyyajñānavilambena śābdabodhāvilambāt `vṛttyā padajanyapadārthānāmavyavadhānenopasthitirevāsattiḥ' svarūpasatyanvayabodhe hetuḥ nahi padārthānāmanupasthitānvayadhīḥ kenāpyanubhūyate iti| arthatātparyyaṃ tvidaṃ padarmetasminnarthe'padārthānvayaṃ bodhayatvityabhisandhāpayitricchā| saṃvādiśukavākye tu bhagavata eva sā| visaṃvādini tu śikṣayitureva| asya saṃśaye vyatirekaniśceye cānvayābodhāt, tad‌bhrameṇānvayabodhadarśanācca tajjñānamapyanvayabodhe hetuḥ| adhikamagre vakṣyate|
`śābdabodhe yogyatāyā jñānaṃ kāraṇam' iti bahavaḥ| sā caikapadārthe'parapadārthasya yādṛśasaṃsargavattvaṃ tādṛśasaṃsargavattaiva, prakṛtaśābdabodhaviṣayasaṃsargabattvamiti yāvat| asti ca `payasā siñciti' ityatra seke payaḥkaraṇatvasya saṃsargaḥ| ata eva karakābhiprāyaprayuktapayasā siñcatīti vākyaṃ na yogyam, payaḥpadārthakarakāyāṃ sekakaraṇatvābhāvāt| yādṛśeti viśeṣamācca `ākāśaṃ śabdaḥ' iti vākyaṃ samavāyasaṃsargamādāya nābhedānvaye yogyam| ayogye'pyetad‌bhrameṇānvayadarśanādetajjñāsyāpi hetutā| nanu saṃsargasya vākyārthatvena tad‌bodhāt prāganupasthityā kathaṃ tad‌bodhasya tatra sattvamiti cet, nahi yogyatājñānasya śābdabodhe niścayatvena hetutāṃ brūmaḥ, yenoktadoṣo'vakāśamāsādayet| kintu saṃśayaniścayasādhāraṇajñānatvena, tasya ca na daurlabhyam, payasā siñcatītyādau sekaḥ payaḥkaraṇako na veti saṃśayātmakasya bhūtale ghaṭo'stītyādau ca prātyakṣikaniścayarūpasyāpi tat‌prāksaulabhyāt| svajananyaśābdabodhasyaivānvayabodhapratibandhakatayoktabodhāttatsambhavāt| kecittu-laukikasannakarṣājanyadoṣaviśe,janyatadvattābuddhitvāvacchinnamprati tadabhāvavattājñānābhāvasya hetutayā śābdabodhasyāpi tatkāryyatāvacchedakākrāntatayā ayogyatājñānasattve tadabhāvādeva śābdabodhavāraṇe kṛtaṃ yogyatājñānasya taddhetutayā| kāryatāvacchedakakoṭau pratyakṣānyatvaniveśenaiva laukikapratyakṣanirāse'pi ghaṭādyupanītabhānasaṃgrahāya tadvihāyājanyāntadvayaniveśaḥ|
naca bādhaniścayābhāvatvāpekṣayābhāvatvāpekṣayā saṃśayaniścayasādhāraṇayogyatājñānatvena hetutve lāghavamiti vācyam, syādevaṃ yadi bādhābhāvasya śābdatvaṃ kāryyatā'vacchedakaṃ kalpyeta, kintu śābdabodasya bādhābhāvakāryyatāvacchedakākrāntatayā taddaśāyāṃ taṃ pratiṣedhāmaḥ| ata eva `ghaṭo'bhidheyaḥ' ityādāvuktbādhāprasiddhāvapi na kṣatiḥ, tatsaṃsargāvacchinnatatprakārakatadviśeṣyakabuddhitvasyaiva tatkāryyatā'vacchedakatvāt, ghaṭābhāve'bhidheyatvapratiyogikatvabhramadaśāmādāya tadabhāvasyāpi prasiddhaścetyahuḥ|
atra vadanti-nahi tadviśiṣṭabuddhisāmānye bādhābhāvaḥ kāraṇam, kintu bādhāvatāre icchasattve āhāryyapratyakṣodayādicchāyā uttejakatvānurodhena pratyakṣatvasyaiva pratibandhyatā'vacchedakatvāvaśyakatayopanītabhānasādhāraṇapratibandyatāyāḥ śābdabodhe'sambhavāt| naca tathāpyanumitisādhāraṇapratibandhyatāyāḥ śābdabodhasādhāraṇyaṃ śaṅkayasa, tatsādhāraṇasya pratyakṣānyajñānatvasya pratibandhyatāvacchekatve pratyakṣānyatvajñānatvayormitho viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāve vinigamanāviraheṇānekapratibandhyapratibandhakabhāvāpatteḥ, pratibandhyatāvacchedakagauravāpatteśca, anumititvasyaiva tadavacchedakatvasyāṅgīkaraṇīyatvāt| ato bādhābhāvasya svātantryeṇaiva śābdabodhaṃ prati hetutā vācyā| tatra toktagauraveṇa saṃsargarūpayogyatājñānasyaiva hetutvamucitam| apica śābdabodhe tadvanyonyābhāvatadabhāvavyāpyavattājñānādīnāṃ pratibandhakatvakalpanā'pekṣayā lāghavād yogyatājñānasyaiva hetutvamucitam| kiñca `payasā siñcati' iti śābdasāmagrīkāle sekaḥ payaḥkaraṇakatvābhāvavāniti mānasavāraṇāya tatraśābdabodhasāmagnyāḥ pratibandhakatvakalpane gauravam| manmate tatra yogyatājñānasyā'vaśyatayā viparītajñānottarapratyakṣe viśeṣadarśanasya hetutayā tadabhāvādeva tadvāraṇāt| viśeṣadarśanasattve tu tadabhāvavyāpyavattānirṇayarūpapratibandhakasattvena śābdasāmāgnāya evābhāvena tathā mānase iṣṭhāpatteḥ|
naca nirṇayaṃ pratyeva viśeṣadarśanasya hetutayā śābdasāmagrīkāle mānasasaṃśayavāraṇāya śābdasāmagnayāḥ pratibandhakatve kalpanīye saṃśayatvamaniveśya sekatvāvacchinnaviśeṣyakapayaḥkaramatvābhāvaprakārakamānasatvameva tatpratibandhyatāvacchedakamiti nātiriktapratibandhyapratibandhakabhāvakalpaneti vācyam, sekatvāvacchinnaviśeṣyakapayaḥkaraṇakatvaprakārakamānase śābdasāmagrīpratibandhakatvāntarasyomayakalpyatayā tenaiva saṃśayavāraṇe tatsaṃśaye pratibandhakatvantarākalpanāt| naca viparītajñānottarapratyakṣe viśeṣadaśarnasya hetutvasiddhāveva yogyatājñānasya hetutvasiddhiriti vācyam, gurūbhūtaśābdasāmagrīpratibandhakatvakalpanāpekṣayā taddhetutvakalpanāyā evocitatvādanayaiva diśeṣṭasādhanatvādijñānānāṃ pravṛttau hetutāsiddhirityalaṃ paramatānuvarṇanena|
`ghaṭaḥ karmatvam' ityādhārādheyabhāvenānvaye nirākāṅkṣavākyād ghaṭīyā karmateti bodhānudayādākāṅkṣā'pi sahakāriṇī| sā ca yatpede yatpadasamabhivyāhāraprayuktaṃ yadviśeṣyakayatsaṃsargakayatprakārakabodhajanakatvaṃ tatpade tatpadanirūpitākāṅkṣā| yathā `ghaṭamānayati' ityatra ghaṭapade karmatāviśeṣyakādheyatāsaṃsargakaghaṭīyā karmateti bodhopayikāmpadanirūpitākāṅkṣā, tāṃ vinā ghaṭaḥ karmatvamityatra ghaṭapadena tādṛśabodhājananāt| evaṃ dhātupade'pyākhyātanirūpitākāṅ‌kṣohyā| ata evānayanaṃ kṛtirityatra nānayānukūlā kṛtiriti bodhaḥ, dhātāvākhyātāsamabhivyāhārāt| iyamevākāṅkṣā vyākaraṇena pratipādyate| tathāca tattapade tattatpadapūrvāparībhāvarūpasambhivyāhāraḥ seti phalati, tāṃ vinā yadyapyuktānākāṅkṣe karmatvapade'mpadatvabhramadaśāyāṃ ghaṭīyātattatpadapūrvāparībhāvarūpasamabhivyāhāraḥ seti phalati, tāṃ vinā yadyapyuktānākāṅkṣe karmatvapade'mpadatvabhramadaśāyāṃ ghaṭīyā karmateti bodhodayena tajjñānaṃ heturnatu svarūpasatī sā| naca janitānvayabodhādvākyāt punarapi tādṛśabodhāpattiḥ, uktākāṅ‌kṣayāstādānīmapyavikaltvāditi vācyam, prakṛtānvayabodhasamānākārabodānupahitatvasyāpi svarūpasata ākāṅkṣātvaina janitānvayabodasthale tadabhāvenāpattyasambhavāt, tātparyyaviṣayatvarūpaprakṛtatvena bodhaviśeṣaṇācca| bodhadvayaṃ jāyatāmiti tātparyyasattvena janitānvaye bodasya dvitīyabodhe nirākāṅkṣatvaṃ svasamānetyukteśca na janitāvāntarabodhasya mahāvākyārthabodhe tattvam| ata eva "ayameti putrorājñaḥ piruṣo'pasāryyatāmā" ityādau rājñaḥ purūṣeṇa sahānvayabodhajanane sarvadā nirākāṅkṣatvam|
navyāstu-yatpadasya yatpadavyatirekaprayuktatātparyyaviṣayībhūtānvayānanubhāvakatvaṃ tena saha tasyākāṅkṣa| `ayameti putro rājñaḥ puruṣaḥ' itivākye rājapuruṣasaṃsargapratīticchayoccarite rājña ityasya puruṣeṇa sākāṅ‌kṣatvavāraṇāyānvayabodhe tātparyyaviṣayatvaṃ viśeṣyaṇam| pūruṣeṇānvaye tātparyyasattve tu sākāṅkṣatvamapi, evañca na tātparyyajñānasya śābdabodhe pṛthagdhetutā anugṛhītārthatātparyyake nirūktā'kāṅkṣājñānābhāvādevānvayabodāpattyābhāvādityāhuḥ| evañca śābdabodhāt prāgavaśyaklṛptatattatpadasamabhivyāhārarūpākāṅśrayaivānvayarūpavākyārthabhānopapattau vākyasya tatra śaktirniṣpramāṇeti naiyāyikā vadanti|
mīmāṃsakādayastu vākyasya tatra lakṣaṇaiva, natu tatra śaktiriti tanmataṃ khaṇḍayituṃ sphodanirūpaṇamityāśayena mūlamavatārayati-siddhānteti| yadvā nanūktarītyā vyākaraṇasya prakṛtīpratyayeṣu tattadarthabodhakatvaśaktipratipādakatvamanupapannam, prakṛtipratyānāṃ kramikāśuvināsinānāvarṇā'tmakatayā'sthiratvena apratyakṣatayā tatra śaktigrahasya kathamapyasambhavena tatpratipādakasyāsya śāstrasyāprāmāṇyaprasaṅgha ityāśaṅkānirākaraṇāya sphodanirūpaṇamityāśayena mūlamavatārayannāha-
siddhāntaniṣkarṣamiti tathācopoddhātasaṅgariratretyavadheyam| tattnmatanirākaraṇantu mūla eva suvyaktam| aṣṭhānāṃ sphodāmāmākarasi tayā tad‌vyāvṛttyālābha ityāśayenāha-anarthakamiti niṣprayojanakamityarthaḥ| durarthakamiti tadupādāne vākyātiriktānāmasaṃgrahāpatteriti bhāvaḥ| samādhatte-tathāpīti| anyeṣām varṇādipañcasphodānāmityarthaḥ|
tatra sphoṭatvaṃ sphuṭati-abhivyaktībhāvatyartho'smāditi vyutpattyā'rthaprakāśakatvam, prakāśaśca-jñānam| tathācārthaniṣṭhaviṣayatāprayojakaśaktimattvaṃ paryyavasyati, varṇasyaiva tattvābhyupagame varṇasphoṭaḥ| padādīnāṃ tattvābhyupagame tu padādispoṭa iti vyavahāraḥ| avāstavattveti varṇasphoṭādīnāmākāṅkṣānivarttakatvābhāvādavāstavatvaṃ śāstrīyaprakriyānirvāhāya paraṃ siddhānte tatsvīkāraḥ| pāramārthikatvantu vākyasphoṭasyaiveti bhāvaḥ| vipratipattiriti saṃśayīyayāvatkoṭayupasthāpakaṃ vākyamityarthaḥ| natu saṃśayajanakaṃ vākyameva sā| parokṣaṃ jñānaṃ niścayātmakameveti prācīnapravādena saṃśayajanakatvasya vākye asambhavāt| vastutasyu saṃśayajanakavākyatvameva vipratipattitvam| tattadabhāvasahacaritadharmavaddharbijñānasyeva samānāneketi sūtreṇa vipratipatterapyāhatyeva saṃśayahetutvotkīrttanādityavadheyam| mūle āsādhuśabdāntargatavarṇavācakatāvicārasyāprastutatvaṃ matvā-sādhviti atra sādhvantargatavarṇatvasāmānādhikaraṇyena vācakatvādisādhane tad‌vākyasya vipratipattitvāsambhavaḥ naiyāyikairapi sādhvantargatānāṃ keṣāñcid vācakatvābhyupagamāt| tādṛśavarṇatvāvacchedena vācakatvādisāvane tu vaiyākaraṇānāṃ pareṣāṃ vikāraṇeṣu bādhaścetyata āha-tat‌smṛtā iti| nanu yathāśrute sādhuśabdāntargatā vācakastadantargatavarṇasmāritā netyarthalābhādetad‌vākyasya vibhinnadharmibodhakatvādvipratipattitvasyaivāsambhavaḥ, ekadharmikaviruddhakoṭikasaṃśayajanakasyaivatattvā dato vyācaṣṭe sāre-sādhuśabdāntargatā iti sādhvantargatavarṇasmāriti ityarthaḥ| yathāśrute sādhvanantargataviraparītānupūrvokavarṇānāṃ vācakatvasya kenāpyanabhyupagamād bādhasiddhasādhanayorāpatteḥ| tatsmāritatvañca-śāstrabodhitādeśādeśibhāva-nimittanimittibhāvānyatarasambandhena| tena asyāpatyam `i' `iyān' ityādernāsaṃgrahaḥ| vidhikoṭiriti samuccayavyāvṛttā kāṭitākhyā viṣayatā saṃśaye āvaśyakīti sūcanāyoktam-koṭiriti| anyeṣām-naiyāyikānām| taiḥ "laḥ karmaṇi" `svaujasamauṭ" ityādivihitapratyayānāmeva vācakatvam, tibvisargādīnāṃ tvādeśismṛtidvārā bodhakatvameva lipivadityabhyupagamāt| tathāca sādhvantargatavarṇasmāritavarmatvāvacchedena matabhedenobhayasādhanānnauktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ| yathuśrutamūloktavipratipattimanusutyāha-sādhuśabda ityādi| nanu sādhvantargatavarṇānāṃ bodhakatvasādhane'pina vācakatvasiddhirata āha-bodhaka vācakā iti| tasyaiveti bodhakatvasyaivetyarthaḥ| prāgiti| śaktinirūpaṇāvasara ityarthaḥ| natviti-bodhakatvasiddhau ca tadanyathānupapattyā padārthāntararūpā śaktirapi sid‌dhyet, tadabhāve tu sā'pi neti vācakatvasāmānyābhāvasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ  naiyāyikamataṃ dūṣayitumupanyasyati-ye tviti| vācakatvābhāve prayojakamāha-teṣāmiti| bahutvena-ityupalakṣaṇam-anugamakadharmābhāvasyāpi, tiṅ‌tvasuptvādīnāme kavacanatvādivadanugamakatvāsambhavāttiṅ‌tvādyupasthiti vinā'pi kṛtyādyarthopasthitervyabhicāreṇa śaktatānavacchedakatvācceti bodhyam| nanu tibādya deśināmapi tattadanubandhabhedena nānātvānnoktirītyā teṣāṃ vācakatvamata āha-edhāmiti| anuprayujyamānadhātuttaraidhādināpi tadarthabodopapatternauktadoṣo'ta āha-brahmeti| ādinā `adya tiṣyo liṅdhuk' ityādisaṃgrahaḥ| lugāderityādinā, lub‌lopayoḥ saḥ tairiti ādeśairityarthaḥ| uktadoṣamāśaṅ‌kyāha-latvasyeti| jātirūpatayeti laḍādileṣu la-ityānugatapratītyā tatsiddhirityabhimānaḥ
navyāstu-śaktatāvacchedakatayā na tatsiddhiḥ| sarvalakārasādhāraṇalatvasyātiprasaktatvāddaśalakāramātravṛttestu śaktigrahāt pūrvamanupasathiteḥ pacannityādau kṛtibodhāpattyā tasya śaktatvā'vacchedakatvāsambhavācca titpatvatastvādikameva tadavacchedakamityāhuḥ| tathācoktavipratipattau prācīnaiḥ saha vihāda eveti bodhyam|
avyabhicārācceti latvāvacchinnaśaktigrahasya kṛtibodhāt pūrva niyameva sattvāt tiptvādīnāṃ śakyatavācchekakatve tu tadavaccinnaśaktigrahaṃ vinā'pi tasādiśaktigrahavat kṛtibodhenā'deśavācakatāvādimate vyabhicāro durvāra iti bhāvaḥ| nanu kāryyatāvacchedakakoṭāvavyavahitottaratvaniviśānneyamāpattirata āha-laḥ karmaṇītīti| ityāhuriti tathācādeśināmeva vācakatvaṃ sapramāṇamiti tadbhāvaḥ| svasādhaketi śvamātaparipoṣaketyarthaḥ| vyavasthiterityādipañcamyantatrayasya pūrvakārikāsthabodhakapadārthe'nvayaṃ pradarśayannādau vyavasathiterityetadvivṛṇoti-vyavasthānurodhāditi `pramāṇena padarthanirddhāraṇaṃ vyavasthā' tadanyathā'nupapatterityarthaḥ| śaktibhramāditi latvāvacchinnaniṣṭhavācakatvasya tatrāropādityarthaḥ| mānābhāvāditi viṣayabādhasyaiva bhramatve tantratayā prakṛte bodhakatvaśaktestatrābādhāditi bhāvaḥ|
nanvādeśānāṃ bahutvameva tatra śaktisvīkāre bādhakamata āha-ādeśināmapīti| vaiyākaraṇaiḥ-śākaṭāya naprabhṛtibhiḥ-vyavastheti, idameva vācakamiti nirddhāraṇānupapatterityarthaḥ, anuśāsanasya sarvatrāpi sattvāditi bhāvaḥ| sarvaiṣāmityasya vinigamakābhāvādityādiḥ| nanu tathāpyādeśāpekṣayā''deśināmalpatvena tatprayuktalāghavādādeśiṣveva vācakatvaṃ setsyatītyata āha-vyabhicāraśceti| nanu vinigamanāvirahādevādeśināṃ vācakatāsiddhirata āha-ādeśānāmiti tathāca gauravādidoṣasya tatparihārasya cobhayoḥ sāmye'pyādeśismaraṇakalpanāvirahaprayuktalāghavasahakṛtaprayoganaiyatyaṃmevodeśānāṃ vācakatve vinigamakamityākhṇḍalārthaḥ| nanu bodhakatāyāḥ śaktitva eva bhavaduktavyavasthopapattiḥ| naca bodhakatvasya śaktitvaṃ tanmatasiddham, kintu saṅketasya padārthantarasya vā tattvam| tathācānuśāsanena yatra sā pratipādyate tatraiva tadaṅgīkāreṇa kathaṃ tibādiṣu tatsiddhiḥ| smaraṇakalpanāgauravaṃ tu prāmāṇikatvādapi naduṣṭamityasvarasāt prayoge śruyamāṇānāṃ tibādīnāṃ vācakatve sādhākāntaramupanyasyatītyāha-sādhakāntaramiti| mukhya iti mukhyatvañca-tasmin‌ śaktigrāhakāntaranirapekṣatvam, vyākaraṇādinā śaktigrahe niyamena tad‌grāhakāntarapekṣaṇāt|
saceti vyāhārajanyaśaktigrahaścetyarthaḥ tiṅādiṣveveti vyavahāreṇa pūrva vākye tad‌grahe'pyāvāṣodvāpābhyāmaṃ paścāttad‌ghaṭakatiṅādiṣveva tannirṇayāditi bhāvaḥ| nanu prayogāntargatavarṇeṣu sarvasiddhavyavahāreṇa śaktigrahe `ghaṭamānaya' ityādyantargataśabādīnāmapi vācakatvāpattiḥ| naca seṣṭā, vikaraṇānāṃ naira'rk‌yamiti siddhāntavyākopāt| kiñcānayanādivyavahārasya loṅādyantaprayogaṃ vinā''sambhavena tādṛśaprayogāntargatavarṇānāṃ tena tattvāsiddhāvapi tadrahitavākyāntargatavarṇeṣu vācakatvamanuśāsanenaiveṣṭavyam| tathāca tatrādeśināṃ vācakatvasiddhāvanyatrāpyādeśiṣu sā kalpyetyasvarasādāha-kiñceti| taddhetviti "taddhetoreva tattve kiṃ tena" iti lāghavamulakaścāyaṃ nyāyaḥ| ubhayorhetutve gauravāttaṃ saṅgamayati-lakārasyeti| bodhāpattiriti bhavanakartṛbodhāpattirityarthaḥ. na kevaleti nyāyāt kavalalakārasya prayogānarhatvāt `bha' ityuktam| lakāradāśāyāṃ śapo'sambhavānna bhavatītyupāttam| tathāca `bhavati ityatrānusaṃhitāllakārādyathā kartṛbodhastathā pratyakṣaśrutalakārādapi karttṛ bodhāpattiriti bhāvaḥ|
nacāsādhutvajñānaṃ pratibandhakam, śaktatvarūpasādhutāyā api sattvādapabhraṃśādapi bodhodayena tadīyajñānasyāpratibandhakatvācceti bhāvaḥ| tādṛśeti āpāditakartṛbodhe ityarthaḥ. samabhivyāhāra iti pūrvāparībhāvāpannavarṇasamudāya ityarthaḥ, āvaśyakatvāttaṃ vinā lakāreṇāpi bodhājananenoktasamabhivyāhārasyāpekṣatvādityarthaḥ| vācakatvaśaktiriti arthabodhakatvarūpā śaktirityarthaḥ. nacāprāptakālatā, varmasphoṭopayogitvāt tasyā iti bhāvaḥ| vācakatvānabhyupagame ādeśiśaktivādimate gauravaṃ pradarśayati-anyatheti uktasamabhivyāhārasya vācaktānabhyupagame ityarthaḥ| gauravamiti yadyapyādeśavācakatāvādimate `bhūti' ityāto vidyamānabhavanakartṛbodhavāraṇāya tādṛśabodhe `bhavati' iti samabhivyāhārasya hetutāyā avaśyakalpyatvād, gauravasāmyameva, tathāpyādeśismṛtyakalpanaprayuktalāghavamakṣuṇṇameveti bhāvaḥ| nanvetāvatā samabhivyāhārasya vācakaṃtvamāyātam, na prayogāntargatadhātupratyayaghaṭakānāṃ varṇānāmata āha-tathāceti vinigamanāvirahāditi naca varṇānāṃ bāhulyameva vinigamakam, uktānupūrvyāstadanugamakatvāditi bhāvaḥ| vastutastu samabhivyāhārasya vācakatve tat‌ghaṭakanānāvarṇānāmavacchedakatāyāṃ gauravam, varṇānāṃ vācakatve tūktānupūrvyā ekasyā eva tattvamiti lāghavasyaiva vinigamakatvamiti bodhyam| nanu prayogāntargatavarmasmāratavarmatvāvacchedena vācakatvasiddhau prakṛtāyāṃ pācakatvābhāvasādhanāya `bhūl' ityato bhavanakriyākarttṛbodhāpādanamanucitam, tādṛśalakārasya prayogāntargatavarmāsmāritatvāt| tathāca noktayuktiḥ samabhivyāhārasya vācakatvasādhikā nāpyatra taddhetunyāyāvakāśaḥ, samabhivyāhārasya lavācakatāgrahopayogitayā śābdabodhe hetutve'pi tadvācakatāyā mānābhāvena tena tadanyathāsid‌dhyasambhavāt| lakārasya vācakatve tvanuśāsanasyaiva mānatvādityaparitoṣānmūlamavatārayati-apiceti| na syāditi sthānino lakārasyobhayatraikyāditi bhāvaḥ| navyanaiyāyikanye tu neyamāpattiḥ, taiḥ prayogāntargatatibādīnāmeva vācakatāyā abhyupagamāt| dhātupratyayalopasthale tattadarthabodhopapādanantubhayoḥ samaprayatnakamityavadheyam| ityastviti tathāca tadubhayaśaktayaiva kartṛlābhaḥ| kṛtyāśrayasyaiva kartṛtvāditi bhāvaḥ| anyeti anākāṅkṣe'pi śāstrapravṛttāvityarthaḥ| śaktatāvacchedakabhedasya tatrāpi sattvāditi bhāvaḥ  sāmānādhikaraṇyānurodhāditi śānacaḥ kṛtivācakatve sarvānubhavasiddhābhedānvayabodhānupapatterityarthaḥ| laṅādesatvabodhanaphalaṃ dhātvarthe varttamānatvapratītireveti bodhyam| pacatikalpamiti idañca svādyantaṃ nāmeti matamanusṛtya| `sattvapradhānāni nāmāni' iti niruktāt dravyaviśeṣyakabodhajanakatvaṃ nāmatvam| tadeva ca nāmārthayoriti vyutpattighaṭakam| ata eva nipātārthasya bhedānvaya iti matenedaṃ dūṣaṇamiti tu pare| lakṣaṇetyasya lasyeti śeṣaḥ|
āpatteriti naca sā taveṣṭeti bhāvaḥ| nanvādeśānāṃ vācakatve ādeśiśaktipratipādaka `laḥ karmaṇi' ityāditrāṇāṃ vaiyarthyamata āha-laḥ karmaṇīti| uktamiti ākhyātaśaktinirūpaṇāvasara iti śeṣaḥ| evañca prayoge śruyamāṇaprakṛtipratyayaghaṭakavarṇeṣu śaktirvarṇasphoṭamiti paryyavasannam  iti varṇasphoṭanirūpaṇam || 13 ||
 padasphoṭanirūpaṇe upajīvyopajīvakabhāvasyāpi saṅgatitvaṃ sūcayan mūlamavatārayati-evamiti uktarītyetyarthaḥ| ādeśānāmapi keṣāñcit prayogāntargatatvādāha-tibvisargādaya iti prayogāntargatavarṇā iti yāvat| nanu tādṛśayāvad‌varṇānāṃ vācakatve kathaṃ padasphoṭasiddhirato bhāvārthamāha-ayamiti samabhivyāhṛtaṃ tūktārtham| siddha iti ānupūrvoviśiṣṭatāvatāṃ varṇānāmavācakatve ānupūrvorūpapadasya vācakatvāśā durāśeti bhāvaḥ| nanu samudāyasya pratyekānatiriktatvena vācakatāyāḥ pratyekavarṇe viśrāmo vācyaḥ, tathāca kathaṃ samuditatādṛśavarmarūpapadasya vācakatetyataḥ pratyekavarmavācakatāṃ nirasyati-prativarṇamiti| anubhavaviruddhatvāditi ghaṭaśabdādamumarthaṃ pratyemītyeva pratīterityarthaḥ| bādhakāntaramapyāha-pratyekamiti| arthavattva iti tadabhyupagama ityarthaḥ| nalopādītyādinā jaśtvādi parigrahaḥ|
tathāca samuditānāmeva teṣāṃ vā cakatvamabhyupeyam| tatra coktayuktyā tatsamudāyasya vācakatvaṃ nirāvādham| sa eva cāsmākaṃ padaśabdavyapadeśya iti bhāvaḥ| nanu pratyekavarṇānāṃ vācakatvasya gauravaparāhatatvādevāsambhavātsamudāyasyaiva tat setsyatītyādeśā vācakāśceti samabhivyāhṛtavarṇānāṃ vācakatāyā hetutvopanyāso viphalaḥ, unmattapralapitatvaśaṅkāyā apasareṇaiva nirāsādityata āha-etacceti samabhivyāhṛtavarṇānāṃ vācakatvopanyasanañcetyarthaḥ| caramavarṇa eveti padānte śrūyamāṇavarṇe evetyarthaḥ| evakāreṇa padaghaṭakatatprāktanavarṇavyudāsaḥ| tatra hetumāha-śakteriti svarūpasambandhātmikāyāṃbodhakāraṇatāyāṃ paryyāptatvāsambhavāccaramavarṇādeva bodhodayenānyatra tatsattve mānābhāvāditi vārthaḥ|
nanu tasyaiva vācakatve tanmātraśravaṇādarthabodhāpattirata āha-pūrvapūrvavarṇeti tatpadaghaṭakapūrvapūrvavarṇetyarthaḥ| matāntareti samabhivyāhṛtavarmānāmavācakatve tatsamudāyarūpapadasya sutarāmavācakatvamiti yanmatāntaraṃ tatkhaṇḍanāyetyarthaḥ| uktamityasya mūla iti śeṣaḥ| vastutastu neyamāpattiḥ| ud‌bodhakatatkalpe dūṣaṇāntaramapyāha-rāmo'stītyādi| arthabodhāpattyeti ud‌bodhakasamavadhānasya phalānumeyatvena tādṛśasthale phalābhāvenod‌buddhasaṃskārābhāvāditi bodhyam|
nanu samabhivyāhāro na samūhaḥ, varṇānāṃ kramikāṇāmāśuvināśināñca tadasambhavādata evāvyavahitottaratvasambandhena na pūrvapūrvavarṇavattvam, tasyaivābhāvāt| nāpi varṇasamavetaṃ padārthāntaram, mānābhāvāt, anyathā tādṛśaśadharmānugatīkṛtavarṇānāṃ vācakatvenaivopapattau padasphodasya nirālambanatā''patteḥ, padaprayuktakāryāṇāṃ varṇeṣveva darśanāccheti kathaṃ padasphoṭasiddhirityata āha-digiti tadarthastu varmasamudāyaḥ padam, na varṇa iti pratītervarṇātiriktapūrvāparībhūtatatsamudāyātmakapadasvīkāra āvasyakaḥ| ata eva `pade varṇaḥ iti vyavahāro'pi svarasataḥ saṅgacchete| nityāśca varṇāsteṣu paurvāparyyañca-pūrvapūrvavarṇānusandhānasyāvyavahitottaratvasambandhenottaravarṇānusandhāne vākyasphovakṣyamāṇarītyā sattvāttena svaviṣayakānusandhānaviśiṣṭānusandhānaviṣayatvarūpamiti nānupapattiriti| yathā caitat tathā vākyasphoṭanirūpaṇe vakṣyata iti| tathāca `varṇasamudāyo vācakaḥ' iti padasphoṭapakṣe paryyavasyati  pāribhāṣiketi pāṇinyādisaṅketitetyarthaḥ| pūrve tu ādeśasmāritavarṇānāṃ bodhakatvanirāsena prakṛtighaṭakavarṇasamudāyarūpasya, pratyayaghakavarṇasamudāyarūpasya ca tasya vācakatoktā| idānīntūbhayaghaṭakavarṇasamudāyasyaiva seti bhedaḥ| `ghaṭ' iti prakṛtiḥ, eneti pratyaya iti prakṛtipratyayayorvibhāgasya jñānāsambhavāditi yojanīyam| sarve sarvapadeti varṇanityatāpratipādakaṃ bhāṣyametat| vasnasāderapi pakṣatvādāha-vyājeneti tathāca niścitasādhyavattvasyaiva dṛṣṭāntatve tantratvena tasya pakṣabhinnatvābhāve'pi na dṛṣṭāntatvamanupapannamiti bhāvaḥ| tatra-vasnasādisthale| vibhāga-prakṛtipratyayayoḥ pārthakyena nirṇaya ityarthaḥ| asya vaiyākaraṇānāmapīti śeṣaḥ| pare tu āmādau pratyayatvasyadṛṣṭatvena vāmityādāvāmo va ityādervasādeḥ pratyayatvamitarāṃśasya prakṛtitvamanumātumaśakyamityuktarītyā na prakṛtipratyayasamudāyūpapadasya vācakatvasiddhiḥ| ghaṭenetyatra tu naśabdasya ṭādeśatvaṃ bhāṣyakṛtaivoktamiti na tatra vibhāgāsiddhiḥ| `ajarghāḥ,acakāt' ityādau tvagatyā 'śiṣyamāṇaṃ lupyamānārthābhidhāyi" iti nyāyāt prakṛtereva vācakatā| astu vā tatrāpi luptapratyayānusandhānād bodhaḥ| prakṛtereva vā svārthaviśiṣṭapratyayārthe lakṣaṇā| sarve sarvapadādeśāḥ" ityāpi varṇānityatvavādināṃ mate dūrāpastamityāhuḥ  yukteraiktayāt kramaprāptaṃ vākyasphoṭanirūpaṇamityāsayena mṛlamavatārayati-suptiṅanteti| tat vācakatvam| samudāya iti padasamudāyarūpe vākya ityarthaḥ| evamiti vākyaśaktivadityarthaḥ| viśiṣya-sūtropāttarūpeṇa| ajñāyamāneṣvityanena tanniṣṭhabodhakatvajñānāsambhavaḥ| sūcyate-samudāyavyatpattyeti samudāyasya vācakatvagraheṇetyarthaḥ| bodhāt-prakṛtyarthaviśiṣṭapratyayārthabodhādityarthaḥ| tatrāpi-suptiṅantarūpapade'pi śaktiriti tatkalpanetyarthaḥ| nanu `ghaṭena' ityādimūlena samayuktikasphoṭadvayopādānāt kathaṃ padasphāṭe sādhye vākyasphoṭasya dṛṣṭāntatvam| kiñcaprathamaṃ padasphoṭāsiddho tadarthasambandharūpavākyārthāprasiddhayā kva vākyārthaśaktikalpanāvasara ityādyoṭaparitoṣāt padasphoṭamevadṛṣṭhakṛtya vākyaśaktiṃ sādhayati-vastuta iti tathāca tatra padeṣveva padārthaśaktigrahāt padopastityā padaśaktigrahasambhavastatra padaśaktyaiva vākyārthabodhaḥ| yatra tadasambhavo hare'vetyādau, tatrānāyattyā vākyaśaktyaitad‌bodha iti| yadyapi vyavasthitāviti padamahimnā pakṣadvayaṃ vyavasthitamiti labhyate, tathāpi āvaśyakavākyasphodenaivopapattau padasphoṭakalpanaṃ nāvaśyakamiti siddhānte vākyasphoṭa eva paryyavasyatīti bodhyam| vkyasphoṭasyāvaśyakatāmeva darśayati-anyatheti vākyaśaktyanabhyupagame ityarthaḥ| tādṛśeti vākyārthabodhajanakatvapūpavākyaśaktijñānarahitasyāpītyarthaḥ| tatsattve tu tatrāpi bodha iṣṭa eveti bhāvaḥ| bodhaprasaṅgaḥ-ghaṭakarmakānāyanānukūlakṛtibodhāpattiḥ| padavṛttijñānajanyapadārthopasthiteḥ sattvāditi bhāvaḥ| ghaṭamānayetyatreveti| atra tādṛśabyutpattirahitasyaityanuṣajyate| nanu śābdabodhakāraṇārthatātparyyajñānarūpasahakāryantarābhāvādeva na bodho'ta āha-satyapīti| bodhābhāvāditi tādṛśabodhābhāvādityartha| tathāca yadbhāvāt kāryābhāvastasta taddhetutayā vākyaśaktirāvaśiyaketi bhāvaḥ| etena vyatirekasahacārakāraṇatāgrāhakaḥ pradarśitaḥ| anvayaṃ pradarśayan vākyaśaktiṃ vyavasthāpayati-tatraiveti ghaṭamānayetyatraivetyarthaḥ| bodhādityanena svarūpasato hetutvasya vyavacchedaḥ| tādṛśasākāṅ‌kṣajñānaniṣṭhabodhahetutvāsmanmate vākyaśaktitvenākāṅ‌kṣāśaktiriti paribhāṣābhede'pi vākyasphoṭasiddhinirābādhetyaśeyanāha-tarhiti| nanu śābdabodhahetvarthopasthitijanakatvaṃ śaktitvam| tattu pade eva na vākye, tanniṣṭhabodhakatāyāstādṛsopasthityajanakatvādata āha-arthopasthāpaketi| śabdavṛttiti śabdaniṣṭhaṃ yad‌bodhakāraṇatvamityarthaḥ| tathācopasthitau śābdahetutvaniveśe etatkalpe prayojanābhāvena tajjñānasya tādṛśopasthityajanakatve'pi śaktitvā'kṣateḥ| bodhakaśabdasya śabdatvena niveśāccāpabhraṃśasyāpi saṃgraha iti bhāvaḥ| nanu vākyaśaktivādimate saṃsargarūpavākyārthasya nānātvāttadbhedena śaktyānantyam| kiñci padavṛttyā vākyaśaktiṃ vinā'pi vākyārthabodhajananādvākyaśaktigrahasya taddhetutāyāṃ vyabhicāraḥ| akhaṇḍavākyapakṣastu nedānīṃ prakrāntaḥ| kāryyatāvcachedake'vyavahitottaratvaniveśena tadvāraṇe'pyanupasthitasaṃsarge vākyaśaktigrahasya śābdabodhāt prāgasambhavena kathaṃ tad‌grahasya śābdabodhakāraṇateti cet satyam, saṃsargasya viśiṣyānupasthitāvapi tadādivat kārakavibhaktiviśiṣṭadhātupadaṃ kārakaviśiṣṭakriyābodhakamiti sāmānyatastad‌grahāt| tātparyyagrahasya tvayā'pyevameva vācyatvāt| anantaraṃ padaviśeṣasamabhivyāhāreṇa tātparyyavaśāt saṃsargaviśeṣeṇa tatkārakaviśiṣṭatatkriyābhānam| nāpi saṃsargā''nantyaprayuktaśaktyānantyam, sambandhasyānugamakasya niyāmakatāpyubhayoḥ samaiveti tanmataniṣkarṣaḥ| nanu syādevoktaprakāro yadi vākyaniṣṭhabodhakatāyāḥ śaktitvaṃ sapramāṇaṃ syāt, tadeva tu netyata āha-yuktaṃ caitaditi| etad vākyaniṣṭhabodhakatvasya śaktitvam| yuktimevāha-viṣayateti śābdabodhamātre tatsāmānya ityarthaḥ| vṛttijñānasyeti vṛttijñānajanyopasthitirityarthaḥ| viṣayatāsambandhenetyanuṣajyate| lāghaveneti anyathā ghaṭapadaśaktimaviduṣastatpada jñānavato yatkañcid‌vṛttijñānavato ghaṭamānayeti vākyād bodhāpattyā ghaṭaśābdabodhe ghaṭārthakapadavṛttijñānajanyopasthititvena hetutā vācyetyarthabhedenānantakāryyakāraṇabhāvakalpena gauravam| asmanmate tu viṣayatāsambandhena ghaṭavṛttijñānajanyopasthitera sattvānna śābde tasya bhānāmityeka eva kāryyakāraraṇabhāvaḥ kalpyata iti lāghavenetyarthaḥ| nanu ghaṭaghaṭatvayorviśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāve viparītyutpannasya ghaṭamānayetyato ghaṭatvaviśiṣṭabodhavāraṇāya ghaṭatvaviśiṣṭabodhe upasthiterghaṭapadavṛttijñānajanyaghaṭatvaviśiṣṭāyā hetutāyā avaśyavācyatayā tata evānatiprasaṅge uktakāryyakāraṇabhāve mānabhāvo'ta āha-vivecetañcaitaditi padapadārthabhedenānantakāryyakāraṇabhāveṣu vṛttipraveśamapekṣya upasthiteḥ padajñānajanyatvenaiva śābdasāmagrīkukṣo praveśa ucitaḥ| tathā sati samāvāyenākāśasya janakatayopasthitasya devadattādeśca śābde bhānāvāraṇāya viṣayatayā śābdasāmānyevṛttijanyopastiterviṣayatayā hetutvaṃ vācyam| evañca saṃsargasya śābde bhānānurodhena saṃsarge'pi vṛttikalpanamāvaśyakam| tathāca padaiḥ padārthānāṃ vākyena vākyār‌thasya bodhāditi vṛttijñānasyeti yathāśrutārthakameva tatra vivecitam| vastutasyu vṛttijñānapadaṃ yathāśrutārthakameva| vṛttijñānasyaityanantaram anuṣaktaviṣayatāsambandhenetyasya vṛttijñānaviṣayabodhakatvaghaṭakabodhīyaviṣayatāsambandhetetyarthaḥ| anyathā vākyavṛttijñānajanyopasthitereva śābdabodhatvena tasmim̐stena sambandhena taddhetutvāsambhavena vākyaśaktergarbhastrāvāditi bodhyam| atra vadanti-padajanyapadārthotimantareṇa śābdabodhānudayāttadṛsapadārthopasthiteḥ śābdabodhe hetuteti tāvannirvivādam| padasya ca prakṛtyādyatmakasyārthe sambandhaṃ vinā tadupasthāpakatvāśambhavāttasmin vṛttyātmakasambandho'pyāvaśyaka eva, natu tatsamudāyarūpapāribhāṣikapadatya tatsamūharūpavākyasya viśiṣṭhārthe saḥ, taṃ vinā'pi tebhya ākāṅkṣādirūpakāraṇasamavadhānādeva viśiṣṭabodhopapatteḥ| ekapadārthaviśiṣṭāparapadārthaśābdatvasyaiva tatkāryyatāvacchedakatvāt| yattu lāghavād vṛttijñānajanyopasthitorvaṣayatāsambandhena hetutvameva vākyaśaktau pramāṇamiti, tanna, svātantryeṇa taddhetutvasyaivāsiddheḥ| tathāhi-tādṛśakāryyakāraṇabhāve vṛttitvena padavṛtterniveśaḥ| kiṃ vākyavṛtteḥ, kiṃ vā vṛttisāmānyasya?| na tāvadadyaḥ, padṛvattijñānajanyopasthiteḥ śābdavodhahetutvasya sarvaiṃrevābhyupagamāt| na dvitīyaḥ, vākyavṛttestataḥ prāgasiddheḥ| ata eva na tṛtīyaḥ, kiñci tava mate vākyaśaktijanyopasthiteḥ śābdabodhātmakatvena tastiṃstadviṣayatvāsambhavādapi noktarītiḥ-sādhīyasī| apica vākyavṛttijñānasya śābsāmānye na svātantryeṇa hetutvaṃ yadā kadācidviparaṇāditā gha ṭādipadaśakta jñānajanyapadārthopasthitidaśāyāṃ ghaṭamānayeti vākyaśaktimaviduṣa ākāṅkṣādivaśācchābdabodhodayena vyabhicārāt| yadapi samabhivyāhārarūpākāṅkṣaiva vākyamiti tadapi na,tathā sati `ghaṭīyā karmatā' ityādibodhe ghaṭamānayeti vākyaṃ sākāṅkṣam| nirākāṅkṣañca-ghaṭaḥ karmatvamiti sarvasiddhavyavāhārānupapattiḥ|?tathāca samabhivyāhārasya śābdabodhahetutve'pi vākyasya tattvaṃ durupapādameveti kva vākyasaktisiddhiḥ| sambhivyāhāraniṣṭhabodhajanakatvagrahavato bodhe tajjñānaṃ heturityapi riktaṃ vacaḥ| anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ samabhivyāharajñānasyaiva śābdadhīhetutvena tanniṣṭhabodhajanakatvajñānasya hetutāyā evābhāvāt| naca tadapi sambhavati, śābdabodhāt prāk‌saṃsargasyaivānupasthiteḥ| sāmānyatastadupasthitāvapi viśeṣarūpeṇa bhānārthaṃ tātparyyajñānādyapekṣaṇe ca mūlaśaithilyāt tava vākyaśakterevāsiddhayāpattiḥ| vakṣyamāṇarītyā śābdabodhāt prāk tadupasthityabhyupagame vākyavṛttijñānajanyopasthityanantaraṃ śābdabodhābhyupagame vā śābdaprāmāṇyabhaṅgāpattiḥ, anadhigatārthagrahakāraṇasvaiya pramāṇatvāt| ata eva sūtrakṛtā "kṛttaddhitasamāsāśca" ityatra samāsagrahaṇaṃ kṛtam| vākyaśaktau tu tadvaiyarthya spaṣṭameva| "sāmāyikaḥ śabdādarthapratyayaḥ" iti kaṇādoklervākyaśaktigrāhakānuśāsanāderabhāvena tad‌grahāsambhavācceti vākyasphoṭa āpātaramaṇīya eveti pūrvoktaraśaktigrahaprakārasya śaithilyaṃ manasi nidhāya tasmin prakārāntaraṃ vaktumagrimagrantha ityāśayenāha-nanviti| apūrvatvāt-śābdabodhāt prāganupasthitatvāt| nanu śābdabodhāt pūrva pade pratyakṣād‌yupasthitapadārthanirūpitaśaktigrahaḥ sulabha eveti pade samamiti mūlamanupapannamata āśayaṃ prakāśayati-pade eveti padānāmebānvitapadārthe śaktiḥ, anvayaśca-sāmānyarūpema jñānamaḥ, ata eva ghaṭādipadādanvitaghaṭādyupasthitau niyamena tatpratiyogyākāṅkṣā bhavati-kimanvito ghaṭaḥ kimanvitaṃ karmatvamityādyākārā, sāmānyadharmaprakārakajñānasya viśeṣadharmaprakārakajijñāsājanakatvāt| viśeṣa rūpeṇa bhāne tvamādipadasamabhivyāhāro niyāmako'ta eva tasya śābde bhānam, padavṛttiviṣayatvāt, aśakyasyāpi bhāne'ti prasaṅgāt| nacaivaṃ ghaṭamānayetyatretarānvitaṃ karmatvamitirītyā'nekadhā saṃsargasya bhānāpattiḥ, tattatpadebhyastathārthopasthitāvapyākāṅkṣāvaśādekadhaiva tadbhānaprasaṅgat| nacākāṅkṣāyāstadbhānaniyāmakatvāvaśyakatvena tatra vṛttikalpanā'pārtheti vācyam, aśakyabhānābhiyaiva tadabhyupagamāt| tathāca padaśaktyaiva saṃsargabhānopapattau tatra na vākyaśaktiḥ, padatvāpekṣayā vākyatvasya gurutvācceti tanmatam| tathācānivitābhidhānamate'pi pade vākyārthaśaktigrahāsambhavaḥ samāna ityarthaḥ| jñāteti padārthānāṃ prāgupasthityā tatra padārthaśaktigrahasambhavāditi bhāvaḥ| ajñātaiveti vṛttyabhyupagamastu śābde bhānānu papattyaiveti bhāvaḥ| padārthavaditi sapmyantādvatīḥ, upameye saptamīdarśanāt| vākyārthe'pi saṃsarge'pītyarthaḥ| idañca bodhyatvaṃ śaktirityabhipretya tad‌gāhaḥ-śaktigrahaḥ| lakṣaṇaśabdasya cihnādau prasiddheḥ prakṛtopayogayarthaparatayā ta vyācaṣṭe- lakṣyata iti| mūle arthe iti nirūpitatvaṃ sampamyarthaḥ| tacchabdārthe śaktijñāne'nvetītyabhipretya vyācaṣṭe-pade'pītyādi tad‌graha iti vākyārthanirūpitaśaktigraha ityarthaḥ| ayaṃ bhāvaḥ-ghaṭamānayetyādivṛddhavyavahārādyasya pade padārthaśaktinirṇayastasya tadvākyaśravaṇe padaiḥ padārthepasthityādisambadhāne māsā tatsaṃsargaupasthitirnānupapannā| parantu mānasasya prāyaḥ saṃśayātmakasyāpi sambhavāttinniścayārtha śabdāṃ''daraḥ| tatra śaktigrahahetulaṅgādernirṇāyakasya sattvena saṃśayakatvāsambhavāditi| nanu śabdaniṣṭhaśaktinirmayasyaiva śābdabodhe hetutā'vadhāraṇena tasya śābdabodhāt prāguktarītyā'sambhavena vākyaśakteḥ śābdabodhahetutvaṃ durghaṭamevetyata āha-vastutastviti|
tad‌grahaḥ viśiṣṭavākyārthaśaktigrahaḥ| tathāca ca śaktigrāhakaśiromaṇinā vyavahāreṇa pūrvaṃ vākye eva śaktigraheṇaṃ tasyaiva śāṃbdabodhahetutvāvadhāraṇāttādṛśavṛtteśca-svāśrayaviṣayakatva-svaviṣayakod‌buddhasaṃskārasāmānādhikaraṇyobhayasambandhena baiśiṣṭayasyedānīntanavākyajñāne'pyakṣatatvānna vṛttijñānasya śābdabodhe hetutāyāṃ vyabhicāparaॉḥ, madhye vākyārthopasthiteranapekṣaṇācca noktadoṣāvasara iti bhāvaḥ| nanu tarhi padasphoṭasya nirālambanatāpattirata āha-āvāpodvāpābhyāmiti ānayanāpasāraṇābhyāmekapadopādānāparāpadānupādānābhyāmityarthaḥ| tadgrahaḥ-pratyekapade pratyekapadārthaśaktigrahaḥ| tathāca pratyekapadaśaktisācitvayenā''kāṅ‌kṣādivaśāt padādvākyārthaśābdabodastadā padasphoṭaḥ, yadā tu tannirapekṣyaiva sā bodhaṃ janayati tadā vākyasphoṭa iti matadvayaṃ paryvasyatīti bhāvaḥ| kecitu-upasthitāḥ padārthastadupasthitirvā śābdabodhahetunatu padajñānamapi, tadvinā'pi śābadbobodayena vyabhicārāt| taduktam-`paśayataḥ svetamārūpaṃ hreṣāśabdañca śrṛṇvataḥ| khuravikṣepaśabdañca śveto'śvo dhāvatīti dhīḥ' iti, tanna, śabdaṃ vinā jāyamānasya ghaṭādicākṣuṣasyāpi
