śābdatvāpatteḥ| tatra paśyāmi, na śābdayāmītyanuvyavasāyānna śābdatvaṃ yadi tadā prakṛte'pi samam, uktapratīteranumānenaiva nirvāhāt, tatra śābdapratyayastpasiddha evetyādi svayamuhyam  upalakṣaṇamiti tvanmate `gabhīrāyāṃ nadyāṃ ghoṣaḥ' ityādau pratyekapadaśaktyā| svaśakyasambandharūpalakṣaṇayā vacoktadiśā'bhīpsitārthanveya bodhāsambhavāt sbabodhyasambandhasya lakṣaṇatvābhyupagamena vākyārthamātre vākyalakṣaṇāṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ| pakṣadvayamiti padavākyabhedena vyavasthitaṃ sphoṭadvayamityarthaḥ| anupapattibhevāha-utpannāmanāmiti varṇānityatāvādimate varṇānāṃ yogyavibhuviśeṣaguṇatvena svottarotpannaguṇanāsyatvādekadā'vasthānāsambhavaḥ| tannityatāvādimate tvāha-abhivyaktānāmiti varṇā nityāḥ kintu tadabhivyaktirevānityā, varṇotpādakatvenānyābhimatānāmeva kaṇṭhādyabhighātādīnāṃ tadabhivyañcakatvābhyupagamāt, yugapadavasthānāsambhava evetyarthaḥ| nanvastu varṇānāṃ gayugapadanavasthānāṃ kimasmākamaniṣṭamata āha-varṇāsamūheti| vākyasphoṭakalpe'pi tadāha-tathāceti| jñānāsambhava itīti āśuvināśināṃ kramīkāṇāṃ melakāsambhavādantimavarṇotpattikāle, tadabhivyaktikāle vā pūrvapūrvavaraṇānāṃ tāvadabhivyaktānāṃ vā'vasthānāsambhavena tatsamūharūpapadasya, vākyasya vā tādātmyena pratyakṣakāraṇasyābhāvāt pratyakṣasyaivā'sambhavena tatroktaśaktigrahasya gaganakusumāyamānatvādityākhaṇḍalārthaḥ| abhivyaktipakṣamādāya samādhatte-uttaravarṇapratyakṣeti| avyavahitottareti etannaye varṇānāṃ nityatvena sarveṣāmeva varṇānāmavyavadhānena svādhikaraṇakṣaṇottarakālavṛttitvādavyavasthāpattyā svasvābhivyaktigatamevāvyavahitottaratvaṃ vācyam| tadāropādeva varmeṣvavyavahitottaratvavyavahāraḥ| ata eva tatropasthiterviśeṣaṇatayā niveśaḥ| taccāvyavahitatve satyuttaratvam| avyavahitatvañca-svadhvaṃsānadhikaramakṣaṇasambanditvam| te `ghaṭita ghaṭa' ityādau na śīdhubhāgasya padatvam| anyanirūpitāvyavadhānāderanyasambandhatvābhāvena svatavsaya paricāyakatayā praveśānnānugamaśaṅkā'pi, uttarottaravarṇepasthiteḥ pūrvapūrvavarṇopasthitidhvaṃsānadhikaraṇasamyasambandhitvāttadadhikaraṇakṣaṇadhvaṃsādhikaraṇatvācca nirūktasambandhena tadvattvamavikalam| itthañca pūrvapūrvavarṇā'śe'laukikasāya caramavarṇā'se laukikapratyakṣasya sambhavāttatrārthe bodhakatvarūpaśakteḥ sugrahatvāduktapadavākyasphoṭasiddhirnirābādheti bhāvaḥ 
iti padavākyasphoṭanirūpaṇam || 18 ||
 akhaṇḍasphodanirūpaṇe'vasarasya saṅgatitvaṃ sūcayannāha-idānīmiti sakhaṇjaspoṭanirūpaṇānanrakamityarthaḥ| akhaṇḍeti padavākyayorakhaṇḍatvañca-avidyamānāvayavakatvam| varṇānāmavayavāghaṭitatvasyāpi siṣādhayiṣitatvādāha-dṛṣṭānteti kṛṣṭāntavākyasyevādighaṭitatvaniyamena tadabhāvāt kathaṃ varmeṣvityadestattvamityāśaṅkaya yathāśabdāntarbhāveṇa mūlaṃ vyācaṣṭe-yatheti| ivetīti avayavā ivetyarthaḥ| tathāca yathāśrutasyaiva dṛṣṭāntatvaṃ nānupapannimiti| `vicir‌pṛthagbhāve' iti pravyupasṛṣṭaghañantabhāvasādhanapravivekaśabdārthaḥ| bhede paryyavasyatītyāśayenāha-bheda iti nanu padādipratītai tadavayavānāṃ varṇānāmupalabhyamānatvāt kathaṃ tadasattvamata āha-ayambhāva iti akhaṇḍameveti vyākhyātārthamevakāravyavacchedyaṃ spaṣṭayati-natviti ananteti aneketyarthaḥ| nanu `kakāraḥ `gakāraḥ' iti pratītireva varṇakalpane mānam| yadi ca varṇābhivyañjakatvenotpādakatvena vā'bhimatavāyusaṃyogaviśeṣabhivyaktasphoṭe eva katvādinā tādṛśapratītiviṣaya iti vibhāvyate, tadāpi `utpannaḥ kakāraḥ, naṣṭaḥ kakāraḥ' ityādipratītyanupapattiḥ| sphoṭātiriktakakārādīnāṃ tvayā'nabhyupagamāt| tasya ca nityatvāt vyañjakaniṣṭhāyā utpattestadviṣayatve sukhādīnāmapi nityatā''pattirata āha-tattad‌varṇotpādakatveneti vaijātye pramāṇaṃ darśayati-varṇājanakatāyā ityādi tathāca tādṛśavaijāt‌yaṃ samavāyena sphoṭāṃśe ādāya āropyoktapratītivailakṣaṇyopapattau na tadanupapattiratiriktakakārādivarṇasādhikā| nā'pyupannaḥ ka ityādipratītistathā, `so-yaṃ kaḥ' ityābhedapratyabhijñārūpabādhakasattvena tādṛśapratītebhramatvāt| nāpi sukhādīnāṃ nityatā''pattiḥ, tannāśakasya svottaravarttiyogyātmakaviseṣaguṇasya jāgarūkatvāditi bhāvaḥ| bhāmatyām-tannāmakavācaspatigranthe| āropyata itīti etadanurodhenevādāyeti padamāropaparatayā vyākhyātam| matāntarantu vakṣyate-eveti| vāyusaṃyoga eveti-kaṣṭatālyābhighātajvāyusaṃyoga evetyarthaḥ| evakārema varṇātmakasphoṭavyavacchedaḥ, samavāyasyānāropitasya vācakatāvacchedakatve lāghavāt| tathāpi sa yogasyaiva kādyātmakatvamastvisyarthaḥ| samādhatte-pratyakṣeti ayamāśayaḥ-kādivarmānāmuktasaṃyogātmakatve'tīndriyatā''pattiḥ| naca seṣṭā, anubhavavirodhāt,| nacoktakatvasyā tīndriyadharmatvena kathaṃ sākṣātkāraviṣayatvamiti vācyam, surabhi candanamiti pratītau cakṣurayogyasyāpi saurabhādeścākṣuṣavipayatvavattasyāpi śrātragranṛhyatvasambhavāt| viṣayabādhena paraṃ sphoṭāṃ'śe katvapratīterbhrantittvamiti| anye tu-kādipratītiryadi sphoṭāṃśe katvādikaṃ samavyaināvagāheta tadā tasyābhrantatvasambhāvanā, kintu svāśrayābhivyaṅgayatvasambandhenai| tadaṃśe vaijātyam| naca svākṣayā'bhivayaṅgayatvasya sambandhatve pramāṇābhāvaḥ, kādipratītereva mānatvāt| viśiṣṭapratītiniṃyāmakaviśeṣo hi sambandhaḥ| ata eva `lohitaḥ sphaṭikaḥ' iti buddheḥ svaśrayasaṃyogasya sambandhatāmāmananti| naca samavāyaviṣayaiva sā bhrāntiḥ| yathākathañcit pratīteḥ pramātvopapattau bhramatvakalpanāyā anyāyyatvāt adhikamagre vakṣyata ityāhuḥ| upasaṃharati-tathāceti vācakatvānupapattyeti padavākyayorvarṇasamūharūpatve uktarītyā varṇānāṃ yugapadavasthānāsambhavena vācakatvagrahānupapattyetyarthaḥ| cetyarthaḥ| uktarītyā kathañcicchaktagrahopapādane'pyavarṇaghaṭitapadavākyayoranityatayā pratyabhijñā'nupapattirduṣpariharaiveti bhāvaḥ| eteneti `apāstam' ityanvayi, pratyabhijñānupapattisiddhākhaṇḍasphoṭasya vācakatvavyavasthāpanenetyarthaḥ| ananubhavāditi
tathāca tatraiva pramāṇābhāvena vācakatvaṃ dūraparāhatamiti bhāvaḥ| teṣām gakārādivarṇānām sphoṭātirikteti gatvaprakārakapratītiviṣayagādisphoṭasyaivāsmābhirvācakatvābhyupagamāditi bhāvaḥ| āśutaravināśināṃ sahotpannānāṃ samudāyāsambhavāduktam-kramavatāmiti| kramikāṇāmapi sthaāyināṃ sahāvasthānāduktam-āśutareti tṛtīyakṣaṇavṛttidhvaṃsapratiyogināmityarthaḥ| asambhāvyamiti pratyakṣe tādātmyena viṣayasya hetutayā samūharūpaviṣayasvaivābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ| pūrvaparveti nanu varṇānityatāvādimate tṛtīyakṣaṇavṛttidhṃvaṃsapratiyogināṃ teṣāṃ pratyakṣasyaivāsambhavāt kathaṃ tajjanyasaṃskārasya sahakāritāsambhavaḥ| tathāhi-kādipratītiḥ kakārādau viśeṣaṇatayā kāditvamavagāhate| viśiṣṭabuddhau viśeṣaṇajñānasya hetutayā kakārādyutpattirdvitīyakṣaṇe nirvikalpakamabhyupetya tṛtīyakṣaṇe viśiṣjñānaṃ vācyam, tacca na sambhavati, `sambaddhaṃ vartamānañca dṛsyate cakṣurādinā' iti vṛddhokteḥ| pratyakṣe viṣayasya kāryyasahabhāvena hetutaya tṛtīyakṣaṇe tadvarṇarūpaviṣayasyābhāvāt| naca kakāragādyutpattikṣaṇotpannakatvādismṛtyaiva dvitīyakṣaṇe eva savikalpasambhava iti vācyam, evamapi prāthamikaviśiṣṭabuddherdurupapādatvāditi cet| atra vadanti-saṃsārasyānāditayā bālasya stanapānapravṛttihetviṣ‍ṭasādhanatvasmṛteriva prāthamikakatvādiviśiṣṭabuddhijanakasmṛteḥ sambhavānna tadanupapattiḥ| kakārādyatpattyanantaraṃ kakāro gakāra iti viśiṣṭabuddhereva ca tādṛśakalpane mānamiti| anye sāmānyatastādṛśaviśeṣaguṇasya kāraṇatā syāttdā kakārādipratyakṣānupapattiḥ prasajyeta apa, saiva na, antimaśabdanāśe suṣuptiprākkālotpannajñānādināśe ca vyabhicārāt| kintu vijādīyagumanāśe baijātyaṃ caramaśabdadādivyāvṛttamiti prathamikaviśiṣṭabuddhiviṣayavarṇavyāvṛttamapi| naca tādṛśavaijātye mānā bhāvaḥ, nāśyatā'vacchedakakāṭipraviṣṭatayaiva tatsiddheḥ| tathācāntimaśabdanāśe tad‌dvitīyakṣaṇasyaiva tādṛśavarṇanāśe'pi na tat tṛtīyakṣaṇasthatad‌vyaktitvena hetutvakalpanānna tṛtīyakṣaṇasthāyinastasya pratyakṣānupapattiruktahetumadbhāvasvīkāre'pītyāhuḥ| atiprasaṅga iti saraś‌śabdaghaṭakasakārākārarephavisargānubhavajanyasaṃskārai rasabodhāpattirityarthaḥ| akhaṇḍaḥ-varṇāghaṭitaḥ| nādābhivyaṅya iti dhvanyatargatavarṇābhivyaṅgaya ityarthaḥ| upapāditatvādityasya sakhaṇḍasphoṭanirūpaṇāvasara iti śeṣaḥ| tādṛśapratyakṣasyāntimavarṇoṃśe laukikasyātītavarṇāṃśe'laukikasya saro rasa ityāpnatiprasaktasyopapāditatvādityarthaḥ| atiriktatya vācakatve dūṣaṇamāha-varṇānāmiti| vikalpagrāsāditi| pratyekavyañjakatve ādyavarṇādeva tadabhivyaktisambhave itaroccāraṇavaiyarthyāpatteratiprasaṅgācca| samudāyasya taktarītyā durupapādakatvena tad‌vayañja katvasyāpyasambhavaduktikatvādityarthaḥ| kaiyaṭānuyāyinastu-pūrvapūrvavarṇakāle ced dvitīyādivarṇetpattiḥ sambhavet tadā syādapi kathañcidbhavaduktaṃrītyā padavākyapratyakṣanirvāhaḥ, saiva na, kintu, `yāvadrakāre vāg vartate na tāvadaukāre tenaiva yatnenaiko varṇa uccāryyate| tenaiva vicchinne tasmin varṇe upasaṃhṛtya taṃ yatnamanyamupādāya dvitīyaḥ pravarttate" iti "paraḥ sannikarṣaḥ"  iti sūtrasthabhāṣyaprāmāṇyāt pūrvavarṇavināśānantaraṃ yatnāntareṇa dvitīyavarṇābhyupagamāt| kiñca bhavanmate varṇapratyakṣasyaivānupapattiḥ, teṣāmuccaritapradhvaṃsitatvena tadutpatterabhivyaktervā kṣaṇikatvenātīndriyakṣaṇāvacchinnatvāt| tathāca "iko yaṇaci" ityādisūtropayogino'yaṃ pūrvo'yaṃ para iti pratyakṣaviṣayārthakedaṃśabdābhivyajyamānapaurvāparyyavyavahārasyāsambhavaḥ| evaṃ naṣṭavidyamānayoḥ sambandhinoravyavahitottaratvasya sambandhatayā vaktumaśakyatayā'pi pūrvoktarītyā padapratyakṣānupapattiḥ| apica gṛhītaśaktikasyaiva bodhakatvamiti niyamenoccāraṇabhedādbhirnnaṣu śaktigrahāsambhavaḥ| ānupūrvyāstattatkālaghaṭitāvyavahitottaratvaghaṭitatvenānanugamakatvācchaktatāvacchedakatvāsambhāvat| akhaṇḍhasphoṭāṅgīkāre tu tattadvarṇopādyavacchinne tasmin pauvāparyyādivyavahāraviṣayatvasya sūpapādatvānnoktānupapattaḥ| tasya cā'nupūrvyantargatasamastavarṇā eva yadyapyabhivyañjakāstathāpi caramavarṇābhivyakta eva sa bodhaheturiti noktavikalpasyāpyavasara ityāhuḥ| nanu vāyusaṃyogānāṃ pratyekamabhivyañjakatvābhyupagame'pi gaurityatra ghatvena ṭatvena ca sphoṭo'bhivyajyatetyata āha-parantviti tathāca vilakṣaṇavāyusyogasya gatvaprakārakaspoṭābhivyatrjakatvābhyupagamānnoktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ| ata eveti vilakṣaṇavāyusaṃyogasya tattadrupeṇa spoṭābivyañjakatvābhyupagamādevetyarthaḥ| tadatirekāsvīkāra iti tattatsaṃyogādaiva katvādiprakārakakakārapratītyupapattau sphoṭātiriktatatkalpane mānābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ| nanu saro rasa ityādāvatiprasaṅgastadavastha eva, satvādirūpeṇābhivyatrjakavāyusaṃyogānāmubhayatra tolyādāta āha-evañceti ghavatvamiti tathācaupādhikabhedamādāya tasminneva ṭākarātmake ghatvaprakārakapratītiviṣayāvyavahitatvagrahaviṣaye yathā ghaṭapadavyavahāraviṣayatā tathā rātmakesphoṭe satvaprakārakapratītiviṣayāvyavahitatvagrahe saraḥpadavyavahāraviṣayateti na saraātmakasphoṭasya rasarūpateti bhāvaḥ| nanu tava mate gakārādivarmasya nityasphoṭarūpatayā nityatvena tad‌dhvaṃsādikaraṇakṣaṇānutpattikatvaviśiṣyatadadhikaraṇakṣaṇātvattikatvarūpā vyavahitottaratvāsambhavāt kathamuktaprakārasambhavo'ta āha-parantviti tathāca varṇānaṃ nityatve'pi tadabhivyaktera nityattrasya sāṃkhyānāmapi sammatatvena tadavyavahitottaratvasya varṇāntaribhivyaktāvavādhena tadādāya ghaṭādipadapratyayāpapattiriti bhāvaḥ| ata eveti abhivyaktinirūpitāvyavahitottaratvasyānupūrvāvyavahāraniyāmakatvādevetyarthaḥ| navīnaistathānabyupagamādāha-vṛddheti| eteneti ānupūrvavacchinnavarṇātmakasphoṭasya vācakatvavyavasthāpanenetyarthaḥ| apāstamityanenānvayi| sphoṭa iti vācakatvenābhimate'khṇḍapadārtha ityarthaḥ| nādya iti naika iryarthaḥ| tatpakṣe dūśaṣaṇamāha-ghaṭapada iti ghaṭa ityānupūrvyavacchinnasphoṭe gṛhītaśaktikasyaityarthaḥ| kalaśapadādityasyāgṛhīta tadavacchinnasphoṭaśaktikādityādiḥ| hodhaprasaṅgāditi ghaṭavodhakalṛptakāraṇatākakalaśa ityānupūrvyavacchinnasphoṭasya tadānīṃ sattvānnityekarūpatvāt tasya varṇasamudāyarūpapadasya vācakatve tu noktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ| arthadhīheturiti uktasthale ca sphoṭasya kalaśarūpaparyyayābhivavyaktayavacchinnaśaktigrahaviṣayatvābhāvānna arthabodhaprasaṅga iti bhāvaḥ| evamiti tatparyyāyajanyaśābdabodhe tatparyyāyābhivyaktayavacchinnaśaktigrahasya hetutvābhyupagama ityarthaḥ| śaktigrahāvaśvambhāveneti nāgṛhītaviśeṣaṇanyāyena tatparyyāye śaktigrahasyāvaśyamabhachyupeyatvāditi bhāvaḥ| ucitatvāditi aucityamevāh-tathā satīti| śaktigrahatveneti tatparyyāyagataśaktigrahatvenetyarthaḥ| lāghavāditi uktarūpāpekṣayaitasya kāraṇatāpacchedakatve lāghavādityarthaḥ| co hetau| pūrvakalpe gauravaṃ prakāśayati-antheti uktivaicitryametat| na dvitīya iti paryyāyasthale sphoṭanānātvamityapi pakṣo netyarthaḥ| antheti pratiparyyāyabhinnānāṃ sphoṭāmāṃkalpanāmityarthaḥ| śaktimiti tattadbhedabhinnānantaśaktikalpanānāpadeṣvityarthaḥ| aneketi paryyāyabhedabhinnetyarthaḥ| sarvasiddhatvāditi tathāca paryyāyeṣu nānāśaktikalpanā nāpūrveti na doṣaḥ| ayamāśayaḥ-varṇā sphoṭā''bhivyatrjakā iti svīkāre bhavaduktadūṣaṇasyāvasaraḥ, nanu sphoṭasya varṇānatirekapakṣe, tatra pṛthak‌śaktikalpanāyāya abhāvena goravāsambhavāt| paryyāyaśaktigrahakāryyatā'vacchedakakoṭāvavyavahitottaratvaniveśena vyabhicāravāraṇantūbhayoḥ samamiti| nanu tvanmate sphoṭānapirekeṇa varṇānāṃ nityatvādutpattighaṭitāvyavahitottaravarṇeṣu pūrvapūrvavarṇavattvarūpaśaktatāvacchedakānupūrvyasambhavo'ta āha-tadavacchedaketi śaktatāvacchedaketyarthaḥ| prāgiti svābhivyaktyadhikaraṇakṣaṇotpattikābhivyaktiviṣayatvamiti grantheneti śeṣaḥ| nanu varṇānāṃ nityatvābhyupagame nityavarṇaireva sphoṭakāryetpattau sphoṭasyauvāsiddhiḥ| tathāha-prānupūrvyā bhavadbhirebopapāditatvāttadviśiṣṭānāmeva teṣāṃ padatvena tatpratyayasya saulabhyena śaktigrahasya sūpapādatvāttadevedaṃ padaṃ vākyamiti pratyabhijñopapatteścetyata āha-digiti tadarathastu yadi svatantrā nityā varṇāḥ syustadā syādeva taiḥ sphoṭānyathāsiddhiḥ| kintu katvādinā pratīyamānaḥ sphoṭa eva kakārādivarma ityabhyupagamenoktānyathāsiddhayasambhavādata eva nānāvarṇakalpanāprayuktagauravaśaṅkāpi neti| nanu ko'sau sphoṭo yadanatiriktā varṇā iti cedtrāhuḥ-īśvarasisṛkṣātmakamāyābindvaparaparyyāyatriguṇātmakāvyaktaprabhavaḥ śabdabrahmāparanāmā cetanādhiṣṭhito'nabhivyaktavarṇaviśeṣo ravaḥ parādiśabdairvyavahniyamāṇo nādaḥ sphāṭa ityucyate| sa ca sarvagato'pi purūṣasya jñātārthavivakṣādhīnaprayantādhiṣṭhitamūlādhārasthapavanenābhivyaktaḥ| pareti vyavahriyate| nabhiparyyantamāgacchātā tenābhivyaktaḥ| paśyantīti| punarhṛdayamāgacchatā tenaivābhivyaktastattadarthollekhijñānaviṣayaḥ paraśrotrāgrāhyatvāt sūkṣmo madhyamā vāgiti| vaktrātu karṇapidhāne sūkṣmataravāyvabhighātenopāṃsuśabdaprayoge ca śrūyate| sa eva cā''syaparyantamāgacchatā tena vāyunā kaṇṭhadeśe gatvā mūrddhānamāhatya pāravṛttya tattatsthāneṣvabhivyaktaḥ paraśrotreṇāpiṃ grahītuṃ śakyo vaikharīti vyavahriyate| uktārthe pramāṇañca--`catvāri vāk‌parimiti (ni) padāni, tāni vidurbrāhmaṇā ye manīṣiṇaḥ, guhā trīṇi nihitā neṅgayanti, turīyaṃ vāco manuṣyā vadanti" iti śrutiḥ, `bindostasmādbhidyamānādravo'vyaktātmako'bhāvat| sa eva śrutisampannaḥ śabdabrahyeti gīyate' iti pūrāṇavākyañca| bhāgavate'pyekādaśaskandhādau sphuṭo'yamarthaḥ| śikṣāyāmapi-ātmā budhdyā sametyārthānmano yuṅkte vivakṣayā| manaḥ kāyā'gnimāhanti sa prerayati mārūtam| sodīrṇo mūrdhnyabhihato vaktramāpadya mārūtaḥ| varṇān janayate-ityanena| tathācata hṛdayāvacchinnamadhyamāyāṃ yo nādāṃśa āntarapraṇavarūpaḥ sa eva vācakaḥ| "oṃkāra eva sarvā vāk‌ seṣā sparśoṣmabhirvyajyamānā nānārūpā bhavati" iti śruteḥ| sarvaprāṇihṛddeśatthatvācva brahmapadavyavahāryarthe'pītyanyatra vistaraḥ| iti pratīteriti anyathā varṇaparamparārūpamālāyāstatsamūharūpapadānatiriktatayā''dhārādheyabhāvāgāhipratītyanupattiriti bhavaḥ| anyatheti pratīteḥ padārthāsādhakatva ityarthaḥ| `kapāle ghaṭaḥ' iti pratītyā bhedāvagāhinyā tastiddhiḥ, pratītestadasādhakatve tu ghaṭādyasiddhaḥ spaṣṭaiva| kapālatatsaṃyogānādāyaiva tādṛśapratītyupapatteriti bhāvaḥ| kausetubhe tviti tuśabdenāruciḥ sūcitā| tad‌bījantu varṇātiriktasphoṭānaṅgīkāre'pi noktapratītyanupapattiḥ| ānupūrvyavacchinnavaraṇānāṃ mālāpadārthatvena tatrānuparvorūpadasyādhārādheyabhāvasambhavāt| ghaṭakapālādīnāṃ vijātīyakāraṇajanyatvena tadbhedasyāpalapitumaśaktayatvenoktātiprasaṅgābhāvācceti| anye tu-na varmānāṃ sphoṭātmakatvam| kintu sphoṭasya varṇābhivyaṅgayatvameva| naca pratyekavyañjakatvapakṣoktadoṣaḥ, prayogāntargatasakalavarṇānāṃ tad‌vyañjakarūpapratibimbajanyatadrūpārūṣitasyaiva pratītyā nānātvena pratītiraupādhikī ekasyaiva mukhasya kṛpāṇadarpaṇādyabhivyañjakavaśāddairdhyavarttu latvādipratītivat| ata evānugamārthaṃ na tatra jātikalpanā'pi| varṇabhinnavyañjakābhāvācca na kadācidapi varṇarāhityena tatpratītistatpratibambasamarpakāśca saṃskārā eva| te ca yena krameṇa cittasthāstenaiva krameṇa vyañjakarūparūṣitatā tasyaityabhyupagamacca na saro sara ityanayoraviśeṣaḥ| vyañjakarūparūṣitasyaiva tasyārthe śaktigrahācca na ghaṭādiparyyāyābhivyaktasphoṭe gṛhītaśaktikasyāprasiddhapadaśravaṇe'rthabodhāpattiḥ, ata evedamekaṃ padamekaṃ vākyamiti vyavahāraḥ svarasataḥ saṅgacchata ityāhuḥ  evamiti akhaṇḍasphoṭasyaiva vācakatve ityathaḥ| aprāmāṇyaprasaṅgamevopapādayati-padasyeti vākyasyāpyupalakṣaṇamidam| akhaṇḍatvāditi prakṛtipratyayavibhāgāśūnyavādityarthaḥ| tathābhūtasyaiva vācakatāyā bhavadbhirupapāditatvāditi bhāvaḥ| śāstrasya vyākaraṇātmakasya| prakṛtipratyayābhyāmiti tābhyāṃ yad‌ vyutpādanam-śaktibodhanaṃ tanmātraprayojanatvādityarthaḥ| tathāca prakṛtipratyayavibhāgena padārthavācakatvabodhakasyāsya śāstrasya padasyākhaṇḍatve'prāmāṇyāpattiriti bhāvaḥ| upeyapratipatyartheti vacanavipariṇāmena uttaratrāpyanveti| upeyasya-bodhanīyasya pratipattaye eṣā kalpanā śritā-svīkṛtā munineti śeṣaḥ, pṛṣṭavān sa upācānnaṃ brahma" iti tasyāpi tathātvaṃ bud‌dhvā pṛṣṭaḥ "mano brahma" iti| tasyā'pyaśitamatannaṃ tredhā bhavati-"prāṇo brahma" iti tasyāpi tathātvaṃ bud‌dhvā pṛṣṭaḥ "mano brahma" iti| tasyā'pyaśitamannaṃ tredā bhavati-"yatsthūlaṃ tatpurīṣam, yanmadhyamaṃ tanmāsaṃ yadaṇīyastanmanaḥ" iti śravaṇādanityatvamabadhārpya punaḥ pṛṣṭaḥ, "vijñānaṃ brahma" iti tasyāpi vṛttyupahitatvaṃ jñātvā punaḥ pṛṣṭenopadiṣṭam-"ānando prahmeti vyajānāt"| tato vastuttvaṃ prāpya sthita iti pratipāditam| nanu neyaṃ vallī pañcakośapratipādikā| pañcamasyānupāyatvādakośatvātasyaivava tatra prahṇatvabodhanādityāśaṅ‌kyānandavallīsthipañcakośodāharaṇamāha-yathā veti tatratyā hi pañcakośā upāyā eva, upadiṣṭāñcamānandakoṣasyāpi vaiṣayikatayā'nityatvenābrahmatvāt kintu `bahma pucchaṃ pratiṣṭhā" ityatra śrūyamāṇabrahmapadasyaiva mukhyabrahmaparatvam| ata evā'dhārārthaṃkaḥ pucchaśabdo'pyupapadyate, tasya lāṅ‌gūlarūpamukhyārthasya bādhat| evañcānandavallayāṃ yathā pañpanakośāḥ sarvādharakabrahmabodhanāyāpāyatayopāttaḥ, teṣāṃ vāstavabrahmatvam, tathehāpyavāstavaprakṛti-pratyayā vācakatvatyutpādāna vāstavsaphoṭaniṣṭhavācakatvabādhanāyetvākhaṇḍalārthaḥ| śravaṇādita iti ādinā mananādiparigrahaḥ|
na śāstramiti etacchāstraṃ vinā'pi śravaṇā'dinā vyabhicārāditi bhāvaḥ| upāyasyopāyāntarādūṣakatve sadṛṣṭāntāṃ yuktimāha-tathāceti vyākaraṇādhyayanasya sphoṭajñānatvaṃ na kāryyatāvacchedakam, kintu tādṛśajñānagatavaijātyam tadavacchinnañva nopāyāntarāditi na vyabhicāraḥ| vaijātyasya prāganupasthitāvapi kāraṇatāgraho vidhivādoktadiśā'vaseya iti bhāvaḥ| mantrajanayamiveti mantrajanyatā'vacchedakamivetyarthaḥ| evamagre'pi| nanu puruṣārthāsādhakasya tasya sampādanavaiyarthyamata āha-tasya ceti sphoṭajñānasya cetyarthaḥ| yajñādīnāmiti kāmanāparityāgena vidhīyamānānāṃ teṣāmityarthaḥ| yajñādītyādinā `tameta vedānuvacanena brahmaṇā vividiṣanti yajñena dānena tasā'nāśakena' iti śrutyuktadānādiparigrahaḥ| śarīrādītyādinā vāgindriyaparigrahaḥ| nanu śarīrādiśuddherapyapūruṣārthatayā taduddeśenāpi pravṛttidurghaṭetyata āhṛ-sākṣāditi svargādītyādinā|ṭapavargaparigrahaḥ| sākṣātsvargahetutvam, paramparayā'pavargahetutvañcetyarthaḥ| svaragahetutve "caikaḥ śabdaḥ" iti bhāṣyapaṭhitaśrutiḥ, paramparayā mokṣahetutve ca, 'dve brahmaṇī beditavye" iti smṛtiḥ pramāṇam| paramparāghaṭakaṃ cātmatattvajñānam "tameva viditvā" iti śrutyā tadatiriktasya sākṣānmokṣahetutvavyavacchedabodhanāditi bodhyam| uktārtha harisammatimāhṛtaduktamiti| tat-nādātmakasphoṭapratipādakaṃ śāstram| apavargasyeti mokṣsayetyarthaḥ| dvāramiti tadupayogītyarthaḥ| hetugarbhaviśeṣaṇamāha-vāṅmalānāmiti|
cikitsitamiti apanayanārthakasannantāt kiteḥ ktapratyaye vāṅmalakarmakāpanayanasādhanaṃ yatastato mokṣadvāramityarthaḥ| panaḥ śuddhau yajñādīnāmiva vāk‌śuddhāveva tasyopayogāditi bhāvaḥ| yataḥ sarvavidyānāṃ madhye pavitramato'dhividyam vidyāsu prakarṣeṇa dīpyata ityarthaḥ. tathocoktam-"teṣāṃ ca sāmargyajuṣāṃ maharṣayo vyākaraṇaṃ nirāhuḥ" iti| idamiti siddhasopānaparvaṇāṃ idaṃ prathamaṃ padasthityadhikaraṇamityarthaḥ| atra-sphoṭe' atītaviparyyāsaḥ -bhrāntiśūnya etattattvajñānavāniti yāvat| kevalām parākhyām|  yogajadharmeṇa pratyakṣīkarotītyarthaḥ| tathāca yogitvāsiddho kimavaśiṣyate tattvaśiṣyate tattvajñāna ityarthaḥ taduktaṃ bhāgavate dvādaśe nādanirūpaṇāntaraṃ-
yadupāsanayā brahma yogino malamātmanaḥ| dravyakriyākārakākhyaṃ dhūtvā yāntyapunarbhavam|
iti bhāvaḥ| alīkatvāsiddheriti|| pañcakośādivat prakṛtipratyayavibhāgasyāpi māyikatvena nātyantā sacchaśaśrṛṅgādivadalīkatvamiti vakṣyamāṇaprāyatvādityarthaḥ| avāstavatve'pi maṇiprabhāpratibambasya maṇiprāptāvivaitasyāpi paramātmadarśane upayogasambhavādatiti bhāvaḥ| ādipadeneti pañcakośādivadityatropāttenādiśabdenetyarthaḥ| nyāyastu samāsavāde prapañcitaḥ utpattivināśapratītisākṣikaṃ varṇānāmanityatvaṃ tādṛśavarṇātmakasphoṭābhyupagame tasyāpyanityatvaprasaṅga ityāśaṅkāsamādhānaparatayā mūlamavatārayati-nanviti varṇajātīnāmiti varṇānāmaḥ tad‌vṛttikatvādijātināñcetyarthaḥ| na syāditi ayaṃ bhāvaḥ-`utpanno naṣṭo vā kāraḥ" ityādipratītiḥ kakārādivarṇātmakaḥ sphoṭastad‌vṛttijātirvotpattyādimattvena viṣayo vācyaḥ| tathāca na sambhavati, dvayoreva nityatvājjāyamānāyāstasyā viṣayabādhena bhramatvāpatteriti tādṛśapratīterbhrantatve iṣṭāpattāvāha-vāyusaṃyogeti| kādipratītīnām, -kakāro gakāra ityādibuddhīnām| bhramatvāpattiriti yathāśrutābhiprāyeṇedam, pūrvoktāntamakalpe bhramatvāprasaṅgāt| naca tvanmate'pi katvādīnāṃ laukikāviṣayatvānupapattistad‌vyañjakaśratrasamavetasamavāyasya laukikasannakarṣasyābhāvāditi vācyam, tanmate katvādīnāṃ vāyusaṃyogavṛttivarmatvena `katvaṃ sākṣātkaromi' iti pratītyā svasamavetavyañjakasamāvāyasyaiva saṃsargatopagamena laukikaviṣayatvānapāyāt| tādṛśānekapratītīnāṃ bhramatvakalpanāpekṣayā etatkalpanāyā evocityāt| `ārope sati nimittānusaraṇam' iti nyāyāt| mūlaṃ tu tanmate itthamavatāraṇīyam, nanu svasamavetābhivyañjakagatasamavāyasya sambandhatā na kenāpyādṛtetyata āha-kalpitānāmiti kalpitānāṃ samavāyasambandhena tadavṛttīnāmapyuktasambandhena tatra kalpitānāṃ vāyusaṃyogagatadharmaviśeṣaṇāmupādhitvamudāttavādipraaikārapratītijanakatvamityarthaḥ| adhikaṃ tvagre vakṣyate| dṛṣṭāntasphuṭīkaraṇāya mīmāṃsakamatamāha-udāttatvādīti ādinā'nudāttatvaparigrahaḥ| udāttatvānudāttatvayoḥ parasparavirodhādvarṇabhedena tatsattā vācyā| varṇabhedaśca tadanityatve, anekatve vā syāt vā syāt, tadeva tu na sambhavatītyāha-tasyaikatvādityādi nityatve pramāṇamāha-tacveti nityatvam, ekatvañ‍cetyarthaḥ| tyāgāyogāditi aviṣayatvāyogādityarthaḥ| bādhiketi vyaktyabhede pratītirbādhaketyarthaḥ| prāgasattve satītyādi svādhikaraṇasamayadhvaṃsavattvasambandāvacchinnapratiyogitākābhāvavattvāvacchinna kālikaviśeṣayaṇatāsambandharūpāyā ityarthaḥ| dvitīyādibhaṇasambandhasya niruktasambandhena svavattvānna tatrātiprasaṅgaḥ|
ata eveti anubhavaviruddhatvādevetyarthaḥ| pratyayaḥ-jñānam| vyavahāraḥ-śabdaprayogaḥ| nanuccāraṇamapi tadutpattyanukūlo vyāpāra eva| tathāca sarvapratītirvarṇānityatvasādhiketyata āha-uccaritatvañceti abhivyaktiviśiṣṭatvaṃ tadviṣayatvam| tathāca tādṛśapratyayābhivyaktiniṣṭhaṃ parṇānirūpitaviṣayitvameva viṣayīkriyate, natūtpāttaniṣṭha varṇavṛttitvāmati na sā'nityatvasādhiketi bhāvaḥ| nanu varṇābhivyaktijakaḥ kaṇṭhatālvādyabhighāta evoccāraṇapadārthaḥ, natu varṇotpattijanaka ityatra kiṃ pramāṇam| kiñci varṇānityatvavādināmutpadyate varṇo varṇaṃmutpādayatītyādiprayogā iṣṭhā eveti nānubhavirodho'pītyata āha-kiñceti utpattyāderityādinā `vinaṣṭo varṇaḥ' iti pratītisākṣikavināśaḥ parigṛhyate| paramparayā-svā?yadhvanivyaṅgayatvarūpayā|nātirikteti-varṇānityatvasādhiketyarthaḥ| natu sphoṭātirikteti tadarthaḥ, taiḥ sphoṭānaṅgīkārāt| nanvevaṃ dhvanyutpattyādestādṛśapratītyā varṇoṣvavagāhane bhrāntatvāpattirata āha-paramparayā varṇoti svāśrayasaṃyogāvagāhinyā `lohitaḥ sphaṭikaḥ' iti pratīteryathā na bhramatvam, tathā'syā apiti bhāvaḥ| avaśiṣyate ityasyotpanno varṇa iti pratīteriti śeṣaḥ| nanu tādṛśapratīterniyamena paramparāsambanvaviṣayatvakalpane gauravamata āha-parantviti| atirikteti adhiketyarthaḥ| tathācānekavarṇatad‌dhvaṃsādikalpanāpekṣayā tatpratīterniyamatastadṛsasambandhāvagāhitvakalpanaiva laghīyasīti bhāvaḥ| yadi varṇasthale dhvaninaiyatyaṃ syāt syādeva tadotpattestad‌ghaṭitaparamparāsambandhena varṇaniṣṭhatā| tatrai3va ca mānābāva ityaśaṅkaya nirācaṣṭe-nacetivarṇapārthakyena dhvanerananubhavāditi bhāvaḥ| hetvasambadhānādapi tatsattvaṃ na ghaṭata ityāha-tadutpādaketi śaṅkhauṣṭhābhighātajavāyusaṃyogāderityarthaḥ| varṇānutpatteriti varṇānabhivyakterittyarthaḥ| darśanāditi dṛśirjñānasāmānyārthaḥ| sattavāditi-tathāca parthakyenānubūyamānāyāḥ pratītejihvābhighāṭajakaṇṭhavāyusaṃyogāderhetośca sattvānna varṇābhivyaktisthale dhananyanaiyatyamityuktasambandaviṣayakapratītirnirābādheti bhāvaḥ| tavaiveti varṇasthale dhvanyasattvavādina evetyarthaḥ| viparītagauravamiti svakāramavacalājjāyamāne dhvanau varṇotpādakasāmāgryāḥ pratibandhakatvakalpanādhvaṃnyakalpanāprayuktalāghavāpekṣayā viparītagauravagrasteti yāvat| teṣāmiti varṇanityatāvādināṃ mīmāṃsakānāmityarthaḥ| evañceti utpattyādīnāṃ varṇāvṛttitva ityarthaḥ|
pratibandyaiveti parābhimatamādhisamādeyam pratipādakamuttaraṃ pratibandastayetyarthaḥ| tathāca tatra mīmāṃsakairutpattyādi pratīte pramātve yaḥ samādhirāśritaḥ sa evāsmābirapyanusarttavya iti bhāvaḥ| tathācākhaṇḍavākyasphoṭasvīkāre na kiñcid bādhakamiti phalitam| nanvetatkalpe padānāmasattvena mīmāṃsakasiddhāntavirodhaḥ| tathāhi-`vrīhibhiryajeta' ityatra yāgena dravyamātrakṣepe vrīhiśratirniyamārthā-`vrīhibhireva nānyaiiti| tatra brīhyabhāve nityakarmaṇaḥ, prāravdhakāmyakarmaṇaśca lopo mā bhūditi pratinidhirupādīyate| brīhitvasya śaktyupalakṣaṇatvena pratinidherapi śrautatvāt| bhavanmate tatra pratinidhyupādāne tadvākyākhaṇḍārthānanuṣṭhānānnityādervilopāpattiḥ|, nīvārakaraṇakakriyāyā anyatvāt| `kriyā na prativanadhīyate, dravyantu pratinidhīyate eva' it paribhāṣāyā ucchedaśca| evam "vrīhibhireva nānyaiiti| tatra vrīhyabhāve nityakarmaṇaḥ, prāravdhakāmyakarmaṇaśca lopo mā bhūditi pratinidhirupādīyate| brīhitvasya śaktyupalakṣaṇakakriyāyā anyatvāt| `kriyā na pratinidhiyate, dravyantu pratinidhīyate eva' iti paribhāṣāyā ucchedaśca| evam "śvetaṃ chāgamālabheta" ityatra kriyāyāśchāgadravyeṇa sambandhaḥ śrautaḥ, dvitīyāśrutyā tasya sākṣātpratipādanāt| śvetaguṇasya tu vākyīyaḥ saḥ| tatsāmānādikaraṇyāttasya nirguṇasya dravyasya niravikaraṇasya guṇasya cāsambhavāt | śvetaguṇasya chāgasambandhamupacīvyaiva kriyāyambandhācchutipratipāditadravyasambandhāt vākyapratipāditagumasambandhasya durbalatvāt, śvetaguṇasya chāgasambandhamupajīvyaiva kriyāsambandhācchutipratipāditadravya sambandhād vākyapratipāditaguṇasambanadhasya durbalatvāt, śvetacchāgābhāve'nyaguṇakaśchāga ālabhyate, natu śvetaguṇayukto meṣaḥ, śrutibādhāpatteḥ, tasya cākhaṇḍapakṣe asambhavāt| evameva itarapadārthanibandhanamīmāṃsānyāyāsambhavo|'pi| akhaṇḍasphoṭasyaiva bhavanmate akhaṇḍārthatvena kena kasya bādho bhavediti cet, atrāhuḥ-akhaṇjavākyādakhaṇḍatattadarthācca rekhāgavayanyāyena padapadārthāvāpodvāpābhyāṃ kalpitapadapadārthamādāya pratinidhyupādanatyopapattiḥ, ṛṣīṇāṃ tattvārthavabodhe'pi tattvabhūtena vastunā vyavahārāsambhavād dhyavahārakāle'nirddhāritatattvāsadṛsaustairnānāprakāreḥ padatadarthānāṃ vyavahriyamāṇatvānna tadvacobhiḥ padapadārthānāṃ vāstavastyatvam, vākyārthasyāpi tadāpekṣikameva, paramārthadaśāyāṃ sarvasyāpi vyāvahārikasyāsatvayatvāditi| naiyāyikāstu-sarvamidaṃ varṇānāṃ nityatve casambhavet| tatraiva tu pramāṇābhāvaḥ, śadvmātrasyakāśasamavāyikāraṇakatvāt| bherīdaṇḍasaṃyogādīnāṃ dhvanyātmakaśabda pratīva kaṇṭhādisthānāvāyvabhighātasyāpi varṇātmakaśabdaṃ pratyanvayavyatirekabhyāṃ nimittatvādhāraṇācca| evañca tādātmyasambandhena janyabhāvasya pratiyogitāsambandhena dhvaṃsatvāvacchinnaṃ prati hetutayoccaritavarṇasya kṣaṇāntaritasyāpratīternaṣṭo varma ityeva pratīteśca, svāvyavahitottaravarmasyaiva nāśakatākalpane pratiyogitāsambandhena yogyavibhuviseṣaguṇanāśamprati svasāmānādhikaraṇya-svāvyavahitottaravarṇasyaiva nāśakatākalpane pratiyogitāsambandhena yogyavibhuviśeṣaguṇanāśamprati svasāmānādhikaraṇya-svāvyavahitapūrvavartitvobhayasambandhena yogyavibhuviśeṣaguṇasya hetutāyā jñānādisthale klṛptatvācca| kaṇṭhatālvādyabhighātasya ca katvādikameva na gauravamiti vācyam, tathā sati kolāhalapratyakṣaprasaṅgāt| nahi kolāhalapratyakṣaṃ katvādiviṣayakam, yena tallaukikaviṣayitāyāḥ kāryyatā'vacchedakaghaṭakatā sambhavyeta| kiñca ghaṭādyutpādakadaṇḍārerapi tadabhivyaktitvasyaiva kāryatā'vacchedakatvāpattau ghaṭādīnāmapi nityatvāpattiriti bahuvyākopaḥ| naca sata evābhivyaktiriti sāṅkhayamatāśrayaṇāduktāpattiriṣṭaiveti vācyam, tanmatasyāpyāpātamanoramatvāt| tathāhi-abhivyaktiḥ satī, asatī vā | nadyāḥ, ghaṭānutpattidaśāyāṃ ghaṭasyeva tasyā api sattvād ghaṭa iti pratītyāpatteḥ| abhivyakterapyabhivyaktyaṅgīkāre tu tasyā api kāryyatvena sattvāduktadoṣo duruddhara eva| antye-svasiddhāntavyāghātaḥ, yuktitaulyena padārthāntarasyāpyutpattisiddheriti| kiñcotpanno ghaṭo naṣṭo ghaṭa iti pratītyā ghaṭādīnāmanityatvavadutpannaḥ kakāro naṣaṭaḥ sa ityavilakṣaṇapratītyā kakārādīnāmanityatvamevāvadhāryyate| katvādīnāṃ sayogadharmatvantu durupapādameva| varṇāsamevatatvena śrotrāgrāhyatvaprasaṅgat| na coktasannikarṣādeva tatpratyakṣopapattiḥ, tathā sati śabdatvādipratyakṣānāpatteḥ| naca tatsambandhasya kakāraṃ śrṛṇomīti pratīto bhāne pramāṇamasti, śabdaṃ śrṛṇomīti pratīteḥ samavāyaviṣayakatvaduktapratīterapi tadviṣayakatvenopapatteśca| samavāyāṃśe sarvatra tādṛśapratītināṃ bhramatvamityapina varṇasamavetatvasya tatrābhyupagamena viṣayavādhāt| evañcavarṇanāmanekatvena tatrodāttatvādipratītirapi svarasataḥ saṅgaccheta iti dṛṣṭāntadaurmikṣyameva| eva़ñca varmaprāgabhāvadhvaṃsāṃnāṃ pramāṇasiddhānāmapalapitumaśakyatvānna tatkalpanāgauravamapi| naca varṇānityatāvādimate so'yaṃ gakāra iti pratyabhijñānupapattiḥ, tasyāstajjātīyatvaviṣayatvenopapādane tu tajjātīyo'yamityākāraḥ, tatra tu svarūpeṇa9tat tatra so'pyamityākāra ityabhyupagamāt, tajjātīyābhedāvagāhinyāstadeboṣadhamityākārāyāḥ pratyabhijñāyāḥ sarvasiddhatvācca| tasmād varṇātmakasphoṭasya vācakatavmasambhavaduktikameva| sphoṭasya dhvanyatiriktatvābhāvācca yogaśāstrādau tasya nirūpaṇaṃ tūpāsananārthameveti prāguktaprāyam| sakhaṇḍasphoṭastu prāgeva nirasta ityānupūrvavyavacchinnavarṇānāṃ vācakatvamūhyamiti vadanti| ityamiti pūrvoktaprakārairityarthaḥ| pañcadheti varṇapadaṃvākyākhṇḍapadākhaṇ‍vākyabhedabhinna ityarthaḥ 
|| iti bhūṣaṇasāradarpaṇe vyaktisphoṭanirūpaṇam||
 kramaprāptaṃ jātisphoṭaṃ nirūpayati-śakyatva iveti nanuktarityā sphoṭātiriktavarṇānāmasattvāttadratajātervācakatvicāraḥ kākadantaparīkṣāsamo'ta āha sāre-ayambhāva iti hakāragakārayorabhede sādhakamāha-sphoṭasyaikatvāditi tadabhinnābhinnasya tadabhinnattvāniyamena hakārātmakasphoṭābhinnagakāre tadabhedāvagāhibuddheḥpramātvāpattirityarthaḥ| anāpatteśceti abhedādeva ca tadādṛśabhedāvagāhibuddheḥ pramātvānupapatterityartha| nanu varṇātmakasphoṭasyaikatve'pi gatvādiviruddhadharmatyāsamūlakabhedapratīternānupapattiḥ| pramāṇyantu tasyā varṇaikyavādimate dūrāpāstamevetyata āha-kiñceti upādhitvābhimatagatvādītyarthaḥ| ādye-sphoṭe gatvābhyupagamapakṣe| tadeva-gatvameva| nanu gatvādervarṇadharmatayā bhedena kathaṃ tasya tadātmakatvamta āha-varṇānityatāvādibhiriti mīmāṃsakādibhirityarthaḥ| atirikteti dharmadharmiṇorabhedābhyupagamādityarthaḥ| gauravamiti gakārādipratītināṃ gatvādiviśiṣṭagakārādiviṣayaktevanopapattisambhavāditi bhāvaḥ| antye sphoṭe gatvādyanabhyupagamakalpa ityarthaḥ| virodha iti| gādipratītyanupapattirityarthaḥ| gatvasyānyadharmasya sphoṭāvṛttitvena tadviśiṣṭaviṣayakapratītyanupapattiriti bhāvaḥ| vāyusaṃyogāvṛttīti-svamate dhvanivṛttīti-matāntare| pūrvoktarītyā tādṛśavaijātyāropeṇa pratīteḥ sūpapādatvādāha-āropyeti| bhramatvāyogāditi sākṣātsambanvena pramātvopapattāvāropitasambandhaviṣayakatve pramāṇābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ| nanvatiriktānekavarṇakalpanāpekṣayā tādṛśapratītīnāṃ bhramatvameva nyāyyamata āha-astu veti tathāca pratīteḥ prāmāṇyanirbahe'le sphoṭakalpanayeti bhāvaḥ| tasya-saṃyogasya| atīndriyatvamiti laukikaviṣayatāśūnyatvamityarthaḥ| doṣa-vabādhakamityarthaḥ| pratibandyottarayati-dharmavaditi yathā atīndriyamātravṛttidharmatvasya sākṣātkārāviṣayatvavyāpyatvaṃ tvayā na svīkriyate, tathā'tīndriyasaṃyogagataviśeṣasya mayā'pītyarthaḥ| yadyatīndriyasyevaindriyakatvamatyantāsambhavaduktikaṃ tadā tādṛśavaijātyasya sphoṭadharmavattvamapi, tathaiva pratyakṣānupapatteriti bhāvaḥ| nanu katvādidharmaṇāmatīndriyatve'pi jñānalakṣaṇayā kādipratītau tadbhāne bādhakābhāva iti cenna, kakāra iti pratyaye, katvaṃ sākṣātkaromītyanuvyavasāye katvāṃśe laukikaviṣayatvāvagāhenīktaprakāro'sambhavāt| `śaṅkhaḥ pītaḥ' iti jñānānantarotpannaḥ pītatvaṃ paśyāmīti pratyayastu bhrama eva| doṣaviśeṣasya tādṛśaviṣayatāniyāmakatvakalpane gauravāditi vibhāvanīyam| upasaṃrati-santyeveti tvabhimatasphoṭātiriktā varṇāḥ santyevetyarthaḥ| paramiti kintvityarthaḥ| gauravāditi tattadvarṇabhedabhinnānantaśaktikalpane gauravādityarthaḥ| nanu tāvadūrṇagatapadatvādijātyanugatīkṛteṣvekasyā eva sakterabhyupagamānna nānātvaprayuktagoravamata āha-ākṛtyadhikaraṇeti| nyāyastu nāmārtha nirūpaṇe prapañcitaḥ| nanu tādṛsajātau mānābhāvā'ta āha-haripadamiti anugatadharma vinā sakalatādṛśapadeṣvekākāratādṛśapratītyanupapatteriti bhāvaḥ| nanvanugatapratītimātrasya jātisamādhakatve vibhutvāderapi jātitvāpattirataḥ kāraṇatāvacchedakatvādinaiva tatsiddhirvācyā, tadabhāvāt prakṛte kathaṃ tasya jātitvamata āha-avaśyeti anyathā tāvatpadānāṃ tattad‌vyaktitvena hetutayā vyabhicāreyaṇāsambhavāditi bhāvaḥ| pratītyavacchedakatvayoriti anugatapratītyavacchedakatvayorityarthaḥ| varṇānupūrvyeti tādṛśajātyabhivyañjakatvenāvaśyakalpyayā tayetyarthaḥ|  nirvāha iti tathāvaśyakalṝptaniyatapūrvavṛttitā'vacchedakatānupūrvyaivānugatapratītikāraṇatāvacchedakatvayoḥ sambhavena tadrūpaviśiṣṭapadāpasthititvena hetutvamanyathāsiddhiriti bhāvaḥ| anyathāsiddhayāpatteriti na ca seṣṭā, pratyakṣā'dipramāṇavirodhāditi bhāvaḥ| nanu jāteḥ śaktaṃtve jātitvaṃ śaktatāvacchedakaṃ vācyam, tasya ca taditarāvṛttitvāviśiṣṭasakalatad‌vṛttitvarūpatayā tattvāsambhavo gauravādata āha-tādātmyeneti abhedenetyarthaḥ| tathāca noktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ| etattattvamabhihitaṃ prāk‌| jātyoriti tayoḥ padayo rephākārasakāravisargaghaṭitatvāviśeṣādrasatvaviśiṣṭopasthāpakatāvacchedakasarastvaviśiṣṭopasthāpakatāvacchedakajātyorekatarasyā eva sattāyā vinigamakābhāvāṃttato vilakṣaṇārthabodhānupapattirityarthaḥ| vāśabdasya pakṣāntaraparatvabhramaṃ nirākāroti-vā tvartha iti avadhāraṇe ityarthaḥ| bheda iti tathāca tatra varṇataulye'pyānupūrvovailakṣaṇyena rasatvaviśiṣṭopasthāpakatāvacchedakajāterniruktāvyavahitottaratvasambandhena raviśiṣṭhasattvādirūpānupūrvyā eva vyañjakatayā tadabhāvena na saraḥ-padādrasatvaviśiṣṭhabodhāpattiḥ, evamapyatrāpīti bhāvaḥ| mūle tāramandavadityatra tāramandaśabdau dharmaparau| tathāca yathā varṇānāṃ nityaikatvamate vijātīyavāyutālvādyabhighātasattve tāratvādinā bhedapratītistadvadityarthaḥ  varṇajātisphoṭamabhipretya śaṅkate sāre-nanviti| jāteriti arthabodhakajāterityarthaḥ| pratyekamiti padāntargatatattadūrṇeṣvityayaḥ| evamagre'pi| arthabodhāpattiriti tadabhivyañjakavarṇasattvāditi bhāvaḥ| yogārthatayeti sphuṭatyartho'smāditi vakṣayamāṇāvayavavyutpattyetyarthaḥ| eteneti-tatpadaghaṭakayāvad‌varṇābhivyaktajātervācakatvāṅgīkāreṇetyarthaḥ| sarvadā-padāśvaṇe'pi| apapāstamiti jāteḥsanātanatve'pyuktasthale vyañjakāsamavadhānāttadabhivyaktyabhāvenabādhasyāpādayitumaśakyatvāditi bhāvaḥ| nanuvarṇajātisphoṭapakṣe padavaṭakayāvadvarmagatajātīnāmevārthabodhakatvamupagantavyam| tatra jānyavarṇagatajāteranyavarṇānabhivayaṅgayatvāttattadvarṇa evābhivyañjako vācyaḥ| tathācoktadoṣastadavastha evetyata āha-ayambhāva iti| uktadoṣa iti tathāca duṣṭatvāt sa pakṣo nāśrayaṇīya iti bhāvaḥ| tatreti peda vākye ca tasyāḥ -padatvādijāteḥ| vyasajyavṛttitvasyeti| paryyāptyākhyavilakṣaṇasambandhena vṛttitvasyetatyarthaḥ| nanu jāteḥ samavetatvasyaiva sārvatrikatayā kathametasyāḥ paryyāptat‌vamata āha-dharmigrāhaketi dharmavatī jātistad‌grahakaṃ mānaṃ padatvasya vākyatvasya vā vācakatvānupapattistatsiddatvādityarthaḥ| pratyekavarṇaviśrāntajātervācakatvasyoktadūṣaṇakabalitatvenāsambhavādvācakatvānupapattyā kalpyamānā padatvādijāti ryadi punaḥ pratyekaṃ viśrāntā syāt tadā tatsiddhireva na syāt, vācakatvānupapatteraparihārādatasyatsādhākameva tatparyāptatvāsādhakameva cānekavyaktyabhivyaṅgyetyasyānekavyaktiparyyāptetyartha iti bhāvaḥ| vastutastu ghaṭādipratyekavyaktyaikye ghaṭa iti sarvāsiddhapratītyā vyavahāreṇa ca siddhyatu ghaṭatvādeḥ pratyekamātravṛttitvam| na hyokavarṇe ghaṭapadādivyavahāro yena padatvādeḥ pratyekavṛttitā syāt kintu padaśabdaḥ padaṃ na prakāra iti vaiparītyamiti vibhāvanīyam| idaṃ purihāvadheyam| padatvaṃ na paryāptam, jātitvādityanumānaṃ tvaprayojakamanukūlatargābhāvāditi vibhāvanīyam| idaṃ punarihāvadheyam| anekavyaktyabhivyaṅgyetyatyasyānupūrvyavacchinnavarṇābhivyaṅgayeḥ| nātaḥ saro rasa ityādau arthabhedabodhānupapattiḥ| evañca pūrvoktārthasyaivāyaṃ prapañcaḥ| ānupūrvovarmayorviśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvavyatyāsaḥ paraṃ vyatiricyate| ata evāgrimagrantasaṅgatiriti| vyajhyate'nayeti vyutpattyā vyaktipadārtho varṇa ityabhipretyāha-dhvanivarṇayoriti varṇānāṃ dhvaninaiyatyādabhedopacāro'ta eva paspaśāyāṃ "atha gaurityatra kaḥ śabda" iti praśne "loke'rthabodhakatvena gṛhīto dhvanivarṇatmakaḥ samūha" ityarthakam| "athavā patītapadārthako dhavaniḥ śabda" ityuktamiti bhāvaḥ| śeṣārtha iti prakṛtakārikottarārddhārtha ityarthaḥ| varṇaṣu dhvanivyavahāre sammatimāha-uktaṃ hīti| kāvyaprakāśa iti `idamuttamamatiśayini vyaṅgaye vācyād dhvanirbudhaiḥ kathitaḥ"| iti vṛttivyākhyāsara iti śeṣaḥ| sphoṭavyaṅgyeti sphoṭarūpaṃ yad vyaṅgyaṃ tad‌vyañ‌jakavarṇātmakaśabdasyetyarthaḥ  nanvetāvatā prabandhena padatvāderjātitvasya, tadvācakatvasya ca vyavasthāpanamaphalam, purūṣārthānapayogitvādityāśaṅkya samādhānaparatayā mūlamavatārayati-nanu kā seti| sā-vācakatvenābibhatā jātiḥ keti yojanā| satyāṃśa iti satyatvam-kālatrayābādhyatvam, tadviparītamasatyātvam| paryyavasitārthamāha-tad‌vyaktīti tad‌vyaktyupalakṣitamityarthaḥ, paramārthabrahyaṇā vyaktīnāṃ vaiśiṣṭyāsambhavāt| ata eva jagatkarttutvādīnāṃ tadupalakṣaṇatvamāmanantīti bodhyam| jātiriti tad‌vyavahāraviṣaya ityarthaḥ| asatyopādhīti vyaktīnāmupādhitvañca, vastuta ekasyā api nānāpratītijanakatvaṃ tadavacchinnam-tadupalakṣitamityarthaḥ| dravyaśabdeti gavādiśabdetyarthaḥ| guṇagatajāteranaṅgīkārādathavā dravyātmako yaśśabdastad‌vācyamityarthaḥ| mīmāṃsakaiḥ śabdasya dravyatvopagamāt| śabdasvarūpatayeti śrutyātmakaśabdarūpatayetyarthaḥ| `dve brahmaṇī veditavye' iti śruteḥ| ayamāśayaḥ-"tasmādetasmādvā ātmata ākāśaḥ sambhūtaḥ" ityāditaittirīyakaśruto brahmaṇo jagadupādānatvaśravaṇādupādeyasya copādānābhinnatvasya lokaprasiddhatvānnāmārthayorapi svābhinnopādānābhānnatve na parasparābhinnatve susthe pṛthakpratīyamānajagataḥ `neha nānā'sti' iti śrutyā bādhe dṛḍhe bādhitārthapratīteradhiṣṭhānasattaikaniyamyatvenāvaśiṣyamāṇabrahmaṇaḥ sadrūpasya sākṣājjñātumaśakyatayopalakṣaṇībhūtarūpanāmanirūpaṇadvārā tajjñānāya sāstrasyopayoga iti| kathamiti saṅgacchatāmityanenānvitam, pūrvāparavirodhāditi bhāvaḥ| mithyājñānarūpāyāstajjanyasaṃskārarūpāyā vā jātitvāsambhavādāha-āpidyaka iti avidyākalpita ityarthaḥ| dharmaviśeṣa iti atra vṛttivāraṇāya dharmeti tad‌vyaktitvādivāraṇāya viśeṣeti pakṣāntaramiti tathāca matabhedenārthadvayasyāpyākarasthitatvānna pūrvāparavirodha iti bhāvaḥ| vastutastu gavādyupādhyavacchinnasattātvameva gotvādivyavahāraniyāmakam, advaitadarśane ca gavāderavacchedakasyābhāvānna sattāyā visiṣṭasattātvam tadānīṃ nirddharmatvenaivāvasthānādityarthaparatayā'pi virodhaḥ suparihara iti bodhyam  yadyapyaviśeṣaṇa vākyaviṣaye'khaṇḍavākyatajapaja9tirūpasphoṭadvayamuktam, tathāpi nādātmakasphoṭakalpe śāstrasya purūṣārthe paramparayopayogitvamantimakalpe tu sākṣādeveti tasyaiva mukhyatvamityāsayena mūlamavatāraṃyati-tabheveti| "ayamātmā" "tatsatyam" ityādi śrutisiddhaṃ yatstayaṃ tatra sā jātirityanenoktamevetyarthaḥ| mūle itthamiti pūrvoktaprakāreṇa adhyastavācakatvebhyaḥ prakṛtyādibhyaḥ pṛthakkriyamāṇam| ata eva nirañjanam-upādhivinirmuktam| ata evākṣaram-avināśi| `yasya sarveṣu bhūteṣu naśyatsu na vinasyati' iti smṛteḥ, sopādhikasyaiva vināśapratiyogitvāt śabdatattvam śabdapadaṃ rūpasyāpyupalakṣaṇam| tena nāmarūpopādānaṃ yattad brahmeveti viśeṣaṇasaṅgataivakāreṇa brahmatādātmyābhāvarūpe viśeṣaṇābogatvavacchede śabdatattvarūpānavayitāvacchedakavyāpakatvaṃ bodhyata prahuḥ-prakarṣeṇa kathayanti| brahmābhedena jānantīti yāvaditi tadarthaḥ| tatra nāmarūpopādānaṃ bodhyate prahuḥ-prakarṣeṇa kathayanti| brahmābhedena jānantīti yāvaditi tadarthaḥ| tatra śabdopādānatvasya tantrāntaregaganādau prasiddheḥ kathaṃ brahmaṇastadupādānatvamityāśaṅkāyāmāha-ayamartha ityādi| nāmarūpeti 'anena jīvenātmanānupraviśya nāmarūpevyākaravāṇi" iti śrutisiddhetyarthaḥ| dvayīti nāmarūpātmakadvyavayavasamūharūpetyarthaḥ| tatreti suṣṭidvayamadhye rupasyeveti akāśādyatmakārthamyevetyarthaḥ| nāmna iti tad‌vācakaśab‌dasyāpītyarthaḥ| tadeva-brahmeva tattvam-upādānamityarthaḥ. `tasmādetasmādvā ātmanaḥ" ityādiśruteḥ| śabdatanmātrākāśopādānatve paryyavasānād‌dvayorapyupādānaṃ brahmaiva| ata eva tayoḥ parasparamabhedasiddhiḥ, svopādānabrahmābhinnatvāt| naca brahmaṇo jagadupādānatve vikāritvā'pattiḥ, `jagadetadvivarttate' iti vadatā mūlakṛtaiva samāhitatvāt| tathāca vyāvahārikaprātibhāsikapadārthanirāsena śrutyā brahmava pariśeṣīkriyate iti prāgukta evārthaḥ| nanu nānātvasya sarvathaiva mithyātve kathaṃ sa pratyayo lokānāmata āha-prakriyāṃśastviti prakṛtipratyayapañcakośādivyavahārāṃśastvityarthaḥ| avidyeti malinasattvājñānavilāsa evetyarthaḥ| tathāca brahmātiriktatvesatānāmapi teṣāṃ pārthakyena pratītiradhiṣṭhānājñānakṛtaiveti na yāvadadhiṣṭhānājñānaṃ tāvat nānnavṛttiriti bhāvaḥ| nanu tādṛśabrahmajñāne kathaṃ śāstrāṇāmupayogā'ta āha-prakriyāṃśa iti prakṛtipratyayādivyutpādanaṃ tvityarthaḥ| avidyeti tathāca pañcakośādinyāyena śāstrāṇāmupayogaḥ, natu tajjñāne sākṣādupayoga iti bhāvaḥ| avidyaivopavarṇyate iti vākyapadīyānantaram-`upāyāḥ śikṣyamāṇānāṃ bālānāmupalālanāḥ| asatye vartmani sithitvā tataḥ satya samīhat' iti ślokaḥ| "samārambhastu bhāvānāmityasya" pūrvārddhantu-ghaṭāṭidarśanāllokaḥ paricchinno'vasīyate||iti|| tadarthastu yathā ghaṭādikaṃ dṛśvā lokā visvaprapañcaḥ paricchinnaḥ kiñcinniṣṭhakāraṇatānirūpakakāryyatāvattvenā'numīyate, evaṃ bhāvānām-ākāśādīnām samārambhastviti-samyagārambhaḥ-jananaṃ yasmāt| bhāvapradhānaśca nirddaśaḥ, tadutpattihetutvādityakṣarārthaḥ, bāvopādānatvāditi yāvat| tādātmyana vā hetuḥ| samārambhāditiśāśvataṃ nityamapi brahma ādimatsakārāṇakamivāvasīyata ityanuṣajyate| pariṇāmyupādānatve tasminnabhyupagamyamāne vikāritvaprasaṅgaśaṅkā| vastuto na tatprasaktiḥ, vivarttāpādanatayā tadevāṅgīkārādrajjvādau prātibhāsikabhujaṅgasyeveti bhāvaḥ| `anādi brahmasāśvatam| iti pāṭhastvanyataravise,ṇopādanavaiyarthyamata āha-brahmaveti tathāca prakṛte brahmapadasya svaprakāśarūpabrahmaparatvānnoktānupapattiritita bhāvaḥ| "tameva bhāntam" ityasya-"na tatra sūryo bhāti na candratārakaṃ nemā vidyato bhānti kuto'yamagniḥ" iti pūrvāddham| śrutītyupalakṣaṇam-yadādityagataṃ tejo jagadbhāsayate'khilam, ityādisma़terapi| svaparaprakāśatvamiti tatra paraprakāśakatve māmuktam| svaprakāśakatvañca-svabhinnāprakāśyatve sati prakāśyatvam, svātmakanityajñāniviṣayatvamiti yāvat| "nityaṃ vijñānamānandaṃ brahma" iti śrutyā brahmaṇo nityaṃ vijñānamānandaṃ brahma" iti śrutyā brahmaṇo nityajñānānandasvarūpatvapratipādanāt prakṛtaśratiḥ 'tvayamevātmanātmānaṃ vesti tvaṃ puruṣottam' iti smṛtiśca svaprakāśatve parāprakāśyatve ca " na tadbhāsayate sūryyaḥ" ityādi smṛtiśca mānam, adhikamanyatrānusanveyam| sūcayatīti sphoṭasvaprakāśapadayoḥ paryyāyatvaṃ bodhayatītyarthaḥ| tathāca jātisphoṭavādimate upādhinirāsadvārā sabdabrahmaṇaḥ svaprakāśatvarūpeṇa puruṣārthopayogini jñāne vyākaramasyopayoga iti siddham| nādasphoṭavādimate yathopayogastathopapāditaṃ prāk| nirvidhneti apratibaddhasvagranthapracārārthamityarthaḥ| ante-granthāvasāne| maṅgalamiti natyātmakaṃ tadityarthaḥ| "maṅgalādīni maṅgalāntāni maṅgalāmadhyāni ca śāstrāṇi prathante" iti bhāṣyakārokśruteriti bhāvaḥ| pūrṇatvamakhaṇḍāndatvamapratihateccatvaṃ vā tena rūpeṇa tannatiśca svīyagranthapracārecchāviṣayasiddhya iti bodhyam 
jñātvā śrīphaṇivākyajālamatulaṃ naiyāyikoktīrapi mīmāsānayamākalayya ca mayā samyakkṛte darpaṇe|
asmin bhūṣaṇasāratāṃ budhajanaḥ sotpaṇṭhasamālokatāṃ yanniśvāsamalīmayo na mukurastattvārthabodhakṣamaḥ || 1 ||
āsīt kurmagirau dharāsuravaraḥ śrīvallabhaḥ kovido dedānteṣu vinodamañjaririti khyātā'sti yannirmatiḥ|
tatsūnurharivallabhaḥ samakarotsadyutimaṇyujbalaṃ śrīmad‌bhūṣaṇasāradarppaṇamimammodāya vidyāvatām || 2 ||
